# Sri-bhasya \[San\]

## Mangalacarana

*śrīḥ \|*

*śriyaḥ kāntāya namaḥ \|*

*śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ \|\| śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-granthamālā \|\|*

***śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣyam \|\| (śrī-bhāṣyam)***

***prathama-adhyāye—prathamaḥ pādaḥ—jijñāsā-adhikaraṇam \|\|***

*akhila-bhuvana-janma-sthema-bhaṅga-ādi-līle vinata-vividha-bhūta-vrāta-rakṣaka-dīkṣe \|*

*śruti-śirasi vidīpte brahmaṇi śrīnivāse bhavatu mama parasmin śemuṣī bhakti-rūpā \|\|*

*pārāśarya-vacaḥ-sudhām upaniṣad-dugdha-abdhi-madhya-uddhṛtāṃ saṃsāra-agni-vidīpana-vyapagata-prāṇa-ātma-saṃjīvanīṃ \|*

*pūrva-ācārya-surakṣitāṃ bahu-mati-vyāghāta-dūra-sthitām ānītāṃ tu nija-akṣaraiḥ sumanasaḥ bhīmāḥ pibantu anvaham \|\|*

*bhagavad-bodhāyana-kṛtāṃ vistīrṇāṃ brahma-sūtra-vṛttiṃ pūrva-ācāryāḥ saṃcikṣipuḥ; tan-mata-anusāreṇa sūtra-akṣarāṇi vyākhyāsyante—*

## Adhyaya 1

## Pada 1

## \|\| 1.1.1 \|\|

*oṃ \|\| atha ataḥ brahma-jijñāsā*

**Комментарий:**

atra ayam atha-śabdaḥ ānantarye bhavati \| ataḥ śabdaḥ vṛttasya hetu-bhāve \| adhīta-sāṅga-śiraska-vedasya adhigata-alpa-asthira-phala-kevala-karma-jñānatayā saṃjāta-mokṣa-abhilāṣasya ananta-sthira-phala-brahma-jijñāsā hi anantara-bhāvinī \|\|

brahmaṇaḥ jijñāsā brahma-jijñāsā \| brahmaṇaḥ iti karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī, kartuḥ karmaṇoḥ kṛti iti viśeṣa-vidhānāt \| yady api sambandha-sāmānya-paryavasite api jijñāsāyāḥ karma-apekṣatvena karma-arthatva-siddhiḥ; tathā api apākṣepataḥ prāptāt abhidhānika-ṣaṣṭhī eva grāhyatvāt karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī gṛhyate \| na ca pratipad-vidhānā ca ṣaṣṭhī na samasyate iti karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhyāḥ samāsa-niṣedhaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ; kṛd-yogā ca ṣaṣṭhī samasyate iti pratiṣedha-saṃkocāt \| brahma-śabdena ca svabhāvataḥ nirasta-nikhila-doṣaḥ anavadhika-atiśaya-asaṃkhyeya-kalyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇaḥ puruṣottamaḥ abhidhīyate \| sarvatra bṛhattva-guṇa-yogena hi brahma-śabdaḥ; bṛhattvaṃ ca svarūpeṇa guṇaiḥ ca yatra anavadhika-atiśayaṃ saḥ asya mukhyaḥ arthaḥ; saḥ ca sarveśvaraḥ eva \| ataḥ brahma-śabdaḥ tatra eva mukhya-vṛttaḥ; tasmāt anyatra tad-upacāreṇa aupacārikaḥ; aneka-artha-kalpanā-ayogāt, bhagavat-śabdavat \| tatra api ādara-smṛtatvāya saḥ eva jijñāsyaḥ \| ataḥ sarveśvaraḥ eva jijñāsā-karma-bhūtaṃ brahma \| jñātum icchā jijñāsā \| icchāyāḥ iṣyamāṇa-pradhānatvāt iṣyamānaṃ jñānam iha vidhīyate \|\|

śrī-bhagavad-bhāṣy-uja-prasthālayaṃ śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣyam \|

mīmāṃsā-pūrva-bhāga-jñātasya karma-aṅga-upasthiti-uttara-phalatva-duḥkha-pariṇata-bhoga-anta-aiśvaryasya brahma-jñāna-sthāna-antarātya-phalatvāt ca pūrva-anantaraṃ jñāna-anantaraṃ tatra eva heto brahma-jñāna-arthi-niṣṭhā bhavati \| tathā aha vṛttikāraḥ—vṛttāt karma-adhigama-anantaraṃ brahma-vividiṣā, iti \| vakṣyati ca karma-brahma-mīmāṃsayoḥ eka-śāstratvam—saṃhitam etat śārīrakaṃ jaiminīyena ṣoḍaśa-lakṣaṇena iti śāstra-ekatva-siddhiḥ, iti \| ataḥ pratipipādayiṣita-artha-bhedāt ṣaṭka-araikavat adhyāya-bhedavat ca pūrva-uttara-mīmāṃsayoḥ bhedaḥ \| mīmāṃsā-śāstram—atha ataḥ dharma-jijñāsā, iti ārabhya anāvṛttiḥ śabdāt anāvṛttiḥ śabdāt, iti evam-antaṃ ṣaḍnati-viseṣaṇaṃ viśiṣṭa-kramam \| tathā hi—prathamaṃ tāvat svādhyāyaḥ adhyetavyaḥ, iti adhyayanena eva svādhyāya-śabda-vācya-veda-artha-akṣara-rāśi-grahaṇaṃ vidhīyate \| tat ca adhyayanaṃ kim-arthaṃ, kathaṃ ca kartavyam iti apekṣāyām āṣṭamaṃ brāhmaṇam upanayanīta tam adhyāpayīta iti etena, śrāvaṇyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vā upākṛtya yathāvidhi \| yuktaḥ chandasi adhīyīta māsān vipra ṣaḍardha-pañcamān \|\| iti ādi-vrata-niyama-viśeṣa-upadeśa-aśikṣitāni vidhīyante \| evaṃ satata-abhyasta-vṛtta-saṃcāri-nirohām-guṇa-upeta-veda-vid-ācārya-upakṛtatayā vrata-niyama-viśeṣa-yuktasya ācārya-upacaraṇāt ca āragahanam akṣara-rāśi-grahaṇa-phalam adhyayanaṃ nirvartyamāyate \| adhyayanaṃ ca svādhyāya-saṃskāraḥ; svādhyāyaḥ adhyetavyaḥ, iti svādhyāyasya kartavyatā-mātram \| saṃskāraḥ hi nāma kārya-antara-yogyatā-karaṇam \| saṃskāryatve ca svādhyāyasya yuktam, dharma-adhikāra-mokṣa-rūpa-puruṣārtha-catuṣṭaya-sādhanatva-avabodhitvāt; japa-ādinā svarūpeṇa api tat-sādhanatvāt ca \|\|

evam adhyayana-vidhi-mātravat niyamavat akṣara-rāśi-grahaṇa-mātre paryavasyati \| adhyayana-gṛhītasya svādhyāyasya svabhāvāt eva prayojanavat-artha-avabodhakatva-darśanāt, gṛhīta-tat-svādhyāyāt avāpyamānāt prayojanavataḥ arthinā āpatataḥ dṛṣṭvā tat-svarūpa-prakāra-viśeṣa-nirṇaya-phala-veda-vākya-vicāra-rūpa-mīmāṃsā-śravaṇe adhiti-vedaḥ puruṣaḥ svayam eva pravartate \| tatra karma-avidhi-rūpe nikhile karmāṇam alpa-sthira-phalatvaṃ dṛṣṭvā adhyayana-gṛhīta-svādhyāya-eka-deśa-upaniṣad-vākyeṣu ca amṛtatva-rūpa-ananta-sthira-phala-āpāta-pratīteḥ tat-nirṇaya-phala-vedānta-vākya-vicāra-rūpa-śārīraka-mīmāṃsāyām adhikaroti \| tathā ca vedānta-vākyāni kevala-karma-phalasya kṣayitvaṃ brahma-jñānasya ca akṣaya-phalatvaṃ darśayanti—tad yathā iha karma-citaḥ lokaḥ kṣīyate evam eva amutra puṇya-citaḥ lokaḥ kṣīyate, antavat eva asya tat bhavati, na hi adhruvaiḥ prāpyate, plavāḥ hi ete adṛḍhāḥ yajña-rūpāḥ, parīkṣya lokān karma-citān brāhmaṇaḥ nirvedam āyāt na asti akṛtaḥ kṛtena \| tad vijñāna-arthaṃ sa gurum eva abhigacchet samit-pāṇiḥ śrotriyaṃ brahma-niṣṭham \|\| tasmai sa vidvān upasannāya samyak praśānta-cittāya śama-anvitāya \| yena akṣaraṃ puruṣaṃ veda satyaṃ provāca tāṃ tattvataḥ brahma-vidyām \|\| brahma-vit āpnoti param, na punaḥ mṛtyave tat evaṃ paśyati, na paśyaḥ mṛtyuṃ paśyati, sa svarāṭ bhavati, tam evaṃ vidvān amṛtaḥ iha bhavati, na anyaḥ panthāḥ ayanāya vidyate, pṛthak ātmānaṃ preritāraṃ ca matvā juṣṭaḥ tataḥ tena amṛtatvam eti iti ādīni \|\|

nanu ca—śāstraiḥ etādhyatayā eva karmāṇi svargādi-phalatvam, svargādīnāṃ ca kṣayitvaṃ; brahma-upāsanasya amṛtatva-phalatvaṃ ca jñāyata eva; anantaraṃ mumukṣuḥ hav-yijñāsāyām eva pravartatām, kimarthaṃ dharma-vicāra-apekṣā ? evaṃ tarhi śārīraka-mīmāṃsāyām api na pravartetām, sāṅga-adhyayanāt eva kṛtsnasya jñātatvāt \| satyam \| āpata-pratīti-nibicata eva, tathāpi nyāya-anugṛhītasya vākya-svārtha-niścāyakatvāt arpaṇāvatāritaḥ api artha-saṃśaya-vipayaryayau na ativartete; ataḥ tat-nirṇayāya vedānta-vākya-vicāraḥ kartavya iti cet, tathā eva dharma-vicāre api kartavya iti paśyatu bhavān \|\|

**( laghu-pūrvapakṣaḥ )**

nanu ca—brahma-jijñāsā yad eva niyamena apekṣate, tad eva pūrva-vṛttaṃ vaktavyam, na dharma-vicāra-apekṣā brahma-jijñāsāyāḥ; adhīta-vedāntasya adhigata-karma-aṅgasya api vedānta-vākya-artha-vicāra-upapannaḥ \| karma-aṅga-upayukta-nīthyopapāduḥpāsana-ādi atra eva cintyante \| tat na adhigata-karmaṇo na śakyaṃ kartum—iti cet, anabhijñaḥ bhavān śārīraka-śāstra-adhikārasya \| asmin śāstre anādi-avidyā-kṛta-vividha-bheda-darśa-nirmitta-janma-jarā-maraṇa-ādi-saṃsārikaḥkha-sāgara-nimagnasya nikhila-duḥkha-mūla-mithyā-jñāna-nirhāṇāya ātma-aikatva-vijñānaṃ pratipipādayiṣitam \| asya hi bheda-avalambi-karma-kāṇḍaṃ ka upayujyate ? pratyuta viruddham eva \| upaniṣad-vicāraḥ tu karma-śeṣa-bhūta eva jñāna-svarūpatva-viśeṣavit eva kriyate \| sa tu na sākṣāt apekṣataḥ \| ataḥ yathā-pradhānaṃ śāstraṃ tad-apekṣitam eva pūrva-vṛttaṃ kim api vaktavyam \|\|

bāḍham \| tad-apekṣitaṃ ca karma-jñānam eva, karma-samṛddhatvāt jñānāt upavarṣa-śruteḥ \| vakṣyati ca—sarvāpekṣā ca yajñādi-śruteḥ aśvavat, iti \| apekṣite ca karmadhyāte kena sambandhaḥ? kena na iti vibhāgaḥ na śakyate jñātum \| avastutā eva pūrva-vṛttam \| na etat yuktaṃ; sakala-viśeṣa-pratyanīka-cin-mātra-brahma-vijñānāt eva avidyā-nivṛtteḥ \| avidyā-nivṛttiḥ eva hi mokṣaḥ \| varṇa-āśrama-viśeṣa-sādhya-sādhana-kartavyatā-adhīna-ananta-vikalpa-āspadaṃ karma sakala-bheda-darśana-nivṛtti-rūpa-jñāna-nivṛtteḥ katham iva sādhanaṃ bhavet ? śrutyā eva karmāṇām anitya-phalatve eva mokṣa-virodhitvaṃ, jñānasya eva mokṣa-sādhanatvaṃ ca darśitam—antavat eva asya tat bhavati, tat yathā iha karma-citaḥ lokaḥ kṣīyate, evam eva amutra puṇya-citaḥ lokaḥ kṣīyate, brahmavit āpnoti param, brahma veda brahma eva bhavati, tam eva viditvā ati-mṛtyum eti—ityādyāḥ \|\|

yad api ca uduktaṃ yajñādi-karma-apekṣā vidhā iti, taṭ astu virodhāt śruti-akṣara-parīkṣanepa cātus-karaṇa-nainyadhāraṇa vidhi-vidhoṣa-sādhupadyate; na phala-utsantaḥ, vividiṣanti, iti śravaṇāt \| vividiṣā-arthaṃ jātāyāṃ jñāna-utpatto śama-ādīnām eva antaraṅga-upāsanaṃ śruti-vākya—śāntaḥ dāntaḥ uparataḥ titikṣuḥ samāhitaḥ bhūtvā ātmany eva ātmānaṃ paśyet—iti \| tad eva

śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-munīndrāya śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣyam \|

janmāntara-daśā-anuvṛtta-anabhiṣaṃhita-phala-viśeṣa-karma-vṛtta-kāryasya vivida-viṣayantaḥ mṛtyuḥ, saḥ eva saḥ ayaṃ āsaṃdeham eva advitīyam, satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma, niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāntam, ayam ātmā brahma, tat tvam asi, iti-ādi-vākya-janya-brahmātma-ādvi-vidyā nivartate \| vākyārtha-jñāna-upayogīni ca śravaṇa-manana-nididhyāsanāni \| śravaṇaṃ nāma vedānta-vākyāni ātmāskatva-vidyā-pratipādakāni iti tanvadarśita ācāryāt lakṣaṇa-yukta-agrāhaṇam \| evam ācārya-upadiṣṭasya arthasya svātantryeṇa eva yuktim iti hetutaḥ pratiṣṭhāpanaṃ mananam; etad-virodhi-ādi-dhevāsanā-tiraskārāreyaḥ arthasya anavarata-bhāvanā nididhyāsanam \| śravaṇa-ādibhiḥ anavaratam abhyasyamānasya vākyārtha-jñāna-anirvṛttiḥ nirvartsyati iti, evaṃ-rūpasya śravaṇasya arthasya eva apekṣitā eva pūrva-vṛttaṃ vaktavyam \| tatra nitya-anitya-vastu-vivekaḥ, śama-dama-ādi-sādhana-saṃpat, iha-amutra-phala-bhoga-virāgaḥ; mumukṣutvaṃ ca etat sādhana-catuṣṭayam \| anena vinā jijñāsā-anupapatteḥ, artha-saṃbhāvāt vedena eva pūrva-vṛttam iti jñāyate \|\|

etat uktaṃ bhavati—brahma-svarūpāt ca a(bā)dhika-vidyā-mūla-paramārthikaṃ bheda-darśanam eva bandha-mūlam \| bandhasya apāramārthikaḥ \| sa ca samūlaḥ apāramārthikatvāt eva jñānena eva nivartyate \| nivartakaṃ ca jñānaṃ tat tvam asi-ādi-vākya-janyam \| tasya asya vākya-janyasya jñānasya svarūpa-uccāraṇe kāryaṃ vā karmaṇāṃ na upayogaḥ \| vivida-viṣayam eva tu karmaṇām upayogaḥ \| sa ca tāpasūranas tasmin nididhyāsana-dvāreṇa tattva-vidyāyā bhavati iti īm upayoga-mātreṇa eva brāhmaṇā vividivanti, icchanti iti \| ataḥ karma-jñāna-samucyogā uttam eva sādhana-catuṣṭayaṃ pūrva-vṛttam iti vaktavyam \|\| (iti laghu-pūrvapakṣaḥ)

**(laghu-siddhāntaḥ)**

atra ucyate—yat uktam avidyā-nivṛttiḥ eva mokṣaḥ; sā ca brahma-vijñānāt eva bhavati—iti; tat syupagamyate \| avidyā-nivṛttaye vedānta-vākya-vidhitsitaṃ jñānaṃ kiṃ-rūpam iti vivecanīyam—kiṃ vākyāt vākyārtha-jñāna-mātram, uta tat-mūla-upāsanātmakaṃ jñānam ? iti \| na tāvat vākya-janyaṃ jñānam, tasya vidyāmānatve api vākyārthe eva siddheḥ, tāvat-mātreṇa vidyā-nivṛtti-anupalabdheḥ ca \| na ca vācyaṃ—bheda-vāsanāyām anirastāyāṃ vākyārtha-vidyā-nivartakaṃ jñānaṃ na janyati, jātaḥ api sambandhaḥ sahasā eva bheda-jñāna-anivṛtti-nidānāya; candra-ekatve jātaḥ api dvitva-candra-jñāna-anivṛttivat; ativṛttam api chinna-mūlatvena na bādhyaṃ bhavati—iti \| satyāṃ sāmagryāṃ jñāna-anudayasya upapatteḥ; satyām api viparīta-bhāvanāyāṃ mādhveśarīḍguṭikā-ādibhiḥ adhika-jñāna-utsaṃti-śrutatvāt \| sati api vākyārtha-jñāne, anādi-vāsanayā mātrayā bheda-jñāna-anuvṛttate iti bhavataḥ na śakyate vaktum \| bheda-jñāna-sāmagrayā api vāsanayā mithyā-rūpatvena jñāna-utsargy eva nivṛttatvāt \| jñāna-anantarām api mithyā-rūpāyāḥ tathā anivṛtto nivartaka-antara-abhāvāt kadācit api na asyāḥ vāsaṇāyā nivṛttiḥ \| vāsanā-kāryaṃ bheda-jñānaṃ chinna-mūlam atha ca anuvartate iti bāliśa-bhāṣitam \| dvitva-candra-

jñānādeḥ tu bāhya-karma-vidhā api mithyā-jñāna-hetau paramārtha-nimitta-ādi-doṣasya jñāna-bādhyatva-prāmāṇyena anivṛttatvāt mithyā-jñāna-nivṛttiḥ viruddhā \| prabala-pramāṇa-bādhitatvena bhagavad-īhārthaṃ tu nivartate \| api ca bheda-vedānta-anirasta-sahakāreṇa jñānena tvat-siddha-prapañcocchedaṃ kadācit api jñānena sūcitaṃ setsyati, bheda-vedāntānām anadhikāra-ālopeṣitatvena parimitatvāt, tad-virodhi-bhāvanā-pāśravaṇa-svādhyānayā tad-dhī-rasa-anuguṇyante \|\|

tataḥ bādhya-artha-jñāna-tulyam eva dhyāna-upāsana-ādi-śabda-vācyaṃ jñānaṃ vedānta-vākyaiḥ vidhitsitam \| tathā ca śrūyate:— śrotavyaḥ mantavyaḥ nididhyāsitavyaḥ; anudhyeya vijñānitī, oṃ iti eva ātmānaṃ dhyāyatha, nicāyya taṃ mṛtyu-mukhāt pramucyate, ātmānam eva lokam upāsīta, ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ śrotavyaḥ mantavyaḥ nididhyāsitavyaḥ; saḥ anveṣṭavyaḥ saḥ vijijñāsitavyaḥ—iti evam ādyāḥ \| atra nididhyāsitavyaḥ ityādinā aikārthyāt, anudhyeya vijñānitī, vijñāya prajñāṃ kurvīta, iti evamādi-niyavidhāyi-śāstraṃ (tasya) dhyāna-upāsanā-kartavyatāvāt, ‘anuvidya’, ‘vijñāya’ iti anayoḥ prajñāṃ kurvīta, vijñānatī iti dhyānaṃ viśiṣyate \| śrotavyaḥ iti ca anuvādaḥ \| svādhyāyasya arthaḥ tu aikārthi-vedaḥ puruṣaḥ prayojanavat eva adhihita-darśana-ābhilṣaṇīyatvā svayam eva śravaṇe pravartate iti śravaṇasya prāptatvāt \| śravaṇa-vidhiḥ ca veda-mananasya, mantavyaḥ iti ca anuvādaḥ \| tasmāt dhyānam eva vidhīyate \| yat api ca—āvṛttiḥ asakṛt upadeśāt, iti \| tat vedam upavī-pāṭhyayā vihitataḥ taṃ vedam upāsanam iti avagamyate, vidy upās yo dhātināṃ yaḥ api etam arthaṃ dhyānāt manom brahma iti upāsīty atra bhāti ca tapati ca kīrtyā yaśasā brahma-varcasena yaḥ evaṃ veda, na saḥ veda akṛtsnaḥ hi eṣaḥ—ātmā iti eva upāsītā, sarvam hi etat eṣa veda sa mayā etat uktī, iti atra anum evaṃ bhagavaḥ devatāṃ śādhi yāṃ devatām upāsse—iti \|\|

dhyānaṃ ca taila-dhārāvat avicchinna-smṛti-santāna-rūpam, dhruvā smṛtiḥ; smṛti-lambhe sarva-granthīnāṃ vipramokṣaḥ; iti dhruvayā smṛtyai tad-upavarṇīyatvāt śravaṇāt \| sā ca smṛtiḥ darśana-samāna-ākārā; bhidyate hṛdaya-granthiḥ chidyante sarva-saṃśayāḥ \| kṣīyante ca asya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare \|\| hṛty anena aikārthyāt \| evaṃ ca sati, ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ iti etena nididhyāsanasya darśana-samāna-ākāratā vidhīyate \| bhavati ca smṛteḥ bhāvanā-prakarṣāt darśana-rūpatā \|\|

vākyakāreṇa etat sarvaṃ prapañcitam vedanam upāsanaṃ syāt tad-viṣaye śravaṇāt, iti paraviṣup-nirast tu mokṣa-sādhanatayā vihitaṃ vedanam upāsanam iti uktam \| sakṛt-pratyayaṃ kuryāt śabda-arthasya kṛtatvāt pratyaya-avidheyatvāt, iti pūrva-pakṣaṃ kṛtvā, siddhaṃ tu upāsana-śabdāt, iti vedanam askṛt āvṛttaṃ mokṣa-sādhanam iti nirṇītam \| upāsanaṃ syāt dhruvā smṛtiḥ darśanāt śabdaḥ, iti tasya eva vedanasya upāsana-rūpasya smṛti-darśanasya dhruva-anusmṛtitvam upavarṇitam \|

sā iyaṃ smṛti-darśana-rūpā pratipāditā \| darśana-ekāntā ca pratyakṣatā-āpattiḥ \| evaṃ pratyakṣatā-akṣama-bhavat-saṃśaya-bhūtāṃ smṛtiṃ vidhitsuḥ āha— na ayam ātmā pravacanena labhyaḥ na medhayā na bahunā śrutena, yam eva eṣa vṛṇute tena labhyaḥ tasya eṣa ātmā vivṛṇute tanūṃ svām \|\| iti \| anena kevala-śravaṇa-manana-nididhyāsanānāmātra-sādhya-rūpa-pratyagātma-darśanāt, yam eva eṣa ātmā vṛṇute tena eva labhyaḥ iti uktam \| priyatamaḥ eva hi varaṇīyaḥ bhavati, praṇayāṃś ca niratiśaya-priyasya eva asya priyatamaḥ bhavati \| yathā ayam priyatamaḥ ātmānaṃ prāpnoti, tathā svayam eva bhagavān prayatate iti bhagavad-gītā-uktam teṣāṃ satata-yuktānāṃ bhajatāṃ prīti-pūrvakam \| dadāmi buddhi-yogaṃ taṃ yena mām upayānti te \|\| iti, priyaḥ hi jñāninaḥ atyartham ahaṃ sa ca mama priyaḥ \| iti ca \| ananta-sākṣātkāra-rūpā smṛti-santatyā manālatva-rūpa-priyatvena svayam api artha-priyā yasya, sa eva pareṇa ātmanā varaṇīyaḥ bhavati iti tena eva labhyate paraḥ ātmā iti uktaṃ bhavati \| evaṃ-rūpā dhruva-anusmṛtiḥ eva bhakti-śabdena abhidhīyate, upāsanā-paryāya-priya-śabdasya \| ataḥ eva śruti-smṛtibhiḥ evam abhidhīyate— tam eva viditvā atimṛtyum eti, tam eva vidvān amṛtaḥ iha bhavati, na anyaḥ panthā ayanāya vidyate \| na ahaṃ vedaiḥ na tapasā na dānena na ca ijyayā \| śakyaḥ evaṃ-vidhaḥ draṣṭuṃ dṛṣṭavān asi māṃ yathā \|\| bhaktyā tu ananyayā śakyaḥ aham evaṃ-vidhaḥ arjuna ! \| jñātuṃ draṣṭuṃ ca tattvena praveṣṭuṃ ca paraṃtapa ! \|\| puruṣaḥ sa paraḥ pārtha ! bhaktyā labhyaḥ tu ananyayā \|\| iti \| evaṃ-rūpāyāḥ dhruva-anusmṛteḥ ātma-lābha-ānāni prakāra-ādīni kathyanti— āhāra-viśuddhau sattva-śuddhiḥ, dhruvā smṛtiḥ \| yadyapi vividiṣanti iti yajña-ādayaḥ vividiṣā-utpattau vinijyujyante; tathāpi tattvena vedanasya dhyāna-rūpasya ahar-ahar upacīyamānasya āprayāṇāt anuvartamānasya brahma-prāpti-sādhanatvāt tad-utpattaye yajñādi-śrama-karmāṇi yāvaj-jīvam anuṣṭheyāni \| kṣayanti ca āprayāṇāt tatra api hi dṛṣṭam, agnihotra-ādi tu tat-kāryāya eva tad-darśanāt, sahakāritvena ca, ityādibhiḥ \|\|

vākyakāraḥ ca dhruva-anusmṛti-vivekādibhyaḥ eva niṣpattim āha— tat-labdhiḥ viveka-vimoka-abhyāsa-kriyā-kalyāṇa-anavasāda-anuddharṣebhyaḥ sambhavāt nirvacanāt, iti \| vivekādīnāṃ svarūpaṃ ca āha jāti-āśraya-nimitta-aduṣṭāt annāt kāya-śuddhiḥ vivekaḥ, iti \| atra nirvacanam— āhāra-śuddhau sattva-śuddhiḥ sattva-śuddhau dhruvā smṛtiḥ, iti \| vimokaḥ kāma-anabhiṣvaṅgaḥ, iti; śāntaḥ upāsīta, iti nirvacanam \| ārambhaṇa-āśīlanaṃ punaḥ punaḥ abhyāsaḥ, iti \| nirvacanaṃ ca smārtam udāhṛtaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa,— sadā tad-bhāva-bhāvitaḥ; iti \| pañca-mahā-yajña-anuguṇā śaktitaḥ kriyā, iti \| nirvacane— kriyāvān eṣa brahma-vidāṃ variṣṭhaḥ; tam etaṃ veda-anuvacanena brāhmaṇāḥ vividiṣanti yajñena dānena tapasā anāśakena, iti ca \| satya-ārjava-ādayaḥ ahiṃsā-ādayaḥ ca kalyāṇāni, iti \| nirvacane— satyena labhyaḥ, teṣām eva eṣa virajaḥ brahma-lokaḥ, ityādi \| deśa-kāla-vayo-avasthā-loka-vastu-ādhi anusmṛteḥ ca tajjaṃ dainyam anabhisvaratvaṃ manasaḥ avasādaḥ iti, tad-viparyayaḥ anava-

sādaḥ \| nirvacane— śrī-rāmāsamā bala-śobhana lakṣmīḥ, iti \| aditya-varṇā juṣṭi-hṛṣṭa-dyuḥ iti, taddidhyogadbudhaḥ \| ati-sattva-ogra-viśeṣa-arthaḥ \| nirvacanam api—, śāntaḥ dāntaḥ, iti \| evaṃ niyama-yuktasya āśrama-vihita-karma-anuṣṭhāna eva vidyā-nirvartyatvam uktaṃ bhavati, yathā ca śrutyantaraḥ— vidyāṃ ca avidyāṃ ca yaḥ tat veda ubhayaṃ saha \| avidyayā mṛtyuṃ tīrtvā vidyayā amṛtam aśnute \|\| iti \| atra avidyā-śabda-abhihitaṃ varṇa-āśrama-vihitaṃ karma \| avidyayā—karmaṇā, mṛtyuṃ—jñāna-utpatti-virodhi prācīnaṃ karma; tīrtvā-apahṛtya; vidyayā-jñānena \| amṛtaṃ-brahma; aśnute-prāpnoti ity arthaḥ \| mṛtyu-taraṇa-upāyatathā pratītaṃ avidyā virodha-rahitaṃ karma eva \| yathā uktam— ijyāśī saḥ api subrahma yajñān jñāna-dhyāpakaḥ \| brahma-vidyām adhyāsya taruṃ mṛtyum avidyayā \|\| iti \| jñāna-virodhi ca karma puṇya-pāpa-rūpam; brahma-jñāna-utpatti(ni)-virodhitvena nimitta-phalatyābhyām api pāpa-śabda-abhidheyatvam \| asya ca jñāna-virodhitvaṃ jñāna-utpatti-hetu-bhūta-śuddha-sattva-virodhi-rajas-tamobivṛddhi-dvāreṇa \| pāpasya ca jñāna-utpatti-virodhitvam— eṣa eva asādhu karma kārayati taṃ yam adhaḥ ninīṣati, iti śrutyā avagamyate \| rajas-tamas-udreka-artha-jñāna-āvaraṇaṃ, sattvasya ca yathārtha-jñāna-hetutvaṃ bhagavatā eva pratipāditam sattvāt saṃjāyate jñānam, ityādinā \| ataḥ ca jñāna-utpattaye pāpaṃ karma nirasanīyam, tad-virodhataṃ ca anabhisaṃhita-phala-anuṣṭhitena (dharmeṇa) karmaṇā; tathā ca śrutiḥ— dharmeṇa pāpam apanudati, iti \| tat evaṃ brahma-upāsanāt jñāte sarva-āśrama-karma-apekṣam \| ataḥ apekṣita-karma-saṃskṛtātāṃ, kevala-karmaṇāṃ svarga-ādi-sthira-phala-vatāṃ ca karma-mīmāṃsā-vedyān iti, sā eva apekṣitā brahma-jijñāsāyāḥ pūrva-vṛttā vaktavyā \| api ca nitya-anitya-vastu-vivekāt api asya, mīmāṃsā-śravaṇam anantareṇa na saṃpadyate \| phala-karaṇī-kartarvyatā-adhikāri-viśeṣa-niścaya-uddeśe karma-svarūpa-daḍhatva-ratisthiratva-asthiratvavāntinyatva-ādīnāṃ duravabodhatvāt \| evaṃ sādhanatvaṃ ca viniyoga-avagamyam \| viniyogaḥ ca śruti-liṅga-ādibhiḥ \| sa ca tatra eva \| udaṅga-āśādhyupāsanāni karma-samṛddhi-arthāni api brahma-dṛṣṭi-rūpāṇi, brahma-jñāna-apekṣāṇi iti iha eva cintanīyāni \| tāni api karmavat anabhisaṃhita-phalāni brahma-vidyotpattakāni iti, tad-ānuṣaṅguṇyāpatannayotanīni uttarāsmin eva saṅgrāhyāni \| teṣāṃ ca karmasu adharmitayā apekṣā sarva-saṃmatā \|\| (iti laghu-siddhāntaḥ) \|

**( mahā-pūrvapakṣaḥ )**

pratyagāhaḥ— aśeṣa-viśeṣa-pratyanīka-cin-mātraṃ brahma eva paramārthaḥ \| tad-atirikta-ki nāma-vidha-jñātṛ-jñeya-bheda-tadvat sarvaṃ tarhi mithyā eva parikalpitaṃ mithyā-bhūtam \| sat eva somya idam agre āsīt ekam advitīyam, atha parā yayā tat akṣaram adhigamyate yat tat reśyam agrāhyam agotram avarṇam acākṣuḥ-śrotraṃ tat apāṇi-pādaṃ nityaṃ vibhuṃ sarva-gataṃ susūkṣmaṃ tat avyayaṃ yat bhūta-yoniṃ paripaśyanti dhīrāḥ; satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma, niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāntaṃ niravadyaṃ nirañjanam, asthūlam an

śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-yathālayāṃ śārīraka-bhāṣyam \|

tatya mataṃ mataṃ yasya na veda saḥ \| adhigataṃ vijānatāṃ vijñānam avijñātāt—na haṣṭeḥ dhāraṃ payaḥ; na manasaṃtāraṃ mantavyaḥ; ānando brahma, idaṃ sarvaṃ yat ayam ātmā, na iha nānā asti kiñcana, mṛtyoḥ sa mṛtyum āpnoti ya iha nānā iva paśyati, yatra hi dvaitam iva bhavati tad itara itaraṃ paśyati yatra tu asya sarvam ātmā eva abhūt tat kena kaṃ paśyet kena kaṃ vijānīyāt, yaḥ vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛttikā iti eva satyam, yadā hi eva eṣa etasmin udaram antaraṃ kurute, atha tasya bhayaṃ bhavati, na sthānataḥ api parasya ubhaya-liṅgaṃ sarvatra hi, māyā-mātraṃ tu kārtsnyena anabhivyakta-svarūpatvāt, pratyastamita-bhedaṃ yat sattā-mātram agocaram \| vacasām ātma-saṃvedyaṃ taj jñānaṃ brahma-saṃjñitam \|\| jñāna-svarūpam atyanta-nirmalaṃ paramārthataḥ \| tam eva artha-svarūpeṇa bhrānti-nirjñānataḥ sthitam \|\|

paramārthaḥ tu eko na anyaḥ asti jagataḥ pate \| \|\| yat etad dṛśyate mūrtaṃ taj jñānātmanaḥ tava \| bhrānti-jñānena paśyanti jagad-rūpam ayoginaḥ \|\| jñāna-svarūpam akhilaṃ jagad etad abuddhayaḥ \| artha-svarūpaṃ paśyanto bhrāmyante mohamaṃbhave \|\| ye tu jñānavidaḥ śuddha-cetasas te akhilaṃ jagat \| jñānātmakaṃ prapaśyanti tvad-rūpaṃ parameśvara ! \|\| tasya ātma-para-deheṣu sataḥ api ekamayaṃ hi yat \| vijñānaṃ paramārthaḥ hi dvaitinaḥ artha-darśinaḥ \|\| yadi anyad asti paraḥ kaḥ api mattaḥ pārthiva-sattama ! tad eva u aham ayaṃ ca anyaḥ vakṣyate paramārthataḥ \|\| veṇu-randhra-vibhedena bheda-ṣaḍja-ādi-dīritaḥ \| abheda-vyāpinaḥ vāyoḥ stathā asau paramātmanaḥ \|\| saḥ ahaṃ sa ca tvaṃ sa ca sarvam etad ātma-svarūpaṃ tyaja bheda-moham \|\| ityādibhiḥ teta

sa rāja-vyañjana-tyāja bhedaṃ paramārtha-dṛṣṭiḥ \|\| viśuddha-jñāna-dehāya nātha-māyādhikaṃ gate \| ātmānaḥ brahmaṇaḥ bhedam asantaṃ kaḥ kariṣyati \|\| brahma-bhāvaṃ tu guḍākeśa ! sarva-bhūta-āśaya-sthitaḥ \|\| kṣetrajñaṃ ca api māṃ viddhi sarva-kṣetreṣu bhārata ! na tad asti vinā yat syāt mayā bhūtaṃ carācaram \|\| ityādibhiḥ veda-śruty-upadeśa-upadeśāḥ ca; nirviśeṣa-cinmātraṃ brahma eva satyam anyat sarvaṃ mithyā, ity abhidhānāt \|\|

\[mithyātvaṃ nāma, pratīyamānatapūrvaka-anyathāvasthita-vastu-jñāna-nirvarttyatvam, yathā rajjuvādhisthāna-sarpa-ādeḥ \| doṣeśa-upahati tatra tat-kalpanam \| evaṃ cinmātra-brahmaṇi pare brahmaṇi doṣa-upariklpta-vividha-deva-tiryaṅ-manuṣya-sthāvara-ādi-bhede sarvaṃ jagad yathāsthita-brahma-svarūpa-avabodha-vādhyaṃ mithyātma-rūpam \| doṣaḥ ca svabhāvataḥ anādi-vividha-vicitra-viṣayakaraḥ sadasadvicchanīya-ajñānād avidyā \| anṛtena hi pratyūḍhāḥ devāṃ satyānāṃ satām ṛtumasthidhānam, na asat āsīt no sat āsīt citvāsī tam āsīt tamasā gūḍham agre aketayam, māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāt māyinaṃ tu maheśvaram, indraḥ māyābhiḥ pururūpaḥ īyate, mama māyā duratyayā, ṛtam abhibhūya asutaḥ yathā jīvaḥ pramuhyate— ityādibhiḥ nirviśeṣa-cinmātraṃ brahma eva nānāvidha-vidyā sadasadvicchanīyayā tirohita-brahmaṃ svāgāt-

nānānātatvaṃ paśyati ity avagamyate \| yathoktam— jñāna-svarūpaḥ bhagavān yataḥ asāv aśeṣa-mūrtiḥ na tu vastu-bhūtaḥ \| tataḥ hi śaila-abdhi-dhara-ādi-medān jānīhi vijñāna-vijṛmbhitāni \|\| yadā tu śuddhaṃ nija-

rūpi sarva-eka-nikṣepo jñāna-samāna-tadvatam \| tadā hi maḍgulupataraḥ phalāni bhavati iti yo dāhyatu vastu-bhedaḥ \|\| śrutasya ca vijñānam upadiśati kiñcid vai citram ā viddhi \| vastu-jātasya \| vijñāna-ekaṃ nija-karmabhair vibhakta-citi-bandhāḍhyam upaitam \|\| jñātaṃ vidhurdaṃ vimalaṃ viśokam aśoba-lobhādi-nirasta-sajjñam \| ekaṃ sadā ekaṃ paramaṃ pareśam vāśudevo na yataḥ anyad asti \|\| saḍrūpam evaṃ bhagato mayoktaṃ jñānaṃ yathā satyam asatyam anyat \| etat tu yat saṃvyavahāra-sūtaṃ tathā api ca uktāṃ bhuvanāśrisaṃ te \|\| iti \|

asyāḥ ca avidyāyā niviśeṣa-cinmātra-brahmātmaka-ekatva-vijñānena nivṛttiṃ vadanti— śrṇu punas tv ekaṃ parārthaṃ, śrṇu paryaṃ mūlyaṃ padyati, śraddhā hṛ vaiva purutimasahayodhasnānastholiniroḍhilayahetubhyaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ vindate, atha soḍaśaṃ gato bhavati, chidyate hṛdaya-granthiḥ chidyante sarva-saṃśayāḥ \| kṣīyante ca asya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare \|\| brahma veda brahma eva bhavati, brahma eva viditvā atimṛtyum eti, nānyaḥ panthāḥ, ity-ādyāḥ ca tataḥ \| atra mṛtyu-śabdena avidyā abhidhīyate \| yathā, santamūlāḥ tava canaṃ pramādaṃ vai mṛtyum ahaṃ bravīmi sadā apramādam amṛtatvaṃ bravīmi— iti \| ayaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma, ity-ādi-śrutiḥ bhedābheda-kṛtāvodya-nirviśeṣa-cinmātra-svarūpa-(brahma eva kevala)-brahmātmaikatva-vijñānaṃ ca, atha yo ’nyāṃ devatām upāste anyaḥ asau anyaḥ aham asmi iti na sa veda, ahaṃkārto hy aṃśaḥ; ātmā iti eva upāsīta, anuvādasi, tvaṃ vā aham asmi bhagavo devate ahaṃ vai tvam asi bhagavo devate, na ca idaṃ soḍaśī yoḍaśī vedaḥ aham asmi, ity-ādi-vākya-siddham \| vakṣyati ca etad eva—

ātmā iti tu upacchanti grāhayanti ca, iti \| yathā ca vākya-kāraḥ ātmā iti eva tu gṛhṇīyāt sarvasya tan niṣpattiḥ, iti \| anena ca brahmātmaikatva-vijñānena mithyā-rūpasya sa-kāraṇasya bandhasya nivṛttiḥ yuktā \|\|

nanu ca, sakala-bheda-nivṛtteḥ pratyakṣa-vicchadā katham iva śāstra-janya-(vi)jñānena kriyate? kathaṃ vā ‘rajju-dveṣā na sarpaḥ’ iti jñānena pratyakṣa-vicchadā sarpa-nivṛttiḥ kriyate? tatra brūyaḥ pratyakṣa-yogi-virodhaḥ; iha tu pratyakṣa-mūlasya śāstrasya pratyakṣasya ca iti cetaḥ; tulyo api virodhe vā kathaṃ bādhya-bādhaka-bhāvaḥ? pūrva-uttarayor hetu-kāraṇa-nyāyavat tad abhāvyam ity iti cetaḥ; śāstratva-atyayaḥ api samānam etat \|

evam uktaṃ bhavati— bādhya-bādhaka-bhāve tulya-bala-apekṣa-nirapekṣatvādi na kāraṇam, jvalana-bhedāt tu mānin pratyakṣaḥ apavado gṛhīta \| tatra hi jvalanaṃ pratyakṣeṇa avagamyate \| evaṃ ca sati brūyaḥ pramāṇayor virodhe yat sambhāvyamāna-anyathā-siddhi, tad eva bādhyam; ananyathā-siddham anavakāśam itarad bādhakam— iti sarvatra bādhya-bādhaka-bhāva-niścaya iti \| tasminn avahitāvidyādi-nivṛtti-saṃśraya-alaṃbhāvyamāna-doṣa-nyāna-vaśīkaraṇa-ajanma-nirviśeṣa-cinmātra brahmātma-bhāva-avabodhena sambhāvyamāna-doṣatva-avakāśa-pratyakṣādi-siddhād vividha-vikalpa-rūpa-bandha-nivṛtti-

kṛteḥ \| saṃbhāvyate ca vividha-vikalpa-bheda-prapañca-ahiṃsādy-akṣayya-ānādi-bheda-vāstavad-virūpa-vidyāstha-doṣaḥ \|\|

nanu, śruta-ādīnāṃ vidyād-vividha-saṃbandhayā nirdeśasya api śāstrastham, jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajeta iti etasmāt eva kalpa-siddho bādhyatvaṃ pratyate \| satyam; pūrva-vicāra-praṣṭhe pūrvoktavat niḥśeṣa-ahṛdaya niravakāśatvena bādhyate eva \| vedānta-vākyeṣu api saguṇa-brahmopāsana-parāṇāṃ śāstrāṇām asya iva nyāyaḥ; nirguṇa-tattvarasya brahmaṇaḥ \| nanu ca, sarva-vedaṃsu yad vitta, parā asya śaktiḥ vividha eva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca, satya-kāma-satya-saṅkalpaḥ, ityādi-śruti-svarūpa-pratipādana-parāṇāṃ śāstrāṇāṃ kathaṃ bādhyatvam? nirguṇa-vākya-samadhyastiti brūmaḥ \|\|

eṣuktā bhavati—akhaṇḍam anantatva-vadanīyaṃ, satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma, nirguṇam, nirañjanam—ityādi-vākyāni nirasta-samasta-viśeṣa-kūṭastha-cinmātra-viṣayatayā brahma iti pratipādayanti; itarāṇi ca saguṇam \| ubhaya-vidha-vākyānāṃ virodhe tena eva apaccheda-nyāyena nirguṇa-vākyānāṃ guṇa-apekṣatvena paratvād vaitogyatvarīti na kiñcit vademaḥ \|\|

nanu ca, satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma ity atra satya-jñāna-ādayo guṇāḥ pratīyante \| na iti ucyate, samānādhikaraṇyena eka-ārthyatva-pratoḥ \| aneka-guṇa-viśiṣṭa-artha-abhidhānaika-tārthyaṃ vacana-viruddham iti cet; abhidhānānāṃ devānāṃ priyaḥ \| eka-arthatvaṃ nāma sarva-padānām artha-aikyem; viśiṣṭa-padārtha-abhidhāne viśeṣaṇānaita padānām artha-daurjanyam; tataḥ tad-eka-arthatvaṃ na sidhyati \| evaṃ tarhi sarva-padānāṃ paryāyatā syāt, aviśiṣṭa-artha-abhidhāyitvāt \| eka-artha-abhidhāyitve api aparyāyatvam abhihitam anāśrayam \| ekatvātatparatmiśrayekasya eva arthyasya tataḥ tad-artha-virodhasya anīkava-paratvena sarva-padānām arthatve eka-arthavan-maryādā ca \|

evam uktaṃ bhavati—lakṣaṇataḥ pratipattavyaṃ brahma sakala-itara-padārtha-virodhi-rūpam \| tad-virodhi-rūpaṃ satyam atena padatvena phalato dyudyate \| tatra satya-padaṃ vikāra-rūpa-padārthana-satyatvastuno vyāvṛtta-brahma-param \| jñāna-padaṃ ca anādi-anta-kāśa-jaḍa-rūpād astuno vyāvṛtta-param \| ananta-padaṃ ca deśataḥ kālato vastutaḥ ca paricchedāt uvyāvṛtta-param \| na ca vyāvṛttiḥ abhāvopoḍhā vā dharmaḥ \| api tu sakala-itara-virodhi brahma eva \| yathā śakti-padāḥ kāruṇya-divyādi-viśeṣitānāṃ padārtha-svarūpam eva, na dharma-antarasya \| evam ekaṃva vastu nissakaletaravirodhyākāratām avagamayat vacanatam eka-artha-paryaṃ ca pada-cayam \| tasmāt ekaṃva brahma svayaṃjyotir-nirghūta-nikhila-viśeṣam uktaṃ bhavati \|\|

evaṃ vākya-artha-pratipādane sati eva, sat eva somya idam agre āsīt ekam advitīyam ityādi-dhirakārtham \| mṛto vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante, sat eva somya idam agre āsīt, ātmā vā idam ekaḥ eva agre āsīt—ityādi-viśva-jani-kāraṇatva-upalakṣitasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpam eṣu te

**prathama-adhyāye—prathama-pāde—jijñāsā-adhikaraṇe mahā-pūrvapakṣaḥ \|**

satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti \| tatra sarveṣāṃ padānām asya eva kāraṇa-vākyena sarveṣu sajātīya-vijātīya-vyāvṛtta-dvitīyaṃ brahma ekam eva \| jñāna-kāraṇatva-upalakṣitasya brahmaṇo 'dvitīyasya pratipipādayiṣite svarūpaṃ tartiricchena vaktavyam \| advitīyatva-śruter vṛṇāto 'pi sadvitīyatāṃ na sahate \| anyathā, nirgṛṇam, nirguṇam, ityādibhiś ca virodhaḥ \| ataś ca etat-lakṣaṇa-vācyam asakhaṇḍa-eka-rasam eva pratipādayati \|\|

nanu ca, satya-jñāna-ādi-padānāṃ svārtha-prahāṇena svārtha-virodhi-vyāvṛtta-vastu-varūpa-upasthāpana-paratve lakṣaṇā syāt \| na eṣa doṣaḥ, abhidhāna-dvāreṇa eva tātparya-vṛtter bīyastvāt \| sāmānādhikaraṇyasya hi ekam eva tātparyam iti sarva-saṃmatam \| nanu ca, sarva-padānāṃ lakṣaṇā na dṛṣṭacarī \| tataḥ kim ? vākya-tātparya-virodhe sati ekasya api na dṛṣṭā \| samabhivyāhṛta-pada-samudāyasya anantatva-bhrānti-nirhṛtte sati hi ekasyāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ vā tad-virodhāya ekatra eva lakṣaṇā na doṣāya \| tathā ca śāstrasthaiḥ eva upapādyate \| kāryatva-avyarthatva-ādibhir nīla-ādika-vākyreṣu sarveṣāṃ padānāṃ lakṣaṇā samāśrīyate \| apūrva-kārya eva liṅ-ādir eva utsukatvāt liṅ-ādibhiḥ kriyā-kāryaṃ lakṣaṇayā pratipādyate; kārya-anvitatva-artha-abhidhāyināṃ ca itareṣāṃ pūrvottaram apūrva-kārya-anvita eva mukhya-artha iti kriyā-kārya-anvitatva-rahitatva-darśanaṃ lākṣaṇikam eva \| ato vākya-tātparya-virodhāya sarva-padānāṃ lakṣaṇāyām api na doṣaḥ \| ata idam eva artha-jātaṃ pratipādayanto vedāntāḥ pramāṇam \|\|

prakṣāḷādi-virodhe ca śāstrasya balīyastvam uktam \| sati ca virodhe balīyastvaṃ vaktavyam \| virodha eva na dṛśyate, nirviśeṣa-sanmātra-brahma-abhidhāna-śruti-arthasya \| nanu ca, ghaṭo 'sti paṭo 'sti iti nānākāra-vastu-viṣayaṃ pratyakṣaṃ katham iva sanmātra-grāhī iti ucyate ? vilakṣaṇa-guṇa-abhāve sati sarveṣāṃ jñānānām eka-viṣayatvena dhārāvāhika-eka-vijñānavad eka-vyavahāra-hetutvaṃ syāt \| satyam; tatra eva vicikitsate \| katham ? ghaṭo 'sti ity atra astitvaṃ taddeśyaṃ vyavahriyate \| na ca hi ayor api vyavahārayoḥ pratyakṣa-mūlatvaṃ saṃbhavati, vyoma-niṣkalaṅka-ānaphalatvāt, pratyakṣa-jñānasya ca eka-kṣaṇa-vartitvāt \| tatra svarūpaṃ vā bhedo vā pratyakṣasya viṣaya iti vicācanīyam \| indriya-grāhya-svarūpa-grahaṇa-tatsatiyogi-smaraṇa-madhyekṣitvād eva svarūpa-viṣaya-dṛḍham adhyakṣa-praṇīyam iti na bhedaḥ pratyakṣeṇa gṛhyate \| ato bhedi-nūla eva bheda-vyavahāraḥ \|\|

kiñ ca bhedo nāma kaścit padārthaḥ ; guṇādivan nirūpituṃ na śakyate \| aindriyāś ca atra vastu-svarūpam, vastu-svarūpe gṛhīte svarūpa-vyavahāravat sarvasmād idaṃ tad-vayavahāra-prasaktaḥ \| na ca vācyam, svarūpe gṛhīte 'pi bhinna iti vyavahārasya pratiyogi-smaraṇa-madhya-apekṣatvāt, tat-smaraṇa-abhāvena tadānīm eva na bheda-vyavahāra iti \| svarūpa-mātra-bheda-vādinaḥ hi pratiyogi-apekṣā ca na apekṣituṃ kṣamā, svarūpa-bhedayoḥ svarūpatva-viśeṣāt \| yathā svarūpa-vyavahāro na pratiyogi-apekṣaḥ; bheda-

vyavahāraḥ api tathā eva syāt \| hariḥ karaḥ iti vat ghaṭaḥ bhinnaḥ iti paryāyatvaṃ ca syāt \| na api dharmaḥ; dharmitve sati tasya svarūpāt bhedasya śravaṇāt; anyathā svarūpam eva syāt \| bhede ca tasya api bheda-tad-dharma-tad-dharmi-svarūpāntarathā \| kiñca jāti-ādi-viśiṣṭa-vastu-grahaṇe sati bheda-grahaṇam, bheda-grahaṇe sati jāti-ādi-viśiṣṭa-vastu-grahaṇa-anyonyāśraya-prasaṅgaḥ \| ataḥ bhedasya durnirūpatvāt sarvam advaitam eva prakāśakaṃ pratyakṣam \|\|

kiñca ‘ghaṭaḥ asti iti’ ‘paṭaḥ asti iti’ ‘ghaṭaḥ anubhūyate’ ‘paṭaḥ anubhūyate’ iti sarvaṃ padārtha-sattā-anubhūti-nirddiṣṭāḥ eva dṛśyante \| atra sarveṣu pratipattiṣu samānākāratayā vartamānam idam iti tat eva paramārthaḥ \| viśeṣaḥ tu vyāvartamānatayā aparamārthaḥ; rajju-sarpa-ādi-vat \| yathā rajjuḥ adhiṣṭhānatayā anuvartamānā paramārthas satī; vyāvartamāna-sarpa-maṇḍala-nubuddha-ārādayaḥ aparamārthāḥ \| nanu ca, rajju-sarpa-ādau ‘rajjuḥ eva na sarpaḥ’ iti-ādi-rajju-adhiṣṭhāna-yāthātmya-jñānena bādhitatvāt sarpa-ādiḥ aparamārthaḥ, na vyāvartamānatvāt \| rajju-ādeḥ api pāramārthye tādṛg-vartamānatayā, kiñca bādhitatvāt \| ataḥ tu ghaṭa-ādīnām abādhitānāṃ katham apāramārthyam? ucyate, ghaṭa-ādau idam iti adhiniṣṭhā kiñcit iti vivecanīyam \| kiṃ ghaṭaḥ asti iti atra paṭa-ādi-abhāvaḥ? siddhaṃ tarhi ghaṭaḥ asti iti etena paṭa-ādīnāṃ bādhitatvam \| ataḥ bādhakābhūtā viṣaya-niṣṭhita-vyavṛttiḥ \| sā vyāvartamānānām apāramārthyaṃ sādhayati \| rajjuḥ eva samānākāra-bādhitam anubhūyate \| tasmāt sat-mātra-atireki sarvam eva aparamārthaḥ \| prayogaḥ ca bhavati—sat-apāramārthyaṃ, anuvartamānatvāt, rajju-sarpa-vat rajju-adviṣayat \| ghaṭatvaṃ ca aparamārthaḥ; vyāvartamānatvāt, rajju-adhiṣṭhāna-sarpa-ādi-vat—iti \| evaṃ sati anuvartamānā anubhūtiḥ eva paramārthaḥ; sā eva satī \|\|

nanu ca, sat-mātra-anubhūteḥ viṣayatvāt tataḥ bhinnam \| na evam; bhedaḥ hi pratyakṣa-ādi-viṣayatvāt eva nirākṛtaḥ \| ataḥ eva sataḥ anubhūti-viṣaya-bhāvaḥ api na pramāṇa-padavīm anusarati \| tasmāt sat-anubhūtiḥ eva \| sā ca svataḥ-siddhā, anubhūtitvāt; anyathā siddhaḥ ghaṭa-ādi-vat ananubhūtitva-prasaṅgaḥ \| kiñca anubhava-apekṣaḥ ca anubhūtena śakyatā kalpayitum; sat sā eva prakāśamānatvāt \| na hi anubhūti-vedyamānā ghaṭa-ādi-vat prakāśā dṛśyate; yena parāyatta-prakāśaḥ abhyupagamyeta \|\|

atha evam mantavye—udayamānam akha-anubhūtau viṣaya-mātra-vyabhāsane, ‘ghaṭaḥ anubhūyate’ iti \| na hi kaścit ‘ghaṭaḥ ayam’ iti jānan tadānīm eva ativiṣaya-bhūtām anirddeśyamānām anubhūtim api anubhavati \| tasmāt āha eka-prakāśa-nirūpitaḥ hi akṣārthika-rajvadhikañcuka-vat anubhūtiḥ anudrāvaḥ eva hetuḥ; tadanantara-saryogatamātārthavit-prakāśitayā aviśeṣeṇa anubhūtiḥ nubhūyate; evaṃ tarhi anubhūteḥ ajñayā arthavat jaḍatvam āpadyate iti cet; kim idam ajaḍatvaṃ nāma? na tāvat sva-saṃvedyāḥ prakāśa-avyabhicāraḥ; sukha-ādiṣu api tat-saṃbhavāt; na hi kadācit api sukha-ādi-asaṃsargaḥ na upapalabhyate; ataḥ anubhūtiḥ tu ayam eva

na anusmṛte, artha-antaraṃ smṛtyā ataḥ anulambhasya sva-ātma-svarūpa-avastha-vyavasthitiḥ \| tad idam anākalita-anubhāva-vibhramasya sva-mati-nirvṛtta-mahimaḥ; anubhūtiḥ atirekiṇo viṣaya-dharmasya prakāśasya hṛdaya-vastu-upalabdheḥ; ubhayam api etat anusmṛtyā eva aśeṣa-vyavahāra-upapattau prakāśa-ākhya-dharma-kartṛtā-anupapattaye eva \| ataḥ na anumitiḥ anubhūyate \| na api jñāna-antara-siddhā \| api tu sarvaṃ sādhy-antram unmūlitaḥ tvayam eva siddhyati \| prayogaḥ ca—anubhūti-tat-saṃbandhinoḥ sarva-dharma-vyavahāraḥ, sva-saṃbandha-adhīna-itare tat-dharma-vyavahāra-hetutvāt; yat-saṃbandhāt artha-antare yat-dharma-vyavahāra-hetuḥ sa tayoḥ sa-viśeṣaṇa-nyādhīnaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ, yathā rūpādi-viṣaya-bhuja-vādaḥ \| rūpādi hi pṛthivy-ādoḥ sva-saṃbandha-āśraya-bhūta-vādī janayatu sva-vismṛta-rūpādi-saṃbandha-adhīna-āśraya-bhūta-vādaḥ \| ataḥ anubhūtiḥ ātmanaḥ prakāśamānatve prakāśate iti vyavahāre ca svayam eva hetuḥ \|\|

sā iyam svayam-prakāśā anubhūtiḥ nityā ca, prāg-abhāva-ādi-abhāvāt \| tat-svabhāvaḥ ca svataḥ-siddhatvāt eva \| na hi anubhūteḥ svataḥ-siddhatāyāḥ prāg-abhāvaḥ tvatodhyataḥ vādhumānandaṃ śakyate \| anubhūti-svabhāva-samartha-mantrī, sataḥ tādṛśya-ayogāt \| tat-sattā-saṃcaye virodhāt eva tat-āśrayaḥ na asti iti kathaṃ sā sva-bhāvam āna-padyati ? evam asati api nāśam apaiti; anubhūtiḥ svayam asatī sva-nāśe kathaṃ pramāṇaṃ bhavet ? na api anyataḥ asya anāśaṃ śakyate, anubhūteḥ anyasya uttarātvāt \| asyāḥ prāg-abhāvaṃ sādhayat pramāṇam ‘anubhūtiḥ iyam’ iti viṣayī-kṛtya tat-abhāvaṃ sādhayet; svataḥ-siddhatvena iyam iti viṣayī-kāra-arhatvāt, na tat-srāg-abhāva-adhyavasthāyāmānam \| ataḥ asyāḥ prāg-abhāva-abhāvāt utpattiḥ na śakyate vaktum iti uktaṃ prati-saṃbandha-pratyayaiḥ api bhāva-vikāra-asattayā na sat iti \|\|

anupapannā evam anubhūtiḥ ātmani nānātvam api na sahate, vyāpakatva-virodha-upalabdheḥ \| na hi upalabhyaṃ nānā-bhūtaṃ dṛṣṭam \| bheda-dṛṣṭeḥ na ātma-subhāvya eva ca rūpādivat anubhūti-dharmatvaṃ na saṃbhavati \| ataḥ anubhūteḥ rūpa-bhavat-svabhāvya-vedanīyaḥ api kaścid anubhavyaḥ na asyā dharmaḥ \| nirbhūrti-nirvikalpa-bhedā saṃvit, ataḥ eva na asyāḥ astarūpa-atiriktaḥ āśrayaḥ jñātā nāma kaścit dṛṣṭaḥ iti sva-prakāśa-rūpā sā iyam ātmā, ajaḍatvāt ca \| anātmatva-vyāptaṃ jaḍatvaṃ saṃvidi vyāvartamānam ātmatvam api hi saṃvidaḥ vyāvartayati \|\|

nanu ca, ahaṃ jānāmi iti jñātṛtā pratīti-siddhā \| na evam; sā bhrānti-siddhā, rajatā iva śukti-śakalasya, anubhūteḥ sva-ātmani kartṛtva-ayogāt \| ataḥ manuṣyaḥ ahaṃ-siddhya-yastambī-bhūta-manumṛtya-svādi-viśiṣṭa-piṇḍa-ātmābhimānavat jñātṛtvam api alpam \| jñātṛtvaṃ hi jñāna-kriyā-kartṛtvam \| tat ca vikriyātmakaṃ jaḍaṃ vikāri-dravyaṃ haṅkāra-āśraya-sthāna-vikriye saṃbhini cin-mātra-ātmani katham iva saṃbhavati ? iti anādi-siddhavāt eva rūpādiḥ iva kartṛtvādeḥ mithyātvam \| suṣupti-mūrcchitayoḥ ahaṅkṛty-aprabodhāt smṛty-anubhava-ardhena na ahamāḥ ahaṅkṛti-gocaratvam \| kintu teṣu iha ahaṅkṛti-gocaratve ca ātmasaḥ abhyu

prāpyamāne deha-rambhe jaḍatva-parākatva-anādi-rūpa-cid-rūpaḥ hi apariharaḥ \| ahaṃ-pratyagocaratvaṃ katham eṣā prasiddha-dehātirikta-kriyā-phalatva-ādibhiḥ ātmanaḥ avyayasya prāmāṇikatāṃ prasiddham eva \| tathā hṛtyeti jñātuḥ api vilakṣaṇa-sākṣī pratyagātmā iti pratipattavyam \| evaṃ savikriya-anubhava-svarūpasya abhivyaktau jaḍaḥ api ahaṃkāra-svasākṣat tathā tasmin vyaktau \| ātmani atha abhivyakti-anabhivyakti-ekānāṃ svabhāvaḥ \| darpaṇa-jala-khaṇḍa-ādiṣu mukha-candra-viśva-gocara-ādikānām sthityā abhivyaktatīti \| tattkaṃ vācyam ‘jñātā ayam ahaṃ’ iti bhramaḥ \| svaprakāśāyāḥ anubhūteḥ kathañcit tad-abhivyaktuḥ jaḍa-rūpa-ahaṃkāreṇa abhivyaktatvam iti mā vocaḥ; ravi-kara-nikara-abhivyakta-cakurtaṭasya tad-abhivyakti-avaśyena anu; jāla-karaśmi-piñjara-āmukha-maṇi-karaṇānāṃ tad-abhivyakteḥ cāmikara-talena skuratara-prakāśaḥ hi dṛṣṭaḥ \| yataḥ hi jñātā asmi iti jñātāvyam aham-artha-viśrama-ātmanmataḥ na pāramārthikaḥ dharmaḥ; ataḥ eva suṣupti-smṛtyoḥ nirlepti \| tatra hi aham-artha-lekha-vigamena svābhāvika-jñāna-anubhava-mātra-rūpeṇa ātmā saṃbhasate \| śrutaḥ eva suṣuvidhitaḥ kadācit nāma ayaṃ na jñātavān iti parāmṛśati \| tasmāt paramārthataḥ nirasta-samasta-bheda-vikalpa-nirviśeṣa-cīt-mātraika-rasa-rūpastha-nitya-saṃvit eva śāstrayā jñātṛ-jñātṛtva-ādikaṃ pi vidhi-niṣedha-cintyaṃ bhedā vivartata iti tan-mūla-bhūta-avidyā-nivṛtti-upāya-nitya-mukta-muddha-mukta-svabhāva-brahma-ātma-saṃvedana-vidyā-pratipattaye sarve vedāntāḥ ārabhyante—iti \|\| (iti mahāpūrvapakṣaḥ)

**mahāsiddhāntaḥ**

tat idam opaniṣada-puruṣa-varaṇīyatā-hetu-guṇa-viśeṣa-virahi-gaṃsana-ādiya-vāsanā-muṣita-aśeṣa-śemuṣīka-ātma-niṣedhana-pada-vācya-raha-sphurata-arthāyāḥ sūtra-prakriyā-sakala-pramāṇa-bhūta-antardhūta-nityatā-hṛṣyamīcīnat-nyāya-sarpiṇī vikatha-vārā-hṛdi-vidhūtaṃ karma lakṣayitviti nyāya-anugṛhīta-vākya-pratyakṣa-ādi-sakala-pramāṇa-bhūma-yāthātmya-virdeśana-taraṇīyam \|\|

tathā hi—nirviśeṣa-vastu-vādimiḥ nirviśeṣe vastuni idaṃ pramāṇam iti na śakyate vaktum, saviśeṣa-vastu-vipayatvāt sarva-pramāṇānām \| yat tu svānubhava-siddham iti svāgoṣṭhi-nirasamayaḥ; saḥ api ātmā-sākṣika-niviśeṣa-anubhavaḥ eva nirastaḥ; ‘idam aham adarśaṃ’ iti kenacid viśeṣeṇa viśiṣṭa-viṣayatayā anubhavamānum \| saviśeṣe api anubhavamānaḥ anubhavaḥ kenacid vidhūtkyābhāvena nirviśeṣaḥ iti nirduṣṭa-pramāṇa-sattā-atirikta-bhinna-sattā-sādharaṇaiḥ svabhāva-viśeṣaiḥ na brahma iti nirṇayhetu-bhūtaiḥ sattā-atirikta-bhinna-sattā-sādhāraṇaiḥ svabhāva-viśeṣaiḥ eva sarve doṣāḥ eva āvirbhihate \| ataḥ kaiścid viśeṣaiḥ viśiṣṭasya eva vastunaḥ anya-viśeṣa-nirasanam iti, na kañcid viśeṣa-vastu-siddhiḥ \| dhiyaḥ hi dhītvaṃ svaprakāśatā ca jñātṛtvi-prakāśana-svabhāvatayā upalabdheḥ \| svāpa-saṃsmṛtiḥ ca saviṣayaḥ eva abhūvam iti svāvasare niṣpanna-samupādi-kriyāmāḥ \| svādyup-

yata-taś ca nirdeśa-vācyaḥ hi aneke viśeṣāsaṃniveśaḥ \| te ca na vastu-mātram iti śakya-upapādanāḥ; paraspara-āśrayatayā mati-ādi vidhy-abhidvi-vāda-darśanāt, svābhimata-vidhy-ādeś ca sva-mata-upapādanāt \| ataḥ pramāṇika-viśeṣa-vidhurā eva vastu-sthitiḥ vaktavyam \|\|

śabdasya tu viśeṣeṇa saviśeṣaḥ eva vastuni abhidhāna-sāmarthyam, pada-tv-ākhya-rūpeṇa pravṛtteḥ \| prakṛti-pratyaya-yogena hi padatvam \| prakṛti-pratyayayoś ca artha-bhedena padasya eva viśiṣṭa-artha-pratipādanam arjanīyam \| pada-deś ca artha-bhedenivṛttitaḥ \| pada-sahitasya vākyasya aneka-padārtha-saṃsarga-viśeṣābhidhāyitvena nirviśeṣa-vastu-pratipādana-asāmarthyam iti na nirviśeṣa-vastuni śabdaḥ pramāṇam \|\|

pratyakṣasya nirvikalpaka-savikalpaka-bhedena dvidhasya na nirviśeṣa-vastuni pramāṇa-bhāvaḥ \| savikalpake jāty-ādi-eka-padārtha-viśiṣṭa-viṣayatayā eva saviśeṣa-viṣayam \| nirvikalpakam api saviśeṣa-viṣayam eva, savikalpake svarūpa-saṃsmṛti-anupapatteḥ viśiṣṭa-hiti-siddhān hetutvāt \| nirvikalpakaṃ nāma kenacid viśeṣeṇa viyuktasya grahaṇam, na sarva-viśeṣa-rahitasya; tathā-bhūtasya kadācid api grahaṇa-adarśanād anupapatteś ca \| kenacid viśeṣeṇa sthitam iti hi sarvā pratītiḥ upajāyate, trikoṇatva-saṃsthāna-viśeṣaṇa vinā kasyacid api padārthasya grahaṇa-ayogāt \| ato nirvikalpakaḥ ekatīya-dravyeṣu prathama-piṇḍa-grahaṇam \| dvitīyādi-piṇḍa-grahaṇaṃ savikalpakam iti ucyate \| tatra prathama-piṇḍa-grahaṇe gotvādeḥ eka-ghaṭākāratā na pratīyate \| dvitīyādi-piṇḍa-grahaṇeṣu eka-anugatasya jñātiḥ \| prathama-jātīya-anusaṃhita-vastu-saṃsthāna-rūpa-gotvādeḥ eka-buddhi-niyamaka-viśiṣṭatvaṃ dvitīyādi-piṇḍa-graṇāvasīyam iti, dvitīyādi-(piṇḍa)-grahaṇasya savikalpakatvam \| sāsanādi-vastu-saṃsthāna-rūpa-gotvādeḥ eka-buddhi-kṛtaṃ prathama-piṇḍa-grahaṇe gṛhyate iti, prathama-piṇḍa-grahaṇasya nirvikalpakatvam; na punas saṃsthāna-rūpa-jāty-ādereka-grahaṇāt \| saṃsthāna-rūpa-jāty-ādereka-indriyaka-tvāviśeṣāt, saṃsthānena vinā saṃsthāninaḥ pratīti-anupapatteś ca prathama-piṇḍa-grahaṇe ’pi sa-saṃsthānam eva vastu iti avati gṛhyate \| ato dvitīyādi-piṇḍa-graheṣu gotvādeḥ eka-buddhi-niyama-viśiṣṭatā saṃsthānivat saṃsthānavat ca sat eva gṛhyata iti teṣu savikalpakatvam eva \| ataḥ pratyakṣasya kadācid api na nirviśeṣa-viṣayatvam \|\|

ata eva sarvatra śrāvaṇa-pratyakṣam api nirastam \| idam ittham iti pratītau idam ittham iti avayoḥ arthaṃ kathaṃ pravṛttaṃ jñāyate ? tatra ayaṃ bhāvaḥ sāsnādi-saṃsthāna-viśeṣaḥ; tad-viśeṣyaṃ dravyam idam aśva iti anayoḥ ayaṃ pratītiḥ uparāhata eva \| tathā hi—prathamam eva vastu pratīyamānaṃ sa-ketetara-vṛttyā tathā eva pratīyate \| vyāvyānviśra gotvādi-saṃsthāna-viśeṣa-viśiṣṭaṃ ca iti artha iti pratīteḥ \| sarveṣāṃ viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāva-vyatikrantoḥ anayoḥ aikātma-bhedaḥ pratītyā eva mukhyataḥ \| tatra daṇḍa-kuṇḍalādayaḥ pṛthak-saṃsthāna-saṃsthitāḥ sva-niṣṭhāś ca kadācid kasyacid icchayā antara-viśeṣaṇatayā avatiṣṭhante \| gotvādayaḥ tu dravya-saṃsthānatayā eva padārtha-bhūtāḥ santaḥ dravya-viśeṣaṇatayā avasthitāḥ \| ubhayatra viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāvaḥ samānaḥ \| tata

evaṃ trayī-bheda-pratipattiḥ ca \| iyaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ—pṛthak-sthiti-tri-pratipatti-yogyā daṇḍādayaḥ; gotvādayaḥ tu nimittena tad-dharmāḥ iti \| ataḥ vastu-virodhaḥ pratīti-parihataḥ iti pratīti-svarūpa-ākāraḥ hi indriya-manasā eva sarva-saṃmataḥ \| tad eva tat-prakāreṇa na ihika-samabhāvāt, iti mukhyatvam upapāditam \| ataḥ pratyakṣasya tad-viśeṣa-viṣayatvena pratyakṣa-dṛṣṭaḥ saṃbandha-viśiṣṭa-viṣayatvāt anumānāt api saviśeṣa-viṣayam eva \| pramāṇam anubhāva-virodhe api sarva-abhyupagata-pramāṇa-madhye eva viṣayaḥ iti na kena api pramāṇena nirviśeṣa-vastu-siddhiḥ \| vastu-gatatva-bhāva-viśeṣaḥ tu eva vastu nirddiśyam iti vaktuṃ jananī-bandhyātma-jīvitatvām iva sva-vāk-virodham api na jānāti \|\|

astu, pratyakṣaṃ samāna-agrahitvena na bheda-viṣayam, bhedaḥ ca vikalpa-mātratvāt u nirūpaḥ iṣyate, tad api jāti-ādi-viśiṣṭaṃ eva vastutaḥ pratyakṣa-viṣayatvāt jāti-ādeḥ ca pratiyogi-apekṣayā vastu-antaratvāt ca bheda-vyavahāra-hetutvāt ca dūra-utsāritam \| saṃvedanatva-upahitam parasya vyavahāra-viśeṣa-hetoḥ tu asmin api tad-hetu-ākāra-hetutvaṃ yuktam iti iṣṭyate bhedasya api saṃbhavatve \| ataḥ eva ca na anavasthā-anyonyāśrayaṇaṃ ca \| eka-svarūpatve api pratyakṣa-jñānasya tasmin eva kṣaṇe vastu-bheda-rūpa-tat-saṃsthāna-rūpa-anekapa-ākāra-hetutvāt kṣaṇa-antarasya eva na kiñcit viruddhaṃ tiṣṭhati \|\|

api ca, sat-mātra-grāhitve ‘ghaṭaḥ asti’ ‘paṭaḥ asti’ iti viviṣṭa-viṣayā pratītiḥ nirūpyate \| yadi ca sat-mātra-atirikta-vastu-saṃsthāna-rūpa-jāti-ādi-lakṣaṇaḥ bhedaḥ pratyakṣeṇa na gṛhītaḥ; kim ittham arthaṃ mahad-acchane nivartate ? sarvatra pratipattṛṣu samānam eva viṣayaḥ cet; tantu-santati-patita-viṣayaḥ sattva-ādi-gaṇa-samūhaḥ śabdā ekaṃkratipattiḥ tu kim iti na smaryante ? viśvaḥ akṣe hasti iti na ca saṃvedanayoḥ eva viṣayatvena uparitanasya gṛhīta-māhitva-viśeṣa-abhāvāt ca smṛteḥ vaikṣaṇyaṃ na syāt \| prati-savedanaṃ viśeṣa-abhyugame pratyakṣasya viśiṣṭa-artha-viṣayatvena idānīṃ ugataṃ bhavati \| sarveṣāṃ saṃvedanānām ika-viṣayatvāyām ekena eva saṃvedanena aśeṣa-grahaṇāt asvadhiyāṃś ca prasajyeta \| na ca cakṣuṣā samānaṃ gṛhyate, tasya rūpa-eka-viṣaya-kārya-saṃvedaka-padārtha-ahitvāt \| na api tvacā, sparśa-eka-vastu-viṣayatvāt \| śrotra-ādīnām api na samāna-viṣayāṇi; kintu śabda-sambandha-lakṣaṇa-viśeṣa-viṣayāṇi eva \| ataḥ samānasya grāhakaṃ na kiñcit iha dṛśyate \|\|

nirviśeṣa-sat-mātrasya pratyakṣeṇa eva grahaṇe tad-dhiyā gamya pratiyati-vipratyanutthā-darśatvam eva syāt \| sat-mātra-grahaṇaḥ prameya-bhāvaḥ ca \| tataḥ jaḍavat mātrañcāyadṛcchayā iṣataḥ \| ataḥ vastu-saṃsthāna-rūpa-jāti-ādi-lakṣaṇaiḥ tad-viśiṣṭa-viṣayam eva pratyakṣam \| saṃsthāna-atiriktaṃ jāti-ekatva-ākāra-buddhiḥ bāhya-padārthānāṃ tāvat eva gotvādi-jāti-vyavahāra-upapattaiḥ; atirekaḥ ca eṣāṃ saṃsthānasya saṃpratipannatvāt ca saṃsthānam eva jātiḥ \| saṃsthānaṃ nāma sva-asādhāraṇaṃ rūpam iti yathā-vastu saṃsthānam anusandheyam \| jāti-gucchena eva bhinnaḥ iti vyavahāra-saṃbhavāt, padārtha-antara-āśrayaṇāt, athavā antara-

vādinākhya-prapañca-tattvābhaṃ gotvādiḥ eva bhedaḥ \| nanu ca ākhyādiḥ eva bhedaḥ cet asmin gṛhīte tad-vyavahāra-dṛṣṭe daṇḍa-vyavahāraḥ api syāt \| satyam, bhedaḥ ca vyāvahriyata eva, gotvādi-vyavahārāt \| gotvādiḥ eva hi sakala-itara-vyāvṛttiḥ; gotvādau gṛhīte sakala-itara-sajātīya-buddhi-vyavahāra-yonivṛtteḥ \| bheda-grahaṇena eva hi evaṃ nivṛttiḥ \| ‘ayam asmāt bhinnaḥ’ iti tu vyavahāre pratiyogi-nirdeśasya tad-apekṣatvāt pratiyogy-apekṣā bhinna iti vyavahāraḥ iti uktam \|\|

yat tu ghaṭādīnāṃ viśeṣaṇāṃ vyāvartamānatvena pāramārthya-yuktatvam, tad-anālocita-vācya-vācaka-bhāva-vyāhatam ukti-viśeṣasya kṣānti-parikareṇa avasam \| hi anyonya-virodhe hi vācya-vācaka-bhāvaḥ \| bādhita-rūpe ca vyāvṛttiḥ \| atra ghaṭa-paṭa-ādiṣu deśa-kāla-bhedena virodhaḥ eva nāsti \| yasmin deśe yasmin kāle yasya sadbhāvaḥ pratipannaḥ; tasmin deśe tasmin kāle tasya abhāvaḥ pratipannaḥ cet; tatra virodhāt balavataḥ bādhakatvaṃ bādhitasya ca nivṛttiḥ \| deśāntara-kālāntara-saṃbandhitayā anubhūteṣu anyadeśakālayoḥ bhāva-pratipattau na virodha iti katham atra vācya-vācaka-bhāvaḥ ? anyatra nivṛttasya atra nivṛttiḥ vā katham ucyate ? rajju-sarpa-ādiṣu tu tad-aṃśa-kāla-saṃbandhitvaṃ bhāvāvasthiteḥ, virodhī bādhakatvaṃ vyāvṛttiḥ ca iti deśa-kālāntara-sthasya deśa-kālāntara-avasthānaṃ mithyātva-vyāpyaṃ na dṛṣṭam iti na vyāvartamānatva-mātra-prayukteṣu \|\|

yat tu, anuvartamānatvāt tat paramārtha iti, tat na siddhyed iti na sādhanam arhati \| ataḥ na samyak eva vastu \| anubhūti-viśeṣayoḥ ca viṣaya-viṣayibhāvena bhedasya pratyakṣa-siddhatvāt bādhitatvāt ca anubhūtiḥ eva satoty etad api nirastam \|\|

grantha-bhūteḥ eva ca yaḥ prakāśātmakatvaṃ; tad viṣaya-prakāśana-velāyāṃ jñātuḥ ātma-sattāyāḥ; na tu sarveṣāṃ saṃvedya-tathya iti niyamaḥ asti, para-anubhavasya hāta-upādāna-ādi-vidhi-śaṅkā-anumāna-jñāna-viṣayatvāt, svānubhavyāpatītasya ‘anāśritaṃ’ iti jñāna-viṣayatva-darśanāt ca \| ataḥ anubhūti-śrute svataḥ siddhā iti vaktuṃ na śakyate \|\|

anubhūteḥ anubhavye ananubhūta-vismaraṇi duruttaram; svagata-tīra-anubhavānāṃ paragata-anubhavānāṃ ca anubhavyatvena anubhūtitva-prasaṅgāt \| para-anubhava-anumāna-anupapattau ca śabdaiḥ saṃbandha-grahaṇa-abhāvena samasta-śabda-vyavahāra-uccheda-prasaṅgaḥ \| ācāryasya jñātvacchamunātha tat-pustikā ca kriyate; sā ca na upapadyate \| na ca ārya-viṣaya-vedana-anubhūtitvam \| anubhūtitvaṃ nāma vartamāna-daśāyāṃ svataḥ ca svāśrayaṃ prati prakāśamānatvaṃ, svataḥ ca svaviṣaya-sādhanatvaṃ vā \| te ca anubhavitṛ-rūpa-vyatireke api svānubhava-siddhe na apahnuta iti na anubhūtitvam agacchati \| ghaṭādeḥ tu anubhūti-rūpa-itara-svabhāva-virahāt; na anubhavyatvam \| yathā aṅgulīṣu anubhavyatve api ananubhavitvavat sūtraḍurbhiḥ; gaganakusumād er anubhavāvyān anubhūtitvāt \| gaganakusumād es tu anubhūtitvam asatyaṃ ucyate,

na anubhāvyatva-vyutpattiḥ iti cet; evaṃ tarhi ghaṭādeḥ eka-jñāna-anvayi-rodhi-vedana-anubhūtitva-vibandhanam, na anubhāvya-mithyātva-itarāt \| anubhūtatva-anubhāve ajñāna-avirodhitvam api tasya; ghaṭādeḥ iva prasajyate iti cet; anubhāvyatve api gagana-kusuma-ādi-vijñāna-avirodhitvam api prasajyate eva \| ataḥ anubhāvyatve anubhūtitvam iti agṛhītam \|\|

yat tu saṃvid-draṣṭṛtva-siddhyā prāgbhāva-ādi-abhāva-adarśa-nirasyate, tad bandhya jātyā eva yadiḥ pradhīyate \| prāgbhāvasya prāk ahāmābhāva-lakṣaṇaḥ na śakyate vaktum; anubhūteḥ eva grahaṇāt \| katham anubhūti-sattā tat-kālīnā eva sva-abhāvaṃ viruddham avagamyate iti cet; na hi anubhūti-sva-samakālīnatvena eva viṣayīkāraḥ atīti asya niyamaḥ; atīta-anāgatayoḥ viṣayatayā prasiddhatvāt \| atha manyase—anubhūti-grāhyatvāvede-siddhe racana-ta-samakāla-prāk-niyamaḥ asau iti; kiṃ dhavaḥ khadiraḥ eva dṛṣṭaḥ, yena niyamaṃ bravīṣi ? hanta tarhi tattve eva darśanāt prāgbhāva-ādi-siddhaḥ iti na tadvattvam \| tat-prāgbhāvaṃ ca tat-samakālavatīm anumanataḥ kaḥ brūyāti ? indriya-janmanaḥ pratyakṣasya hi eṣa svabhāva-niyamaḥ; prastuta-samakālavatitaḥ padārthasya grāhakatvam; na sarveṣāṃ jñānānāṃ pramāṇānāṃ ca; smaraṇa-anumāna-āgama-yogi-pratyakṣa-ādiṣu kāla-antara-vartinaḥ api grahaṇa-darśanāt \| ataḥ eva ca pramāṇasya prameya-avinābhāvaḥ \| na hi pramāṇasya sva-samakālavatitinā avinābhāvaḥ artha-saṃbandhaḥ; api tu yad-deśa-kāla-avisaṃvādi-kriyā-yogya-artha-avabhāsate, tasya yathā-vidha-ākāra-kriyā-sākṣya-prayojakatā \| ataḥ idam api nirastam—smṛtīnām bāhya-viṣayā naṣṭeṣu arthaṃ smṛti-darśanāt—iti \|\|

atha ucyeta—na tāvat saṃvid-grāha-abhāvaḥ pratyakṣa-avedyaḥ, avartamānatvāt \| na ca pramāṇa-antara-avedyaḥ; liṅga-ādi-abhāvāt \| na hi saṃvid-grāha-abhedyatve asmiṃ liṅgam upalabhyate \| na anupalabdhiḥ api kasyacit hṛdyate \| na ghaṭa-āmarśitṛvyaḥ dṛśyateḥ \| antaratajjñāna-abhāvaḥ pramāṇa-abhāvāt eva na setsyati—iti; yathā ca svataḥ-siddhatva-vidhi-bhaṅge parīkṛtya pramāṇa-abhāvaḥ asya alabdhhatvaṃ ca; yogya-anupalabdhyā abhāva-sammatiḥ iti anubhāgam udbhāvān \|\|

kiṃ ca—pratyabhijñānaṃ sva-viṣayaṃ ghaṭa-ādikaṃ sva-kāla-jñāte santam sādhayatasya na sarvadā sattā-sambhāvam uhyate iti ghaṭādeḥ pūrva-uttara-kāla-sattā na pratīyate \| tadvat atiprasaṅgaḥ saṃvedanasya kāla-paricchinnatayā pratītiḥ \| ghaṭa-ādi-viṣayam eva saṃvedanaṃ svayaṃ kāla-anavacchinnaṃ pratītaṃ cet; saṃvedana-viṣayaḥ ghaṭādiḥ api kāla-anavacchinnaḥ pratīyeta iti nitya-sthitiḥ \| nityaṃ ca etat saṃvedanaṃ svataḥ-siddhaṃ nityam eva iti pratīyeta \| na ca tathā pratīyate \| evam anumāna-ādi-sāṃvidhaḥ api kāla-anavacchinnaḥ pratītāḥ ca; sva-viṣayam api kāla-anavacchinnatvāt prakāśayanti iti te ca sarve kāla-anavacchinnāḥ nityāḥ syuḥ; indriya-anupalabdhatvāt viṣamāṇam \| na ca nirviṣayā kācit saṃvit asti; anupalabdheḥ \| viṣaya-prakāśanatva-yoga-upalabdheḥ eva hi saṃvidaḥ svarūpa-prakāśatā samarthitā \| saṃvicio

viṣaya-prakāśana-tatparatva-bhāva-virodhe sati svayaṃ-prakāśatva-asiddheḥ; anubhūteḥ anubhavitr-ananya-mādhyatvāt ca saṃvid-dravyatvena sthāyāḥ \| na ca svāpam adraṣṭṛ chidru sarva-viṣaya-jñā kevalaṃ saṃvidita-rūpā iti vācyam; yogya-anupalabdhi-aparāhatatvāt \| tārkika-daśātva-anubhūtim upetāṃ devāḥ; tathā prabodha-samaye tat-sandhānaṃ sthāyāḥ; na ca tad-rahitā \|\|

nanu bhūtasya padārthasya smaraṇa-niyamaḥ na dṛṣṭacaraḥ \| atat-smaraṇa-abhāvaḥ katham anubhāva-bhāvaṃ sādhayet ? ucyate \| nikhila-saṃskāra-tiraskṛti-kara-deha-vibhāga-ādi-śrava-lekhu-vireheṣu asmaraṇa-niyamaḥ jñāna-bhāvam eva sādhayati \| na kevalam asmaraṇa-niyamāt anubhāva-abhāvaḥ; suṣupti-sthasya ‘iyantaṃ kālaṃ na kiñcit aham ajñāsiṣam’ iti pratyavamārśena eva siddheḥ \| na ca satsu anubhūte tat-smaraṇa-niyamaḥ viṣayāt ched iti virahāt ahaṅkāra-avibhāvāt iti śaṅkate vaktum; arthāntara-anubhavasya arthāntara-abhāvasya ca anubhūti-arthāntara-asmaraṇe tulya-abhāvāt \| tathā api daśātvam arthaḥ jñātaṃ iti ca vadhyate \|\|

nanu svāpa-ādi-daśāsu api saviśeṣaḥ anubhavaḥ asti iti pūrvam uktam \| satyam uktam; saḥ tu ātma-anubhavaḥ; sa ca saviśeṣaḥ eva iti sthāpayiṣyate \| iha tu sakala-viṣaya-virahiṇī nirāśrayā ca saṃvit vidhīyate \| kevalā eva saṃvit ātma-anubhavaḥ iti cet; (na), sā ca sādhayituṃ hi upapādayiṣyate \| ataḥ anubhūti-smṛtau svayaṃ sva-prakāśatā na sādhayati iti prāgabhāva-asiddheḥ śakyate vaktum \| anubhūteḥ ananya-mādhyatva-saṃbandha-upapādanena ākhyātaḥ api asiddhiḥ nirastā \| tasmāt ca prāgabhāva-adhyāsiddhyā saṃvidaḥ anuvṛttitva-rūpa-sthitimati \|\|

yat ucyate anutpannā vikāra-antara-nirasanat; tat api anupapannam, prāgabhāve dṛṣṭicārāt \| tasya hi janma-abhāve api vināśaḥ dṛśyate \| bhāve vicchitti viśeṣaṇe tarka-kuśalatā-āviśkṛtā bhavati \| tathā ca bhavad-vibhinnatā-vidhāyadṛṣṭā eva vividha-vikāra-rūpeṇa tattvakṣānokhyādṛntavatī ca iti tasmāt na aikāntyam \| tarhi vikārasya eva mithyātvam iti cet; kiṃ bhavataḥ paramārtha-bhūtaḥ api asti vikāraḥ, yena idaṃ dūṣaṇam arthavat bhavati ? na hi atra abhyupagamyate \|\|

yat api anubhūteḥ ujjvalatvāt rūpa-vibhāgaṃ na sahate iti; tat api na upapadyate, ajasra-saṃvāsanto deha-indriya-ādikasthaḥ vibhaktatvāt; anāditvena ca abhyupagatāyāḥ avidyāyāḥ ātmānaḥ vyatirekasya āvaraṇa-grahaṇa-upapatteḥ \| saḥ vibhāgaḥ mithyā-rūpaḥ iti cet; janma-pratibaddhaḥ paramārtha-vibhāgaḥ kiṃ kṣaṇic-cidu-rūpatvāt ? avidyāyāḥ ātmanaḥ paramārthataḥ vibhāga-abhāve vastutaḥ hi avidyā eva syāt ātmā \| abādhita-pratipatti-siddha-īśvara-bheda-sambandhena darśana-bhedaḥ api samarthitaḥ eva, deha-bhedāt dehendra-bhedavat \|\|

yadi na asya dṛṣṭeḥ śiṣṭa-rūpayā dṛśyaḥ kaścit api dharmaḥ asti; hṛdyatvāt eva teṣāṃ na dṛṣṭi-dharmatvam—iti ca; tat api sva-abhyupagataiḥ pramāṇa-siddhaiḥ nityatva-svayaṃ-prakāśatva-ādi-dharma-saṃpannaiḥ

kārtikam \| na ca te saṃvedana-mātre, svarūpa-bhedāt \| īśvarasya eva svābhyaṃ prati kasyacit viṣayasya prakāśanaṃ hi saṃvedanam \| svayaṃ-prakāśatā tu sva-sattayā eva sva-āśrayāya prakāśamānatā \| prakāśaś ca cit-acit-viśeṣa-padārtha-sādhāraṇo vyavahāra-yogyatvam \| sarva-kāla-vartamānatvaṃ hi nityatvam \| ekatva-naikarūpyāt cheda iti \| teṣāṃ jaḍatvāt asahāyatāyām api tathā-bhūteḥ api caitanya-dharma-bhūteḥ antar-naikatva-sampariṇāmaḥ \| saṃvidi tu svarūpa-virekeṇa jaḍatvādinā anīkarūpa-nitya-bhāva-rūpo bhāva-rūpo vā dharmo na abhyupetavyaḥ; tat-tad-viṣaya-vedyatvā kim api na evaṃ bhavet \|\| api ca saṃvit-siddhiḥ vā na vā? siddhati cet; svayaṃtā sthāt \| na cet; tucchatā, gagana-kusumādi-kam \| siddhiś ca saṃvit iti cet; kasya kaṃ prati iti vaktavyam \| yadi na kasyacit siddhyati; sā tarhi na siddhiḥ \| siddhir hi puruṣam eva kathañcit cikarṣati bhavati \| bhāsana iti cet; ko’yam ātmā? nanu saṃvit-vedya-rūpaḥ \| satyam uktam; duṣṭaṃ tu tat \| tathā hi; kasyacit durghaṭasya śukti-rajata-jñānaṃ prati siddhi-rūpatayā tattva-vedhinī sā saṃvit-rūpaṃ katham iva samānam anubhaveta? etat uktaṃ bhavati—anubhūtiḥ iti svātmyaṃ prati sva-sattayā eva kasyacit vastuno vyavahāra-yogya-guṇa-pādāna-rūpāvo jñānatvāt tat-saṃvit-āśrayatayā sa-karmaka-aujaḍya-viduratva-ātmano dharma-viśeṣaḥ ‘ghaṭam ahaṃ jānāmi, imam arthaṃ jñātum icchāmi, paṭam ahaṃ saṃvetsi’ iti sarveṣām ātma-sākṣikaḥ prasiddhaḥ \| etat-svabhāvatayā hi tasyāḥ svayaṃ-prakāśatā bhavatā abhyupagantavyā \| asya sa-karmakasya kartuḥ dharma-viśeṣasya karma-kartṛ-kartṛtva-vyāptiḥ budhyate sma iti \| tathā hi; asya kartuḥ isvaratvaṃ kartṛ-dharmasya saṃvedana-ākhyasya mukha-duḥkhādeḥ iva acetana-sthiti-nirodhaś ca pratyakṣa-anubhaveta \| kartuḥ stharyaṃ tāvat ‘saḥ eva ayam arthaḥ pūrvaṃ mayā jñātaḥ’ iti pratyabhijñā-pratyakṣa-siddham \| ‘ahaṃ jānāmi’, ‘aham ajñāsiṣam’, ‘jñātum eva mayā idānīṃ jñānaṃ nāsti’ iti ca saṃvit-śūnyatvādyāḥ pratyabhijñā iti kutas tat-deśyaṃ? evaṃ kṣaṇa-bhaṅginy-ātma-viraha ātmanaḥ sa-guṇena pūrva-dṛṣṭe smaryetaḥ ‘idam aham adrākṣam’ iti pratyabhijñā ca na ghaṭate; anena eva jñātuḥ ātmasya na hi etena pratyabhijñānaṃ bhavet \|\| kiñ ca anubhūteḥ ātmatva-abhyupagame tathā nityatve api pratisaṃdhānātāṃ bhavat tad avasthaḥ \| pratisaṃdhānaṃ hi pūrva-apara-kāla-vyāpinam anubhavitāram upasthāpayati; na anubhūti-mātram; aham eva idam pūrvam api anubhūya iti \| bhavatoktyā anusṛtena hi anubhavitṛtva-rūpasya \| anubhūteḥ asyūritā na eva \| saṃvit-kṣaṇam kācit virūpatayā nirviṣayā vākyatā-anupapattiḥ saṃbhavati iti durūpam \| tasmāt anubhūtā saṃvit-cheda saṃvit-upalabdhi-rūpa-ātmatvam \| anubhūti-mātram eva paramārtha iti nirupakrama-hetutā-śāśanāś ca nirākṛtāḥ \|\| nanu ca ‘ahaṃ jānāmi’ iti asmat-pratyaye yojana-saṃbhavaḥ prakāśa-antara-siddhyartham

ātmā \| tasmāt tat-svarūpa-nirdhārita-aśeṣa-puruṣārtha-lakṣaṇaḥ ahaṃ jānāmi iti siddhyat-samarthaḥ śithra-mātra-atirekī pumartha eva \| nanu evam, ‘ahaṃ jānāmi’ iti dharmi-dharmitayā pratyakṣa-sthita-virodhāt eva \| kiñca, ahaṃ-arthaḥ na cetasā pratyakṣaṃ nāmnaḥ bhavet \| ahaṃ-buddhyā parāmarśati pratyag-arthaḥ hi siddhyet \|\| nirasta-akhila-duḥkhaḥ aham ananta-ānanda-bhāk svarāṭ \| bhaveyam iti mokṣa-arthī śravaṇa-ādau pravartate \|\| aham-artha-vināśe asya mokṣaḥ iṣyadhvaḥ sthiti \| apaspadeśaḥ mokṣakṣā-śastra-avagataḥ \|\| madi naṣṭe api mattaḥ anyā kācit jñāti-sthiratā \| iti tat-prāptaye yatnaḥ kasyāpi na bhaviṣyati \|\| sva-saṃvedyatvā jñāṇasya astitā vijñāta-ādi ca \| sva-saṃdarśi-viyogaḥ tu jātiśā eva na siddhyati \|\| hetu-śleṣasya yamabhāve chedana-ādeḥ asiddhivat \| ataḥ aham-arthaḥ na antave pratyag-ātmā iti niścitam \|\| śrīkantāsmare kena, śrījānāṃdhyeti ca śrutiḥ \| śrutyaḥ vetti taṃ prāhuḥ kṣetrajñaḥ iti ca smṛtiḥ \|\| jñātā śrutiḥ ityasya sūtrakāraḥ api \| jñaḥ ataḥ eva iti ataḥ jñāsaḥ iti atimātram iti sthitam \|\|

ahaṃ-pratyaya-siddhaḥ brahmavarthaḥ; pumartha-rūpa-ayogyaḥ pumarthaḥ \| tathā ahaṃ jānāmi iti siddhaḥ jñātā pumarthaḥ iti vacanaṃ jananī me vandhyā iti avidyā-hata-arthaṃ ca \| nanu na asau jñātā aham-arthaḥ ca adhīra-śarīraḥ; svayaṃ-prakāśatvāt \| caitanya-svabhāvatā hi svayaṃ-prakāśatā \| yaḥ prakāśa-svabhāvaḥ; saḥ arthān dhīniṣprakāśaḥ; dīpavat \| nahi dīpa-ādeḥ sva-prabhāvalinibhititvena prakāśatva-svabhāvin-prakāśatvaṃ ca \| kiṃ tarhi ? dīpasya sva-prakāśa-svabhāvaḥ svayam eva prakāśate; anyān api prakāśayati sva-prabha yā \|\|

evat uktaṃ bhavati—yathā kam eva tejaḥ dravyaṃ prabhā-bhāva-dṛṣṭeṇa avitiṣṭhate \| yadyapi prabhā prabhāvat dravya-āṇubhūtā; tathāpi tejaḥ dravyam eva, na śaukya-ādi-dravya-guṇaḥ \| sva-āśraya-dravyasya api vartamānatvāṅg paktatvāt ca śaukya-ādi-vaidharmyeṇa; prakāśatvāt ca tejaḥ dravyam eva; na artha-antaram \| prakāśatvaṃ ca svarūpa-rūpayā evaṃ ca prakāśatvavāt \| asti āstu guṇavat vyavahāraḥ nityatadravyatvaccheṣeṇa eva ikṣaṇibhanaḥ \| na ca āśraya-avyayāḥ eva viśeṣaṇīḥ pravartante; praśleṣyudyamte, maṇiṣu maṇi-prabhṛtīnāṃ vināśa-prasajyeta \| dīpa-dravya-viśisti-utpattiḥ kadācit api na syāt \| nahi viśāraṇa-svabhāvayā dīpa-śrīdruḥ-kula-mātraṃ niyamena piṇḍībhūtāḥ ūrdhvam ucyate tataḥ pakṣāntupat eva tīvī-mūrccha-madhya-eka-rūpāḥ viśeṣaṇīḥ pravartanteḥ iti śakyaṃ vaktum \| anantasaprabhātāḥ eva dīpāḥ pratikṣaṇam udapekṣā viniryuṇta iti pustaka-lakāra-ājakamodina-patātāti-nāśaḥ vināśādhā-vagamyate \| prabhāyaḥ sva-āśraya-samīpe prakāśādhi-prayogeṇa adhikārnityādi-upalabdhi-vyavasthānam; anyatra ālocamairuṣyati divat \| evam ātmā cit-rūpaḥ eva caitanyupakaḥ iti \|\|

cit-rūpatā hi svayaṃ-prakāśatā \| tathā hi śrūyate— “sa yathā saindhava-ghanaḥ anantaraḥ abāhyaḥ kṛtsnaḥ rasa-ghanaḥ eva, evaṃ vā are ayam ātmā anantaraḥ abāhyaḥ kṛtsnaḥ prajñā-ghanaḥ eva”.

vijñāna-ghanaḥ eva, ānandaṃ puruṣa-svayaṃ-jyotiḥ bhavati, na vijñātuḥ vijñāteḥ viparilopaḥ vidyate, atha yaḥ veda idaṃ jighrāṇi iti saḥ ātmā, katamaḥ ātmā iti yaḥ ayaṃ vijñāna-mayaḥ prāṇeṣu hṛdi antar-jyotiḥ puruṣaḥ, eṣaḥ hi draṣṭā spraṣṭā śrotā ghrātā rasayitā mantā boddhā kartā vijñāna-ātmā puruṣaḥ, vijñātāram are kena vijānīyāt, jñāna-ātmā eva ayaṃ puruṣaḥ, taṃ paśyaḥ mṛtyuṃ paśyati na rogaṃ na uta duḥkhatām, saḥ uttamaḥ puruṣaḥ, na upajanaṃ smaran idaṃ śarīram, evam eva asya paridraṣṭuḥ imāḥ ṣoḍaśa-kalāḥ puruṣāyaṇāḥ puruṣaṃ prati astaṃ gacchanti, tasmin nā evaṃ saṃjñā nāṃ bhavati, ayaḥ antaraḥ ātmā vijñāna-mayaḥ—ityādayaḥ \| lakṣyati ca jñaḥ ataḥ eva iti \|\| antaḥ-svayaṃ-jyotiḥ ayaṃ ātmā jñātvai, na prakāśa-mātraṃ \| prakāśatvaṃ devatva-kāśaḥ, dīpa-ādi-prakāśavat \| tasmāt jñānam, bhavitum arhati saṃvit \| saṃvit-vyutpatti-jñāna-ādi-śabda-sambandhi-śabdā iti ca śabda-artha-vidaḥ \| nahi loka-vedayoḥ caitanya-atireka-kartṛtva-karmutvaṃ kartṛ ca prayogaḥ dṛśyate \| yathā uktam agadvāsudevācāryaiḥ; tat na evam prekṣyam, ajaḍatvam iti kim apekṣyam? sva-saṃtānuṣūla-prakāśatvam iti cet; tathā sati dīpa-ādīśvarṇakāśyam \| saṃvid-itaritarūpa-kāśa-dharmitva-sukham eva atibhinnīvedyākāśya \| atyāścaryāt prakāśatā-nāśatvam api mukha-ādi-dṛśvyabhicārānirastam \| yat ucyate, sukha-ādi-rūpa-bhinnirūpa-kāśaḥ api anayāse prakāśamānatayā ghaṭādivajjatvena ātmā iti \| jñānaṃ vā kiṃ svarūpaṃ prakāśate? tat api hāyaryadveṣa-aham-arthasya jñātṛtva-svarasate, ahaṃ sukhī iti avajñānāyutham iti \| ataḥ svasaṃ prakāśamānatvavalambana-jñatvaṃ saṃvidhi-siddham \| tasmāt sva-ātmānaṃ prati sva-saṃvedyā eva siddhyadvajjaḥ aham-arthaḥ eva ātmā \| jñānasya api prakāśatā tat-saṃbandha-āyattā \| tat-kṛtā eva hi jñānasya sukha-ādeḥ iva sva-āśraya-caitanyaṃ prati prakaṭatvam itaraṃ prati aprakaṭatvaṃ ca \| ataḥ na itihāsmatra ātmā; api tu jñānavān arthaḥ \|\| atha yat uktam, anubhūtiḥ paramārthataḥ nirviṣayā nirāśrayā ca satī bhrāntyā jñātṛtvatāḍaśrayate, rajatattvā iva śuktiḥ, niradhiṣṭhāna-bhrama-anupapatteḥ—iti \| tat ayuktam; tathā sati anubhava-sāmānādhikaraṇyena anubhavitā aham-arthaḥ pratīyeta, ‘anubhavitaraham’ iti; puraḥ-avasthita-abhāsvara-dravya-ākāratayā rajata-ādivat \| atra tu pṛthak abhāsamānā eva bhūtiḥ yathā antaram aham-arthaṃ viśinaddhi, daṇḍaḥ iva devadattam \| tathā hi ‘anubhavāmi aham’ iti pratītiḥ \| tat evam asmad-artham anubhūtiḥ viśiṣṭaṃ prakāśayati anubhavāmi aham iti pratyayaḥ daṇḍa-mātre ‘daṇḍī devadattaḥ’ iti pratyayavat viśeṣaṇa-bhūta-anubhūti-mātra-avalambanaḥ katham iva pratijāyeta? yat api uktaṃ sthuṇākoṇi-nyāya-videha-ātmābhimānavat eva jñātṛtvaṃ pratibhāsatāv jñātṛtvam api mithyā iti, tat ayuktam; āsthayā abhinnatayā anubhūteḥ api mithyātvaṃ syāt, dṛṣṭatā eva pratītiḥ \|

saṃskāratayā upamardita-ccharatvātābādhita-vedānugṛhītatvaṃ mithyātva-virodhi cetaḥ; hetutvaṃ sati tattvābādhena jñātṛtvam api na mithyā \|\|

yad uktam—adhikriyātmatano jñāna-kriyā-kartṛtva-svarūpaṃ jñātṛtvaṃ na saṃbhavati \| ato jñātṛtvaṃ vikriyātmakaṃ jaḍe vikāra-rūpatvayatna-pariṇāmāhaṅkāra-sthityabhimiti na jñātṛtvam ātmanḥ; api tu antaḥkaraṇa-rūpasya ahaṅkārasya \| kartṛtvādihi rūpādi-viṣaya-dharmaḥ; kartṛtvaidehendriya-prayogecchatve ca ātmano’bhugamanmāpāne dehasya eva anātmatvavarāyañjatyādivāsañjyaś ca—iti \| na etad upapadyate—dehasya caitanatyaprakṛti-pariṇāmatvahaṅkṛtvatvarāśitvavidyāṅgatvaṃ karaṇa-rūpasya ahaṅkārasya, caitana-sādhāraṇa-lakṣaṇatvāc ca jñātṛtvasya \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—yathā dehādidaṃ śabda-parāñcavihihetumithantavyanoindradṛṣṭavartyatvaṃ vivicyate, evam antakaraṇa-rūpāhaṅkāraḥ api tadindratvād eva tatra eva hetumithantsamādhirīcyata iti \| ato virodhād eva na jñātṛtvam ahaṅkārasya, harititvavat \| yathā harititvaṃ tarkarmaṇaḥ ahaṅkārasya na api upagamyate, tathā jñātṛtvam api na tatkarmaṇaḥ api upagantavyam \| na ca jñātṛtvaṃ vikriyātmakam \| jñātṛtvaṃ hi jñāna-guṇāśrayatvam \| jñānaṃ ca asya nityasya svabhāvika dharmaḥ ca ena nityam \| nityatvaṃ ca ātmano śruti-smṛtī, ityādibhir vakṣyati \| śrotṛt eva atyatra na iti vyapadeśena jñānāśrayatvaṃ ca svabhāvikam iti vakṣyati \| asya jñāna-svabhāvatvaṃ maṇi-prabhṛtīnāṃ prabhākhyatvavat cit jñānāśrayatvam api aviruddham ity uktam \| svayaṃ ca paricchinnam eva jñānaṃ saṅkoca-vikāsārhaṃ nityam upapāditam \| ataḥ kṣetrajñasya ayaṃ karmaṇā saṅkucita-vistaraṃ tat tatkarmānuguṇa-taratama-bhāvena vartate \| tat ca etad īśvareṇa vyavasthitam \| tasmin īśvara-dvāraṃ jñāna-prasara-saṅkocaḥ syād astamayabhayaveśaḥ śrūyate \| jñāna-prasare tu kartṛtvam asya eva \| tat ca na svabhāvikam; api tu karmakṛtanimittavikriyā-svarūpaḥ eva ātmā \| evaṃ kartrvikriyātmakaṃ jñātṛtvaṃ jñātarūpatayā tena eva na kadācid api jaḍasya ahaṅkārasya jñātṛtva-saṃbhavaḥ \|

jaḍatva-rūpatayāgrahaṅkārasya cit-saṃnidhānena tacchāyāpannayā tat sambhavati iti cet; kā iyaṃ cit-chāyāpattiḥ ? kim ahaṅkāra-cchāyāpatti-saṃvid ? uta saṃvicchāyāpattiḥ ahaṅkārasya ? na tāvat saṃvidaḥ, saṃvidaḥ jñātṛtvānabhyupagamāt \| na api ahaṅkārasya, uktarītyā tasya jaḍasya jñātṛtvāyogāt, dṛgoraśvagrīvad bhūdevādhaḥ na hy acākṣuṣāṇāṃ chāyā dṛṣṭā \| atha, arinisaṃparkadīpindiṣṭhavyacitrasamparka-jñātatvopalandhiḥ iti cet; na etat, saṃvidi vastuto jñātṛtvānabhyupagamād eva na tatsaṃparkād ahaṅkāre jñātṛtva-tadupalabdhiḥ api \| ahaṅkārasya tu acaitanasya jñātṛtāsaṃbhavād eva sutarāṃ na tatsaṃparkāt saṃvidi jñātṛtvaṃ tadupalabdhiḥ vā \|\|

yad uktam, uṣmagna na vahneto jñātṛtvam iti \| ahaṅkāraḥ tad bhūteḥ mithyājñāna-saṃsthāna-sthāyivātubhūtim abhivyakti, ādarśādivad iti; tad api asamam; ātmanaḥ tasya evaṃ jyotiṣo jaḍa-

svarūpa-anudāra-abhidhyāna-ca-yogāt \| tat uktaṃ— śāstra-antara iva āditya-mahat-dvāro jahātakaḥ \| svayaṃ-jyotiṣā bhāsamānaṃ dhyāyati iti na yukti-sam \|\| iti \| svayaṃ-prakāśa-anubhava-adhīna-siddhayaḥ hi sarve padārthāḥ \| tatra tat-apratyakāraḥ api tat-udāra-anudita-tattva-itara-tva-svarūpa-prakāśana-ākhya-artha-siddhi-hetu-bhūtam anubhava-abhidhyāna-kriyā-saṃvidaḥ parihṛtā iti \| kiñca a-hat-dvāra-anubhavayoḥ svarūpa-ādi-virodhāt anubhūteḥ anubhavitṛ-svarūpaṃ ca na dvaya-udbhavāt īdṛśa-bhāvaḥ \| yathoktaṃ— vyaktaḥ tu vyajñcya-vacavyaṃ na ca syāt ati-hṛdayataḥ \| vyaktavyaṃ cet anubhūtiḥ iva asmat-irayā yathā ghaṭe \|\| iti \| na ca ravi-kara-nikarāṇāṃ sva-abhivyakti-kara-tala-abhidhyānatva-vat saṃvid-abhidhyāna-a-hat-dvāra-siddhatvaṃ ca saṃvidaḥ sādhīyaḥ; tathā api ravi-kara-nikarāṇāṃ kara-tala-abhidhyānaṃ ca abhāvāt, kara-tala-pratihata-natayaḥ hi raśmayaḥ bahula-āraṇyam eva sphuṭa-taram upalabhyante iti tat-udāra-hetutvāt kara-talasya na abhidhyānakatvam \|\| kiñca asya saṃvit-svarūpasya anādi-ākāra-anityābhivyaktiḥ kiṃ-rūpā ? na tāvat utpattiḥ; svataḥ-siddhatvāt jñānasya eva upādhātmyaguṇa-bhāvāt \| na api tat-prakāśanam, tasya anubhava-antaram anubhāvyatvāt \| tatra eva ca na tat-anubhava-sādhana-yogaḥ \| sa hi vidyā; jñeya-caitanya-saṃbandhena eva vā, yathā jāti-nimitta-vyakti-grahaṇe vyakti-darśanādenaṃ nayana-ādeḥ indriya-saṃbandha-hetutvena; dṛṣṭānta-karaṇa-āpanna-tvena vā, yathā para-tattva-avabodhana-sādhanasya śāstrasya śābda-samānām \| yathokta-karaṇānām abhūtivāt tat-saṃbandha-hetuḥ iti \|\| kiñca anubhūteḥ anubhavya-tva-abhyupameya-prahaṇe na tat-anubhava-sādhana-anugraha-sambhavaḥ; sa hi anubhavaḥ anubhavetṛ-pratiniyati-vyadhirasamena bhavet; yathā rūpa-ādi-grahaṇe pravṛtti-nirapekṣaṃ tamas-nirāsena cakṣuṣaḥ dīpa-ādinā \| na ca iha tathā-vidhaṃ nirasanīyaṃ saṃbhavyate \| na tāvat saṃvidi vartamānaṃ tat-jñāna-utpatti-nirodhi kiñcit upalabdhi-rūpam asti \| asti hi ajñāna-smṛtiḥ ca eva, ajñānasya ahaṅkāra-āspada-ucchvāna-anubhava-mātra \| jñānam eva hi ajñānasya nivartakam \| na ca saṃvid-āśraya-kṣetrajñātasya saṃbhavati; jñāta-samāna-āśrayatvāt samāna-viṣayatvāt ca jñātṛ-bhāva-viṣaya-bhāva-nirhite jñāna-mātre sākṣiṇi na jñānaṃ bhavitum arhati \| yathā jñāna-āśraya-kṣetrajña-vijñānatvena ghaṭa-ādeḥ nija-anāśrayatvam, tathā jñāna-mātre api jñāna-āśrayatva-abhāvena na ajñāna-āśrayatvaṃ syāt \| saṃvidaḥ ajñāna-āśrayatva-abhyupagame api ātma-svarūpa-āśrayatayā avasthayā jñāna-viṣayatva-mātreṇa jātena na tat-gata-ajñāna-nivṛttiḥ \| jñānaṃ hi sva-viṣaye eva ajñānaṃ nivartayati, yathā rajjvādi \| ataḥ na kena api kathañcit saṃvid-āśrayam ajñānam ucchedyet \| anya ca sat-adarśanena eva nīyasya ajñānasya svarūpam eva durnirūpam iti upariṣṭāt vakṣyate \| jñāna-grābhāva-cchandasya ca ajñānasya jñāna-utpatti-virodhitva-abhāvena na tat-virasena tat-jñāna-sādhana-anugrahaḥ \| ataḥ na kena api prakāreṇa a-hat-dvāraḥ anubhūteḥ abhivyaktiḥ \|\|

na ca svāśrayatayā avidyā-jñānayoḥ avidyā-jñāna-bādhya-jñānakānāṃ svabhāvaḥ; pradeśoparikṣya-darśanāt, yathāsthita-padārtha-vyatireka-guṇa-svabhāvatvāt ca jñāna-tat-sādhanayoḥ; upagrāhakasya ca \| tat ca svataḥ-prāmāṇya-nyāya-siddham \| na ca darpaṇādi-samaveta-rūpi-mukham; api tu bāhya-vastu-pratikalpana-rūpa-doṣa-hetuḥ \| tadvat advayaḥ ca tanmātrayatva-ābhāsaḥ \| avidyā-jñāna-sthalaḥ api tathā eva \| na ca etat tathāhṛdūreṇa saṃvidi svaprakāśāyāṃ tādṛśa-doṣāpātanaṃ saṃbhavati \| vyaktiḥ tu jātiḥ iti ākāraḥ iti tad-āśrayatayā pratītiḥ; na tu vyaktiḥ jñāyatvāt \| ataḥ antaḥkaraṇa-bhūta-atadīya-rūpa-sthayā saṃvit-phala-labdhaiḥ dvaitatve doṣa-leśaḥ vā na kiñcit iha kāraṇam iti māḍhyūrarasya jñātṛtvaṃ tathā upapattibhyām \| tasmāt svataḥ eva jñānatayā siddha-brahmārthaḥ eva pratyagātmā; na śāstra-mātram \| ahaṃ-bhāva-niṣedhe tu jñāteḥ api na pratyaktva-siddhiḥ iti uktam \|\|

tamoguṇābhimāna-parāya-anubhava-mātra-avācchāhārthasya vivikta-spaṣṭa-pratibhāsam abhedya-prabodhāt ahaṃmityākāra-anusaraskurvāṇāḥ tad-upāttau api na ahaṃ-bhāvitām \| bhavad-abhimatayā anubhūteḥ api tatra eva grathitaṃ vaktavyam \| na hi mṛta-utthitaḥ kaścit deha-atiriktatā-artha-tattva-pratyabhijñākārā jñātitaraham ajñāna-sākṣitayā avatiṣṭha iti evaṃvidhāṃ svāpa-smṛtim anusarati \| evaṃ hi mṛta-utthitasya paramarśa-sukham aham avāpsam iti \| anena pratyavamarśena tadānīm api aham-artha-svethām anubhūtatvaṃ jñātṛtvaṃ ca jñāyate \| na ca vācyaṃ, yathā idānīṃ mukhaṃ bhavati, bhūyān tadānīm—svāpa-smṛti-vedyā pratipattiḥ iti; asmad-parīkṣātvipanneḥ \| na ca aham-artha-adhyātmānaḥ ’syataḥ eva tadānīm aham-arthasya suvidvat-anusandhānatva-upapattiḥ; yat sukha-supti-tad-āśayāḥ prāgbhūtaṃ vastu suṣupti-sthitaḥ ‘madde kurvan madde sukham aham asvapsam etat vocam’ iti parāmṛśati \| ‘etāvantaṃ kālaṃ na kiñcit aham ajñāsiṣam’ īti ca parāmṛśati cet; tataḥ kim? na ‘kiñcit iti kṛtsna-niṣedha’ iti cet ‘(na) aham avediṣam’ iti veditoḥ aham-arthasya anuguneḥ vedya-viṣayaḥ hi sa pratiṣedhaḥ \| na kiñcit iti niṣedhasya kṛtsna-viṣayatve bhavad-dhṛmata-anubhūtiḥ api pratiṣiddhā syāt \| supti-samaye tvayuḥ ‘dhiyamānam aham artha-mātrāmāṃ jñātāram anubhavitīti parāmṛśyan na kiñcit vediṣam iti vedate tasya pratiṣedhyamāne tasmin nu kāle niṣidhyamānatayā virodhi-siddhi-mukha-vartamānasya jñātuḥ aham-arthasya carama-siddhena eva ‘na kiñcit aham avediṣam’ iti parāmarśena rādhayitavyam arthaṃ devānām eva sādhayatu \| ‘mām ayam na jñātavān’ iti aham-arthasya api tatvataḥ anumātṛdhāraṃ pratīyāt iti cet; sva-anubhava-svacavacanayoḥ virodham api na jānanti bhavantaḥ \| ‘ahaṃ mām na jñātavān’ iti hi anubhava-vacane bhāṣiti kiṃ niṣidhyate iti cet; sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ bhavatā, tat ucyate—aham-arthasya jñātṛtva-vṛtteḥ na svarūpaṃ niṣidhyate; api tu prabodhāmayeyajña-dhiyamānatayā aham-arthasya vyāpiṣabhāvidiviśiṣṭatā \| ahaṃ mām na jñātavān iti ukte viṣayaḥ vivecanīyaḥ \| jāgarita-tatsthānusṛtahita-jātyādi-viśiṣṭaḥ aham-arthaḥ mām iti asya viṣayaḥ \| svāpyavasthā-pratisiddha-acid-rūpatva-anubhava-ekatābhāḥ-

**mayūkhaḥ ahaṃ-saṃśayasya viṣayaḥ \| atra suptaḥ ahaṃ na iha īśaḥ aham iti ca mām api na jñātavān aham iti eva khalu anubhava-ākāraḥ \|\|**

kiñ ca, suṣuptau ātma-jñāna-sākṣitve na asti iti hi bhavadīyā prakriyā \| sākṣitvaṃ ca sākṣāt jñātṛtvam eva \| na hi ajñānas sākṣitvam \| jñātā eva hi loke devadattas sākṣī iti vyapadiśyate; na jñāna-mātram \| smarati ca bhagavān pāṇiniḥ sākṣāt draṣṭari saṃjñāyām, iti sākṣāt jñātṛm eva sākṣi-śabdam \| sa ca ayaṃ sākṣī jānāmi iti pratīyamānaḥ aham-artha eva iti kutaḥ tava anīm aham-arthaḥ na pratīyeta ? asmin svayam avabhāsamānaḥ aham iti eva avabhāsata iti svāpa-ādi-avasthāsu api ātmā prakāśamānaḥ aham iti eva avabhāsata iti siddham \|\|

astu, mokṣa-daśāyām aham-arthaḥ na anuvartata iti; tad api aphalam \| tathā sati ātma-nāśa eva apavargaḥ prakāra-antareṇa pratijñātaḥ syāt \| na ca aham-arthaḥ dharma-mātram; yena tad-vinośe api dharmiṇi tyucyāvicchinna svarūpa-sthitiḥ iṣṭā \| prakṛta-svabhāvam eva aham-arthaḥ ātmanaḥ \| jñānaṃ tu tasya dharmaḥ, ‘ahaṃ jānāmi, jñānaṃ me jātam’ iti ca aham-artha-paryantaṃ jñāna-pratīteḥ eva \| api ca yaḥ paramārthataḥ bhrāntyā vā aṇḍya-parisphurat-duḥkhaḥ; vidyayā svātmanā anubhūyate—ahaṃ duḥkhī iti; sa eva etad-duḥkha-jātaṃ mām evam mocaya katham aham ānukūla-svasthaḥ bhaveyam iti upakrama-īkṣatām asya eva tattat-sādhane pravṛtteḥ \| sa sādhanānuṣṭhānena yadi ahaṃ eva na bhaviṣyāmi iti avajñeta; apanudet eva asau mokṣa-kathā-prastāvāt \| tataḥ tad-adhikāri-virahāt eva sarvaṃ mokṣa-śāstram apramāṇaṃ syāt \| aham-upalakṣitaṃ prakāśamānam eva apavarga-avadhi-sthita iti cet; kim anena ? mayi na ahe api kim api prakāśamānam apavarga-avadhi-sthita iti matvā na hi kaścid buddhimān pravartate \| ataḥ aham-arthasya eva jñātṛtayā siddhyataḥ pratyag-ātmatvam \| sa ca pratyag-ātmā muktau avahanti eva prakāśate, svasmai prakāśamānatvāt \| yaḥ yaḥ svasmai prakāśate, sa sarvaḥ aham iti eva prakāśate, yathā tathā anubhava-samāna-deśaḥ bhūta-ādiṣu matas-saṃsādharmimā \| yaḥ punaḥ aham iti na cakāsti; na asau svasmai prakāśate, yathā ghaṭa-ādiḥ \| svasmai prakāśate ca ayaṃ mukta-ātmā \| tasmāt aham iti eva prakāśate \| na ca aham iti prakāśamānatve na tasya antatvaṃ śārīratva-praśrayaḥ, mokṣa-virodhaḥ, antatva-ahetutvāt ca ahaṃ-pratyayasya \| ajñānaṃ nāma svarūpa-jñānam adhyastānāṃ viparīta-jñānaṃ vā ? aham iti eva ātma-svarūpa-sphitiḥ iti svarūpa-jñāna-rūpaḥ ahaṃ-pratyayaḥ na ajñānam āpādayati; kutaḥ saṃsāritvam ? api tu tad-viṣayitva-ajñānasya eva \|\|

brahma-ātma-svabhāva-pariccheda-nivṛtti-nirdoṣa-avidyā-nāma hi vāmadeva-ādi-vat aham iti eva ātmānu bhavad-darśanāt ca \| śrūyate hi—saḥ ahaṃ tat-pratyag-viparīyaveśaḥ pratiṣedhe ahaṃ manuḥ abhavaṃ sūryaḥ ca, iti, grahaikaḥ prathamajāṃ vartimi ca bhaviṣyāmi ca, iti ādi \| sakala-itara-jñāna-virodhataḥ

sat-śabda-pratyaya-mātra-bhājaḥ parasya brahmaṇo vyavahāraḥ advayam eva—“hanta aham imāḥ tisraḥ devatāḥ; bahu syām prajāyeya, saḥ īkṣata lokān nu sṛjā iti \| tathā—“asmāt ātmanaḥ tattveṇa u ’ham akṣa \| dīpi ca uttamaḥ \| ataḥ asmin loke vede ca prathitaḥ puruṣottamaḥ \|\| “aham annam ā \| guḍākeśa, na veda aham jātu na āsam, ahaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ pralayaḥ tathā, ahaṃ sarvasya prabhavaḥ mattaḥ sarvaṃ pravartate, teṣām ahaṃ samuddhartā mṛtyu-saṃsāra-sāgarāt, ahaṃ bīja-pradaḥ pitā, veda aham samatītāni—iti-ādiṣu \| yahaṭhṛtve ca avasanḥ sva-cchandaḥ kaḥ ca tasya haṃkārasya kṣetra-antar-bhāvaḥ bhagavatā upadiśyate mahā-bhūtāni ahaṃkāraḥ buddhiḥ avyaktam eva ca iti ? ucyate—svarūpa-upadheṣu sarveṣu aham iti eva upadeśāt anyat ca ātma-svarūpam iti pareṣām ahaṃ iti eva pratyag-ātmanaḥ svarūpam \| avasthā-pariṇāma-bheda-stha-ahaṃkārasya kṣetra-antar-bhāvaḥ bhagavatā upadiśyate \| saḥ tu anātmāni dehe ’haṃ-kāraṇa-hetutvena ahaṃkāraḥ iti ucyate \| atha tvaṃkāra-śabdasya amṛta-tantra-yaveśyaṃ cin-mātra-vyatiriktasya vyutpattiḥ iṣṭavyā \| ayam eva tvaṃkāraḥ utkhāḍanāya anā-hetuḥ vacanāni śāstreṣu bahūni he yatayā pratipādyate \| tasmat sukha-ād-ākāreṇa atāḍa bahiḥ asmāt sākṣāt asagocarā eva \| śarīra-gaḥ acaya tvaṃ bahiḥ-vicyaṃ \| yathoktaṃ bhagavatā parāśareṇa—śrutayaḥ vāyu-avidyāyāḥ svarūpaṃ kula-nandana ! \| ātmani ātma-su-buddhiḥ īti \| yadi ha ātmānam eva ātmā, tadā ātmani ātmābhimāne śarīre atī-mātra-pratimāsaḥ syāt; na jñānatva-pratimāsaḥ \| tasmāt jñātā śarīraṃ eva ātmā \| tad uktam—ataḥ pratyakṣa-siddhatvam ukta-nyāya-anugamyatvāt \| avidyā-yogataḥ ca ātmā jñātā dehe iti bhāsate \|\| iti \| tathā ca deha-indriya-manāḥ-prāṇa-dhībhyaḥ anyaḥ ananya-sādhanaḥ \| nityaḥ vyāpī prati-kṣetra-ātmā bhinnaḥ tu asukhī \|\| iti \| ananya-sādhanaḥ—sva-prakāśaḥ \| vyāpī—ati-smṛtasya sa-cetana-antaḥ-praveśābhyabhāvāt \|\| madhvantaṃ doṣam ullekena anyathā-siddhi-saṃbhavanayā sakala-bheda-avalambi-viśva-pratyakṣasya śāstra-bādhyatva-vidhiti; kaḥ ayaṃ doṣaḥ iti vaktavyam, pratyūlena pratyakṣasya anyathā-siddhiḥ \| anādi-veda-vāsanā eva hi doṣaḥ iti cet; bheda-vāsanatva-asmita-sthira-dṛḍhāvasthita-vastuni parijñāna-hetutvaṃ kim atra śātra-paryā ? anena eva śāstra-virodhaḥ āropyata iti cet, anena upādhi-anugata—śāstrasya nirasta-nikhila-viśeṣa-vastu-bodhitva-niścaye sati bheda-vāsanayā doṣatva-niścayaḥ; bheda-vāsanayā doṣatva-niścaye sati śāstrasya nirasta-nikhila-diśeṣa-vastu-bodhitva-niścaya iti \| kiñca, yadi bheda-vāsanā-mūlatvena pratyakṣasya viparīta-arthatvam; śāstram api tat-mūlatvena tathā eva syāt \| ayaṃ ca upeta-doṣa-mūlatve ’pi śāstrasya pratyakṣa-āgata-sakala-bheda-nirasana-hetutvena paratvāt pratyakṣasya bādhakam iti \| tatra; doṣa-mūlatve jñāte sati paratva-kiñcit-karasya \| rajju-sarpa-jñāna-nimitta-bhaye sati śāntaḥ asmi iti parijñānāt kena cit ‘na ayaṃ sarpaḥ mā bhaiṣīḥ’ iti ukte ’pi bhaya-anivṛtti-darśanāt \| śāstrasya ca doṣa-mūlatvaṃ śravaṇa-velāyām eva jñātam; śravaṇa-avagata-akhila-bheda-mithyātma-ekatva

vijñāna-abhyāsa-rūpa-svapna-mānādeḥ \| api ca idaṃ śāstram; etan-mata-sthāpakatvena; pratyakṣaṃ tu saṃśayāt na doṣam iti kena ucyate tvayā? na tāvat saṃsāri-siddhā nitya-nirviśeṣa-viśeṣa-anubhūtiḥ asmabhyaṃ bhavayati; tasyāḥ sarva-viṣaya-parastvāt śāstra-kṣapāta-virohāt ca \| māyaiḥ nirdoṣaikaṃ pratyakṣaṃ, doṣa-mūlatvena viparīta-artha-avagāḥ \| tan-mūlatvāt eva na anyathā api pramāṇāni \| avastu-apakṣa-tādṛśa-pramāṇa-anupapatti-mātra svābhimata-artha-siddhiḥ \| nanu vyāvahārika-pramāṇam eva vyavahāraḥ asmākam upapadya eva \| kaḥ ayaṃ vyāvahārikaḥ nāma? āpta-svato-nirūddhaḥ yukti-bhinna-vatitaḥ na tathā avasthita iti cet; kiṃ tena prayojanam? pramāṇatvā pratipanne api yukti-mūla-bādhāt eva pramāṇa-kāraṇa-abhāvāt \|\|

atra ucyate—śāstra-pratyakṣayoḥ itaretara-vidyā-mūlatve api pratyakṣa-viṣayasya śāstreṇa bādhaḥ dṛśyate; śāstra-viṣayasya tad-viditīya-kṣaṇaḥ paścāt tad-abādha-darśita-viśeṣa-anubhūti-mātraṃ brahma eva paramārthaḥ—iti \| tat ayuktam, avabhāsitasya api doṣa-mūlasya apāramārthya-nirṇayāt \| evaṃ yuktaṃ bhavati—yathā sakala-itara-kāraṇa-ādi-doṣa-rahita-puruṣāntara-go cara-giri-śṛṅga-agra vasantataruṇa-kiraṇa-jyotiḥ-ālambana-tinisrūpa sarvasya timira-doṣa-viśeṣeṇa dvi-candra-jñāna-viviṣayaṃ jāyate \| na tatra bādhaka-pratyayaḥ asti iti na tat mithyā na bhavati iti tad-viṣaya-bhūtaṃ dvi-candratvam api mithyeta \| doṣaḥ hi apārtha-jñāna-ahetuḥ \| tathā brahma-ajñāna-avidyā-mūlatvena bādhaka-antara-hitam api sva-viṣayeṇa brahmaṇā mithyā iti \| bhavanti ca atra prayogāḥ—vivāda-adhyāsitaṃ brahma mithyā, avidyāvat utpanna-jñāna-viṣayatvāt, prapañcavat \| brahma mithyā, jñāna-viṣayatvāt, prapañcavat \| mithyā, prapañcavat \| brahma mithyā, asattva-hetu-janya-jñāna-viṣayatvāt, prapañcavat eva \|\|

na ca vācyaṃ svānatmya hasti-artha-vijñānasya asatyasya paramārtha-subhāṣumātra-bhittiḥ nirhetu-bhāvat vividyā-mūlatvena śāstrasya api śāstrasya paramārtha-bhūta-brahma-viṣaya-pratipatti-hetu-bhāvaḥ na viruddha iti \| svāpnasya nitya-asatyatva-bhāvāt \| tatra hi viṣaya-guṇeṣu eva mithyātvam; teṣām eva hi bādhaḥ dṛśyate; na jñānasya; na hi mayā svapne velāyām anvabhūtaṃ jñānam api na vidyate iti kasyacit api pratyayaḥ jāyate \| darśanaṃ tu vidyate arthaṃ na smarāmi iti hi bādhaka-pratyayaḥ \| māyāvinaḥ mantra-auṣadha-ādi prabhavaṃ māyāmayaṃ jñānaṃ satyam eva śrī-bhāṣyasya ca hetuḥ; tatra api jñānasya abādhitatvāt \| viṣayeṣu indriya-ādi-doṣa-janyaṃ rajata-ādi sarpa-ādi-vijñānaṃ satyam eva bhaya-ādi-hetuḥ \| satyatvāvadṣṭe api svāntarni sarva-auṣadhi-drāna-dagdhu-buddhiḥ \| satyena hāḍḍūviṣṇudrimiragṛheṣuśrutā \| vastu-bhūtaḥ eva jalāśayaḥ mukha-ādi pratibhāsī vastu-bhūta-mukha-ādi-viṣaya-niścaya-hetuḥ \| evaṃ saṃvedana-anubhava-nisvarūpa-artha-kriyā-kāritvāt ca satyatvam avasīyate \| hasti-aśva-ādīnām abhāve api kathaṃ tad-buddhya-satyāḥ bhavanti iti cet; na evaṃ, buddhīnāṃ śālabhanakṣamādriyāmāt \| arthasya pratibhāsamānatvam eva hi ātmakanatve

apekṣitam; pratijñā-mānatā śāstrasya doṣa-vahāt \| sa tu bādhitaḥ asatyaḥ iti ucyate \| avadhṛtā hi buddhiḥ tat-saṃvedyutvam \|
