rekhayā varṇa-pratipattau api na asaṃkhyātasya buddhiḥ; rekhāyāḥ tattvāt \| nanu varṇa-smṛtau pratipakṣā rekhā varṇa-buddhi-hetuḥ; varṇi-smṛtau tu asatyā \| na evam, varṇi-smṛtāyāḥ asatyāyāḥ upayuktatva-prasaṅgāt \| asataḥ nirupākhyasya hi upayukte na dṛṣṭam upapannaṃ ca \| atha tasyāḥ varṇa-buddhau upayuktasya eva tattva-saṃyatatva-buddhiṃ sthānu, buddhi-smṛty-apadeśe; upāya-upayayoḥ aikya-prasaṅgaḥ ca, upapannā varṇa-buddhiḥ iti aviśeṣāt \| rekhāyāḥ abhidhānāt varṇam āropayate vaiśakṣyam, evaṃ rekhā-gām aviṣaya-mānasaṃvarṇātmakatvasya sukhpratvādeḥ rekhādhīna-asaṃvedya-artha-pratipatti-sthānam \| atha piṇḍa-viśeṣe devadatta-ādi-śabda-saṅketa-vat śuktyādi-rekhā-viśeṣe śveta-prāya-varṇa-viśeṣa-saṅketayā rekhā-viśeṣe varṇa-viśeṣa-buddhi-hetuḥ iti \| hanta tarhi satyadeve satya-pratipattiḥ; rekhāyāḥ saṃketasya ca satyatvāt \|\|

rekhā-gavyāt api satya-gavaya-buddhiḥ sādharmyāt nibaddhā, sādharmyaṃ ca satyam eva \| na ca eka-rūpasya śabdasya nāda-viśeṣaṇa-artha-bheda-buddhi-hetutve api asatyatasya pratipattiḥ; nānā-nāda-abhivyakta-arthatvena eva śabdasya tattathārthāt niṣṭajñāna-rūpatvena artha-viśeṣa-stha-sambandha-gṛhaṇa-avadhārthaiḥ bheda-buddheṣu varṇite hetutvāt \| śabdasya artha-rūpatvam api na sādhīyaḥ; ekārthā eva eka-rūpasya eva śveta-prāyavene śabdatvāt \| ataḥ asatyāñcchāhātatva-prabhṛti-viṣaya-pratipattīndu rūpavattā \|\|

nanu na śāstrasya gagana-kusuma-vat asattvam; prāk vā anānātva-buddhi-bodhyatvāt \| utpanne jñāne hi asattvaṃ śāstrasya \| na tathā śāstraṃ nirasta-sakala-bheda-cin-mātra-brahma-jñāna-upāyaḥ \| pradeśopārthattvāt asti eva śāstram, asti iti tu buddheḥ \| na evam; atha hi śāstre asti śāstram iti buddhiḥ mithyātvāt \| tataḥ kim? idaṃ tataḥ—mithyābhūta-śāstra-janya-jñānasya mithyātveta tad-vīṣayasya api brahmaṇaḥ mithyātvam; yathā dhūmadhvaja-gṛhīta-tāpa-janya-agni-jñānasya mithyātveta tad-vīṣayasthāneḥ api mithyātvam \| pakṣonmukha-vādha-darśana-āśrālitvam; śūnyam eva tattvam iti vādhena tasyāḥ api vādha-darśanāt \| śruti-nusmṛtiḥ iti cet; etat api śruti-nusmṛtiḥ iti tvayā eva uktam \| pakṣasya ayādhādaraṇaṃ tu tvayā eva iti alam ativistara-kuṭarka-pariśrameṇa \|\|

**śruti-virodhaḥ**

yad uktaṃ vedānta-tatvāni api nirviśeṣa-ahaṅkāra-vastu-mātra-pratipādana-parāṇi, na eva eva mocayet iti parāmarśaḥ, ideyamādenīti; tad ayuktam, eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñā-upapādana-mukhena sac-chabda-vācyasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ jagad-upādānatvam, jagat-pratimitatvam, sarvajñatā, sarva-śakti-yogaḥ, satya-saṅkalpatvam, sarva-niyantṛtvam, sarvādhāratvam, sarva-antaryamitvād aneka-kalyāṇa-guṇa-viśiṣṭatvam, kṛtsnasya jagataḥ tasya āsmakatāṃ ca pratijñāya, evaṃ-bhūta-brahma-ātmakatvam sīti śvetaketuṃ prati upadeśāya

pravṛttatvāt karaṇam \| prasiddha-śruty-artho vedāntaiḥ eva \| atra api ātma-grahaṇa-adhikaraṇe nirguṇataram upapādayiṣyate \| atha parā yathā tat akṣaram, ityādi prākṛtāt heya-guṇāt pratiṣidhya nityatva-vibhutva-sūkṣmatva-sarvagatatva-vyayatva-bhūta-yonitva-asaṅkhyādi-kalyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇaḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ pratipāditaḥ \| satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti atra api sāmānādhikaraṇya-sthāna-eka-viśeṣaṇa-vidhiḥ iti eka-artha-siddhānurūpatayā na nirviśeṣa-vastu-siddhiḥ \| pravṛtti-nimitta-bhedena eka-artha-vṛttitvaṃ hi sāmānādhikaraṇyam \| tatra satya-jñānādivat vastu-śabda-paryāṇa-tat-sad-yogādi-virodhy-ākāra-dvayena eka-ākāra-dvayakaiḥ asminn eva arthe padānāṃ pravṛttau nimitta-bhedo vyapadeśya-paryāṇaḥ \| idaṃ tu viśeṣa eka-sminn eva pakṣe padānāṃ mukhyārthatā; aparasmiṃs tu teṣāṃ lakṣaṇā \| na ca ajānātīnāṃ pratyayī-kartā vastu-svarūpam eva; ekam eva padena svarūpaṃ pratipannam iti pada-antara-prayogo vaiyarthyaṃ, tathā sati sāmānādhikaraṇya-asiddheḥ \| eka-smin vastuni vartamānānāṃ padānāṃ nimitta-bhedena āśrayatvāt \| na ca eka-rūpya-arthasya viśeṣaṇa-bhedena viśiṣṭatā-abhidhāna-eka-arthatvaṃ padānāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇya-virodhi; ekaśya eva vastunaḥ aneka-viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭatā-pratipādana-paratvāt sāmānādhikaraṇasya \| bhinna-pravṛtti-nimittānāṃ śabdānām eka-sminn arthe vṛttis sāmānādhikaraṇyam, iti hi śābdikāḥ \|\|

yuktam, ekam eva advitīyam iti advitīyatvaṃ guṇataḥ api sāhityāyaṃ na sahate; ataḥ sarva-śākhā-pratyaya-vyayena kāraṇa-vākyatām arhatī iti avastu-praśipākṣa-paratvam abhyupagamanīyam \| kāraṇatva-upari-lakṣitatayā advitīyasya brahmaṇaḥ lakṣaṇam idam ucyate, satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti \| ato lilakṣayiṣitaṃ brahma nirguṇam eva; anyathā nirguṇam, niranjanam, ityādi-vidhi-virodhaḥ ca—iti; tad api asat; jagad-upādānasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpa-niruktau adhikāntara-nicāraṇena vivicitra-śakti-yoga-pratipādana-paratvāt advitīya-padasya \| tatra eva vivicitra-śakti-yogam eva avagamayati—tad aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tat tejaḥ asṛjata, ityādi \| aviśeṣeṇa advitīya-niruktaṃ nimitta-antara-samaviniyogaḥ kathaṃ jñāyata iti cet; sisṛkṣoḥ eva brahmaṇaḥ upādāna-kāraṇatvaṃ tat eva somya idam agra āsīt ekam eva, iti pratipāditam \| kāryāpatti-lakṣaṇayā ca buddhasya nimitta-antaram iti tat eva advitīya-padena niṣidhyata iti avagamyate \| sarva-niṣedhe hi svāsthya-śruta-vākya-uparasa-siddhādhīnatā nityānta-vākyasya nividdhāḥ sahyuḥ \| sad eva, khalu idam agra āsīt bhagavo viparītaṃ phalam; sarveṣām api kāraṇa-vācināṃ sarveṣānta-vācināṃ guṇa-nāma-ātra-upasaṃhāre hetv-abhāvāt \| ataḥ kāraṇa-vākyasya bhāva-arthaṃ satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti anena sa-viśeṣam eva pratipādyata iti vijñāyate \|\|

na ca nirguṇa-vākya-virodhaḥ prākṛta-heya-guṇa-viṣayatvāt teṣāṃ nirguṇam, niranjanam, niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāntam, ityādīnām \| jñāna-mātra-svarūpa-vādinyaḥ api śrutayaḥ brahmaṇaḥ jñāna-svarūpatām abhidadhati; na tāvatā nirviśeṣa-jñāna-mātram eva tattvam, jñātuḥ eva jñāna-svarūpatvāt \|

jñāna-svarūpatayā eva tasya jñāna-āśrayatvaṃ maṇi-prabhṛtīnām api vidyut-prabhā iti uktam \| jñātṛtvaṃ ca eva hi sarva-viṣayatayā vadanti— asmat sarvaṃ jānāti, sa taṃ kṣetrajñaṃ, yaḥ sarvajñaḥ, yaḥ sarvavit, eṣa īśānaḥ lokasya sṛjā iti, nityaḥ nityānāṃ cetanaś cetanānām eko bahūnāṃ yo vidadhāti kāmān, jñāto draṣṭā śrotā, tasmin akṣaraṇi paraṃ maheśvaraṃ taṃ devānāṃ paramaṃ ca daivatam \| patiṃ patīnāṃ paramaṃ parastād vidāma devaṃ bhuvaneśam īḍyam na tasya kāryaṃ karaṇaṃ ca vidyate na tat-samaś ca abhyadhikaś ca dṛśyate \| parā asya śaktiḥ vividhā eva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca evaṃ ātmā upahantātmā vijaro vimṛtyur viśoko vijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ satyakāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpaḥ—ityādyāḥ śrutayo jñātṛtvaṃ pramukhān kalyāṇa-guṇān jñāna-svarūpatayā eva brahma-gatān svābhāvikān vadanti; samasta-heya-pratyanīkatāṃ ca \| nirguṇa-vākyānāṃ saguṇa-vākyānāṃ ca viṣayam apahatyopamedya-adhyāropitasya hetṛ-guṇān pratiṣiddhasya satyakāma-satya-saṅkalpatva-pramukhyaḥ iti brahmaṇaḥ kalyāṇa-guṇa-nivṛttayoṃ śrutiḥ eva vivinaktīti saguṇa-nirguṇa-vākyayoḥ virodha-abhāvāt aspatatvaṃ mithyā-vidhvasana-āśrayaṇam api na āśaṅkanīyam \|\|

bhogātra-māhātmyataḥ pravate, ityādinā brahma-guṇa-antarasya, sa ye śatam iti upakramaṇa kṣetrajña-ānanda-atiśaya-pramukhatvāt yato vāco nivartante, aprāpya manasā saha, ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān, iti brahmaṇaḥ kalyāṇa-guṇa-ānantyasya apareṇa vadasīti śrutiḥ \| saḥ aśnute sarvān kāmān saha, brahmaṇā vipaścitā, iti brahma-vedana-phala-bhūtam adhīyasya parasya vipaścitaḥ brahmaṇaḥ guṇa-ānantyaṃ bravīti \| vipaścitā brahmaṇā saha sarvān kāmān samāśnute \| kāmyan iti kāmāḥ—kalyāṇa-guṇāḥ \| brahmaṇā saha tad-guṇān sarvān aśnute ityarthaḥ \| dahara-vidyāyāṃ, tasmin yat antaḥ tat anveṣṭavyam, iti vaktum upakrāntasya vaktuṃ rahasya-śabdaḥ \| phala-upāsanayoḥ prakāraś ca iyaṃ, atha kratuḥ asmin loke puruṣaḥ bhavati tathā itaḥ pretya bhavatīti śrutyā eva siddham \|\|

satyam anantaṃ tasya matam, avijñātaṃ vijānatām, iti brahmaṇaḥ jñāta-aviṣayatvam uktvā cait; brahma-vit āpnoti param, brahma veda brahma eva bhavati, iti jñāna-mokṣa-upadeśaḥ na sthāt \| asan eva bhavati, asat brahma iti veda cet, asti brahma iti cet veda, santam enaṃ tataḥ viduḥ, iti brahma-viṣaya-jñāna-sadbhāva-asadbhāvābhyām ātma-nāśa-anātma-astitvaṃ ca vadanti \| ataḥ brahma-viṣaya-vedana-upāsana-parṇānāṃ sarva-śrutayaḥ vidadhati \| jñānaṃ ca upāsana-ātmakam \| upāsyaṃ ca brahma saguṇam ityuktam \| yato vāco nivartante, aprāpya manasā saha, iti brahmaṇaḥ anantasya aparicchinna-guṇasya vāṅ-manas-agocaratvavir iti paricchedya-yogyatva-vacanena brahma-jñāna-viruddha-vartanāṃ brahma vijñātam amatam ityuktam, aparicchinnatvāt brahmaṇaḥ \| anyathā satyam anantaṃ tasya matam, vijñātam avijñānatām iti mata-vijñātitva-vacanaṃ tatra eva virudhyeta \|\|

yaḥ tu na hṛṣṭeṣṭha-śūra...na sarvajñatā asya iti śrutiḥ hi chinna iti atiriktaṃ adhuraṃ mantāram ca pratiṣedhati iti; tad-ānantya-guṇa-yogitayā jñātuḥ ānanda-tva-rūpatāṃ kṛtaka-siddhāṃ mantvā na tathā asya nāmaṃ paśyeḥ, na mantreyoḥ; api tu brahmaṃ mantāram api ātmānaṃ dahimita-tirūpam eva paśyet ity-ādi-nidyatīti parihṛtam \| athavā hṛṣṭeṣṭha-śūraṃ mantāraṃ jīvātmānaṃ pratiṣidhya sarva-bhūta-antarātmānaṃ paramātmānam eva upāstv iti vākyārthaḥ; anyathā vijñātāram are kena vijānīyāt, ity-ādi-vijñātṛtva-śruti-virodhaḥ ca \|\|

ānandaḥ brahma iti ānanda-mātram eva brahma-svarūpaṃ pratīyata iti yuktam; tad-jñāna-āśrayasya brahmaṇo jñānaṃ svarūpam iti vakti iti parihṛtam \| jñānam eva hi anukūlam ānandaḥ iti ucyate \| vijñānam ānandaṃ brahma jñāna-svarūpam eva jñānaṃ śabdyati arthaḥ \| ata eva bhavati-bheka-rasutā \| asya jñāna-svarūpatvaṃ ca etatvāt api śruti-śata-samadhigatam iti uktam \| hṛṣṭeṣṭhe sa eko brahmaṇa ānandaḥ, ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān, iti vyatireka-nirdeśāt ca na ānanda-mātraṃ brahma; api tu ānandī \| jñātṛtve eva hi ānanditvam \|\|

yat idam uktam yatra hi dvaitam iva bhavati, neha nānā asti kiñcana, mṛtyoḥ sa mṛtyum ā-pnoti yaḥ iha nānā eva paśyati, yatra tu asya sarvam ātmā eva abhūt takena kaṃ paśyet, iti bheda-niṣedhaḥ bahudhā hṛṇyata iti; tat-kṛtsnasya jagato brahma-kāryatayā tad-atarthādhikatayā ca tad-ātmakatve-

na uktyā, tat-sarva-niṣṭha-nānātvaṃ pratiṣidhyate; na punaḥ bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti bahu-bhavata-saṅkalpa-pūrvakaṃ brahmaṇo nānātvaṃ śruti-siddhaṃ pratiṣidhyata iti parihṛtam \| nānātva-niṣedhādi idam parasārtha-viṣayati ca uktam; pratyakṣādi-sakala-pramāṇa-avagataṃ nānātvaṃ durārohaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pratiṣidhya tad eva bādhyata iti upahāsa-āspadam idam \|\|

raddhā hi eva eva abhimuditam antaraṃ kurute, atha tasya bhayaṃ bhavati, iti brahmaṇi nānātvaṃ paśyataḥ bhaya-prāptiḥ iti yuktam; tat sat, yat vā khalu idaṃ brahma tat-jalān iti śānta upāsīta, iti tathā-nātatvāspṛdhānasya śānti-hetutvopadeśāt \| tathā hi—sarveṣya utpatti-tat-sthiti-silaya-krameṇa tad-ātmakatvānusandhānena eva śāntiḥ vidhīyate \| prato yathāvasthita-deha-triṣaṇḍa-manuṣya-stāvarādi-smebhinnaṃ jagad brahātmakam iti usandhānasya śānti-hetutvā abhayaprāpti-hetutvena na bhaya-hetutva-prasaṅgaḥ \| evaṃ tarhi atha tasya bhayaṃ bhavati iti kim ucyate ? idam ucyate raddhā hi eva eṣa etasmin adrśye anātmye anirukte anilayane abhayaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ vindate, atha saḥ abhayaṃ gataḥ bhavati, iti abhaya-prāpti-hetutvena brahmaṇi yā pratiṣṭhā abhihitā; tasyā vicchede bhayaṃ bhavati iti \| yathā uktaṃ maharṣibhiḥ-- anu-muhūrtaṃ kṣaṇaṃ vā api vāsudevo na cintyate \| sā hāniḥ tat-mahat-chidraṃ sā bhrāntiḥ sā ca vikriyā \|\| ity-ādi \| brahmaṇi pratiṣṭhāyā anantasva-kālo viccheda eva \|\|

yad uktam, śrautaṃ sthānataḥ api, iti sarva-viśeṣa-rahitaṃ brahma iti ca vaktavyataḥ api, tatra, saviśeṣaṃ brahma eva hi tatra vakṣyati \| māyā-mātraṃ tu, iti ca svāpānām amathyārthānāṃ jāgarita-avasthā-anubhūta-padārtha-vaidharmyeṇa māyā-mātratvam ucyate iti jāgarita-avasthā-anubhūtānām iva pāramārthikatvam eva vakṣyati \|\|

**smṛti-purāṇa-vākyaiḥ**

smṛti-purāṇayoḥ api nirviśeṣa-jñāna-mātram eva paramārthaḥ iti upapāditam iti pratīyate iti yad abhihitam; tat atat—

yo mām ajam anādi ca vetti loka-maheśvaram \| mahataḥ param avyaktam avyaktāt puruṣaḥ paraḥ \| te dhyāna-yoga-anugatā apaśyan devātma-śaktiṃ sva-guṇaiḥ nigūḍhām \| nityaḥ nityānāṃ cetanaḥ cetanānām \| pradhāna-kṣetrajña-patiḥ guṇa-īśaḥ \| patiṃ sarvavid-ātma-īśvaraṃ śāśvataṃ śivam acyutam \| jñaṃ jñau dvau ajau īśa-anīśau \| eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt \| na brahmā na īśānaḥ \| kāraṇaṃ tu dhyeyaḥ \| yaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu tiṣṭhan sarvebhyaḥ bhūtebhyaḥ antaraḥ yaṃ sarvāṇi bhūtāni na viduḥ yasya sarvāṇi bhūtāni śarīram \| yaḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni antaro yamayati \| bhūta-bhṛt ca bhūta-sthaḥ mama ātmā bhūta-bhāvanaḥ \|\| ahaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ pralayaḥ tathā \| mattaḥ parataraṃ na anyat kiñcit asti dhanañjaya \| mayi sarvam idaṃ protaṃ sūtre maṇi-gaṇā iva \|\| viṣṭabhya aham idaṃ kṛtsnam eka-aṃśena sthito jagat \|\| uttamaḥ puruṣaḥ tu anyaḥ paramātmā iti udāhṛtaḥ \| yo loka-trayam āviśya bibharti avyayaḥ īśvaraḥ \|\| yasmāt kṣaram atītaḥ aham akṣarāt api ca uttamaḥ \| ataḥ asmi loke vede ca prathitaḥ puruṣottamaḥ \|\| sarva-bhūta-stham ātmānaṃ vikāra-rā guṇa-ādi-doṣaḥ ca mune vyatītaḥ \| atīta-sarva-āvaraṇaḥ akhila-ātmā tena saṃstūtaṃ yat bhavān antarāle \| samasta-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ātmakaḥ asau sva-śakti-laiśa-āvṛta-bhūta-vargaḥ \| icchā-gṛhīta-abhimata-uru-dehaḥ saṃsādhita-aśeṣa-jagat-hitaḥ asau \|\| tejo-bala-aiśvarya-mahā-avabodha-suvīrya-śakti-ādi-guṇa-eka-rāśiḥ \| paraḥ parāṇāṃ sakalā na yatra kleśa-ādayaḥ santi parāvara-īśe \|\| sa īśvaro vyaṣṭi-samaṣṭi-rūpaḥ avyakta-svarūpaḥ prakaṭa-svarūpaḥ \| sarveśvaraḥ sarva-dṛk sarva-vettā samasta-saktiḥ parameśvara-ākhyaḥ \|\| saṃjñāyate yena tat asta-doṣaṃ śuddhaṃ paraṃ nirmalam eka-rūpam \| sandṛśyate vā api adhigamyate vā tat jñānam ajñānam ataḥ anyat uktam \|\| śuddhe mahā-vibhūti-ākhye pare brahmaṇi śabdyate \| maitreya bhagavat-śabdaḥ sarva-kāraṇa-kāraṇe \|\| saṃjñā iti tathā bharti bhakāra-arthaḥ ayam udgataḥ \| netā gamayitā straṣṭā gakāra-arthaḥ tathā mune \|\| aiśvaryasya samagrasya vīryasya yaśasaḥ śriyaḥ \| jñāna-vairāgyayoḥ ca eva ṣaṇṇāṃ bhagaḥ iti īraṇā \|\| vasanti tatra bhūtāni bhūta-ātman akhila-ātmani \| sa ca bhūteṣu aśeṣu vakāra-arthaḥ tataḥ avyayaḥ \|\| jñāna-śakti-bala-aiśvarya-vīrya-tejāṃsi aśeṣataḥ \| bhagavat-śabda-vācyāni vinā heyaiḥ guṇa-ādibhiḥ \|\| evam eva mahā-śabdaḥ maitreya bhagavān iti \| parama-brahma-bhūtasya vāsudevasya na anyagaḥ \|\| tatra pūjya-padārtha-ukti-paribhāṣā-samanvitaḥ \| śabdaḥ ayaṃ na upacāreṇa tu anyatra hi upacārataḥ \|\| samastā-śakti-rūpāṇi tat karoti janeśvara \| deva-tiryaṅ-manuṣya-ākhyā-ceṣṭāvanti sva-līlayā \|\| jagatām upakārāya na sā karma-nimittajā \| ceṣṭā tasya aprameyasya vyāpina-vyāhata-ātmikā \|\| evaṃ-prakāram amalaṃ nityaṃ vyāpakam akṣayam \| samasta-

heya-rahitaṃ viṣaya-arthaṃ paramaṃ padam \|\| varaḥ parāṇāṃ paramaḥ paramātmā aṇḍa-samāśritaḥ \| rūpa-varṇa-ādi-nirdeśa-viśeṣaṇa-vivarjitaḥ \|\| akṣarāya avināśākhyaṃ pariṇāma-rdi-janvabhiḥ \| varjitaṃ śakyate vaktuṃ prasava-ādyāḥ asti iti kevalam \|\| sarvatra asau samastaṃ ca vasati atra iti vai yataḥ \| tataḥ sa vāsudeva iti vidvadbhiḥ paripaṭhyate \|\| śrīḥ tat brahma paraṃ nityam ajaram akṣaram avyayam \| eka-svarūpaṃ ca sadā heya-abhāvāt ca nirmalam \|\| tat eva sarvam eva etat vyakta-avyakta-svarūpavat \| tathā puruṣa-rūpeṇa kāla-rūpeṇa ca sthitam \|\| śrutiḥ vā api mayā aṇḍakṣyātā vyakta-avyakta-svarūpiṇī \| puruṣa-prāṇa-bhedaḥ līyate paramātmani \|\| paramātmā ca sarveṣām ādhāraḥ parameśvaraḥ \| viṣṇu-nāmā sa vedeṣu vedānteṣu ca gīyate \|\| dve rūpe brahmaṇaḥ tasya mūrtaṃ ca amūrtam eva ca \| kṣara-akṣara-svarūpe te sarva-bhūteṣu ca sthite \|\| akṣaraṃ tat paraṃ brahma kṣaraṃ sarvam idaṃ jagat \| eka-ideśa-sthitasya agneḥ jyotsnā vistāriṇī yathā \|\| parasya brahmaṇaḥ śaktiḥ tathā idam akhilaṃ jagat \|\| vicitrā śaktiḥ parā proktā kṣetrajña-ākhyā tathā aparā \| avidyā karma-saṃjñā anyā tṛtīyā śaktiḥ iṣyate \|\| yayā kṣetrajña-śaktiḥ sā veṣṭitā nṛpa sarvagā \| saṃsāra-tāpān akhilān avāpnoti ati-santatān \|\| tayā tirohitatvāt ca śaktiḥ kṣetrajña-saṃjñitā \| sarva-bhūteṣu bhūpāla tāratamyena vartate \|\| pradhānaṃ ca pumān ca eva sarva-bhūta-ātma-bhūtayā \| viṣṇu-śaktyā mahā-buddhe vṛtau saṃśrayarmiṇau \|\| tayoḥ sā eva pṛthag-bhāva-kāraṇaṃ saṃśrayasya ca \|\| yathā sarvagaṃ jale vātaḥ bibharti kañjika-uṣitam \| śaktiḥ tathā vikoṇoḥ pradhāna-puruṣa-ātmanḥ \|\| tat etat akṣayaṃ nityaṃ jagat muni-vara akhilam \| āvirbhāva-tirobhāva-janma-nāśa-ādi-vikalpavat \|\| ityādinā paraṃ brahma svabhāvataḥ eva nirasta-nikhila-doṣa-gandhaṃ samasta-kalyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇamakaṃ jagat-utpatti-sthiti-saṃhāra-antaḥpraveśa-niyamana-ādi-līlaṃ pratipādya, kṛtsnasya cit-acit-vastunaḥ sarva-avasthā-avasthitasya pāramārthikam eva parasya brahma-śarīratayā rūpatvaṃ śarīra-śarīri-bhāva-atirikta-mukhya-aikya-vijñāne tu tajjñādatma-anādhikyaśyena vādhitatvāt, tadbhūmita-bhūtasya cit-vastunaḥ śarīraṇa-sthitim avicchinna-matreṇā kṣetrajña-śabdena sthitiṃ ca uktavā, kṣetrajña-avasthāyāṃ puṇya-pāpa-ātmaka-karma-rūpa-avidyā-veṣṭitatvena svābhāvika-jñāna-rūpa-dharmatumasthānam iti ūnyathā śāstrāṇi adumādhānaṃ ca pratipāditam iti, paraṃ brahma sacitraṃ, tad-vibhūtiṃś ca jagat api pāramārthikam eva iti jñāyate \|\| śveta-sīti-nīla-deśyaḥ ca deva-manuṣya-ādi-viṣyudhi-pariṇāma-viśeṣaiḥ śūnya-hṛdaya-stha-ātma-saṃnanta-rūpaṃ tadṛśa-abheda-rahitatvena tadṛśa-bheda-vādi-veda-ādi-śabda-agocaraṃ jñāna-satkaika-lakṣaṇaṃ sva-saṃvedyaṃ yoga-yug-manasā ataḥ na gocaraḥ iti ucyate iti, anena na prapañca-apalāpaḥ \| kathyate madhumayat iti ca idaḥ; tat ucyate—abhīmat prakaraṇe sāṅkhyaikaiḥ jaganmayā yoga-siddhāya yoga-abhyasāt pratyāhāra-upasaṃskṣepa uktvā, dhāraṇā-siddhi-arthaṃ śubha-āśrayaṃ vaktuṃ parasya brahmaṇaḥ viṣṇoḥ śakti-śabda-abhidheyaṃ rūpa-dvayaṃ mūrta-amūrta-vibhāgena pratipādya, tṛtīya-śakti-karma-ākhyā-avidyā-veṣṭitasya pracchinna-cit-ākhyaṃ kṣetrajñaṃ mūrtasya vibhāgaṃ bhāvanādyānanyathāśubha-cintyam uktvā, dvitīyasya karma-ākhyā-avidyā-virahitaḥ cir-vibhūtyuktasya jñānākā-

kāryastha-sūrti-saṃkhyā-vibhāgasya tiṣyad-vayogidheyatayā yogapadyam anapekṣatvāt svataḥ-siddhi-virahāt ca śubha-āśrayatve pratipiddhya, paraśakti-rūpa-mūrti-paricchitti-rūpaṃ kṣetrajña-samūrtī ca paraśakti-rūpayamanasaḥ kṣetrajñatva-āpādanetuṃ sanātanī yathā-vyāpta-karma-rūpa-avidyā caitanya-vyakti-traya-āśrayī bhagavat-sādhāraṇam ādi-varṇa-anyādi-vedānta-siddhaṃ mūrti-rūpaṃ śubha-āśrayaḥ iti uktam \| atra pariśuddhatara-svabhāvasya śubha-āśrayatā-arhātāṃ vaktuṃ pratyastamita-samastaṃ yat iti ucyate \| tathā hi—

na tatra agṛjyā śakyaṃ nṛpa cintayituṃ yataḥ \| dvitīyaṃ viṣṇu-saṃjñasya yogi-dhyeyaṃ paraṃ padam \|\| samasta-aśrakatve ca rūpaṃ yatra pratiṣṭhitāḥ \| tat vīṣaṇavaikhyaṃ rūpam anyat ararehitam \|\| iti ca vadati \| tathā cakṣur-mukha-sanaka-sanandana-ādīnāṃ jagad-aṇḍa-vartinām avidyā-vaidhuryeṇa śubha-āśrayatā-arhātām uktvā, kṣetrajñānām eva pakṣa-ayoge na adṛśtatva-ādīnāṃ svabhāva-pakṣāṇāṃ ca svataḥ-śuddhi-virahāt rūpavatāṃ jñānaikena śubha-āśrayatā niṣiddhā—ābrahma-stava-paryantā jagad-antaryavasthitāḥ \| prāṇinaḥ karma-janyita-sasaṃsāra-vaśādvinaḥ \|\| yatas tato na te dhyāne dhyāninām upakārakāḥ \| avidyā-antargatāḥ sarve te hi saṃsāra-gocarāḥ \|\| pradyuddha-statva-bodhāḥ ca dhyāte na eva upakārakāḥ \| naisargikaḥ na vai bodhaḥ teṣām anyatvayaḥ yataḥ \|\| tasmāt tat amalaṃ brahma nissādyā eva bodhavat \|\| ityādinā parasya brahmaṇaḥ viṣṇoḥ ekasya eva sva-asādhāraṇā eva śubha-āśrayaḥ iti uktam \| ataḥ atra na bheda-kalāpaḥ pratīyate \|\|

jñānatva-rūpa-nirūpaṇā api jñāna-athatiriktasya artha-jātasya kṛtsnasya na mithyātvaṃ pratipādyate, jñāna-svarūpa-paramātmanaḥ deva-manuṣya-ādi-artha-ākāreṇa ābhāsataḥ bhrānti-nirdeśena ābhāṣya-vacanāt \| na hi śuktikāyā rajatatayā avabhāsaḥ bhrāntiḥ iti ukte jagati kṛtsnaṃ rajata-jātaṃ mithyā bhavati \| jagad-brahmaṇoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyena aikya-śrutitaḥ, brahmaṇaḥ jñāna-svarūpasya artha-ākāratā bhrāntiḥ iti ukte sati ajñātarasya kṛtsnasya mithyātvam uktaṃ syāt iti cet; tat rasat; yasmin śāstre parasya brahmaṇaḥ viṣṇoḥ nirasta-aśeṣa-anādini khila-doṣa-gandhasya samasta-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ātmakasya mahā-vibhūteḥ pratipattayā tasya bhrāntitva-anasaṃbhavāt \| sāmānādhikaraṇyena aikyam pratipādaṃ ca bādhā-sahamiruddhaṃ caitanya-tanmātratva-upapāditam \| ataḥ ayam api ślokaḥ na artha-svarūpasya bādhakaḥ \|\|

tathā hi—yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante, yena jātāni jīvanti, yat prayanti abhisaṃviśanti, tat bhijijñāsasva, tat brahma iti jagaj-janma-ādi-kāraṇaṃ brahma iti avadhārite sati, itihāsa-purāṇābhyāṃ vedaṃ samupabṛṃhayet \| bibheti alpaśrutāt vedaḥ mām ayaṃ pratariṣyati \|\| iti śāstra-āgārhasya itihāsa-purāṇābhyām upabṛṃhaṇaṃ kāryam iti vijñāyate \| upabṛṃhaṇaṃ nāma viditi-asakala-vedana-arthānāṃ svayoga-mahima-sākṣātkṛta-veda-tatva-arthānāṃ vākyaiḥ svāvagāta-veda-vākya-arthaika-rūpaṇam \| sakala-śākhā-gatasya vākyasya arthasya alpa-bhāga-śravaṇāt udvigna-manena tena vinā nirṇaya-ayogāt upabṛṃhaṇaṃ hi kāryam eva \|\|

tatra pulastya-vasiṣṭha-vara-pradāna-labdha-para-devatā-pāramārthya-jñānavataḥ bhagavataḥ parāśarasya abhagavatve doṣaṃ dyotayan mitrīyaḥ paripapraccha—śroṣyasi me dharmaṃ śrotuṃ tvattaḥ yathā jagat \| babhūva bhūyaḥ ca yathā mahābhāga bhaviṣyati \| yat-mayaṃ ca jagat brahman yataḥ ca etat carācaram \| līnam āsīt yathā yatra layaṃ yāsyati yatra ca \|\| ityādinā \| atra brahma-svarūpa-viśeṣavati bhūta-sūkṣma-bheda-prakāra-tad-ādhāraṇa-svarūpavati viśeṣaḥ ca pṛṣṭaḥ \| brahma-svarūpa-viśeṣa-pratipādye utpatti ca etat carācaram iti nimitta-upādānayoḥ pṛṣṭatvāt dharma-nimittana sṛṣṭi-sthiti-laya-karma-bhūtaṃ jagat kim-ātmakam iti pṛṣṭam \| tasya ca uttaraṃ jagat ca sa iti \| idaṃ ca tādātmyam antaryāmi-kṛpena asya vyāpitṛtvam, na tu vyāpya-vyāpakayoḥ aikya-vyaṅga-kṛtam; yat-mayam iti praśnayoḥ uttara-dvaya-jagat ca sa iti sāmānādhikaraṇyam \| yat-mayam iti mayat na vikāra-arthaḥ, pṛthak praśnatvāt iti arthaḥ \| na api prācurya-arthitva-ārthikaḥ, jagat ca sa iti uttara-anupapatteḥ \| tadā hi viṣṇu-devyam itarat sarvam iti ucyeta \| ataḥ prācurya-arthaḥ eva \| śruta-prakṛta-vacane mayaṭ iti mayaṭ \| hareḥ eva jagataḥ śarīratayā tan-mayatvam eva \| tasmāt yat-mayatvasya prativacanaṃ jagat ca sa iti sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ jagat-brahmaṇoḥ śarīra-ātman-bhāva-nibandhanam iti niścīyate \|\|

anyathā nirviśeṣa-vastu-pratipādana-pare śāstreṣu api upākhyānamāne sarva-upetāni praśna-prativacanāni na saṅgacchante \| tadvidarāhaṃ harate ca śāstre na saṅgacchate \| tathā hi sati prapañca-bhramasya kim-adhiṣṭhānam iti eka-padasya praśnasya nirviśeṣa-jñāna-mātram iti eka-rūpeṇa ekaḥ eva uttare syāt \| jagat-brahmaṇoḥ eka-śabda-vyavahāre ca sāmānādhikaraṇye sati asattya-rūpatva-ādi-kalyaṇa-guṇa-gaṇa-vattva-ananta nilikhita-heya-pratyanīkatā ca bādhyeta \| sarva-upaniṣat-saṃvedyaṃ ca brahma bhedet \| ātma-śarīra-bhāve evaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ mukhya-vṛttim iti sthāpyate \| ataḥ—viṣṇoḥ sakāśāt udbhūtaṃ jagat tatra eva ca sthitam \| sthiti-saṃyama-kartā asau jagataḥ asya jagat ca saḥ \|\| iti saṅkṣepa-ukta-arthaḥ paraḥ purāṇa-niṣṭha-arthasya vistareṇa vaktuṃ parabrahma-bhūtaṃ bhagavantaṃ viṣṇuṃ stuvan sva-apekṣaṇa-avasthitaṃ adhikārāya iti ślokena prathamaṃ praṇamya tam eva hiraṇyagarbha-avasthatayā sthūla-rūpa-pitṛ-bhūmi-pradhāna-kāla-kṣetrajña-samasti-vyakti-rūpeṇa avasthitaṃ ca namaskaroti \| tatra jñāna-svarūpam iti ayaṃ ślokaḥ kṣetrajñasya brahma-ātmatayā avasthitasya paramātmanaḥ svarūpam āha \| tat-samātraṃ nirviśeṣa-vastu-patītiḥ \|\|

yadi nirviśeṣa-jñāna-rūpa-brahma-adhiṣṭhāna-bhrama-pratipādana-paraṃ śāstram; tarhi, nirguṇasya aprameyasya śuddhasya api amala-ātmanaḥ \| kathaṃ sarga-ādi-kartṛtvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ abhyupagamyate \|\| iti codayaṃ, śrutitaḥ tu eva bhāvānām anudhyānānām īśvaraḥ \| yathā iḍyaḥ brahmaṇaḥ tādṛk sarvadhā bhāvyatamayam \|\| iti parihāraḥ ca na ghaṭate \| tathā hi sati, nirguṇasya brahmaṇaḥ kathaṃ sarga-ādi-kartṛtvam ? na brahmaṇaḥ pāramārthika-sargaḥ; api tu bhrānti-

pariniṣṭhitavān—iti ca udhya-parihāro hi āsāt \| utpādya-adhikārya satyavidy-upayukta-paripūrṇa-karma-vaśyeṣu dṛṣṭam iti satyavid-guṇa-rahitasya paripūrṇayā karma-vaśyasya karma-saṃbandha-anarhasya kathaṃ jagādi-kartṛtvaṃ madhyugamyate iti ca udhyam \| dṛṣṭa-kṛta-visajātīyasya brahmaṇo yathodita-svabhāvasya eva jalādi-visajātīyasya ambuvat e rodasvādi-vidhiśri-yogavat tadvaśakti-yogo na virudhyate iti parihāraḥ \|\|

paramārthatvam eva ekaḥ; dṛṣṭasya api na kṛtsnasya apāramārthyaṃ vadati \| api tu kṛtsnasya tadātmaka tvāt taduccatirekeṇa avasthitasya apāramārthyam \| tad eva u pādyati—tad eva mahimā yena vyāptam etat carācaram iti \| yena tvayā idaṃ carācaraṃ vyāptam; atas tvattvātmakam eva idaṃ sarvam iti tvadanyaḥ ko’pi na asti \| atas svavilakṣatayā tvam eva ekaḥ paramārthaḥ \| ata idam ucyate—tad eva mahimā, yā saṃvegyā asi iti—iti \| anyathā tad eṣā śrāntiḥ iti vaktavyam \| jagataḥ pṛte tvaminayādīnāṃ padānāṃ lakṣaṇā ca syāt \| līlayā mahodbhūdharto bhagavān mahāvarāhasya stuti-viśeṣaṇa-virodhaḥ ca \|\|

yataḥ kṛtsnaṃ jagat jñānātmakaṃ tathā ajaḍam tathā vyāptena tava mūrtteḥ, tasmāt ca-dārmakatvāprabhavābhādayogavirahiṇī etatkevat eva manuṣyādi-rūpam iti śrānti-jñānena paśyanti iti āha yad eva diṣyate iti \| na kevalaṃ vastutas sadātmakaṃ jagad-deha-manusyādyātmakam iti darśanam eva śramaḥ, jñānākārātmanā ātmanaṃ deva-manushyādy-arthāntaravat darśanam api śramaḥ iti āha jñāna-svarūpam akhilam iti \|\|

ye punar vidyākhyāto jñāna-svarūpāvacidarśanaiḥ sarvasya bhāvatayā ātmakatvānuśravādyacodyogoyupa-pariśuddha-manaḥ ca te deva-manuṣyādi-vyākṛti-pariṇāma-viśeṣa-śarīra-rūpam idaṃ akhilaṃ jagat śarīra-atirikta-jñāna-svarūpātmakam svacchoṭaraṃ ca paśyanti iti āha ye tu jñānavidaḥ iti \| anyathā ślokānāṃ paunaruktyam, padānāṃ lakṣaṇa, artha-virodhaḥ, prakaraṇa-virodhaḥ śāstrātārthya-virodhaḥ ca \|\|

tathā ca amaradeveṣu satoprekṣyam darśyatra sarvaga-ātmasu jñāna-ekārakatayā mamato vā satsu deva-manuṣyādi-vyākṛti-pariṇāma-viśeṣa-rūpiṇus saṃsṛṣṭa-samastāsu devādy-ākāreṇa dṛte darśanam atathyam iti ucyate \| piṇḍa-gata-mātramātam api dṛtaṃ na pratiṣidhyate \| deva-manuṣyādi-vividha-vicitra-piṇḍeṣu vartamānaṃ sarvāntaram avastu samasta ityarthaḥ \| yathoktaṃ bhagavatā śuni ca eva śvapāke ca paṇḍitās samadarśinaḥ nirdoṣaṃ hi samaṃ brahma ityādiṣu \| tasmāt samaradeveṣu satopapati dehātiriktaṃ vastu iti svapara-vibhāgasya auṇḍavat \|\|

yad api uktaṃ paraḥ ko’pi ityatra api na asmābhiḥ pratīyate; yadi mataḥ paraḥ ko’pi anye iti evam ityarthaṃ parasādhāraṇa-śabdayoḥ prayogopāt \| tatra para-śabdat svārtha-niruktam avacanam \| anya-śabdas tasya api jñāna-ekārakatvāt anyākāratva-pratiṣedha-arthaḥ \| evam uktaṃ bhavati—yadi mad-vyatiriktaḥ ko’pi ātmā mad-ākāra-bhūta-jñānākārāt anya-ākāraḥ asti, tadā ahaṃmamakāraḥ ayam

vācā agocara-kara iti śakyate vyapadeṣṭum \| na ca evam iti; sarveṣāṃ jñāna-eka-ākāratvena samānatvād eva iti \|\|

heya-puruṣa-viśeṣeṇa dṛśyāv api ākāra-vaiṣamyam ātmanaṃ na svarūpa-kṛtam, api tu deva-ādi-piṇḍa-praveśa-karma-nibandhanam; na ātmīyam \| dṛśyante ca aṇikarṣa-pravarttanāṃ vāyv-ekṣaṇāṃ na svarūpa-vaiṣamyam; api tu ākāra-sāmyam eva \| teṣāṃ vāyu-deva-eka-ākārāṇāṃ randhra-bheda-nibandhana-kṛto hi ghaṭa-jala-sindhu-bhedaḥ \| evam ātmanāṃ deva-ādi-sindhu-bhedaḥ \| yathā taijasa-ātma-pāñcikatva-vaśāt sūtānāṃ padārthānāṃ tantvadvaya-vaineyam eva, na svarūpa-vaiṣamyam; tathā vāyavīyānāṃ sthānānām api svarūpa-bhedo vijñeyaḥ \|\|

saureṃ sa ca tvam iti sarveṣāṃ pūrvokti-jñānaika-kāra(re)ratvaṃ tac-chabdena parāmṛśya tat-samānādhikaraṇatāṃ lakṣaṇyādo nāmārthānāṃ jñānaikatā-kara iti uparaṃhṛta deva-ādi-ākāra-bhedānāṃ tu bhedam āha iti āha \| anyathā dehātirikto 'ham ātmopadeśya-svarūpe ahaṃ tvaṃ sarvetaratātma-svarūpam iti bheda-nirdeśo na ghaṭate \| ahaṃ-tvam-ādi-śabdānāṃ mukhyatveṇa sarvetaratātma-svarūpa-nirṇayena samānādhikaraṇyānupalakṣaṇatvād api na saṃgacchate \|\|

lo 'pi vyopadeśa-karoti iti āha tattvājñaṃ bhedaṃ paramārtha-dṛṣṭiḥ iti \| kutra ca evaṃ nirṇaya iti cet, dehātma-viveka-viṣayatayā upadeśasya \| tac ca piṇḍaḥ pṛthag yataḥ puṃsaḥ śiraḥ-pāṇi-ādi-lakṣaṇaḥ iti prakramāt \|\|

vinirjñātakaijñāne iti ca nāma-svarūpaikyam aparam; na api jīva-paryāyaḥ \| ātma-svarūpaikyam uktvā eva nirviśeṣam \| jīva-paryāyo 'pi svarūpaikyaṃ dehātmanaur iva na saṃbhavati \| tathā ca śrutiḥ: eko suprarṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte \| tayoḥ anyaḥ pippalaṃ svādv atti ananaśnan yaḥ abhicākaśīti śrutaṃ viśvanto muktasya loke guhāṃ praviṣṭau parame parārdhye \| chāyā-tapo brahma-vido vadanti pañcāgnayo ye ca triṇāciketāḥ \|\| ataḥ pratibuddha-śāstrātā janānāṃ sarveṣām, iti āha \| asminn api śāstre sa sarva-bhūta-prakṛti-vikārāś ca guṇa-ādi-doṣāś ca mune vyatītaḥ \| atīta-saṃcicaraṇo 'śivātmā tena āhutaṃ yad-bhavān-antarāle \|\| samasta-kalyāṇa-guṇātmakko 'sau paraḥ parāṇāṃ sakalā na yatra kleśādayaḥ santi parāvareśe avidyā karma-saṃjñā tṛtīyā śaktiḥ iṣyate \| yayā kṣetrajña-śaktiḥ sā veṣṭitā nṛpa sarvagā \|\| iti bheda-vyapadeśāt, ubhe api hi bhedena enam adhīyate bheda-vyapadeśāc ca anyaḥ; adhikaṃ tu bheda-nirdeśāt iti-ādi-sūtreṣu ca ayam ātmāni tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaro yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ ya ātmānam antaro yamayati ajñena ātmanā saṃpariṣvaktaḥ; ajñena ātmanā anvāruḍhaḥ ity-ādibhiś caitanyātmya-nīkārareṇa svarūpa-nirṇayāt \| na api sādhana-anuṣṭhānena nirmukta-avidyasya pareṇa svarūpaikya-saṃbhavaḥ;

avidyā-āśrayatva-upāyasya tad-avāhitatva-asambhavāt \| yathoktam— puram āsīt ātmane ’bhūḥ paramārtha iti īyate \| mithyā etat anyat dṛśyaṃ hi na eti tad-vastutāṃ yataḥ \|\| iti \| muktasya tu tadvatta-upaniṣede ’ti bhagavad-gītāsu uktam— idaṃ jñānam upāśritya mama sādharmyam āgatāḥ \| sarge ’pi na upajāyante pralaye na vyathanti ca \|\| iti \| iha api— ātma-bhāvaṃ nayati evaṃ tad-brahma dhyāyitaḥ mune \| vikāryam ātmanaḥ śaktyā loham ākarṣakaḥ yathā \|\| iti \| ātma-bhāva-ātmanaḥ svabhāvam \| na hi ākarṣakatva-hāpita-nirākṛṣyamāṇasya \| vakṣyati ca jāḍya-apāra-varjaṃ prakaraṇāt asannihitatvāt ca bhoga-mātra-samya-liṅgāt ca mukta-upasṛpya-vyapadeśāt ca iti \| vṛttir api jāḍya-apāra-varjaṃ samāno jyotiṣā iti \| dramiḍa-bhāṣyakāraḥ ca devatā-sāyujyāt varaṃśi api devatāvat-kathārtha-siddhiḥ tasya āti ity āha \| śrūyathā ya iha akṣaram anuvidya vrajanti eṣāṃ satyāḥ kāmāḥ teṣu sarveṣu lokeṣu kāma-cāraḥ bhavati, brahma-vit āpnoti param, saḥ ’śnute sarvān kāmān saha brahmaṇā vipaścitā, eṣam ānanda-mayam ātmānam upasaṅkramya, imān lokān kāmān ī kāma-rūpī anusaṃcaran, sa tatra paryeti, raso vai saḥ; rasaṃ hi eva ayaṃ labdhvā ānandī bhavati, yathā nadyaḥ syandamānāḥ samudre astaṃ gacchanti nāma-rūpe vihāya \| tathā vidvān nāma-rūpāt vimuktaḥ parāt-paraṃ puruṣam upaiti divyam, tadā vidvān puṇya-pāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyam upaiti iti ādyāḥ \|\| para-vidyāsu sarvāsu saguṇam eva brahma upāsyam, phalaṃ ca eka-rūpam eva, ataḥ vidyā-vikalpa iti sūtra-kāreṇa eva ānandādayaḥ pradhānasya vikalpaḥ ’viśiṣṭa-phalatvāt iti-ādi-sūtritam \| vākya-kāreṇa ca saguṇasya upāsyasya vidyā-vikalpa-śrutiḥ— yuktaṃ tad-guṇaka-upāsanāt iti \| bhāṣya-kṛtā vyākhyātaṃ ca yady api sa-cittataḥ iti-ādinā \| brahma veda brahma eva bhavati iti atra api nāma-rūpa-ādi-muktaḥ parāt-paraṃ puruṣam upaiti divyam nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyam upaiti paraṃ jyotiḥ upasampadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate iti-ādau abhisaṅkhyārthi prāptata-nāma-rūpasya vinirmuktasya nirañjanatva-śrutatvāt eva idasya jñāna-ekākāratayā brahma-prakāratā-ucchāde \| prakārasya ca tattvacchavahāro mukhya eva; yathā serṃ goḥ iti \|\| atha api vijñānaṃ prāpakaṃ prāpye pare brahmaṇi pārthiva \| prāpaṇī-rūpas tu eva ātmā prakṣīṇa-aśeṣa-bhāvanaḥ \|\| iti para-brahma-dhyāna-ante ārūḍhā param-brahmatva-prāptiḥ āśeṣa-bhāvanā— karmīyaḥ canā-brahma-bhāvānīṣṭa-bhāvanā iti bhāvanā-trayaḥ rahitaḥ prāpaṇīya iti abhidhāya, kṣetra-jñaḥ karaṇī jñānaṃ karaṇaṃ tasya vai dvija \| niṣpādya mukti-kāryaṃ hi kṛta-kṛtyaṃ nivartate \|\| iti karaṇasya para-brahma-dhyāna-rūpasya prakṣīṇa-aśeṣa-bhāvana-ātma-svarūpa-prāptyā kṛta-kṛtyatvena nivṛttiḥ ucyate na abhāvitva-anuyogyaṃ muktyā, niṣṭā-abhāvam āpanne kṣetra-jñeṣu paramātmanā \| bhavati abhedī bhedaḥ ca tasya ajñāna-kṛtaḥ bhavet \|\| iti muktasya svarūpam āha \| niṣṭhāvān brahmaṇaḥ bhāvaḥ svabhāvaḥ \| na tu svarūpaikyum, tad-rūpa-bhāvamātra

iti dvitīya-bhāve śabda-ananyavād, pūrvokta-artha-virodhāc ca \| yad brahmaṇaḥ prati-kṣaṇa-śobhamānatvaṃ tathā-pariṇata-nṛtta-prabhā-bhāvitam \| pradeśa-mātra-hasta-vākyau paramātmanā abhede bhavati bheda-rahito bhavati \| jñāna-eka-ākāratayā paramātmana eka-prakāratayā asya tasmāj jño devādiḥ \| tad-avyoge asya karma-rūpa-jñāna-mūlaṃ; na svarūpa-kṛtam \| sa tu devādi-bhedaḥ para-brahma-dhyānena mūla-bhūta-ajñāna-rūpaṃ karmaṇi vinaṣṭe hetv-abhāvād vibhavītam iti abhedo bhavati \| yathoktam eka-rūpam etad bāhya-karma-dṛṣṭi-rājaḥ \| devādi-bhedopacārante (na asya upacāraṇe) na asya upacāraṇe hi saḥ \|\| iti \| etad eva vivṛṇoti vibheda-janakaijñāne nāśam atyantikaṃ gate \| ātmano brahmaṇo bhedam asantaṃ kaḥ kariṣyati iti \| vibhedaḥ

vidyā-bhedaḥ—deva-tiryaṅ-manuṣya-ādi-avāra-ātmakaḥ \| yathārkaṃ śonakena api catur-vidho 'pi bhedo 'yaṃ mithyā-jñāna-nibandhanaḥ iti \| ātmani jñāna-rūpe devādi-rūpa-vividya-bheda-hetu-bhūta-karma-ātyāghāte para-brahma-dhyānena tasya atyantika-nāśaṃ gate sati hetv-abhāvāt santaṃ paramātvaṃ brahmaṇa ātmano devādi-rūpa-bhedaṃ kaḥ kariṣyati iti arthaḥ \| avidyā karma-saṃjñā anyā iti hi atra eva uktam \|\|

kṣetrajñaṃ ca api māṃ viddhi iti avināśana-dharmi rūpeṇa sarvasya ātmatvam eva abhidhānam \| anyathā kṣaraḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni kūṭa-stho 'kṣara ucyate, uttamaḥ puruṣas tu anya iti-ādibhiḥ virodhaḥ \| ananyābhisthleṇa sarvātmasālatvaṃ tad eva bhagavatā abhihitam— īśvaraḥ sarva-bhūtānāṃ hṛd-deśe 'rjuna tiṣṭhati iti, sarvasya ca ahaṃ hṛdi sanniviṣṭaḥ iti ca \| aham ātmā guḍākeśa sarva-bhūta-āśaya-sthitaḥ iti ca tad eva ucyate \| bhūta-śabdo hi ātma-paryanta-deha-vacanaḥ \| tatra sarvabhamayātmā tatra eva sarva-bandhaṃ tac-charīratayā pṛthag-avasthānaṃ pratiniṣedhyate na tadarhita vinā pratyagāditi \| bhāratīhasya upasaṃhārasya arthyam iti sarvaṃ ca api anantam ayam \| tata idam ucyate—yadā hi bhūtimat sattvaṃ śrīmad ūrjitam eva vā \| tat tad eva avagaccha tvaṃ mama tejo-'ṃśa-sambhavam \|\| viṣṭabhya aham idaṃ kṛtsnam ekāṃśena sthito jagat \|\| iti \|\| ataś ca astreṣv a nirviśeṣa-vastu-stuti-pādanam asti \| na api arthejñātasya jñānatva-vyatipādanam \| na api cid-viśiṣṭānāṃ svarūpa-ekatva-nirdeśaḥ \|\|

**atha sarva-vidyā-anupapattiḥ**

prati ucyate—nirviśeṣe svayaṃ-prakāśe vastuni doṣa-parikalpita-mithyā-viśeṣa-tathā-yathā-tattva-vikalanaṃ sarvaṃ jagat \| doṣaś ca bhavatirodhāvidyārtha-viṣaya-vidyā-ekarī śabda-brahma-nirvacanīyā anādi-avidyā \| sā ca avaśyam abhyupagamanīyā; anṛtena hi pratyūḍhāḥ iti-ādibhiḥ śrutibhiḥ, brahmaṇas tacchvasthādi-vākya-samānādhikaraṇya-avagatā jīve anādi-avidyā-anupapattyā ca \| sā tu na satī, śrātivādyopayogāt \| na api asatī, khyāti-vādyoḥ āśraya-yogāt \| ataḥ koṭi-dvaya-vinirmuktā eva avidyā iti tattva-vidaḥ— iti \| tad ayuktam; sā hi kim āśritya bhramaṃ janayati ? na tāvaj jīvam āśritya; avidyā-parikalpitatvāj jīva-bhāvasya \| na api brahmāśritya; tasya svayaṃ-prakāśa-jñānaika-svarūpatvena avidyā-virodhitvāt \| sā hi

**prathama-adhyāye—prathama-pāde—jijñāsā-adhikaraṇe mahā-siddhānte sarva-vidyā-anuguṇya-caḥ \|**

**jñāna-bādhyatva-bhinnatā \|**

jñānam ekaṃ paraṃ brahma tat-vicāryaṃ mṛṇ-mayam \| ajñānaṃ ca iti raskur-jñāḥ pramukha-sthitanta \|\| jñānaṃ brahma iti cet jñāna-mahātmasya nivartakam \| brahma-tat-prakāśatvāt tat ca api hi anivartakam \|\| jñānaṃ brahma iti vijñānam arhitaṃ caitanya-ātmam eva tā \| brahmaṇaḥ ajñāna-anumītitvaṃ sattva-dṛśyā iva prasajyate \|\| jñāna-svarūpaṃ brahma iti jñāne tasya avidyāyāḥ bādhakam, na svarūpa-bhūtaṃ jñānam iti cet, ubhayoḥ api brahma-svarūpa-prakāśatve satyatvāt tasya avidyā-virodhitvam ubhayatarasya na iti viśeṣa-anavagamāt \|\|

etat uktaṃ bhavati—jñāna-svarūpaṃ brahma iti anena jñānena brahmāṇi yat svabhāve avasīyate; sa brahmaṇaḥ svarūpa-prakāśatvena svayam eva prakāśatā iti avidyā-virodhitvena na kaścit viśeṣaḥ svarūpa-viṣaya-jñānayoḥ—iti \| kiṃ ca anubhava-rūpasya brahmaṇaḥ anubhava-antara-anubhāvyatvena bhavatāṃ na tat-viṣayaṃ jñānam asti \| ataḥ jñānam ajñāna-virodhi caitanyam eva virodhi bhavati iti na asya brahma-āśrayatva-saṃbhavaḥ \| śukty-ādi-arthaḥ tu sva-yāthātmya-prakāśaḥ sva-vismarṣa-stha-jñāna-virodhitaḥ tat-nivartane ca jñāna-antaram apekṣate \| brahma tu sva-anubhavi-siddha-sva-yāthātmyam iti sva-jñāna-virodhi eva \| tena eva nivartaka-antaraṃ ca na apekṣate \|\|

atha ucyeta—brahmaṇaḥ atiriktasya mithyātva-jñānam ajñāna-virodhi—iti \| na; idaṃ brahma-vyatirikta-mithyātva-jñānaṃ kiṃ brahma-yāthātmya-jñāna-virodhi ? uta prapañca-satyatva-rūpa-ajñāna-virodhi iti vivecanīyam \| na tāvat brahma-yāthātmya-jñāna-virodhi, pratibandhya-bhāvāt \| jñāna-ajñānayoḥ eka-viṣayatvena hi virodhaḥ \| prapañca-mithyātva-jñānaṃ tar satyatva-saṃsthānena virudhyate \| tena prapañca-satyatva-rūpa-ajñānam eva bādhitam iti brahma-svarūpa-ajñānaṃ tiṣṭhati eva \| brahma-svarūpa-jñānaṃ nāma, tasya sa-dvitīyatvam eva; tat tu tat-vyatiriktasya mithyātva-jñānena nihataṃ; svarūpaṃ tu sva-anubhava-siddham iti cet; brahmaṇaḥ advitīyatvaṃ svarūpaṃ sva-anubhava-siddham iti tat-virodhi sa-dvitīyatva-rūpa-ajñānaṃ tat-bādhyaṃ na syātām \| advitīyatvaṃ dharma iti cet; anubhava-rūpasya brahmaṇaḥ anubhāva-dharma-virahasya bhavatā eva pratipāditatvāt \| ataḥ jñāna-svarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ virodhi eva na ajñānam asti anu \|\| iti āśraya-anupapattiḥ \|

kiñca, avidyayā, prakāśa-antara-sahaṃ brahma tirohitam iti vadata svarūpa-nāśaḥ eva uktarasya āt \| prakāśa-tirodhānaṃ nāma prakāśaḥ ayaṃ iti pratibandho vidyamānasya vināśo vā \| prakāśa-sva-anuguṇya-da-avasthāpānena prakāśa-tirodhānaṃ prakāśa-nāśaḥ eva \|\| iti tirodhāna-anupapattiḥ \|

api ca, nirviṣayā nirāśrayā sva-prakāśya-anubhūti-sattvāśraya-doṣa-bādānata-na-āśraya-ananta-viṣayān tān manubhavati iti atra kim ayaṃ sva-āśraya-doṣaḥ para-sa-āśrūtaḥ ? uta apara-sa-artha-bhūta iti vivecanīyam \| na tāvat para-sāḥ; anabhyupagamāt \| na api apara-sāḥ; tathā sati hi dṛṣṭvena vā dṛśyatvena vā dūṣitena vā dūṣitena bādhyam upagamanīyaḥ \| na tāvat dṛśiḥ; dṛśi-svarūpa-bheda-anabhyupagamāt;

asmad-vidhāna-bhūta-arthaḥ tu sākṣāt dṛṣṭaḥ nirdeṣṭum aśakya-prakṛṣṭa-naya-paramādhyaniṣṭha-upāsanāt ca \| dṛḍha-dṛṣṭa-yogitva-darśanāya hareḥ eva kārya-nikareṇa sūkṣma-doṣa-antara-apekṣaṇāvasthā syāt \| atha etat-parijihīrṣayā paramārtha-satyam eva brahma-svarūpaṃ doṣaḥ iti cet; brahmaṇaḥ ca bhedaḥ, prapañca-darśanasya etat-mūlaṃ syāt; kiṃ prapañca-mithyātva-antara-parikalpanena? brahmaṇaḥ doṣaḥ eva bhavati tasya nitya-cetanā-nirmokṣaḥ ca syāt \| ataḥ yāvat brahma-svarūpa-tiraskāra-mārthika-doṣānugupamaḥ; na tāvat ajñāna-rūpa-apāditaḥ bhavati \|\| iti rūḍha-upapattiḥ \|

anirvacanīyatvaṃ ca kim abhisretasya? sat-asadbhyāṃ lakṣaṇatvāt niriti cet; tathā-vidhasya vastunaḥ pramāṇa-pūrvakatvena anirvacanīyatvaṃ syāt \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—sarve hi vastu-jātāḥ pratīti-siddha-vyavasthāpyam \| sarvā ca pratītiḥ sad-ākārā \| sad-ākāratām astu pratīti-sat-asat-vilakṣaṇaṃ viṣayaḥ iti abhyupagamamāne sarvaṃ sarva-pratīti-viṣayatvaṃ syāt—iti \|\| iti anirvacanīyatva-anupapattiḥ \|

atha syāt—vastu-svarūpa-tirodhāna-kara-jñāna-antara-bādhya-rūpa-vidhāyakatva-abhīpādanena sat-asat-anirvacanīya-dharma-vidhānāt na viṣayatvaṃ ca vastu-yāthātmya-jñāna-nivartyaṃ jñāna-aprāmāṇya-adarśanena bhāva-rūpam eva kiṃcit vastu pratyakṣa-anumānābhyāṃ pratīyate \| tat u hi iti brahma-yoga-āsanāśrāditare svaprakāśa-cit-nāśa-vapuṣi tena eva tirohitatva-rūpe pratyagātma-saṃghāta-stha-rajju-jñāna-jñeya-vibhāga-rūpa-adhiṣṭhātāḥ \| tasya athā vastu-ādi-viśeṣaṇa-adhyāsa-rūpe jagatāṃ jñāna-bādhyatve api ajñāna-ādi-vastu laṅghana-rūpa-adhyāsaḥ api jāyate \| dṛṣṭatvaṃ mithyā-śabdasya tad-vācakatvaṃ ca mithyā-bhūtasya arthasya mithyā-bhūtam eva kāraṇaṃ bhavitum arhati iti hetu-balāt avagamyate \| kāraṇa-ajñāna-viṣayaṃ pratyakṣaṃ tāvat "aham ajñaḥ mām anyaṃ ca na jānāmi" iti aparokṣa-avabhāsaḥ \| arthaṃ tu na jñāna-aprāmāṇya-viṣayaḥ \| sa hi bādha-pramāṇa-gocaraḥ \| arthaṃ tu ahaṃ sukhī iti vat aparokṣaḥ \| abhāva-ākhyā pratyakṣatva-abhyupagama-doṣa-prasaṅgaḥ na ātma-jñāna-abhāva-viṣayaḥ; anubhava-ekādhānām api jñānasya vidyamānatvāt; adhyātmana-sthaṃ jñāna-abhāvasya eva upapattayeṣa \| evam uktaṃ bhavati—aham arthaḥ iti asmat anubhavaḥ caitra-mano-dharmatayā jñānasya ca pratiyogitayā avagataḥ iti vā, na vā? asti cet virodhāt eva na jñāna-abhāva-anubhava-saṃbhavaḥ \| tau cet avagatau pratiyogi-jñāna-tad-vyāpekṣaḥ jñāna-abhāva-anubhavaḥ turam na saṃbhavati \| jñāna-abhāvasya tu caitanya-vedye abhāva-adhya-pramāṇa-viṣayatve ca upapatti-saṃmānā \| anya-jñāna-tarayaḥ bhava-rūpatve dharmi-pratiyogi-jñāna-saṃskṛtasya api virodha-abhāvāt ayam anubhavaḥ bhāva-rūpa-ajñāna-viṣayaḥ eva abhyupagantavyaḥ—iti \| tat tu ca bhāva-rūpam ajñānaṃ vastu-yāthātmya-ābhāsatvena sākṣi-vedyatvena viśadyate \| mā evam; sākṣi-caitanyaṃ na vastu-yāthātmya-viṣayam, api tu ajñāna-viṣayam; anyathā mithyā-artha-bhāsa-anupapatteḥ \| na hi ajñāna-viṣayeṇa jñānena ajñānaṃ nivartate iti na virodhaḥ \|\|

nanu ca evam bhāva-rūpa-vastu-jñānaṃ viṣaya-viśeṣakayā vastunā eva sākṣi-caitanyasya viṣayaḥ bhavati \| sa viṣayaḥ prakāśamānatvena siddhiḥ iti kathyamāna, sākṣi-caitanya-yogyatvasya eva adhyasta-jñānaṃ viṣayaḥ-

kim ucyate? na evaṃ doṣaḥ; sarveṣu eva vastuṣu jñātatayā ajñātatayā vā sākṣi-vaitatyasya viṣaya-bhūtatvam \| tatra jaḍatvena jñātatayā siddhatve eva pramāṇa-vyavahāra-apekṣā \| ajaḍasya tu pratyag-vastunaḥ svayaṃ siddhatvāt na pramāṇa-vyavahāra-apekṣā iti saṃvedanasya vyāvartakatvena eva bhāgaḥ yujyate \| tasmāt jñātṛtvaṃ hitvena pratyakṣeṇa bhāva-dvayena eva ātmā pratīyate \|\|

tat idaṃ bhāsva-rūpam ajñānam anumānena api siddhyati—vivāda-adhyāsitaṃ pramā-pradhānaṃ svavyāghātaka-vyatirikta-svaviṣaya-āvaraṇa-svabhāva-kāryaikatva-avastvantara-pūrvakam, aprakāśita-artha-prakāśakatvāt, andhakāre prathama-utpanna-pradīpa-prabhāvat—iti \| āloka-abhāva-mātraṃ vā rūpa-darśana-abhāva-mātraṃ vā tamaḥ na ghaṭyate iti, tattve bhāsva-rūpa-jñāna-sādhye, nidarśanatva-upakrāmat iti cet; ucyate—bahutva-viratatyagdharthayoḥ yena rūpatayā ca upalabdhiḥ iti anantaram eva tamaḥ iti nirakṣyat—iti \|\|

atra ucyate—aham arthaḥ mānaṃ ca na jñānam iti atra upapatti-sahitaṃ kevalena ca pratyakṣeṇa na bhāsva-rūpam ajñānaṃ pratīyate \| yaiḥ tu jñāna-grāhya-abhāva-viṣayatve virodhaḥ uktaḥ; sa hi bhāsva-rūpa-jñāne api tulyaḥ \| viṣayatvenaśyadvaiva ca ajñānasya vyāvartakatayā pratyaya-arthaḥ pratipannaḥ vā, apratipannaḥ vā? pratipannaḥ cet; tatsaṃskṛpa-jñāna-viṣayatvaṃ tat ajñānaṃ tarhi pratiṣedhe katham iva tiṣṭhati? apratipannaḥ cet āvārakatvāyam avidyamāna-śūnyam ajñānaṃ katham anubūyeta? atha vivāda-svarūpa-avabhāsa-jñāna-virodhī; aviditaḥ ca āvarakaḥ tu pratīyate iti akṣayas viṣaya-jñāne sati api na ajñāta-tulya virodhaḥ iti \| hanta tarhi jñāna-rūpa-abhāve api vivāda-dvaya-svarūpa-viṣayaḥ; āśraya-pratiyogi-jñānaṃ tu anivārya-tatsvarūpa-viṣayam iti na kasyacit virodhaḥ anyatra abhineśāt \| bhāsva-rūpa-jñānasya api hi ajñāna-imiti-siddhataḥ prāk-abhāva-siddhau iva sāpekṣatvam asti eva \| tathā hi—ajñānam iti jñāna-abhāvavat anubhava-virodhī vā? prathayamānam api tatsvarūpa-jñāna-apekṣāvayaśrayaṇīya \| yat yapi tama-svarūpa-pratipatto prakāśa-apekṣā na vidyate; tathā api prakāśa-virodhī iti etat-ākāreṇa pratipatto prakāśa-pratiyogitva-apekṣā asti eva \| bhavad-abhimata-ajñāne na kathañcit asvarūpeṇa siddhyati; api tu ajñānam iti eva \| tathā sati jñāna-abhāvavat tad-apekṣatvaṃ samānam \| jñāna-grāhya-abhāvatu bhavatā api abhyupagamyate, pratīyate ca iti abhyupetau jñāna-grāhya-abhāvaḥ eva ahaṅkāraḥ mānaṃ ca na jñānam iti anubhūtaḥ iti anubhūta-ātmakam \|\|

nitya-mukta-svaprakāśa-caitanya-eka-rūpasya brahmaṇaḥ ajñāna-anubhavaḥ ca na saṃbhavati; sva-anubhava-svarūpatvāt \| sva-anubhava-svarūpam api tirohita-svarūpam ajñānam anubhavati iti cet; kim idaṃ tirohita-svarūpatvam? aprakāśita-tatsvarūpatvam iti cet; sva-anubhava-svarūpasya katham aprakāśita-svarūpatvam? sva-anubhava-svarūpasya apuruṣataḥ aprakāśita-svarūpatva-mātraṃ ca iti cet; evaṃ tarhi prakāśa-ākhya-dharma-ānuguṇyena prakāśasya eva svarūpatvāt anyatatsvarūpatā eva svābhittiḥ pūrvavat doṣaḥ \|\|

kiñca brahma-jñapti-virodhād ahetu-bhūtam eva idaṃ satyaṃ svayam anubhūtaṃ sat brahma tiraskaroti iti, brahma-tiraskṛtya svayaṃ tattvaṃ bhaviṣyati iti aśvanīyasya upāya-śravaṇam \| anubhūtena eva tiraskaroti iti cet; pratyatirohita-tat-svarūpam eva brahma-jñānam anubhūyate; tathā virodhi-kalpanā niṣprayojanā \| syāt; ajñāna-svarūpa-kalpanā ca \| brahma-goḍā-jñāna-darśana-vat ajñāna-kāryatayā abhimata-prapañca-darśanasya api saṃbhavāt \| kiñca brahma-goḍā-jñāna-anubhavaḥ kiṃ svataḥ ajñātaḥ vā ? svataḥ ced ajñāna-anubhavasya svarūpa-prayukta-vedana-imiṣasthatvāt \| anubhūti-svarūpasya brahma-goḍā-jñāna-anubhava-svarūpatvena mithyā-rajata-bādhaka-jñānena rajata-anubhavasya iva api nirvṛcita-śuktikaṃ jñānena ajñāna-anubhūti-rūpa-brahma-svarūpa-niścitatvāt \| ajñātaś cet; kiṃ tat jñātu ? ajñāna-antaraṃ iti cet; anavasthā syāt \| brahma tiraskṛtaṃ svayam anubhaviṣayaḥ bhavati iti cet; tathā sati idṛśam ajñānaṃ kāca-ādi-dṛṣṭam iva asatyā brahma tiraskaroti iti jñāna-bādhyatvam ajñānasya na syāt \|\|

api idam ajñānaṃ svayam anādi, brahmaṇaḥ svara-sākṣitvaṃ brahma-svarūpa-tiraskṛtiṃ ca yugapad eva karoti \| ataḥ na anavadya-dvayo doṣa iti; na evam, sva-anubhava-svarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpa-tiraskṛtim antareṇa sākṣitva-ayogāt \| hetv-antareṇa tiraskṛtam iti cet; tadīya-anāditvam apāstam \| anavasthā ca pūrvoktā \| atiraskṛta-tat-svarūpasya eva sākṣitva-upapatteḥ brahmaṇaḥ tat-anubhava-ekatānatā ca na syāt \|\|

api ca avidyayā brahmaṇi tirohite tat brahma na kiñcid api prakāśate ? uta kiñcit prakāśate ? pūrvasmin kalpe prakāśamāna-svarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ aprakāraḥ duṣkalpāpita-smaskṛtatā \| uttarasmin kalpe sat-cid-ānanda-eka-rase brahmaṇi kaḥ ayam aṃśaḥ tiraskriyate ? kaḥ vā prakāśate ? niroṃ nirviśeṣe prakāśa-mātre vastuni ākāra-dvaya-asaṃbhavena tiraskāraḥ prakāśāṃśa-yugapad saṃghaṭate \| atha sat-cid-ānanda-eka-rasaṃ brahma avidyayā tirohita-svarūpam aviśadham iva lakṣyata iti; prakāśa-mātra-svarūpasya viśadatā-aviśadatā vā kiṅkṛtā ? etad uktaṃ bhavati—para-saṃśleṣa-viśeṣaḥ prakāśa-viṣayaḥ; tasya saṃkalā-avabhāsaḥ viśada-avabhāsaḥ; kaṭipaya-viśeṣa-rahita-avabhāsaḥ aviśada-avabhāsaḥ \| tatra ca ākāraḥ ajñāti-gocaritaḥ asmin naḥ prakāśa-mātrasya eva prakāśa-viśeṣyaṃ na vidyate \| yathārthaḥ pratibhāsaḥ tasmin naḥ tad-viṣaya-prakośaḥ viśadaḥ eva \| avastunaḥ ca prakāśaḥ api avaiśadyaṃ na saṃbhavati \| viṣaye api svarūpe pratīyamāne tat-atirikta-viṣaya-viśeṣa-apratītiḥ eva avaiśadyam \| tasmād viṣaye nirviśeṣe prakāśa-mātre brahmaṇi svarūpe prakāśamāne tat-atirikta-viṣaya-viśeṣa-apratīti-rūpa-avaiśadyaṃ nāma ajñāna-kāryaṃ na saṃbhavati \| api ca idam avidyā-karṇam avaiśadyaṃ tarka-saṃnoti yāvat citta-vṛtteḥ, na vā ? avicchinna-avabhāsāḥ \| nityaṃ ca vastu kiñcit iti vivecanīyam \| viṣaya-dṛṣṭa-svarūpam iti cet; tat-viśada-svarūpaṃ pramāṇitam, na vā ? asti, ced avidyā-kāryam avaiśadyaṃ tat-viśiṣṭaṃ na syātām \| so

cet nokṣasya kāryatayā anityatā syāt \| atha āhāra-ādi yathā asya adhikaraṇa-devatā-saṃbhavaḥ pūrva-uktaḥ \| api ca aparimāṇa-doṣa-mūlakam avidyā nirvidyāna-saṃbhavāt api duḥkha-pādaḥ; śrama-hetu-bhūta-doṣa-doṣābhyavabaddha-vidyānām aparamārthasya api śrama-upapattiḥ \| tatra sarva-śūnyatvam eva syāt \|\|

yat uktam anumatena api bhāva-rūpam ahānāṃ siddhye'tiḥ; tat ayuktam; anumāna-asaṃbhavāt \| na etat anumānam ? sat-saṃyuktam; duḥkhaṃ tu tataḥ; atra ādeśya-niyamatā-jñāna-antara-sādhena viruddhatvāt itiḥ \| tatra ajñāna-antara-sādhane hetau anekāntyam \| sādhane ca tad-deśa-sambandha-sākṣitvaṃ nivārayati \| tatraśra ajñāna-kalpanā niṣphalā syāt \| dṛṣṭāntaḥ ca sādhana-vikalaḥ; doṣa-prabhayā adhyakṣīrthakathākatva-mātrāt \| sarvatra jñānasya eva hi prakāśakatvam; sati api doṣe jātena vinā viṣaya-prakāśa-abhāvāt \| indriyāṇām api jñāna-utpatti-hetutvam eva; na prakāśakatvam \| pradīpa-prabhāyāḥ tu cakṣuḥ-indriyasya jñānam utpādayataḥ virodhi-tamo-bhinirasana-dvāreṇa upakārakatva-mātram eva \| prakāśaka-jñāna-upanno vyādhita-māṇa-cākṣuḥ-indriya-upakāraka-hetutvam eva doṣasya prakāśakatva-vyavahāraḥ \| mārga-viśrutiḥ utpadya-prakāśakatva-abhyupagamena doṣa-prabhā nirdiśitā; api tu jñānasya eva sva-viṣaya-āvaraṇa-nirasana-pūrvakaṃ prakāśakatvam iti uktam iti cet; na hi virodhi-nirasana-mātraṃ prakāśakatvam; api tu aparihoccaḥ; vyavahāra-yogyatā-āpādanam iti yāvat \| tatra jñānasya eva \| yathā-ukta-kāraṇa-āsanna-prakāritārtha-prakāśatvam aṅgīkurataḥ; tat indriyāṇām upakārakatvena prakāśita-artha-prakāśakatvam aṅgīkaraṇīyam \| tathā sati teṣāṃ sva-nirvartyaṃ tat-svatara-pūrvakaṃ vā ābhāsa-dṛṣṭoḥ vaikāntyam iti alam etena \|\|

**prati-prayogāḥ ca—**

vivāda-adhyāsita-smānaṃ na jñāna-mātra-brahma-āśrayam; ajñānatvāt; śuktikā-ajñānavat \| jñāna-āśrayaṃ hi tat \| vivāda-adhyāsita-smānaṃ na jñāna-mātra-brahma-āvaraṇam, ajñānatvāt, śuktikā-ajñānavat \| viṣaya-āvaraṇaṃ hi tat \| vivāda-adhyāsita-smānaṃ na jñāna-nivartyam; jñāna-viṣaya-anāvaraṇatvāt \| yat u jñāna-nivartyaṃ smānaṃ tat nu jñāni-viṣaya-āvaraṇam; yathā śuktikā-ajñānam \| brahma na ajñāna-āśrayam; jñātṛtva-virahāt; ghaṭa-ādivat \| brahma na ajñāna-āvaraṇam; jñāna-aviṣayatvāt \| yat jñāna-āvaraṇaṃ tat jñāna-viṣaya-bhūtam; yathā śuktikā-ādi \| brahma na jñāna-nivartyaḥ ajñānam; jñāna-viṣayatvāt \| yat u jñāna-nivartya-jñānam, tat jñāna-viṣaya-bhūtam; yathā śuktikā-ādi \| vivāda-adhyāsitaṃ pramāṇa-jñānaṃ sva-prāgabhāva-atirikta-jñāna-pūrvakaṃ na bhavati, pramāṇa-jñānatvāt, bhavad-abhimata-ajñāna-sādhana-pramāṇa-jñānavat \| jñānaṃ na vastutaḥ vināśakam; śakti-viyoge api adūṣaṇa-viraha iti jñānatvāt \| yat aduṣṭaḥ vināśakaṃ tat śakti-viyoge api aduṣṭaṃ jñāna-samānaṃ ca dṛśyam; yathā īśvara-yogi-prasthūti-jñānam, yathā ca mudgara-ādi \| bhāva-rūpam ahānaṃ na jñāna-vināśyam; bhā-rūpatvāt; ghaṭa-ādivat iti \| atha ucyeta—bādhaka-jñānena pūrva-jñāna-utpadyāṃ bhaya-ādīnām, vināśo

harṣate—iti \| na evaṃ; na hi jñānena teṣāṃ vināśaḥ; kṣaṇikatvena teṣāṃ svayam eva vināśāt; kāraṇa-nivṛttyā ca paścāt utpatteḥ \| kṣaṇikatvaṃ ca teṣāṃ jñāna-dvayīnika-artha-kṣaṇān eva ukta-upalambhe; adhyāsa-anupalabdheḥ ca ātyamadhye \| akṣaṇikatve ca bhaya-ādīnāṃ bhaya-ādi-hetu-bhūta-jñāna-santāna-aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣāṃ jñānānāṃ bhaya-ādi-upalabdhetve ’neka-kṣaṇa-upalabdha-prasaṅgaḥ ca \| svapna-antima-dvaya-vṛttitvena tad-anantara-pūrva-kṛti-smṛtiḥ yathā-viśeṣaṇa-upādānena pratyaya-anukūlatā ca avikṛtā \| ataḥ anumānena api na śābda-prajñāna-viśiṣṭaḥ \| śruti-virodha-ādi-sthūlya-jñāna-āsmāt siddheḥ anantaraṃ ca eva vakṣyate \|

mithyā-arthatvaṃ hi mithyā-bheda-upādānaṃ śaśi-viṣāṇa-ādi iti adhikaraṇa-nyāyena parihriyate \| ataḥ anirvacanīya-jñāna-viṣayā na kācit api pratītiḥ asti \| pratīti-śānti-vādhe ’pi na tathā smṛti-upanataḥ ayam \| pratīyamānā eva hi pratīti-śāntitva-artha-viṣayaḥ \| āhuḥ: pratītibhiḥ pratīyamānena ca atulya-kakṣyam āsāṃ viṣaya iti na yujyate kalpayitum \| śukti-ādiṣu rajata-dṛṣṭānte: pratīti-kāle ’pi tasya asti iti bādhena bādhyeṣṭha-anyathā-bhānatā-yogāt ca sat-asat-nirvacanīyam uktam eva idaṃ rajataṃ doṣa-vaśāt pratīyata iti kalpanīyam iti cet; tat-kalpanayā madhyastha-anyathā-bhāna-tyājanīya-padāt; anyathā khāta-ayukyam āpnoti vyātisṛṣṭi-niṣedha-kramatva-anupapatteḥ dṛṣṭatā-parityāga-kāraṇa-bhūta-sat-kalpanā-yogāt \| kalpyamānaṃ hi idam anirvacanīyam, na tāvat nirvacanīyam iti pratīyate; api tu paramārtha-rajatam iti eva \| anirvacanīyam iti eva pratītaṃ cet, śānti-vādhyāḥ pradhvastheti asaṃbhavaḥ \| ataḥ anyathā-anyathā-bhāna-viruddhe pratīti-viśruta-vādha-mahatām anupapatteḥ antara-nyāya-parihāryatvāt ca, śukti-ādiṣu eva rajata-adhākāreṇa aprabhāsata iti bhavataḥ api upagantavyam \|\|

iti ādi antaravādināṃ ca sukha-duḥkha-ādi gati-anyathā-bhāsādvaya āśrayaṇīyaḥ— asatyatā-vipakṣe sat-antaratnaḥ; ātmatva-anātmakatā-vipakṣe arthin-manaḥ; adhyāti-vipakṣe ’pi anya-viśeṣa-pratyaviśeṣaṇatvena jñānatvasya iṣṭatvena ca; viṣayānāṃ taṭatva-pakṣe ’pi vidhyānātatvena eva \|\|

kiṃ ca anirvacanīyam apūrvai-rajatam atra jātam iti vadata tasya janma-kāraṇaṃ vaktavyam \| na tāvat antaḥsītiḥ; tasyāḥ saṃvid-rūpatvena tad-udgatau prāk-avasthāna-ayogāt \| nirviṣayā jātā tad-uttarādhaṃ tad eva viṣayī-karoti iti mahatām ati-upahasam \| artha-indriya-ādi-jñāne doṣaḥ; tataḥ tasya puruṣa-śraddhatvena artha-tat-kārya-stha-upākartavya-yogāt \| na api indriyāṇi, teṣāṃ jñāna-kāraṇatvāt \| na api buddhāni indriyāṇi, teṣām api sva-kārya-bhūte jñāne eva hi viśeṣakatvam \| anādi-avidyā-jñāna-upādānatvaṃ tu pūrvam eva nirastam \|\|

kiṃ ca apūrvam anirvacanīya-arthi idaṃ vadan bhavān rajata-ādi-buddhi-śabdayoḥ katham iva viṣayī kriyate, na ghaṭa-ādi-buddhi-śabdayoḥ ca \| rajata-ādi-sādharmyāt iti cet; tarhi tat-sādhyam iti eva

pratīti-śabdaḥ khyātām \| rajata-ādi-jāti-viyogād iti cet; sā kiṃ paramārtha-bhūtā ? aparamārtha-bhūtā vā ? na tāvat paramārtha-bhūtā, tasyā aparamārtha-niyogāt \| na api aparamārtha-bhūtā, paramārtha-vidyā-yogāt, aparamārthe paramārtha-buddhi-śabdayoḥ niṣiddhatva-yogāc caitanyam apariṇata-akartṛ-nirasanena \|\|

athavā—yathārthaṃ sarva-vijñānam iti vaidikānāṃ matam \| śruti-smṛtibhyas tathā eva sarva-loka-pratītitaḥ \|\| baddhaḥ sthāmi iti saṃkalpa-pūrvair dṛḍha-grahaiḥ \| tasmāc cittena kena api śrutyā eva coditam \|\| citrakūṭakara-prakhyaiḥ hi pratyakṣeṇa upalabhyate \| pradyanne rohitaṃ rūpaṃ tejasas tad apārthivam \|\| śuklaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ pṛthivyāś ca evam eva triṣv apy āḥ \| śrutyā eva darśitā tasmād asvaṃ sarvatra saṃgatiḥ \|\| purāṇe ca eva mottaṃ devatve mṛṣṭvam ekame \|\| nānā-vīryāḥ pṛthag-bhūtās tattve saṃhatiṃ vinā \| na aśaknuvan prajāḥ sraṣṭum asamāgamya kṛtsnaśaḥ \|\| sametya anyonya-saṃyogaṃ paraspara-samāśrayāḥ \| mahat-ādyā viśeṣāntā hy aṇḍam utpādina tataḥ \|\| sūtra-kāraḥ api bhūtānāṃ trivṛttvaṃ tathā avadat \| ayātmakatvāt tu bhūyastvād iti, tatra abhidhāsyati \|\| somākhye ca purāṇe ’mṛṇaṃ śruti-coditam \| soma-avayava-saṃsthātvād dharitaṃ nyāya-vidbhir viduḥ \|\| dvīḍhībhāve ca nīvara-haritaṃ dvīḍhībhāvataḥ \| tad eva sahasraṃ tasya yat tu dṛśyate deśa-bhāk \|\| śukti-ādi-rūpya-tiraskāro bhūtair atra īdṛśaḥ \|\| rūpya-ādi-sadṛśa-aṃśasya śukti-ādiṣv apalabhyate \| śrāntasya yātra sadṛśaḥ pratītir api niścitāḥ \|\| kadācit cakṣur-ādeś tu doṣāḥ śukti-aṃśa-varjitāḥ \| rajata-aṃśo gṛhītaḥ adoto rajata-arthī pravartate \|\| doṣa-hāno tu śukti-aṃśo gṛhīta iti niścitet \| ato yathārtha-rūpya-ādi-jñānaṃ śuktir iti śuktiṃ ca bādhya-bādhaka-bhāvaḥ api bhūyastvena upapadyate \| śukti-bhūyas tv alpa-saṃkhya-sākalya-graha-rūpataḥ \|\| na ato mithyā-artha-saṃsarga-viṣayatva-nibandhana \| evaṃ sarveṣāṃ sarvatra vyavahāra-vyavasthitiḥ \|\|

svapne ca prāṇināṃ puṇya-pāpa-anuguṇaiḥ bhagavatā eva tat-puruṣa-mātra-anubhāvyāḥ tat-kāla-vartamānās tathā-bhūtā arthāḥ sṛjyante \| tathā hi śrutiḥ: svapna-viṣayā na tatra rathā na ratha-yogā na panthāno bhavanti, atha rathān ratha-yogān pathaḥ sṛjate, na tatra ānandā mudaḥ pramudo bhavanti, atha ānandān mudaḥ pramudaḥ sṛjate, na tatra veśāntāḥ puṣkariṇyaḥ sravantyo bhavanti, atha veśāntān puṣkariṇyaḥ sravantīḥ sṛjate, sa hi kartā iti \| yad api sakala-itara-puruṣa-anubhavyatayā tadānīṃ na bhavati \| tathā api tat-puruṣa-mātra-anubhavyatayā tathā-vidhān arthān īśvaraḥ sṛjati iti sa hi kartā \| tasya satya-saṃkalpasya āścarya-śakteḥ tathā eva vidhaṃ kartuṃ ca saṃbhavati ity arthaḥ \| na eṣu mukheṣu jāgarti kāmaṃ kāmaṃ puruṣo nirmimāṇaḥ \| tad eva śukraṃ tad brahma tad eva amṛtam ucyate \|\| tasmin lokāḥ śritāḥ sarve tad u na atyeti kaścana iti ca \| sūtra-kāraḥ api sandhye sṛṣṭiḥ āha hi nirmātāraṃ ca eke putra-ādayaḥ ca iti sūtra-dvayena svāpnikasya api jīvasya brahma-mātra-anukūlaṃ māyā-mātraṃ tu kārtsnyena anabhivyakta-svarūpatvāt iti-ādinā na jīvasya saṅkalpa-mātreṇa

ghaṭṭatvam upagate, jīvasya svābhāvika-satya-saṅkalpatvādeḥ hatasya saṃsāra-daśāyām anabhivyakta-svarūpatvāt, īśvarasya eva tat-sadṛśa-śarīrādhyakṣatayā āścaryabhūtā sṛṣṭhiḥ iyam; tasmin loka-viśrāntasya eva tu na asti iti kathyate paramārtham eva tatra ghaṭṭā iti abhyupate—iti pariharati \| avakāśa-dikṣu śayānasya svapna-dṛśaḥ sva-deheṣu eva deśāntara-gama-antarājya-abhimukha-śirasstheṣu vā yad vā puṣpa-māla-phala-bhūta-abhyānādeḥ saṃkalpatādeḥ tān-antara-sṛṣṭeḥ ca upapadyate \|\|

pīta-śaṅkhādeḥ tu nayana-viparīta-dravya-saṃśleṣā nāyana-raśma-prasāradibhiḥ saṃyujyate \| tatra pitta-āsaṅghe-pita-raśmi-bhūta-rayuga-adhikāmā na gṛhṇāti \| ataḥ suvarṇa-nirmita-saṅkhavat pīta-śaṅkhaḥ iti pratīyate \| pitrādyāḥ dūrāntar-pītimā ca ārti-saṃśaya-ālya-varyadharye gṛhyate \| piśāca-gṛhītena tu svatantra-nikāra-tatraya-aditāmāniyat sūkṣmam api gṛhyate \| tad-grahaṇa-janita-saṃskāraḥ svicchanāyamara-mimir-doṣaḥ syam api pradhyate \|\|

japā-kusuma-samīpa-varti-sphaṭika-maṇiḥ api tat-prabhā-abhibhūtatayā raktaḥ iti gṛhyate \| japā-kusuma-prabhā vistatā api svaccha-dravya-saṃyuktayā sphuṭataram upalabhyate iti upalabdhi-vyavasthā-vindam \|\|

marīcikā-jala-jñānataḥ api tejo-vṛścikāyoḥ api amunigo vidyamānatvāt vidyam-udakṣeṇa tejo-vṛścikyoḥ asaḥ cet deha-ādi-abhimānam unigo grahaṇa-ādi-āvedanam \|\|

alāta-cakrasya jñānasya dūtataragamanena sarva-deśa-saṃyugapad-antarāla-agrahaṇāt tathā pratītiḥ upapadyate \| cakra-bhrāntī-ta-abhyantara-tāla-agrahaṇa-pūrvaka-tad-deśa-saṃyukta-nabhastra-graṇam iva \| karivardha-tarāla-abhāvāt antarāla-jānam, karivat-bhedyavad-yagraṇam iti viśeṣaḥ \| ataḥ tad api yathārtham \|\|

darpaṇa-dibṣu nija-mukha-ādi-vṛttiḥ api yathārthā \| darpaṇa-di-vṛtti-hīnānacyaḥ hi nāyana-raśmayaḥ darpaṇa-nideśa-grahaṇa-pūrvakaṃ nija-mukha-ādi gṛhṇanti \| tatra api atīndriyāt antarāla-agrahaṇāt tathā pratītiḥ \|\|

diṅmohaḥ api digantara-sthāsyam diśā viṣayamālatvāvaṣṭhavyatena diṅmātra-ādi-vyuktaḥ digāla-rūpaḥ gṛhyate \| ataḥ digantara-pratītiḥ yathārtham eva \|\|

dvi-chanda-jñānam ādā api aṅgula-upacchada-karma-atimahadihimanmānya-tejorāśi-bhedena sāmagrī-bhedāt asama-bhedyam anyena parideśaṃ candra-grahaṇaṃ dvaitam bhavati \| na ekaṃ sāmagrī sva-deśa-aviśiṣṭaṃ candraṃ gṛhṇāti \| dvitīyā kimiśra-graṇaviśraṃ-samīpekṣa-grahaṇa-pūrvakaṃ candraṃ sva-deśa-vimuktaṃ gṛhṇāti \| ataḥ sāmagrī-bhedena guṇa-padeśavad-iśiṣṭa-candra-grahe grahaṇa-bhedena grāhya-ākāra-bhedāt deśa-dvaya-grahaṇa-abhāvāt ca dvau candrau iti bhavati pratīti-viśeṣaḥ \| deśāntarasya tadiśeṣatvaṃ deśāntarasya agṛhītatva-svadeśa-candrasya ca nirasta-rāhutvena bhavati \| tatra sāmagrī-dvayaṃ pāramārthikam \| tena deśa-dvaya-viśiṣṭa-candra-grahaṇa-dvayaṃ ca pāramārthikam \| grahaṇa-dvitvena candraikā eva grāhya-ākāratva-dvayaṃ ca pāramārthikam \|

tatra viśeṣaṇa-pada-viniṣṭhiya-candra-grahaṇa-ādi ekaḥ eva candraḥ grāhyaḥ iti grahaṇe pratyabhijñānatvat kevala-cakṣuṣ-samarthayā śirāvāñchāśuṣka-jñānaṃ tathā eṣā avatiṣṭhate \| hṛṣṭo’śuṣkoro’ksamāmbandhanamabhidīrva timira-ādiḥ doṣa-bhinnaṃ cākṣuṣaṃ tejas-sāmagrīdhyaṃ bhavati iti kāryakaram \| apagate tu doṣe svadeśa-viśiṣṭhasya candrasya eka-rūpyeṇa upalabdheḥ śabdaḥ iti bhavati pratyayaḥ \| doṣa-kṛtaṃ tu sāmagrī-dvaitvaṃ tatsaṃtaṃ grahaṇa-dvaitvaṃ tatsaṃtaṃ grāhya-ākāra-dvaitvaṃ ca iti niravadyam \| atat sarvaṃ vijñāna-jātaṃ yathārtham iti siddham \| vyākhyāntarāṇi dṛṣaṇāni taiḥ na eva prapīḍitāni iti na tatra yatnaḥ kriyate \|

athavā kim tena bahunā upapādana-prakāreṇa \| pratyakṣa-anumāna-āgama-ākhyaṃ pramāṇa-jātam āgama-madhyaṃ ca nirasta-nikhila-doṣa-gandham anadhika-atiśaya-asaṅkhyeya-kalyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇaṃ sarvajñaṃ satya-saṅkalpaṃ paraṃ brahma-guṇaucchitaṃ kiṃ na paśyati ? kiṃ na upaśyate ? bhagavatā hi pareṇa brahmaṇā kṣetrajñasya pāpa-anuguṇe tad-rūpam eva āyāśilaṃ jagat sṛjatā sukha-ḍuḥkha-upeksā-phalatvam avāgubhāvyāḥ padārthāḥ sarva-sādhāraṇa-anubhaviṣyāḥ, kecana tat-tad-uruṣa-mātra-anubhava-viṣayāḥ tantat-kālīna-avasthāna-saṃyātāḥ ca sṛjyamāyāḥ sahajyante \| tatra bādhya-bādhaka-bhāvaḥ samānubhaviṣayatvayā tadvihantayā ca upapadyate iti sarvaṃ samañjasam \|\| iti pramāṇa-anupapatti yathāsaṃsthātiḥ \|

yat tu anṛta-mṛta-darśa-nirvacanīyam ajñānaṃ śruti-siddham iti; tat asat; anṛtena hi pratyūḍhāḥ iti ādi-bandhutā-śabda-sthāni-vacanīya-anabhidhāyitvāt \| śvetaḥ artha-viṣayaḥ hi atra śabdaḥ \| śrotam iti karma-vaci, śrāntaṃ pivantau iti vacanāt \| śrāntaṃ—karma phala-abhisaṃdhi-rahitaṃ parama-puruṣa-ārādhana-veṣaṃ tad-artha-bhūtam \| atra tad-artha-niruktaṃ sāṃsārika-phalaṃ karma-kṛtaṃ brahma-adhikāri-rodhi; etaṃ brahma-lokaṃ na vindanti anṛtena hi pratyūḍhāḥ iti vacanāt ca \| nāśanāsamo sacchāchīti iti atra api sad-avacchedhaḥ cidvicid-vastu-viṣayaḥ \| utpatti-velāyāṃ sad-avacchedamihitaśyāviśraddhihacchiṣṭa-yoga-vastunotpathya-kāle acit-samṛṣṭi-bhedhe tamaḥ-śabda-abhidheye vastuni pralaya-sthiti-pādana-parattvāt asya vākyasya \| na atra kasyacit sad-asat-nirvacanīya ucyate; sad-asatoḥ kāla-virodhe asad-bhāva-mātra-vacanāt \| atra tamaḥ-śabda-abhihitasya acit-samṛddhiṃ śruti-antarād avamyate adhyaktamasare līyate, akṣare tamasi līyate iti \|\|

satyam, tamaḥ-śabdena acit-samṛṣṭi-rūpāyāḥ prakṛteḥ sūkṣma-avasthā ucyate \| yasyāḥ tu māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāt iti māyā-śabdena abhidhānāt anirvacanīya(vāci)tvam iti cet; na evam; māyā-śabdasya nirvacanīya-acitattve na hṛṣṭam iti \| māyā-śabdasya mithyā-arthatvena anirvacanīya-acit-sṛṣṭim iti cet; tad api na asti iha \| na hi sarvatra māyā-śabdaḥ mithyā-viṣayaḥ; āścarya-rakṣa-upādi-vastuṣu eva māyā-śabda-prayogāt \| yathoktam—śrītena māyā-sahasraṃ tat śambarasya śuṣṇam imita \| bālasya rakṣatā

deha-indriyebhyaḥ sūcitam \|\| iti \| ataḥ māyā-śabdaḥ vicitra-artha-kāraka-abhidhāyī \| prakṛteḥ ca māyā-śabda-abhidhānaṃ vicitra-artha-karatvāt eva \| asmān māyī sṛjate viśvam etasmiṃś ca anyaḥ māyayā sanniruddhaḥ iti māyā-śabda-vācyāyāḥ prakṛteḥ vicitra-artha-kārakatvaṃ darśayati \| parasu-prakṛtyasya ca tad-vattayā atra eṇa māyitvam ucyate; jñānatvena \| jīvasya eva hi māyayā nirodhaḥ śrūyate tasmin anyo māyayā sanniruddhaḥ iti, anādi-māyayā suptaḥ yadā jīvaḥ prabudhyate iti ca \| indraḥ māyābhiḥ puru-rūpaḥ īyate iti atra api vicitrā-śaktayaḥ abhidhīyante \| ataḥ eva hi bhūri tvac-chete rājati iti ucyate \| na hi mithyā-bhūtāḥ kacid dhriyante \| mama māyā duratyayā iti atra api guṇa-mayī iti vacanataḥ ca tri-guṇa-ātmikā prakṛtiḥ ucyata iti na śruti-nirasta-satva-vacana-upādāna-apratipādanam \| vācya-vācaka-padeśa-anuprapacyā \| na hi satyam asi iti jīva-para-eka-upadeśe sati sarva-jñe satya-sa-kūtsthe sakala-jagat-utpatti-sthiti-vināśa-hetu-bhūte tac-chabda-vācye prakṛte brahmaṇi viruddha-ajñāna-parikalpana-hetu-bhūtā kācid vacana-vyutpattiḥ dṛśyate \| aikya-upadeśaḥ tu sva-aṃśakena api jīvasya arīrakasya brahmaṇaḥ eva abhidhānāt upapannataraḥ \| anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi iti praveśya vastutaḥ paramātma-paryantatvaṃ hi nāma-rūpa-śabda-vastunaḥ \| ataḥ na brahma-ajñāna-parikalpanam \|\| iti pramāṇa-anupapattī avidyāyāḥ śruti-tātparya-apati-siddhi-vinirāsaḥ \| itihāsa-purāṇayoḥ api na brahma-ajñāna-vādaḥ kvacid api dṛśyate \| nanu jñānāt eva viṣṇuḥ iti brahma ekaṃ eva tattvam iti pratijñāya jñāna-svarūpaḥ bhagavān yataḥ asau iti ślokāḥ vidyā-viruddha-bheda-bhinnasya jagataḥ jñāna-eka-svarūpa-brahma-ātma-vijñu-mithyatvam abhidhāya yadā tu śuddhaṃ nija-svarūpam iti jñāna-eka-rūpasya eva brahmaṇaḥ svarūpa-rūpa-vasthiti-velāyāṃ vastu-bheda-abhāva-darśanena ajñāna-nivṛttimattvam eva sthirīkṛtya vastu asti kim adho ghaṭatvam iti śloka-dvayena jāgrat-pratilabhya-kāraṇā api vastu-bhedanām asatyatvam upapādya sat-mātra-vijñānam uta iti pratijñātaṃ brahma-vyatiriktasya asatyatvam upasaṃhṛtya vijñānam ekam iti jñāna-svarūpe brahmaṇi bheda-darśana-nimitta-ajñāna-mūlaṃ nija-karma vā iti sphuṭīkṛtya jñānaṃ viśuddham iti jñāna-svarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpaṃ viśodhya sattā ca eva bhavato mayā uktā iti jñāna-svarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ eva sattvaṃ na anyasya; anyasya ca asatyatvam eva; tasya mucyatas tattvaṃ vyavahārikam iti tattvaṃ tava upadiśyati hi upadeśaḥ dṛśyate \|\|

naiteyate \|

atra bhuvana-kośasya vistīrṇaṃ svarūpam uktvā, pūrvam uktaṃ rūpāntaraṃ saṃkṣepaḥ śrotum abhidhīyate \| citra-cihna-miśraṃ jagati dvitaye vāṅ-manasayoḥ caram saṃvedya-rūpa-bhede jñāne kāla-antaratā artha-dṛṣṭā vāṅ-mano-bhedaḥ avikāra-nityaḥ asti-śabda-vācyāḥ \| adīnāṃś tu dṛśya-karma-nimitta-pariṇāma-bhedaḥ hināśīti nāsti-śabda-viṣayaḥ \| ubhayaṃ tu para-brahma-bhūta-vāsudeva-śarīratayā tad-ātmakam iti etad-rūpaṃ saṃkṣepeṇa abhihitam \| yathā hi—

yat uktaṃ vaiṣṇavaḥ kāyaḥ tato viśva babhūva \| padmākārā madhusūtā pūrvāntadhyāgidiśyutā \|\| iti uktaḥ viṣṇoḥ īśvaratvena aṅgu-pariṇāma-bhūtaṃ brahmāṇḍam api viṣṇoḥ kāyaḥ, tasya ca viṣṇuḥ ātmā iti saṅkalpa-mūrti-gata-atadarśya-upadeśa-upabṛṃhaṇasya samānādhikaraṇyasya jyotiṣi viṣṇuḥ iti ārabhya brahma-mānasya śarīra-ātma-sambhāvaḥ eva nirvacanam iti āha \|\| api ca śāstraṃ pūrvaṃ vedya-vedakatvasya tāni sarvāṇi tad vapuḥ satyaṃ vai hareḥ tanuḥ saḥ eva sarva-bhūta-ātmā viśva-rūpaḥ yataḥ avyayaḥ iti \| tat idaṃ śarīra-ātma-sambandhena tādātmyam sāmānādhikaraṇyena vyapadiśyate jyotiḥ iti viṣṇuḥ iti \| avāsyātmakam nāstyātmakam ca jagat anantaṃ vastu viṣṇoḥ kāyatvāt viṣṇu-ātmakam iti uktam \| idam asyātmakam, idaṃ nāstyātmakam, anyaṃ ca nāstyātmakatve hetuḥ abhinyāha— jñāna-svarūpaḥ bhagavān yataḥ asau iti \| praveśaḥ ca asmat-nāḍī-sthitasya bhagavataḥ jñānam eva svābhāvikaṃ rūpam, na deva-manuṣya-ādi-vastu rūpam \| yataḥ evam, tataḥ eva avikṛta-pūrva-manuṣyālaghīya-śarīrasya tadvi jñāna-vijṛmbhitāḥ— tasya jñāna-eka-ākārasya sadā deva-ādi-ākāreṇa sva-ātma-sambandhi-anusaṃdhāna-mūlā deva-ādi-ākāra-anusaṃdhāna-mūlaka-mūrtāḥ iti arthaḥ \| yat tathāvidhaḥ tu kṣetrajña-karma-nigūḍa-pariṇāma-āspadaṃ, tat tat-svabhāva-hitā-śabda-abhidheyam, itarat hitā-śabda-abhidheya-nitya-cyutiṃ bhavati \| tat eva viṣṇoḥ iti śraddhā tu buddheḥ nija-rūpā iti \| yat etat jñāna-eka-ākāra-mātrasya tu deva-ādi-ākāreṇa sva-ātmani vaividhya-anusandhāna-mūla-sarva-karma-kṣayāt cintitaḥ api pariśuddhaṃ nija-rūpaṃ bhavati, tadā deva-ādi-ākāreṇa ahaṅkṛta-ātma-saṅkalpana-mūla-karma-amala-bhūta-asaṃdroṇāryā vastu-bhūta vastuṣu eva na bhavanti— ye deva-ādiṣu vastu-bhūta-ātma-sambandha-abhimāneṣu bhaumya-bhūtā deva-manuṣyālaghīya-śarīra-ādi-vastu-bhedāḥ; te tad-anubandha-bhūta-karmasu vinaṣṭeṣu na bhavanti iti avidhvastuḥ kāryācikāyasya aviśeṣa-yogitayā nāstitā-śabda-abhidheyatvam, itarasya sarvadā nijasi ddha-jñāna-eka-ākāratvena astitā-śabda-abhidheyatvam iti arthaḥ \| pratikṣaṇam anyathā-bhūtatayā kadācit cikāra-avasthā-yogine adhvaṃsatvena nāstitā-śabda-abhidheyatvam eva iti āha— vastu asti kim iti \| asti-śabda-abhidheye hi avisahya-paryanta-hīna-sattā-eka-rūpaḥ padārthaḥ, tasya kadācit api nāstitva-buddhi-anarhatvāt \| aviruddhaḥ tu kiñcit karhicit tathābhūtaṃ na dṛṣṭam \| tataḥ kim iti āha pañcātmakatvam iti \| yaḥ etu pratikṣaṇam anyathāvaṃ yāti laghutara-uttara-avasthā-prāptyā pūrva-pūrva-viśeṣaḥ jahāti iti tasya pūrva-avasthāyām itara-avasthāyāṃ na pratiṣṭhānam asti \| anantara-sthāyāṃ tasya nāsti-śabda-abhidheyatvam eva \| tathā hi upalabhyate iti āha mahī ghaṭatvam iti \| sva-karmaṇā deva-manuṣya-ādi-śrī-yogena sthitinā tām āśritya deva-mano-bhūta-ciddhaḥ tu pratikṣaṇam anyathā-bhūtam ālakṣyate— anubhūtaḥ iti arthaḥ \| evaṃ sati kim paryāyaiḥ vastu asti-śabda-arham ādi-madhya-paryanta-hīnaṃ sattva-eka-rūpam ālakṣitam asti kim? na hi asti iti abhiprāyaḥ \| yasmāt evam, tasmāt jñāna-svarūpa-ātma-sva-vyatiriktam aciddhastu kadācit vikārivat ca kevala-asti-śabda-vācyaṃ na bhavati iti āha tasmāt jñānamūte iti \|\|

ātmā tu sarvaiḥ jñāna-eka-ākāratayā devādi-viśeṣa-pratyanīka-svarūpaḥ api devādi-śarīra-praveśa-hetu-bhūta-jñātṛtva-vividha-karma-mūla-devādi-viśeṣātma-buddhimattvena tena rūpeṇa bahu-āsana-subhitaḥ iti tat-duḥkha-sukhāni na ātma-svarūpa-prayuktāni iti āha avidyānaikarṇi iti \| ātma-svarūpaṃ tu karma-gṛhītam, tataḥ eva mala-rūpa-prākṛta-saṃsṛṣṭi-hīnam; tataḥ ca tat-smayukta-soka-moha-lobha-ādi-agheya-guṇānādi, upacaya-apacaya-ananta-vaiprekṣyam, tataḥ eva sarva-karṣam; tat ca vāstudeva-śarīram iti tat-ātmakam, anantāmakathya kalyāṇa-svabhāvāt iti āha jñānaṃ viṣṇuḥ ityādi \| cit-aṃśa-sadeha-rūpatayā sarvadāharśi-śabda-vācyaḥ \| avidyāśasta kṣaṇa-pariṇāminitvena sarvadā nāśa-garbhaḥ iti sarvadā na asti iti abādhaviṣayaḥ \| evaṃkāvidyavidyātmakam jāgrat-sukha-veśarīraṃ tava ātmakam iti jagat-yāthātmyaṃ samyag uktam iti āha siddhā eva pūrvam iti \| atra satyam, asatyam iti, yat asti, yat na asti iti prakāra-traya-upasaṃhāraḥ \| etat jñāna-eka-ākāratayā sarvaṃ aśabdācigat-svarūpeṇa devādi-vinimagnaṃ bhavānīśitaṃ deva-mukhya-ādi-hitvena samyag vyavahāra-arhaṃtaṃ yat ete; tatra hetuḥ; karma-vairūpyam iti āha yat tu yat iti \| yat eva viṣṇugīti— yat yaḥ paśyir iti \| jagat-yāthātmya-jñāna-prayojanaṃ mokṣopāyatanarniṣṭhāṇāha acchedeta iti \|\|

atra nirviśeṣe pare brahmaṇi tat-āśraye sat-asarvānivacanīye ca ajñāne, jagat-saṃrakalpitatve vāḍmukhaṃ kiñcit api padaṃ na dṛśyate \| avitāritāsābdābhipreyaṃ cicaraṃcivātmakam kṛtsnaṃ jagat parasya pareśasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ viṣṇoḥ kāryatvena tad-ātmakam; jñāna-eka-ākāratayā ātmane devādi-vividha-ākāra-anubhavaidinapariṇāme ca hetuvarcasyātmānarjñāna-virodhi kṣetrajñānāṃ karma iti pratipādanāt; asitānāritasatyāṣṭaya-śabdānāṃ ca sat-asarvānivacanīyatva-vidhāna-sāmathryacinta \| na atyantasatyāśabdāvititasatyāśabda-virodhinaḥ \| asatyaḥ tādṛśam asatyaṃ hi pratīyate; na anirvacanīyatvam \| atra ca avidyotthūni na atyantasatyāśabdaḥ na tucchatva-mithyātva-paraḥ prayuktaḥ; api tu vināśitva-paraḥ \| ataḥ tu asti kim mithyā ghaṭatvam iti api vināśitvam eva hi apavāritam; na nirpramāṇatvaṃ, jñāta-bādhyatvaṃ vā; eka-jñānena ekasmin kāle anubhūtasya kāla-antare pariṇāma-viśeṣeṇa anyathā-upalabdhyā na astitva-upapādanāt \| tucchatvaṃ hi pramāṇa-saṃbandhanarhaṃ tvam \| bādhaḥ api yat-deśa-kāla-ādi-saṃbandhitayā yat asti iti upalabdhasya, tasya tat-deśa-kāla-ādi-saṃbandhitayā na asti iti upalabdhiḥ; na tu kāla-antare anubhūtasya kāla-antare pariṇāma-ādinā na asti iti upalabdhiḥ; kāla-bhedena virodha-abhāvāt \| ataḥ na mithyātvam \|\|

etat uktaṃ bhavati—jñāna-svarūpam ātma-vastu ādi-madhya-paryanta-hīnaṃ satata-eka-rūpam iti svataḥ eva sat asti iti āśabda-vācyam \| acetanāṃ tu kṣetrajña-bhoga-bhūtaṃ tat-karmanyugapariṇāmi vināśi iti sarvadā narastayaṃ na asti iti na atyantasatyāśabda-abhidheyam iti \|\| yathā uktam—

astu kāla-taraṇa-ādi na anya-saṃsṛtiḥ vai \| pariṇāma-ādi-sambhūtaṃ tat astu hṛt tava kim \|\| anāhataḥ paramārthaḥ ca prājñaiḥ abhyupagamyate \| taru nahi na saṃdeha-anādṛṣṭayā upapāditam \| iti \|\|

deśa-kāla-sama-viśeṣa-apekṣayā astitva-anāstitva-yogini vastuni kevala-asti-buddhi-bodhyatvam-paramārthaḥ iti uktam \| ātmanaḥ eva kevala-asti-buddhi-bodhyatvam iti sa paramārthaḥ iti uktam \| śrotuḥ ca maitreyyasya vijñātāram are kena vijānīyāt yathāsthānaṃ pradhānataḥ \|\| iti anumānāt ca, jyotīṃṣi viṣṇuḥ iti ādi-samānādhikaraṇyam aśarīra-bhāve eva nivadhyate; cit-acit-ekatayā avasthita-astitva-kṛtsna-yogini brahmaṇaḥ jñānasya karma-nimittatva-bhāva-avikalpatvena prādhānyam, acit-svarūpasya tat-karma-nimitta-pariṇāmitvena aprādhānyam iti pratīyate \|\| iti avidyāḥ smṛti-vṛ-purāṇa-siddhānta-nirāsāḥ \| iti pramāṇa-upapattiḥ \|

yat uktaṃ nirviśeṣa-brahma-vijñānāt eva avidyā-nivṛttiṃ vadanti śrutayaḥ iti \| tat astu, veda aham etaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntam, āditya-varṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt, tam eva viditvā amṛtaḥ iha bhavati, na anyaḥ panthā vidyate ayanāya sarve nimeṣāḥ jajñire vidyutaḥ puruṣāt adhi na tasya īśe kaścana tasya nāma mahat yaśaḥ ye evaṃ viduḥ amṛtāḥ te bhavanti iti ādi-aneka-vākya-virodhāt \| brahma-guṇa-saviśeṣatvāt eva sarveṣu api vākyeṣu saviśeṣa-jñānāt eva mokṣaṃ vadanti \| śodhaka-vākyāni api saviśeṣam eva brahma pratipādayanti iti uktam \| teṣu acit-ādi-vākyeṣu sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ na nirviśeṣa-vastu-aikyam, tat-tat-vastvyoḥ saviśeṣa-brahma-abhidhāyitvāt \| tatra hi sarvaṃ satyam, jñānam, amṛtaṃ, jagat-kāraṇaṃ brahma parama-mūrti tat akṣaraṃ bahu syāmi iti ādiṣu tat tu eva prakṛtatvāt \| tat-samānādhikaraṇaṃ rūpaṃ ca acit-viśiṣṭa-jīva-śarīrakaṃ brahma pratipādayati, prakāra-dvaya-viśiṣṭa-eka-vastu-paratvāt sāmānādhikaraṇyasya \| prakāra-dvaya-parityāge śrutibhiḥ nimitna-bheda-asaṃbhavena sāmānādhikaraṇyam eva pariraktaṃ syāt; dṛśyoḥ padayoḥ lakṣaṇā ca \| śodhyaṃ devadattaḥ iti atra api na lakṣaṇā, bhūta-vartamāna-kāla-sambandhi-tattvayoḥ avirodhāt \| deśa-bheda-virodhaḥ ca kāla-bhedena parihṛtaḥ \| tat akṣaraṃ bahu syāmi iti ukta-virodhaḥ ca \| eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñānaṃ ca na ghaṭate \| jñāna-svarūpasya nirasta-nikhila-doṣasya sarveśasya samasta-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ātmakasya jñānaṃ tat-kārya-ananta-apuṣkāra-artha-prapañcaṃ ca bhavati \|\|

bādhya-arthatve ca sāmānādhikaraṇyasya sva-icchayā ca ārohita-dharma-lakṣaṇā nirūḍhi-lakṣaṇā ca iti lakṣaṇā-dvayam eva doṣaḥ \| iyaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ—na iha rajanī iti vat virodhiṣu asmin bādhyamānasya parikalpanam; tat-pradhāna-adhiṣṭhāna-atirikta-dharma-nirūpaṇena bādhyam upapattimat \| adhiṣṭhānaṃ tu prāk-nirohitatam atirohita-svarūpaṃ tat-pradhāna-upasthāpyate iti ca itra, prāk-adhiṣṭhāna-abhāve tat-āśraya-bhrama-bādhyayoḥ asaṃbhavāt \| bhrama-āśrayam adhiṣṭhānam atirohitam iti cet; tat eva adhiṣṭhāna-svarūpaṃ bhrama-virodhi iti tat-aṃśaḥ mutaraḥ na tat-āśraya-bhrama-bādhaḥ \| ataḥ adhiṣṭhāna-atirikta-asamyag-dharma-tat-nirodhānām upagame

śārīri-bodhe duḥkha-padī \| adhikāre hi puruṣa-mātra-ākāre pratīyamāne tad-atiriktaṃ pāramārthike rājate tirohite sarvā eva vyavahāraḥ \| rājata-upadeśena ca tad-bhrānti-bhrāntiḥ; na adhiṣṭhāna-mātra-upekṣeṇa; tasya prakāśamānatvena na upadeśa-pravṛttiḥ; bhrānti-pramāṇitvāt ca \| jīva-īśvara-utkrama-jagat-kāraṇa-brahma-paratve mukhya-vṛttaṃ pada-dvayam \| prakāśya-viśiṣṭa-eka-vastu-pratipādanena sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca siddham \| nirasta-nikhila-doṣasya samasta-kalyāṇa-guṇātmakasya brahmaṇo jīva-antaryāmitvasya aikyaṃ paraṃ pratipāditaṃ bhavati \| upakrama-anukūlatā ca \| eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñā-upapattiḥ ca \| sūkṣma-cid-acid-viśiṣṭa-śarīrasya brahmaṇaḥ sthūla-cid-acid-viśiṣṭa-śarīratvena kāryatvāt san-mūlāḥ somya imāḥ prajāḥ sad-āyatanāḥ sat-pratiṣṭhāḥ iti; sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma taj-jalān iti śāntaḥ iti ca \|\| tathā śruti-antarāṇi ca brahmaṇaḥ tad-upādani-rūḍhasya cid-acid-vastutvaṃ śarīra-ātma-svabhāvena tat-ātmaṇyaṃ vadanti santaḥ prativiṣṭa-śāstā janānāṃ sarva-ātmā yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivyāḥ antaro yaṃ pṛthivī na veda yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yaḥ pṛthivīm antaro yamayati sa te ātmā antaryāmī amṛtaḥ yaḥ ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaro yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ yaḥ ātmānam antaro yamayati sa te ātmā antaryāmī amṛtaḥ yaḥ pṛthivīm antare sañcaran yasya pṛthivī śarīram, yaṃ pṛthivī na veda eṣa sarva-bhūta-antarātmā apahata-pāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ antaḥ praviṣṭaḥ śāstā janānāṃ sarva-ātmā tad eva anuprāviśat, tat anupraviśya, sat ca tyat ca abhavat, ityādīni \| atha api anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi iti brahma-ātmaka-jīva-anupraveśena eva sarveṣāṃ vastūnāṃ śabda-vācyatvaṃ ca pratipāditam \| tad-guṇa-sāramyam, sat ca tyat ca abhavat iti anena aikyam adhyajījīvaty api brahma-ātmakatvaṃ brahma-anupraveśāt eva vyapadiśyate \| ataḥ acit-cit-samastasya sarvasya vastu-jātasya brahma-tādātmyam ātma-śarīra-bhāvāt eva vyapadiśyate \| tasmād brahmaṇi avistīrṇasya kṛtsnasya tattvataḥ tena eva vastutvāt tasya pratipādakaḥ api śabdaḥ tat-paryantam eva sva-artham abhidhatte iti \| antara-svar-śabdānāṃ loka-vyutpatti-anugata-tat-tat-padārtha-viśiṣṭa-buddhi-abhidhāyitvaṃ siddham iti etad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvam iti pratijñāta-arthasya etad-ātmyam iti sāmānādhikaraṇyena viśeṣa upasaṃhāraḥ \|\| ataḥ nirviśeṣa-vastu-aikya-vādinaḥ bheda-abheda-vādinaḥ kevala-bheda-vādinaḥ ca vaiyadhikaraṇyena sāmānādhikaraṇyena ca brahma-ātma-svabhāva-upadeśa-asambhavaḥ parihṛtāḥ tuḥ \| ekasmin vastuni kṛtsna

tathātmyam upadiśyate ? tat na eva iti cet; tat saṃvākyena bādhāt iti na tādātmya-upadeśa-aśeṣam ahat kiñcit \| kiñcit abhedenirātmam iti cet; tat tu na sāmānādhikaraṇya-tādātmya-upadeśa-avaiyarthyam \| sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ tu brahmaṇi prakāratva-pratipādanena virodham abādhate \| bheda-abhedayos tu brahmaṇi eva upādhiḥ saṅgatiḥ yuktā jīvātmanā doṣā brahmaṇi eva prāduṣyuḥ iti nirasanāt nikhila-doṣa-kalyāṇa-guṇātmaka-brahmātmatva-upadeśā hi virodhāt eva parityaktārthyaḥ \| svābhāvika-bheda-abheda-vāde api brahmaṇaḥ tattve eva jīva-āśraya-mātrāt guṇa-doṣāḥ ca svābhāvikā bhaveyuḥ iti nirdeśa-brahma-tādātmya-upadeśo viruddha eva \| kevala-bheda-vidhā ca artha-abhiyogaḥ kenāpi prakāreṇa artha-asaṃbhavāt eva brahmātmatva-upadeśā na saṃbhavanti iti sarva-vedānta-vyapariṇāmaḥ iti āha \|\|

nikhila-doṣa-nirasta-siddha hṛtsthasya brahma-śarīra-svābhāva-siddhim anataḥ hṛtsthasya brahmātmatva-upadeśāt sarvaṃ satyam upapāditaṃ bhavati \| jāti-guṇayoḥ iva dravyāṇām api śarīratvena viśeṣaṇatvena ‘gauḥ aśvaḥ manuṣyaḥ devaḥ jātaḥ puruṣaḥ kṣatriyaḥ’ iti sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ loke vedānteṣu api dṛśyate \| jāti-guṇayoḥ api dravya-prakāratvam eva khaṇḍaḥ gauḥ śuklaḥ paṭaḥ iti sāmānādhikaraṇya-nibandhanam \| manuṣyatvādi-viśiṣṭha-piṇḍānāṃ manuṣyādīnāṃ prakāratvena eva padārthatvāt manuṣyaḥ puruṣaḥ caṇḍaḥ yauvītāmasā jātaḥ iti sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ sarva-anugataḥ iti prakāratvena eva sāmānādhikaraṇya-nibandhanam, na paraspara-vyāptā jāti-ādayaḥ \| sva-viśrāmaḥ eva hi dravyāṇāṃ kratavit kacit dravya-virodhānye matvarthaḥ api ayogyaḥ dṛḍhaḥ; daṇḍī kuṇḍalī iti; na pṛthak pratipattiḥ stheyyam aharṇi dravyāṇām; teṣāṃ viśeṣaṇatvaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyatvena avaśyam eva \|\|

yadi gauḥ aśvaḥ manuṣyaḥ devaḥ puruṣaḥ yogivit daṇḍaḥ ātmā karmabhijātiḥ iti atra khaṇḍaḥ muṇḍaḥ gauḥ; śuklaḥ paṭaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ paṭaḥ iti jāti-guṇavat ātma-prakāratvaṃ manuṣyādi-śarīrāṇām iṣyate; tarhi jātitvayoḥ iva api prakāra-prakāriṇoḥ śarīra-ātmanYoḥ api niyamena saṃsṛṣṭatvam asti syāt \| na ca evam dṛśyate; na hi niyamena go-rūpādivat ca ātma-āśrayatvāt ca ātmānā saha manuṣyādi-śarīraṃ paśyanti \| ataḥ manuṣyaḥ ātmā iti sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ lākṣaṇikam eva \| tat anena; manuṣyādi-śarīrāṇām ātmāṃśikatvāt athavā aṃśa-eka-prayojanatvasya tat-prakāratvaṃ ca jātyādivat tulyam \| ātmanaḥ śarīra-mātra-ātmaviśleṣe śarīrasya vināśākhya-gamyate \| ātmanaḥ prayojanatvaṃ ca tat-karma-phala-bhoga-arthaṃ tat eva sūtrāt chāt \| tat-prakāratvam api devaḥ manuṣyaḥ iti ātmaviśeṣaṇatā eva pratīteḥ \| etat ca hi gavādi-śabdānāṃ vyakti-paryantatve hetuḥ \| etat-saṃbhavāt ca eva daṇḍa-kuṇḍala-ādīnāṃ viśeṣaṇatve daṇḍī kuṇḍalī iti matvarthīya-pratyayaḥ \| deva-manuṣyādi-piṇḍānām ātmāṃśikatvāt eva eka-prayojanatvāt tat-prakāratva-vācya-bhāvāt ca devaḥ manuṣyaḥ ātmā iti loka-vedayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyena vyavahāraḥ \| jātyādivat ca aniyaman saha-pratītiḥ api yogyatā-apekṣatvāt \| śāsanantvāt ca mukhatvāt ca mukhyā śarīra-grahaṇe velāyām ātmā na gṛhyate \|

pṛthak śruti-apekṣasya prakāra-antara-kalpakatvaṃ durghaṭam iti mā vocaḥ; jāti-ādi-vat ante ’deha-āśrayatva-deha-prayojanatva-ādi-śeṣaṇatve ’śarīratva-vyāpti tat-prakāra-antaraka-sambhava-ātmakatvāt \| saha upalambha-niyamas tat eka-sāmagrī-vedyatva-nibandhana ity uktam \| yathā cakṣuṣā pṛthivī-ādi-indriya-sannikarṣa-siddhaṃ gandhitvaṃ svābhāvikam api na gṛhyate; evaṃ cakṣuṣā gṛhyamāṇaṃ śarīram ātma-prakāratā-eka-rūpa-svabhāvam api na tathā gṛhyate; ātma-svarūpe cakṣuḥ-asaṃyogī-bhāvāt \| na etāvatā śarīrasya tat-prakāratva-svabhāva-virodhaḥ \| tat-prakāratā-eka-svabhāvatvam eva sāmānādhikaraṇya-nibandhanam \| ātma-prakāratayā pratipadyate tasmād etu śabdais saha eva prakāratayā pratipādyati \|

nanu ca śabdair api vyavahāre śarīra-śabdena śarīra-mātraṃ gṛhyata iti na ātma-paryantatā śarīra-śabdasya \| na evam; ātma-prakāra-śarīrasya eva śarīrasya padārtha-viveka-darśanāt nirūpaṇa-niṣkarṣaka-śabdaiś ca evam; yathā gauḥ aśvaḥ śuklatva-bhākṛtiḥ guṇa ity-ādi-śabdāḥ \| śruto go-ādi-śabda-vedyā eva manuṣya-ādi-śabdā ātma-paryantāḥ \| evaṃ daiva-manuṣya-ādi-hiṇḍat-viśiṣṭhānāṃ jīvānāṃ paramātma-śarīratayā tat-prakāratvād bodhātmaka-vacinna-śabdāḥ paramātma-paryantāḥ \| ataḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ prakāratā eva cit-acit-vastunaḥ padārthatvam iti tat-sāmānādhikaraṇyena prayogaḥ \| ayam artho vedārtha-saṅgrahe samarthitaḥ \| idam eva śarīra-ātma-bhāva-lakṣaṇaṃ tādātmyam ātmā iti tu upagacchanti grāhayanti ca iti vakṣyati; ātmā eva tu gṛhṇīyād iti ca vākyakāraḥ \|\|

atra eva tattvam—acit-vastunaḥ cit-vastunaḥ parasya ca brahmaṇo bhogyatvena bhoktṛtvena ca īśitṛtvena ca svarūpa-vivekam āhuḥ kāścana śrutayaḥ— asmān māyī sṛjate viśvam etasmiṃś ca anyo māyayā saṃniruddhaḥ; māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāt māyinaṃ tu maheśvaram kṣaraṃ pradhānam amṛta-akṣaraṃ haraḥ kṣara-ātmānau īśate deva ekaḥ amṛta-akṣaraṃ hara iti bhoktā nirdiśyate, pradhānam ātmanaḥ bhogyatvena harati iti haraḥ \| sa kāraṇaṃ karaṇa-adhipa-adhipo na ca asya kaścit janitā na ca adhipaḥ pradhāna-kṣetrajña-patir guṇa-īśaḥ patiṃ viśvasya ātma-īśvaraṃ śāśvataṃ śivam acyutam jña-ajñau dvau ajau īśa-anīśau nityaḥ nityānāṃ cetanaḥ cetanānām ekaḥ bahūnāṃ yo vidadhāti kāmān bhoktā bhogyaṃ preritāraṃ ca matvā tayoḥ anyaḥ pippalaṃ svādu atti anaśnan anyaḥ abhicākaśīti pṛthag ātmānaṃ preritāraṃ ca matvā juṣṭaḥ tataḥ tena amṛtatvam eti ajām ekāṃ lohita-śukla-kṛṣṇāṃ bahvīṃ prajāṃ janayantīṃ sarūpām \| ajaḥ hi ekaḥ juṣamāṇaḥ anuśete jahāti enāṃ bhukta-bhogām ajaḥ anyaḥ \|\| samāne vṛkṣe puruṣaḥ nimagnaḥ anīśayā śocati muhyamānaḥ \| juṣṭaṃ yadā paśyati anyam īśam asya mahimānam iti vīta-śokaḥ \|\| ity-ādyāḥ \|\| stutāv api—
