akṣaraṃ iti iyaṃ me bhinnā prakṛtir aṣṭadhā \|\| aparā iyam itas tu anyāṃ prakṛtiṃ viddhi me parām \| jīva-bhūtāṃ mahābāho yayā idaṃ dhāryate jagat \|\| sarva-bhūtāni kaunteya prakṛtiṃ yānti

sādhikām \| kalpaśato punas tāni kalpādo viśṛjāmi aham \|\| prakṛtiṃ svām avaṣṭabhya visṛjāmi punaḥpunaḥ \| bhūta-grāmam imaṃ kṛtsnam avaśaṃ prakṛteḥ vaśāt \|\| mama adhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate sa-carācaram \| hetunā anena kaunteya jagat hi parivartate \|\| prakṛtiṃ puruṣaṃ ca eva viddhi anādī ubhau api \| mama yoniḥ mahat-brahma tasmin garbhaṃ dadhāmi aham \| saṃbhavaḥ sarva-bhūtānāṃ tataḥ bhavati bhārata \|\| iti \| jagad-yoni-bhūtaṃ mahad brahma madīyaṃ prakṛti-ākhyaṃ bhūta-sūkṣmam acit-vastu yat; tasmin acetana-ākhye garbhe yat cetayitṛ-jīva-śarīra-saṃśleṣa-ādi-rūpa-sthāvara-nānātmāvicinmiśrāṇāṃ sarva-bhūtānāṃ saṃbhavo bhavati iti arthaḥ \|\|

evaṃ bhoktṛ-bhogya-rūpeṇa avasthitayoḥ sarvāvasthā-avasthitayoḥ bhinna-cittoḥ parama-puruṣa-śarīratayā tacchidyatvena tad-apṛthak-siddhiṃ parama-puruṣasya ca ātmatvam āhuḥ kāścit śrutayaḥ— yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivyā antaro yaṃ pṛthivī na veda yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yaḥ pṛthivīm antaro yamayati iti ārabhya ya ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaro yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ ya ātmānam antaro yamayati sa ta ātmāntaryāmyamṛtaḥ iti \| tathā yaḥ pṛthivīm antare sañcaran yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yaṃ pṛthivī na veda iti ārabhya yaḥ akṣaram antare sañcaran yasya akṣaraṃ śarīraṃ yam akṣaraṃ na veda yaḥ mṛtyum antare sañcaran yasya mṛtyu-śarīraṃ yaṃ mṛtyuḥ na veda eṣa sarva-bhūta-antarātmā apahata-pāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ \| atra mṛtyu-śabdena tamaḥ-śabda-vācyaṃ sūkṣmāvastham acit-vastu abhidhīyate; avyām eva ucyate api avyaktam akṣare līyate, akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate iti vacanāt; antaḥ-praviṣṭa-śāstā janānāṃ sarva-ātmā iti ca \|\|

evaṃ sarvāvasthā-avasthita-cid-acid-īśvara-rūpīratayā tat-saṃhāraḥ parama-puruṣa eva kāryāvastha-kāraṇāvastha-jagad-rūpeṇa avasthita itīmarthaṃ jñāpayituṃ kāścit śrutayaḥ kāryāvasthaṃ kāraṇāvasthaṃ ca jagat sa eka eva āhaḥ— sa eva somya idam agra āsīd ekam eva advitīyaṃ tad aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tat tejo ’sṛjata iti ārabhya sanmūlāssomyenāssarvāḥ prajāssadāyatanāssatpratiṣṭhāḥ, aitad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvaṃ tat satyaṃ sa ātmā tat tvam asi śvetaketo iti \| tathā saḥ akāmayata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti, sa tapaḥ atapyata, sa tapaḥ taptvā, idaṃ sarvam asṛjata iti ārabhya satyaṃ ca anṛtaṃ ca satyam abhavat iti ādyāḥ \|\|

athā api śruty-anatarasiddha-bhinna-cittoḥ parama-puruṣasya ca svarūpa-viveka-smāritam— ṛtāhaṃbhimāristaro devatā anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi iti, tat sṛṣṭvā, tat eva anupraviśat, tat anupraviśya, sat ca tyat ca abhavat,... vijñānaṃ ca avijñānaṃ ca, satyaṃ ca anṛtaṃ ca satyam abhavat iti ca \| anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya iti jīvasya brahma-ātmakatvaṃ, tat anupraviśya, sat ca tyat ca abhavat...vijñānaṃ ca avijñānaṃ ca iti anena api acit-ātma-śarīra-bhāva-nibandhanam iti

vijñāyate \| evaṃ-bhūtasya nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇaṃ śrutatve brahma vyākṛtam āsīt, tasmāt nāma-rūpābhyāṃ vyākriyata iti apāṇuktam \| ataḥ kāryāvasthaḥ kāraṇāvasthaḥ ca sthūla-sūkṣma-cid-acid-vastu-śarīraḥ parama-puruṣaḥ eva iti kāraṇa-kāryayoḥ ananyatvena kāraṇa-vijñānena kāryasya jñātatvaṃ pratijñātena sarva-vijñānaṃ samīhitam upapadyataram \| agnim ārkṣitaḥ devatāḥ anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi iti, tisraḥ devatāḥ iti sarvam acid-vastu nirdiśya tatra svātmaka-jīva-anupraveśena nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇa-vacanāt, sarvaṃ vācaka-śabdāḥ acid-viśiṣṭa-jīva-viśiṣṭa-paramātmanaḥ eva vācakāḥ iti kāraṇāvastha-paramātma-vācinā śabdena kāryāvacina-śabdasya sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ mukhyatamam \|\|

ataḥ sthūla-sūkṣma-cid-acid-ākāraṃ brahma eva kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ ca iti brahma-upādānaṃ jagat \| sūkṣma-cid-acid-vastu-śarīrakaṃ brahma eva kāraṇam iti brahma-upādānatve api saṃghātastha-upādānatvena cid-acitoḥ brahmaṇaḥ ca svabhāva-sāṃkarya-utpatti-prasaṅgaḥ \| yathā śukla-kṛṣṇa-rakta-tantu-vṛnda-upādānatve api citra-paṭasya tantutva-praveśaḥ eva śauklya-śaitya-saṃbandhaḥ iti kāryāvasthāyām api na sarvatra varṇa-saṃkaraḥ; tathā cid-acid-īśvara-saṃghāta-upādānatve api jagataḥ kāryāvasthāyām api bhoktṛtva-bhogyatva-niyantṛtva doṣam asāṃkaryaḥ \| tantūnāṃ pṛthak-sthiti-yogyānām eva puruṣa-icchayā kadācit saṃhatānāṃ kāraṇatvaṃ kāryatvaṃ ca \| iha tu cid-acitoḥ tad-viśiṣṭasya ca parama-puruṣa-śarīratvena tat-svarūratvayaṃ padārtha-devatātmaka-māraḥ parama-puruṣasya eva sarve-śabda-vācyaḥ iti viśeṣaḥ \| svabhāva-bhedaḥ tastha-anupraveśaḥ ca tatra ca atra na tulyaḥ \| evaṃ ca sati parasya brahmaṇaḥ kārya-anupraveśaḥ api svarūpa-anyathābhāva-abhāvāt vikṛtatvam upapadyataram \| sthūlāvasthasya nāma-rūpa-vibhāga-vibhaktasya cid-acid-vastunaḥ ātmatayā avasthāna-saṅkhyekṣa-rūpapatra taram \| avasthāntara-āpattiḥ eva hi kāryatā \| nirguṇa-vādaḥ ca parasya brahmaṇaḥ heya-guṇa-saṃbandha-pratyupapadyate \| apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ vijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ iti heya-guṇa-gaṇa-pratikṣepya satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpaḥ iti kalyāṇa-guṇān vidadhatī iyaṃ śrutiḥ eva anyaḥ sāmānyena avagataṃ guṇa-nirvedaṃ heya-guṇa-viṣayaṃ vyavasthāpayati \| jñāna-svarūpaṃ brahma hi vāṅ-manasasa-rva-indriyasya sarveṣāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ heya-pratyanīka-kalyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇa-ākaratvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ svarūpaṃ jñānānika-rūpīyaṃ svayaṃ-prakāśatayā jñāna-svarūpaṃ ca iti adhyavasīya upapadyataram \| parasya brahmaṇaḥ sarvavit parā asya śaktiḥ vividhā eva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca vijñātāram are kena vijānīyāt ityādikāḥ jñātṛtvam avedayanti; satyaṃ jñānam ityādikāḥ ca jñānaika-nirūpaṇīyatayā svaprakāśatayā ca jñāna-svarūpatām \| saḥ akāmayata bahu syām tadā aikṣata bahu syām tat nāma-rūpābhyāṃ vyākriyata iti brahma eva sva-saṅkalpa-upādhi-citra-sthira-sthūla-rūpeṇa nānā-ākāraṃ avasthitam iti tat-svarūpanikā brahmātmakatva-nānātva-matatvamit tat-pratipādyate mṛtyoḥ saḥ mṛtyum āpnoti yaḥ iha nānā iva paśyati na iha nānā asti kiñcana yatra hi dvaitam iva bhavati tat itaraḥ itaraṃ paśyati, yatra tu asya sarvam ātmā eva abhūt tat kena kaṃ paśyet kena kaṃ vijānīyāt ityādiṣu \| na punaḥ bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti ādi-śruti-siddhaṃ sva-saṅkalpa-kṛtaṃ brahmaṇaḥ

nānā-nā-rūpa-bhāvatvena nānā-prakāratvam api nirdiśyate \| śrutvā draṣṭavyaḥ sarva-bhūtānām ātmā ityādi niṣedha-vākyataḥ ca tat sthāpitam sarvaṃ taṃ parādāt yaḥ anyatra ātmanaḥ sarvaṃ veda tasya ha vā etasya mahataḥ bhūtasya niḥśvasitam etat yad ṛgvedaḥ yajurvedaḥ ityādinā \|\|

evaṃ cit-acit-viśeṣaṇāṃ svabhāva-bhedaṃ svarūpa-bhedaṃ ca vadantīnāṃ kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaṃ kārya-kāraṇayoḥ ananyatvaṃ ca vadantīnāṃ sarvāsāṃ śrutīnām avirodhaḥ; cit-acitoḥ paramātmā ca sarvadā śarīra-ātma-bhāvaṃ śarīra-bhūtayoḥ kāraṇa-daśāyāṃ nāma-rūpa-vibhāga-anarha-sūkṣma-daśāpattiṃ kārya-daśāyāṃ ca tad-arha-sthūla-daśāpattiṃ vadantībhiḥ śrutibhiḥ eva jñāyata iti brahma-ajñāna-vādi-aupādhika-brahma-bheda-vāda-ādi-anyathā-anya-nyāya-mūlatayā eva sakala-śruti-virodhasya na kathaṃcit apakāśaḥ dṛśyate \| cit-acit-viśeṣaṇāṃ pṛthak-svabhāvatayā tat-tat-śruti-siddhānāṃ śarīra-ātma-bhāvena prakārakāritayā śrutibhiḥ eva pratipannānāṃ śruti-antareṇa kārya-kāraṇa-bhāva-pratipādane kārya-kāraṇayoḥ aikya-pratipādane ca hi avirodhaḥ \| yathā—āgni-iṣṭa-ādīnāṃ khaṇḍagānāṃ dravya-cit-arthaiḥ pṛthag-bhūtaiḥ samudāya-anuvāda-vākyena samudāya-dravyatva-mātrāt darśa-pūrṇamāsābhyām iti adhikāra-vākyaiḥ kāminaḥ kartavyatayā vidadhāti; tathā cit-acit-viśiṣṭa-avikṛta-brahma-pratipādanāt kṣaraṃ pradhānam amṛta-akṣaraṃ haraḥ kṣara-ātmānau īśate devaḥ ekaḥ patiṃ viśvasya ātma-īśvaram ātmā nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ ityādi-vākyaiḥ pṛthak pratipādya yasya pṛthivī śarīram yasya ātmā śarīram avyaktam akṣare līyate, akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate, tamaḥ pare deve ekībhavati divyaḥ devaḥ ekaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ ityādibhiḥ vākyaiḥ cit-acitoḥ sarva-avasthā-avasthitayoḥ paramātma-śarīratāṃ paramātmanaḥ tad-ātmatāṃ ca pratipādya śarīri-bhūta-paramātma-abhidhāyibhiḥ, nistadvrahma-ātma-śabdaiḥ kāraṇa-vākyaiḥ kārya-vākyaiḥ ca paramātmā ekaḥ eva iti pṛthak-pratipannaṃ vastu-trayaṃ sat eva somya idam agre āsīt etat-ātmyam idaṃ sarvam sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma ityādi-vākyaiḥ pratipādyate \| cit-acit-vastu-śarīriṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ paramātma-śabdena abhidhāne hi na asti virodhaḥ; yathā manuṣya-piṇḍa-śarīrakasya ātmaviśeṣasya ayam ātmā mukhaḥ ayaṃ kaṇṭhaḥ anābhidhānāt iti asmatistareṇa \|\| iti nirvartaka-anupapattiḥ \|

yat punar idam uktam brahma-asaṃkalpa-vijñānena eva avidyā-nivṛttiḥ uktā iti, tat ayuktam, bandhasya pāramārthikatvena jñāna-nivartyatva-abhāvāt \| puṇya-apuṇya-rūpa-karma-nimita-deva-ādi-śarīra-praveśa-tat-prayukta-sukha-duḥkha-anubhava-rūpasya bandhasya mithyātvaṃ kathaṃcit na śakyate vaktum? evaṃ tvad-artha-niti-nirukti-rūpa-bhāṣā-santāna-uttara-parśa-mukha-prasāda-labdhā iti pūrvam eva uktam \| bhavad-abhimatasya ayam ātmasya yathāvasthita-vastu-viparīta-viṣayasya mithyā-rūpatvena bandha-vicchedaiḥ eva phalaṃ bhavati, mithyā etat anyat dravyaṃ hi na eti tat-dravyatāṃ yataḥ iti śāstrāt; uttamaḥ puruṣaḥ tu anyaḥ pṛthag ātmānaṃ preritāraṃ ca matvā iti jīva-ātma-śiva-sana-atīreyasya tad-anya-bhūmiṇaḥ brahmaṇaḥ jñānaṃ parama-puruṣārtha-lakṣaṇa-mokṣa-

śābda-nirūpadeśāt ca \| api ca bhavat-abhimatasya api nivartaka-jñānasya mithyā-rūpa-dvaitasya nivartaka-antaram mṛgyam \| nivartaka-jñānividṃ sva-virodhi sarvam bheda-jātam nivartate iti aṇikhatārathyam eva naśyati iti cet; tat-svarūpa-darśa-nivartanānām kālpanikatvena vināśatkalpam ākalpa-kalpanā-avidyāyā nivartaka-jñāna-samvedyopam \| tad-vināśaḥ brahma-svarūpam eva iti cet; tathā sati nivartaka-jñānāt eva viśeṣe na syāt, tad-vināśaḥ hi hitam tad-upacaya-saṃbhavāt \|\|

api ca kim āśraya brahma-vyatirikta-dṛśyanniṣedha-vipakṣāsanārtha kaḥ ayam jñātā? adhyāsa-rūpaḥ iti cet; tasya niṣedhyatayā nivartaka-jñāna-karma-bhāvāt tarka īśānupapattiḥ \| brahma-svarūpam iti cet; brahmaṇaḥ nivartaka-jñānam prati jñātṛtvam kim svarūpam; uta adhyastam? adhyastam cet, ayam adhyāsaḥ tan-mūla-avidyā-antaram ca nivartaka-jñāna-viṣayatayā tiṣṭhet eva \| nivartaka-jñāna-antara-tad-upagate tasya api tri-rūpatayā jñātṛpekṣāvastā syāt \| brahma-svarūpam eva jñātṛtvedam iti cet pakṣaḥ parigṛhītaḥ syāt \| nivartaka-jñāna-brahmaṇoḥ aikyam jñātā ca brahma-prayatiriktatvena sva-nirvāhya-niñojitam iti vacanam ‘bhūtale vyatiriktam kṛtsnam devadattena chidyasva’ iti asyām chedana-kriyāyām asya chettuḥ asvācchedana-kriyāyāḥ ca cheda-anupraveśa-vacanavat upahāsyam \| adhyastaḥ jñātā sva-nāśa-hetu-bhūta-nivartaka-jñāne svayam kartā ca na bhavati, sva-nāśa-prāyajana-puruṣārthatvāt tasyāsyā brahma-svarūpatā-abhyupagame bheda-darśana-tan-mūla-avidyādīnām kalpyam eva na syāt \| ittham anena viśuddham udgīrṇam bhavatā \|\|

iti niścityam upapattiḥ \|

tasmāt anādi-karma-prabhāha-rūpa-ajñāna-mūlatvā buddhasya tad-viśeṣeṇayuktatva-lakṣaṇa-jñānāt eva \| tad-darśiṣṭrāhāraharadugdhiya mānaparasubhagurārādhanaiṣām cayā tasya buddhiḥ viśeṣa-saṃskṛtatayā abhicinta-karma-labhyā \| tatra kevala-karma-grāhyatvāsthira-kūlavat, atamiśrita phala-parama-puruṣa-ārādhanaiṣaṇām karmaṇām upāsanātmaka-jñāna-upalabdhiḥ dāreṇa brahma-upāsakasya pumartha-rūpān tasthirya-phalatvam ca karma-svarūpa-jñānāt iha na jñāyate; kevala-ākāra-vīryāgipūrvakathyotsava-rūpa-karmāvādātām ca na sambhavati iti karma-vicāra-anantaram tataḥ eva hetoḥ brahma-vicāraḥ kartavyaḥ iti adhyāt yad uktam \|\|

iti mahā-siddhāntaḥ \|

**\[ atha sūtrākāra-abhimata-sūtrārtha-yojana-ārambhaḥ: \]**

tatra pūrva-pakṣa-vādī manyate—vṛddha-vyavahāra-anvayatra śabdasya bodhakatva-vyavadhāraṇa-asaṃbhavāt, vyavahārasya ca kārya-buddhi-pūrvakatvena kāryārthe eva śabdasya prāmāṇyam iti ka.pyelv eva veda-arthaḥ \| ataḥ na vedāntāḥ pariniṣṭhite pare brahmaṇi pramāṇa-bhāvam anubhavanti \| na ca putra-janma-ādi-nisthavastuv iṣaya-vākyeṣu hṛṣṭahetum kāla-atyayatinām arthānām anityatvāt sulabha-sukha prastāvāt ihaiṇṭavyaviyoginiyatām bhāvanyā ca priya-artha-pratipatti-nimitta-sukha-vikāsāt dīrgha-jñāna-artha-

virodha-buddhi-hetutva-niścayaḥ; na api vyutpanna-itara-vad-viśeṣārthasya pada-antara-arthānvayena prakāra-artha-niścayena vā śabdasya siddhavat-puruṣābhidhāna-śakti-niścayaḥ; jñāna-kāryābhidhāyi-pada-samudāyasya tad-artha-viśeṣa-niścaya-rūpatvāt tasya \|

na ca sarvābhihitasya nāma sarvā rejur eva iti śabda-śravaṇa-samanantaram bhava-nirvṛtta-darśanena sarpa-abhāva-buddhi-hetutva-niścayaḥ; ajñāta-niṣṭhe nirviṣayam eva etan mithyā bahirindriya-artha-bodhas tu bahu bhavitru-niṣṭha-hetuḥ saṃbhū viśeṣa-niścayayo’gāt \| kārya-buddhi-prasūnyākhilena śabdasya pravṛtti-kārya-virodha-hetum avagatam iti sarva-padānāṃ kārya-paratvena sarveḥ padaiḥ kāryasya eva viśiṣṭasya pratipādanāt anya-anvita-arthya-mātre pada-aśakti-niścayaḥ \| iṣṭa-sādhanatā-buddhis tu kārya-buddhitvena pravartti-hetuḥ; na svarūpeṇa, atīta-anāgata-varttamāne api kārya-buddhiṣu pratyayam upalabdheḥ \| iṣṭa-upāyo hi mat-pakṣa-mātre na siddhyati; ato mat-śāsitavya iti buddhir yatra jāyate, tatra eva pravarttate \| ataḥ kārya-buddhir eva pravṛtti-hetuḥ iti pravarttakasya eva śabda-vācyatayā kāryasya eva veda-vedyatvād viśeṣa-phala-darśanā api lakṣaṇa-ananta-sthira-phalādhiniṣṭheḥ, akṣayyaṃ ha vai cāturmāsya-yājinas sukṛtaṃ bhavati ityādibhiḥ karmaṇā eva sthira-phalatva-pratipādanāc ca karma-phala-api-asthiratva-brahma-jñāna-phala-ananta-sthiratva-jñāna-hetuko brahma-vicāra-ārambho na yuktaḥ—iti \|\|

atra abhidhīyate—nikhila-loka-vidita-śabdārtha-saṃbandha-avadhāraṇa-prakāram apamṛśya sarveṣāṃ nāma laukikānāṃ kārya-anvayitva-avadhāraṇaṃ śrāmaṇikaṃ na bahu manyate \| evaṃ kila bālāś cet artha-saṃbandham avadhārayanti—mātā-pitṛ-prabhṛti-nirantaratam tamulādevā śiśu-prānugraṇaiḥ śirā-nirdeśaiś ca enam idam itthaṃ ca avadhārayan-nirīkṣaṇaiḥ aṅgulyā nirdiśya nirdiśya taṃ taṃ śabdais tat-sūdīta-varṇaiś ca bahuśaḥ śikṣitānāṃ stanandhayānāṃ taiḥ eva śabdaiḥ abhinava-āvṛtta-pratyarthaṃ svātmanāṃ buddhyutpattiṃ dṛṣṭvā śabda-arthayoḥ saṃbandha-antara-darśanāt sārūpya-vidyut-para-ajñānāc ca teṣu evam teṣāṃ śabdānāṃ prayogo bodhaka-nibandhana iti niścinvanti \| punar ca vyutpanna-itara-śabdeṣu api asya śabdasya ayam arthaḥ iti pūrva-vṛddha-śikṣita-sat-saṃvāda-bahulānām artha-manaskāra para-pratyayanāya tan-mukhya-vivakṣiti vākya-jātam prayuñjate \|\|

prakāra-antareṇa api śabda-artha-saṃbandha-avadhāraṇaṃ susākam—kenacid vṛkṣeṇa hasta-ceṣṭitena pitā te sukham āste iti vedayatāya jñāpaya iti preṣitaḥ kaścid tatra āpane prabhuḥ pitā te sukham āste iti śabdaṃ prayuṅkte \| pārśvasthaḥ anyaḥ vyutpitsus tasmāt ceṣṭā-viśeṣāt tata āpāne prabhutimāṃ jñātvā anugatas tatra āpīnāya prayuktimāṃ śabdaṃ śrutvā ayaṃ śabdas tam artha-buddhi-hetur iti niścinoti iti kārya-artha eva vyutpattir iti nirbandho nirnibandhanaḥ \| ato vedāntataḥ pariniṣpannaṃ paraṃ brahma, tad-upāsanaṃ ca aparimita-phalaṃ bodhayati iti tat-niṣpanna-phalo brahma-vicāraḥ kartavyaḥ \|\|

kārya-arthatve api vedasya brahma-vicāraḥ kartavya eva \| katham ? ātmā vā are draṣṭavya-

śrotavyaḥ mantavyaḥ nididhyāsitavyaḥ; śrotavyaḥ vijijñāsitavyaḥ; vijñāya prajñāṃ kurvīta; daharaḥ asmin antar-ākāśaḥ tasmin yat antaḥ tat anveṣṭavyaṃ tathā va vijijñāsitavyam; tat na api dahraṃ gārgaṃ viśokaḥ tasmin yat antaḥ tat upāsitavyam ityādibhiḥ pratipadya upāsana-viṣaya-kārya-adhikṛtatvena brahma-vit āpnoti parastād iti brahma-svarūpa-viśeṣaṇānāṃ duḥkha-asaṃbhinna-deśa-viśeṣatva-rūpa-caryādivat, ruci-sama-tṛpti-prītivat, apunar-āvṛttatvavat na asādhya-sādhana-bhāvaḥ ca, kārya-upayogitayā eva siddhaḥ \|\| grāmasya ityādikām api vākyeṣu na kāryārthaṃ kṣupītiḥ; bhavad-abhimata-kāryasya durnirūpatvāt \| kṛti-sādhyam api kṛti-uddeśyaṃ hi bhavataḥ kāryam \| kṛti-uddeśyatvaṃ ca kṛtimattvaṃ, kṛtimattvaṃ ca haṭhāt prāptum iṣṭatamatvam \| iṣṭatamaṃ ca sukhaṃ vartamāna-duḥkhasya tabiṣṭa-nivṛttau \| tatra iṣṭa-sukha-ādīnām puruṣeṇa sva-prayatnataḥ yadi taddasiddhiḥ pratītā, tataḥ prayatna-icchuḥ pravartate puruṣaḥ iti na kartr̥-pañca-viṣayasya kṛty-adhīna-siddhatvam antareṇa kṛti-uddeśyatvaṃ nāma kiñcit upalabhyate \| iṣṭa-icchā-viṣayasya prerakatvaṃ ca prayatna-adhīna-siddhatvena eva, tatra eva pravṛtteḥ \| na ca puruṣa-anukūlatvam kṛti-uddeśyatvaṃ; yat sukham eva puruṣa-anukūlam \| na ca duḥkha-nivṛttiḥ puruṣa-anukūlaṃ; puruṣa-anukūlaṃ sukhaṃ tad-atiriktaṃ duḥkham iti hi sukha-duḥkhayoḥ svarūpa-vivekaḥ \| duḥkhasya pratikūlatayā tat-nivṛttiḥ iṣṭā bhavati; na anukūlatayā \| anukūla-pratikūlatva-vyatireke svarūpeṇa avasthitiḥ hi duḥkha-nivṛttiḥ \| atas sukha-vyatiriktasya kriyādheḥ anukūlatvaṃ na saṃbhavati \| na sukha-arthatayā tasya api anukūlatvam; duḥkha-ātmakatvāt tasya \| sukha-arthatā api tadvat ātma-icchāyām eva bhavati \|\| na ca kṛtiṃ prati śeṣitvaṃ kṛti-uddeśyatvam, bhavat-pakṣe śeṣitvasya anirūpaṇāt \| na ca pareṣa-śabdavat kṛti-vyāpyatvaṃ śeṣatvam iti tad-gati-saṃbandhī śeṣitva-vyavahāraḥ, tathā sati kṛteḥ eva śeṣatvena taṃ prati tat-sādhyasya śeṣitva-bhāvāt \| na ca pareṣa-śabda-vṛttitvāpeśeṣatvena para-śeṣī; uddeśyatvasya eva nirūpyamāṇatvāt, pradhānasya api bhūty-oddeśya-pratyarthatva-darśanāt \| pradhānasya bhṛtya-poṣaṇe api sva-uddeśena pravarttata iti cet, bhṛtyaḥ api hi pradhāna-viṣaye sva-uddeśena eva pravarttate \| kārya-svarūpasya eva atirūpaṇāt, kāryaṃ prati saṃbandhī śeṣaḥ tat-saṃbandhī śeṣī iti asaṃgataṃ \| na api kṛti-prayojanatvaṃ kṛti-uddeśyatvam; puruṣasya kṛty-ārambha-prayojanatvam eva hi kṛti-prayojanam \| sa ca icchā-viṣayaḥ \| tasmāt icchā-vyatirikta-kṛty-uddeśyatva-anirūpaṇāt kṛti-sādhyatā-kṛti-taddhānatva-rūpaṃ kāryaṃ durnirūpam eva \|\| niyoge api sākṣād-vidhi-viṣaya-samūha-duḥkha-nivṛttiḥ niyamena yāvad-ātma-sādhanatayā eva uddeśyaṃ kṛti-sādhyatvaṃ ca \| ataḥ eva hi tasya kriyā-atiriktatā; anyathā kriyā eva kāryaṃ syāt; svarga-kāma-samarpada-samabhivyāhāra-anupapattau liṅādi-vācyaṃ kāryaṃ svarga-asādhanam eva iti kṣaṇabhaṅgika-matiḥ ekasya sthiram

svarga-asādhana-mūrvam eva kāryam iti svarga-asādhanata-ullekhena eva hi apūrva-vyutpattiḥ \| ataḥ prathamam ananyārthatayā pratipannasya kāryasya ananyārthatva-nirvāhaṇāya apūrvam eva paścāt svarga-asādhanaṃ bhavati iti upahāsyam, svargakāma-pada-anvita-tat-kārya-abhidhāyi-padena prathamam avagata-ananyārthatā-anābhidhānāt, sukha-ḍuḥkha-nivṛtti-tadrāsādhaneşyāindrasthānanyārthasya kṛti-sādhyatā-pratīti-paryavasānāt ca \|\|

api ca kim idaṃ niyogasya prayojanattvam? sukha-viprayogasya api anukūlatvam eva iti cet, kiṃ niyoga-sukham? sukham eva hi anukūlam \| sukha-viśeṣa-viprayoga-aparyantaṃ vilakṣaṇaṃ sukha-antaram iti cet; kiṃ tatra pramāṇam iti vaktavyam \| sva-anubhavaḥ cet, na; viṣaya-viśeṣa-anubhava-sukha-viprayoga-anubhava-sukham iti bhavataḥ api na anubhūyate \| śāstreṇa niyogasya puruṣārthatayā pratipādanāt paścāt tu bhokṣyate iti cet; kiṃ tad-viyogasya puruṣārthatva-vādī śāstram? na tāvat laukikaṃ vākyam; tasya duḥkha-ātmaka-kriyā-viṣayatvāt, tena sukha-ādi-sādhanatayā eva kṛti-sādhyatā-mātra-pratipādanāt \| na api vaidikaṃ; tena api svarga-ādi-sādhanatayā eva kāryasya pratipādanāt \| na api nitya-naimittika-karaṇa-aṃśaḥ; tasya api tad-abhidhāyitvāt svargakāma-vākyastha-apūrva-vyutpatti-pūrvakam iti uttara-ītyā tena api sukha-ādi-sādhana-kārya-abhidhāna-savarjanīyatvam \| niyataḥ hi phalasya karmaṇaḥ anuṣṭhitasya phalatvena tadānīm anubhūyamāna-abādha-ārogyatā-ādi-vyatirekeṇa niyogasya sukham anubhavantukalatve ca niyoga-sukham iti atra na kiñcana pramāṇam upalabhāmahe \| arthavāda-ādiṣu api svarga-ādi-sukha-prakāra-kīrtanaṃ ca viyoga-rūpa-sukha-prakāra-kīrtanaṃ bhavatām api na hṛdyataram \|\|

ato vidhi-vākyaiḥ api dhātu-arthasya kartuṃ vyāpāra-sādhyatā-mātraṃ śabda-anusāsana-siddham eva liṅ-ādeḥ vyāpāra-ārthatvam adhyavasīyate \| dhātu-arthasya ca yāga-ādeḥ jyotiṣṭomatvabhimarasamutthasampārādhanarūpatā, samārādhitatva-rūpa-brahma-ādi-phala-siddhiḥ ca iti phalam ata upapatteḥ iti atra pratipādayiṣyate \| ato vedāntāḥ pariniṣpannaṃ paraṃ brahma bodhayanti iti brahma-upāsana-phala-ānantaryaṃ sthiratvaṃ ca siddham \| cāturmāsya-ādi-karmasu api kevalasya karmaṇaḥ kṣayi-phalatva-upadeśāt akṣaya-phala-śravaṇaṃ vāyavyāntarikṣaṃ ca etad amṛtam iti-ādivat aupacārikaṃ mantavyam \| ataḥ kevalānāṃ karmaṇām alpa-sthira-phalatvāt, brahma-jñānasya ca ananta-sthira-phalatvāt nirṇaya-phalaḥ brahma-vidyā-ārambhaḥ yuktaḥ iti sthitam \|\| 1 \|\| iti jijñāsā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha janmādi-adhikaraṇam 2**

kiṃ punaḥ tad brahma yat vijijñāsyam ucyate iti atra āha—

## \|\| 1.1.2 \|\|

*janmādi asya yataḥ*

**Комментарий:**

janmādi iti bhūta-sthiti-pralayasya \| tad-guṇa-saṃvijñānaḥ bahuvrīhiḥ \| asya—acintya-vividha-vicitra-racanarasya niyata-deśa-kāla-phala-bhoga-brahmādi-stamba-paryanta-kṣetrajña-miśrasya jagataḥ;

yataḥ— yasmāt sarveśvarāt nikhila-heya-pratyanīka-svarūpāt satya-saṅkalpāt jñānānandādi-ananta-kalyāṇa-guṇāt sarvajñāt sarvaśakteḥ parama-kāruṇikāt paramātṛbhūtaḥ mumukṣuḥ tititrayāḥ pravartante tad brahmeti sūtrārthaḥ \|\|

mukhyaṃ vāṇijyaṃ, karṣaṇe pitṛ-sukha-sāra, adhīhi bhagavo brahma, iti āraṃbhya, yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante, yena jātāni jīvanti, yat prayanti abhisaṃviśanti, tad vijijñāsasva, tad brahmeti śrūyate \| tatra saṃśayaḥ— kim asmād vākyād brahma lakṣaṇataḥ pratipattuṃ śakyate na vā iti \| kiṃ prāptam? na śakyam iti \| na tāvat janmādayo viśeṣaṇatvena brahma lakṣayanti, aneka-viśeṣaṇa-vyāhṛtatvena brahmaṇo ’nekatva-prasaṅgaḥ; viśeṣaṇatvaṃ hi vyāvartakatvam \| nanu devadattasya śyāmo yuvā lohitākṣaḥ sama-parimāṇa ity atra viśeṣaṇa-bahutve ’pi eka eva devadattaḥ pratīyate; evam atrāpy ekaṃva brahma bhavati \| na evam; tatra pramāṇāntareṇa eka-vyakti-tirekasminn eva viśeṣaṇānām upasaṃhāraḥ anyathā tatrāpi vyāvarttakatva-anekatva-parihāryam \| atra tu aiteṣaiva viśeṣeṇa lilakṣayiṣitatvāt brahmaṇaḥ pramāṇāntareṇa evam anavagatām iti vyāvartakaiḥ bhedena brahma-bahutvam avarjanīyam \|\|

brahma-śabdaś ca adhyayānīyaṃ pratīyata iti cet \| ajñāta-gotrasya ajigāmiṇaḥ puruṣasya khaṇḍo muṇḍaḥ pūrṇa-śṛṅgo gauḥ ity ukteḥ go-padeśe ’pi khaṇḍatvādika-vyāvartaka-bhedena go-vyakti-bahutva-pratītiḥ brahmatva-yogād api bahu-vastveṣuḥ \| ata eva lilakṣayiṣite vastv eṣāṃ viśeṣaṇānāṃ saṃbhūya lakṣaṇatvam avamatya upahasan \| na api upalakṣaṇatvena lakṣayanti, ākārāntara-apratipattir evaḥ \| upalakṣaṇānām ekena ākāreṇa pratipattasya kenacid ākārāntareṇa pratipatti-siddhatvaṃ hi dṛṣṭam— yasya ayaṃ sārasaḥ sa devadatta-kedāra ityādiṣu \| nanu ca asatyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahmeti pratiprakārakasya jagaj-janmādīny upalakṣaṇāni bhavanti \| na; itaretara-pratiprakāra-apekṣatvena ubhayor lakṣaṇa-vākyayor vīṇāśravaṇāt \| ato na lakṣaṇato brahma pratipattuṃ śakyata iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte ’bhidhīyate—jagat-luddhi-sthiti-vilayaṃpada-lakṣaṇa-bhūtaṃ brahma pratipattuṃ śakyate \| na ca upalakṣaṇa-upalakṣyākāra-yatirikta-ākārāntara-apratipattir brahma-apratipattiḥ \| upalabdhaṃ hy anavadhika-atiśaya-bṛhad vaṛṣṇaṃ ca; bṛhatvādi-niṣedhadye ’vāt \| tad-upalakṣaṇa-bhūtāś ca jagaj-janma-sthiti-layāḥ \| yato yena yad iti prasiddha-vastu-daiśena yathā-prasiddhi janmādhikāram ucyate \| prasiddhaś ca sad eva somya idam agre āsīd ekam eva advitīyam nendakṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti tattjo ’śṛjataitejaskarṇam eva sacchabdatvācyasya nikhila-upādāna-rūpa-kāraṇatvena \| tarhi sad eva somya ekam eva advitīyupādānatāṃ pratipādya aikṣaṇa-anvayādhyānantaraṃ pratiniṣedhya na aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tat tejo ’śṛjataitejaskarṇam eva pratipādanāt \| tasmād yasmāt jagaj-janma-sthiti-layās tan tad brahmeti janma-sthiti-layāḥ sva-nimitta-upādāna-bhūtaṃ vastu brahmeti lakṣayanti \| jagan-nimitta-upādānatā-atiśayaṃ ca ekatva-satya-

saṅkalpa-vicitra-śaktitva-ādi-ākāra-gṛhītena pratipakṣaṃ brahma hi ca janma-ādīnāṃ tathā pratipakṣasya lakṣaṇatvena na akāraṇa-antara-apratipatti-rūpa-anapagatiḥ \|\|

jagat-janma-ādīnāṃ viśeṣaṇatayā lakṣaṇatve api na kaścit doṣaḥ \| lakṣaṇa-bhūtatvāt api viśeṣaṇāni sva-virodhi-vyāvṛttiṃ vastu lakṣayanti \| ajñāta-vastu-rūpe vastu ekaikasmin lilakṣayiṣite api paraspara-avirodhi-eka-aneka-viśeṣaṇa-lakṣyatvaṃ na bhedam āpādayati \| viśeṣaṇānām eka-āśrayatayā pratīteḥ ekasmin eka-upasaṃhārāt \| khaṇḍatvāt vastu virodhāt eva gotva-vyakti-bhedam āpādayanti \| atra tu kāla-bhedena janma-ādīnāṃ na virodhaḥ \| śroto vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante iti ādi-kāraṇa-vākyena pratipakṣasya jagat-janma-ādi-kāraṇasya brahmaṇaḥ sakala-itara-vyāvṛttaṃ svarūpam abhidhīyate satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti \| tatra satya-padaṃ nirupādhika-sattā-yogi brahma āha \| tena vikāra-rūpa-janma-bhetetara-sattulyatve tat-mūlaṃ dyāvṛttaḥ; nāma-antara-bhajana-ādi-avasthāntara-yogena tayoḥ vikāra-upādhika-sattā-yoga-rahitatvāt \| jñāna-padaṃ nitya-asaṃkucita-jñāna-eka-ākāram āha \| tena kadācit saṃkucita-jñānatvena muktā vyāvṛttāḥ \| ananta-padaṃ deśa-kāla-vastu-pariccheda-rahitaṃ svarūpam āha \| saguṇatvāt svarūpasya svarūpeṇa guṇaiḥ ca ānantyam \| tena pūrva-ukta-dvaya-anatkoṭi-dvaya-vilakṣaṇa-asatīyā-svarūpa-guṇā nityā vyāvṛttāḥ; viśeṣaṇānāṃ vyāvartakatvāt \| tataḥ satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti etena vākyena jagat-janma-ādinā ajñāta-svarūpaṃ brahma sakala-itara-vastu-visajātīyam iti lakṣyata iti na aprayojana-śravaṇam \| ataḥ sakala-sajagaj-janma-ādi-kāraṇaṃ nirastaṃ sarvaṃ satya-saṅkalpaṃ sarvajña-śakti brahma lakṣaṇataḥ pratipattuṃ śakyata iti siddham \|\|

ye tu nirviśeṣa-vastu jijñāsyam iti vadanti, tan-mate brahma-jijñāsā, janma-ādi asya yataḥ iti asaṅgataṃ syāt; niratiśaya-bṛhattvaṃ bṛhaṇaṃ ca brahma iti nirvacanāt, tat ca brahma jagat-janma-ādi-kāraṇam iti vacanena \| evam uttareṣu api sūtra-gaṇeṣu sūtrita-ahetu-bhukti-gaṇeṣu ca īkṣaṇa-ādi-apradeśyānāṃ sūtrāṇi sūtra-ukta-ahetu-bhūta-pakṣa na tatra pramāṇam \| tarkāḥ ca sādharmya-siddha-aparisādhana-dharmitva-tarvastu-viṣayatvāt atra nirviśeṣa-vastuni pramāṇam \| jagat-janma-ādi-śabdaḥ yataḥ tat bṛhaṃ iti svataḥ apekṣā-pādakena na nirviśeṣa-vastu-siddhiḥ; bhrama-mūlatvāt tam, ajñāna-sākṣi brahmaṇi upagamāt \| sākṣitvaṃ hi prakāśa-eka-svarūpatve ucyate \| prakāśatvaṃ tu jaḍa-adravya-tarkaṃ svayaṃ parasya ca vyavahāra-yogyatā-pādaka-svabhāvena bhavati \| tathā sati saviśeṣatvam \| tasmāde prakāśaḥ ca eva na syāt; tucchatvena syāt \|\| iti janma-ādi-adhikaraṇam \|\|2\|\|

**atha śāstra-yonitva-adhikaraṇam 3**

jagat-janma-ādi-kāraṇaṃ brahma vedānta-vedyam anuyuktam \| tat ayuktam; tat hi na vākya-pratipādyam \| anumānena siddheḥ iti āśaṅkya āha—

## \|\| 1.1.3 \|\|

*śāstra-yonitvāt*

**Комментарий:**

śāstram yadi yoniḥ— kāraṇam, pramāṇam, tat u ca śāstra-yoniḥ; tasya bhāvaḥ śāstra-yonitvam; tasmāt \| bṛhat-jana-kāraṇatvāt śāstrasya tat-yonitvam brahmaṇaḥ \| atyanta-atīndriyatvena pratyakṣa-ādi-pramāṇa-viṣayatathā brahmaṇaḥ śāstra-eka-pramāṇakatvāt uru-phalabrahma-śrayo vā imāni bhūtāni iti-ādi-vākyam bodhyatva eva vedya-arthaḥ \|\|

nanu śāstra-yonitvam brahmaṇo na saṃbhavati, pramāṇatva iva vedānta-vākya-brahmaṇaḥ; upapāte tu śāstra-maryādat \| kim tarhi tatra pramāṇam ? na tāvat pratyakṣam \| tat dhi dvi-vidham— indriya-saṃbhavam yoga-saṃbhavam ca iti \| indriya-saṃbhavam ca bāhya-saṃbhava-mānasa-saṃbhavam ca iti dvi-dhā \| bāhya-indriyāṇi vidyamāna-sannikṛṣṭa-yogya-sva-viṣaya-viśeṣa-jana-ganāni na sarva-artha-sākṣātkāra-ādi-nirviśeṣa-samartha-puruṣa-viśeṣa-viṣaya-bodha-janakāni \| na api āntaram; āntara-sukha-duḥkha-ādi-vyakti-rikta-bahiḥ-viṣayeṣu tasya bāhya-indriya-anapekṣa-pravṛtti-anupapatteḥ \| na api yoga-janma; bhāvanā-prakarṣa-paryanta-janmanaḥ tasya vijñāta-abhyāsatve api pūrva-anubhūta-viṣaya-smṛti-mātratvāt pramāṇyam iti kutaḥ pratyakṣatā ? tat-vihita-ra-viṣayatve kāraṇa-abhāvāt; tathā sati tasya bhrama-rūpatā \| na api anumānam viśeṣe-dṛṣṭam sāmānyato dṛṣṭam vā \| atīndriye vastuni sambandha-avadhāraṇa-virahāt na viśeṣato dṛṣṭam \| samasta-vastu-sākṣātkāra-tan-nirmāṇa-samartha-puruṣa-viśeṣa-niyatam sāmānyato dṛṣṭam api na liṅgam upapadyate \|\|

nanu ca jagataḥ kāryatvam tat-upādāna-upakaraṇa-saṃpradāna-prayojana-abhijña-kartṛkam kalpayatām \| acetana-ārabdhatvam jagat-su eka-cetana-adhiṣṭhitena eva vyāptam \| sarvam hi ghaṭa-ādi-kāryam tat-upādāna-upakaraṇa-saṃpradāna-prayojana-abhijña-kartṛkam dṛṣṭam \| acetana-ārabdha-rogam sva-śarīra-eka-cetana-adhiṣṭhānam ca \| sādhayati idam jagataḥ kāryatvam \| ucyate \| kim idam eka-cetana-adhiṣṭhatvam ? na tāvat svatantra-utpatti-sthititvam; dṛṣṭānto hi sādhya-vikalaḥ syāt \| na hi aro’gam sva-śarīram eka-cetana-nyasta-tpatti-sthiti; tat-śarīrasya bhoktuṇam bhogya-ādi-sarva-cetanānām adṛṣṭa-janyatva-anuvṛtti-sthitatvāt \| kiñca, śarīra-utpattiḥ iva sva-anya-vatsa-vadevatā-rūṇā sthitiḥ api avasthāpyatiḥ ekeṇa na cetanasya apekṣate \|

prāṇa-lakṣaṇā tu sthitiḥ pakṣatvābhimate kṣiti-jala-vahni-īśvarato na saṃbhavati iti pakṣa-apākṣaṇugata-aneka-rūpām sthitim na upalabhāmahe \| tathā ayatna-prayuktatvam tat-adhīnatvam iti cet, aneka-cetana-sādhyebhyo gurutara-sthalila-mahīruha-ādiṣu vyabhicāraḥ \| cetana-mātra-adhīnatve siddha-sādhyatā \|\|

kiñca, upapattibhiḥ siddhāntam jīvānām eva lāghavena kartṛtva-samupagamaḥ yuktaḥ \| na ca jīvānām upakṣīṇa-ācchanna-bhinnatvāt kartṛtva-saṃbhavaḥ; sarveṣām eva cetanānām pṛthivī-ādi-upādāna-nyāya-anugraha-karaṇa-sākṣātkāra-sāmarthya-aśaktiḥ; yathā idānīm pṛthivī-ādayo yāga-ādharma-pratyakṣam adhyante \| upakaraṇa-bhūta-gaṇa-ādi-śarīka-sama-pūrva-diṣṭa-devatā-ādi-adhisākṣātkāra-sām eve api cetanānām na kartṛtva-anupapattiḥ;

tat-sākṣāt-kāra-anapekṣaṇāt kārya-saṃśayaḥ \| śakti-smaraṇāt kāra eva hi kārya-saṃśayoge; śaktiḥ tu jñāna-mātre eva upayujyate, na sākṣāt-kāraḥ; na hi kulāla-ādiṣu kārya-karaṇa-bhūta-daṇḍa-cakra-ādi-vacana-icchā-smṛtim api sākṣāt-kṛtya ghaṭa-karaṇikā-ādi-kāryam ārabhate \| iha tu cetanānāṃ samāna-vyagatatva-prāpti-śakti-viśeṣaṇāṃ kārya-saṃśayo na anupapannaḥ \|\|

kiṃ ca, yat cetana-kriye śakya-upādāna-ādi-jñānaṃ ca tad eva tad-apekṣā-kartṛkaṃ dṛṣṭam \| mahī-mahīdhara-mahārṇava-ādi tu atyāya-kriyā-samavāya-upādāna-ādi-jñānaṃ ca iti na cetana-kartṛkam \| ato ghaṭa-maṇika-ādi sajātīya-cakradhakriya-śakya-upādāna-ādi-jñānavat anuguṇatva eva kāryatvaṃ buddhi-svakartṛ-pūrvakatva-sādhye prabhavati \| kiṃ ca, ghaṭa-ādi-kārya-manoviśeṣaṇa-upayama-mahimikāmā śarīreṇa pariṣkṛta-vārajñā-kartṛmena nirmitaṃ dṛṣṭam iti tathāvidham eva cetanaṃ kartāraṃ sādhyatvayaṃ kāryatva-hetuḥ viṣaya-dhiṣṭhat-puruṣa-sārvajñya-ādi-aviśeṣa-pratīti-sādhana-viruddha-sthaḥ \| na ca etāvatā sarva-anumnāna-uccheda-prasaṅgaḥ \| liṅga-liṅgi-pramāṇa-antara-yogye liṅga-bala-upasthāpitā viparīta-viśeṣāḥ tu pramāṇa-pratihata-gayo nivartante \| iha tu sakala-itara-pramāṇa-viṣaye liṅgīni viśva-lanirmāṇa-cature prati avyatireka-apagata-vinanābhāva-niyamā dharmāḥ satvaṃ eva aviśeṣeṇa prasajyante; nivartaka-pramāṇa-abhāvāt eva tiṣṭhante \| ata āgamāt ete katham īśvara-siddhyati ?

atra āhaḥ \| sāvayavatvāt eva jagataḥ kāryatvaṃ na pratyākhyātuṃ śakyate \| bhavanti ca prayogāḥ— vivāda-adhyāsitaṃ bhū-bhūdhara-ādi kāryaṃ, sāvayavatvāt, ghaṭa-ādivat \| tathā vivāda-adhyāsita-savani-jaladhi-mahīdhara-ādi kāryaṃ, mahattve sati kriyāvattvāt, ghaṭavat \| tanu-bhavana-ādi kāryaṃ, mahattve sati mūrtatvāt, ghaṭavat— iti \| sāvayave tu dravye tu idam eva kriyate na itarat iti kāryatvasya niyāmakaṃ sāvayavatva-atireki rūpa-antaraṃ na upalabhyāmahe \| kāryatvavati nityaṃ śabdayi-kriyatvaṃ śakya-upādāna-ādi-jñānavatvaṃ ca upalabhyate iti cet; kārya-cetanatvam idaṃ api viṣaye jñāna-śakte kārya-anumeye iti etatra api sāvayavatvāt vinā kāryatvaṃ jñātam iti te ca pratyakṣa eva iti na kaścit viśeṣaḥ \| tathā hi ghaṭa-maṇika-ādiṣu huteṣu kārya-darśana-anumita-kartṛ-gatatva-vibhūti-śakti-jñānaḥ puruṣaḥ adṛṣṭa-pūrvaṃ vivicya sarva-viśeṣaṃ narendra-vacana-mātra-iccha adhyavasita-caitra-viśeṣaṇa tasya kāryatvaṃ niścitya tadānīm eva kartṛ-tat-jñāna-śakti-caitryam anuminoti \| ataḥ tanu-bhavana-ādeḥ kāryatve siddhe sarva-sākṣāt-kāra-ratna-vibhūtimati ripuṇaḥ kaścit puruṣa-viśeṣaḥ asmadvartī eva \|\|

kiṃ ca sarva-cetanānāṃ dharma-adharma-nimitter api sukha-duḥkha-upabhoge cetana-anadhitiṣṭhati sati acetanyoḥ phala-hetutva-anupapatteḥ sarva-karma-luguna-sarva-phala-pradāna-tatparaḥ kaścit apekṣyaḥ; bādhaka-anājña-vidhurasya vāsanāt ar-caitanasya deśa-kāla-ādi-aneka-parikara-sannidhāne api guṇa-ādi-nirguṇa-sādhanatvāt darśanāt \| bīja-aṅkura-ādeḥ pakṣa-antara-bhūta tasmāt viśva-rāṣaṇaṃ śrī-triveṇī-lāghava-manasikṛtā vinirmimat \| tata eva sukha-ādi-vibhāga-avicāra-vacanasam api tathā eva \| na ca lāghavena ubhaya-vādi-samprati-

pratyakṣa-kṣetrajña-ātmasu eva īśvara-adhiṣṭhātṛkatvena yuktam; teṣāṃ sūkṣma-cid-acit-svarūpa-darśana-aśaka-niścayāt darśanāt guṇe ca hi saṃbandha kalpanā \| na ca kṣetrajña-deha-īśvara-sthā-śarīra-liṅga-prayogaḥ asti \| ataḥ pramāṇa-antarataḥ na tad-viṣaya-anupapattiḥ \| samarthitaṃ pūrva-kṛta-niyamaka-kāryatva-hetunā siddhyat svābhāvika-kartṛtva-ādi-kṣetra-kāratva-niyama-śakti-saṃpannaḥ eva siddhyati \|\|

yat karmaṇi adhyāropa-darśanena dharma-viśeṣa-viṣayīkaraṇa-sādhanaṃ anumīyate, tadvat umāne dṛṣṭānta-bhittatva-nirvacanam; sapakṣe sahatā-hānaṃ sarveṣāṃ kāryatva-ādi-hetu-bhūtānāṃ ca dharmāṇāṃ liṅgini aprāptiḥ \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—kenacit kiñcit kriyamāṇaṃ sva-utpattye kartṛ-svarūpa-nirmāṇa-sāmarthyaṃ sva-upādāna-upakaraṇa-jñānaṃ ca apekṣate; na svasya asāmarthya-najñānaṃ ca, hetutva-abhāvāt \| sva-nirmāṇa-sāmarthyaṃ sva-upādāna-upakaraṇa-jñāna-asāmarthyayā iva sva-upātta-anupapanna-arthaṃ saṃbandhitayā darśana-mātreṇa akiñcitkarasya artha-antara-jñāna-ādeḥ hetutva-kalpanā-ayogāt iti \|\|

kiṃ ca, kiṃ-pramāṇa-vṛtti-atirikta-artha-jñāna-arthaṃ kiṃ sarva-viṣayaṃ kriyā-upeyogi ? uta, katipaya-viṣayam ? na tāvat sarva-viṣayam; na hi kulāla-ādhiḥ kriyamāṇa-ghaṭa-ādi-riktaṃ kim api na jānāti \| na api katipaya-viṣayaṃ; sarveṣu kartṛṣu tat-samāna-aśakya-niyamena sarvathām ajñāna-ādeḥ dharmi-cārāt \| ataḥ kāryatvasya asādhakanānām āna-īśvara-vādīnāṃ liṅgini aprāptiḥ iti na viparīta-sādhanatvam \|\|

kulāla-ādīnāṃ daṇḍa-cakra-adhiṣṭhānaṃ śarīra-dvāreṇa eva dṛṣṭam iti jagat-upādāna-upakaraṇa-adhiṣṭhānam īśvarasya śarīrasthā anupapannam iti cet; seṅkarṣa-mātreṇa eva para-śarīra-gata-bhūta-vetāla-garala-ādi-upamarditva-darśanāt \| katham aśarīrasya para-śarīranhupasaṃkalpa iti cet; na śarīra-upabhoga-saṃkalpaḥ, śarīrasya saṃkalpa-hetutva-abhāvāt \| manaḥ eva hi saṃkalpa-hetuḥ \| tat ca muktagānām īśvare api; kāryatvena eva jñāna-śaktivat manasaḥ api prāpyatvāt \| mānasara-saṃkalpasya aśarīrasya eva, saśarīrasya eva samanaskatvāt iti cet; manasaḥ nityatvena deha-apāye api manaḥ-saṃyogena kāryatvāt \|\|

ato vicitra-avyavasthā-niveśa-viśeṣa-tadupavanika-arthi-nirmāṇe puṇya-pāpa-paravaśaḥ parimita-śakti-jñānaḥ kṣetrajñaḥ na prabhavati iti nikhila-bhuvana-nirmāṇa-caturaḥ advitīya-aparimita-jñāna-śakteḥ aśarīraḥ śaṅkara-pāśa-ādhava-parihṛṣṭam-ananta-vistāra-vicitra-racanā-prapañcaḥ puruṣa-viśeṣaḥ īśvaraḥ anumānena eva siddhyati \| ataḥ pramāṇa-antara-apekṣya-vāda-grahaṇaḥ na etahārthaṃ brahma pratipādyam iti \|\|

kiṃ ca, atyanta-abhinnayoḥ eva mṛd-dravya-kulālyoḥ nimitta-upādānatva-dṛṣṭāntena ākāra-ādeḥ niratiśaya-dravyasya kāryatva-upapattayā ca na ekam eva brahma kṛtsnasya jagataḥ nimitta-upādānaṃ ca pratipādayituṃ śakyate iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte brūmaḥ— yataḥ jvalana-lakṣaṇaṃ brahma janma-ādi-vākyaṃ bodhayati eva \| kutaḥ ? śāstra-eka-pramāṇakatvāt brahmaṇaḥ \| yad uktaṃ sāvayava-draviṇā kāryaṃ sarvaṃ jagat, kāryaṃ ca tad-bhinnaṃ kartṛ viśeṣa-

pūrvaṃ dṛṣṭam iti nikhila-jagat-nirmāṇa-nipuṇa-ātma-niveśaka-rāga-vedana-caturaḥ kaścit unmeya iti; tat puṣkalam; mahī-mahīruha-ādīnāṃ kāryatve’pi ekadā ekena eva nirmitā ity atra pramāṇa-abhāvāt \| na ca ekasya ghaṭasya iva sarva-ātmakam kāryatvaṃ, yena eka eva ekaḥ kartā syāt \| pṛthag-bhūteṣu kāryeṣu kāla-bheda-kartṛ-bheda-darśanena kartṛ-kāla-bheda-niyama-adarśanāt \| na ca kṣetrajñānāṃ vicitra-ajñānimāṇi-śaktyā kāryatva-balena tad-atirikta-kalpanāyām aneka-kalpanā-anupapatter ekaḥ kartā bhavitum arhati iti; kṣetrajñānām eva upacita-puṇya-viśeṣaṇāṃ śakti-vaicitrya-darśanena teṣām eva atiśayita-adṛṣṭa-saṃhāra-kāryā ca taj-jñāti-lakṣaṇa-kārya-hetutva-saṃbhavāt, tad-atirikta-ātma-sādhaka-prabala-pramāṇa-abhāve \| na ca guṇa-pratibandhi-citra-sthāṇi-cittasya pramāṇa-bala-udbodhi-rodhataḥ; adarśanāt, karmaṇa eva vicitra-atiśayitāt \| kāryatvena sarva-utpatti-niyāyakaḥ kalpyamānaḥ darśana-anuguṇena kalpanā-arhaḥ virodha-abhāvāt ca \| ato buddhimat-eka-kartṛtve sādhye kāryatvasya anaikāntyam, pakṣasya asiddhi-viśeṣaṇatvaṃ, sādhya-vikalatā ca dṛṣṭāntasya; sarva-nirmāṇa-catura-saṃyogasya prasiddheḥ \| buddhimat-kartṛkatva-mātre sādhye siddha-sādhyatā \| sārvajñya-sṛṣṭi-adhikartṛkasya kartṛ-vivekasya sādhaka-viveke kāryatvaṃ kiṃ guṇa-pratyupadhāna-sarva-vastu-gam? uta krameṇa; tad-upadhāna-sarva-vastu-gam? guṇa-pratyupadhāna-sarva-vastu-gatve kāryatvasya asiddhatā \| krameṇa upadhāna-sarva-vastu-gatve aneka-kartṛkatva-sādhāna-śiṣṭatā \| ajñāna-aneka-kartṛkatva-sādhane pratyakṣa-anumānaiḥ śāstra-virodhaḥ; kumbhakāro jāyate, rathakāro jāyata iti-ādi-śravaṇāt \|\|

api ca sarveṣāṃ kāryāṇāṃ śarīra-saṃbandhī ca satya-ādi-guṇa-kāya-ādi-apukha-ādi-avayavarthanena sattva-ādika-mūlatvam anvaya-vyabhicāriṇyasya \| kārya-vaicitrya-hetu-bhūtāḥ kāraṇa-gatā viśeṣāḥ sattva-ācayam; teṣāṃ kāryāṇāṃ tan-mūlatva-āpādanaṃ tad-guṇa-mukhātaḥ karaṇa-vikāra-dvāreṇa, puruṣasya ca tad-yogaḥ karma-mūlaḥ—iti kārya-viśeṣa-saṃbhave jñāna-śaktimat-kartṛ-karma-śeṣaḥ kārya-hetutvena eva vyavasthā-praṇītaḥ; jñāna-śakti-vaicitryasya ca karma-mūlatvāt \| icchātaḥ kārya-ārambha-hetutve’pi viṣaya-viśeṣa-viśiṣṭatāyāḥ sattva-ādi-asattva-ācitrī-mūlatvena karma-saṃbandha-avajjanīyaḥ \| ataḥ kṣetrajña eva kartā; na tad-vilakṣaṇaḥ kaścit anumāna-siddhyati \|\|

bhavanti ca prayogāḥ— tat pṛthivī-ādi kṣetrajña-kartṛkaṃ, kāryatvāt, ghaṭavat \| īśvaraḥ kartā na bhavati, prayojana-śūnyatvāt, mukta-ātmavat \| īśvaraḥ kartā na bhavati, aśarīratvāt, tathā eva \| na ca kṣetrajñānāṃ sva-śarīra-adhiṣṭhāne vyabhicāraḥ; tatra dharma-adṛṣṭa-sahakārīrasya mantravat \| vivakṣita-viṣayaḥ kālo na loka-sṛjyaḥ; kālatvāt, vartamāna-kālavad iti \|\|

api ca, kim īśvarasya śarīra-adhikāro vā kāryaṃ karoti? na tāvat śarīraḥ; śarīrasya kartṛtva-anupapatteḥ \| manasā api api kāryāṇi sa-śarīrasya eva bhavanti; manaso nityatve’pi aśarīrasya muktasya tat-kārya-darśanāt \| na api śarīraḥ; vikalanā-sahatvāt—tat śarīraṃ kiṃ nityam uta anityam?

na tāvat sidhyat; sādhyasya tasya nityatve jagataḥ api nityatva-avirodhāt īśvara-asiddheḥ \| sādhya-nityam; tad-vyatiriktasya naśvarirahetoḥ tad-anitya-bhāvāt \| svayam eva hetuḥ iti cet; aśarīrasya tad-ayogāt; acetana-śarīreṇa saśarīraḥ iti cet; anavasthānāt \| sa kim saṭāpaṭaraḥ nitya-śarīraḥ vā? aśarīratvāt ca eva na sahāya-antaraḥ \| na api nitya-śarīraḥ kāryaṃ karoti mukta-ātmavat \| kāryaṃ jagad-icchā-mātra-vyāpāra-kartṛ kiñcit jñamāne pakṣasya asiddha-viśeṣatvam, dṛṣṭāntasya ca sādhya-hīnatā \|\|

ataḥ durjñāna-tulya-yogakṣemān darśana-anumāna-arahita iti śāstra-eka-pramāṇakaḥ parabrahma-bhūta-sarveśvaraḥ puruṣottamaḥ \| śāstre tu sakala-itara-pramāṇa-paridṛṣṭa-samasta-vastu-vijātīyaṃ sārva-aiśvarya-satya-saṅkalpatva-ādi-mithyātva-avidhaka-atiśaya-parimita-udāra-guṇa-sāgaraṃ nikhila-heya-pratyanīka-svarūpaṃ pratipādayati iti na pramāṇa-antara-ahita-vastu-sādharmya-prayukta-doṣa-gandha-prasaṅgaḥ \| etat tu nimina-upādānayoḥ aikyam ākāśa-ādeḥ nityatva-brahma-vyatirekasya kāritvaṃ ca adhyupalambha-sādhyaṃ pratipādana-nimittam, tad api aviruddham iti prakṛtiḥ ca pratijñā-dṛṣṭānta-anuparodhāt, na viyat aśruteḥ ca iti pratipādayiṣyate \| ataḥ pramāṇa-antara-agocaratvena śāstra-viṣayatvāt sataḥ vā imāni bhūtāni iti vākya-mukta-lakṣaṇaṃ brahma pratipādayati iti siddham \|\| 3 \|\| iti śāstra-yonitva-adhikaraṇam 3.

**atha samanvaya-adhikaraṇam 4**

evaṃ pramāṇa-antara-agocaraṃ brahma; tathā api pravṛtti-nivṛtti-paratva-abhāvena siddha-rūpaṃ brahma na śāstraṃ pratipādayati iti āśaṅkya āha—

## \|\| 1.1.4 \|\|

*tat tu samanvayāt*

**Комментарий:**

prakāśaḥ ca ādi-nitya-śruti-śabdaḥ \| tat tu—śāstra-pramāṇakatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ sambhavet \| kutaḥ? samanvayāt—parama-puruṣārthatayā anvaya-samanvayaḥ, parama-puruṣārtha-bhūtasya eva brahmaṇaḥ abhidheyatayā anvayāt \| evam iva samanvitaḥ hi aupaniṣadaḥ pada-samudāyaḥ: sataḥ vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante sad eva somya idam agre āsīt ekam eva advitīyam tad aikṣata bahu syām prajāyeya iti tat tejaḥ asṛjata brahma vā idam eka eva agre āsīt ātmā vā idam eka eva agre āsīt tasmāt vā etasmāt ātmanaḥ ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ ekaḥ ha vai nārāyaṇaḥ āsīt satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma ānandaḥ brahma iti evam-ādiḥ \| na ca vyutpatti-nisiddha-pari-niṣpanna-vastu-vidhi-pāna-samarthānāṃ pada-samudāyānām akhila-jagad-utpatti-sthiti-vināśa-hetu-bhūta-aśeṣa-doṣa-pratyanīka-aparimita-udāra-guṇa-sāgarāt sarva-adhika-atiśaya-ānanda-svarūpe brahmaṇi samanvitānāṃ pravṛtti-nivṛtti-rūpa-prayojana-virahāt paratvam; sva-viṣaya-avabodha-parvasāyitvāt sarva-pramāṇānām \| na ca prayojana-anuguṇā pramāṇa-pravṛttiḥ \| prayojanaṃ hi pramāṇa-anu-

**prathama-adhyāye-prathama-pāde-samanvaya-adhikaraṇam 4 \|**

guṇaś ca \| na ca pravṛtti-nivṛtti-rūpa-vyatiriktaḥ prayojana-śūnyatvam; puruṣārtha-pratipratīteḥ \| tathā sva-dharma-pareṣv api putraḥ te jātaḥ, na ayaṃ sarpa iti-ādiṣu harṣa-bhaya-nivṛtti-rūpa-prayojanatvaṃ dṛṣṭam \|\|

atra āha—na vedānta-vākyāni brahma pratipādayanti, pravṛtti-nivṛtti-avyatirikta-śāstratvāt artha-antaravat \| yat api pratyakṣa-ādīni vastu-yāthātmya-bodhe paryavasyanti; tathā api śāstra-prayojana-paryavasāni eva \| na hi laukika-vedyoḥ prayojana-rahitasya kasyacid api vākyasya prayoga upalabhyate \| na ca kiñcit prayojanam uddiśya vākya-prayogaḥ śravaṇaṃ vā saṃbhavati \| tat ca prayojanaṃ pravṛtti-nivṛtti-sādhyethān iṣṭa-prāti-parihāra-ātmakam upalabdham—artha-arthī rājā kulaṃ gacchet, mandāgnir nikhu pibet, svarga-kāmo yajeta, na kalañjaṃ bhakṣayet iti-evam-ādiṣu \| yat api anissiddhas tu pareṣv api putraḥ te jātaḥ, na ayaṃ sarpo rajjuḥ eva iti-ādiṣu harṣa-bhayān nivṛtti-rūpa-puruṣārtha-niścayo dṛṣṭaḥ iṣṭatamaḥ; tatra kiṃ putra-janma-mātreṇa puruṣārtha-siddhiḥ; uta tat-jñānam iti vivecanīyam \| sataḥ api ajñātasya arthasya puruṣārthatvena tat jñānam iti cet; tarhi asaty arthe jñānam eva puruṣārtha-siddhyataḥ arthy arthataratvābhāvena prayojana-paryavasāyitā api śāstrasya na artheṣu eva prāmāṇyam \| tasmāt tvaṃ pravṛtti-nivṛtti-paratvena jñāna-paratvena vā prayojana-paryavasāni iti kasyāpi vākyasya pariniṣpanne vastuni tātparya-saṃbhavāt vedāntāḥ pariniṣpannaṃ brahma pratipādayanti \|\|

atra kaścid āha—vedānta-vākyānām api kārya-paratvaṃ brahmaṇi pramāṇa-bhāvam anubhavati \| katham ? niṣprapañcam advitīyaṃ jñānaika-rasaṃ brahma anādi-avidyayā sa-prapañcatayā pratīyamānaṃ niṣprapañcaṃ mumukṣitaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prapañca-vilaya-dvāreṇa vidhi-viṣayatvam iti \| kaḥ asau dṛṣṭa-dvaita-prapañca-vilaya-dvāreṇa sādhanānaikarasa-brahma-viṣayo vidhiḥ ? na dṛṣṭeḥ draṣṭāraṃ paśyer na mateḥ mantāraṃ manvīthā iti-ādiḥ \| dṛṣṭa-dṛśya-rūpa-bhedahuyaṃ dṛṣṭi-mātraṃ brahma kuryād iti arthaḥ \| svataḥ-siddhasyāpi brahmaṇo niṣprapañcatā-rūpeṇa kāryatvam aviruddham iti \|\|

tad ayuktam \| niyogāvadhyarthāditā hi niyogaḥ, niyojya-viśeṣaṇam, viṣayaḥ, karaṇam, itikartavyatā, prayoktā ca vaktavyāḥ \| tatra hi niyojya-viśeṣaṇam upādeyam \| tac ca nimittaṃ phalam iti dvidhā \| atra kiṃ niyojya-viśeṣaṇam, tac ca kiṃ nimittaṃ phalaṃ va iti vivecanīyam \| brahma-svarūpa-yāthātmya-anubhavena niyojya-viśeṣaṇaṃ tarhi na tat-nimittam, jīvāti-vidūratarasiddhatvāt \| nimittaṃ ca tasya nitya-darśana-pakṣe atīta-kālam api jīvan nirmimas tat niṣṭheṣṭādivañcita-tad-dhetu-bhūtān prasañguḥ \| na api phalam, niyogika-phalatvena svargādivad anityatva-prasaṅgāt \|\|

kiṃ ca atra niyoga-viṣayaḥ ? brahma eva cet, tasya nityatvena nityatvāt, abhāva-arthatvāc ca \| niṣprapañcaṃ brahma sādhyam iti cet, sādhyatve api phalattvam eva \| abhāva-arthatvāc ca vidhi-viṣayatvam \| sādhyatvaṃ ca katham ? kiṃ brahmaṇaḥ ? uta prapañca-nivṛtteḥ ? na tāvad brahmaṇaḥ,

siddhāntāt, anityatva-pratiteś ca \| atha prapatti-nivṛttiḥ; na tarhi brahma-upāsādhakatvam \| prapanna-nivṛttiḥ eva vidhi-viṣaya iti cet, tasyāḥ phalatvena vidhi-viṣayatva-ayogāt \| prapatti-nivṛttiḥ eva hi mokṣaḥ, sa ca phalam \| atha ca niyoga-viṣayatve niyogāt prapatti-nivṛttiḥ prapatti-nivṛttyā niyoga iti itara-itara-āśrayatvam \|\|

api ca, kiṃ nivartanīyaḥ prapañcaḥ mithyā-rūpa-satyo vā ? mithyā-rūpatve jñāna-nivartyatvāt eva niyogena na kiñcit prayojanam, niyogaḥ tu nivartaka-jñānam utpādya nivartayet prapañcam iti cet, tat-svabhāvayāt eva jātatvāt niyogena na prayojanam \| vākya-artha-jñānāt eva brahma-vyatiriktasya kṛtsnasya mithyā-bhūtasya prapañcasya bādhitatvāt samparkikasya niyogasya asiddhiś ca \| prapañcasya nivartyatve prapañca-nivartakaḥ niyogaḥ kiṃ brahma-svarūpam eva ? uta tat-savyatiriktaḥ ? yadi brahma-svarūpam eva, nivartakasya nityatayā nivartya-prapañca-sadbhāvaḥ eva na saṃbhavati \| nityatvena niyogasya viṣaya-anugamana-sādhyatvaṃ ca na ghaṭate \| atha brahma-svarūpa-vyatiriktaḥ; tasya kṛtsna-prapañca-nivṛtti-rūpa-viṣaya-anugamana-sādhyatvena prayoktā ca naṣṭaḥ iti āśraya-abhāvāt asiddhiḥ \| prapañca-nivṛtti-rūpa-viṣaya-anugamena eva brahma-svarūpa-vyatiriktasya kṛtsnasya nivṛttatvāc ca niyoga-niṣpādyaṃ mokṣa-ākhyaṃ phalam \|\|

kiñca, prapañca-nivṛtteḥ niyoga-karaṇatva iti-kartavyatā-sādhāna-samucitatvasya ca karaṇatva-ayogāt na karaṇatvam \| katham iti-kartavyatā-abhāva iti cet, iṣyām— adhyaitikartavyatā bhāva-rūpā ? abhāva-rūpā vā ? bhāva-rūpā ca karaṇa-śarīra-niṣpanna-tat-puruṣa-kārya-bheda-bhinnā \| ubhaya-vidhā ca na saṃbhavati \| na hi buddhi-ārūḍha-mithyā-atiriktasya prapañcasya nivartakaḥ kaḥ api dṛśyate iti dṛśya-arthe na saṃbhavati \| na api niṣpannasya karaṇasya kārya-atiśayavat-tpuruṣa-saṃbhavati; anupagrāhaka-jñāna-sadṛśā eva kṛtsna-prapañca-nivṛtti-rūpa-karaṇa-svarūpa-siddheḥ \| brahmoḍhitadīyatva-vijñānaṃ prapañca-nivṛtti-rūpa-karaṇa-śarīraṃ niṣpādayati iti cet, tena eva prapañca-nivṛtti-rūpaḥ mokṣa-siddhaḥ iti na karaṇa-ādi-niṣpādyam aviśiṣyate iti pūrvam eva uktam \| abhāva-rūpatve vā abhāvāt eva na karaṇa-śarīraṃ niṣpādyate \| na api u (grāhakaḥ) grahaḥ \| ataḥ niṣprapañca-viṣayaḥ vidhiḥ na saṃbhavati \|\|

anya-uktyā āha— yady api vedānta-vākyānāṃ na pariniṣpanna-vastu-svarūparatayā prāmāṇyam tathā api brahma-svarūpaṃ siddhyet eva \| kutaḥ ? dhyāna-vidhi-sāmarthyāt \| evam eva hi samāmananti— ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ nididhyāsitavyaḥ yaḥ ātmā apahata-pāpmā saḥ anveṣṭavyaḥ sa vijijñāsitavyaḥ śāntaḥ dāntaḥ uparataḥ titikṣuḥ ātmānam eva lokam upāsīta iti \| atra dhyāna-viṣayaḥ hi niyogaḥ sva-viṣaya-bhūta-atyantaṃ dhyeya-ākāri-rūpeṇa upasthitī dhyeyam apekṣate \| sa ca dhyeyaḥ sva-vākya-nirdiṣṭaḥ ātmā \| sa kiṃ-rūpaḥ iti apekṣāyāṃ tattva-rūpa-viśeṣa-samarpakatvena satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma

tad eva somya idam agre āsīt ity evam-ādīnāṃ vākyānāṃ dhyāna-vidhi-śeṣatayā prāmāṇyam iti vidhi-viṣaya-bhūta-dhyāna-śarīratayā brahma-viṣaya-brahma-svarūpa-aikyaṃ tātparyam asti eva \| ataḥ ekam eva advitīyam tat satyaṃ sa ātmā tat tvam asi śvetaketo neha nānā asti kiṃcana ity-ādibhiḥ brahma-svarūpam ekam eva satyam, tad-anya-tiriktaṃ sarvaṃ mithyā iti avagamyate \| pratyakṣa-ādibhiḥ bheda-avagatiḥ ca karma-sādhanaṃ bhedaḥ pratīyate \| bheda-mithyātve paraspara-virodhe satyānāthavidyā-mūlatvena api bheda-pratīti-upapatteḥ abhedaḥ eva paramārthaḥ iti niścīyate \| tatra brahma-dhyāna-niyojina tat-sākṣātkāra-rūpeṇa nirasta-samasta-avidyā-kṛta-viṣaya-bheda-hitaḥ ayaṃ sākṣād-bhāva-rūpa-upadeśaḥ prāpyate \| na ca vākyāt advaita-yathārtha-jñāna-mātreṇa brahma-bhāva-siddhiḥ; anupalambe, vividha-bheda-darśana-anuvṛtteḥ ca \| tathā sati śravaṇa-ādi-vidhāna-anarthakyaṃ syāt \|\|

atha ucyeta—rajjuḥ eva na sarpa iti upadeśena sarpa-bhaya-nivṛtti-darśanāt, rajjuḥ eva etad bandhasya mithyā-rūpatvena jñāna-bādhyatayā tasya vākya-janya-jñānena eva nivṛtti-yuktā, na niyogena \| niyoga-sādhyatve mokṣasya anityatvaṃ syāt svarga-ādi-vat \| mokṣasya nityatvaṃ hi sarva-vādi-saṃpratipannam \| kiṃ ca dharma-adharmayoḥ phala-hetutvaṃ sva-kāla-anubhava-guṇa-śarīra-upādānatva-āropeṇa iti brahma-vidyā-varānantya-bahu-vighna-śarīra-saṃbandha-rūpa-saṃsāra-eka-phalatva-varjanīyam \| tasmāt ca dharma-nāśaḥ mokṣaḥ \| tathā ca śrutiḥ— na ha vai sa-śarīrasya sataḥ priya-apriyayor apahatiḥ asti, aśarīraṃ vāva santaṃ na priya-apriye spṛśataḥ iti \| aśarīratva-rūpe mokṣe dharma-adharma-sādhya-priya-apriya-vināśa-śravaṇāt na dharma-sādhyam aśarīratvam iti vijñāyate \| na ca niyoga-viśeṣa-sādhya-phala-viśeṣavat dhyāna-niyoga-sādhyam aśarīratvam; aśarīratvasya svarūpeṇa sādhyatvāt \| yathāha śrutayaḥ— aśarīraṃ śarīreṣu anavastheṣu avasthitam \| mahāntaṃ vibhum ātmānaṃ matvā dhīraḥ na śocati \|\| apāṇaḥ hi amanāḥ śubhraḥ ajaḍaḥ hi ayaṃ puruṣaḥ ity-ādyāḥ \| ataḥ aśarīratva-rūpaḥ mokṣaḥ nityaḥ iti na dharma-sādhyaḥ \| tathā ca śrutiḥ— anyatra dharmāt anyatrāt adharmāt anyatrāsmāt kṛta-akṛtāt \| anyatra bhūtāt ca bhavyāt ca yat tat paśyasi tat vada iti \|\|

api ca utpatti-ādi-vikṛti-saṃskṛti-rūpeṇa catur-vidhaṃ hi sādhyatvaṃ mokṣasya na saṃbhavati \| na tāvat utpādyaḥ; mokṣasya brahma-svarūpatvena nityatvāt \| na api prāpyaḥ; ātma-svarūpatvena brahmaṇaḥ nitya-prāptatvāt \| na api vikāryaḥ; dhyāna-ādi-kṛta-nityatvasya bhaṅgatvena \| na api saṃskāryaḥ \| saṃskāraḥ hi doṣa-apanayanena vā guṇa-ādhānena vā sādhyate \| na tāvat doṣa-apanayanena; nitya-śuddhatvāt brahmaṇaḥ \| na api atiśaya-ādhānena; anādheya-atiśaya-svarūpatvāt \| nitarāṃ nirvikāratvena sva-āśrayāyāḥ para-āśrayāyāḥ ca kriyayā aviṣayatvāt na nirbandhena adhyā-ādivat api saṃskāryatvam \| na ca deha-sthayā snāna-ādi-kriyayā ātmā saṃskriyate; kiṃ tu avidyā-gṛhītaḥ taratama-bhāvaḥ ahaṃ-kartā \| tat-phalam upabhoge api tathā eva \| na

vākhya-kṛt-vaiṣamyaṃ tat-saṃkṣiptatvāt \| tathā ca mantra-varṇaḥ— satyo retayaḥ vipascitaṃ svāhā iti anusañcayo abhivyakrānti iti \| ātmana i-dyamāneṣu bhoktṛtvānumane piṣaḥ, ekaḥ devaḥ sarva-bhūteṣu gūḍhaḥ sarva-vyāpī sarva-bhūtāntar-ātmā \| karma-adhyakṣaḥ sarva-bhūtādhivāsaḥ sākṣī cetā kevalaḥ nirguṇaḥ ca \|\| sa paryagāt śukram akāyam avraṇam (sna) sthāviram śuddham apāpa-viddham iti ca avidyā-gṛhīta-anantaḥ-kartṛr-ātma-svarūpam anādhyā-nityāyaṃ nitya-muktaṃ nirvikāraṃ niṣkalpayate \| tasmāt ātma-svarūpatvena na sādhyaḥ mokṣaḥ \|\|

yathā eṣaṃ kiṃ vākyārtha-jñānena kriyate iti cet; mokṣa-pratibandha-nivṛtti-mātram iti brūmaḥ \| tathā ca śrutuḥ— tvaṃ hi naḥ pitā yaḥ asmākam avidyāyāḥ paraṃ pāraṃ tārayasi iti śrutaṃ hi evam eva bhagavad-bodhena tarati śokam ātmavit iti saḥ aham bhāva-rūpaḥ asmi taṃ mā bhagavān śokasya pāraṃ tārayatu tarasye mṛtyum abhayam tasmai paraṃ darśayati bhagavān sanat-kumāraḥ iti-ādayaḥ \| tasmāt vidyā-artha eva mokṣasya pratibandha-nivṛttiḥ vākyārtha-jñānena kriyate \| nivṛttiḥ tu sādhyā api pradhvaṃsābhāva-ākhyā na vinaśyati \| brahma veda brahma eva bhavati tam eva viditvā atimṛtyum eti iti-ādi vacanaṃ mokṣasya vedanā-ananta-sambhavitāṃ pratipādayitum yogya-vyadhānaṃ pratikṣipati \| na ca

vidhi-kriyā-karma-vaṃdevena vā dhyāna-kriyā-karma-vaṃdevena vā kāryān praveśaḥ; ubhaya-vidha-karmatva-pratiṣedhāt— anyad eva tat viditāt atho aviditāt adhi yena idam sarvaṃ vijānāti taṃ kena vijānīyāt iti \| tat eva brahma tvaṃ viddhi na idam yat idam upāsate iti ca \| na ca etāvatā śāstrasya nirviṣayatvam; avidyā-kalpita-bheda-nivṛtti-paratvāt śāstrasya \| na hotavyā brahma viṣayī-karoti iti śāstram; api tu aviṣayaṃ pratyag-ātma-svarūpaṃ pratipādayat avidyā-kalpita-jñānājñeya-vibhāgaṃ nivartayati \| tathā ca śāstram— neti hyoṭhodeśyaṃ paśyati iyam-ādiḥ \| na ca jñānaṃ deva-bandha-nivartir iti śravaṇa-vidhi-artham; svābhāvya-anūtanākṣetarar-vikalpa-vimukhīkṛṇḍatarena vākyārthāvagati-hetutvāt teṣām \|\|

na ca jñānam āśu-bandha-nivṛttiṃ dṛṣṭā iti vācyam; bandhasya mithyā-rūpatvena jñānottara-kālaṃ sthiti-anupapatteḥ \| ata eva na śarīra-pātāt ūrdhvam eva bandha-nivṛttiḥ iti vaktuṃ yuktam \| nahi mithyā-rūpa-bandha-nivṛttau rajju-yāthātmya-jñānātirekeṇa sarpa-vicāra-samapekṣate \| yadi śarīra-saṃbandhaḥ pāramārthikaḥ, tadā hi tat-vināśāpekṣā \| sa tu brahma-paririktatayā na pāramārthikaḥ \| param tu bandhaḥ na nivṛttaḥ; tasya jñānam eva na jātam iti avagantavye, jñāna-kārya-darśanāt \| tasmāt charīra-sthitiḥ bhai-vatu vā mā vā; vākyārtha-jñāna-samanantaraṃ muktaḥ eva asau \| ataḥ na dhyāna-niyoga-sādhyaḥ mokṣaḥ iti na dhyāna-vidhi-śeṣatā brahma-parisiddhiḥ \| api tu satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma anandam asi brahma iti tat-paratayā eva pada-samudāyena siddhyati iti \|\|

tat yuktaṃ, vākya-artha-jñāna-sādhya-bandha-nivṛtti-anupapatteḥ \| yady api mithyā-bhūtaḥ bandhaḥ jñāna-bādhyaḥ \| tathā api bandhasya aparokṣatvāt na parokṣa-rūpeṇa vākya-artha-jñānena sa bādhyate, rajju-ajñāna-parokṣa-sarpatātaḥ vartamānāyāṃ na ayaṃ sarpaḥ rajjuḥ eva iti āpta-upadeśa-janita-parokṣa-sarpa-viparīta-jñāna-mātreṇa bhaya-vinivṛtti-darśanāt; āpta-upadeśasya tu bhaya-vinivṛtti-hetutvaṃ vastu-yāthātmya-aparokṣa-niścita-smṛti-hetutvena \| tathā hi—rajju-sarva-darśana-bhayāt parāvṛttaḥ puruṣaḥ na ayaṃ sarpaḥ rajjuḥ eva iti āpta-upadeśāt, na tatra stu-pa-yāthātmya-darśane pravarttaḥ ta eva pratyakṣeṇa dṛṣṭvā bhayāt nivarttate \| na ca śabdaḥ eva pratyakṣa-jñānaṃ janayati iti vaktuṃ yuktam, tatra vyāpti-nirṇaya-abhāvāt \| jñāna-sāmagrī-viśiṣṭriyā ca eva hi aparokṣā avabhāti \| na vākya-anatibhisaṃhita-phala-karma-upāsana-pūrvitakāpy asya śravaṇa-manana-nididhyāsanaiḥ vigalita-anājñā-viṣayasya puruṣasya vākyena eva aparokṣa-jñānaṃ janyati, niścita-pratibandhe tatpare api puṃsi jñāna-sāmagrī-viśeṣaṇa-āgam-indriya-adarśano sva-viṣaya-niyama-atikrama-adarśanena tat-yogāt \|\|

na ca dhyānasya vākya-artha-jñāne apekṣatā, itaretara-āśrayatvāt—vākya-artha-jñāne jāte tat-viṣaya-dhyānasya, dhyāne nirvṛtte vākya-artha-jñānam iti \| na ca dhyāna-vākya-artha-jñānayoḥ bhinna-viṣayatvam, tathā sati dhyānasya vākya-artha-jñāne apekṣatā na syāt \| na hi anya-dhyānam anya-anugraham upapādayati \| jñāta-artha-smṛti-santati-rūpasya dhyānasya vākya-artha-jñāna-pūrvakatva-vacanena asya, dhyeya-brahma-viṣaya-jñānasya hetu-anapekṣā-śaṅkā-abhāvāt \| na ca dhyāna-mūlaṃ jñānaṃ vākya-artha-rūpam, vividatkaṃ jñānaṃ tattvam asi-ādi-vākya-janyaṃ miti yuktam \| dhyāna-mūla-siddhaṃ vākya-anta-rajjukaṃ jñānaṃ tattvam asi-ādi-vākya-janya-jñānatvena eka-viṣayam, bhinna-viṣayaṃ vā? eka-viṣayatve tat eva itaretara-āśrayatvam \| bhinna-viṣayatve dhyānena tat-īna-mukhya-vākyatā-saṃbhavaḥ \| kiñca dhyānasya dhyeya-dhyātṛ-dhyāna-eka-rūpa-brahma-apekṣatvāt niṣprapañca-brahma-ātmakatva-viṣaya-vākya-artha-jñāna-uttaratno haṭha-dāreṇa mokṣaḥ ayaṃ iti vākya-artha-jñāna-mātrāt avidyā-nivṛttiṃ vadatāḥ śravaṇa-manana-nididhyāsana-vidhāna-samanvayam eva \|\|

yataḥ vākyāt aparokṣa-jñāna-saṃbhavāt adhyartha-jñānena avidyā na nivarttate; tataḥ eva jīvan-muktiḥ api dūra-utsāritā \| kā ca iyaṃ jīvan-muktiḥ? sa-śarīrasya eva mokṣaḥ iti cet, mātu me vandhyā iti vadana-saṅkātārthaṃ vacaḥ; yat sa-śarīratvaṃ bandhaḥ; aśarīratvam eva mokṣaḥ iti tvayā eva śruti-nirūpaṇāt \| atha sa-śarīratva-vibhāve vartamāne yasya ayaṃ pratibhāsaḥ mithyā iti pratyayaḥ, tasya sa-śarīratva-nivṛttiḥ iti \| na, mithyā iti pratyayena sa-śarīratvaṃ nivṛttaṃ cet, kathaṃ sa-śarīrasya muktiḥ? ajīve ato jñi muktiḥ sa-śarīratva-mithyā-pratibhāsa-nivṛttiḥ eva iti kaḥ ayaṃ jīvan-muktiḥ iti viśeṣaḥ? atha sa-śarīratva-pratibhāsaḥ bādhitaḥ api yasya hiñcat-jñānavat anuvarttate, sa jīvan-muktaḥ iti cet; na, brahmaṇi atirikta-sakala-vastū-viṣaya-bādhaka-jñānasya kāraṇa-bhūta-avidyā-karma-ādi-doṣa-sa-śarīratva-pratibhāsena saha tena eva bādhita iti bādhita-anuvṛttiḥ na śakyate vaktum \|

hita-antatvād tu tat-pratibhāsa-ahetu-bhūta-jñāna-bādhakatva-ajñāna-aviṣayatvena abādhitatvāt, hita-candra-apratimā-jñātṛ-niṣṭhatvāt \| kiñca śruteḥ eva tāvat eva ciraṃ yāvat na vimokṣye atha sampatsye, iti sahakāri-niṣṭhasya śarīra-pāta-mātra-sapekṣatve mokṣa iti vadantī iyaṃ śrutiḥ jīvan-muktiṃ vārayati \| saṃpadā jīvan-muktiḥ āśaṅkyate cet na api nirastā—veda an imaṃ lokam amum ca parityajya ātmānam anvicchet \| buddhe kṣema-prāpaṇe tat-dhāna-aiśvarya-prativiruddham \| buddheś ca kṣema-prāpaṇam āha eva na duḥkham upalabheta \| etena paraṃ vyākhyātam iti \| etena jñāna-mātra-mokṣaś ca nirastaḥ \| avat-sakala-veda-nivṛtti-rūpā muktir jīvato na saṃbhavati \|\|

tasmād upāsanā-niṣṭhena brahma-aparokṣa-jñānam ekā eva bandha-nivṛttiḥ \| na ca niyoga-asādhyatve mokṣasya anityatva-prasaṅktiḥ; pratibandha-nivṛtti-mātrasya eva sādhyatvāt \| kiñca—na niyogena sākṣāt bandha-nivṛttiḥ kriyate, kintu niṣprapañca-jñāna-eka-rasa-brahma-aparokṣyatānena \| niyogaḥ tu tat-aparokṣya-jñānaṃ janayati \| kathaṃ niyogasya jñāna-utpatti-hetutvam iti cet, kathaṃ vā bhavataḥ agni-sṛṣṭi-huta-kalānāṃ karmaṇāṃ vedana-utpatti-hetutvam? nirmalya-dvāra iti cet, mama api tathaiva \| mama tu nirmale manasi śāstreṇa jñānam utpadyate, tava tu niyogena manasi nirmale jñāna-sāmagrī kartavyeti cet, dhyāna-niyogāt nirmalaṃ mana eva sādhanam iti brūmaḥ \| kena avagamyata iti cet; bhavati vā karma-anirmalena nirmalaṃ bhavati, nirmale manasi śravaṇa-manana-nididhyāsana-saṃskṛta-itara-viṣaya-vimukhasya eva śāstraṃ nirvartaka-jñānam utpādayatīti kena avagamyate? nirvidya vidvān yas tena dānena tapasā anāśakena śrotriyo mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ brahma veda brahma eva bhavati—iti-ādi-śāstraiḥ iti cet, mama api śrotriyo mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ brahmavid āpnoti param na cakṣuṣā gṛhyate na api vācā manasā tu viśuddhena hṛdā manīṣā manasābhikḷptaḥ iti-ādi-śāstraiḥ dhyāna-niyogena mano nirmalaṃ bhavati, nirmalaṃ ca mano brahma-aparokṣa-jñānaṃ janayatīti avagamyate—iti niravadyam \|\|

na idaṃ yat idam upāsate iti upāsyatvaṃ pratiṣiddham iti cet, na evam; na atra brahmaṇa upāsyatvaṃ pratiṣidhyate; api tu brahmaṇo jagad-dhetutvaṃ pratipādyate \| yat idaṃ jagad upāsate prāṇinaḥ; na etad brahma; tat eva brahma tvaṃ viddhi, yat ācārya-mukhād vedyaṃ yena vāg abhyudyate—iti vākyārthaḥ \| anyathā tat eva brahma tvaṃ viddhi iti virudhyate \| dhyāna-vidhi-vaiyarthyaṃ ca syāt \| ato brahma-sākṣātkāra-ekena dhyāna-niyogena eva aparamārtha-bhūtasya kartṛtvasya dṛṣṭa-dṛśyatva-rūpa-svarūpa-bandhasya nivṛttiḥ \|\|

yady api kañcit kumāraṃ—maitreyo maitryā vā rodho na vidyata iti; tat ayuktam \| na hi śarīroṣṇa-tamaḥ-prakāśādī va-dūṣābhiveka-nimna-tarutūni sraṅgacchate \| atha ucyet—sarvaṃ hi vastu-jātaṃ pratīti-vyavasthāpyam \| sarvaṃ ca siddhābhāsam pratīyate—kāraṇa-ātmanā jāti-ātmanā ca abhinnam, kārya-ātmanā vyakta-ātmanā ca bhinnam \| chāyātapatvad itu virodha-sahān avasthāna-lakṣaṇo bhinnādhārakaḥ paraḥ \|

kāryākārayyoḥ jātīya-vṛttyor tathā-bhūyam api na upapadyate \| pratyuta ekaiva vastu dvi-rūpaṃ pratīyeta; yathā ‘mṛdā ghaṭaḥ’ ‘khaṇḍo gauḥ’ ‘muṇḍo gauḥ’ iti \| na ca ekatvaṃ kiñcit api vastu loke dṛṣṭa-caram \| na ca dvi-guṇādi-vailakṣaṇya-viśeṣaḥ bheda-pramarśī hṛyate iti na vastu-virodhaḥ; mṛt-suvarṇa-gava-aśvādi-ātmanā avasthitasya eva ghaṭa-mukuṭa-khaṇḍa-badvādi-ātmanā ca avasthānāt \| na ca abhinnasya bhinnasya ca vastunaḥ abhedaḥ bhedaḥ ca ekaḥ eva ākāraḥ iti īkṣa-śaṅkā \| pratītitvādi-varūpyaṃ hetu, pratītitvāt eva bhinna-abhinnatvam iti daṇḍam apaśyu upanayanam \| na hi visphārita-akṣaḥ puruṣaḥ ghaṭa-śarāva-kaṇḍa-muṇḍādiṣu vastu-punar-mṛd-gamātrāsu ‘idaṃ mṛt, ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ; idaṃ gotvam, iyaṃ vyaktiḥ’ iti vivekaṃ śaknoti \| api tu ‘mṛdā ghaṭaḥ; khaṇḍo gauḥ’ iti eva prateti \| anubhūti-śuddhyā evaṃ kāraṇa-agrahaṇam iti mithyā, vyāvṛtti-buddhyā iti etat kāryaṃ vyaktim iti vivinakti iti cet, na evam vivikta-ākāra-anupalabdheḥ \| na hi sudūkṣmam api nirīkṣamāṇaḥ idam anubudhnīmaḥ, idaṃ ca vyāvartamānabhiti puraḥ avasthite vastuni ākāraṃ ca upalabhyate \| yathā saṃsthita-rūpaṃ yat kāryaṃ viśeṣeṇa caitalya-buddhyā apajāyate; tathā eva saṃkaraṇe sati abhimanye ca ekatva-buddhiḥ aviśeṣa-prajāyate \| evam eva deśataḥ kālataḥ ākāratā-āśraya-atyanta-vilakṣaṇeṣu api vastusu tat eva iti pratyabhijñā jāyate \| ataḥ dṛśyatmakam eva vastu pratīyeta iti kāryākārayoḥ jātīya-vṛttyoḥ ekatvena ubhaya-āyattane bhāṇa-upapatteḥ pratīti-virāhatvam \|\|

athoceta—mṛdā ghaṭaḥ khaṇḍo gauḥ iti vat devaḥ ahaṃ manuṣyaḥ aham iti sāmānādhikaraṇyena pratītiḥ ātma-śarīrayoḥ api bhinna-abhinnatvaṃ syāt; ataḥ idaṃ bheda-abheda-upapādanaṃ nija-sadanirhita-kuhavad-vapy-ālāyavat iti; tat idam anākalita-bhinna-bheda-sādhāraṇa-sāmānādhikaraṇya-tattva-yāthātmya-avabodha-vilasitam \| tathā hi—abādhitaḥ eva pratyayaḥ sarvatra arthaṃ vyavasthāpayati \| dehādi-ātma-abhimānaḥ tu ātma-yāthātmya-gocaraiḥ sarvaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ bādhiyamānaḥ rajjuṣu abhidhukṣu abharasarīropaham eva sādhyati khaṇḍo gauḥ, muṇḍo gauḥ iti sāmānādhikaraṇyasya na kenacit vastu-indu-bādhā hṛyate \| tasmāt ativispaṣṭaḥ \|\|

ataḥ eva jaiva-īśoḥ api brahmaṇaḥ na atyanta-bhinnaḥ \| api tu brahma-ātmakatvena bhinna-abhinnaḥ \| tatra abhedaḥ eva svābhāvikaḥ; bhedaḥ tu aupādhikaḥ \| katham avagamyate iti cet; tat tvam asi satyoḍaḥ asmi brahma ayam ātmā brahma iti ādiḥ abhyupasmṛtibhiḥ; brahma eva dhyātavya-pṛthivī iti prakṛtya brahma-dāśā brahma-dāsā brahma ime kitavā uta \| śrīpuṃś ca brahmaṇaḥ jātaḥ striyaḥ brahmonta vā pumān \|\| iti ārtha-abhijñānāṃ saṃhitā-upaniṣadi bāhulyena abheda-śravaṇāt ca \| nityaḥ nityānāṃ cetanaḥ cetanānām eko bahūnāṃ yaḥ vidadhāti kāmān jña-ajñau dvau ajau īśa-anīśau kṣaram pradhānam amṛta-akṣaraṃ haraḥ teṣāṃ saṃyoga-hetuḥ aparaḥ api dṛṣṭaḥ. pradhāna-kṣetrajña-patiḥ guṇa-īśaḥ saṃsāra-mokṣa-sthiti-bandha-hetuḥ sa kāraṇaṃ karaṇa-adhipa-adhipaḥ tayoḥ anyaḥ pippalaṃ svādu atti anaśnan anyaḥ abhicākaśīti yaḥ ātmani

viguṇaṃ ca prajñena ātmanā sampariṣvakto na bāhyaṃ kiñcana veda... prājñena ātmanā anvārūḍhaḥ utsṛjati ityādi prajāna eva vivikta-ātmatayā anugamīti ityādi-nirbheda-śravaṇāt ca, jīva-pratyabhijñā-bheda-yavasthā-praṇāśe \| tatra brahma veda brahma eva bhavati ityādi-nimokṣa-daśāyāṃ jīvasya brahma-svarūpa-pratipattaya-deśāt, atra tu asya sarvātmābhūtakena kaṃ paśyet iti tadānīṃ bheda-īśvara-darśana-niṣedhāt ca bheda-svabhāvikaḥ iti ucyate \|\|

nanu ca brahma-putre sarvadā kāmāt saha brahmaṇā vipariṣvajyate iti suṣuptau tava tānīṃ bhedaḥ pratīyate \| vakṣyati ca prajāghāparvajaṃ prakaraṇāt asminn iti tattva-abhedaḥ bhoga-mātra-śamya-liṅgāt ca iti \| na evaṃ vastu anyaḥ asā anyasmin dṛṣṭe api ādi-śruti-virodhāt tara-abheda-pratiṣedhāt \| saḥ aśnute sarvān kāmān saha brahmaṇā vipaściteti sarva-kāmā-sahaṃ brahma-sukteḥ—sarva-guṇa-anvitaṃ brahma-āsuktaḥ iti uktaṃ bhavati \| anyathā brahmaṇā saha iti ādhāre brahmaṇaḥ prasajyeta \| prajāghāparvajasmitratra muktasya bhedānuvṛttyate sati arthasya nyūnatā-śaṅkāṃ vakṣyate; anyathā saṃkhyā-viśiṣṭa-saṃvedana-śabdatva ityādi-nivirodhāt \| tasmāt bheda eva svābhāvikaḥ \| bhedaḥ tu jīvānāṃ parasparāt brahmaṇaḥ, parasparaṃ ca buddhi-ādi-pradeha-upādhi-kṛtaḥ \| yadi api brahma niravayavaṃ sarvagataṃ ca; tathā api ākāśa iva ghaṭādīnāṃ buddhi-ādi-upādhinā brahmaṇi api bheda-saṃbhavatā eva \|\|

na ca bhinne brahmaṇi buddhi-ādi-upādhi-saṃyogaḥ; buddhi-ādi-upādhi-saṃyogāt brahmaṇi bhedaḥ iti itaretara-āśrayatvam; upādheḥ tat-saṃyogasya ca kṛtrimatvāt, tattva-avāhasya ca anāditvāt \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—pūrva-karma-saṃbaddha-jñānāt svataṃbaddha eva upādhiḥ upadyate, tayuktam karmā \| evaṃ bīja-aṅkura-nyāyena karma-upādhi-saṃbandhayoḥ anāditvāt na doṣaḥ—iti \| ataḥ jīvānāṃ parasparaṃ brahmaṇā ca abheda eva svābhāvikaḥ \| bhedaḥ tu aupādhikaḥ \| upādhīnāṃ punaḥ parasparaṃ brahmaṇā ca abhedavat bhedaḥ api svābhāvikaḥ, upādhīnām anupādhi-antara-ārabhāvāt, tat-svabhāva-bheda-anabasthātā ca \| ataḥ jīvaka-nigūḍhaṃ brahmaṇi bhinna-abhinna-rūpāvau eva upādhayaḥ upapadyante iti \|\|

atra ucyate—advitīya-saṃdhita-ānanda-brahma-dhyāna-vidhi-adhikaraṃ vedānta-vākya-jātam iti vedānta-vākyaiḥ bhedaḥ pratīyate \| bheda-varcibhiḥ karma-śāstraiḥ pratyabhāva-dhṛtyā eva bhedaḥ pratīyate \| bheda-abhedayoḥ paraspara-virodhāt anādi-avidyā-mūlatayā api bheda-pratīti-upapannatara-bheda eva paramārthaḥ iti uktam \| tatra yat uktaṃ bhetābhedayoḥ sthyoḥ api pratīti-siddhavān na virodha iti \| tat ayuktam, karma-adhikṛta-vyatirikta-pāvakaṃ hi tasmāt tasya bhedaḥ \| tat itaretaratvaṃ vā bhedaḥ \| tayoḥ sthābhāva-tathābhāva-rūpayoḥ ekatra saṃbhavam anuttaraḥ kaḥ bravīti ? kāraṇa-ātmanā jāti-ātmanā vā abhedaḥ kārya-ātmanā vyakti-ātmanā ca bhedaḥ—iti ākāra-īkṣeta-virodhaḥ iti cet; vikalpa-asahatvāt \| ākāra-bhedāt avirodha vadat: kim ekasmin ākāre bhedaḥ, ākāra-antare ca abhedaḥ iti abhiprāyaḥ ? uttama-ākāram

yogi-vahu-jātīya-bhāva-āpattiḥ ? pūrvasmin kalpe vyakti-jātaḥ bhedaḥ, jāti-vāśra-abhedaḥ iti na aikyaṃ hi āmakaṭā \| jāti-vyaktiḥ iti ca ekam eva vastu iti cet, tad-vikāra-bhedāt eva avirodhaḥ pariṇāntara-sthān \| ekasmin vilakṣaṇa-tadvahi-yogaḥ viśiṣṭanīyuktam \| dvitīye tu kalpe prati-anya-vilakṣaṇa-ākāra-rūpa-sanniviṣṭaṃ ca tad-āśraya-bhūtaṃ vastu iti(tathā)citra-paṭa-nyāyena api pratyagān-anyonyavailakṣaṇyam eva upapattitaṃ syāt; na punaḥ bhedaḥ \| ākāra-dvaya-niṣṭha-prāṇa-virodhe tad-āśraya-bhūte vastuni bhinna-abhinnatvam iti cetaḥ; svasmāt dhi lakṣaṇaṃ sva-āśrayam ākāra-dvayaṃ svasmin icchada-dharma-dvaya-sāmādeśa-nirvāhakaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ? avilakṣaṇaṃ tu kathaṃ vā syāt ? ākāra-dvaya-tattvoḥ ca hi āmakaṭāni-upagame nirvāhika-antara-apekṣā-anavasthā syāt \| na ca saṃpratipanneṣu api vastuṣu eṣu ekatra pratiniṣedham āyate \| yataḥ idānīm vyakti sarvatra prakāra-vakrityā eva sarvā pratītiḥ \| tatra prakāra-aṃśaḥ jātiḥ, prakāri-aṃśaḥ vyaktiḥ iti na eka-ākārā pratītiḥ \| ataḥ eva jīvasya api brahmaṇaḥ bhinna-abhinnatvaṃ na saṃbhavati \| tasmin abhedasya ananyathāsiddha-mūlatvāt ācārya-vidyā-mūlaḥ eva bheda-pratyayaḥ \| tattve evam brahmaṇaḥ ekatvaṃ, tad-mūlāḥ ca janma-sarā-maraṇa-ādayaḥ doṣāḥ prāduṣyuḥ \| tataḥ ca yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit eṣaḥ ātmā apahata-pāpmā ityādīni śāstrāṇi bādhyeran \| na evam, ajñāna-ādi-doṣāṇām avaratha-arthatvāt \| bhavataḥ tu upādhi-brahma-vyatiriktaṃ vastu-antara-samabhyupagacchataḥ brahma-upayoga-adhisaṃgṛhīta-kutarkāḥ ca jīvatva-śāstra-vādyaḥ doṣāḥ paramārthataḥ eva bhaveyuḥ \| na hi brahmaṇi niravaye acchedye śuddha-ciyamānā upādhyaḥ acchidyā bhicā vā saṃbadhyate \| api tu brahma-svarūpe saṃyuñjya tasmāt na eva sva-ākāreṇa muñcati \|\|

yadi manyeta—upādhi-upahitaṃ brahma jīvaḥ \| sa ca aṇuḥ parimāṇaḥ \| aṇutvaṃ ca avacchedakatvam anatilaṅghanāt \| sa ca avacchedaḥ jñāna-ādiḥ \| evam upādhīyihate’pi saṃdehamānāḥ doṣāḥ anugṛhīte pare brahmaṇi na saṃbadhyante—iti \| ayaṃ praśnaḥ—kiṃ upādhinā chinnaḥ brahma-khaṇḍaḥ aṇu-rūpaḥ jīvaḥ ? uta acchinnam eva aṇu-rūpa-upādhi-saṃyuktaḥ brahma-pradeśa-viśeṣaḥ ? uta upādhi-saṃyuktaṃ brahma-svarūpam ? atha upādhi-saṃyuktaṃ caitanya-antaram ? atha upādhiḥ eva ? iti \| acchedyatvāt brahmaṇaḥ prathamaḥ kalpaḥ na hṛdyate \| ādimān ca jīvasya syāt \| ekasya sataḥ dvaidhīkaraṇaṃ hi chedanam \| dvitīye tu kalpe brahmaṇaḥ eva pradeśa-viśeṣe upādhi-saṃbandhāt doṣa-adhikāra-sarvaṃ doṣāḥ tatra eva syuḥ \| upādhiḥ ca syāt \| ākāśe ca acchinnatvāt kṛtsnasya brahmaṇaḥ ākārṣaṇaṃ syāt \| niraṃśasya vyāpinaḥ ākārṣaṇaṃ na saṃbhavati iti cetaḥ; tarhi upādhiḥ eva gacchati iti pūrva-uktaḥ eva śeṣaḥ syāt \| acchinna-brahma-pradeśaḥ sarva-upādhi-saṃsargaḥ sarveṣāṃ ca jīvānāṃ brahmaṇaḥ eva pradeśatva-ekivena pratisaṃdhānaṃ syāt \| pradeśa-bhedāt avitisandhāne ca ekasya api sva-upādhi gacchati pratisaṃdhānaṃ na syāt \| tṛtīye tu kalpe brahma-svarūpasya eva upādhi-saṃbandhena jīvatva-āpatteḥ tarhiti-

riktatvād iti abhyupasiddheḥ syāt \| sarvaṃ ca deha eka eva jīvaḥ syāt \| turīye tu kakṣe brahmaṇo 'nya eva jīva iti jīva-bhedasya aupādhikatve parityaktaṃ syāt \| carame vādi-kalpa eva pariśuddhiḥ syāt \| tasmāj jñeyā-śabdena kṛtsnasya bhedyā-vidyā-mūlatvam eva abhyupagantavyam \| ataḥ pravṛtti-nivṛtti-prayojana-paratvaṃ ca śāstrasya prāmāṇye 'pi dhyānādi-vidhi-śeṣatayā vedānta-vākyānāṃ brahma-svarūpe prāmāṇyam upapadyam iti \|\|

tad etad uktaṃ—dhyānādi-vidhi-śeṣatve 'pi vedānta-vākyānām artha-satyatve prāmāṇya-ayogāt \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—brahma-svarūpa-gocarāṇi vākyāni kiṃ dhyāna-vidhi-śeṣa-vākyatām āpannāni brahma-svarūpe prāmāṇyaṃ pratipadyante; uta svatantrāṇi eva ? eka-vākyatve dhyāna-vidhi-paratvena brahma-svarūpe tātparyaṃ na saṃbhavati \| bhinna-vākyatve pravṛtti-nivṛtti-prayojana-virahād anvaya-bodhakatvam eva \| na ca vācyam—dhyānaṃ nāma smṛti-saṃtati-rūpam \| tac ca smartavya-ākāra-nirūpaṇāya armiti dhyāna-vidheḥ smartavya-viśeṣa-ākāṅkṣāyām idaṃ sarvaṃ yad ayam ātmā brahma sarvānubhūḥ; satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti ādīni brahma-svarūpa-tad-viśeṣa-parāṇi samarpayanti iti \| teṣāṃ ca vākyatām āpannāni evaṃ-sūtrāve prāmāṇyam iti \| dhyāna-vidheḥ smartavya-viśeṣāpekṣatve 'pi nāma brahma iti ādi-vidhi-vidheya-stuti-etātparya-viśeṣeṇa dhyāna-vidher upapattiḥ; dhyeya-tattva-anveṣaṇāt \| ato vedānta-vākyānāṃ pravṛtti-nivṛtti-prayojana-vidhura-brahma-jñāna-vidhi-śeṣatve 'pi dhyeya-viśeṣatva-rūpa-samarpaṇa-mātra-paryavasānāt, svātantrye 'pi bāla-turaguccha-candra-na-vākyavat jñāna-mātreṇa eva puruṣārtha-paryantatā-siddheś ca pariśiṣṭa-na-vastu-satyatā-gocaratvābhāvāt brahmaṇi śāstra-pramāṇakatvaṃ na saṃbhavati iti prāptam \|\|

tatra pratividhatte—tat tu samanvayāt iti \| samanvayaḥ—samyag-anvayaḥ; puruṣārtha-prayojanavad dravyaḥ \| parama-puruṣārtha-bhūtasya anta-vidhika-niśreyasa-ānanda-svarūpasya brahmaṇo 'dhigatasya aḍravāc, tac-chāstra-pramāṇakaṃ siddhyati eva iti arthaḥ \| nirasta-nikhila-doṣa-niratīśaya-ānanda-svarūpatayā paramātmā iyaṃ brahma bodhyam iti etaj-jñāna-avakaraṇaḥ pravṛtti-nivṛtti-nirasta-avicchinnāna prayojana-paryavasāyīti bruvāṇe rāja-kula-vāsinaḥ puruṣasya kaileyaka-kula-anantra-veśena prayojanam ucyatāṃ śrute \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—anādi-karma-rūpa-avidyā-veṣṭita-rohitāpara-vastu-tathā-rasyasvarūpāvabodhānāṃ deva-manuṣyādi-asiddha-vidyādhar-kinnara-kiṃpuruṣa-yakṣa-rākṣasa-viśāna-manusya-paśu-mṛga-kīṭa-sarīsṛpa-vṛkṣa-gulma-latā-yūthavīrṇoṃ strī-puṃ-napuṃsaka-bheda-bhinnānāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ vyavasthita-cara-sthāvara-rūpaika-bhogya-viśeṣāṇāṃ duḥkhatānāṃ svasya ca viśeṣān umubhavatve svarūpa-bahu-guṇa-vibhava-ceṣṭitatva-adhikāritrāya-ānanda-jananaṃ paraṃ brahma asti iti bodhyate eva vākyaṃ prayojana-paryavasāyi \| pravṛtti-nivṛtti-vidhis tu yāvad-upāyārthitva-bodhena prayojana-paryavasāyi \|\|

evaṃ-bhūtaṃ paraṃ brahma kathaṃ prāpyata iti apekṣāyāṃ brahmavid āpnoti iti param ātmānam eva lokam upāsīta iti vedanā-nididhyāsana-śabdārtha-rūpāsanaṃ brahma-prāpty-upāyatvayā vidhīyate \| yathā svaveśmani

nirdhūtatviti vākyena nirdhūta-sūtrāṃ jñātvā vṛtta-samū prajānāṃ ca pravadate \| yathā ca kiścit rājakuṃāro bāla-kiḍā-sakto narendra-bhavanāt niṣkānto mārga-dṛṣṭo naṣṭa iti rājñā vijñātaḥ svayaṃ ca ajñāta-pitṛkaḥ kenacit vṛddhena āryaṇa vardhitaḥ adhīta-veda-aśeṣa-śāstra-artha-saṃskalyāṇa-guṇa-ākarṣi-śabda ‘pitā te sarva-loka-adhipatiḥ; gāmbhīrya-āryatva-saukumārya-aucitya-parākrama-āditu guṇa-putraḥ tvam eva naṣṭaḥ putra dṛṣṭaḥ; putratve niściti’ iti kenacit viniyuktena prabhuktaṃ vākyaṃ śṛṇoti iti cet; tadānīm eva ahaṃ tāvat jīvataḥ putraḥ; mandiṣṭā ca sarva-saṃpadamudra iti niratisādhāraṇaḥ samunnito bhavati \| rājā ca sva-putraṃ jīvan-maraṇa-bhīti-manohara-darśanaṃ vividha-tatkāla-vedyaṃ śrutvā ham āsamasta-puruṣārthī bhavati \| prajā-anupadāne ca pravadate \| prajānāṃ sañjñuccheda ca iti \|\|

yatunaḥ pariniṣpanna-vastu-gocārasya vākyasya taj-jñāna-mātreṇa api puruṣārtha-paryavasānāt bāla-ātura-ādi-ucchedanana-vākya-vārtha-śrutau prāpta-artham iti, tasmāt \| artha-sūtra-abhāve nirviśe, jñāta-uṣṭyarthaḥ puruṣārthaṃ na bhavati \| bāla-ātura-rāja-amṛta-artha-sañjñānyā harṣa-viṣayatāḥ \| teṣām eva tasminn eva jñāne vidyamāne yathā-abhimata-viṣayo jāyeta tataḥ tadānīm eva harṣa-vidyo nivarteta \| aupaniṣadeṣu api vākyebhyaḥ brahma-astitva-tat-svabhāva-niścaye brahma-jñāne sati api puruṣārtha-paryavasānaṃ na syāt \| ataḥ yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante iti ādi-vākyaṃ nikhila-jagad-eka-kāraṇaṃ

nirasta-nikhila-doṣa-gandhaṃ sārvajña-satya-saṅkalpatva-ananta-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākaraṃ anavadhika-atiśaya-ānandaṃ brahma asti iti bodhayati iti siddham \|\|4\|\| iti samanvaya-adhikaraṇam \|\|4\|\|

**atha īkṣati-adhikaraṇam, 5**

## \|\| 1.1.5 \|\|

*īkṣateḥ na aśabdam*

**Комментарий:**

yato vā imāni iti ādi-jagat-kāraṇa-vādi-vākyam avivādam apratīta-arthaṃ sarvajñaṃ sarva-śakti samasta-heya-pratyanīka-kalyāṇa-guṇa-eka-bhājanaṃ brahma jijñāsyam iti uktam \| idānīṃ jagat-kāraṇa-vādi-vākyānām ānu-bhāvika-pradhāna-ādi-śruti-paratānāṃ hānopāye—īkṣateḥ na aśabdam iti adhikaṃ \|\| idam āmnāyate chāndogye—sat eva somya idam agre āsīt ekam eva advitīyam, tat aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tat tejaḥ asṛjata iti ādi \| tatra sandehaḥ—kiṃ sat-śabda-vācyaṃ jagat-kāraṇaṃ para-ukta-mānumānika-pradhānam, uta ukta-lakṣaṇaṃ brahma iti \| kiṃ prāptam ? pradhānam iti \| kutaḥ ? sat eva somya idam agre āsīt ekam eva advitīyaṃ śabda-vācyasya cetana-acetana-bhūtasya sarvasya jagataḥ tadātmyaṃ ‘vipattidana-aharūpa-vikāra-vācyasya vastunaḥ kāraṇa-avasthāṃ vadati \| kāraṇa-bhūta-dravya-avasthāntara-āpattiḥ eva hi kāryatā \| ataḥ yat dravyaṃ yatva-svabhāvaṃ ca kārya-nirūpyaṃ tat-svabhāvaṃ tat eva dravyaṃ kāraṇa-avastham \| sattva-ādi-sthā ca kāryam iti guṇa-sāmya-avasthaṃ pradhānam eva hi kāraṇam \| taddeṣa-sahita-tat-sakala-viśeṣa

samāna-āśritiḥ na eva sauṣye deśe āmodita-īkṣā iva udita-upniṣad-vidheyatve \| tataḥ eva ca kārya-kāraṇayoḥ ananyatvam \| tathā sati eva kavidānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñā-upapattiḥ \| anyathā yathā sauṣyeṇa mṛt-piṇḍena vyāddṛṣṭi-phalaka-āṣṭhādi-ananta-vastu-nirṇaya-upadeśatvaṃ ca iti jāḍya-kāraṇa-vādi-vākyena mahīṣaṇā kṛtinā etat pradhānam eva pratipādyate pratijñā-dṛṣṭānta-rūpeṇa anumānena veda-vākyaṃ svārthiti sacchabda-vācyam ānumānikam eva \|\|

iti evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate—īkṣateḥ na śabdam iti \| yasmin śabde eva pramāṇaṃ na bhavati tat aśabdam, ānumānikaṃ pradhānam iti arthaḥ \| na tat jagat-kāraṇa-vādi-vākya-pratipādyam; kutaḥ? īkṣateḥ—sacchabda-vācya-saṃbandhitayā apara-viśeṣa-abhidhāyini īkṣateḥ hetoḥ iti śravaṇāt na iti aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti \| īkṣaṇa-kriyā-yogyaḥ caitanaḥ pradhāne na saṃbhavati \| ataḥ īdṛkṣaṇa-lakṣaṇa-cetana-viśeṣa-sarvaiḥ śabda-śaktiḥ puruṣottama-sacchabda-artha-viṣayaḥ \| tathā ca, sarveṣu eva sṛṣṭi-prakaraṇeṣu īkṣā-pūrviṇā eva sṛṣṭiḥ pratīyate saḥ īkṣata lokān nu sṛjā iti saḥ imān lokān asṛjata saḥ īkṣāñcakre…saḥ prāṇam asṛjata ityādiṣu \| laghu ca kārya-anuguṇā eva kāraṇe bhavitavyam \| satyam; sarva-kārya-anuguṇaḥ eva sarvaiḥ śabda-śaktiḥ satya-saṅkalpaḥ puruṣottamaḥ sūkṣma-vicit-vicit-vastu-śarīrakaḥ \| yathā āha parāsya śaktiḥ vividhā eva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca ayam ātmā sarveśvaraḥ sarvavit yasya jñānamayaṃ tapaḥ tasmāt etat brahma nāma rūpam annaṃ ca jāyate yat akṣaraṃ śarīre yasya mṛtyuḥ śarīram…eṣaḥ sarva-bhūta-antarātmā iti \|

tat etat—na vilakṣaṇatvāt ityādiṣu prativādibhiḥ ucyate \| atra sūkṣma-avasthāni na pradhāna-pratipādana-yogyāni iti ucyate \| vastu-virodhaḥ tu tatra eva parihariṣyate \| yuktaḥ tu pratijñā-dṛṣṭānta-yogāt anumāna-rūpā iva idaṃ vākyaṃ iti, tat asat; hetu-upādānāt \| śruta-anugṛhīta-śruti-smṛtikaḥ ca vijñānena sarva-vijñāne pratipipādayiṣite sarvāsām na tat-saṃbhave mantavyasya tat-saṃbhava-mātra-darśanāya hi dṛṣṭānta-upādānam \| īkṣati-adhika-praṇād eva hi anumānāni atha āśaṅkā-anudyogāt \|\|5\|\|

atha syāt—na cetanatvaṃ mukhyam īkṣaṇam iha ucyate, api tu pradhāna-gataṃ gauṇam īkṣaṇam, tat tejaḥ aikṣata tāḥ āpaḥ aikṣanta iti gauṇa-īkṣaṇa-sāhacaryāt \| bhavati ca acetaneṣu api cetana-dharma-upacāraḥ \| yathā dṛṣṭi-pratīkṣā-āśrayāḥ svarṇaṃ bījaṃ pratisañjvarvanta \| ataḥ gauṇam īkṣaṇam antima-āmnāya-āḍhūmanubhāya parihriyate—

## \|\| 1.1.6 \|\|

*gauṇaḥ cet na ātma-śabdāt*

**Комментарий:**

yat uktaṃ gauṇa-īkṣaṇa-sāhacaryāt iti sataḥ api īkṣaṇa-vyapadeśaḥ sarva-nityatva-vivakṣā-miśrayaḥ gauṇaḥ iti \| tatra, etad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvaṃ tat satyaṃ saḥ ātmā iti sacchabda-pratipāditasya ātma-śabdena vyapadeśāt \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—yena etat ātmyam idaṃ sarvaṃ, saḥ ātmā iti caitanya-cetraprajña-upadeśena sat ātmatva-upadeśa-arhaṃ na tu etena pradhāne saṃgacchate iti \| ataḥ tejobannānām api paramātmāṃ-

vā asti iti tejaḥ-prabhṛtīni api śabdāḥ paramātmanam eva vācakāḥ \| tathā hi “hanta aham imās tisro devatā anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi iti paramātma-anupraveśāt eva tejaḥ-prabhṛtīnāṃ vastutvaṃ tat-nāma-bhāktvaṃ ca iti “tat teja aikṣata, “tā āpa aikṣanta iti api mukhya-aikṣaṇa-vyapadeśaḥ \| anta-samāhārya-pāṭhena “tat aikṣata iti atra gauṇatva-āśaṅkā dūrīkaroti iti sūtra-abhiprāyaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

**iti atra na pradhānaṃ sat-śabda-vācyam—**

## \|\| 1.1.7 \|\|

*mukteḥ ca mokṣa-upadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

mumukṣoḥ cetana-kotaḥ “tat tvam asi iti sat-ātmakatva-anusandhānam upadiśya, tad-bhiṣajya “tasya tāvat eva ciraṃ yāvat na vimokṣye atha sampatsye” iti śarīra-pāta-mātra-anantaraṇī brahma-sampatti-lakṣaṇo mokṣaḥ iti upadiśati \| yadi ca pradhānam acetanaṃ kāraṇam upadiśyeta; tadā tad-ātmakatva-anusandhānasya mokṣa-sādhanatva-upadeśaḥ na upapadyate \| “yathā kratuḥ asmiṃl loke puruṣo bhavati tathā itaḥ pretya bhavati iti tad-bhiṣajyaṃ cetana-saṅgatiḥ eva sthāpya \| na ca mātā-pitṛ-sahasrebhyaḥ api vatsala-taraṃ śāstram evam vidhātavyatyāmarda-hita-nirhetu-bhūtam ātma-vicintanam upadiśati \| pradhāna-kāraṇa-vādinaḥ api hi pradhāna-niṣṭhasya mokṣaṃ na abhyupagacchanti \|\| 7 \|\|

**iti atra na pradhānam—**

## \|\| 1.1.8 \|\|

*heyatva-avacanāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

yadi pradhānam eva kāraṇaṃ sat-śabda-abhihitaṃ bhavet, tadā mumukṣoḥ cetana-koteḥ asatāmakatvaṃ mokṣa-virodhitvāt cetanena eva upadekṣyaṃ syāt \| na ca tat iṣyate, pratyuta upādeyatvena eva “tat tvam asi “tasya tāvat eva ciram iti upadiśyate \|\| 8 \|\|.

**iti atra na pradhānam—**

## \|\| 1.1.9 \|\|

*pratijñā-virodhāt*

**Комментарий:**

pradhāna-kāraṇatve pratijñā-virodhaḥ ca bhavati \| vācya-upakrama loka-vijñānena sarva-vijñānaṃ pratijñātam \| tat ca kārya-kāraṇayoḥ ananyatvena kāraṇa-bhūta-dṛṣṭānta-ātmakābhūta-cetana-acetana-prapañcasya jñātasya aiśeṣa-upapatter yuktam \| tat tu pradhāna-kāraṇatve cetana-vargasya pradhāna-kāryatva-abhāvāt pradhāna-vijñānena cetana-varga-vijñāna-siddhiḥ virudhyate \|\| 9 \|\|

**iti atra na pradhānam—**

## \|\| 1.1.10 \|\|

*svāpyayāt*

**Комментарий:**

tat eva sat-śabda-vācyaṃ prakṛti āha “svam apīto bhavati” iti yatra etat puruṣaḥ svapiti nāma satā saumya tadā sampanno bhavati svam apīto bhavati tasmāt enaṃ svapitī iti ācakṣate svaṃ

hyasito bhavati iti \| suṣuṃ jīvaṃ satā saṃpannaṃ, svam apītiḥ—svasmin pralīnaḥ iti vyapadiśati \| pratyakṣa-sva-kāraṇe layaḥ \| na ca acetanaṃ pradhānaṃ cetanasya jīvasya kāraṇaṃ bhavitum arhati \| svam apīto bhavati—ātmanā eva jīvaḥ apīto bhavati iti arthaḥ \|\| chāndogyaśiro’ṣaṃ tad-ātma-bhūtaṃ brahma eva jīva-śabdena abhidhīyata iti nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇa-śruti-uktam \| teja-jīva-śabda-abhidheyaṃ brahma suṣupti-kāle api pralaya-kāla iva nāma-rūpa-parityakta-abhāvāt kevala-sat-śabda-abhidheyam iti satā somya tadā saṃpanno bhavati svam apīto bhavati iti ucyate \| tathā samāna-karaṇe nāma-rūpa-parityāga-bhāvena prāk eva parityaktānāṃ prājñena ātmanā saṃpariṣvakto na bāhyaṃ kiṃcana veda na antaram iti ucyate \| āmokṣāj jīvasya nāma-rūpa-parityāga eva hi svaccha-vistṛta-viṣaya-jñāna-udayāyaḥ \| suṣupti-kāle hi nāma eva vihāya satā saṃpariṣvaktaḥ punar api jāgarad-avasthāyāṃ nāma eva parityajya tat-tat-nāma-lābho bhavati iti śruti-antare spaṣṭam abhidhīyate—yadā suptaḥ svapnaṃ na kaṃcana paśyati atha asmin prāṇaḥ eva ekadhā bhavati.. etasmād ātmanaḥ prāṇā yathāyatanaṃ vipratiṣṭhante \| tathā—sa iha vyāghro vā siṃho vā vṛko vā varāho vā daṃśo vā maśako vā yad yad bhavanti iti ca \| tathā suṣuptaṃ jīvaṃ prājñena ātmanā saṃpariṣvaktaḥ iti ca vadati \| tasmāt sat-śabda-vācyaḥ pūrvaṃ brahma sarvajñaḥ parameśvaraḥ puruṣottama eva \| tad āhu vṛttikāraḥ—satā somya tadā saṃpanno bhavati iti, saṃpatti-asaṃpattibhyām itarad vyapodiṣṭe, prājñena ātmanā saṃpariṣvaktaḥ iti ca āha iti \|\| 10 \|\|

**īkṣateḥ na pradhānam—**

## \|\| 1.1.11 \|\|

*gati-sāmānyāt*

**Комментарий:**

ātmā vā idam eka eva agre āsīd iti-ādi-bhinna-viṣaya, sa īkṣata lokān nu sṛjā iti, sa imāl lokān asṛjata iti asyā etasmād ātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ; ākāśād vāyuḥ, vāyoḥ agniḥ, anter āpaḥ; adbhyaḥ pṛthivī tarhi ha vā etasya mahato bhūtasya niśvasitam etad yad ṛgvedaḥ iti-ādiṣu vibidhāyāṃ yā gatiḥ—pravṛttiḥ, tatra sarvatra api—tat-tat-sāmānādhikaraṇyasya; teṣu ca sarveṣu sarveśvaraḥ kāraṇatvam avagamyate \| tasmāt ca api sarveśvara eva kāraṇam iti niścīyate \|\| 11 \|\|

**īkṣateḥ na pradhānam—**

## \|\| 1.1.12 \|\|

*śrutatvāt ca \| 1 \| 1 \| 12 \|\|*

**Комментарий:**

śruteḥ ca hi asyām upaniṣadi asya sat-śabda-vācyasya ātmatvena nāma-rūpayor vikṛtya evaṃ sarvajñatvaṃ sarva-śaktitvaṃ sarvādhāratvam amṛtatvam ātmatva-ādikaṃ satya-saṃkalpatvaṃ śrutatvāt—anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi sat-mūlāḥ somya imāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ sat-āyatanāḥ sat-pratiṣṭhāḥ etad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvaṃ tat satyaṃ sa ātmā yena aśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavati amataṃ matam avijñātaṃ vijñātam iti granthaḥ ca na asti sarvaṃ tad asmin samāhi-
