tam u (a)tismṛta kāma-sambhūhitāḥ, eṣa ātmā apahṛta-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ vijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpaḥ iti \| tathā ca śrūyante tāni śrūn tasya vaśya-pratihata-rahitā loke na vai śrutaṃ na eva ca tasya liṅgam \| sa kāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipādhipaḥ na ca asya kaścit janitā na ca adhipaḥ \|\| sarvāṇi rūpāṇi vicitya dhīraḥ; nāmāni kṛtvā abhivadan yadā āste ajjanaḥ pratipūrṇa-śāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā viśvātmanāṃ parāyaṇaṃ patiṃ viśvasya ātmeśvarasu brahma kiṃśrajñāt paramaṃ dṛśyate śrūyate api vā, antaḥ bahiḥ ca tat sarvaṃ vyāpya nārāyaṇaḥ sthitaḥ eṣa sarva-bhūta-antarātmā apahata-pāpmā divyaḥ devaḥ ekaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ ityādīni \| tasmāt jñāna-eka-ākāratayā cid-acitoḥ na pradhāna-ādiśritiyā ātmamātram \| ataḥ sarvajñatva-aiśvarikatva-saccheṣa-īśvaraḥ nirasta-nikhila-doṣa-anadhyasta-adhika-atiśaya-ānandaḥ ca yathākhyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇa-ogha-mahārṇavaḥ puruṣottamaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ eva nikhila-jagat-eka-kāraṇaṃ jijñāsyaṃ brahma iti sthitam \|\|

ataḥ eva nirviśeṣa-cinmātra-brahma-vāde api sūtrakāreṇa abhisandhyuktiḥ nirastaḥ veditavyaḥ; śāstra-mādhika-mukhya-īkṣaṇa-ādi-vyapadeśaḥ api jijñāsyaṃ brahma iti sthāpanāt \| nirviśeṣa-vāde hi sādhitva-para-paramādhikam \| vedānta-vedyaṃ brahma jijñāsytayā pratijñātam \| tat ca caitanyam iti īkṣateḥ na aśabdam ityādibhiḥ sūtraiḥ pratipādyate \| caitanyatvaṃ nāma caitanya-guṇa-yogaḥ \| ataḥ īśvara-guṇa-virahiṇaḥ pradhāna-puruṣatvam eva \| kiñca nirviśeṣa-prakāśa-mātra-brahma-vāde tasya prakāśa-dharmī durupapādam \| prakāśaḥ hi nāma svasya parasya ca vyavahāra-yogyatām āpādayan vastu-viśeṣaḥ \| nirviśeṣasya vastunaḥ tu tad-ubhaya-rūpatva-abhāvāt ghaṭādivat acittvam eva \| tad-ubhaya-rūpatva-abhāve api tad-akṣamatvam asti iti cet, tarhi, tad-akṣamatvaṃ hi tattvam āpadyeta \| sāmarthya-upayogaḥ hi nirviśeṣa-vādaiḥ parityaktaḥ syāt \| atha śruti-sāmānya-vākyam ekaḥ viśeṣaḥ abhyupagamyate iti cet, hanta tarhi tat eva sarvajñatā, sarva-śaktitvaṃ, sarveśvara-īśvaratvaṃ, sarva-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākaratvaṃ, sakala-itara-pratyanīka-cetya vācyasadguṇānantatvāḥ \| śaktimatvaṃ ca kārya-viśeṣa-anuguṇatvam; tasya kārya-viśeṣe kartṛ-rūpatva-upayamaḥ; kārya-viśeṣasya niṣpramāṇatve tad-eka-nirūpaṇīyaṃ śaktimattvam api niṣpramāṇakaṃ syāt \| kiñca nirviśeṣa-vastu-vāditau vastutvam api niṣpramāṇam \| pratyakṣa-anumāna-āgamasya anubhavaḥ saviśeṣa-gocaraḥ iti pūrvam eva uktam \| tasmāt cid-acetana-acetana-ātmaka-jagat dehaṃ bahu syām iti īkṣaṇa-kṣamaḥ puruṣottamaḥ eva jijñāsyaḥ iti siddham \|\| 12 \|\| iti īkṣatyadhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|

**atha ānandamayādhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

evaṃ jijñāsitasya brahmaṇaḥ cetana-bhedyatva-mūna-jaḍa-rūpatva-rajastamomaya-pradhāna-ādi-ākṛtiriktā \| idānīṃ karma-nibaddhāt triguṇa-ātmaka-trivṛt-sṛṣṭi-nirmīta-nāma-vidyā-ānanda-duḥkha-bhāgāt cinmajjanena aśuddhāt śuddhatvaṃ ca pratyagātmanaḥ anvaya-rahita-deha-pratyānīka-tirahita-ānandaṃ brahmaṇaḥ vipratipādyate—

## \|\| 1.1.13 \|\|

*ānandamayaḥ abhyāsāt \| 1 \| 1 \| 13 \|\|*

**Комментарий:**

tṛtīyā adhīyate—sa vā eṣaḥ puruṣe annarasamayaḥ iti prakṛtya tasmāt vai etasmāt vijñānamayāt, anyaḥ antaraḥ ātmā ānandamayaḥ iti \| tatra sandehaḥ—kim ayam ānandamayaḥ bandha-mokṣa-bhāginaḥ pratyag-ātmanaḥ jīvā-śabda-abhilapanīyāt anyaḥ paramātmā; uta sa eva iti \| kiṃ yuktam ? pratyag-ātmā iti \| kutaḥ ? tatsaiva eva śārīraḥ ātmā iti ānandamayasya śārīratva-śravaṇāt; śārīraḥ hi śarīra-saṃbandhī jīvātmā \|\|

nanu ca jagat-kāraṇatayā pratipāditasya brahmaṇaḥ mukha-pratipattaye annamayādīnām upakramya tasya eva jagat-kāraṇam ānandamayaḥ iti upadiśati; jagat-kāraṇaṃ ca tat aikṣata iti īkṣaṇa-śravaṇāt saṃsraṣṭuṃ īśvarā iti uktam \| satyam uktam; sa tu jīvātmā atiricyate, anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya; satyaṃ tu iti caiteketau iti kāraṇatayā nirdiṣṭasya jīvātmano nādhikaraṇya-nirdeśāt \| sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ hi ekatva-pratipādana-param; yathā saḥ ayam devadattaḥ iti ādau \| īśitṛtvādikaṃ ca buddhi-cetanasya jīvasya upapadyate eva \| ataḥ brahmavit āpnoti param iti jīvasya avidyā-saṃsarga-viyuktaṃ svarūpaṃ prāpyatayā upadiśyate \| avibhukta-svarūpasya lakṣaṇam ittham ucyate satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti \| tat hi sphāṭikaḥ iva mokṣaḥ \| na ha vai saśarīrasya sataḥ priya-apriyayoḥ apahatiḥ asti, aśarīraṃ vāva santaṃ na priya-apriye spṛśataḥ iti \| ataḥ jīvasya avidyā-viyuktaṃ svarūpaṃ prāpyatayā prakāśamānam ayam iti upadiśyate \| tathā hi śākhā-candramasyena evaṃ-svarūpa-darśīyam anuḥ puruṣaḥ iti śarīraṃ prathamaṃ nirdiśya tadanantara-bhūtaṃ tasya dhārakaṃ pañca-buddhi-gamyam, tasya api antarabhūtaṃ manaḥ, tadanantara-bhūtāṃ ca buddhiṃ, prāṇamayaḥ manomayaḥ vijñānamayaḥ iti tataḥ tatra buddhi-avacaraṇa-krameṇa nirdiśya, sarvāntara-bhūtaṃ jīvātmānam anyaḥ antaraḥ ātmā ānandamayaḥ iti upadiśati antarātma-paramparāṃ samāpayati \| ataḥ jīvātma-svarūpam eva brahmavit āpnoti iti prakrāntaṃ brahma; tat eva ānandamayaḥ iti upadiśyami iti niścīyate \|\|

nanu ca, brahma pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā iti ānandamayāt paratayā eva brahma iti pratīyate \| na evam; brahma eva svasya bhāva-viśeṣeṇa puruṣa-vidhatva-bahutāṃ śiraḥ-pakṣa-puccha-bhedena vyapadiśyate \| yathā annamayaḥ dehaḥ avayavī svasya adanatiriktaiḥ sva-avayavaiḥ eva śiraḥ iti ādinā śiraḥ-pakṣa-puccha-vattayā nirdiṣṭaḥ; tathā ānandamayaṃ brahma api svasya adanatiriktaiḥ priyādimir nirdiśitam \| tatra avayavavena rūpitānāṃ priya-moda-pramodānām abhyastayā akhaṇḍa-rūpam ānandamayaṃ brahma pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā ucyate \| yadi ca ānandamayāt aranyat brahma abhidhīyate, tasmāt vā etasmāt ānandamayāt anyaḥ antaraḥ ātmā brahma iti nirdiśyet \| na evaṃ nirdiśyate \| evaṃ-bhūtaṃ bhavati—brahmavit āpnoti param iti prakrāntaṃ brahma satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti lakṣaṇataḥ tailetara-vyāvṛttākāraṃ pratipādya

tad eva tasmāt vā etasmāt ātmanaḥ ākāśa-saṃbhūtena nirdiśya tasya sarva-antaratvena ātmatvaṃ vyadhāyaka-māsamayādi-dṛṣṭu tad-anantaratayā ākāśena nirdhāraṃ prāṇamayādeḥ niṣkriyaḥ anyaḥ antaraḥ ātmā ānandamayaḥ iti ātma-śabdena nirdiśyamānaḥ ānandamaye samaparyati \| ataḥ ātma-śabdena prakāntaṃ brahma ānandamayaḥ iti niścīyate—iti \|\|

nanu ca brahma pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā ity uktyā asamāna eva sa bhavati, asat brahma iti veda cet, asti brahma iti cet veda, santam enaṃ tato viduḥ iti brahma-jñāna-asānādhyāmimanasduvāsamduvā darśayati; na ānandamaya-jñāna-ajñāna-phalam \| na ca ānandamayasya priya-modādi-rūpeṇa sarvato’bhivistarasya saḍruvāsamduva-brahmaṇā āśaṅkā yuktā \| ataḥ na ānandamayam adhikṛtya ayaṃ ślokaḥ udāhṛtaḥ \| tasmāt na ānandamaya-ātmad brahma \| nanu; idaṃ pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā pṛthivī pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā atharvāṅgirasaḥ pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā mahaḥ pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā iti ukteṣu tatra tatra udāhṛtāḥ brahma vai prajāḥ prajāyante ity-ādi-ślokāḥ yathā na puccha-mātra-pratipādana-parāḥ; api tu amayādi-puccha-pratipādana-parāḥ; evam atra api ānandamaya-avayavaṃ brahma iti ślokaiḥ; na ānandamaya-vyatiriktasya pucchasya \| ānandamayad eva brahmatve api priya-modādi-rūpeṇa rūpitastha-aparicchidyamānandasya sadruvāsamduvajnāna-āśaṅkā yuktiva \| puccha-brahmaṇaḥ api aparicchidyamānandatvaṃ ca hi aprasiddhatā \| śiraḥ-prabhṛty-avayavivābhāvāt u brahmaṇaḥ na ānandamayaḥ brahma iti cet; brahmaṇaḥ pucchatva-pratiṣṭhatva-abhāvāt puccham api brahma na bhavet \| atha avidyā-parikalpitasya vastunaḥ tu athā satya-bhūtatvāt brahmaṇaḥ pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā iti rūpa-mātra-nimitta ucyate; hanta tarhi tasya aśuddha-vyatiriktasya ānandamayasya brahmaṇaḥ priya-śirastvādi-rūpaṃ bhaviṣyati \| evaṃ ca satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti vikāra-āspada-jḍa-paricchinna-vastv-antaram vyāhṛtya ānandamayaḥ iti upadiśyate \| tatraś ca jaḍa-vikāra-ānanda-rūpe brahmaṇi ānandamayaḥ iti madra prāṇamaya iva svārthikaḥ draṣṭavyaḥ \| asmat-avidyā-parikalpita-vividha-vivicitra-devādi-viśeṣa-bhinnasya jīvātmanaḥ tasāmāvikaṃ rūpam avikāraṃ sukhakatānām ānandamayaḥ iti ucyate iti ānandamayaḥ pratyagātmā \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—ānandamayaḥ abhyāsāt \| ānandamayaḥ paramātmā \| kutaḥ ? abhyāsāt saḥ aśnute sarvān kāmān saha brahmaṇā vipaścitā iti hṛdaya-rūpaḥ saḥ yaḥ ha vā u nivasante hṛdayena manute vākyena śāstṛ-guṇita-ananta-kramaṇa niratisaya-brahma-śiraskaḥ abhyasyamānaḥ ānandaḥ ananta-duḥkha-bhiśra-parimita-sukha-alabhāgini jīvātma-saṃbhavan nikhila-heya-pratyanīkaṃ kalyāṇa-ikatānaṃ sakala-itara-vilakṣaṇaṃ paramātmānam eva svābhyam anāpekṣati \| yathā āha tasmāt vai etasmāt vijñānamayāt, anyaḥ antaraḥ ātmā ānandamayaḥ iti \| vijñānamayaḥ hi jīvaḥ; na buddhi-mātram, madhya-pratyayena vyatireka-smatoteḥ \| prāṇamaye tu anyathā svārthikatā apekṣyate \| iha tu tadvataḥ jīvasya saṃbhavāsaṃ-

na evaṃ nyāyyam \| vaddhaḥ muktaḥ ca pratyag-ātmā jñātṛtva-śrutyā hi \| prāṇamayādeḥ ca madhvartha-saṃbhojanādeḥ eva vakṣyate \| kathaṃ tarhi vijñānamaya-viṣaya-śloke vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanute iti kevala-vijñāna-śabda-pādānam upapadyate ? jñātuḥ eva manas-svarūpam api svaprakāśatayā vijñānam iti ucyate iti na doṣaḥ, jñānaika-rūpāḥ ca jñātṛ-svarūpasya \| svarūpa-nirūpaṇa-dharma-śabdā hi dharma-mukhena dharmi-svarūpam api pratipādayanti, gavādi-śabdavat \| kṛtyaḍuṭo bahulam iti vā kartari lyuṭ aśrīyate \| tasya āditvaṃ vāḍaśīya nandigrahītyādinā kartari lyuḥ \| ataḥ eva ca vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanute, karmāṇi tanute api ca iti yajñādi-kartṛtvaṃ vijñānasya śrūyate \| buddhi-mātrasya hi na kartṛtvaṃ saṃbhavati \| acetanasya hi cetana-upakaraṇa-bhūtasya vijñānamayāpracāge tu ekasamarpitatvena na caitanya-mātrasya kartṛtvaṃ śrūyate \| ataḥ eva caitana-mātra-caitane ca svātantryādayaḥ nityatva-anitvayattvādi-virodhi-viśeṣa-viṣaya nirdeśārthaṃ vijñānaṃ ca avijñānaṃ ca iti vijñāna-śabdena tadguṇaṃ caitanaṃ vadati \| tathā antaryāmi-brāhmaṇe yaḥ vijñāne tiṣṭhan iti asya kāṇva-paṭhāntarasya paryāyasya sthāne yaḥ ātmani tiṣṭhan iti paryāyam adhīyānā mādhyaṃdināḥ kāṇva-paṭhāntaṃ vijñāna-śabda-nirdiṣṭaṃ jīvam iti sphuṭīkurvanti \| vijñāna-pratīti ca napuṃsaka-liṅgaṃ vastutvābhiprāyam \| tad evaṃ vijñānamayāt jīvātmatvataḥ paraḥ ātmā ānandamayaḥ \| yad api vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanute iti śloke jñāna-mātrasya eva pratipādye te na jñātā; tathā api anyaḥ antaraḥ ātmā vijñānamayaḥ iti taddhātu jñātā eva pratipadyate; yathā asau ādityaḥ prajāḥ prajāyante iti atra śloke kevala-anna-pādāne api saḥ vai eṣaḥ puruṣaḥ annarasamayaḥ iti atra na ānamātraṃ nirdiśya, api tu tanmayaḥ tad vikāraḥ \| etat sarvaṃ hṛdi nidhāya sūtrakāraḥ svayam eva jñatva-śruteḥ ca iti anantaraṃ eva vadati \|\|

prakṛtaṃ jñātṛ-kārakatvaṃ nirihasya anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya sat ca tyat ca abhavat iti ca jīvātma-sāmānādhikaraṇya-nirdeśāt jñātṛ-kāraṇam api jīva-svarūpāt antariricyata iti dṛṣṭvā jīvasya eva svarūpaṃ brahmavid āpnoti param iti prakrāntam abuddhau ānandaṃayaḥ iti upadiśyate iti; tadguṇatvam, jīvasya cetanatve sati api tad aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tattvajaḥ abhūt juneti svasajña-pūrvaka-anantara-vidhā-śrutyā upapannaḥ \| śuddha-avasthā api hi tasya saguṇa-cid-yāpāra-asaṃbhavaḥ jñātvā paravaśaṃ bhūmā hi asau alīḍavat iti ayogāt adhīyate \|\|

kāraṇa-bhūtasya brahmaṇaḥ jīva-svarūpatayā anuprāṇane anena jīvena ātmanā satyaṃ ca amṛtaṃ ca iti asmin sāmānādhikaraṇya-nirdeśaḥ katham upapadyata iti cet; kathaṃ vā nirasta-nikhila-doṣaṃ vānmayasya satya-saṅkalpasya sarveśvarasya sarva-śakteḥ svātantrya-dvitīya-amṛtasya eka-śrutyapanna-guṇa-gaṇa-ātmaka samatkalakāraṇa-bhūtasya brahmaṇaḥ nānāvidha-ananta duḥkha-ākāra-madhyotta-viśala-ativividha-sthira-cara-atma-kṣetraṃ jīva-svarūpatvam ? anyatarasya mithyātvena upapadyata iti cet, kasya bhoḥ ? kiṃ heya-saṃbandhasya ? kiṃ vā heyasya

naika-kalyāṇa-kartṛtva-svabhāvatvam? heya-pratyanīka-kalyāṇa-kartṛtvasya brahmaṇo jñānādi-avidyā-āśrayatvena heya-saṃbandha-mithyā-pratibhāsau mithyā-rūpau iti cet; vipratiniṣiddham idam abhidhīyate, brahmaṇo heya-pratyanīka-kalyāṇa-kartṛtva-nitya-anātha-avidyā-āśrayatvena ānanda-duḥkha-viparyaya-mithyā-pratibhāsa-āśrayatvaṃ ca iti \| avidyā-āśrayatvaṃ tad-ānanda-duḥkha-pratibhāsa-āśrayatvaṃ ca eva hi heya-saṃbandhaḥ \| tat-saṃbandhitvaṃ tasya prayanīkattvaṃ ca viruddham eva \| tathā api tasya mithyātvāt na virodhaḥ iti mā vocaḥ \| mithyābhūtam atpuruṣārtha eva, paritrasanāya sarvaṃ vedāntā ārabhyante iti brūṣe \| niratiśaya-puruṣārtha-upāśraya-heya-pratyanīka-kalyāṇa-katānatayā virudhyate \| kiṃ kurmaḥ? yena aśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavati iti eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñānaṃ pratijñāya sat eva somya idam agre āsīt iti ādinā nikhila-jagat-eka-kāraṇatām, tat ha aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti satya-saṅkalpatāṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ pratipādya tat eva brahmaṇaḥ satyaṃ sat iti sāmānādhikaraṇyena ananta-duḥkha-āśraya-jīva-aikyaṃ pratipāditam; tad-ananyaga-upapathyā brahmaṇaḥ eka-avidyā-āśrayatvādi parikalpanīyam iti cet; śruta-upapanna-heya-anya-pakṣaṃ viruddhaṃ ca na kalpanīyam \| atha heya-saṃbandha eva pāramārthikaḥ; kalyāṇa-ekatva-bhāvatā tu mithyābhūtā; hanta eṣa tāpanīyi-niśita-vetanaḥ jijīviṣayā pravṛttaḥ śāstraṃ tāpanyā abhihitena eva tasya pāramārthikī, kalyāṇa-ekatva-bhāvaḥ tu śruti-nirākalpitaḥ iti bodhayatam api anjīvayati \| atha iha heya-parijihīrṣayā brahmaṇo nirviśeṣa-cinmātra-eka-rūpa-atirikta-jīva-tanu-duḥkhitvādikaṃ satya-saṅkalpatva-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākaratva-jagat-kāraṇatvādi api mithyābhūtam iti kalpanīyam iti cet; aho bhavataḥ vākya-artha-paryālocana-kuśalatā! eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñānaṃ sarvaṃ ca mithyātve sarvasya jñātatavya-abhāvāt setsyati \| yathā eka-vijñānaṃ paramārtha-viṣayam, tathā eva sarva-vijñānam api yadi paramārtha-viṣayaṃ tadā karṇatvaṃ ca, tathā taj-jñānena sarva-vijñānam iti śakyate vaktum \| na hi paramārtha-yuktikā-jñānena tad-āśrayam aparamārtha-rajataṃ jñātaṃ bhavati \|\|

atha ucyeta—eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñāyā ayam arthaḥ;—nirviśeṣa-(cinmātra)-brahma-mātram eva satyam, anyat asatyam iti \| na tarhi yena aśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavati amataṃ matam avijñātaṃ vijñātam iti śrūyeta; yena śrutena aśrutam api śrutaṃ bhavati iti hi asya vākyasya arthaḥ \| kāraṇatva-upalakṣaṇa-nirviśeṣa-vastu-mātram eva satyam iti etat vijñātaṃ, yathā saumyakena mṛt-piṇḍena sarvaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vijñātam iti dṛṣṭāntaḥ api na ghaṭate \| mṛt-piṇḍa-vijñānena hi tad-vikārasya jñātatā nirdiśitā \| tatra api vikārasya satyatvāt abhipretā iti cet; mṛttikārasya rajju-rūpa-ahi-vidvat-satyatvaṃ śuṣkṣaḥ api siddham iti pratijñāta-artha-saṃbhāvanā-pradarśanāya yathā sa uṣṭi iti prasiddhavat dṛṣṭāntaḥ na yujyate \| na ca tadvat satyāvivākyataya jñāna-anupatteḥ prāmāṇika-rajata-satyatā-mātra-vacana-kāṅkṣitam anuguṛhītaṃ vā pramāṇam upalabhāmaha iti \| ayam arthaḥ tad-ananyatvam ārambhaṇa-śabdādibhyaḥ ityatra vakṣyate \|\|

tathā sat eva somya idam āsīt ekam eva advitīyam tat ha aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tat tejaḥ asṛjata hanta aham imāḥ tisraḥ devatāḥ anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe

vyākṛtvāṇi sat-mūlāḥ somya imāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ sat-āyatanāḥ sat-pratiṣṭhāḥ ... aindātmyaṃ idaṃ sarvam iti ādinā asya jagataḥ sat-ātmakatā, sṛṣṭeḥ pūrva-kāle nāma-rūpa-vibhāga-anarham, jagad-rūpatve satya-saṅkalpatvāt ca asya brahmaṇaḥ sva-vyatirikta-nimitta-antara-anapekṣatvam, sṛṣṭi-kāle api sevānantara-sthyatra-sadṛśeṇa bahu syām iti anukma-asādhāraṇa-sthūlatva-viśeṣaḥ; yathā aṇḍaḥ kṛtamatartva-vicitratvacāṃ vilakṣaṇa-krama-viśeṣa-viniṣedha sṛṣṭeḥ; samasta-veda-vedye api vastuni svāsmaka-jīvātṛ-praveśena eva ananta-nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇam, sva-vyatiriktasya samastasya sva-nūlatvam, sva-āyatanatvam, sva-pratyantvam, svena eva jīvanaṃ, sva-pratibuddha-nitya-ādhānata-viśeṣa-ākāra-ekaśaramadhidyāḥ pratipāditāḥ \| tat-saṃnidhyattvā prakaraṇa-antara-gata-pada-padārtha-vādi-nirasti-nikhila-dopavat-sarvajñatvaya-aiśvaryavat-satya-

yathā ca vindhya-aṭavyāṃ mada-mudita-mātaṅga-guṇita-vṛkṣaḥ api padavī-āvarta-viśeṣa-viśiṣṭaḥ eva arthaḥ pratīyate; evaṃ vedānta-vākyeṣu api samānādhikaraṇa-nirdeśeṣu tat-tad-viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭam eva brahma pratipattavyam \| na ca viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāvam antarā viśiṣṭākāraṃ vastu eva viśeṣyam; adi tu sarva-viśeṣaṇa-svarūpam eva viśeṣyam \| tathā hi bhinna-pravṛtti-nimittānāṃ śabdānām ekasminn arthe vṛtti-samānādhikaraṇyam iti, anyena nibaddhyā vā padāntara-pratipādya-ākāra-āhāra-kāra-antara-yuktatayā tasya eva vastunaḥ padāntara-pratipādyatve samānādhikaraṇya-kāryam \| yathā ‘devadattasya ami yuvā lohita-akṣaḥ dīrgha-nīla-kuṇḍala-utpala-āvattaḥ’ iti \| yatra ca ekasmin vastuni samanvaya-ayogyaṃ viśeṣa-padaṃ samānādhikaraṇya-padarśa-nivṛttam; tatra api anyatarat-pada-mukhya-vṛttim āśrīyate, na dvayam, yathā ‘gauḥ bāhīkaḥ’ iti \| nīla-utpala-ādiṣu tu viśeṣaṇa-mukhya-padārtha-ubhayam eva ubhaya-viśiṣṭaṃ pratipadyate \|\|

atha manye eka-viśeṣaṇa-pratisaṃbandhitvena nīla-rūpāṇāṃ viśeṣaṇa-antara-pratisaṃbandhitvādi-lakṣaṇam iti ghaṭa-paṭyādi-vaikalya-abhinirdeśaḥ iti apratipattavyaṃ-bhāvāt samānādhikaraṇa-śabdasya na viśiṣṭa-pratipādana-paratvam, api tu viśeṣaṇa-mukhena svarūpa-mukhyatvaṃ tat eva pratipādana-paratvam eva iti \| syāt etat evam, yadi viśeṣa-pada-pratisaṃbandha-mātram eva nīla-rūpam \| na ca etat asti; ekasmin api puṣpa-saṃsthānamaye madhyam eva viśiṣṭatvam eva hi ekatvaṃ nirūḍham \| ayogyatā ca pramāṇatva-liṅgā ghaṭatva-paṭatvayoḥ \| nīlatvatvamitvayoḥ tu daṇḍitva-kuṇḍalitva-dṛḍhavattva-sattvacādhyandivat ca virodhaḥ na upapadyate \| na kevalam avirodhaḥ evam; pravṛtti-nimitta-bhedena eka-artha-niṣṭhatvaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyam upapādyate dharma-dvaya-viśiṣṭatām \| anyathā ‘svarūpa-śābdatve aneka-pada-prayogaḥ nimitta-abhāvāt samānādhikaraṇyam eva na syāt \| viśeṣaṇānāṃ sva-saṃbandha-nāśena vastu-svarūpa-upalakṣaṇa-paratve sati ekena api vastu-upalakṣitatim upalakṣaṇa-antaram anarthakam eva; upalakṣaṇa-antara-upalakṣya-ākāra-bheda-abhyupagame tena kāraṇena sa-viśeṣatva-prasaṅgaḥ \|\|

‘saḥ ayam devadattaḥ’ iti atra api lakṣaṇā-āśrayaḥ na vidyate, virodha-abhāvāt \| deśāntara-saṃbandhitasya atītasya saṃnihita-deśa-saṃbandhitayā vartamānatva-virodhe dhātuḥ \| ata eva hi saḥ ayam iti pratyabhijñā kāla-dvaya-saṃbandhinaḥ vastunaḥ eka-mukha-pādyate vastu-nirūpya-rūpādi-vidhiḥ \| anyathā pratīti-virodhe sati sarvaṃ kṣaṇikatvam eva syāt \| deśādi-asaṃbandha-virodhaḥ tu kāla-bhedena parihriyate \|\|

yataḥ samānādhikaraṇya-padānām aneka-viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭa-eka-artha-vācakatvam; ata eva adhyāya-kahāyakyā vijñānasya sauṃ śrīṇāti ityādaraṇādi-viruddha-eka-artha-vācyā brahma-sārathyā vidhīyate \| tat uktam arthatvena brahma-guṇa-sair-eka-karmopiyamastvayi iti \|\|

tanvam pūrvapakṣī manyate—pradhāna-rūpeṇa iti padam āha iti ca guṇasya api dravya-prakāratāṃ

svabhāva-dārḍhya-paryanta-sevāṅginā anabhisandhiḥ; tathā api eka-hāyana-vacana-niyamaḥ aṅginaḥ na saṃbhavati; eka-hāyanyā krīṇāti, tat ca aṅgayā eva śuddhyavidhāna-saṃbhavāt \| tataḥ ca āruṇyā iti vākyaṃ kiñcit prakaraṇa-vihita-saṃbandha-paryanta-sevāṅginā anabhiyogaviṣayam iti \| aruṇayā iti strī-liṅga-nirdeśaḥ prakaraṇa-vihita-saṃbandha-aṅga-dravyāṇāṃ pradarśanārthaḥ \| tasmāt eka-hāyana-vacana-niyamaḥ aṅginaḥ na sthāpayati \|\|

atra abhidhīyate— arthakatve dravya-guṇayoḥ eka-nirvartya-kriyam asthyāt \| aruṇa-eka-hāyanyā ity āruṇya-viśiṣṭa-dravya-eka-hāyanī-dravya-vāci-padayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyena eka-arthatve siddhe sati eka-hāyanī-dravya-eka-prayojya-āruṇyā iti padena eva viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāvena saṃbandhitayā abhihitatvāt, krayasthaya-karman vyāpāra-virodhāt aruṇimnaḥ kraya-sādhana bhūta-eka-hāyana-vacana-niyam asthyāt \| ayaṃ eka-hāyanyā kraya-saṃbandhavat aruṇima-saṃbandhaḥ api vākyātmakam astyāt; tadā vākyasya aśuddhyavidhānaṃ syāt \| na ca etat asti \| aruṇā iti padena eka-hāyany-abhidhitsita-dravya-abhidhitsam \| eka-hāyanī-pada-sāmānādhikaraṇyena tasya eka-hāyanī-dravya-śeṣatvām āmanāyate; na guṇa-saṃbandhaḥ; viśiṣṭa-dravya-eka-deśe iva hi sāmānādhikaraṇyasthāryaḥ \| bhinna-pravṛtti-nimittānāṃ śabdānām ekasminn arthe vṛttiḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam iti hi sāmānādhikaraṇya-lakṣaṇam \|\|

ataḥ eva hi raktaḥ paṭaḥ bhavati ityādika-eka-arthya-vivakṣā bhavati \| paṭasya bhavana-kriyā-saṃbandhe hi vākya-vyāpāraḥ; rāga-saṃbandhaḥ tu rakta-padena eva abhihitaḥ; rāga-saṃbandhi dravyaṃ paṭaḥ iti etāvat-mātraṃ sāmānādhikaraṇya-avaśeyam \| evaṃ kena guṇena gārhyaṃ barhiṣvī tena tena padena samavetana karyate vā viviśyamātram upasthāpya sāmānādhikaraṇyena sarva-viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭaḥ arthaḥ ekaḥ iti jñāpitvā tasya kriyā-saṃbandha-abhidhānam abhihitam—devadattasya āgaḥ yuvā lohitākṣaḥ daṇḍī kuṇḍalī tiṣṭhati; śuklena vāsasā yajeta; nīla-saroja-bhānayaḥ; nīla-utpala-bhānayaḥ; gām ānaya śuklāṃ śobhāna-akṣīm asyāṃ paṅktau puroḍāśa-pradāna-kṣamatāṃ nirvedtu iti \| evam aruṇa-eka-hāyanyā piṅgākṣyā somaṃ krīṇāti iti \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—yathā kāṭhaka-sthālyām odaṃ pacet yena eka-kāraka-viśiṣṭa-ekāṃ kriyāṃ yugapat vadati ekaḥ; tathā sāmānādhikaraṇya-pada-sūcita-abhihitam ekaikaṃ kārakaṃ tat-sat-kārakat-pratipatti-velāyām eva eka-viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭaṃ yugapat pratipannaṃ kriyāyām anveti iti na kaścit virodhaḥ; bādhitaḥ ca āśaṅkaḥ; kāṭhaka-samaya-parimāṇe bhāṇḍe pāyasaṃ śālyodanaṃ samarthaḥ pācakaḥ pacet ityādivat—iti \|\|

yat-padārtha-dravyaka-vākyastha-guṇa-śabdaḥ kevala-guṇa-abhidhāyī iti aruṇā iti padena kevala-guṇasya api vā abhidhānam iti; tat na upapadyate, loka-vedayoḥ dravya-vāci-pada-samānādhikaraṇasya guṇa-vācinaḥ kvacid api kevala-guṇa-abhidhānatadarśanāt \| upātta-dravyaka-vākyasthaṃ guṇa-padaṃ kevala-guṇa-abhidhāyī iti etat apy-

sahṛttvam, padasya śuklatā iti-ādi-vyāpāta-sambandhena eva guṇa-viśiṣṭatva-pratīyānāt \| padasya śuklatā iti atra śauklya-viśiṣṭa-pada-pratītivat samāna-viśeṣaṇa-nirdeśa-kṛtā; na punaḥ śabda-sambandha-kṛtā \| tathā eva padasya śubhro bhāgaḥ iti-ādivu samāna-viśeṣaṇa-nirdeśe śauklya-viśiṣṭa-dravyaṃ pratīyate yat punaḥ karma-kartṛ-bhāva-ādi-avacchedyatayā hariṇaḥ karmakatvo na saṃbhavati iti; tad api virodhi-guṇa-rahita-dravya-āviṣṭa-samānādhikaraṇa-guṇa-padasya tad-āśraya-guṇa-abhidhānena kriyā-padatva-avirodha-avadūnam \| rāhonti bhoktṛ-nyāyena hariṇa-śabde dravya-vācye siddhe dravya-guṇayoḥ karma-sādhanatvam upapannaṃ arthāntara-paratvāt asiddhyatā-doṣa-param uktam \| ato yathoktaḥ eva arthaḥ \|\|

tasmāt tat tvam asi iti-ādi-śāstra-adhikaraṇye pada-dvaya-abhihita-viśeṣaṇa-parityāgān eva eka-pratipattiḥ varṇanīyam \| tatra na ādhikya-upahita-nāmādheya-kṛtsna-bhāginī śuddhacuddha ca bhāva-avasthā-cetana-ādi-aśeṣa-antarbhūtam aśeṣa-heya-pratyanīkān anavadhika-kalyāṇa-eka-tānaṃ paramātmānam api upagacchato na saṃbhavati \| aṃśupagacchataḥ api samānādhikaraṇa-padānāṃ yathāvasthita-viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭe aikya-pratipādana-paratva-āśrayaṇe tvaṃ-pada-pratipanna-sakala-doṣa-bhāgitvaṃ parasya prasajyeta iti cait; na etad evam, tvaṃ-padena api jīva-antaryāmiṇaḥ parasya eva abhidhānāt \|\|

etat uktaṃ bhavati—sat-cabda-artha-hitaṃ nirasta-nikhila-doṣaṃ prāpyaṃ satyam aśuklam apītān avadhika-atisāya saṃkhyeya-kalyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇau samasta-kāraṇa-bhūtaṃ paraṃ brahma bahu syām iti saṅkalpya tejo-banna-prabhṛtiṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat sṛṣṭvā tasmin deva-ādi-vicitra-sansthāna-saṃsthite jagati caitanaṃ jīva-vargaṃ sva-karma-guṇeṣu śarīreṣu ātmatayā praveśya svayaṃ ca sveccheya jīva-antarātmatayā anupraviśya evaṃ-bhūtaṃ sarva-prakāramu deva-ādi-ākāreṣu sadṛśeṣu nāma-rūpe vyākaroti \| evaṃ-rūpa-sṛṣṭātasya eva vastunaḥ śabda-vācyatvaṃ ca akarot iti arthaḥ \| anena jīva-antarātma-jīvena mayā iti nirdeśo jīvasya brahma-ātmakatvaṃ

darśati \| brahma-ātmakatvaṃ ca jīvasya jīva-antarātmatayā brahmaṇaḥ anupraveśāt iti avagamyate; idaṃ sarvam abhūtat, yat idaṃ kiṃ ca, tat sṛṣṭvā, tat eva anupṛviśat, tat anupraviśya, sat ca tyat ca abhavat iti asya idaṃ sarvam iti nirdiṣṭaṃ cetana-acetana-vastu-bṛndaṃ sadatyaccabdābhyāṃ vijñāna-avijñāna-śabdābhyāṃ ca vibhajya nirdiśya citraṃ ca api brahmaṇaḥ anupraveśa-abhidhānāt \| ataḥ eva nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇasya eva vācaka-śabda avicita-jīva-viśiṣṭa-samānavataḥ acitaḥ iti avagatyam—iti \|\|

kiṃ ca etad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvam iti cetana-miśraṃ prapañcaṃ idaṃ sarvam iti nirdiśya tat satyaṃ ātmā iti pratipāditam \| evaṃ ca sarvaṃ cetana-acetanaṃ prati brahma ātmatvena sarvaṃ sat cetanaṃ jagat tasya śarīraṃ bhavati \| tathā ca śrūyante śruti antaḥ-praviṣṭa-śāstā janānāṃ sarva-ātmā yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivyāḥ antaraḥ yaṃ pṛthivī na veda yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yaḥ pṛthivīm antaraḥ yamayati sa ta ātmā antaryāmī amṛtaḥ iti śāstrasya ya ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaraḥ yam ātmā

na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ ya ātmānam antaraḥ yamayati sa te ātmā antary amṛtaḥ ityādi śruteḥ; pṛthivīm antare sañcaran yasya pṛthivī śarīram, yaḥ apam antare sañcaran yasya āpaḥ śarīram ityādi asya yaḥ akṣaram antare sañcaran yasya akṣaraṃ śarīram yam akṣaraṃ na veda eṣa sarva-bhūta-antarātmā apahata-pāpmā divyaḥ devaḥ ekaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ ityādīni sarvam etat jagat tasya śarīratvena nirdiśya tasya ātmatvena paramātmānam upadiśanti \| ataḥ cetana-vācinaḥ api śabdāḥ cetanasya ātmatayā ātmasbhūtaṃ cetana-śarīrakaṃ paramātmānam eva abhidadhati \| yathā acetana-deva-ādi-saṃsthāna-piṇḍa-vācinyaḥ śabdāḥ tataḥ śarīraka-jīvātmanam eva vācakāḥ; catvāraḥ pañcadaśa-rātrāḥ devatvaṃ gacchanti ityādiṣu; devāḥ bhavanti iti arthaḥ \| śarīrasya śarīriṇaṃ prati prakāratvāt prakāra-vācināṃ ca śabdānāṃ prakāriṇi eva paryavasānāt śarīra-vācināṃ śabdānāṃ śarīri-paryavasānaṃ nyāyyam \| prakāraḥ hi nāma ittham iti iti pratīyamānaḥ vastuni ittham iti pratīyamānaḥ aṃśaḥ \| tasya tad-atirikta-vastu-aikṣaṇena tat-pratītiḥ tad-vastu-aikṣaṇāt tasmin eva paryavasānam ucitam iti tasya pratipādakaḥ api śabdaḥ tasmin eva paryavasyati \| ataḥ eva gauḥ aśvaḥ manuṣyaḥ ityādi-prakāra-bhūta-ākṛti-ādinā śabdāḥ prakāriṇi piṇḍe aparyavasyantaḥ piṇḍasya api cetana-śarīratvena tat-prakāratvāt piṇḍa-śarīraka-cetanasya api paramātma-prakāratvāt ca paramātmani eva paryavasyanti iti sarva-śabdānāṃ paramāni eva vācyaḥ iti paramātma-vācitvena sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ mukhyam eva \|\|

madhu khaṇḍaḥ gauḥ khaṇḍa-śuklaḥ iti jāti-guṇa-abhidhānam eva padānāṃ dravya-vācitvena saha sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ dṛṣṭam; dravyāṇāṃ tu dravya-antara-prakāratve matvarthīya-pratyayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ, yathā daṇḍī kuṇḍalī iti \| na eṣaḥ; jātiḥ vā guṇaḥ vā dravyaṃ vā tena eva ekena eva sāmānādhikaraṇye prayojakam, artha-ucitaḥ tu vyabhicārāt \| yasya padārthasya kasyacit aprakāratvaṃ ca sadbhāvaḥ; tasya tad-atyanta-visadṛśa-sthiti-pratīti-nirasta-avacināṃ śabdānāṃ svārtha-bheda-viśiṣṭa-dravya-vācitvāt abhinirviśiṣṭa-dravya-vācitā śabdena sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ yuktam eva \| yatra punaḥ pṛthagbhūtasya dravya-viśeṣasya sva-niṣṭhuratvena dravyasya kasyacit viśeṣavi-dravya-antara-prakāratvam iṣyate, tatra matvarthīya-pratyayaḥ iti niravadyam \|\|

tat evaṃ paramātmanaḥ sarīratayā tat-prakāratva-abhidāyi-viśiṣṭa-jīvasya api jīva-nirdeśa-aviśeṣa-rūpā ahaṃtva-abhidhāyi-śabdaḥ paramātmānam eva apekṣate iti śrutanvamatīti sāmānādhikaraṇyena upasaṃhṛtaḥ; evaṃ ca sati paramātmānaṃ prati bodhasya śarīratayā dravyavadgīvatā dharmaḥ paramātmānaṃ na spṛśanti, yathā svakīrīratā bālatva-yuvatva-artha-viruddhatvayaḥ dharmaḥ jīvaṃ na spṛśanti \| ataḥ śrutanvamatīti sāmānādhikaraṇye tattvāt jagat-kāraṇa-bhūtaṃ satya-saṅkalpaṃ sarva-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākaraṃ nirasta-samasta-heya-pratyanīkaṃ paramātmānam ācaṣṭe; tvanmiti ca tasya saśarīra-jīva-śarīrakam ācaṣṭa iti sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ mukhyatamam; prakaraṇa-virodhaḥ ca śruti-virodhaḥ brahmaṇi niravadhye kathyāṅka-

tāte avidyādi-viśeṣaṇa-sambhavac ca \| ato jīva-samānādhikaraṇyam api viśeṣaṇa-bhūta-ajīva-caya-vyapadeśa-vācyatvāt iti vijñānamayāt jīvāt asya eva ānandamayaḥ paramātmā \|\|

yuktam tasya eva śārīra ātmā iti ānandamayasya śārīratva-śravaṇāt jīvāt anyatvaṃ na saṃbhavati iti, tat ayuktam; asmin prakaraṇe sarvatra tasya eva eva śārīra ātmā, yaḥ pūrvasya iti paramātmana eva śārīratvena vidhānāt \| katham \| tasmāt vā etasmāt ātmanaḥ ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ iti ākāśādi-heya vargasya parama-kāraṇatvena prastutaḥ vidyā-viruddhasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ ātmatvena hi apadeśāt labdha-niṣṭha-ākāśādi-nāma-samaya-paryantānāṃ tat-śarīratvam avagamyate \| yasya pṛthivī śarīram, yasya āpaḥ śarīram, yasya tejaḥ śarīram, yasya vāyuḥ śarīram, yasya ākāśaḥ śarīram, yasya akṣaraṃ śarīram, yasya mṛtyuḥ śarīram, evam sarva-bhūta-antarātmā apahata-pāpmā divyaḥ devaḥ ekaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ iti subāla-śrutyā sarva-etanānāṃ paramātma-śarīratvaṃ spaṣṭam abhidhīyate \| ataḥ tasmāt etasmāt ātmanaḥ iti evam-ādi-mayaḥ paramātmā eva śārīra ātmā iti ucyate \| prāṇamayaṃ prakṛtya āha tasya eva eva śārīra ātmā, yaḥ pūrvasya iti \| pūrvasya annamayasya yaḥ śārīra ātmā śruti-antara-siddhaḥ parama-kāraṇa-bhūtaḥ paramātmā; sa eva tasya prāṇamayasya api śārīra ātmā iti arthaḥ \| evaṃ manomaya-vijñānamayayoḥ draṣṭavyam \| ānandamaye tu eṣa eva iti nirdeśaḥ tasya anya-ātmatvaṃ darśayitum \| tat kasya? vijñānamayasya api pūrva-uktayā nītyā paramātmā eva śārīra ātmā iti avagataḥ \| evaṃ sati vijñānamayasya yaḥ śārīra ātmā, sa eva ānandamayasya api śārīra ātmā iti ukte ānandamayasya adhyātma-sādhānāt paramātma-svabhāvasya paramātmanaḥ svayam eva ātmā iti āgamyate \| evaṃ ca svayam atiriktaṃ cetana-acetana-vastu-jātaṃ sva-śarīram iti sa eva nikhila-adhika-śārīra ātmā \| ataḥ eva eṣa param brahma adhikṛtya pravṛttaṃ śāstraṃ śārīrakam iti abhimukhena abhidhīyate \| ato vijñānamayāt jīvāt anya eva paramātmā ānandamayaḥ \|\|13\|\|

āhuḥ—na ayam ānandamayaḥ jīvāt anyaḥ; vikāra-śabdasya mayaṭ-pratyayasya śravaṇāt \| mayaṭ-vaitayoḥ iti prakṛtya nityaṃ vṛddha-śarādibhyaḥ iti vikārārthe mayaṭ smaryate \| vṛddha-śarām ānanda-śabdaḥ \| nanu prācurye api mayaṭ asti tat-prakṛta-vacane mayaṭ iti smṛteḥ \| yathā annamayaḥ yajñaḥ iti \| sa eva ayaṃ bhaviṣyati \| na evam; asambhavaḥ iti upakrame vikāra-arthatvaṃ dṛṣṭam, atra aupacittya-daśāyām api vikāra-arthatvam eva yuktam \| kiñca prācurya-arthatve api jīvāt anyatvaṃ na sidhyati \| tathā hi—ānanda-pracuraḥ iti ukte duḥkham iśram a-sarva-jñeyam \| ānandasya hi prācuryaṃ duḥkhasya alpatvam avagamyate \| duḥkham iśram eva hi jīva-ātmasu \| ataḥ aupacittya-prādhikaraṇyatvam eva yuktam \| kiñca loke mayaṭaṃ hiraṇmayaṃ dārumayā iti ādiṣu vede ca pūrṇamayaḥ juhūḥ rāḍīmayaḥ caruḥ darbhamayī rasanā iti ādiṣu mayaṭaḥ vikāra-arthe prayoga-bāhulyāt sa eva prathamataraḥ dhiyam adhi-

rohati \| jīvasya jñāna-ānanda-vikāratvam asti eva; tasya svata ānanda-rūpasya satatsaṃsāritvāvathā tādṛk-kāraḥ eva iti \| ato vikāravattveno madhusūdyasya śravaṇād ānandamayo jīvād anatirikta iti \| tad eva sūtra-bhāṣya pariharati—

## \|\| 1.1.14 \|\|

*vikāra-śabdāt na iti cet na prācuryāt \| 1 \| 1 \| 14 \|\|*

**Комментарий:**

na iti uktam \| kutaḥ ? prācuryāt—parasmin brahmaṇi ānanda-prācuryāt \| prācurya-arthe ca mayaṭ-saṃbhavāt \| etad uktam bhavati—śāstra-guṇottara-krameṇāmadhyasya mānasyānandasya jīvāśravatāsaṃbhavād brahmāśrayodramānanda iti niścite sati tasmin brahmaṇi vikārāsaṃbhavāt prācurye'pi mayaḍ-vidhi-saṃbhavān nandamayaḥ paraṃ brahma iti \| aupacaryāt prayogaś ca ayaṃ ca mayaṭo vikārārthatva-gaurava-virodhān saṃbhavati \| kiṃ ca aupacaryaṃ prāṇa-mayaḥ eva paridyaktam, tatra vikārārthatāsaṃbhavāt \| anatastatra pañca-vṛtti-vṛttiḥ prāṇa-vṛtti-nimitta-mātreṇa prāṇa-mayatvam; prāṇa-apāna-ādi tu pañcasu vṛttiṣu prāṇa-vṛtteḥ prācuryād vā \| na ca prācuryaṃ mayaṭ-prayogasya prītinihita, aghasamayo yathā: śakaṭa-mayī yātrā ityādiku darśanāt \|\|

yuktam ānanda-maya-mukhya-sukhadaśrutatām avagamya-yogaiḥ; tadusat \| tat-saṃstutvaṃ hi tat-prakṛtatvam; tac cetarasya sataṃ na avagamyati; api tu tasya eva evaṃ nirvartyati \| itarasya drāvāmadhu-bhāvaḥ tu pramāṇāntara-virodhī \| iha ca pramāṇāntareṇa tad-abhāvo 'vagamyate apahata-pāpma ity-ādinā \| tair eva avaitava vaktavyaṃ, brahmānatvasya prabhūtatvamayyānandadyāśrayatvopadiśata iti \| ucyate ca tatra sa ekaḥ mānuṣaḥ ānandaḥ ity-ādinā jīva-ānandāpekṣayā brahmānando nirativāyadśāpantraḥ prabhūta iti \|\|

pañcoktaṃ jīvasya jñāna-ānanda-vikāratvaṃ saṃbhavati iti; tad api na upapadyate, jīvasya jñāna-ānanda-sarūpatvasya kenicidvākāreṇa mṛd iva ghaṭadyākāreṇa pariṇāma-sakala-śruti-smṛti-nyāya-virodhaḥ \| saṃsāra-daśāyāṃ tu karmaṇā jñānānando saṃkucitāv iti upapāditavyate \| ataś ca ānandamayo jīvād anyaḥ paraṃ brahma \|\|14\|\|

itasya jīvād anya ānanda-mayaḥ paraṃ brahma—

## \|\| 1.1.15 \|\|

*tad-dhetu tu vyapadeśāc ca \| 1 \| 1 \| 15 \|\|*

**Комментарий:**

ko hi eva anyāt kaḥ prāṇyāt, yad eva ākāśa ānando na syāt, eṣa hi eva ā'nandayāti iti eṣa eva jīvānandayati iti jīvātmanānanda-hetutvaṃ vyapadiśyate \| ataś ca ānanda-dhigatyā jīva-ānanda-priāvyaṣṭhasya ānanda-mayaḥ paramātmā iti vijñāyate \| ānandasya eva atra ānanda-rūpatvena ucyata iti ca anantarasya eva vakṣyate \|\|

**itaś ca jīvād anyat ānanda-mayaḥ—**

## \|\| 1.1.16 \|\|

*māntra-varṇikam eva ca gīyate \| 1 \| 1 \| 16 \|\|*

**Комментарий:**

satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti mantra-varṇa-uditaṃ brahma ānanda-maya iti gīyate \| tad tu jīva-svarūpād anyat paraṃ brahma \| tathā hi—brahma-vit āpnoti param iti jīvasya prāpyatayā brahma nirdiṣṭam \| tad eṣābhyuktā iti tatu—brahma abhimukhī-kṛtya pratipādyatayā parigṛhya, ṛg eṣā adhyudāhṛtā \| brāhmaṇa-uktasya arthasya vaiśadyam anena mantreṇa kriyata ity arthaḥ; jīvasya upāsakasya prāpyaṃ brahma tasmād vilakṣaṇam eva \| anantaraṃ ca tasmād vā etasmād ātmana ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥ ity ārabhya itarottara-brahmāntam pañca tad eva viśadī-kriyate \| ato jīvād anya ānanda-mayaḥ \|\|16\|\|

atra āha—mukhya-upāsakāyatvasya bhedena bhavitavyam; tathā api na vastv-antaraṃ jīvāsa-mantra-varṇikaṃ brahma; kintu tat-saṃghopāsakasya nirasta-samastāvidyā-gandhi-viśeṣa-cin-mātra-ekasya śuddha-svarūpam, tad eva satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti mantreṇa viśodhyate \| tad eva ca yato vāco nivartante, aprāpya manasā saha iti vāṅ-manasāgocara-tayā nirviśeṣam iti gamyate \| atas tad eva māntra-varṇikam iti tasmād anitarikta ānanda-maya iti \| ata uttaraṃ paṭhati—

## \|\| 1.1.17 \|\|

*na itaraḥ anupapatteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

parasmād īśvaro jīva-avasthābhilapyo muktāvasthaḥ api na bhavati māntra-varṇikaḥ \| kutaḥ? anupapatteḥ \| tathā-vidhasya asato nirupādhikaṃ vipriścitatvaṃ nopapadyate \| idaṃ eva hi nirupādhikaṃ vipriścitatvaṃ saḥ akāmayata, bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti satya-saṅkalpatva-pradarśanena vivriyate \| vividhaṃ paryacinatvaṃ hi, vipriścitatvam \| pūrvedatarāhita-tārakya-cchabda-vācyasya yac-chabdasyā lopaṃ kṛtvā lugādito vipriśca-cchabdaḥ \| yady api muktasya vipriścitatvaṃ saṃbhavati; tathā api tasya eva amṛta-saṃsāra-daśāyām avipriścitatvam upasthiti iti nirupādhikaṃ vipriścitatvaṃ nopapadyate \| nirviśeṣa-cin-mātratā-pakṣasya muktasya vividha-yathā-saṃbhavāt muktasya vipriścitatvaṃ na saṃbhavatīti na kena api pramāṇena nirviśeṣaṃ vastu pratipādyata iti ca pūrvam eva uktam \| yato vāco nivartante iti ca vākyaṃ yadi vāṅ-manasayoḥ brahmaṇo nivṛttim abhidadhīta; na tataḥ nirviśeṣatāṃ vastuno abhyupagataṃ śakyam; api tu vāṅ-manasayoḥ asādhāraṇāṃ vṛtteḥ; tathā ca sati tasya tucchatvam eva āyate \| brahma-vit āpnoti ity ārasya brahmaṇo vipriścitatvaṃ jagat-kāraṇatvaṃ jñānānandaikatvān atiropradhānānanda-vipulatvaṃ kāma-deva-vidacitra-makarasya kṛtsnasya raśatvaṃ śrīya-mantri-veda-uktānāma-katvaṃ bhayābhaya-hetutvaṃ vāyav-ādityādīnāṃ praśāsitṛtvaṃ śāstra-puṇyātīta-vakṣeṇa niratiśayānandatvam ananyacchotṛkaṃ pratipādya vāṅ-manasayoḥ brahmaṇi prabhūta-prāmāṇena niṣpramāṇakaṃ brahma-śūnyatā iti bhrānta-jalpitam \| yato

vācaḥ nivartante iti pañca-śabda-nirdiṣṭa-pūrvaṃ ānandaṃ brahmaṇaḥ vidvān iti ānanda-śabdena pratinirdiśya tasya brahma-saṃbandhitvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ iti vyatireka-nirdeśena pratipādya tat eva vāṅ-manasa-yogyaṃ vidvān iti tad-vettṛbhiḥ vedyatvaṃ jagad-utpatti-ādi-vākya-darśanaṃ vācya-antaraṅgaṃ ca syāt \|\|

ataḥ śāstra-anuguṇyena uttara-krameṇa brahma-ānandasya api niratiśayatāṃ vaktum adhya nirdiśyamānayā asamavāt eva vāṅ-manasayoḥ etataḥ nivṛttiḥ: yataḥ vācaḥ nivartante iti ucyate \| evam iyam sārahitaṃ brahma ānandaṃ vihāya kutaścana na bibheti iti ucyate \| kiñca asya mānavarṇikasya viparyāsaḥ saḥ akāmayata iti ārabhya bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti antasya vakṣyamāṇatva-mūlatvāt jagat-janma-sthiti-jagad-antaryāmitvena ekatma-svarūpa-anyatvaṃ suspaṣṭam \|\|17\|\|

itaḥ ca ubhaya-vyavasthā-sthita-ānandamayaḥ asau ānandamayaḥ--

## \|\| 1.1.18 \|\|

*bheda-vyapadeśāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

tasmāt etasmāt ātmanaḥ iti ātasya mānavarṇikaṃ brahma vyapadeśam asat-prāṇa-mano-maya eva jīvādi tasya bhedaṃ vyapadiśati tasmāt vā etasmāt vijñānamayāt, anyaḥ antaraḥ ātmā ānandamayaḥ iti \| ataḥ jīvādi eṣa vyapadeśārthaṃ mānavarṇikaḥ ānandamayaḥ abhy eṣa iti jñāyate \|\|18\|\|

itaḥ ca jīvāt anyaḥ--

## \|\| 1.1.19 \|\|

*kāmāt ca na anumāna-apekṣā*

**Комментарий:**

jīvasya avidyā-paravaśasya ajña-kāraṇatve hṛṣṭanīyā ātmābhivyakathā-avidyā-śabda-abhidheya-acid-vastu-saṃbandha-apekṣā \| tathā eva hi bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti anvasaṃsṛjata iti asya sva-kāma eva vividha-vicid-acid-vastu-sṛṣṭiḥ idaṃ sarvam asṛjata, yad idaṃ kiñca iti āmanāyate \| ataḥ asya ānandamayasya jagat-hetutaḥ na ānumānika-acid-ravya-saṃbandha-apekṣā pratīyate \| ataḥ ca jīvāt asya ānandamayaḥ \|\|19\|\|

itaḥ ca--

## \|\| 1.1.20 \|\|

*asmin asya ca tad-yogaṃ śāsti*

**Комментарий:**

asmin ānandamaye, asya jīvasya, tad-yogaṃ ānanda-yogam, śāsti śāstram raso vai saḥ, rasaṃ hi eva ayam labdhvā ānandī bhavati \| rasa-śabda-abhidheya-ānandamaya-lābhāt evaṃ jīva-śabda-abhilakṣaṇīyaḥ ānandī bhavati iti ucyamāne yaḥ ca asau ānandī bhavati, saḥ saḥ eva iti avagamyataḥ kaḥ brahma iti darśaḥ \|\|

evam ānanda-mayaḥ paraṃ brahma iti nirdiśyate sati yathā eṣa ākāśa ānandaḥ; vijñānam ānandaṃ brahma ityādibhyānanda-śabdenānanda-maya eva parāmṛśyate; yathā vijñāna-śabdena vijñāna-mayaḥ \| ata eva ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān iti vyatireka-nirdeśaḥ \| ata eva ca ānanda-mayam ātmānam upasaṅkrāmati iti phala-nirdeśaś ca \| uttare ca anuvāke pūrva-anuvākoktānām upasaṃhārādhītānām arthaṃ brahma iti vyajānāt prāṇo brahma iti vyajānāt mano brahma iti vyajānāt vijñānaṃ brahma iti vyajānāt iti pratipādanāt ānando brahma iti api ānanda-mayasya eva pratipādanam iti vijñāyate; tat eva ca tatrāpi ānanda-mayam ātmānam upasaṅkramya iti upasaṃhṛtam \| ataḥ pradhāna-śabdattilapy asyārthāntara-bhūtasya parasya brahmaṇo jīva-śabdābhilapanīyasya api vāstuto ’khila-rasaṃ siddham \|\| 20 \|\|

iti ānanda-mayādhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha antarādhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|**

yady api manda-puṇyānāṃ jīvānāṃ kāmāṅgala-sthitiḥ iti sthitānanda-yoge bhayābhyaya-hetutvam iṅgādi na saṃbhavati; tathāpi vilakṣaṇa-puṇyānām arciś-candra-jāpati-prabhṛtīnāṃ saṃbhavati eva iti mātrā-śaṅkāṃ nirākaroti—

## \|\| 1.1.21 \|\|

*antaraḥ tad-dharmopadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

iha āmnāyate chāndogye atha ya eṣaḥ antar-āditye hiraṇ-mayaḥ puruṣo dṛśyate hiraṇya-śmaśruḥ hiraṇya-keśa āpraṇakhāt sarva eva suvarṇaḥ, tasya yathā kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkam evam akṣiṇī tasya ut iti nāma sa eṣa sarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhyaḥ uditaḥ ud eti ha vai sarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhyaḥ ya evaṃ veda, tasya ark ca sāma ca geṣṇau iti adhidaivatam; atha ya eṣaḥ antar-akṣiṇi puruṣo dṛśyate sa eva ark tat sāma tat ukthaṃ tat yajus tat brahma tasya etasya tat eva rūpaṃ yad amuṣya rūpaṃ yāv amuṣya geṣṇau tau geṣṇau yat nāma tat nāma iti \|\|

tatra sandihyate—kim ayam akṣy-āditya-maṇḍalāntar-vartī puruṣaḥ puṇyopacaya-nimitteśvaryām ādityādi-śabdābhilapyo jīva eva; āhosvit tad-vitiriktāḥ paramātmā iti \| kiṃ yuktam? upacita-puṇyo jīva eva iti \| kutaḥ? sa-śarīratva-śravaṇāt \| śarīra-saṃbandho hi jīvānām eva saṃbhavati \| karmānuguṇa-priyāpriya-yogāya hi śarīra-saṃbandhaḥ \| ata eva hi karma-saṃbandha-rahitasya mokṣasya prāptavyam aśarīratvena ucyate na ha vai sa-śarīrasya sataḥ priyāpriyayor apahatir asti, aśarīraṃ vā va santaṃ na priyāpriye spṛśataḥ iti \| saṃbhavati ca puṇyātiśayāt jñānādhikyam, śaktyādhikyaṃ ca \| ata eva loka-kāmeśvaratvādi tasya upapadyate \| tata eva ca upāsyatvam, phala-dāyitvam, pāpa-kṣapaṇa-karatva eva mokṣopayogitvaṃ ca \| manuṣyebhya upacita-puṇyāḥ kecic jñāna-

vitsādi-miradhikarā hṛdyante; tataḥ siddhānta-bandhiyaḥ; tatasra devāḥ; tataḥ caitra-ādayaḥ \| ato hārditva-anyatam eva eka-ekasmin kale puṇya-viśeṣa-upacitam aiśvaryaṃ prāptaḥ jāgrat-puruṣasya api karoti iti ahaṅkārāt vajagad-anantara-ātma-bhāva-ādi-vyapadiṣṭham eva upacita-puṇya-viśeṣe sarveśe sarveśatāṃ vaktte \| tato na jīvāt atiriktaḥ paramātmā nāma kaścid asti \| evaṃ ca sati ayadulam anubrahmavyādyo jīvātmanaḥ tad-rūpa-mithyā bhavanti \| mokṣa-śāstrāṇy api tattva-rūpa-tataḥ-tad-upāya-upadeśa-parāṇi iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate—ananta-sadṛśa-upadeśāt; antar āditye antar akṣiṇi ca, yaḥ puruṣaḥ tatrañcyate, saḥ jīvāt anyaḥ paramātmā eva; kutaḥ? tad-dharma-upadeśāt—jīva-ādi-saṃbhavena itara-viniṣṭaratve ca ramānantāno dharmā upasaṃhṛtāpān tad itiḥ saḥ eva sarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhyaḥ uditaḥ iti-ādinā upadeśate \| apahata-pāpmatvaṃ hi apahata-karmatvam; karma eva pratyag-ātma-yathita-svāmithya-arthaḥ \| karma-adhīna-sukha-duḥkha-ādhāritvena karma-vaśyā hi jīvāḥ \| ataḥ apahata-pāpmatvaṃ jīvāt anyasya paramātmanaḥ eva dharmaḥ \| tadvat evaṃ rūpa-ādikam loka-kāmaśitvam, tasmin ca api evam ādikaṃ sarva-bhūta-antarātmatvaṃ ca tasya eva dharmaḥ \| yathā āha eṣaḥ ātmā apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ avijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpaḥ iti; tathā eṣaḥ sarva-bhūta-antarātmā apahata-pāpmā divyaḥ devaḥ ekaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ iti \| yaḥ akṣaram ayad, bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti iti-ādi-satya-saṅkalpatva-pūrvaka-samasta-cid-acid-vastu-sūkṣma-upāyaḥ niratiśayika-satya-saṅkalpatvam, vāṅ-manasā-aparimita-guṇa-pariccheda-rahitān avadhika-atiśaya-ānanda-upāyaḥ iti avyakta-karma-saṃsāra-svabhāvikā dharmāḥ jīvasya na saṃbhavanti \|\|

yat tu śarīra-saṃbandhāt jīvāt atiriktam iti uktam; tad asat, na hi sa-śarīratvaṃ karma-vaśyatāṃ sādhayati, satya-saṅkalpatve ca icchayā api śarīra-saṃbandha-saṃbhavāt \| atha ucyeta—śarīraṃ nāma triguṇa-ātmaka-prakṛti-pariṇāma-rūpa-bhūta-saṅghātaḥ; tat-saṃbandhaḥ ca apahata-pāpmanaḥ satya-saṅkalpasya puruṣasya icchayā na saṃbhavati, apuruṣārthatvāt \| karma-vaśyasya tu svarga-ādi-upaphala-upabhogāya anicchataḥ api tat-saṃbandhaḥ durjñeyaḥ iti \| syāt etad evaṃ yadi guṇa-mayaḥ prākṛtaḥ asya dehaḥ syāt; na tu sva-abhimata-sva-anurūpaḥ ajāhataḥ eva iti sarvam upapannam \|\|

etad uktaṃ bhavati—parasya brahmaṇaḥ nikhila-heya-pratyanīka-ananta-jñāna-ānanda-eka-svarūpatayā sakala-itara-vicakṣaṇasya svābhāvika-anavadhika-atiśaya-asaṅkhyeya-kalyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇāḥ ca santi \| tad eva sva-abhimāna-eka-mahāparicchinna-divya-adbhuta-nitya-niravadya-niratiśaya-aujvalya-rūpeṇa bhogyatayā ananya-ucita-ananta-guṇa-gaṇa-nidhi-divya-rūpam api svābhāvikam asti \| tad-deva-upāsaka-anugraheṇa tat-tad-viparyaya-rūpaṃ saṃsthānaṃ karoti apāra-kāruṇya-sauśīlya-vātsalya-ādi-guṇa-nidhiḥ; nitarāṃ nirvicchinna-heyān api apahata-pāpmā paramātmā paraṃ brahma puruṣottamaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ iti \|\|

śato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante sat eva somya idam āsīt ātmā vā idam eka eva agre āsīt eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt na brahmā na īśānaḥ ityādiṣu nikhila-jagat-eka-kāraṇatayā avātatya parasya brahmaṇaḥ satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma, vijñānam ānandaṃ brahma ityādi-bheda-śruteḥ svacchūmitva-vyavacchede \| nirguṇam nirañjanam apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ avijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpaḥ san tasya kāryaṃ karaṇaṃ ca vidyate na tat-samaḥ ca abhyadhikaḥ ca dṛśyate \| parā asya śaktiḥ vividhā eva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca \| tam īśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśvaraṃ taṃ devatānāṃ paramaṃ ca daivatam na kāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipādhipaḥ na ca asya kaścit janitā na ca adhipaḥ sarvāṇi rūpāṇi vicintya dhīraḥ, nāmāni kṛtvā abhivadan yat āste veda aham etaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntam, āditya-varṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt sarve nimeṣāḥ jajñire vidyutaḥ puruṣāt adhi ityādiṣu parasya brahmaṇaḥ prākṛta-heya-guṇāt prākṛta-heya-deha-saṃbandhe tat-mūla-karma-vaśyatā-saṃbandhe ca pratiṣidhya kalyāṇa-guṇān kalyāṇa-rūpaṃ ca vadanti \|\|

tat idaṃ svābhāvikam eva rūpam upāsaka-anugraheṇa tat-tat-vipada-muguṇa-ākāraṃ deva-manuṣya-ādi-sansthāṃ karoti sva-icchayā eva parama-kāruṇikaḥ bhagavān \| tat idam sāha śrutiḥ—ajāyamānaḥ bahudhā vijāyate iti \| smṛtiḥ ca ajaḥ api san avyaya-ātmā bhūtānām īśvaraḥ api san \| prakṛtiṃ svām adhiṣṭhāya saṃbhavāmi ātma-māyayā \|\| parityāṇāya sādhūnāṃ vināśāya ca duṣkṛtām, iti \| sādhyaḥ hi u, pāṭhakaḥ; tatra tatra aṇam eva uddheyam, āduḥ jijñāsuḥ tu duṣkṛtāṃ vināśaḥ; saṅkalpa-mātreṇa api tat-upapatteḥ \| prakṛtiṃ svām iti prakṛtiḥ—svabhāvaḥ \| svam eva svabhāvam āsthāya, na saṃsāriṇāṃ svabhāvam iti arthaḥ \| ātma-māyayā iti sva-sandūlpa-icchā-pa jñāna iti arthaḥ \| māyā vayunaṃ jñānam iti jñāna-paryāyam api māyā-śabdaṃ naighaṇṭukā adhīyate \| āha ca bhagavān parāśaraḥ—samasta-aśakti-rūpāṇi tat karoti jana-īśvara \| deva-tiryaṅ-manuṣya-ākhyā-ceṣṭāvanti sva-līlayā \| jagatām upakārāya na sā karma-nimittajā \|\| iti \| mahābhārate ca avatāra-rūpasya api aprākṛtatvam ucyate—na bhūta-saṃsthānaḥ dehaḥ asya paramātmanaḥ iti \| ataḥ parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ evaṃ-rūpatva-vacanāt yam api tasya eva dharmaḥ \| ata āditya-maṇḍala-stha-adhikaraṇaḥ āditya-ādi-jīva-vyatiriktaḥ paramātmā eva \|\|21\|\|

## \|\| 1.1.22 \|\|

*bheda-vyapadeśāt ca anyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

āditya-ādi-jīvebhyaḥ bhedaḥ vyapadiśyate asya paramātmanaḥ—yaḥ āditye tiṣṭhan ādityāt antaraḥ yam ādityaḥ na veda yasya ādityaḥ śarīraṃ yaḥ ādityam antaraḥ yamayati yaḥ ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaraḥ yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ yaḥ ātmānam antaraḥ yamayati yaḥ akṣaram antare sañcaran yasya akṣaraṃ śarīraṃ yam akṣaraṃ na veda yaḥ mṛtyum antare sañcaran yasya mṛtyuḥ śarīraṃ yaṃ

muktiḥ na veda eva sarveśvara-atitaratama-avyavahāra-mātrā divyaḥ devaḥ ekaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ iti ca ārkṣya-pāṭhataḥ pāramyaḥ paramātmanaḥ svavici jīvāt hariḥ eva na vyapadiśya tena amṛta-rāmatvena eva vyapadiśati \| avastha-bhedyaḥ hiraṇyagarbha-ādi-jīva-vyodṛśyaḥ eva paramātmā iti siddham \|\| 22 \|\| iti antara-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|

**atha ākāśa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|**

śrutaḥ vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante iti jagat-kāraṇaṃ brahma iti avadhṛte \| kiṃ tat jagat-kāraṇatva-niścaya-upāyāṃ śrīdeva somya idam agre āsīt, śrītejaḥ ajaḥ aśrujata śrīātmā vā idam ekaḥ eva agre āsīt śrī imān lokān sṛjata śrīsabhīkṣā etasmāt ātmanaḥ ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ iti sādhāraṇeṣu śabdeṣu kāraṇe nirdiṣṭe īkṣaṇa-viśeṣa-ānanda-viśeṣa-rūpa-viśeṣaḥ ca prabhāvāti-apradhāna-kṣetrajña-ādi-vyāvṛtti-liṅgaiḥ yuktaḥ \| itara-avībhāśāśādi-viśeṣa-śabdeniścīya jagat-kāraṇatva-jñānāṃ artha-ādivat dyakāya-ādi-śabda-abhipretayā prasiddha-cittva-vibhutva-jñairaiścitra-mukha-lakṣaṇam eva brahma iti pratipādyate śrīākāśaḥ tat-liṅgāt iti atra etat vākya-upetena \|

## \|\| 1.1.23 \|\|

*ākāśaḥ tat-liṅgāt*

**Комментарий:**

idam āmnāyate chāndogye—śrīasya lokasya kā gatiḥ iti ākāśaḥ iti ha uvāca sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni ākāśāt eva samutpadyante ākāśaṃ prati astaṃ yanti ākāśaḥ hi eva ebhyaḥ jyāyān ākāśaḥ parāyaṇam śrīiti \| tatra sandehaḥ—kiṃ prasiddha-ākāśaḥ eva atra ākāśa-śabdena abhidhīyate, uta uttara-lakṣaṇā eva brahma iti \| kiṃ prāptam? prasiddha-ākāśaḥ iti \| kutaḥ? śabdaiḥ rūḍham abhigamye vastuni yaḥ eva arthaḥ vyutpatti-siddhaḥ śabdete pratīyeta saḥ eva gṛhītavyaḥ \| ataḥ prasiddha-ākāśaḥ eva cara-acara-bhūta-jātasya kṛtsnasya kāraṇam \| asat-sadāt anirdishtaṃ brahma \|\| na rtakṣāt pūrva-kṛt-ghaṭa-ādim iva cetanāt jīvāt ca vyatiriktaṃ brahma yuktam \| satyam uktam, ayuktaṃ tu tat \| tathā hi śrutaḥ vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante......tat brahma iti ukte kutaḥ imāni bhūtāni jāyante iti ādi-viśeṣa-apekṣāyāṃ sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni ākāśāt eva samutpadyante iti ādinā viśeṣa-jñātaḥ jagat-janma-ādi-kāraṇam ākāśaḥ eva iti niścīyate sati śrīdeva somya idam agre āsīt iti ādi-vacanam api sad-vācaka-śabda-sādhāraṇa-ākāra-artham eva viśeṣa-prakāśakṣiyarthi \| śrīātmā vā idam ekaḥ eva agre āsīt iti ādi-vākyam anadoṣapi tatra iva vartate \| tatra api hi cetanāntaratvaṃ na saṃbhavati; yathā mṛt-ātmakaḥ ghaṭaḥ iti \| ātmanā utthābhīti vyutpattyā sutarām ākāśaḥ api ātma-śabdaḥ vartate \| ataḥ ubhaya-ākāśaḥ eva kāraṇaṃ brahma iti niścīyate sti īkṣaṇāti-anantatva-guṇāḥ gauṇāḥ varṇanīyāḥ \| yadi hi sādhāraṇa-śabdaiḥ eva sat-ādibhiḥ kāraṇam abhyadhāsyata; īkṣaṇa-anarthanirūḍhena caitanya-viśeṣaḥ eva kāraṇam iti niracekṣyata \| ākāśa-śabdena tu viśeṣaḥ eva niścitaḥ iti na artha-antara-abhāvyāt liṅga-samāhiti \|\|

nanu ātmanan ākāśa-saṃbhūtaḥ iti ākāśasya api kāryatvaṃ pratīyate \| satyam \| sarvavyām eva ākāśa-vākya-adarśanāt \| sūkṣma-avasthā sthūla-avasthā ca iti avasthā-dvayam asti \| tatra ākāśasya sūkṣma-avasthā kāraṇam, sthūla-avasthā tu kāryam \| ātmanan ākāśa-saṃbhūtaḥ iti svayam eva sūkṣma-rūpāt svayaṃ sthūla-rūpa-saṃbhūtaḥ iti arthaḥ \| san-mūlāḥ hi vā imāni bhūtāni ākāśād eva samutpadyante iti sarveṣāṃ jagata ākāśād eva prabhava-ādi-ādi-viśravaṇād eva hi kāraṇaṃ brahma iti niścitam \| yata evaṃ prasiddha-ākāśa-anatiriktaṃ brahma, ata eva ca ya eṣa ākāśa ānando na syāt ākāśo ha vai nāma-rūpayor nirvahitā iti evam-ādi-nirdeśo 'pi upapannataraḥ \| ataḥ prasiddha-ākāśa-anatiriktaṃ brahma iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte brūmaḥ—ākāśaḥ tat-liṅgāt \| prākāśa-śabda-abhidheyaḥ prasiddha-ākāśād etan-nānārtha-antara-bhūto yathokta-lakṣaṇaḥ paramātmā eva \| kutaḥ ? tat-liṅgāt—nikhila-jagat-eka-kāraṇatvam, sarveṣām ucchrāya-asattvam, parāyaṇatvam iti-ādīni paramātmani liṅgāni upalabhyante \| nikhilatara-pādaṃ hi avikṛta-puruḥ prasiddha-ākāśa-śabda-abhidheyasya na upapadyate, cetana-vastunas tat-kāryatva-asambhavāt \| parāyaṇatvaṃ ca cetanānāṃ parama-prāpyatvam \| tat ca acetanasya heyasya sakala-puruṣārtha-virodhitvān na saṃbhavati \| sarveṣām ucchrāya-asattvaṃ ca nirupādhikaṃ sarvaiḥ kalyāṇa-guṇais sarvebhyo niratisaya-ucchrāyaḥ \| tad acetano na upapadyate \|\|

yad uktaṃ jani-kāraṇa-viśeṣatā-aṅgīkāre ākāśa-śabdena viśeṣa-samarpanād yan yat sa eva tad-rūpam eva varṇanīyam iti, tad ayuktam; san-mūlāḥ hi vā imāni bhūtāni ākāśād eva samutpadyante iti prasiddhavat nirdeśāt \| prasiddhavat nirdeśo hi pramāṇa-antara-adhigama-apekṣate \| pramāṇa-antarāṇi ca yad eva somya idam agre āsīt iti evam-ādīni iha eva vākyāni \| tāni ca yathodita-prakāreṇa eva brahma pratipādayanti iti tat-pratipāditaṃ brahma ākāśa-śabdena prasiddhavat abhidhīyate \| saṃbhavati ca parasya brahmaṇaḥ prakāśakatvād ākāśa-śabda-abhidheyatvam—ākāśate, ākāśayati ca iti \| kiñca anena ākāśa-śabdena viśeṣa-samarpana-akṣameṇa api cetanānāṃ pratyakṣa-abhimata-kāraṇatvam acetana viśeṣa-abhidhānena na etad īdṛśaṃ bahu syāṃ prajāyeya gaur akāmayata, bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti-ādi-vākya-vihita-upādhi-carita-sāṅkhya-satya-saṅkalpatva-ādi-viśiṣṭa-pūrvoktha-śruti-nipāta-samarthana-vyāyasyikaraṇaṃ na pramāṇa-padavīm adhirohati \| evam avagacchanti viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭa-pūrvoktha-śruti-nipāta-samarthanika-vākyāni sāmānye caitanya-anubhava-asambhavana-akṣamatvaṃ na hāpayete \|\|

yat tu idaṃ śabdasya ananta-kāmato na bhavati, mṛdaṃ ghaṭa iti-ādi-darśanādi yad uktam; tatra ucyate—yadi api cetanāvat acetanam api bhavituṃ asat-śabdaḥ prayujyate; tathāpi śarīra-sthiti-cid-vidhayā samā-śabdasya prayogāt cyutiḥ ātmā vā idam eka eva agre āsīt ātmanan ākāśa-saṃbhūtaḥ iti-ādiṣu śarīra-pratisaṃbaddhi-cetana eva pratīyate \| yathā go-śabdasya na ekārthyāt aviditve api prayoga-

prāṇa-vṛttitvādi-dyunaiva svataḥ pratīyate; arthāntara-prāṇaḥ tu tat-sādhāraṇa-nirdeśa-apekṣaḥ; tathā svataḥ prāptau śarīra-pratisambandhi-cetanā-abhidhānena eva saḥ īkṣata lokān nu sṛjā iti saḥ aikṣat, bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti ādi-tat-tad-ātma-vinirdeśāḥ eva sthiteḥ upakurvanti \| evaṃ vākya-śeṣa-avadhārita-tan-mādhāraṇa-aneka-apūrva-artha-vihiṃsu nirbīja-lagudeśa-kāraṇa sat eva somya idam iti ādi-vākya-siddhaṃ brahma eva ākāśa-śabdena prasiddhavat sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni iti ādi-vākyena nirdiśyate iti siddham \|\|23\|\| iti ākāśādhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|

**atha prāṇādhikaraṇam \|\|6\|\|**

## \|\| 1.1.24 \|\|

*ataḥ eva prāṇaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

idam āmnāyate chāndogye—prastotaḥ yā devatā prastāvam anvāyattā iti prastutya katamā sā devatā iti prāṇaḥ iti ha uvāca sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni prāṇam eva abhisaṃviśanti prāṇam abhyujjihate sā eṣā devatā prastāvam anvāyattā tām cet avidvān prāstoṣyaḥ mūrdhā te vyapatiṣyat iti \| atra prāṇa-śabdaḥ mukhya-prāṇa-artha-vṛttiḥ chāyāpattiḥ itte parasmin eva brahmaṇi vartate, tat-sādhāraṇa-nikhila-jagad-avekṣaṇa-krama-ādi-darśanāt siddhavat viśeṣaḥ \|\|

adhikā śaṅkā tu—kṛtsnasya bhūta-jātasya prāṇa-adhīna-sthiti-pravṛtti-ādi-darśanāt prasiddhaḥ eva prāṇaḥ jagat-kāraṇatayā nirdeśam arhati iti \| parihāraḥ tu—śilā-kāṣṭha-ādiṣu caitanya-abhāve ca tad-bhāvāt sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni prāṇam eva abhisaṃviśanti prāṇam abhyujjihate iti na upapadyate iti \| ataḥ prāṇapati sarvāṇi bhūtāni iti kṛtvā paraṃ brahma eva prāṇa-śabdena abhidhīyate \| ataḥ prasiddha-ākāśa-prāṇa-ādeḥ rūpasya eva nikhila-jagad-eka-kāraṇam amṛtatva-ātma-ātma-bhāva-aiśvarya-satya-satya-dūra-vadhvāna-natva-kalyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇaṃ paraṃ brahma eva ākāśa-prāṇa-ādi-śabda-abhidheyam iti siddham \|\|24\|\| iti prāṇādhikaraṇam \|\|6\|\|

**atha jyotiradhikaraṇam \|\|10\|\|**

ataḥ paraṃ jagat-kāraṇatva-vyājena yena kena api niratisaya-yogeśvaryeṇa yuktaṃ jyotiḥ-indra-ādi-devadeśāntara-siddhaṃ vyabhicāryamānaṃ paraṃ brahma taiḥ abhidīyate jyotiḥ-caraṇa-abhidhānāt iti ādinā \|\|

## \|\| 1.1.25 \|\|

*jyotiḥ-caraṇa-abhidhānāt*

**Комментарий:**

idam āmnāyate chāndogye atha yat ataḥ paraḥ divaḥ jyotiḥ dīpyate viśvataḥ pṛṣṭheṣu sarvataḥ pṛṣṭheṣu anuttameṣu uttameṣu lokeṣu idaṃ vāva tat idam asmin antaḥ puruṣe jyotiḥ iti \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim ayam jyotiḥ-śabdena nirdiṣṭaḥ niratisaya-dīpti-yukta-arthaḥ prasiddha-āditya-ādi-dyotiḥ eva kāraṇa-

bhūtaṃ brahma, utpatta-samasta-cicid-vastu-sajātīya-parama-kāraṇa-bhūtataḥ ananta-sabhāsa-deśa-saṅkhyā-saṅkhyā-rūpaḥ puruṣottama iti \| kiṃ yuktam? prasiddham eva jyotiḥ iti \| kutaḥ? prasiddhavat-deśody-ākāśa-prāṇādivat sva-vākya-upāsana-para-sāmarthyāt liṅga-viśeṣa-darśanāt, parama-puruṣa-pratyabhijñāna-asaṃbhavāt, kaukṣeya-jyotir-viṣaya-upadeśāt ca prasiddham eva jyotiḥ kāraṇatva-vyāpti-vistṛti-yoga-ujjvala-ākāraṃ brahma iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—jyotiḥ-śravaṇa-abhidhānāt \| dyu-saṃbandhi-karaṇa nirdiṣṭaṃ niratastaya-dīpti-yuktaṃ jyotiḥ parama-puruṣaḥ eva \| kutaḥ? śrutāt do tasya sarvā bhūtāni tripāt asya amṛtaṃ divi iti etya eva dyu-saṃbandha-śravaṇena sarva-bhūta-abhidhānāt \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—yady api atha yat ataḥ paraḥ divaḥ jyotiḥ iti asmin vākye parama-puruṣa-asādhāraṇa-liṅgaṃ na upalabhyate; tathā api pūrva-vākye dyu-saṃbandhitayā parama-puruṣasya nirdeśāt dīptam api dyu-saṃbandhi-jyotiḥ asya eva iti pratyabhijñāyate iti \| kaukṣeya-jyotir-viṣaya-upadeśaḥ ca phalāya tad-ātmakatva-anusandhāna-vidhiḥ iti na kaścit doṣaḥ \| kaukṣeya-jyotiḥ brahma tad-ātmakatvaṃ bhagavatā svayam eva uktam—ahaṃ vaiśvānaraḥ bhūtvā prāṇināṃ deham āśritaḥ iti \|\|25\|\|

## \|\| 1.1.26 \|\|

*chandaḥ-abhidhānāt na iti cet na tathā cetaḥ-arpaṇa-nigamāt tathā hi darśanam*

**Комментарий:**

pūrvasmin vākye gāyatrī vai idaṃ sarvam iti gāyatra-ākhyaṃ chandaḥ abhidhāya tat etat catuṣpāt vākyaṃ mithyubhāratatparaḥ tadvān anya mahimā iti asya ṛcaḥ api chandaḥ-viṣayatā-mātra parama-puruṣa-abhidhānam iti cet; tat na, tathā cetaḥ-arpaṇa-nigamāt—na gāyatrī-śabdena chandaḥ-mātram iha vidhīyate, chandaḥ-mātrasya sarvātmakatva-anupapatteḥ; api tu brahma eva gāyatrī-cetaḥ-arpaṇena nigamyate \| brahmaṇi gāyatrī-sādṛśya-anusandhānaṃ phalāya upadiśyate iti arthaḥ \| saṃbhavati ca pādaḥ tasya sarvā-

bhūtāni, tripāt asya amṛtaṃ divi iti catuṣpadaḥ brahmaṇaḥ catuṣpadayā gāyatryā ca sādṛśyam \| catuṣpadā ca gāyatrī kvacit dṛśyate \| tathā ayam indrasya ca upatiḥ, balena pṛthivī, kukṣyavetau dyauḥ, simisumāsariḥ iti \| tathā hi anyatra api gāyatryāḥ chandaḥ-abhidhāyī śabdaḥ anyatra antare prayujyamānaḥ dṛśyate \| yathā saṃvarga-vidyāyām te vai ete pañca anye pañca anye daśa saṃpadyante iti ārabhya sā eṣā virāṭ annādī ucyate \|\|26\|\|

itaḥ ca gāyatrī-śabdena brahma eva abhidhīyate—

## \|\| 1.1.27 \|\|

*bhūtādi-pāda-vyapadeśa-upapatteḥ ca evam*

**Комментарий:**

bhūta-pṛthivī-śarīra-hṛdayāni nirdiśya sā eṣā catuṣpadā iti vyapadeśaḥ brahmaṇā eva gāyatrī-śabdena abhidheyam upapadyate \|\|27\|\|

## \|\| 1.1.28 \|\|

*upadeśa-bhedāt na iti cet na ubhayasmin api avirodhāt*

**Комментарий:**

pūrva-vākye śrīnivāsa-mūrtaṃ divi iti divaḥ adhikaraṇatvena nirdeśādi-dṛś ca divaḥ paraḥ hṛdi adhi-divena nirdiśyamānasya api bhinna-rūpatvena pūrva-vākya-uktaṃ brahma param iti eva pratyabhijñāyate iti cec; tan na, ubhayasminn api upadeśe artha-sāmarthyena pratyabhijñāyāḥ avirodhāt; yathā vṛkṣāgre śyenaḥ vṛkṣāgra-stha-rathya iti \| tasmāt sarva-puruṣa eva niratiriśaya-tejaskaḥ divaḥ paraḥ jyotiḥ iti ucyata iti pratipādyate \| śrutāvānasya mahimā, ato jyāyāṃś ca puruṣaḥ; pādaḥ asya viśvā bhūtāni, tripāt asya amṛtaṃ divi iti pratipāditasya catuṣ-padaḥ parama-puruṣasya veda aham etaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntam, āditya-varṇaṃ tamasas tu pāre iti abhihitam aprākṛta-rūpasya tejaḥ adhyaprākṛtam iti tadetatya sa eva jyotiḥ-śabda-abhidheya iti niravadyam \|\| iti jyotir-adhikaraṇam \|\| 10 \|\|

**atha indra-prāṇa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 11 \|\|**

niratiriśaya-divya-jyotiḥ-śabda-abhidheyaṃ prasiddha-varitṛtvaṃ parama-puruṣam evaṃ yuktam \| idānīṃ kāraṇatva-vyapadeśa-bhūtatva-prāpti-upāsana-upāsyatvena śruta indra-prāṇa-ādi-śabda-abhidheyaḥ api parama-puruṣaḥ eva iti āha—

## \|\| 1.1.29 \|\|

*prāṇaḥ tathā anugamāt*

**Комментарий:**

kauṣītaki-brāhmaṇe pratardana-vidyāyāṃ pradarśano ha vai daivodāsir indraṃ dhāmo ’pajagāma yuddhena ca pauruṣeṇa cetyārasya varaṃ vṛṇīṣveti vaktārimindraṃ prati tvam eva me varaṃ vṛṇīṣva yaṃ tvaṃ manuṣyāya hitatamaṃ manyase iti pratardanokte, sa hovāca prāṇo ’smi prajñātmā taṃ mām āyur amṛtam ityupāssveti śrūyate \| tatra saṃśayaḥ— kim asyaṃ hitatamopāsanakamaitreyoprāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭo jīva eva; uta tad-atiriktaḥ paramātmeti \| kiṃ yuktam? jīva eveti \| kutaḥ? indra-śabdasya jīva-viśeṣa eva prasiddheḥ; tat-samānādhikaraṇasya prāṇa-śabdasyāpi tatraiva vṛtteḥ \| ayam indrābhimānī jīvaḥ pratardanena tvam eva me varaṃ vṛṇīṣva yaṃ tvaṃ manuṣyāya hitatamaṃ manyase ityuktaḥ māmayurupāssveti svātmaivopāsanaṃ hitatam upadiśet \| hitatamaśrāmṛtatvaprāptyupāya eva \| jagatkāraṇopāsanasaṃvāyāmṛtatvaprāptihetutā tasya tāvad eva ciraṃ yāvan na vimokṣye atha sampatsye ityavagatā \| ataḥ prasiddha-jīva-bhāva indra eva kāraṇaṃ brahma \| ityāśaṅkāpāmardhīyate—prāṇastathānugamāditi \| ayam indra-prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭo na jīva-mātram; api tu jīvādyantarbhūtaṃ paraṃ brahma \| sa eva prāṇa eva prajñātmā ānando ’jaro ’mṛtaḥ ityādi-praśna-śabdābhyaṃ prastutasyānantaramātṛśabdasamānādhikaraṇyenānugamo hi tathā satyopapadyate \|\| 29 \|\|

## \|\| 1.1.30 \|\|

*na vaktuḥ ātma-upadeśāt iti cet adhyātma-saṃbandha-bhūmā hi asmin*

**Комментарий:**

yat uktam indra-prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭasya ānandādi ajaraḥ amṛtaḥ iti anena akāryatvaṃ paraṃ brahma iti, tatra api ucyate; mām eva vijānīhi prāṇaḥ asmi prajñātmā taṃ mām āyuḥ amṛtam iti upāssva iti vaktā ha upendraḥ tricīrṣāṇaṃ tvāṣṭram ahanam iti avamardinā tvāṣṭra-vadhādibhiḥ prajñāta-jīva-bhāvasya svātmānam eva upāsyaṃ prativedanāya upadiśati \| ataḥ upakrame jīva-viśeṣaḥ iti ayate sati ānandādi ajaraḥ amṛtaḥ iti ādi-viśeṣa-parihāraḥ trivat-anuguṇaḥ eva varṇanīyaḥ iti cet; pariharati—adhyātma-saṃbandha-bhūmā hi asmin—ātmani yat saṃbandhavat tat adhyātma-saṃbandhaḥ \| tasya bhūmā-bhūyastvam,—bahutvam ityarthaḥ \| ātmani adhyeyatayā saṃbandhyamānānāṃ bahutvena saṃbandha-bahutvam \| tat ca asmin vaktari paramātmā eva hi saṃbhavati \| tathā rātha-mātreṣu nainiriṣpitā nābhāvā adhvitā evam eva etā bhūta-mātrāḥ prajñā-mātrāsu ārpitāḥ prajñā-mātrāḥ prāṇe arpitāḥ saḥ eva prāṇaḥ eva prajñātmā ānandadaḥ ajaraḥ amṛtaḥ iti bhūta-mātrā-śabdena acetana-vastu-jātam abhidhāya prajñā-mātra-śabdena tad-ādhāratayā cetana-vargaṃ ca abhidhāya tasya api ādhāratayā prakṛtam indra-prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭaṃ nirdiśya tam eva ānandadaḥ ajaraḥ amṛtaḥ iti upadiśati \| tat evaṃ cetana-acetana-ātmaka-kṛtsna-vastu-ādhāratvaṃ jīva-antaryāmini abhūtam indu paramātmani eva oapapadyata ityarthaḥ \|\|

athavā, adhyātma-saṃbandha-bhūmā hi asmin—paramātma-sādhāraṇa-dharma-saṃbandhaḥ adhyātma-saṃbandhaḥ \| tasya bhūmā—bahutvaṃ hi asmin prakaraṇe vidyate \| tathā hi—prathamaṃ tvam eva me varaṃ vṛṇīṣva yaṃ tvaṃ manuṣyāya hitatamaṃ manyase iti, mām upāssva iti ca paramātma-sādhāraṇa-mokṣa-sādhana-upāsana-karmatvaṃ prāṇa-śabda-nirddiṣṭasya indrasya pratīyate \| tathā eṣa eva sādhu karma kārayati taṃ yam ebhyaḥ lokebhyaḥ unniniṣati eṣa eva asādhu karma kārayati taṃ yam adhaḥ ninīṣati iti sarvasya karmaṇaḥ kārayitṛtvaṃ ca paramātma-dharmaḥ \| tathā nathā ratha-māreṣu nainiriṣpitā nābhāvārā adhvitā evam eva etā bhūta-mātrāḥ prajñā-mātrāsu ārpitāḥ prajñā-mātrāḥ prāṇe arpitāḥ iti sarvādhāratvaṃ ca tasya eva dharmaḥ \| tathā saḥ eṣa prāṇaḥ eva prajñātmā ānandadaḥ ajaraḥ amṛtaḥ iti eva ete api paramātmanaḥ eva dharmāḥ \| eṣa loka-adhipatiḥ eṣa sarveśaḥ iti ca paramātmanā eva saṃbhavati \| tat evam adhyātma-saṃbandha-bhūmā atra ukta-vidyamānatvāt paramātmā eva iyaṃ prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

kathaṃ tarhi prajñāta-jīva-bhāvasya eindrasya svātmānam upāsyatva-upadeśaḥ saṃgacchate; tatra āha—

## \|\| 1.1.31 \|\|

*śāstra-dṛṣṭyā tu upadeśaḥ vāmadevavat*

**Комментарий:**

prajñāta-jīva-bhāva-indrāṇāṃ mām eva vijānīhi mām upāssva iti upāsyasya brahmaṇaḥ sarvātmatvena upadeśaḥ ayaṃ na pramāṇa-antara-prāpta-svātma-laukikatvaḥ; api tu śāstreṇa svātmatva-dṛṣṭaḥ \|

etad uktaṃ bhavati—anena jīvātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi aitadātmyam idaṃ sarvam antaḥ praviṣṭa-śāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā ya ātmāni tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaraḥ yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ ya ātmānam antaraḥ yamayati eṣa sarva-bhūta-antarātmā apahata-pāpmā divyaḥ devaḥ ekaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ ity ebhyām ṛtā śāsanaṃ jīvātma-śarīrakaṃ paramātmānam avagamya jīvātma-vācibhiḥ ahaṃtva-ādi-nirdeśakānām api paramātmani eva paryavasānaṃ jñātvā mām eva vijānīhi, mām upāssva iti svātma-śarīrakaṃ paramātmānam eva upāsyatvena upadiśa iti \| vāmadeva-śrut— yathā vāmadevaḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ sarva-antarātmatvaṃ sarvasya ca tac-charīratvaṃ śarīra-vācināṃ ca śabdānāṃ śarīriṇi paryavasānaṃ paśyan aham iti svātma-śarīrakaṃ paraṃ brahma nirdiśya tat-tat-sāmānādhikaraṇyena mantra-anuvādaiḥ vyapadiśati tad vai etat paśyan ṛṣiḥ vāmadevaḥ pratipede ahaṃ manuḥ abhavaṃ sūryaḥ ca, ahaṃ kakṣīvān ṛṣiḥ asmi vipra, ityādinā \| yathā ca prahlādaḥ sarvam attas tatsya sa eva aham avasthitaḥ \| mattas sarvam ahaṃ sarvaṃ mayi sarvaṃ sanātane \|\| ityādi vadati \|\|31\|\|

asmin prakaraṇe jīva-vāci-śabdaiḥ viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭa-prakāra-abhibhṛṣṭha-upāsya-mūtasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ abhidhāne kāraṇaṃ codūpūrvakam āha—

## \|\| 1.1.32 \|\|

*jīva-mukhya-prāṇa-liṅgāt na iti cet na upāsā-traividhyāt āśritatvāt iha tadyogāt*

**Комментарий:**

na vācaṃ vijijñāsīta vaktāraṃ vidyāt indraḥ ahaṃ prati tvāṣṭram ahanaṃ arummukhāni atīva sālāvṛkebhyaḥ prāyacchaṃ, ityādinā jīva-liṅgāt, yāvat etasmin śarīre prāṇaḥ vasati tāvat āyuḥ atha khalu prāṇaḥ eva prajñātmā idaṃ śarīraṃ parigṛhya utthāpayati iti mukhya-prāṇa-liṅgāt ca na ayam asaṃbandha-bhūmā iti cet, upāsā-traividhyāt hetoḥ \| upāsanā-traividhyam upadiṣṭaṃ tatra cchdena-anabhidhānam— nikhila-kāraṇa-bhūtasya brahmaṇaḥ sarva-rūpeṇa anusandhānam, bhoktṛ-varga-śarīrakatva-anusandhānam, bhogya-bhoga-upakaraṇa-śarīrakatva-anusandhānaṃ ca iti trividham anusandhānam upadeṣṭum ity arthaḥ \| tatra idaṃ trividhaṃ brahma-anusandhānaṃ prakaraṇāntareṣu api āśritam—satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma, ānandaḥ brahma iti vyapadiśya svarūpa-mukhya-dharmānus; tasmāt vā etasmāt ātmanaḥ ākāśaḥ, tatra ṣṭpaviṣaya, sadd vyuhābhavat, niruktaṃ ca aniruktaṃ ca, nilayanaṃ ca anilayanaṃ ca, vijñānaṃ ca avijñānaṃ ca, satyaṃ ca anṛtaṃ ca satyam abhavat ity ābhiṣu bhogya-bhoktṛ-tat-sādhana-bhogya-bhoga-upakaraṇa-śarīratayā ca anusandhānam \| iha api prakaraṇe tad-vividha-anusandhānaṃ puruṣatva aiśvaryaṃ \|\|

etad uktaṃ bhavati—yatra hiraṇyagarbha-jīva-viśeṣaṇaṃ prahati-ādi-acetana-viśeṣaṇaṃ ca paramātma-asādhāraṇa-dharma-yogāt tad-abhidhāyināṃ śabdānāṃ paramukhya-vāci-śabdeś sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ vā dṛśyate, tatra paramātma-sahastaciddvividha-śeṣa-bhūta-śarīratva-anusandhānaṃ pratipipādayiṣitam iti \|

ataḥ anena indra-prāṇa-śabda-nirdeśaḥ jīva-arthāntara-bhūtaḥ paramātmā iti siddham \|\|32\|\|

iti indra-prāṇa-adhikaraṇam \|\|11\|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye prathamasya adhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ \|\|1\|\|

śrīḥ astu \|

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ \|

**prathama-adhyāye—dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ sarvatra-prasiddhi-adhikaraṇam \|\|1\|\|**

prathame pāde adhīta-vedaḥ puruṣaḥ karma-brahma-sādhāraṇa-vedānta-karma-bhāga-pratyabhijñānāt kevala-karmāṇām alpa-sthira-phalatvam avagamya, vedānta-vākyaiḥ ca āpātataḥ pratīta-niratisthira-phala-brahma-svarūpa-tad-upāsana-samupajāta-aparimita-puruṣārtha-lakṣaṇa-mokṣa-apekṣaḥ adhīta-sapariśiṣṭa-veda-vākyānāṃ parasmin brahmaṇi niścita prāmāṇya-sādara-tatparatva-vitarkaḥ tyāhārya-śarīraka-mīmāṃsā-śravaṇa-arthī bhava iti uktaṃ śāstra-ārambha-siddhaye \|\| (jijñāsā 1)

ananta-vicitra-acit-trūpa-cetana-bhogya-bhoga-upakaraṇa-bhoga-sthāna-lakṣaṇa-nikhila-jagad-udaya-vibhava-laya-mahānanda-eka-kāraṇaṃ paraṃ brahma śrutaḥ vā imāni bhūtāni iti-ādi-vākyaiḥ bodhyam iti ca pratipādi \|\| (janmād. 2)

jagad-aika-kāraṇaṃ paraṃ brahma sakala-itara-pramāṇa-aviṣayatvā śāstra-eka-pramāṇakam iṣṭam adhyasam \|\| (śāstra. 3)

śāstra-pramāṇakatvaṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ pravṛtti-nivṛtta-vyatirikte api svarūpe eva parama-puruṣārtha-bhūte parasmin brahmaṇi vedānta-vākyānāṃ samanvaya-abhihṛtam iti uktam \|\| (samanv. 4)

nikhila-jagad-aika-kāraṇatayā vedānta-vedye brahma ca īkṣaṇa-ādi-avyāhatumānityadhānānādyaśrīntara-bhūta-cetana-viśeṣa evam upapāditavān \|\| (īkṣatya. 5)

sa ca svābhāvika-nikhila-kriyā-ānanda-vibhūtiḥ ca nikhila-cetanasya ātmatayā hetutva-satya-saṅkalpatva-samasta-cetana-acetana-antarātmatva-ādi-viśiṣṭa-muktābhyupāya-avasthā-jīva-acchabda-milita-manīya-pratyarthāntara-bhūtaḥ iti ca samarthitam iha \|\| (ānand. 6)

sa ca aprākṛta-karma-nimitta-sadvasādhāraṇa-divya-rūpaḥ iti udīritam \|\| (antar. 7)

ākāśa-prāṇa-ādi-acetana-viśeṣa-abhidhāyibhiḥ jagat-kāraṇatayā prasiddhavat nirdiśyamānat sakala-itara-cetana-acetana-vilakṣaṇasya eva iti samarthitam iha \|\| (ākāśā. 8, prāṇā. 9)

para-tattva-sādhāraṇa-niratiśaya-abhiyuktyoti-śabda-abhidheyaḥ dyu-saṃbandhitayā pratyabhijñānāt param eva abhidharmāhi \|\| (jyoti. 10)

parama-kāraṇāsādhāraṇāmṛtatvābhihita-guṇaḥ parama-puruṣaḥ eva śāstra-hṛdayaiḥ nirdiśyate adhīyata iti asmin na hi \|\| (indraprāṇāḥ 11)

tad evam nirūpita-samastetara-pramāṇa-saṃbhāvanāmūrnisthābandhuphalayam āḍūpalatā-yathā-pariṇato dāra-guṇa-sāgaratayā svetara-samasta-vastu-vilakṣaṇaḥ paraṃ brahma puruṣottamaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ eva vedānta-vedyaḥ iti uktam \| ataḥ paraṃ dvitīya-tṛtīya-caturṣu pādeṣu, yady api vedānta-vedyaṃ brahma eva, tathā api kānicid vedānta-vākyāni pradhāna-kṣetrajñātmaka-bhūta-vastu-viśeṣa-śabda-rūpa-tipādana-parāṇi iti āśaṅkya tad-viśiṣṭa-sanmukhena tat-tad-ātma-yogi-tat-tat-kalyāṇa-guṇākaratvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pratipādyate \| tatra āspādya-upādi-liṅgakāni vākyāni dvitīye pāde vicāryante; spaṣṭa-liṅgakāni tṛtīye; tantarviṇipatanacchāyānusārīṇi caturthe \|\|

## Pada 2

## \|\| 1.2.1 \|\|

*sarvatra prasiddha-upadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

idam āmnāyate chāndogye atha khalu kratu-mayaḥ puruṣaḥ yathākratuḥ asmin loke puruṣaḥ bhavati tathā itaḥ pretya bhavati saḥ kratuṃ kurvīta manaḥ-mayaḥ prāṇa-śarīraḥ bhā-rūpaḥ satya-kāmaḥ \| atra saḥ kratuṃ kurvīta iti pratipāditasya upāsanasya upāsyaḥ mano-mayaḥ prāṇa-śarīraḥ iti nirdiśyata iti pratīyate \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kiṃ manomayatv-ādi-guṇakaḥ kṣetrajñaḥ; uta paramātmā iti \| kiṃ yuktam ? kṣetrajñaḥ iti \| kutaḥ ? manaḥ-prāṇayoḥ kṣetra-upakaraṇatvāt, paramātmanaḥ tu aprāṇaḥ hi amanāḥ iti tattva-niṣedhāt ca \| na ca sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma iti pūrva-nirdiṣṭaṃ brahma-upāsyatayā saṃbaddhuṃ śakyate, śāntaḥ upāsīta iti upāsana-upakaraṇānti-niṣṭhupapanna-bhūta-brahmātmakatva-upadeśena-upāsana-stavāt \| na ca saḥ kratuṃ kurvīta iti upāsanīya-upāsyatā-ākāṅkṣita-abhyantara-stham api brahma saṃbadhyata iti yuktaṃ vaktum; sva-vākya-upāttena manomayatv-ādi-guṇakena nirākāṅkṣatvāt; mano-mayaḥ prāṇa-śarīraḥ iti anena arthātayā nirdiṣṭasya viśiṣṭa-vipariṇāma-mātreṇa-ubhaya-ākāṅkṣā-śānti-nivṛtti-siddheḥ \| evaṃ niścīyate jīva-dehe eṣa tat brahma luṇḍa-mūṣārastha-brahmavat api jīvaḥ eva mūrdhanyaṃ prabhutva-nityadhyavasīyata iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte brūmaḥ—sarvatra prasiddha-upadeśāt \| manomayatv-ādi-guṇakaḥ paramātmā \| kutaḥ ? sarvatra—vedānteṣu parihasitaṃ ca brahmaṇi prasiddhasya manomayatvādeḥ eva upadeśāt \| prasiddhaṃ hi manomayatv-ādi brahmaṇaḥ \| yathā—mano-mayaḥ prāṇa-śarīra-netā saḥ yaḥ eṣaḥ antaḥ hṛdaya ākāśaḥ, tasmin ayam puruṣaḥ mano-mayaḥ; amṛtaḥ hiraṇmayaḥ hṛdā manīṣā manasā abhikl̥ptaḥ yaḥ enaṃ viduḥ amṛtāḥ te bhavanti ena cakṣuṣā gṛhyate na api vācā manasā tu viśuddhena, tathā prāṇasya prāṇaḥ atha khalu prāṇaḥ eva prajñānande śarīraṃ parigṛhya utthāpayati sarvāṇi ha vai imāni bhūtāni prāṇam eva abhisaṃviśanti prāṇam abhyujjihate iti-ādiṣu \| manomayatvaṃ—viśuddhena manasā grāhyatvam \| prāṇ-

śarīratvaṃ prāṇasya api ādhāratvaṃ niyantṛtvaṃ ca \| evaṃ ca sati eṣaḥ me ātmā antar-hṛdaye etad brahma iti brahma-śabdaḥ api mukhyaḥ eva bhavati \| aprāṇaḥ hi amanāḥ iti manaḥ-prāṇau jñānaṃ prāṇanaṃ sthitiṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ niṣedhati \|\|

atha vā sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma taj-jalān iti śāntaḥ upāsīta iti atra upāsanaṃ vidhīyate—sarvātmakam brahma śānta-rasam upāsīta iti \| saḥ kratuṃ kurvīta tasya eva guṇopādāna-arthaḥ anuvādaḥ \| upadeśāḥ ca guṇāḥ manomayatvādayaḥ; antaḥ sarvātmakam brahma manomayādi-viśiṣṭam upāsīta iti vākya-arthaḥ \| tatra sandehaḥ—kim iha brahma-śabdena pratyagātmā nirdiśyate; uta paramātmā iti \| kiṃ yuktam? pratyagātmā iti \| kutaḥ? tasya eva sarva-veda-sāmānādhikaraṇya-nirdeśopapatteḥ \| sarva-śabda-nirdiṣṭaṃ hi brahma-vihita-sambandha-paryantaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat \| brahma-viśeṣyasya pratyagātmanaḥ jñāna-avidyā-mūla-karma-viśeṣopādhikaḥ viśeṣaḥ eva \| paraṃ tu brahmaṇaḥ sarvajñasya sarveśvarasya apahata-pāpmanaḥ nirasta-samasta-avidyādi-doṣa-gandhasya samasta-jñānākara sarvajñasya na upapadyate \| pratyagātmani api kavi-kalpita-brahma-śabdaḥ prayujyate \| ataḥ eva paramātmā paraṃ brahma iti parameśvarasya karvicchaviśeṣaṇaḥ nirdeśaḥ \| pratyagātmanaḥ ca nirmuktopādhau hantavyaṃ ca vidyate saḥ ghānatyāya kalpate iti śruteḥ \| avidyukta-karma-nimittatvāt janma-sthiti-layānāṃ taj-jalān iti hetu-nirdeśaḥ api upapadyate \| tad ayam arthaḥ—ayaṃ jīvātmā ’svataḥ aparicchinna-svarūpatvena brahma-bhūta-saṃjñakānāṃ deva-tiryaṅ-manuṣya-varāhānāśva-vihite—iti \|\|

atra pratividhīyate—sarvatra prasiddhopadeśāt \| sarvatra— sarvaṃ khalu idam iti nirdiṣṭe sarvasmin jagati brahma-śabdena tadātmatayā (abhi) vidheyamānaṃ paraṃ brahma eva—na pratyagātmā \| kutaḥ? prasiddhopadeśāt, taj-jalān iti hetutaḥ sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma iti prasiddhopadeśāt \| brahmaṇaḥ jagattva-āvṛtta-brahmaṇaḥ loka-veda-sādhāraṇyena jagattvāt ca hetoḥ brahmātmakaṃ sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ jagat iti ukte yasmin jagaj-janma-sthiti-layāḥ vedānteṣu prasiddhāḥ tad eva atra brahma iti pratīyate \| tad ca param eva brahma \| tathā hi yataḥ vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante, yena jātāni jīvanti, yat prayanti abhisaṃviśanti, tad vijijñāsasva, tad brahma iti upakramya ānandaḥ brahma iti vyajānāt, ānandāt eva khalu imāni bhūtāni jāyante ityādinā pūrvānuvāka-abhihita-anadhikāri-traya-anantarbhāginaḥ vivakṣitaḥ paramātma brahmaṇaḥ eva jagad-utpatti-sthiti-layāḥ nirdiśyante \| tathā saḥ kāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipa-adhipaḥ na ca asya kaścit janitā na ca adhipaḥ iti karaṇādhipasya jīvasya adhipaḥ paraṃ brahma eva kāraṇaṃ vyapadiśyate \| evaṃ sarvatra parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ kāraṇatvaṃ prasiddham \| ataḥ parabrahmaṇaḥ jātatvāt asmin pralīnatvāt tena prāṇanāt tadātmakatayā tādātmyam upapannam \| antaḥ sarvaṃkāraṃ sarveśarīraṃ sarvatimuktaṃ paraṃ brahma śāntaḥ bhūtvā upāsīta iti śruteḥ ca parasya brahmaṇaḥ sarvasmin ātmatvam upapādya

tasya upāsanatva-pūrva-dṛṣṭiḥ \| paraṃ brahma hi kāraṇa-avasthaṃ kārya-avasthaṃ sūkṣma-sthūla-cid-acid-vastu-śarīratayā sarvadā sarvātma-svarūpam \| evaṃ-bhūtatvāt tasya pratipādane parasya brahmaṇaḥ sakala-heya-pratyanīka-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākaratvaṃ na vihanyate, prakāra-bhūta-śarīra-gatānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ prakāriṇam asaṃsṛṣṭatvāt; pratyuta niratiśaya-aiśvarya-pādanena guṇa-āḍhyaṃ bhavati iti pūrvam eva uktam \|\|

yuktaṃ jīvasya sarvātma-sāmyam upapadyate iti; tat asat, jīvānāṃ pratiśarīraṃ bhinnānām anyonya-ātmatayā asaṃbhavāt \| muktasya api anavicchinna-svabhāvatayā api jāgata-ātmatvaṃ jagaj-janma-sthiti-pralaya-kāraṇatva-nimittaṃ na saṃbhavati iti jagad-vyāpāra-varjaṃ ity atra vakṣyate \| jīva-karma-nimitatvāt jagaj-janma-sthiti-layānāṃ sa eva kāraṇam ity api na sādhīyaḥ, tat-karma-nimitatve api īśvarasya eva jagat-kāraṇatvāt \| ataḥ paramātmā eva brahma-ātma-śabda-abhidheyaḥ \| imam eva sūtra-artham āviṣkṛtaṃ bahu manyate, yathā āha itikāraḥ sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma tajjalān iti \|\| 1 \|\|

## \|\| 1.2.2 \|\|

*vivakṣita-guṇa-upapatteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

vakṣyamāṇāḥ ca guṇāḥ paramātmani eva upapadyante manomayaḥ prāṇa-śarīraḥ bhārūpa-satya-saṅkalpaḥ ākāśātmā sarva-karmā sarva-kāmaḥ sarva-gandhaḥ sarva-rasaḥ sarvam idam abhyāttaḥ avākī anādaraḥ iti \|

manomayaḥ—pariśuddhena manasā ekena grāhyaḥ; viveka-nimitta-ukti-sādhana-ṣaṭka-anugṛhīta-paramātma-sopāsana-nirmalīkṛtena hi manasā gṛhyate \| anena heya-pratyanīka-kalyāṇa-ekatānatayā sakala-itara-vilakṣaṇa-svarūpatā ucyate; malina-manobhiḥ malina-ātmā eva grāhyatvāt \| prāṇa-śarīraḥ—jagati sarveṣāṃ prāṇānāṃ dhārakaḥ; prāṇaḥ yasya śarīram—ādheyaṃ vidheyaṃ śeṣabhūtaṃ ca sa prāṇa-śarīraḥ \| ādheyatva-vidheyatva-śeṣatvāni śarīra-śabda-arthān iti miśritam upapādayiṣyate \| bhārūpaḥ—bhāsvara-rūpaḥ; aprākṛta-svādhāraṇa-niratiśaya-kalyāṇa-divyatva-rūpatvena niratiśaya-dīpti-yukta ity arthaḥ \| satya-saṅkalpaḥ—apratihata-saṅkalpaḥ \| ākāśātmā—ākāśavat-sūkṣma-svacchaka-svarūpaḥ \| sakala-itara-ākāra-śūnyatayā ākāśa-śabda-vācyam amūta iti vā ākāśātmā \| svayaṃ ca prakāśate anyān api prakāśayati iti vā ākāśātmā \| sarva-karmā—krīḍate iti karma; pūrvaṃ jagad-ākhyaṃ karma asau sarva-karmā; sarvā vā kriyā yasya asau sarva-karmā \| sarva-kāmaḥ—kāmyante iti kāmāḥ; bhogya-bhoga-upakaraṇa-ādayaḥ; te pariśuddhāḥ sarva-vidhāḥ tasya santi ity arthaḥ \| sarva-gandhaḥ sarva-rasaḥ—aśabdam asparśam iti-ādinā prākṛta-upādhi-sādhita-viśeṣaṇa-āśaṅkāḥ svābhāvikāṇāṃ niravadyāḥ niratiśayāḥ kalyāṇāḥ sva-bhoga-bhūtāḥ sarva-vidhāḥ gandha-rasāḥ tasya santi ity arthaḥ \| sarvam idam abhyāttaḥ—uktaṃ sama-paryantaṃ sarvam idaṃ kalyāṇa-guṇa-jātaṃ svīkṛtavān \| abhyātta iti bhuktā brāhmaṇā iti-vat kartari ktaḥ pratipattavyaḥ \| avākī—vāk-rahitaḥ; śoḍhya nāsti ity avākī \| kuta ity āha—anādara iti \| avāpta-samasta-kāmatvena anya-ādara-rahitaḥ \| ata eva avākī—ajalpakaḥ; paripūrṇa-

bandhya-nāḍyūha-ādivat tat-sva-paryantaṃ nihitaṃ jagad-goḷīyasya jīva-prāsārīta ity arthaḥ \| ta ete vicitratā-guṇāḥ paramātmā eva eteṣu upacyante \|\|2\|\|

## \|\| 1.2.3 \|\|

*anupapattes tu na śārīraḥ*

**Комментарий:**

tasmin guṇa-sāgaraṃ parilīyantaṃ kṣetrajña-kalpasya śarīra-saṃbandhi-nivṛtta-aparicchinna-duḥkha-saṃbandha-yogyasya baddha-mukta-avasthasya jīvasya prastuta-guṇa-leśa-saṃbandha-ayogād api na upacyata iti na asmin prakaraṇe śārīra-parigraha-śaṅkā jāyata ity arthaḥ \|\|3\|\|

## \|\| 1.2.4 \|\|

*karmakartṛ-vyapadeśāc ca*

**Комментарий:**

pūrvasmin: pretya abhisaṃbhavitā asmi iti prāptavyatayā paraṃ brahma vyapadiśyate, prāptṛtayā ca jīvaḥ \| ataḥ prāpyā jīva upāsakaḥ, prāpyaṃ paraṃ brahma iti āgam iti prāptṛ-prāpya-bhedena iti vijñāyate \|\|4\|\|

## \|\| 1.2.5 \|\|

*śabda-viśeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

eṣa me ātmā antar-hṛdaye iti śārīra-baddhayā nirddiṣṭaḥ, upāsakas tu prathamayā \| evaṃ samāne prakaraṇe vājināṃ ca śruto śabda-viśeṣa-śrute jīva-paryāyaḥ; yathā vrīhī vā yavo vā śyāmāko vā śyāmāka-taṇḍulo vā evam ayam antarātman puruṣo hiraṇmayaḥ yathā jyotiḥ adhūmam iti \| atra antarātmani iti saptamyantena śārīro nirddiśyate; puruṣo hiraṇmayaḥ iti prathamayā upāsyaḥ \| ataḥ para eva upāsyaḥ \|\|

itaś ca śārīra-baddhaḥ—

## \|\| 1.2.6 \|\|

*smṛteś ca*

**Комментарий:**

sarvasya ca ahaṃ hṛdi saṃniviṣṭo mattaḥ smṛtir jñānam apohanaṃ ca yo mām evam asaṃmūḍho jānāti puruṣottamam īśvaraḥ sarva-bhūtānāṃ hṛd-deśe arjuna tiṣṭhati \| bhrāmayan sarva-bhūtāni yantra-ārūḍhāni māyayā \|\| tam eva śaraṇaṃ gaccha iti śārīra-upāsakaṃ paramātmānaṃ ca upāsyaṃ smṛtir darśayati \|\|6\|\|

## \|\| 1.2.7 \|\|

*arbhaka-okastvāt tad-vyapadeśāc ca na iti cen na nicāyyatvād evaṃ vyoma-vac ca*

**Комментарий:**

alpa-āyatanatvam arbhaka-okastvaṃ; tad-vyapadeśaḥ—alpatva-vyapadeśaḥ \| eṣa me ātmā antar-hṛdaye ity aṇīyān hi hṛdaya-āyatane sthitatvāt aṇīyān vrīher vā yavād vā ityādinā aṇīyastvasya svarūpeṇa vyapadeśāc ca na ayaṃ paramātmā, api tu jīva eva; saṅgāta susūkṣmaṃ tad vayaṃ madbhūta-yoniṃ paripaśyanti dhīrāḥ ityādibhiḥ paramātmanaḥ api hi astvacāmātraṃ, jīvasya ca arāgra-mātrattva-vyapadeśād iti cet;

na tat evam, paramātmana eva hṛdaya-gaha-nilaye niyāyyatvena vyapadiśyataḥ; evaṃ niyāyyatvena evaṃ brahmatvena, evam upāsyatvena iti yāvat \| na punaḥ aṇīyastvam eva asya svarūpam iti \| vyoma-vat vyapadiśyate—svābhāvikaṃ mahattvaṃ ca atra eva vyapadiśyate—jyāyān pṛthivyāḥ jyāyān antarikṣāt jyāyān divaḥ jyāyān ebhyo lokebhyaḥ iti \| ataḥ upāsanārtham eva alpavattva-pradeśaḥ \| tathā hi sarvaṃ khalv idaṃ brahma tat-jalān iti śāntaḥ upāsīta iti sarvātma-nirmalaya-kāraṇatvena sarvatra ātmādi-anutpatti-vaiśadyena upacitena ca sarvātmakaṃ brahma upāsīta iti upāsaṃ vidhāya atha khalu kratu-mayaḥ puruṣo yathā-kratuḥ asmin loke puruṣo bhavati tathā itaḥ pretya bhavati iti yathā iha upāsanaṃ prāpya-siddharthi-dhāya sa kratuṃ kurvīta iti guṇa-vidhānārtham upāsanam anuṣṭha manomayaḥ prāṇa-śarīro bhārūpaḥ satya-rūpa ākāśātmā sarva-karmā sarva-kāmaḥ sarva-gandhaḥ sarva-rasaḥ sarvam idam abhyāttaḥ avāky anādaraḥ iti jagad-īśvarya-viśiṣṭasya svarūpa-guṇāṃś ca upadeśyān pratipādya eṣa me ātmā antar-hṛdaye aṇīyān vrīheḥ vā yāvā sarṣapāt vā śyāmākāt vā śyāmāka-taṇḍulāt vā iti upāsakasya hṛdayopāsyatvena tad-ātma-sayoga-pārasya parama-puruṣa-yogāsanārtha-samarthanāmukatā eṣa me ātmā antar-hṛdaye jyāyān pṛthivyāḥ jyāyān antarikṣāt jyāyān divaḥ jyāyān ebhyo lokebhyaḥ sarva-karmā sarva-kāmaḥ sarva-gandhaḥ sarva-rasaḥ sarvam idam abhyāttaḥ avāky anādaraḥ iti ananta-hṛdaya-sthopāsyamānasya prāpya-ākāraṃ nirdiśya eṣa me ātmā antar-hṛdaya etat brahma iti evaṃ-bhūtaṃ paraṃ brahma parama-kāraṇena samujjihīviṣaya-upāsana-smṛty-artham iti śikhita-manīṣam anusandhānaṃ vidhāya etam itaḥ pretya abhisaṃbhavitā asmi iti yathā iha upāsanaṃ prāpti-niścayānusandhānaṃ ca vidhāya iti yasya syāt addhā na vicikitsā asti iti aihika-prāpya-prāpti-niścayopetatva-upāsakasya prāptaḥ na saṃśayaḥ asti iti upasaṃhṛtam \| ataḥ upāsanārtham eva uktirūpam aṇīyastvaṃ ca \|\|7\|\|

## \|\| 1.2.8 \|\|

*saṃbhoga-prāptir iti cet na vaiśeṣyāt*

**Комментарий:**

jīvasya eva parasyāpi brahmāṇḍa-karoṣāntarvartitvam aṇu-pramāṇaṃ cetaḥ, tat-dehe eva śarīra-saṃbandha-prayukta-sukha-duḥkhopabhoga-prāptir iti cet, tat na, hetu-vaiśeṣyāt—na hi śarīrāntarvartitvam eva sukha-duḥkhopabhoga-hetuḥ; api tu puṇya-pāpa-rūpa-karma-paravaśatvam; tat ca prahṛta-pāpmanaḥ paramātmanaḥ na saṃbhavati \| tathā ca śrutiḥ apahṛta-pāpmatvaṃ svābhāvi iti anabhisaṃbadhya adhikārokatva iti \|\|8\|\|

iti sarvatra-aprasiddhy-adhikaraṇam \|\|1\|\|

**atha antar-adhikaraṇam \|\|2\|\|**

yadi paramātmā na bhoktā, evaṃ tarhi sarvatra bhoktṛtayā pratīyamāno jīva eva svādityādy-ākhyāḥ—

## \|\| 1.2.9 \|\|

*antarā carācara-grahaṇāt*

**Комментарий:**

ṛtvalokāvasthāyate asya brahma ca kṣatraṃ ca ubhe bhavata odanaḥ \| mṛtyur yasya upasecanaṃ ka itthā veda yatra saḥ \|\| iti \| atra odana-upasecana-sūcita-attā kiṃ jīva eva, uta paramātmā iti sandihyate \| kiṃ yuktam? jīva iti \| kutaḥ? bhoktṛtvasya karma-nimittatvāt jīvasya eva tat-sambhavāt \| atra ucyate—antarā carācara-grahaṇāt \| attā paramātmā eva \| kutaḥ? carācara-grahaṇāt—carācarasya kṛtsnasya attavye hi tasya eva sambhavati \| na ca idaṃ karma-nimittaṃ bhoktṛtvam; api tu jagaj-janma-sthiti-laya-hetu-bhūtasya parasya brahmaṇo viṣṇoḥ saṃhṛtṛtvam; soḍḍhyannaḥ paramātmā iti tad-viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyanti sūrayaḥ \| tathā ca mṛtyur yasya upasecanam iti vacanāt, brahma ca kṣatram iti kṛtsnaṃ carācaraṃ jagat iha attavyatayā eva na gṛhyate \| upasecanaṃ hi nāma svayam apramāṇaṃ sat anya-svādya-hetuḥ \| ata upasecanatvena mṛtyor api adyamānatvāt tad-upasicamānasya kṛtsnasya brahma-kṣatra-pūrvakasya jagataḥ carācarasya attavyatva-mātraṃ vivakṣitam iti gamyate \| īdṛśaṃ ca attṛtvaṃ saṃhāra eva \| tasmāt īdṛśaṃ jagad-upasaṃhṛti-rūpaṃ bhoktṛtvaṃ paramātmana eva \|\| 9 \|\|

## \|\| 1.2.10 \|\|

*prakaraṇāt ca*

**Комментарий:**
