prakaraṇaṃ ca idaṃ parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ—mahāntaṃ vibhum ātmānaṃ matvā dhīro na śocati na ayam ātmā pravacanena labhyo na medhayā na bahunā śrutena \| yam eva eṣa vṛṇute tena labhyas tasya eva ātmā vivṛṇute tanuṃ svām \|\| iti hi prakṛtam \| ka itthā veda yatra saḥ iti api hi tat-samādāhare tasya durvijñeyatvam eva pūrvavattvaṃ pratyabhijñāyate \|\| 10 \|\| atha syāt—na ayaṃ brahma-kṣatra-attatva-sūcitaḥ puruṣaḥ apahata-pāpmā paramātmā; anantaram ṛtaṃ pibantau sukṛtasya loke guhāṃ praviṣṭau parame parārdhye \| chāyā-ātapau brahmavido vadanti pañcāgnayo ye ca triṇāciketāḥ \|\| iti karma-phala-bhuk eva sahitīyam abhidhānāt \| dvitīyaḥ ca prāṇe buddhiḥ vā syāt \| ṛta-pānaṃ hi karma-phala-bhoga eva; sa ca paramātmanaḥ na saṃbhavati; buddhiḥ api etad-bhoktṛ-jīvasya upakaraṇa-bhūtayā kathañcit pāna-ukti-upapattiḥ bhavati iti tayoḥ anyatareṇa sahitīyo jīva eva pratipattavyaḥ \| tad-eka-prakaraṇatā-pūrva-prakṛtoḍāḍopi sa eva bhavitum arhati iti \| tatra ucyate—

## \|\| 1.2.11 \|\|

*guhāṃ praviṣṭau ātmānau hi tad-darśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

na prāṇa-jīvau buddhi-jīvau vā guhāṃ praviṣṭau ṛtaṃ pibantau iti ucyete; api tu jīva-paramātmānau hi tathā chāyātapa-darśyate \| kutaḥ? tad-darśanāt \| asmin prakaraṇe jīva-parayoḥ eva guhā-praveśa-rūpa-veśaḥ dṛśyate \| paramātmanaḥ tāvat taṃ durdarśaṃ gūḍham anupraviṣṭaṃ guhā-hitaṃ gahvare stham purāṇam \| adhyātma-yoga-adhigamena devaṃ matvā dhīro harṣa-śokau jahāti \|\| iti \| jīvasya api yā prāṇena

saṃbhavasya avidita-nivṛttatvam ayī \| yuhāṃ praviśya tighnatī yā bhūti-bhidyañjāyat \|\| iti \| karma-phala-upabhogāya d iti jñātvā ucyate \| prāṇena saṃbhavati—prāṇena saha vartate \| devatā-mayo—indriya-adhīna bhogaḥ \| yuhāṃ praviśya tighnatī—hṛdaya-puṇḍarīka-varttinī \| bhūti-bhidyañjāyat—pṛthivī-ādi-viśiṣṭa-bhūta-saṃhitā deva-ādi-viśeṣa vividhā jāyate \| evaṃ ca sati santaṃ pitrāttā d iti hi apadeśaḥ chātriṇo gacchanti iti iva pratipattavyaḥ \| brahma-prāpto'pi aprayojaka-rūpeṇa pāti kartuṃ jīva-upayoga-padyate \|\|12\|\|

## \|\| 1.2.12 \|\|

*viśeṣaṇāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

asmin prakaraṇe jīva-paramātmanoḥ upāsyatva-upāsakatva-prāpyatva-prāptṛtva-vidiśīhiḥ sarvatra pratipādyete \| tathā hi-brahma-jajñaṃ deva-mīḍyaṃ viditvā nicāyya imaṃ śāntim atyantam eti iti \| brahma-jajño jīvaḥ; brahmaṇo jātatvāt jaḥtvat \| taṃ deva-mīḍyaṃ viditvā—jīvātmanām anuprāsakaṃ brahma-ātmatvena avagamya ity arthaḥ \| tathā yasmāt kṣaro jānamānā akṣaraṃ brahma yat param \| asmāt vinirgatau pāraṃ nāviciketaṃ śakemahi \|\| iti upāsyaḥ paramātmā ucyate \| nāviciketaṃ—na aviciketasya karmaṇaḥ prāpyam ity arthaḥ \| ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi śarīraṃ ratham eva ca ity-ādi-viśeṣasako jīva ucyate \| tathā vijñāna-sārathiḥ yaḥ tu manaḥ-pragrahavān naraḥ \| saḥ adhvanaḥ pāram āpnoti tat viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam \|\| iti prāpya-prāptṛtva-viśiṣṭau jīva-paramātmanau \| iha api chāyā-tapau iti anena sarva-jñatvābhayāṃ tāv eva nirdiśya vyapadiśyete \|\|

atha syāt ṛtaṃ pibantau iti vivikṣitā manuṣye asti iti eke nāyam asti iti ca eke iti jīva-svarūpa-yāthātmya-praśna-upakramāt pūrvam idaṃ prakaraṇaṃ jīva-param iti pratīyata iti \| na etad evam, na hi jīvasya dehātirikta-sthiratarvāsitva-vijñāna-dvāryaṃ praśnaḥ; tathā sati pūrvaṃ vara-dvaya-vara-upanyupanataḥ \| tathā hi—pituḥ tum avedasa dakṣiṇa-kratum abhivellaṃ dīrgham āna-dakṣiṇa-yūpeta kratu-vaiguṇyaṃ manyamānena kumāreṇa naciketasā āstikāgyereṣeṇa sva-ātmanā api pituḥ kratu-sadguṇyam iṣṭatā kasmai māṃ dāsyasīty asaṃhṛta-pitrāṃ dṛṣṭvā sva-nirbandharṣotpitr-vacanam-ṛtyu-saritaṃ pratibhinnaṃ sva-mata-tad-veṣo'pi yame tad-darśanāt ca vitro rātroṣoṣaṇa sva-upavāsa-bhīta tat-pratividhāna-pravṛtta-mṛtyu(pratte)śraddhe vara-traye āstitya-mātriteṣām prathamena vareṇa sva-ātmānaṃ prati pituḥ prasādo vṛtaḥ \| etaiḥ sarvaṃ dehātiriktam ātmānam ajānato na upapadyate \| dvitīyena ca vareṇa śīrṇa-dehātma-subhūvya-phala-sādhana-bhūta-agni-vidyā vṛtā \| tadvat dehātirikta-ātmanāniścayasya na saṃbhavati \| atas tṛtīyena vareṇa yat idaṃ vicikitse pretya prete vivikṣitā manuṣye asti iti eke nāyam asti iti ca eke \| etad vidyām anuśiṣṭaḥ tvayā ahaṃ varāṇām eṣa varaḥ tṛtīyaḥ \|\| iti; atra para-puruṣārthe dharma-sūtra-ādi-tal-lakṣaṇa-mokṣa-yāthātmya-jñānāya tad-upāya-bhūta-paramātma-upāsana-para-varāśas tac-chāstrābhyāṃ praśnaḥ kriyate \| evaṃ

ca dheyaṃ preta iti na śarīra-vidyā-mātra-niṣprāpyam; api tu sarva-bandha-vinirmukti-nimitta-prāpyam \| yathā taṃ preyaṃ saṃgāyati iti \|\|

ayarthaḥ—mokṣārthinaḥ tu mumukṣe prete—sarva-bandha-vinirmukte tattva-svarūpa-viṣayā vāṅ-niviḍa-pratipatti-nimitta-ādi-śāstra-arthamāhikā yā iyaṃ vivikitsā, tatpranenayā tattva-svarūpa-āthātmyam tvayā anuśiṣṭaḥ ahaṃ vidyāṃ—jānīyām—iti \| tathā hi bahudhā viśravaṇyate—keviddhīstimātrasya asmat-svarūpophicchinna-lakṣaṇaṃ mokṣam ācakṣate \| anye citti-mātrasya eva sattodevyasamayam \|

apare pāṣāṇa-kalpasya ātmano jñāta dṛśya vaivṛṣikāṇo'ccheda-lakṣaṇaṃ kevalya-rūpam \| apare tu apahata-pāpmānaṃ paramātmānam anupagacchantaḥ tad-vaiśeṣya-pārśva-saṃgānimitti-ājña-vidyā-abhāva-rūpa-āpādyapānena tad-dṛṣṭiva-lakṣaṇaṃ mokṣam abhidhatte \| trayyanti-nirṇītāḥ tu nikhila-jaḍa-eka-kāraṇatayā aśeṣa-heya-pratyanīka-ananta-jñāna-ānanda-eka-svarūpasya svābhāvika-anavadhika-atiśaya-āsaṅkhyeya-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākarasya sakala-itara-vilakṣaṇasya sarvātmasbhūtasya parasya brahma-parasya tathā prakārasūtasya sūkta-aparicchinna-jñāna-svarūpasya paramātma-anubhava-eka-rasasya jīvasya anādi-karma-rūpa-avidyā-tirohita-svarūpasya avidyā-uccheda-pūrvaka-svābhāvika-paramātma-anubhavaimeva mokṣam ācakṣate \| tatra mokṣa-svarūpaṃ tat-sādhanaṃ ca tattvamasāvidyām iti naciketasa pṛṣṭe mṛtyuḥ tasya arthasya duravabodhatva-darśanena vividha-bhoga-vitaraṇa-pralobhane ca enam parīkṣya yogyatām abhijñāya parāvara-ātma-tattva-vijñānaṃ paramātma-prāptiṃ tatpradhāti-lakṣaṇaṃ mokṣaṃ ca taṃ durdarśaṃ gūḍham anupraviṣṭaṃ guhāhitaṃ gahvareṣṭhaṃ purāṇam iti tanniṣṭhoḥ paraṃ parikīrtyamānena upadiśya tad-apekṣitān ca viśeṣān upadiśeti sarvaṃ samagrasam \| ataḥ paramātmemāsi iti siddham \|\| 1.2 \|\| iti akṣi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha antara-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.2.13 \|\|

*antaḥ upapatteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

idam āmananti cakṣur-devāḥ yaḥ eṣaḥ antaḥ akṣiṇi puruṣaḥ dṛśyate eṣaḥ ātmā iti ha uvāca etad amṛtam abhayam etad brahma iti \| tatra saṃdehaḥ—kim ayam akṣi-ādhāratayā nirdiśyamānaḥ puruṣaḥ pratibimba-ātmā, uta cakṣur-indriya-adhiṣṭhātā devatā-viśeṣaḥ, uta jīva-ātmā, atha paramātmā iti \| kiṃ yuktaṃ ? pratibimba-ātmā iti \| kutaḥ ? pratibimba-sambhavāt; dṛśyate iti aparokṣa-abhidhānāt ca \| jīva-ātmā vā, tasya api hi cakṣuṣi viśeṣeṇa sannidhānāttrasiddhiḥ upapadyate \| uktāñcalitaṃ hi cakṣuṣṭhīya jīvāsmaccharīre sthitiḥ māti niścivarṇita \| raśmibhiḥ eṣaḥ asmin pratistiṣṭhati iti śruti-siddhayā cakṣuḥ-pratiṣṭhaḥ devatā-viśeṣaḥ vā \| evaṃ ca pratibimba-ādivatropanīyerṣāmaṇyamaḥ \|\| iti prāpte pracakṣmahe—antaḥ upapatteḥ \| akṣi-antaraḥ paramātmā \| kutaḥ ? eṣa

ato hi ha uvāca etad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvam iti evaṃ saṃkṣepam iti ācakṣate, evaṃ hi sarvāṇi vāmāni abhisaṃyanti eṣa u eva vāmanīḥ, eṣa hi sarvāṇi vāmāni nayati, eṣa u eva bhāmanīḥ; eṣa hi sarveṣu lokeṣu bhāti iti eṣāṃ guṇānāṃ paramātmani eva upapatteḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## \|\| 1.2.14 \|\|

*sthānādi-vyapadeśāc ca*

**Комментарий:**

cakṣuṣi sthiti-niyaman-ādyaḥ paramātmana eva yaḥ cakṣuṣi tiṣṭhan iti evam-ādo vyapadiśyante \| ataś ca ya eṣo ’ntar akṣiṇi puruṣaḥ iti sa eva pratīyate \| ataḥ prasiddha-viruddhaś ca paramātmatvam upapadyate \| tata eva hṛdi apete iti sākṣātkārasya padeśo ’pi yogibhir dhyāyamānatvād upapadyate \|\| 14 \|\|

## \|\| 1.2.15 \|\|

*sukha-viśiṣṭābhidhānād eva ca*

**Комментарий:**

itaś ca akṣy-ādhāraḥ puruṣottamaḥ—kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma iti prakṛtasya sukha-viśiṣṭasya brahmaṇaḥ upāsanārthābhidhānārthaṃ saṃyad-vāmādi-viśeṣābhidhānārthaṃ ca ya eṣo ’kṣiṇi puruṣaḥ iti abhidhānāt, ekakāryo nerepekṣo hetor nityam iti \|\|

na ca arcir-vidyā-vyapadeśāt kaṃ brahma iti prakṛtaṃ brahma na iha sannidhīyate \| tathā hi—ācāryaḥ prāṇo brahma kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma iti brahma-vidyām upadiśya atha ha enam gārhapatyo ’nuśaśāsa iti ārabhya agnīnām upāsana-samṛddhiṃ hitvā \| na ca arcir-vidyā brahma-vidyāṅgam iti śakyaṃ vaktum; brahma-vidyā-phala-ānanty-ānantaryād virodheṣv adhikṛta-phalāśravaṇāt \| ucyate—prāṇo brahma khaṃ tat sukham asmā etad brahma iti upamantraṃ brahma-saṃśabdanāt, ācāryas tu te gatiṃ vakta iti agnināṃ vacanāc ca gati-upadeśāt pūrvaṃ brahma-vidyāyā asamāpteḥ tan-madhya-gataḥ arcir-vidyā brahma-vidyāṅgam iti niścīyate; atha ha enam gārhapatyo ’nuśaśāsati brahma-vidyādhikṛtatvena agni-nivacana-upadeśāc ca \| kiñca vyādhibhiḥ pratipūrṇo ’smi iti brahma-prāpty-arthinā rikte na avidyā-karma-uparati-viparyaye ’pi janma-jarā-maraṇāhibhūta-bhaya-upaplavāya upakośalāya eṣā saumya te asmad-vidyātma-vidyā ca iti samuccīty-upadeśāt mopekṣaka-phalātma-vidyāṅgatvam arciḥ-vidyāyāḥ pratīyate \| evaṃ ca aṅgatva-gate sati phalānukīrtanam artha-vāda iti gamyate \| na ca atra mokṣa-virodhi-phalaṃ kiñcid iṣyate, apahate pāpa-kṛtyāṃ lokī bhavati sarvam āyur eti jyog jīvati na asya avara-puruṣāḥ kṣīyante upa vayaṃ bhuñjāmo ’smiṃś ca loke ’muṣmiṃś ca iti amīṣāṃ phalānāṃ mokṣādhikṛtasyānuguṇatvāt \| apahate pāpa-kṛtyām—brahma-prāpti-virodhi pāpaṃ karma apahanti \| lokī bhavati—virodhīni pāpe niras te brahma-lokaṃ prāpnoti \| sarvam āyur eti—brahmopāsana-samāpto yāvad āyur apekṣitasya, tat sarvam eti \| jyog jīvati—dhyāyan āvirbhūtaṃ yad brahmāpi jīvati \| na asya avara-puruṣāḥ kṣīyante—asya śiṣya-praśiṣyādayaḥ puṣyocchrāyatvād api

brahma-vit eva bhavati, nārāyaṇa-brahma-vid-yukte bhavati iti ca śrūyamāṇe brahma-vidyā-phalatvena śrūyate \| upa vayasāṃ bhujāmaḥ asmiś ca lokas tuṣṭiś ca—vayaṃ ananta-stomenam puruṣam—yāvad brahmayāmi vicinveyaḥ paripālayāma iti \| ataḥ asmin vidyāyāṃ brahma-vidyā-aṅgatvena tarasa-vidyā-anāvirodhāt upaviniśiṣṭaṃ prakṛtā eva brahma-upāsana-sthāna-avidyārthaṃ codyate \|\|

nanu ācāryas tu te gatiṃ vaktā iti gati-mātra-pariseṣaṇād ācāryo gati-vedayitā iti gamyate; tat kathaṃ samāna-guṇa-vidyaḥ codyate \| tad dhi codyate \| ācāryas tu te gatiṃ vaktā iti asya ayam abhiprāyaḥ:—brahma-vidyām upadiśya proṣite gurau tad-anuśāsanārtham āpūrṇa-kālam anuṣṭhīyituṃ sva-paricaraṇa-gītā gārhapatya-ādayo guroḥ anantaraṃ brahma-svarūpa-mātraṃ tad-upābhūtāṃ ca agni-vidyām upadiśya ācāryāt eva vidyā vihitā sādhiṣṭhaṃ prāpyata iti śruti-artha-jñālena sādhu-bhakta-prātyartham ācārya eva saṃyad-vāmatva-ādi tat-pūrvaṃ brahma tad-upāsana-sthānam arciḥ-ādikaṃ ca gatim upadiśati iti matvā ācāryas tu te gatiṃ vaktā iti avocan \| gati-grahaṇam upadeśya-vidyā-śeṣa-pradarśanārtham \| ata eva ante api ahaṃ tu te tad vakṣyāmi tathā puṣkara-palāśe āpaḥ na śliṣyante evam evaṃ-vidi pāpaṃ karma na śliṣyata iti upakramya saṃyad-vāmatva-ādi kalyāṇa-guṇa-viśiṣṭaṃ brahma-arciṣṭhāna-upāsana-sahitam arciḥ-ādikaṃ ca gatim upadideśa \| ataḥ kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma iti sukha-viśiṣṭasya prakṛtasya eva brahma-upāsana-adhyayana-adhyāhāraḥ paramātmā \|\| 15 \|\|

nanu ca kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma iti paraṃ brahma abhihitam iti katham avagamyate, parasmin ākāśa-dhāratanaya-abhidhānāṃ bruve; yāvatā kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma iti prasiddha-ākāśa-laukika-sukhayoḥ eva brahma-dṛṣṭi-vidhīyatā iti pratibhāti, nāma brahma mano brahma iti ādi-vacana-sārūpyāt \| tatra āha—

## \|\| 1.2.16 \|\|

*ata eva ca sa brahma*

**Комментарий:**

etas tu yaḥ ca kaṃ teṣu avasthita mukha-viśiṣṭasya ākāśasya abhidhānam, ata eva kha-ādi-vidheyasya ākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma \| etad uktam bhavati—agnibhiḥ prāṇaḥ brahma kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma iti yukte upakosalaḥ uvāca vijānāmi ahaṃ yat prāṇaḥ brahma kaṃ ca tu khaṃ ca na vijānāmi iti \|\| asya ayam abhiprāyaḥ:—na tāvat prāṇa-ādiṣu atīkośasanam agnibhiḥ vihitam, janma-jarā-maraṇa-ādiṣu abhaya-bhītasya mumukṣoḥ mokṣa-upadeśāya pravṛttatvāt \| ataḥ brahmaṇaḥ vaiparyam upadiśyam \| tatra prasiddhaḥ prāṇa-ādibhiḥ samāna-adhikaraṇaṃ brahma nirdiṣṭaḥ; teṣu ca prāṇa-viśiṣṭatve jagad-dharaṇa-yogena vā prāṇa-aśarīratayā prāṇasya niyantṛtvena vā brahmaṇaḥ upapadyate iti vijānāmi ahaṃ yat prāṇaḥ brahma iti yuktavat \| tathā sukha-ākāśayoḥ api brahmaṇaḥ śarīratayā tat-niyāmakatvena viśeṣaṇatvam uttārayoḥ vā acchabda-kṛtayā niratiśaya-ānanda-rūpa-brahma-sadṛśa-parama-puruṣatvena vā ? tatra dṛṣṭhānaḥ tayā īśvarīrātayā viśeṣatve vaiṣayika-sukha-bhūta-ākāśa-yogi-niyāmakatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ tasya ādi iti svarūpa-

varga-atītāṃ sthān, anyaḥ anya-avacchedena eva paricchinna-ānandaka-varūpatvaṃ brahma asmi iti asya etat-prakāra-nirddhārita-viṣayā kaṃ ca tu eva ca na vijñānam iti uktavān \| upakosalava emamātraṃ jānantaḥ ajñayaḥ \| yathā va kaṃ taiva evaṃ yadā eva evaṃ tadā eva kam iti upare \| brahma asukha-kharūpatvam eva aparicchinna-siddhyarthaḥ \| ataḥ prāṇa-carīratayā prāṇa-viśiṣṭaṃ yad brahma, tad eva aparicchinna-mukhava brahmatvaṃ vetti nigamitāḥ prāṇāṃ ca ha asmai tadākāśaṃ ca uvariri \| ataḥ kaṃ brahma evaṃ kañjadhyāparicchinna-sukhaṃ brahma pratipāditam iti paraṃ brahma eva tatra prakṛtam, tad eva ca atra akṣi-ādhāratayā abhidhīyate iti akṣi-ādhāraḥ paramātmā \|\| 16 \|\|

## \|\| 1.2.17 \|\|

*śruta-upaniṣatka-gati-abhidhānāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

śruta-upaniṣatkaṃya—adhigata-parama-puruṣa-yāthātmya-jñāna-saṃchedyatvāt śrutyantara-pratipādyamānā arcirādikā gatiḥ yaḥ, tām upānaravṛtti-lakṣaṇa-parama-puruṣa-prāptikarīm upakosala-upadiṣṭa-puruṣaṃ śrūtvate “eḍeṣi me mā abhisaṃbhavati arcirādi ha” iti upakramya “candramaṃso vidyutaṃ tat puruṣaḥ amānavaḥ sa enān brahma gamayati eṣa deva-pathaḥ brahma-pathaḥ etena pratipadyamānā imaṃ mānavam āvartaṃ na āvartante” iti antena upadiśati \| ataḥ adhyamarddit-puruṣaḥ paramātmā \|\| 17 \|\|

## \|\| 1.2.18 \|\|

*anavasthiteḥ asaṃbhavāt ca na itaraḥ*

**Комментарий:**

pratibimba-vādī mādhvikṣaṇi niyamena avasthāna-brahmatva-vādīnāṃ ca nirupādhikānāṃ tejaḥ-saṃghātaḥ paramātmatvaṃ itaraḥ chāyādiḥ akṣi-puruṣaḥ bhavitum arhati \| pratibimbasya tāvat puruṣāntara-sannidhāna-āyattatvāt ca niyamena avasthāna-saṃbhavaḥ \| jīvasya api sarvendriya-vyāpāra-anuguṇatayā sarvendriya-karandhre sthāna-viśeṣe vṛttiḥ iti cakṣuḥ api na avasthānam \| devatāyāḥ ca raśmi-nirveśaḥ asmin pratiṣṭhitaḥ iti raśmi-yogāt asthānaccanirdeśāntara-avasthitatva-pārīndriya-adhiṣṭhāna-upapattena cakṣuṣi avasthānam \| saṃbhāvitevo nirupādhikatvāt tatvayoḥ na saṃbhavati eva \| tasmāt akṣi-puruṣaḥ paramātmā \|\| 18 \|\| iti antara-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha antarāmy-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

sthānādi-divya-upadeśāt caitanyaṃ akṣi-puruṣe titupitṛtyādismā pratiṣṭhāyamānaṃ cakṣuṣi sthiti-niyamanādikaṃ paramātmanaḥ eva iti siddhaṃ chāyādiṣuṣasya paramātmatvaṃ sādhitam \| idānīṃ tat eva samarthyate—

## \|\| 1.2.19 \|\|

*antaryāmy-adhidaiva-adhilokādiṣu tad-dharma-vyapadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

kāṇvāḥ mādhyandināḥ ca vājasaneyika-saṃbhāmanitaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivyāḥ antaraḥ yaṃ pṛthivī na veda yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yaḥ pṛthivīm antaraḥ yamayati eṣa te ātmā antaryāmī amṛtaḥ

iti \| evam akhila-āyana-tarikṣa-avyākriya-viṣaya-dūta-apāka-ākāśa-ārambhatvena istu deveṣu ca sarveṣu bhūteṣu prāṇa-vākya-mukhyatvena anantarastha-vijñānatetatsvātma-sādhyeṣu ca tiṣṭhan tasya tad-antarabhūtaṃ tat-tad-dehaṃ tanu-śarīrakaṃ tasya amṛtaṃ kaścit nirdiśya eṣa te ātmā antaryāmī amṛtaḥ iti upadiśyate \| mādhyandina-pāṭhe tu yasyaṃbhu lokeṣu tiṣṭhan yasyaṃbhu deveṣu yasyaṃbhu yajñeṣu iti ca paryāyaḥ \| yo vijñāne tiṣṭhan ityasya paryāyasya sthāne ya ātmani tiṣṭhan iti paryāyaḥ \| sa te ātmā antaryāmī amṛtaḥ iti ca viśeṣaḥ \| tatra saṃśayate—kim ayam antaryāmī pratyag-ātmā, uta paramātmā iti \| kiṃ yuktam? pratyag-ātmā iti \| kutaḥ? vākya-ārambhe eṣa śārīra iti karaṇāyatna-anātmabhūteḥ; evaṃ draṣṭre śrotreti-ādi muktvā upadeśāt, na anyaḥ ataḥ asti draṣṭā iti brahma-svarūpa-nirvedāt ca iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate—antaryāmī adhivādhilokādi-bhūtaṃ tad-amṛta-upadeśāt \| adhidaivādi-lokādi-pṛthivī-hiteṣu vākyebhyaḥ śrūyamāṇaḥ adṛśyatva-ādi-guṇakaḥ ātmā paramātmā nārāyaṇaḥ \| kāṇvavāṭasiddhyorthivādimṛdyo vākyebhyaḥ adhikāryadhilokādim iti vākyāni mādhyandina-pāṭhe santi iti jñāpanārtham adhidaiva-adhilokādibhiḥ suṣumopohādānam \| tat evam ubhayeṣu api vākyebhyaḥ tv ayam paramātmā ity arthaḥ \| kutaḥ? tad-amṛta-upadeśāt; parasmātma-dharmā hi amṛtaṃ, pratyeka evaṃ satsu sarva-loka-saṃbhūta-sarva-deva-adhīśrīyamayāt iti \| tathā udāharaṇam—ya imaṃ ca lokaṃ paraṃ ca lokaṃ sarvāṇi ca bhūtāni yaḥ antaraḥ yamayati iti upakramya saḥ antaryāmīṇaḥ brahma iti \| tasya ca etasya yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan iti ārabhya uktam \| tat iha etat paryallī kān sarvāṇi ca bhūtāni sarvān devān sarvāṃś ca vedān sarvāḥ ca yajñān antaḥ praviśya sarva-prakāra-niyamanam, sarva-śarīratayā sarvasya ātmatvaṃ ca sarvajñātatsyaḥkhaduḥkhaluptaphalatvāt anyasya na saṃbhavati \| tathā hi antaḥ praviṣṭaḥ śāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā śrītadṛśaḥ, tat eva anupraviśat, tat anupṛviśya, sat ca tyat ca abhavat ityādīni opaniṣadāni vākyāni paramātmanaḥ eva sarvasya praśāsitṛtvaṃ sarvasya ātmatvaniyātīti vadanti \| tathā subāla-upaniṣadi na etat eva viśvanāma āsīt dakṣalunādharṣimāḥ prajāḥ prajāyante divyaḥ devaḥ ekaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ; dakṣaḥ ca brahmā ca nārāyaṇaḥ; śrotraṃ ca śrotavyaṃ ca nārāyaṇaḥ iti ārabhya ananta-śarīre nihitaḥ guhāyām ajaḥ ekaḥ nityaḥ yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yaḥ pṛthivīm antare sañcaran yaṃ pṛthivī na veda yasya āpaḥ śarīram ityādi, yasya mṛtyuḥ śarīraṃ yaḥ mṛtyum antare sañcaran yaṃ mṛtyuḥ na veda eṣa sarva-bhūta-antarātmā apahata-pāpmā divyaḥ devaḥ ekaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ iti parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ sarva-niyantṛtvaṃ sarva-śarīratvaṃ sarvasya niyantṛtvaṃ ca pratipādyate \|\|

svābhāvikaṃ ca amṛtatvaṃ paramātmanaḥ eva dharmaḥ \| na ca parastha-ātmanāḥ karaṇāyatanaṃ draṣṭṛtva-ādikam; akhilu svabhāvataḥ eva sarvajñatā-tattva-samūlatvāt cha svataḥ eva \| tathā ca śrutiḥ parā asya śaktiḥ vividhā eva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca iti \| na ca darśana-śravaṇa-ādi-śabda-

akṣurādhikaraṇa-janmanaḥ jñānasya vācakaḥ; api tu rūpa-siddhākārasya \| sa ca rūpa-siddhākāraḥ karmātiriktatva-sambhāvikānāṃ jīvasya cakṣurādi-karaṇa-janmanaḥ; paraḥ tu svataḥ eva \| śrutyā jyotiṣṭomotistha dṛṣṭeḥ iva evam api pūrva-vākya-uditā-trivṛt-kṛdduragyo brahma na asti iti vadati \| śrayam pṛthivī na veda śrayam ātmā na veda iti evam-ādi-viṣayaiḥ pṛthivī-ātmādi-niyāmya-antary-upalakṣamāṇaḥ eva niyamyatā iti yuktam eva, tathā eva śrutyā adṛśyaḥ hi amṛtaḥ śrotrī iti niyamasya anāyogyotosist dṛṣṭeḥ ityādinā tasya niyantṛ-niyamantaret niṣidhyate \| śṛṇuṣa ta ātmā śrasa ta ātmā iti ca na iti vyatireka-viśiṣṭa-nirviśeṣya jīvasya evaṃyogodṛśyamānondartayāminī na pratyagātmā bhavitum arhati \|\| 19 \|\|

## \|\| 1.2.20 \|\|

*na ca smārtam atad-dharma-abhilāpāt śārīraḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

smārtam pradhānam \| śārīraḥ jīvaḥ \| smārtam ca śārīraḥ ca na antaryāmi, ātmādi-nilopatvat—tayoḥ asambhāvita-dharma-abhilāpatvāt \| svabhāvataḥ eva sarvasya draṣṭṛtvam, sarvasya niyantṛtvam, sarvasya ātmatvam, svataḥ eva amṛtatvam ca tayoḥ na saṃbhāvanā-mūlam arhati \| etat uktam bhavati—yathā smārtasvetanaṃ sarvendriya-niyantṛtva-sarvavikāsvāditvam na arhati, tathā jīvaḥ api, atadṛsattvāt—iti \| amīṣām guṇānām paramātmanyevatvaḥ, pratyagātmani vyatirekaḥ ca sūtra-dvayena darśitaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

nirdeśam ca hetv-antaram āha—

## \|\| 1.2.21 \|\|

*ubhaye api hi bhedena enam adhīyate*

**Комментарий:**

ubhaye—mādhyandināḥ kāṇvāḥ ca, antaryāmiṇaḥ niyāmya-cetanena bāṇādibhiḥ acetanam ’evaṃ śārīram api vilakṣya adhīyate—śraya ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaraḥ yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīram yaḥ ātmānam antaraḥ yamayati sa ta ātmā antaryāmī amṛtaḥ iti mādhyandināḥ, śrayaḥ vijñāne tiṣṭhan ityādi ca kāṇvāḥ paramātmā-niyāmyatayā tasmāt vilakṣaṇatvena enam adhīyate īrśayam \| ataḥ antaryāmi pratyagātmanaḥ vilakṣaṇaḥ adṛhat-pāpmā paramātmā nārāyaṇaḥ iti siddham \|\| 21 \|\|

iti antaryāmy-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha adṛśyatvādi-guṇaka-adhikaraṇam \|\|**

## \|\| 1.2.22 \|\|

*adṛśyatvādi-guṇakaḥ dharma-ukteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

ātharvaṇikāḥ adhīyate—śratha parā yayā tat akṣaram adhigamyate, yat tat adreśyam agrāhyam agotram avarṇam acakṣuḥ-śrotram tat apāṇi-pādam, nityam vibhum sarvagatam susūkṣmam tat avyayam yat bhūta-yonim

paripaśyanti dhīrāḥ;, iti; tathā anantaram akṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ iti \| tatra saṃdihyate—kiṃbhūta adhyakṣatvādi-guṇakam aśaram akṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ ca prakṛti-puruṣau; atha ubhayatra paramātmā iti \| kiṃ tāvat prāptam? prakṛti-puruṣau iti \| kutaḥ? adhyakṣarasya adṛśye tu anātmaviṣaye iha śrūyate, akṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ iti ca sarveṣām adhikāryabhūtāt akṣarāt asmāt paraḥ kṣetrajña-samaṣṭi-puruṣaḥ pratīyate \| evam uktaṃ bhavati—rūpādimattva-sthūlarūpa-cetana-pṛthivyādi-mūlāśrayaṃ dṛśyatvādikaṃ pratividhyamānaṃ pṛthivyādi-samasta-atītaṃ sūkṣmarūpa-cetanam etat pradhāna-paryapti, tat ca pradhānam eva \| tasmāt paratvaṃ ca samaṣṭi-puruṣasya prasiddham \| tad-adhiṣṭhitaṃ ca pradhānaṃ mahadādi-viśeṣa-paryantaṃ vikāra-jātaṃ prasūta iti tatra draṣṭā upapadyate yathā ūrṇanābhiḥ sṛjate gṛhṇate ca yathā pṛthivyām oṣadhayaḥ saṃbhavanti \| yathā sataḥ puruṣāt keśa-lomāni tathā akṣarāt saṃbhavati iha viśvam \|\| iti \| ataḥ asmin prakaraṇe pradhāna-puruṣau eva pratipādyete iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte brūmaḥ-- adṛśyatvādi-guṇakaḥ dharmoktiḥ \| adṛśyatvādi-guṇakaḥ akṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ ca parama-puruṣaḥ ekaḥ; kutaḥ? tad-dharmokteḥ, yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit yasya āditvā sarvajñatva-avikāsa-tatpūrva eva dharmā ucyante \| tathā hi yathā tad akṣaram adhigamyate ityādinā adṛśyatvādi-guṇakam aśaram abhidhāya akṣarāt saṃbhavati iha viśvam iti tasmāt dṛśyatvaṃ bhavaṃ ca abhidhāya yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit yasya jñānamayaṃ tapaḥ \| tasmāt etat brahma nāma-rūpam annaṃ ca jāyate \|\| iti bhūta-yoneḥ akṣarasya sarvajñatvādiḥ pratipādyate \| paścāt akṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ iti ca prakṛtam aśyatvādi-guṇakaṃ bhūta-yoni-akṣaraṃ sarvajñam eva paratvena vyapadiśyate \| ataḥ akṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ iti akṣara-śabdaḥ pañcamyantaḥ prakṛtam adṛśyatvādi-guṇakam aśaraṃ na abhidhatte, tasya sarvajñasya viśva-yoneḥ sarvasmāt paratvena tasmāt ananyasya parasya asaṃbhavāt \| ataḥ aḍyākṣara-śabdaḥ bhūta-sūkṣma-cetanaṃ brūte \|\| 22 \|\|

**itaratra na pradhāna-puruṣau—**

## \|\| 1.2.23 \|\|

*viśeṣaṇa-bheda-vyapadeśābhyāṃ ca na itarau*

**Комментарий:**

viśinaṣṭi hi prakaraṇa-praśnānāṃ ca puruṣāt ca bhūta-yoneḥ akṣaraṃ vyāvartayati ityarthaḥ; eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñopapādanāribhiḥ tathā sādhyam akṣarasya bhedaḥ ca vyapadiśyate akṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ ityādinā \|\|

tathā hi—sa brahmavidyāṃ sarva-vidyā-pratiṣṭhām atharvaṇāya prāha iti sarva-vidyā-pratiṣṭhābhūtā brahmavidyā prakrāntā; paravidyā eva ca sarva-vidyā-pratiṣṭhā; tasmin na sarva-vidyā-pratiṣṭhāṃ vidyāṃ babhūpa atharvāṅgirāḥ pṛcchan aṅgirasā prāptāṃ jijñāsuḥ śaunakaḥ ha vai mahāśālaḥ aṅgirasaṃ vidhivat upasannaḥ papraccha kasmin nu bhagavaḥ vijñāte sarvam idaṃ vijñātaṃ bhavati iti \| brahmavidyayā sarva-vidyābhyatvāt abrahma-vijñānena sarvaṃ vijñātaṃ bhavati iti hṛtvā brahma-svarūpam etena pṛṣṭam \| tasme

sa ha uvāca dve vidye veditavye iti ha sma yad brahma-vidaḥ vadanti parā ca eva aparā ca iti \| brahmavit surā ha vidye veditavye—brahma-viṣaye parokṣa-aparokṣa-rūpe dve vijñāne upādeye iti arthaḥ \| tatra parokṣaṃ śāstra-janyaṃ jñānam, aparokṣaṃ yoga-janyam; tayoḥ brahma-prāpti-upāya-bhūtam aparokṣaṃ jñānam; tat ca bhakti-rūpāpannam, śravaṇaiva dhṛte tena labhyaḥ iti ayaṃ viśeṣaṇa-gaṇastāvā; tad-upāya-śramamajanyaṃ viveka-ādi-sādhana-saptaka-anugṛhītaṃ jñānam, śṛṇvantaḥ veda-anuvacanena brāhmaṇāḥ vividiṣanti yajñena dānena tapasā anāśakena iti śruteḥ \| āha ca bhagavān parāśaraḥ: śravaṇa-ādi-hita-karmāṇi ca karma ca uktaṃ mahāmune \| āgama-utthaṃ vivekāt ca dvidhā jñānaṃ tathā ucyate \|\| iti \|\|

śratparā ṛg-vedaḥ yajur-vedaḥ iti ādinā śadharmaśāstrāṇi iti antena āgama-utthaṃ brahma-sākṣātkāra-hetu-bhūtaṃ parokṣa-jñānam uktam \| sāṅkhya sa-itihāsa-purāṇasya sa-dharma-śāstrasya sarva-mīmāṃsasya vedasya brahma-jñāne avagati-hetutvāt \| śatha parā yayā tat akṣam adhigamyate iti upāsanātmakaṃ brahma-sākṣātkāra-lakṣaṇaṃ bhakti-rūpāpannaṃ jñānam \| śratat adṛśyam agrāhyam iti ādinā parokṣa-aparokṣa-rūpa-jñāna-dvaya-viṣayasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpam ucyate \| śrayonirnityasyate gūḍhe vaiṣya ādinā yathokta-svarūpādarasamudbrahmaṇaḥ akṣarāt kṛtsnasya cetana-acetana-ātmaka-prapañcya-opattiḥ uktā; viśvavittir

dantāḥ ca cetana-mātrasya \| śraṣṭa cīyate brahma tataḥ akṣam abhijāyate \| annāt prāṇaḥ manaḥ saryaṃ lokaḥ karmasu ca amṛtam \|\| iti brahmaṇaḥ viśvotpatinprakāraḥ ucyate \| tapasā—jñānena, śayasya jñāna-mayaṃ tapaḥ iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt; cīyate—upacīyate; śabdaḥ syāt nimiti sūkṣma-rūpeṇa jñānena brahma sūkṣmaṃ nikhilaṃ bhavati iti arthaḥ \| tataḥ akṣam abhijāyate—avyata iti artham, viśvasya bhoktṛ-vargasyā bhogya-bhūta-bhūta-sūkṣmamadhyākṛtaṃ paramātma-śarīraṃ jāyata iti arthaḥ \| prāṇa-manāḥ-bhūti ca svavyāpi-varga-phala-sādhana-bhūta-karma-paryantaṃ sarvaṃ vikāra-jātaṃ tasmāt eva jāyate \| śayaḥ savajñaḥ sarvavit iti ādinā sūkṣma pukaraṇa-bhūtaṃ prāgvedyamasaraṇḍaūlpadivikṣmuktam \| sarva-jñātasya yadhūlatayadarasmādubrahmaṇaḥ akṣarāt etat tu kārya-kāraṃ brahma nāma-rūpa-vikāraiṃ bhoktṛ-bhogya-rūpaṃ ca jāyate \|\|

tad etat satyam iti parasya brahmaṇaḥ nirupādhika-satyatvam ucyate \| śramantreṣu karmāṇi kavayaḥ yāni apaśyan tāni tretāyāṃ bahudhā santatāni, tāni ācaveta niyataṃ satya-kāmāḥ iti sāṅgya-satya-sthūla-padādhikaraṇāguṇaka-śarīraṃ puruṣaṃ svataḥ satyaṃ kāmayamānaiḥ statmādhye phalatādṛśebhyo viraktāḥ śreyaḥ-sukha-samaya-setuṃ karma-vidhi dhāni varṇa-āśrama-ācitāni tretāgnipu bahudhā santatāni kṣamīyāvacana iti śreṣa vaḥ panthāḥ iti ārasya śreṣa vaḥ puṇyasumṛtaḥ brahmalokaḥ iti antena karma-anugrāha-kāraḥ, śragni-sūryeticetispu karmasu eketarakṣayaipuṇyopariteṣām anuṣṭhātānām api niṣphalatvam, api ānuṣṭitasya ca anuṣṭhita-samvavidhāya śuddhāḥ hi ete adṛḍhāḥ yajña-rūpāḥ aṣṭādaśa-uktam avaraṃ yeṣu karma \| etat śreyaḥ ye abhinandanti mūḍhāḥ jarā-mṛtyuṃ te punaḥ eva api

yanti \|\| iti ādinā phalābhisandhi-pūrvakā eva jñāna-viśuddhatayā ca avarṇaṃ karma-nivṛttāṃ punar-āvṛttim uktvā jana-prade ye hi apavarganīyāhīnā punar api phalābhisandhi-rahitaṃ jñāninā adyuktaṃ karma brahma-prāptaye bhavati iti praśasya para īśya lokam iti ādinā kevala-karma-phaleṣu viraktasya yathodita-karma-anuṣṭhitaṃ brahma-prāpti-upāya-bhūtaṃ jñānaṃ jijñāsamānasya ca ācārya-upasadanaṃ vidhāya na etat tasyaṃ yathā sudīptāt iti ādinā ṣoḍaśa-vidyā-sthita-vikāro hi ṣoḍaśa-kalatvena pūrvokta-upāsanasya bhūta-yoneḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ parama-puruṣa-sthānaika-īśvaratva-pāṇḍyāḥ saha sarva-bhūta-antarātmatayā viśva-karīratvena viśva-kalpatvam, tasmahidvyadṛṣṭiṃ ca viṣaya-bhūmīdhāya abhisamnihita-nityādinā tasya eva akṣarastha-avyākṛta-antatvataḥ api puruṣāt parabhūtasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ parama-vyomni pratiṣṭhitasya anavadhika-atiśaya-ānanda-rūpasya hṛdaya-guhām upāsana-prakāram upāsanasya ca para-bhakti-rūpatvam upāsīnasya vidyā-avidyā-mokṣa-pūrvakaṃ brahma-samaṃ brahma-anubhavaṃ ca upadiśya upasaṃhṛtam \| ata evaṃ viśeṣaṇaiḥ medhya-pradeśāḥ ca na asmin prakaraṇe pradhāna-puruṣau pratipādye \|\|

medhya-pradeśaḥ api hi tāmyāṃ parasya brahmaṇaḥ anya vidyate \| divyaḥ hi amūrtaḥ puruṣaḥ sabāhya-ābhyantaraḥ hi ajaḥ \| aprāṇaḥ hi amanāḥ śubhraḥ hi akṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ \|\| iti ādibhiḥ akṣarāt avyākṛtāt paraḥ yaḥ samabhipuruṣaḥ tasmāt api parabhūtaḥ adravyatvādhigunakṣarāt janma-vidhi-hitaḥ paramātmā iti arthaḥ \| akṣarāt iti vā, na kṣarati iti ca akṣaram \| tad-vyapadeśaḥ api sva-vikāra-vyāmaya vā mahad-ādi-vibhāmantara-abhinavayoḥ akṣara-abhāvāt akṣaraṃ kathyacid ucyate \|\|23\|\|

## \|\| 1.2.24 \|\|

*rūpa-upanyāsāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

agniḥ mūrdhā cakṣuṣī candra-sūryau diśaḥ śrotre vāk vivṛtāḥ ca vedāḥ \| vāyuḥ prāṇaḥ hṛdayaṃ viśvam asya padbhyāṃ pṛthivī hi eṣa sarva-bhūta-antarātmā \|\| iti īdṛśaṃ rūpaṃ sarva-bhūta-antarātmatvaṃ paramātmanaḥ eva sambhavīti ataḥ ca paramātmā \|\|24\|\| iti adṛśyatvādi-guṇaka-adhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|

**atha vaiśvānara-adhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|**

## \|\| 1.2.25 \|\|

*vaiśvānara-sādhāraṇa-śabda-viśeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

idam āmananti chāndogāḥ ātmānam eva imam vaiśvānaraṃ saṃprati adhyemi tam eva naḥ brūhi iti prakramya yaḥ tu etam evaṃ prādeśa-mātram abhivimānam ātmānaṃ vaiśvānaram upāste iti \| tatra saṃdehaḥ—kiṃ vaiśvānaraḥ ātmā paramātmā iti śabda-niścayaḥ, uta na iti \| kiṃ prāptam ? aśabda-nirṇayam iti \| kutaḥ ? vaiśvānara-śabdasya rūḍhatvaṃ vṛttiṃ prayojadarśanāt—jāṭhara-agniḥ tāvat ayam agniḥ vaiśvānaraḥ yena mantraḥ pacyate yat idam adyate tasya eva ghoṣaḥ bhavati yat etat karṇau apidhāya śṛṇoti

tu yat uktam iti abhi na enam ghoṣam śṛṇoti iti \| mahā-bhūta-tṛtīye ca viśvasmā agnim bhuvanāya devā vaiśvānaram ketum ahām amṛdhan \| iti devatāyām vaiśvānarasya sumatī syāma rājā hi kam bhuvanānām abhi-śrīḥ iti \| paramātmani ca sadā sārye hṛdya-yajñī vaiśvānare prāpyante iti, sa eṣa vaiśvānaraḥ viśva-rūpaḥ prāṇaḥ agniḥ hṛdaye iti ca \| vākya-upakramādi-phala-paryantānām api liṅgāni sarvāṇi upāsyam netum śakyāni iti \|\|

evam prāpte abhidhīyate—vaiśvānaraḥ sādhāraṇa-śabda-viśeṣāt \| vaiśvānaraḥ paraḥ eva ātmā; kutaḥ ? sādhāraṇa-śabda-viśeṣāt—viśeṣaṇata iti viśeṣaḥ; sādhāraṇasya vaiśvānara-śabdasya paramātma-sādhāraṇeṣu sarva-viśeṣa-pramāṇa-tātparya-arthaḥ \| tathā hi—aupacanyavādayaḥ pañca me mahad-yaśaḥ sametya ko na ātmā kim brahma iti vicārya uddālakaḥ ha vai bhagavantaḥ ayam āṅgira saṃprati imam ātmānam vaiśvānaram adhyeti tam hanta abhyāgacchāma iti uddālakasya vaiśvānara-ātma-vijñāna-sabhavasya tam abhigacchuḥ \| saḥ ca uddālakaḥ eva anvaiśvānara-ātma-vijñāsūn abhilakṣya ātmabhya tatrā-kutsana-dīḍitvam matvā asau ha uvāca aśvatim bhagavantaḥ ayam kaikeyaḥ saṃprati imam ātmānam vaiśvānaram adhyeti tam hanta abhyāgacchāma iti \| te ca uddālaka-ṣaṣṭhāḥ tam aśvatim abhyājagmuḥ \| saḥ ca tān madhu-cchvīti-ādi pṛthak abhyarcya san me stenaḥ iti-ādinā adhyamāṇaḥ ha vai bhagavantaḥ aham asmi iti anena ātmanaḥ vṛtte brāhmaṇatayā pratigraha-yogyatām jñāpayan eva brāhma-viduḥ api pratigraha-pariharaṇīyām vihita-karma-kartavyatām ca prajñāpya yāvat eka-ekasmai ṛtvije dhanam dāsyāmi tāvat bhyaḥ ca dāsyāmi vasantu bhagavantaḥ iti avocat \| te ca samuṣitāḥ vaiśvānara-ātmānam jijñāsamānāḥ samit-pāṇayaḥ tam upasthitāḥ \| tat eva ko na ātmā kim brahma iti jīvātma-samabhūtam brahma jijñāsamāneṣu upaniṣatsu vaiśvānara-udāhṛta-kathā-garbhasya pṛcchyaśānataḥ vaiśvānara-ātmā paramātmā iti vijñāyate \| ātma-śabdaḥ ca ayam mukhyasya praśastatvāt ca ātmasu vaiśvānara-udantamya vyavahārāt brahma-śabdaḥ tāne nirdiśyamānaḥ vaiśvānara-śabdaḥ brahma vidhita iti vijñāyate \| kiñca sarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣu ātmasu annam atti iti nikhila-loka-antargataḥ bhoktā pradeśena ha asya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante iti ca vakṣyamāṇam vaiśvānara-ātma-vijñāna-phalam vaiśvānara-ātmānam param brahma iti jñāpayati \|\| 25 \|\|

**itaraḥ ca vaiśvānaraḥ paramātmā—**

## \|\| 1.2.26 \|\|

*smaryamāṇam anumānam syāt iti*

**Комментарий:**

śruta-artha-vivṛti-artham athavā vibhāgena vaiśvānarasya rūpam iha upaḍiśyate \| tat ca śruti-smṛtibhyām parama-puruṣa-rūpatayā prasiddham \| tathā hi tat eva idam iti samarthya-varṇe—pratyabhijñāyamānam vaiśvānarasya parama-puruṣatve anumānam—liṅgam iti arthaḥ; iti-śabdaḥ prakāra-vacanaḥ; idaṃ-bhūtam rūpam pratyabhijñāyamānam vaiśvānarasya paramātmatva-dyotakam anumānam syāt \| śruti-smṛtyoḥ hi parama-puruṣam eva etat rūpam prasiddham \| yathā

ārambhe agninām dhīḥ cakṣuṣoḥ candra-sūryau diśa-śrotre vāk vivṛtāḥ ca vedāḥ \| vāyuḥ prāṇaḥ hṛdayaṃ viśvam asya padbhyāṃ pṛthivī hi eṣa sarva-bhūta-antar-ātmā \|\| iti \| agniḥ hi dyu-lokaḥ; asau vai lokaḥ agniḥ iti śruteḥ \| smaranti ca munayaḥ dyauḥ mūrdhā yasya viprā vadanti khaṃ vai nābhiḥ candra-sūryau ca netre \| diśa-śrotre vidhi pādau kṣitiḥ ca saḥ acintya-rūpā sarva-bhūtā praṇetā \|\| iti, asya agniḥ āsyaṃ dyoḥ śīrṣaṃ khaṃ nābhiḥ caraṇau kṣitiḥ \| sūryaḥ candra-maḥ ca eva tasya loka-ātmanaḥ nanuḥ \|\| iti ca \| iha ca dyu-mūrdhatvaḥ vaiśvānarasya mūrdha-viṣayatvena ucyate \| tathā hi—tam ropanya-vamṛdhibhiḥ ṛṣibhiḥ ātmānam eva imam vaiśvānaraṃ saṃprati adhyepi tam eva naḥ brūhi iti pṛṣṭaḥ kaikeya-rājyaḥ vaiśvānara-ātmanam upadiśan viśeṣa-praśnāyāṃ prapannānāṃ vaiśvānara-ātma-vettṝn kiṃsvit jñātaṃ kiṃsvat ātmani iti vijñāya jñāta-ajñātāṃśam upajñutsayā tān eka-ekaṃ papraccha \| tatra aupastanya kam tvam ātmānam upāsse iti pṛṣṭe divam eva bhagavaḥ rājan iti tena ca ukte divi tasya pūrvaṃ vaiśvānara-ātmasu buddhiṃ nivartya vaiśvānarasya dyauḥ śīrṣaṃ ca iti ca upadiśaṃs tathā vaiśvānara-śiras-tayā divaḥ su-tejā iti guṇa-nāma-dheyaṃ prāṇicchayat \| evaṃ satya-prātihāra-āditya-vāyu-ākāśa-pṛthivī-toya-ekaikaikam upāsamānānāṃ kaphitānāṃ, viśva-rūpaḥ, pṛthak-vartmā, bahulaḥ, rayiḥ, pratiṣṭhā iti ekaika-guṇa-nāmadheyāni vaiśvānara-ātmana-kṣaḥ—prāṇa-sandeha-vasthi-pācayavatvaṃ ca upadiśya \| sandehaḥ madhya-kāyaḥ ucyate \| ataḥ evaṃ-bhūta-su-mūrdhatvāt-viśiṣṭaḥ parama-puruṣaḥ eva rūpīrati vaiśvānaraḥ parama-puruṣaḥ eva \|\| 26 \|\|

**punar api anirjñeyam eva ākāśa-śabdaṃ pariharati—**

## \|\| 1.2.27 \|\|

*6 śabdādibhyaḥ antaḥ-pratiṣṭhānāt ca na iti cet na tathā dṛṣṭi-upadeśāt asaṃbhavāt puruṣam api ca enam adhīyate*

**Комментарий:**

yat uktaṃ vaiśvānaraḥ paramātmā iti niśrīyate iti, tatra, śabdādibhyaḥ antaḥ-pratiṣṭhānāt ca aharasya ca parihṛta pratīyamānatvāt \| śabdaḥ tāvat agināṃ vaiśvānara-vidyā-prakaraṇe sa eṣaḥ agniḥ vaiśvānaraḥ iti vaiśvānara-samānādhikaraṇatayā avatīrṇe śrūyate; agni-saṃskāraḥ ca hṛdayaṃ gārhapatyaḥ manaḥ anvāhārya-pacanaḥ āsyam āhavanīyaḥ iti vaikṣvānitasya hṛdaya-ādiṣu ayaninya-kalpanaṃ kriyate \| tat yat pūrvaṃ prathamam āgacchet tat homīyaṃ sa yāṃ prathamām āhutiṃ juhuyāt prāṇāya svāhā iti ādinā prāṇāhuti-ādhāratvaṃ ca vaiśvānarasya avagamyate \| tathā vaiśvānarasya asmin puruṣe antaḥ-pratiṣṭhānaṃ vājasaneyinaḥ samāmananti sa yaḥ ha etam evam agniṃ vaiśvānaraṃ puruṣa-vidhaṃ puruṣe antaḥ-pratiṣṭhitaṃ veda iti \| śrataḥ agni-śabda-sāmānādhikaraṇyāt agni-caita-uparikalpanā-pātra-hṛdaya-ādi-āspada-antaḥ-pratiṣṭhānāt ca vaiśvānarasya jāṭharatvam api pratīyate iti na kenacit paramātmatvam iti cet \| tatra; tathā dṛṣṭi-upadeśāt—pūrvoktasya trailokya-śarīrasya parasya brahmaṇo vaiśvānarasya jāṭharāgni-śarīratayā

tad-dhiśiṣṭy-upasanniveśāt \| agni-śabdābhihitaṃ na kevalaḥ jāṭharaḥ pratipadyate; api tu jāṭharāgni-viśiṣṭaḥ paramātmā \| katham idam avagamyata iti cet, asaṃbhavāt—jāṭharasya kevalasya trailokya-śarīratva-saṃbhavāt \| trailokya-śarīratayā pratipadya-veśvānaram-ānādhikaraṇau jāṭhara-viṣayatayā pratīyamānau śiraḥ-śabdaḥ jāṭhara-śarīratayā tad-dhiśiṣṭaṃ paramātmānam eva abhidadhāti iti arthaḥ \| yathoktaṃ bhagavatā—ahaṃ vaiśvānaraḥ bhūtvā prāṇināṃ deham āśritaḥ \| prāṇāpāna-samāyuktaḥ pacāmi annaṃ catur-vidham \|\| iti \| jāṭharānala-śarīraḥ bhūtvā iti arthaḥ \| atas tad-dhiśiṣṭy-upasanniveśāt dṛśyate \| kiñca puruṣam api ca enam adhīyate vājasaneyinaḥ; saḥ eṣaḥ agniḥ vaiśvānaraḥ yat puruṣaḥ iti \| na hi jāṭharasya kevalasya puruṣatvam \| paramātmanaḥ eva hi nirupādhikaṃ puruṣatvam \| yathā sahasra-śīrṣā puruṣaḥ puruṣaḥ eva idaṃ sarvam, ityādau \|\|27\|\|

## \|\| 1.2.28 \|\|

*ataḥ eva na devatā bhūtaṃ ca*

**Комментарий:**

uktaṃ ca hetubhyaḥ devatā-mātraṃ tṛtīyasya mahā-bhūtatvam api na vaiśvānaratva-prasaktam \|\|28\|\|

## \|\| 1.2.29 \|\|

*sākṣāt api avirodhaṃ jaiminiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

vaiśvānara-samānādhikaraṇa-sthāni-śabdasya jāṭharāgni-śarīratayā tad-dhiśiṣṭasya paramātmanaḥ vācakatvam, tathā eva paramātmana upāsyatvaṃ ca uktam \| jaiminiḥ tu ācāryaḥ vaiśvānara-śabdavat vini-śabdasyāpi paramātmanaḥ eva sākṣāt abhidhānena vācakatve na kaścit virodhaḥ iti manyate \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—yathā vaiśvānara-śabdaḥ sādhāraṇaḥ api paramātma-sādhāraṇa-dharma-viśeṣitaḥ viśveṣāṃ narāṇāṃ netṛtvādīnāṃ guṇānāṃ paramātmani eva abhidhāti iti niścīyate; evam agni-śabdaḥ api acyamanādinā yena ayaṃ guṇena yoga-rūḍhatvena vartate; tathā eva guṇasya nirupādhikasya kāṣṭhāgatasya paramātmani saṃbhavāt smṛti-prakaraṇe paramātma-sādhāraṇa-dharma-viśeṣitaḥ paramātmānam eva abhidadhat iti \|\|29\|\|

prastute tasmin evaṃ prādeśa-mātram abhivimānam iti vyapariṇḍakasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ hi amūrtitvāt dhyanta-prādeśa-saṃbandhitayā mātrayā paricchinnatvaṃ katham upapadyate ? tatra āha—

## \|\| 1.2.30 \|\|

*abhivyakteḥ iti āśmarathyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

upāsakābhivyakti-arthaṃ prādeśa-mātratvaṃ paramātmanaḥ iti āśmarathya-ācāryaḥ manyate \| dyauḥ mūrdhā ādityāḥ cakṣuḥ, vāyuḥ prāṇaḥ, ākāśaḥ madhya-kāyaḥ, āpaḥ vastiḥ, pṛthivī pādau iti hi amūrti-prādeśa-saṃbandhitayā mātrayā paricchinnatvam ahar-ahar abhidhyāyamānataḥ viyata-mātrasya hi abhivyaktiḥ eva hetuḥ bhavati \|\|30\|\|

mūrdhni-amūlya-paryanta-viśeṣaḥ puruṣa-vidhatvaṃ parasya brahmaṇaḥ kim-artham iti cet, tatra āha—

## \|\| 1.2.31 \|\|

*anumṛteś ca vidhiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

tathā upāsanārtham iti bādariḥ ācāryo manyate \| śrūyate ca evam agnihotram ātmatāṃ vaiśvānarasya upāste sa sarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣu ātmasu annam atti iti brahma-prāptaye hi upāsanam upadiśyate \| evam eva anitā—uttara-kāraṇe puruṣākāratvārthaḥ \| sarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣu ātmasu vartamānaṃ yat taṃ bhogyaṃ tad atti—saṃbandha vartamānaṃ svataḥ eva anavadhikātiśaya-ānandaṃ brahma anubhavati \| yaḥ tu sarvaḥ karmavaśyaiḥ asmiḥ pratyekam anyasādhāraṇam annaṃ bhuñjate, tanmukhyabhis tyājyatvād iha na gṛhyate \|\|31\|\|

yadi paramātmani vaiśvānaraḥ; kathaṃ tarhi ṛṣi-smṛtīnāṃ vaidyādivat prādeśaḥ, yāvatā jāṭhara-nirapekṣam evaṃvad upacyata iti āha—

## \|\| 1.2.32 \|\|

*saṃpatter iti jaiminis tathā hi darśayati*

**Комментарий:**

atha paramātmanaḥ eva vaiśvānarasya dyu-mūrdhatva-prabhṛti-antarīkṣatvasya samārādhana-bhūtāyāḥ upāsakaiḥ ahaḥ kriyamāṇāyāḥ prāg-āhutaiḥ agnihotra-avacchedena ātmāyāmṛta-prabhṛtīnāṃ vaidyavat prādeśaḥ iti jaiminiḥ ācāryo manyate \| tathā hi—paramātma-upāsana-upacitā eva phalaṃ prāg-āhutyā agnihotra-saṃpattiḥ ca darśayati iyaṃ śrutiḥ: sa yaḥ idam avidvān agnihotraṃ juhoti yathā aṅgārān apohya bhasmani juhuyāt tādṛk tat syād atha yaḥ etad evaṃ vidvān agnihotraṃ juhoti tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣu ātmasu hutaṃ bhavati tathāpekṣā-kāla-tulya-sarve prāteḥ pradyotataḥ eva ha asya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante iti \|\|32\|\|

## \|\| 1.2.33 \|\|

*āmananti ca enam asmin*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ parama-puruṣe dyu-mūrdhatvādi-viśiṣṭe vaiśvānare asmin upāsaka-śarīre prāg-āhuti-ādhāratvaya āmananti ca śrutasya ha vā etasya ātmanaḥ vaiśvānarasya mūrdhā eva sutejāḥ iti ādinā \| aparyayaḥ—śrūyate caivem prādeśa-mātram abhivimānam ātmānaṃ vaiśvānar upāste iti trailokya-śarīrasya paramātmanaḥ vaiśvānarasya upāsanaṃ vidhāya sarveṣu lokeṣu iti ādinā brahma-prāptiṃ ca phalam upadiśya arthaghoṣa-upāsana-kṛtya-bhūtaṃ prāṇa-agnihotraṃ śrutasya ha vā etasya ātmanaḥ iti ādinā upadiśati; yaḥ pūrvam upāsyatayā upadiṣṭaḥ vaiśvānaraḥ tasya avayava-bhūtān anyadīyān dehe mukte api viśvasṛpādinām ādyātmikam upāsaka-śarīraṃ mūrdhādi-padānteṣu saṃpādayati \| mūrdhā eva sutejāḥ—upāsakasya mūrdhā eva paramātma-mūrdha-bhūtasya dyoḥ iti arthaḥ \| cakṣuḥ viśvarūpaḥ—ādityaḥ iti arthaḥ \| prāṇaḥ pṛthagvartmā—vāyuḥ iti arthaḥ \| saṃdeho bahula—upāsakasya madhya-kāyaḥ eva paramātmasya madhya-kāya-bhūtaḥ ākāśaḥ iti arthaḥ \| vastiḥ eva rayiḥ—

asya daharaḥ eva tad-adbhūtasya āpaḥ iti arthaḥ \| pṛthivī eva pādau—asya pādau eva tat-pāda-bhūtā pṛthivī iti arthaḥ \| evam upāsakaḥ sva-śarīre paramātmānaṃ trailokya-śarīraṃ vaiśvānaraṃ saṃnihitam upasaṃdhāya svakīya-dyuroloma-hattvam-annāstyāni prāṇa-hṛdayādeḥ asya paramātmanaḥ vaiśvānarasya vaidika-bahir-gārhapatyāhavanīya-pacanāhavanīya-agnihotra-upekaraṇa-bhūta-antar-uktatvaṃ prāṇahutaiḥ ca agnihotreṣu eva parikalpya vihitaṃ prāṇāgnihotreṇa paramātmānaṃ vaiśvānara-saṃjñayā upacitaṃ uraḥ eva vediḥ lomāni barhiḥ hṛdayaṃ gārhapatyaḥ ityādinā upaviśyate \| ataḥ paramātmā puruṣottamaḥ eva vaiśvānaraḥ iti siddham \|\| 33 \|\| iti vaiśvānara-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye prathamasya adhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ \|\|

śrīḥ astu

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**prathama-adhyāye tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ—dyubhvādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Pada 3

## \|\| 1.3.1 \|\|

*dyubhvādi-āyatanaṃ sva-śabdāt*

**Комментарий:**

ātharvaṇikāḥ adhīyate yasmin dyauḥ pṛthivī ca antarikṣam otaṃ manaḥ saha prāṇaiḥ ca sarvaiḥ; tam eva ekaṃ jānanātha ātmānam anyāḥ vācaḥ vimuñcatha amṛtasya eṣa setuḥ iti \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim ayaṃ dyu-pṛthivyādinām āyatnatvena śrūyamāṇaḥ jīvaḥ, uta paramātmā iti \| kiṃ yuktam? jīvaḥ iti \| kutaḥ? arāḥ iva ratha-nābhau saṃhatāḥ yatra nāḍyaḥ tasmin eṣaḥ antaḥ carate bahudhā jāyamānaḥ iti parasmin eva śloke pūrva-vākya-prastutā dyu-pṛthivyādi-āyatanaṃ praiti punar api sadatmyam tena paramṛśya tasya nāḍy-ādhāratvam uktvā, punar api saḥ eṣaḥ antaḥ carate bahudhā jāyamānaḥ iti tasya bahudhā jāyamānatvaṃ ca ucyate; nāḍī-saṃbandhaḥ deva-rūpeṇa bahudhā jāyamānatvaṃ ca jīvasya eva dharmaḥ \| asmin api śloke otaṃ manaḥ saha prāṇaiḥ ca sarvaiḥ iti prāṇa-pañcakasya manasaḥ ca āśrayatvam ucyamānaṃ jīva-dharmam eva \| evaṃ jīve niścite sati dyu-pṛthivyādi-āyatnatva-rūpaṃ yathā-rūḍhi-cetaḥ tad-anugatatvam iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—dyubhvādi-āyatanaṃ sva-śabdāt \| dyu-pṛthivyādinām āyatanaṃ paraṃ brahma \| kutaḥ? sva-śabdāt eva parabrahma-asādhāraṇa-śabdāt \| amṛtasya eṣa setuḥ iti parasya brahmaṇaḥ asādhāraṇa-śabdaḥ \| tam eva viditvā amṛtaḥ iha bhavati, na anyaḥ panthāḥ ayanāya vidyate iti sarva-upaniṣadi-vākyeṣu saḥ eva amṛtatva-prāpti-hetuḥ śrūyate \| nitarāṃ ca bandhana-arthatvāt setuḥ—amṛtasya prāpakaḥ iti arthaḥ \| setuḥ iva vā setuḥ—nadyādiṣu setuḥ hi kūlasya pratilambhakaḥ—saṃsāra-arṇava-pāra-sthitam amṛtatvaṃ pratilambhakaḥ iti arthaḥ \| ātma-śabdaḥ ca nirupādhikaḥ parasmin brahmaṇi mukhya-vṛttaḥ; ātmanoti iti

hrāsamāḥ; śvetara-samastatya niyantṛtvena vyatiriktasya eva saṃbhavati \| atas tat api tasya eva śabdaḥ \| tat sarveśvara-sarvavidyādi-upapāditānāṃ parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ śabdāḥ \| nāḍī-ādhāratvaṃ tasya api saṃbhavati śatataṃ śirābhis tu lambaty ākośa-sannibhis tiryaś ca tasyāṃ śikhāyāṃ madhye paramātmā vyavasthitaḥ iti śravaṇāt \| bahudhā jāyamānaḥ ity api parasmin brahmaṇi saṃgacchate—ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate, tasya dhīrāḥ parijānanti yonim iti devatīnāṃ samāśrayaṇīyatvaya tat-tat-jātīya-rūpa-saṃsthāna-guṇa-karma-samanvitaḥ svakīyaṃ svabhāvam ajahat eva svecchayā bahudhā vijāyate paraḥ puruṣaḥ ity abhidhānāt \| smṛtir api ajaḥ api sann avyayātmā bhūtānām īśvaraḥ api san \| prakṛtiṃ svām adhiṣṭhāya saṃbhavāmi ātma-māyayā \|\| iti \| manaḥ-prabhṛti-jīvi-karaṇa-ādhāratvaṃ ca sarva-ādhārasya parasya eva upapadyate \|\| 1 \|\|

**itthaṃ parama-puruṣaḥ—**

## \|\| 1.3.2 \|\|

*mukta-upasṛpya-vyapadeśāc ca*

**Комментарий:**

ayaṃ dyubhvādi-āyatana-bhūtaḥ puruṣaḥ saṃsāra-bandha-muktair eva prāptavyatayā vyapadiśyate yadā paśyaḥ paśyate rukma-varṇaṃ kartāram īśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahma-yonim \| tadā vidvān puṇya-pāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paraṃ sāmyam upaiti \|\| yathā nadyaḥ syandamānāḥ samudre astam gacchanti nāma-rūpe vihāya \| tathā vidvān nāma-rūpād vimuktaḥ parāt paraṃ puruṣam upaiti divyam \|\| iti \| saṃsāra-bandha-avimuktaḥ eva hi vidyut-puṇya-pāpā nirañjanā nāma-rūpābhyāṃ vinirmuktāś ca \| puṇya-pāpa-nibandhana-vicitra-saṃsṛti-prayukta-nāma-rūpa-bhāvanena eva hi saṃsāraḥ \| ato vidyut-puṇya-pāpa-nirmukteḥ prakṛti-saṃsarga-rahitaḥ pareṇa brahmaṇā paraṃ sāmyam āpnoti; prāptavyatayā nirdiṣṭo dyubhvādi-āyatana-bhūtaḥ puruṣaḥ paraṃ brahma eva \|\| 2 \|\|

para-brahma-sādhāraṇa-śabdādibhiḥ param eva brahma iti prasādhya, pratyagātma-sādhāraṇa-śabda-bhāvāc ca ayaṃ paraḥ eva iti āha—

## \|\| 1.3.3 \|\|

*na anumānam atac-chabdāt prāṇabhṛc ca*

**Комментарий:**

yathā dharma-prakarṣe pratiyogaka-āśrava-sāmānya-dharmaṃ ta pratiṣedhaḥ; evaṃ prāṇabhṛt-īśayoḥ \| anumīyate iti anumānaṃ para-uktaṃ pradhānam ucyate, anumāna-asmitatvād anumānam iti vā \| atac-chabdāt—tat hi śabda-asādhāraṇi-ity arthaḥ \| arthān eva yad vyakti-nistha-vyāpāraḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

itthaṃ na pratyagātmā—

## \|\| 1.3.4 \|\|

*bheda-vyapadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

samāne vṛkṣe puruṣaḥ nimagnaḥ anīśayā śocati muhyamānaḥ \| juṣṭaṃ yadā paśyati anyam īśam asya mahimānam iti vītaśokaḥ \|\| ityādibhir jīva-adhilakṣaṇa-cetanāṃ vyapadiśyate \| anīśayā

bhogya-bhūtayā prakṛtyā mukhyam ānandaḥ ca iti jīvaḥ \| ayaṃ yadā svarūpa-ānandaṃ sarva-deśaṃ dhomamātram, asya īśvarasya mahimānaṃ ca nikhila-jagad-adhyumanantaraṃ paśyati; tadā vīta-śoko bhavati \|\| 4 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.5 \|\|

*prakaraṇāt*

**Комментарий:**

prakaraṇaṃ ca idaṃ parasya brahmaṇaḥ iti adṛśyatva-ādi-guṇako dharma-ukteḥ iti atra eva pradarśitam \| nāḍī-saṃbandha-bhūyājyāyamānatvavamanas prāṇa-ādhāratvaṃ ca prakaraṇa-viccheda-āśaṅkā-mātram atra parihṛtam \|\| 5 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.6 \|\|

*sthityadanābhyāñ ca*

**Комментарий:**

dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte \| tayoḥ anyaḥ pippalaṃ svādu atti anasnan anyaḥ abhicākaśīti \|\| iti ekasya karma-phala-adanam, anyasya ca karma-phalam anasnataḥ eva dīpyamānatayā śarīra-anta-sthitimatvaṃ pratipādyate \| tatra karma-phalam anasana-hetumān eva sarvajña-amṛta-setutva-saravitmādyukṣādhāyatanaṃ bhavitum arhati, na punaḥ karma-phalam adaṃ śocan pratyagātmā \| ato dyubhvādi-āyatanaṃ paramātmā iti siddham \|\| 6 \|\| iti dyubhvādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha bhūma-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.3.7 \|\|

*bhūmā saṃprasādāt adhi upadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

idam āmnanti chandoyogāḥ: atha nāma vācayati nāmabu joeti nāma abhijānanāti sa bhūmā, atha yaśrānyat paśyati anacchu gocarayad vijānāti tad alpam iti \| atra ayaṃ bhūma-śabdo bhāva-pratyaya-anto vyutpādyate \| tathā hi pṛthvadiṣu bahu-śabdaḥ paṭhyate \| tataḥ pṛthvadibhyaḥ imanicvā, iti imanic-pratyaye kṛte bahoḥ lopo bhū ca bahoḥ, iti prakṛti-pratyayayoḥ vikāre bhūma iti bhavati \| bhūmā-bahutvāniryarthaḥ \| atra ca ayaṃ bahu-śabdo vaipulya-vācī, na saṅkhyā-vācī, yatra anyat paśyati... tad alpam iti tad-apratipakṣa-pratiyogitva-śravaṇāt \| alpa-śabda-nirdiṣṭa-dharma-pratiyoginānparatvaṃ ca eva dharmi-paratve nirūpyeta; na dharma-mātra-paraḥ \| tad evaṃ bhūma iti vipulo hi arthaḥ \| vaipulya-viśeṣaḥ ca itastheyabagaataḥ tarati śokam ātmavit iti prakramya bhūma-vijñānam upadiśya ātmā eva idaṃ sarvam iti tasya eva upasaṃhārāt, atra saṃśayate—kiṃ ayaṃ bhūma-guṇa-viśiṣṭaḥ pratyagātmā, uta paramātmā iti \|\| kiṃ yuktam? pratyagātmā iti \| kutaḥ? śrutaṃ hi eva me bhagavadṛśebhyaḥ tarati śokam ātmavit iti ātma-jñānāya upasanne nāradāya nāma-ādi-vijñāna-paryanteṣu ātmasyateṣu upadiṣṭeṣu asti bhagavo nāmnaḥ bhūyaḥ; asti bhagavo vācaḥ bhūyaḥ; ityādiṣu praśnāḥ; vācāva nāmnaḥ bhūyasī mano vāv vācaḥ bhūmaḥ ityādīni ca prativacanāni, prāṇa-prāptau ca etad dṛśyate; prāṇe tu na paśyamaḥ \|

ataḥ prāṇa-paryanta eva ayam asmad-deha iti pratīyate \| tena eva prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭaḥ prāṇa-sahacārī pratyag-ātmā eva; na vāyu-viśeṣa-mātram \| prāṇo ha pitā prāṇo ha mātā ity ādyas ca prāṇasya cetanatā-samarthayanti \| pitṛhā...mātṛhā ity ādinā sa-trāpeṣu pitṛ-prabhṛti-śubha-deha-kāriṇi hiṃsā-karma-nimitta-upakrāma-vacanāc ca eva viprāṇa-upahati-antoṣa-pada-kāritva-laukika-jñāna-vacananāc ca hiṃsā-yogya-cetana eva prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭaḥ \| agrāṇeṣu sthāvareṣv api cetaneṣu madhye bhāva-abhāvayoḥ hiṃsātmaka-bhāva-darśanāc ca hiṃsā-yogyatayā nirdiṣṭaḥ prāṇaḥ pratyag-ātmā eva iti niścīyate \| ata eva ca arāti-vighātana-ādi-anubandhena prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭaḥ para iti na śaṅkanīyam, parasya hiṃsā-prasaṅga-abhāvāt, jīva-ādi-itarasya tad-deya-bhoga-upakaraṇa-bhūtasya dhvastatvāc citta-suno jīvasya sthititve na pratyag-ātmā eva arāti-vighātana-ādi-paratantraś ca \|\|

ayam eva ca prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭo bhūmā; asti-bhāvāt prāṇa-bhūmaḥ iti praśnasya, ‘ato vāva prāṇa-bhūmaḥ’ iti prativacanasya ca āśayād bhūma-śabdena ātma-prakaraṇa-prakaraṇa-yogāc ca \| kiñca prāṇa-vidinaḥ ativāditvam uktvā tam eva eṣa tu vā ativadati iti pratyabhijñāya satya-saṅgena ativadati iti tasya satya-vacanaṃ prāṇa-upasanā-aṅgatayā upadiśya upādeyasya satya-vacanasya śodhitayā pūrva-nirdiṣṭa-prāṇa-yāthātmya-vijñānaṃ yadā vai vijānāti atha satyaṃ vadati iti upadiśya tad-asiddhy-arthaṃ ca manana-śraddhā-niṣṭhā-prakṛtānām upadiśya tad-ārambhāc ca prāpti-amṛta-prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭa-pratyag-ātma-svarūpasya sukha-rūpatā-jñānam upadiśya tasya ca sukhasya vipulatā bhūmā eva vijijñāsitavyaḥ iti upadiśate \| tad evaṃ pratyag-ātman ekādhikāra-viśuddhaṃ rūpaṃ vipula-kham iti upadiśyati tarati śokam ātmavid iti upakrama-virodhaś ca \| ato bhūma-guṇa-viśiṣṭaḥ pratyag-ātmā, yata evaṃ bhūma-guṇa-viśiṣṭaḥ pratyag-ātmā, atas tv āhamaṃ pratyag-ātmāni aham eva adhastād aham upariṣṭāt ity ādyasya aham eva idaṃ sarvam iti pratyag-ātmano vaiśvam upadiśyati \| evaṃ pratyag-ātmā eva nirṇīyate sati tad-anuguṇatayā vākya-śeṣo netavya iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte ’bhidhīyate—

bhūmā saṃprasādād adhyupadeśāt \|

bhūma-guṇa-viśiṣṭo na pratyag-ātmā; api tu param-ātmā \| kutaḥ? saṃprasādād adhyupadeśāt; saṃprasādaḥ--pratyag-ātmā, eṣa saṃprasādaḥ asmāt śarīrāt samutthāya paraṃ jyotiḥ upasampadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate iti upaniṣad-vacana-siddheḥ \| saṃprasādāt pratyag-ātmanaḥ adhikatayā bhūma-viśiṣṭasya satya-śabda-abhidheyasya upadeśād iti arthaḥ \| satya-śabda-abhidheyaṃ ca paraṃ brahma \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—yathā nāma-vidyaṃ prāṇa-paryanteṣu pūrva-pūrva-adhikatayā uttara-uttara-abhidhānāt pūrveṣām uttareṣām artha-antaratvam, evaṃ prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭāt pratyag-ātmanaḥ adhikatayā nirdiṣṭa-satya-śabda-abhidheya-tat-samādhigamanam evaṃ; satya-śabda-nirdiṣṭa eva bhūma iti tatra ayaṃ paraṃ brahma eva bhūma-guṇa-viśiṣṭam iti \| tad āha vṛttikāraḥ: bhūmā tu eva iti bhūmā brahma nāma-ādiṣu alpā,

ātmā ṛddhivat nayopadeśād iti \| prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭasya adhikṛtatayā satya-saṃkalpatvaḥ katham avagamyata iti cet; saḥ vā eṣaḥ evaṃ paśyan evaṃ manvāna evaṃ vijānan atīvādī bhavati iti prāṇa-vidaḥ ativāditvam uktvā eṣaḥ tu vā ativadati yaḥ satyena ativadati iti satya-vit eva ativādinaṃ kurvan evaṃ samāhitatvādikaḥ vyāhared iti \| ataḥ eva eṣaḥ tu vā ativadati ity atra prāṇa-ativādinaḥ na pratyabhijñā \| ataḥ anya-ativāditva-nimittaṃ satyaṃ pūrvokta-avidyādi-nivarttanāt prāṇād adhikam iti vijñāyate \|\|

nanu ca prāṇa-vedinaḥ eva satya-vadanam aṅgatvena upadiṣṭam, ataḥ prāṇa-prakaraṇa-avicchedād ity uktam \| na etat, kasmai—lūnāhena hi ativādī ātmāḥ pratīyate, na tu naṣṭhe iva ativādita-satya-vadana-aṅga-viśiṣṭhātma-jñāḥ \| eṣaḥ tu vā agnihotrī yasmayaṃ vadati yād divā agnihotraṃ tarātrāv atithiḥ; pratītaḥ ca agnihotriṇaḥ satya-vadana-aṅga-vidhānān miti hi dṛṣṭā gatiḥ viśiṣyate \| atra tu avivādha-antaratva-niṣṭaṃ satya-śabda-abhidheyaṃ paraṃ brahma pratīyate \| satya-śabdaḥ ca satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma ityādiṣu parasmin brahmaṇi prayuktaḥ; anantatva-viśiṣṭha-ativādinaḥ pūrvaṃ samādhikartavyaṃ saṃbhavati iti vākya-svara-siddham anyatvaṃ na bādhitavyam \| ativāditvaṃ hi vastu-antarāt puruṣārthasya arthitva-antatva-upāyam vastu-bodhikam \| nāmādiṣu apayanta-upāyatvata-nikāntatva-upāya-prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭasya uttarāmvāditvāt prāṇa-viśeṣita-vāditvam \| tathā api samitiṣaya-puruṣārthatva-amṛtatvāt puruṣārthatva-upāyasya parabrahmādinaḥ eva sākṣāt ativāditvam iti eṣaḥ tu vā ativadati yasmāt te ativadati iti yuktam \| satyena iti tṛtīyam lakṣaṇe tṛtīyā; satyena pareṇa brahmaṇā upāsanena upalakṣitaḥ yaḥ ativadati ity arthaḥ \| ataḥ eva ca śiṣyaḥ prāyuktaḥ saḥ ahaṃ bhagavan satyena ativadāni iti \| ācāryaḥ ca satyaṃ tu eva vijijñāsitavyam ity āha \| ārāmataḥ prāṇaḥ iti ca prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭasthaman utpattiḥ ucyate \| ataḥ tarati śokam ātmavit iti prakrāntaḥ ātmā prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭād anyaḥ iti gamyate \|\|

yat tu uktam asti bhagavaḥ prāṇād bhūyaḥ iti praśnasya, asti vāva prāṇād bhūyaḥ iti prativacanasya ca darśanāt prakrāntaḥ ātma-upadeśaḥ prāṇa-upadeśa-paryantasānaḥ gamyate iti; tad ayuktam \| na hi praśna-prativacana-stha-abhāvaḥ artha-nityatvaṃ gamayate; pramāṇa-antareṇa api tat-sambhavāt \| uktaṃ ca pramāṇa-antaram \| asti bhagavaḥ prāṇād bhūyaḥ ity apṛcchataḥ api abhiyāyaḥ—nāmādi-śraddhā-paryanteṣu eva enena puruṣārtha-śūnyatayā pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ mati-jñāna-anteṣu uttarottare pūrva-viṣayeṣu tat-saṃdṛṣṭena ācārya-cchātritvāditvaṃ notkam \| prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭa-prakaraṇasya ayāparyavasiteṣu puruṣārtha-bhūta-sthitārthaṃ saṃvate saḥ vā eṣaḥ evaṃ paśyan evaṃ manvāna evaṃ vijānan atīvādī bhavati iti adhikānta-vastu-vāditvam uktam \| ataḥ adṛṣṭha-ātma-padeśa-samāptāḥ iti matvā śiṣyaḥ bhūyaḥ na papraccha \| ācāryaḥ ca evaṃ darśya api sa-atiśayaṃ mantvā nir-atisaya puruṣārtha-bhūte satya-śabda-abhidheyaṃ paraṃ brahma eṣaḥ tu vā ativad

tīti svayam eva upadiśet \| śiṣyaḥ api parama-puruṣārtha-rūpe parimita-brahma-guṇa-parikṣīṇe tat-svarūpa-bahu-upāsana-vyādhāta-sumahattayā śrīraṅge bhagavat-samāhita-vacanaḥ ati prārthayāmāsa \| tataḥ brahma-sākṣātkāra-nimitta-anivādi-vat-vikṣipte brahma-sākṣātkāra-upāya-bhūtaṃ brahma-upāsanam satyaṃ tu eva vijijñāsitavyam iti upadiśya śravaṇa-āhita-artha-lābha-manasya manana-upadeśena śravaṇam artha-viddhaṃ matvā śravaṇa-upāsana-bhūtaṃ brahmaṇi śraddhām śraddhā tu eva vijijñāsitavyā iti upadiśya tad-upāya-bhūtāṃ ca tan-niṣṭhām niṣṭhā tu eva vijijñāsitavyā iti upadiśya tad-upāya-bhūtaṃ ca tad-anurodha-pravṛtta-rūpaṃ kṛtim api kṛtiḥ tu eva vijijñāsitavyā iti upadiśya śravaṇa-ādi-upakrama-saṃhṛta-tilakṣye prāpya-bhūtasya satya-saṅkalpa-īhitasya brahmaṇaḥ sukha-rūpatāṃ jñātavyā iti sukhaṃ tu eva vijijñāsitavyam iti upadiśya niravadhikaya-vipulam eva sukhaṃ parama-puruṣārtha-rūpaṃ bhavati iti tasya eva brahmaṇaḥ sukha-rūpasya niravadhikaya-vipulatāṃ jñātavyā iti bhūmā tu eva vijijñāsitavya iti upadiśya niravadhikaya-vipula-sukha-rūpasya brahmaṇaḥ lakṣaṇam ucyate yatra na anyat paśyati na anyat śṛṇoti na anyat vijānāti sa bhūmā, iti \| ayam arthaḥ—anavadhika-atiśaya-mukha-rūpe brahmaṇi anubhūyamāne tataḥ anyat kim api, na paśyati anubhavitā, brahma-svarūpa-ādi-bhūta-tatvātāc ca kṛtsnasya vastu-jātasya \| ataḥ aiśvarya-paryāya-vidyun-niturvādi-viśiṣṭaṃ niratiśaya-mukha-rūpaṃ brahma anubhavatā tad-vyatiriktasya vastunaḥ abhāvāt eva kim api anyat paśyati iti; anubhavasya sarvasya sukha-rūpatayā eva duḥkhaṃ ca na paśyati \| tad eva hi sukham, yad bhūmāne puruṣa-anukūlaṃ bhavati \|\|

nanu ca etasmin jagat brahmaṇaḥ anyatvāt jagat-bhūmānaṃ duḥkha-hetuṃ parimita-sukha-rūpaṃ ca bhavat katham iva brahma-bhūtarikena tad-arśana-kathānād bhūmāne sukha-rūpatvam eva bhavet ? ucyate—karma-vaśyānāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ brahmaṇaḥ anyatvena anubhūyamānaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat tat-karma-anurūpaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca parimita-sukhaṃ ca bhavati \| ataḥ brahmaṇaḥ anyatayā paricchinna-rūpeṇa duḥkhena ca, jagat anubhavya karmi-nimitta-avākarma-upavidyā-vimuktasya tad eva jagat-bhūrti-pūrṇa-viśiṣṭa-brahma-anubhavantāṃ sukham eva bhavati \| yathā pitra-upahitaṃ pīyamānaṃ payaḥ pitta-āturasya enatva-mukhaṃ viparītaṃ ca bhavati; tad eva payaḥ pitta-āmuktasya sukhatvena eva bhavati; yad eva rāja-putrasya pitṛ-tulīopākaram asti atha eva anusandhīyamānaṃ priyatyam-anugatāṃ tathātva-anusandhāne viparyasaṃ bhavati; tathā niratiśaya-ānanda-rūpasya brahmaṇaḥ anava- dhika-atiśaya-amṛtāṃś caikatvā-guṇa-amṛtasya lolī-loka-rūpaṃ tad-anarakaṃ ca anukūtatīyamānaṃ jagat niratiśaya prīteḥ ca bhavati eva \|\|

ataḥ jagat-aiśvarya-aviśiṣṭam anavadhika-anutiśaya-mukha-rūpaṃ brahma anubhavantaḥ ataḥ anyat kim api na paśyati; duḥkhaṃ ca na paśyati \| etad eva upapādayati vākya-seṣaḥ sa vā eṣa evaṃ paśyan evaṃ manvāna evaṃ vijānan ātma-ratiḥ ātma-krīḍaḥ ātma-mithunaḥ ātma-ānandaḥ sa svarāṭ bhavati tasya sarveṣu

loke kāma-cāraḥ bhavati atha yaḥ anyathā ataḥ viduḥ anye rājānaḥ te kṣayya-lokāḥ bhavanti teṣāṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu akāma-cāraḥ bhavati iti \| svarāṭ—akṣaya-viṣayaḥ \| anya-rājānaḥ—karma-viṣayāḥ \| tathā na paśyaḥ mṛtyuṃ paśyati na rogaṃ na uta duḥkhatām \| sarvaṃ ha paśyaḥ paśyati sarvam āpnoti sarvaśaḥ \|\| iti ca \| tad-virodha-sukha-kṛttvaṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ ānandamayaḥ abhyāsāt ity atra prapañcitam \| ataḥ prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭa-akṣadyātmanaḥ api ananta-bhūtatvya satya-śabda-abhidheyasya brahmaṇaḥ bhūma-śabdābhyaṃ paraṃ brahma \|\| 7 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.8 \|\|

*dharma-upapatteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

asya bhūmnaḥ ye dharmāḥ āmnāta-pūrvāḥ; te api pariniṣpanne apavargyante \| eṣa muktiḥ iti svābhāvika-muktitveṣu, sve mahimni iti ananya-ādhāratvam, sa eva adhastāt ityādi sa evaṃ sarvam iti sarvātmakaṃ, ātmataḥ prāṇaḥ iti akṣa-prāṇa-prabhṛti-sarvasya upādānaka-varnirvāhyaṃ hi dharmāḥ paramātmanaḥ eva \| yat u aham eva adhastāt ityādinā sarvātmakatvam upadiśya, tasya bhūma-viśiṣṭasya brahmaṇaḥ ahaṃ-graha-upāsanam upadiśyate, atha ataḥ ahaṃkāra-ādeśaḥ iti ahaṃ-graha-upadeśa-upakramāt \| ahaṃ-arthaḥ ca pratyagātmanaḥ api hi ātmā paramātmā iti antaryāmitvāt jñāṇa-viśeṣitvam \| ataḥ pratyag-arthasya paramātma-upāsyāmāna-dharmavattvāt brahmaṇaḥ api paramātma-svaprakāsayīti pratyagātma-śarīrakatvena paramātmānuśandhāna-arthe apahaṃ-graha-upadeśaḥ \| paramātmanaḥ svaṃ vaiśvaryaṃ tathā sarva-nityatvāt pratyagātmanaḥ anya-ātmā paramātmā \| tad eva etad atha ataḥ ātmādeśaḥ ityādinā ātmā evaṃ sarvam iti anena ucyate \| etad eva upādāyiṃtuṃ pratyagātmanaḥ aḍyātman bhūtāt parātmanaḥ sarveśyatva-pūrtiḥ ucyate tasya ha vai etasya evaṃ paśyataḥ evaṃ manvānasya evaṃ vijānataḥ ātmataḥ prāṇaḥ ātmatā ākāśaḥ ityādinā \| upāsakasya antaryāmitayā avasthitasya paramātmanaḥ sarva-deva-yogititva-arthaḥ \| ataḥ paramātmanaḥ pratyagātma-śarīrakatva-jñāna-arthitārtham ahagraha-upāsanaṃ kartavyam \| tasmāt bhūma-viśiṣṭaḥ paramātmā iti siddham \|\|8\|\| iti bhūma-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha akṣara-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.3.9 \|\|

*akṣaram ambara-anta-dhṛteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

vājasaneyinaḥ gārgyāṃ ca ante samāmananti aho vācā etad akṣaraṃ gārgi brāhmaṇāḥ abhivadanti asthūlam anaṇu ahrasvam adīrgham alohitam asneham acchāyam ityādi \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim etad akṣaraṃ pradhānam, jīvaḥ vā, uta paramātmā iti \| kiṃ yuktam ? pradhānam iti \| kutaḥ ? akṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ ityādi akṣara-śabdasya pradhāne prayoga-darśanāt adastūlatvādayaḥ ca tatra samanvayāt \| yayā tad akṣaram adhigamyate ityādiṣu parasmāt api akṣara-śabdaḥ dṛśyate iti cet na, pramāṇa-antara-siddha-śruti-prasiddhayoḥ pramāṇa-antara-siddhasya prathama-pratīteḥ; pratīti-parityāge virodha-abhāvāt \| kiñca, pūrvaṃ

mārgaṃ divo, yad aviṣpṛthivyāḥ iti asya sarvasya kāla-viśeṣa-vartinaḥ kāraṇa-bhūta-ākāśa-ādhāratve pratipādite karmimattu khadyākāśa autsya protasya vyomāśasya api kāraṇaṃ tad-adhāra-bhūtaṃ kim iti pṛṣṭe pradyumna-nāma akṣaraṃ sarva-vikāra-kāraṇatayā tad-ādhāra-bhūtaṃ pramāṇāntara-prasiddhaṃ pradhānam iti pratīyate; ataḥ akṣaraṃ pradhānam \|\|

iti prāpta ucyate—akṣaram ambara-anta-dhṛteḥ \| akṣaraṃ—paraṃ brahma; kutaḥ ? ambara-anta-dhṛteḥ; ambarasya—ākāśasya, antaḥ—pāra-bhūtasya avyākṛta-ambara-antāḥ; tasya dhṛteḥ—tad-ādhāratayā’ḍyākṣa-trayopadeśād iti yāvat \| ambara-arthaḥ—karmimattu khadyākāśa autsya protasya vyākṛta-ākāśa-śabda-anirdeśo na vāyumad-ambaram, apitu tat-pāra-bhūtam avyākṛtam, antasthasya avyākṛtasya api ādhāratvenāvyāmanākṣaraṃ na avyākṛtaṃ bhavitum arhati iti \| nanu ākāśa-śabda-nirdiṣṭo na vāyumān iti katham avasyate ? ucyate—yad ūrdhvaṃ gārgi divo yad aviṣpṛthivyā yad antara dyāvā-pṛthivyau ime yad bhūtaṃ ca bhavat ca bhaviṣyat ca iti ācakṣate ākāśa eva tad otaṃ ca protaṃ ca iti ukte trai-kālya-vartino vikāra-jātasya ādhāratayā nirdiṣṭa ākāśo na vāyumad-ākāśo bhavitum arhati; tathā api vikāra-antara-antargatvāt \| ataḥ avyākāśa-śabda-nirdiṣṭo bhūta-sūkṣmam iti pratīyate \| tatarasthāpi bhūta-sūkṣmasya ādhāra-bhūtaṃ kim iti pṛcchyate karmimattu khadyākāśa autsya protas iti \| ataḥ tad-ādhāratayā nirdiśyamānam akṣaraṃ na pradhānaṃ bhavitum arhati \|\|

yat tu śrūti-prasiddhāt pramāṇāntara-prasiddhaṃ pradhānaṃ pratīyata iti, tatra, akṣara-śabdasya avyakta-śaktyā svārtha-pratipādane pramāṇāntarāpekṣaṇāt; saṃbandy-agrahaṇa-daśāyām artha-svarūpaṃ yena pramāṇena avagamyate, na tad-artha-pratipādane tasya apekṣaṇīyam \|\| 9 \|\|

evaṃ tarhi akṣara-śabda-nirdiṣṭo jīvaḥ astu, tasya bhūta-sūkṣma-paryantasya kṛtsnasya acit-hetunādhāratvopapatteḥ; astu ullalita-dyumaṇaviśeṣagopakṣatreṣu avyaktamandare līyate vyasyāvyaktaṃ śarīraṃ pradhānākṣaraṃ śarīraṃ akṣara-saṃvarṇi bhūtāni kūṭa-sthaḥ akṣaraḥ ucyate iti ādiṣu pratyag-ātmani api akṣara-śabda-prayoga-darśanād iti atra uttaram—

## \|\| 1.3.10 \|\|

*sā ca praśāsanāt*

**Комментарий:**

sā ca ambara-anta-dhṛtiḥ asya akṣarasya praśāsanāt eva bhavati iti abhidhīyate etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi sūrya-candramasau vidhṛtau tiṣṭhataḥ, etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi dyāvā-pṛthivyau vidhṛte tiṣṭhataḥ, etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi nimeṣāḥ muhūrtāḥ ahorātrāṇi ardha-māsāḥ māsāḥ ṛtavaḥ saṃvatsarāḥ iti vidhṛtāḥ tiṣṭhanti ityādinā \| praśāsanam—prakṛṣṭaṃ śāsanam \| na ca etādṛśaṃ svātmāsādhitrasarva-vastu-vivaraṇaṃ baddha-mukta-ubhaya-avasthasya api pratyag-ātmana saṃbhavati \| ataḥ puruṣottama eva praśāsitṛ akṣarasya \|\| 10 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.11 \|\|

*anya-bhāva-vyāvṛtteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

anya-bhāvaḥ—anyatvam, pradhānādi-vyāvṛttiḥ \| adhyakṣasya parama-puruṣa-antaryāmitvaṃ vākyasthena vyāvartyate “ya etat akṣaraṃ gārgi aviditvā draṣṭṛ śrotaṃ mantṛ vijñātaṃ vijñātu na anyat ataḥ asti draṣṭṛ na anyat ataḥ asti śrotṛ, na anyat ataḥ asti mantṛ, na anyat ataḥ asti vijñātṛ, etasmin nu khalu akṣare gārgi ākāśaḥ otaḥ ca protaḥ ca” iti \| atra draṣṭṛtva-śrotṛtvāt upadeśāt asya rakṣasya cetana-bhūta-pradhāna-bhāvaḥ vyāvartyate; sarveśvarasya eva sataḥ sarvasya draṣṭṛtvādi pradeśāt ca pratyagātma-bhāvaḥ vyāvartyate \| ataḥ iman-manomaya-vyāvṛttiḥ akṣarasya parama-puruṣatvaṃ draḍhayati \|\|

evaṃ vākya-sāmānya-vṛttiḥ—anyasya sādhraṇa-vākya-vṛttiḥ asya vākya-vṛttiḥ; yathā etat akṣarasmāt sarvaṃ sat eva iyaṃ draṣṭṛ ca sat-svabhava-tiriktasya samastasya ādhāra-bhūtam, evam amnātāt antasya draṣṭṛ ca sat etasya ādhāra-bhūtam amnātā asti iti vadan “na anyat ataḥ asti draṣṭṛ” ityādi-vākyaiḥ śodhyaṃ sat rūpaṃ vyāvartya tasya ādhārasya pradhāna-bhāvaṃ pratyagātmatvaṃ ca pratiṣedhati \|\|

kiñca “etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi dyāvā-pṛthivī vidhṛte tiṣṭhataḥ praśāsini yajamānaṃ devāḥ darvī pitaraḥ anvāyatāḥ” iti śrutiṃ smāraṃ vā yāga-sādhya-ādhikikaṃ sarvaṃ karma yasya ājñayā pravartate; tat akṣaraṃ para-brahma-bhūtaḥ puruṣottamaḥ eva iti vijñāyate \| api ca “yaḥ vā etat akṣaraṃ gārgi aviditvā asmil loke juhoti yajate tapaḥ tapyate bahūni varṣa-sahasrāṇi antavat eva asya tat bhavati yaḥ vā etat akṣaraṃ gārgi aviditvā asmāt lokāt praiti sa kṛpaṇaḥ; atha yaḥ etat akṣaraṃ gārgi viditvā asmāt lokāt praiti sa brāhmaṇaḥ” iti yat ajñānāt saṃsārādhiḥ yat jñānāt ca amṛtatva-prāptiḥ tat akṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma iti siddham \|\| 11 \|\| iti akṣara-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha īkṣati-karma-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.3.12 \|\|

*īkṣati-karma vyapadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

ātharvaṇikāḥ satyakāmā brahma-vidyā-udyate “yaḥ punar etaṃ trimātreṇa om iti etena eva akṣareṇa paraṃ puruṣam abhidhyāyīta sa tejasi sūrye sampannaḥ; yathā pādodaraḥ tvacā vinirmucyate evaṃ ha eva sa pāpmanā vinirmuktaḥ sa sāmabhiḥ unnīyate brahma-lokaṃ sa etasmāt jīva-ghanāt parāt paraṃ puruṣam īkṣate” iti \| atra dhyāyati-īkṣati-sāmādhikaraṇya-viṣayau, dhyāna-phalatvāt eḥ dhyeyaḥ; “yaṃ paśyanti yatayaḥ kṣīṇa-doṣe puruṣaḥ” iti nyāyena dhyāti-viṣayasya eva prāpyatvāt, “paraṃ puruṣam iti ubhayatra karma-bhūtasya ekasya pratyabhijñānāt ca \|\|

tatra saṃśayate—kiṃ siddhaṃ paraṃ puruṣam iti nirdiṣṭaḥ jīva-saṃghāta-rūpaḥ aṇḍa-adhipatiḥ aśuddhaḥ, uta sarveśvaraḥ puruṣottamaḥ iti \| kiṃ yuktam? samāṣṭi-kṣetraḥ iti \| kutaḥ? “sa yaḥ ha vai tat brahma”

vanaspatiṣu prāṇa-anusandhānād ūrdhvam abhyāgati bahavaḥ yāva sa heta lokaṃ jayati iti prakaraṇa-samānaṃ praṇavam upāsīnasya manuṣya-loka-prāptim abhidhāya, dvitra-mātr-upāsīnasya antarikṣa-loka-prāptim abhidhāya, tri-mātr-upāsīnasya prāptavya-abhidhīyamāno brahma-lokaḥ aṇḍa-antarikṣa-vartī jīva-saṃsṛti-rūpasya caturmukhasya loka iti vijñāyate; tad vatenā vaideśa-pāṇas-tuṣṭekābhidhṛti-viśvabhuk eva \| etasmāj jīvana-antarāt pariniśca vaideśamya-ādhikyaḥ para-deha-indriya-adhiṣṭhānam udbhūta-ajīva-cchid-rupād aṇḍa-brahma-loka-vāsinaḥ samaṣṭi-cid-rūpasya caturmukhasya paras tve nopapadyate \| ataḥ atra nirdiśyamānaḥ paraḥ puruṣaḥ samaṣṭi-puruṣa-rūpam eva \| evaṃ caturmukhatve nirdiṣṭe ajarad-vākyayoḥ yathā-kathañcit netatvayā \|\|

iti prāpte pratyavasthite—īkṣati-karma-vyapadeśāt \| īkṣati-karma saḥ—paramātmā \| kutaḥ ? vyapadeśāt—vyapadiśyate hi īkṣati-karma paramātmavena \| tathā hi—īkṣati-karma-viṣayatayā udāhṛte śloke yat tat draṣṭavya-grāhyaṃ vivarṇam acchāyatam asaṃbhavam apāraṃ paraṃ ca iti \| paraṃ śāntam ajaram amṛtam abhayaṃ hi paramātman, evaṃ-rūpam, etasmadūtamayamavedyaṃ brahma veda sampratiṣṭhyaḥ \| etasmāj jīva-ghanāt parāt param iti ca paramātmana eva vyapadeśaḥ; na caturmukhasya, tasya api jīva-ghana-śabda-gṛhītatvāt \| yat ca hi karma-nimittaṃ dehitvaṃ sa jīva-ghana iti ucyate; caturmukhasya api tattvāc, ate yo brahmāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrvam iti ādi \|\|

yat punaḥ tad-gamana-antariṣṭha-lokasya upari nirdiśyamāno brahma-loko brahma-lokaḥ trutukha-loka iti pratīyate, antasthastha-pratumukha iti; tad yuktam yat tat acchātanam ajaram abhayaṃ ityādi naiśkarmyaṇaḥ paramātmatvena nirdiśyate sati īkṣituḥ sthānatayā nirdiṣṭo brahma-loko na śarīra-bhūta-bhūta-loko bhavitum arhati \| kiñca yathā pādodarastvaca vinirmucyate evaṃ ha vai sa pāpmanā vinirmuktaḥ sāmbhiropyate brahma-lokam iti sarva-pāpa-vinirmuktasya prāptavya-yogamyatāṃ na caturmukha-sthānam \| ata eva ca udāharaṇa śloke idam eva brahma-lokam adhikṛtya śrūyate yat tat kavayo vedayanta iti \| kathyaḥ—

sūryaḥ \| hiraṇmayaṃ ca vaiṣṇavaṃ padam eva, tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyanti sūrayaḥ iti evam-ādiṣu \| na ca anantarikṣāt paraḥ trutukha-lokaḥ; madhye svarga-loka-ādīnāṃ bahūnāṃ sadbhāvāt \| ata etat ha satyakāma paraṃ ca aparaṃ brahma yad oṅkāraḥ tasmāt vidvān etena eva āyatanena ekataram anveti iti prativacane yad varaṃ kāryaṃ brahma nirviṣṭaṃ tad aihika-āmuṣmikaven vidyā viṣayam eka-mātra praṇavam upāsīnānām aihikaṃ manuṣya-lokam avāpti-rūpam phalam abhidhāya, dvitra-mātr-upāsīnatāthā āmuṣmikam antarikṣa-śabda-upalakṣitaṃ phalaṃ ca abhidhāya, tri-mātreṇa para-brahma-arcinā praṇavena paraṃ puruṣaṃ dhyāyatāṃ param eva brahma prāptavyam upadiśati iti sarvaṃ samañjasam \| ata īkṣati-karma paramātmā \|\|2\|\|

iti īkṣati-karma-adhikaraṇam \|\|4\|\|

**atha daharādhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|**

## \|\| 1.3.13 \|\|

*daharaḥ uttarebhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

idam āmnāti chandogāḥ: atha yad idam asmin brahma-pure daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma daharaḥ asmin antarākāśaḥ tasmin yad antaḥ tad anveṣṭavyaṃ tad vāva vijijñāsitavyam iti \| tatra sandehaḥ— kim asau hṛdaya-puṇḍarīka-madhya-vartī dahara-ākāśaḥ mahā-bhūta-viśeṣaḥ, uta pratyag-ātmā, atha paramātmā iti \| kiṃ tāvad yuktam ? mahā-bhūta-viśeṣaḥ iti \| kutaḥ ? ākāśa-śabdasya bhūta-ākāśe brahmaṇi ca prasiddheḥ api bhūta-ākāśe prasiddhi-prakarṣāt, tasmin yad antaḥ tad anveṣṭavyam iti anveṣṭavya-antaratayā pratīteḥ ca \|\|

idam eva prāptam abhidhīyate—daharaḥ uttarebhyaḥ \| dahara-ākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma; kutaḥ ? uttarebhyaḥ vākya-gatebhyaḥ hetubhyaḥ \| eṣaḥ ātmā apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ avijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpaḥ iti nirūpādhika-sat-sambhūtatva-ātmatvādikaṃ satya-kāmatvaṃ satya-saṅkalpatvaṃ ca iti dahara-ākāśe śrūyamāṇā guṇāḥ dahara-ākāśaṃ paraṃ brahma iti jñāpayanti \| atha ye iha ātmānam anuvidya vrajanti etān ca satya-kāmān teṣāṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu kāma-cāraḥ bhavati ityādinā yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate saḥ asya saṅkalpāt eva samuttiṣṭhati tena saṃpannaḥ mahīyate iti anena dahara-ākāśa-vedinaḥ satya-saṅkalpatva-phala-prātidarśanena api ātmā dahara-ākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma iti avagamyate \|\|

yāvān vā ayam ākāśaḥ tāvān eṣaḥ antaḥ-hṛdaya ākāśaḥ iti upamāna-upameya-bhāvāt ca dahara-ākāśasya bhūta-ākāśatve na upapadyate \| hṛdaya-avaccheda-nimbandhanaḥ upamāna-upameya-bhāvaḥ iti cet, tathā sati hṛdaya-avacchinnasya ghaṭa-āvṛttyādi-sarva-kriyatvaṃ na upapadyate \| nanu ca dahara-ākāśasya paramātmatve api bāhya-ākāśa-upameyatvaṃ na saṃbhavati jyāyān pṛthivyāḥ jyāyān antarikṣāt iti ādyaiḥ sarvasmāt jyāyastva-śravaṇāt \| na evam; dahara-ākāśasya hṛdaya-puṇḍarīka-madhya-vartitva-pratīti-apahṛti-nipūṇataratvasya bāhyasya; yathā adhikṛte api savitari ‘hṛdaya-puṇḍarīkaṃ savitā’ iti vacanaṃ gati-māndya-nivṛtti-param \|\|

atha syāt—eṣaḥ ātmā apahata-pāpmā ityādinā dahara-ākāśaḥ na nirdiśyate, daharaḥ asmin antarākāśaḥ tasmin yad antaḥ tad anveṣṭavyaṃ iti dahara-ākāśāt varṇitasya tasya upasthāpyam hṛdayaṃ tena prakṛtatvāt iha eṣaḥ ātmā apahata-pāpmā iti tat-saṃbandha-viśeṣa-viṣayaṃ nirdiṣṭaṃ muktatvāt; syāt etad evam, yadi śrutiḥ eva dahara-ākāśaṃ tad-vartinaṃ ca na vyamaṃkṣyat; vyamaṃkṣīt tu sā; tathā hi atha yad idam asmin brahma-pure daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma daharaḥ asmin antarākāśaḥ tasmin yad antaḥ tad anveṣṭavyam iti brahma-pura-ākhya-deha-upāsyatayā sanihita-para-brahmaṇaḥ puratve na upāsya-ākārāntaraṃ nirdiśya tan-madhya-varti

ca tattvavyabhūtaṃ puṇḍarīka-ākāram antatparimāṇaṃ hṛdayaṃ parasya brahmaṇaḥ veśmatayā adhiṣṭhāya sarvajñaṃ sarvaśaktim āśrita-vātsalya-sauśīlya-jaladhim upāsaka-anugrāhya tasmin veśmani sannihitaṃ sūkṣmatayā dhyeyaṃ dahara-ākāra-śabdena nirdiśya tat-sannihitaṃ ca akhila-heya-pratyanīka-svabhāvataḥ nirasta-nikhila-heyatva-satyakāmatvādi-divya-upādhika-anavadhika-atiśaya-kalyāṇa-guṇa-jātaṃ ca dhyeyaṃ tad-vedya-dharmiṇi upadiśyate \| atra tad-vedya-dharmī iti tat-śabdena dahara-ākāśaḥ, tad-antar-varti guṇa-jātaṃ ca parāmṛśya tad-ubhayam antavedyam iti upadiśyate; padyaśmin abhyudaye daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma iti atra tasmin dahara-puṇḍarīka-veśmani yaḥ dahara-ākāśaḥ, yat ca tad-antar-varti-guṇa-jātam, tad-ubhayam avedyam iti vivakṣitam iti arthaḥ \|\|

dahara-ākāśa-śabda-abhidheyasya para-brahmatvaṃ tasmin yat antaḥ iti nirdiṣṭasya ca tad-guṇatvam, tat-śabdena ubhayaṃ parāmṛśya ubhayasya api avedyatvathā vidhātuṃ ca katham anumatam iti cet; tat anuhitam anāśaṅkya—

śyāmāt ca ayam ākāśa-śatāvadeśaḥ antardeya ātmānaḥ iti dahara-ākāśasya atismṛtānavidyā ubhe asmin dyāvā-pṛthivī antar eva samāhite ubhau agniḥ ca vāyuḥ ca sūrya-candramasau ubhau vidyut-nakṣatrāṇi iti prakṛtasya eva dahara-ākāśamasyāśritī nirdiśya tasya sarva-jagat-ādhāratvam avedyam, yāvān vai ayam ākāśaḥ tāvān eṣaḥ antar-hṛdaye ākāśaḥ iti yat ca asti sarvaṃ tat asmin samāhitam iti punar api asmin iti tasya eva dahara-ākāśaṃ parāmṛśya, tasmin sarvopasaṃhṛtya loke yat ropayajātam asti, yat ca manoratha-mātra-agocaram iha na asti, sarvaṃ tat asyejagatsmin dahara-ākāśe samāhitam iti niratisaya mahattvaṃ dahara-ākāśasya avidhāya, tasya dahara-ākāśasya deha-ākhya-brahma-pura-antar-vartitvena api dehasya jarā-pradhvaṃsāde sati api parama-kāraṇa-ātmaṇā nitya-amṛtatvena nirvikāratvam uktvā, tata eva amṛtatvaṃ brahma-puram iti tam eva dahara-ākāśaṃ satya-bhūtaṃ brahma-ākhyaṃ puraṃ nikhila-jagad-āvāsa-bhūtam iti upapādya, asmin kāmāḥ samāhitāḥ iti dahara-ākāśam asmin iti nirdiśya, kāmya-bhūtāḥ ca guṇāḥ kāmāḥ iti nirdiśya, teṣāṃ dahara-ākāśa-antar-vartitvam uktvā, tava eva dahara-ākāśasya kāmya-bhūtatva-kalyaṇa-guṇa-viśiṣṭaṃ tasya ātmatvaṃ ca eṣaḥ ātmā apahata-pāpmā iti ādinā satya-saṅkalpaḥ iti antena sphuṭīkṛtya atha yaḥ veha prajā anveṣṭavyaḥ tīyāryaḥ ca teṣāṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu kāmacāraḥ bhavati iti etena tad-vidaṃ guṇa-aṣṭakaṃ tad-viśiṣṭaṃ dahara-ākāśa-śabda-nirdiṣṭam ātmānaṃ ca avidyuṣām itara-vyatiriktam āyamiṣyate ca karma kurvatām anantara-phala-avāptim asatya-saṅkalpatvaṃ ca avidhāya atha yaḥ iha ātmānam anuvidya vrajanti etān ca satyān kāmān teṣāṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu kāmacāraḥ bhavati iti ādinā dahara-ākāśa-śabda-nirdiṣṭam ātmānaṃ tad-antar-vartitaḥ ca kāmya-bhūtān apahata-pāpmatva-ādikān guṇān vijānātām ṛtarāṇyāśrayaṇam tasya parasya puruṣasya prasādāt eva sarva-kāma-cāritva-satya-saṅkalpatā ca ucyate \| ataḥ dahara-ākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma,

tad-antarvarti bāhya-hṛtāpma-tvādi kāmya-guṇa-jālam, tad-ubhaya-naiṣṭhyaṃ vijijñāsitavyam iti ca udyata iti nirṇīyate \| tad etad adhikaraṇe api spaṣṭam iti satimayattvariti kāmacavyapadeśa ityādinā \| ata eva etadyo hetubhyo daharākāraḥ param eva brahma \|\|13\|\|

**itaḥ ca daharākāraḥ paraṃ brahma—**

## \|\| 1.3.14 \|\|

*gati-śabdābhyāṃ tathā hi dṛṣṭaṃ liṅgaṃ ca*

**Комментарий:**

satyaṃ hiraṇya-nidhi nihitam abhijñāḥ upari upari sañcarantaḥ na vindeyuḥ evam eva imāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ ahar ahar gacchantyaḥ etaṃ brahma-lokaṃ na vindanti anṛtena hi pratyūḍhāḥ iti evam iti prakṛtaṃ daharākāraṃ nirdiśya tathā ahar ahaḥ saṃveśaṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ gamanam, gantavyasya tasya daharākārasya brahma-loka-śabda-nirdeśasya daharākārasya parabrahmatāṃ gamayataḥ \| katham anyoḥ asyā parabrahmatva-sādhakatvam ity ata āha—tathā hi dṛṣṭam iti \| parasmin brahmaṇi sarveṣāṃ kṣetrajñānām ahar ahaḥ suṣupti-kāle gamanam antra āmnātam dṛṣṭam—sa yatra etat khalu somya asarvaḥ prajāḥ sati saṃpadya na viduḥ sati saṃpadyāmahe iti, sataḥ āgamya na viduḥ sataḥ āgacchāmaha iti ca \| tathā brahma-loka-śabdasya parasminn abrahmaṇi dṛṣṭaḥ eṣa brahma-lokaḥ samrāḍ iti ha uvāca iti \| mā bhūt atra brahmaṇi gamana-darśanam; etad eva tu daharākāre sarveṣāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ pralaya-kāle eva nirasta-nikhila-duḥkhānāṃ suṣupti-kāla-vidyamān śrama-mātrasya paratra eva paryantaṃ liṅgam \| tathā brahma-loka-śabdaḥ samānādhikaraṇa-vṛttyā asmin daharākāre pratyutpannāmodasya brahmatve prayogāntara-nirapekṣaṃ paryāptaṃ liṅgam ity āha—liṅgaṃ ca iti \| niṣāda-sthapati-nyāyena ṣaṣṭhī-samāsa-samānādhikaraṇa-samāsaḥ nyāyyaḥ \|\|

atha vā ahar ahar gacchantyaḥ iti na suṣupti-viṣayaṃ gamanam ucyate; api tu antarātmatvena sarvadā vartamānasya daharākārasya parama-puruṣārtha-bhūtasya upāyupeyaḥ ahar ahar gacchantyaḥ—sarvāḥ asmin kāle vartamānāḥ tam ajānantyaḥ taṃ na vindanti—na labhante; yathā hiraṇya-nidhi nihitaṃ tatsya upari sarvadā vartamānāḥ api na labhante, tadvat ity arthaḥ \| aicha evam atra ātmatvena sthitasya daharākārasya upari bahiryamāsitānāṃ sarvāsāṃ prajānām asaṃjñatīnānāṃ sarvadā gatiḥ tasya daharākārasya parabrahmatāṃ gamayati \| tathā hi anyatra parasya brahmaṇaḥ antarātmatayā avasthitasya sva-niyāmyābhiḥ svabhimavatībhiḥ sarvābhiḥ prajābhiḥ avedanaṃ dṛṣṭam \| yathā antaryāmi-sitabrāhmaṇe yaḥ ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaraḥ yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ yaḥ ātmānam antaraḥ yamayati iti, adṛṣṭaḥ draṣṭā aśrutaḥ śrotā iti ca \| mā bhūt atra darśanam; svayam eva divyaṃ niravadhiṣṭānāṃ vāgvapurmukharṇabhāvasya asya hṛdayasthasya upari tad-ādhāratayā ahar ahar vartitā sarvāsāṃ prajānām asaṃjñatīnānāṃ gatiḥ iti parabrahmatve paryāptaṃ liṅgam \|\|14\|\|

**itaś ca dahara-ākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma—**

## \|\| 1.3.15 \|\|

*dhṛteś ca mahimnaḥ asmin upalabdheḥ*

**Комментарий:**

atha yaḥ ātmā iti prakṛtaṃ dahara-ākāśaṃ nirdiśya sa setuḥ vidhṛtiṣāṃ lokānām asaṃbhedāya iti asmin adhikaraṇe śrūyamāṇe dahara-ākāśasya para-brahmatvaṃ gamyate; upādhi-karaṇaṃ hi parasya brahmaṇo mahimā sarveśvara eva bhūtādhipatiḥ eva bhūta-pālaḥ eva setuḥ vidharaṇaḥ eṣāṃ lokānām asaṃbhedāya iti, tasya eva vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi sūrya-candramasau vidhṛtau tiṣṭhataḥ iti-ādiṣu; sa ca ayaṃ tasya parasya brahmaṇo dhāryatva-ākhyo mahimā asmin dahara-ākāśa upalabhyate; ato dahara-ākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma \|\| 15 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.16 \|\|

*prasiddheś ca*

**Комментарий:**

ākāśa-śabdaḥ ca parasmin brahmaṇi prasiddhaḥ kaḥ hi eva anyāt kaḥ prāṇyāt, yad eṣa ākāśa ānando na syāt; sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni ākāśāt eva samutpadyante iti-ādiṣu \| apahata-pāpmatvādi-guṇatvāt ca prasiddheḥ bhūta-ākāśābhidheyatāyāḥ aucityam \|\| 16 \|\|

evaṃ tāvat dahara-ākāśasya bhūta-ākāśatvaṃ pratikṣiptam \| adha idānīṃ dahara-ākāśasya pratyag-ātmatvam āśaṅkya nirākṛtya upakrānte—

## \|\| 1.3.17 \|\|

*itara-parāmarśāt saḥ iti cet na asaṃbhavāt*

**Комментарий:**

yad uktaṃ vākya-upakrāmāt dahara-ākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma iti, tad ayuktaṃ, vākyasya eva paramārthitasya jīvasya eva sāmānya-param iti atha ya eṣa saṃprasādaḥ asmāt śarīrāt samutthāya paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate eṣa ātmā iti ha uvāca etad amṛtam abhayaṃ etad brahma iti \| yady api śarīrāt asmin antare ākāśa iti hṛdaya-puṇḍarīka-madhya-vartitayā upadiṣṭhasya ākāśasya upamāna-upameya-bhāvāt asaṃbhavāt bhūta-ākāśatvaṃ na saṃbhavati, tathā api vākya-upakrānta-pradyumnāsaṃbhavaṃ yuktam āśrayitum \| ākāśa-śabdaḥ api prakāśādi-vyogāt jīva eva vartitavyam iti cet; atra uttaraṃ—na asaṃbhavāt iti \| na ayaṃ jīvaḥ; na hi apahata-pāpmatvādayaḥ guṇā jīve saṃbhavanti \|\| 17 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.18 \|\|

*uttarāt cet āvirbhūta-svarūpaḥ tu*

**Комментарий:**

uttarāt—prajāpatir vākyāt ca, jīvasya api apahata-pāpamatvādi-guṇa-yogaḥ niścīyata iti cet \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—prajāpati-vākyaṃ jīva-param eva \| tathā hi ya ātmā apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ avijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpaḥ saḥ anveṣṭavyaḥ sa vijijñāsitavyaḥ sarvān ca lokān āpnoti sarvān ca kāmān yaḥ tam ātmānam anuvidya vijānāti iti prajāpatir vacane niścīya-

rūpeṇa aśūnya-anaivedya-ātma-svarūpa-jijñāsayā prajāpatim upaseduḥ \| madhyate prajāpati-nigiritasvapna-sukhadavasthaṃ jīvātmānaṃ śarīraṃ krameṇa śubhra-mudrikādi-aparicchinna-viṣaya-upadiśya tatra tatra śobhayamaparyante pariśuddha-ātma-svarūpa-upadeśa-yogyāya tasmai madhyate madhyannadyaṃ vā idaṃ śarīram ātaṃ mṛtyunā tat asya amṛtasya aśarīrasya ātmanaḥ adhiṣṭhānam u iti śarīrastha-āvidyutanām ātmastha-āvidyuditam aśarīrasya ca tasya amṛtatva-svarūpatāṃ bodhayitvā na ha vai sa-śarīrasya sataḥ priya-apriyayoḥ apahatiḥ asti, aśarīraṃ vava santaṃ na priya-apriye spṛśataḥ iti karma-bandha-rahitasya yoginaḥ tat-anuguṇa-sukha-duḥkha-anivṛttyartham nirdiśya etat-abhāvam avidyāya evam eva eṣa samprasādaḥ asmāt śarīrāt samutthāya paraṃ jyotiḥ upasampadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate iti jīvātma-svarūpam eva śarīra-viyuktaṃ muktiḥ deśa \| saḥ uttamaḥ puruṣaḥ sa tatra paryeti jakṣaṭ krīḍan ramamāṇaḥ strībhiḥ vā yānaiḥ vā jñātibhiḥ vā na upajanaṃ smaran idaṃ śarīram iti prāpyasya parasya jyotiṣaḥ puruṣottamatvaya, nitya-virodhasya paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpannasya pratyagātmanaḥ brahma-loke yatheṣṭa-bhoga-avāptim, priya-apriya-vidyut-karma-nirmita-sarīreṣu ayukpadanāthinanuṣandhānaṃ ca abhidhāya sa yathā prayogyaḥ ācaraṇe yuktaḥ evam eva asmin śarīre prāṇaḥ yuktaḥ iti yatheokta-svarūpasya eva saṃsāra-daśāyāṃ karma-tattvaṃ śarīreṣu eva prathākaṣṭayogāt bhavata iti abhidhāya atha yatra etat ākāśam anuviṣaṇṇaṃ cakṣuḥ saḥ cākṣuṣaḥ puruṣaḥ darśanāya cakṣuḥ asya yaḥ veda idaṃ jighrāṇi iti saḥ ātmā gandhāya ghrāṇam asya yaḥ veda idam abhivyāharāṇi iti saḥ ātmā abhivyāhārāya vāk asya yaḥ veda idaṃ śṛṇavāni iti saḥ ātmā śravaṇāya śrotram, atha yaḥ veda idaṃ manvāni iti saḥ ātmā manaḥ asya daivaṃ cakṣuḥ iti cakṣuḥ iti cakṣuḥ vā etena karaṇena rūpāṇīni paśyan asya ca jñātavyaṃ pratyakṣyaṃ tataḥ eva śarīra-indriyebhyaḥ asya vyatirekam upapādya saḥ vā eṣaḥ etena (daivena) cakṣuṣā manasā etān kāmān paśyan ramate ye ete brahma-loke iti tasya eva vidyut-karma-nirmita-sarīrendriyasya manaḥ-ṣaṣṭhāvidhitena divyena svābhāvikena jñānena sarva-kāma-anubhavam uktvā taṃ vā etaṃ devāḥ ātmānam upāsate tasmāt teṣāṃ sarve ca lokāḥ ātmāḥ sarve ca kāmāḥ iti evam vidham ātmānaṃ jñāninaḥ jānanti iti abhidhāya sarvān ca lokān āpnoti sarvān ca kāmān yaḥ tam ātmānam anuvidya vijānāti iti ha prajāpatiḥ uvāca iti evaṃ vidham ātmānaṃ vidyuktam eva loka-sarva-kāma-avāptyuphalahitaṃ brahma-anubhavaṃ phalam abhidhāya upasaṃhṛtam \| ataḥ tatra upahata pāpmatvādi-viguṇakaḥ jīvasya tatha prakāraḥ jñeva eva veditavyaḥ \| ataḥ jīvasya apahata-pāpmatvādayaḥ sambhavanti \| ataḥ dahara-vākya-śeṣe śrūyamāṇasya jīvasya apahata-pāpmatvādi-viguṇa-saṃbhavāt tam eva dahara-ākāśaḥ iti niścīyate—iti ca iti \|\|

tatra āha—āvibhūta-svarūpaḥ tu iti \| pūrvaṃ smṛti-tirohita-apahata-pāpmatvādi-viguṇaka-svarūpaḥ paścāt vimukta-karma-bandha-śarīra-samudbhūtaḥ paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpannaḥ āvirbhūta-svarūpaḥ tu apahata-pāpma-ityādi-viguṇa-viśiṣṭatvena prajāpatinā avabodhayiten; dahara-vākye tu ati rohitatsvabhāva-apahata-

pādas tv ādi-viśiṣṭa eva dahara-ākāraḥ pratīyate \| āvirbhūta-svarūpasya api jīvasya asaṃbhāvanīya-setutva-sarvaloka-vidharaṇa-ādi asādhāraṇa-śabda-nirdeśanānāṃ cetana-acetanayoḥ niyantṛtvaṃ dahara-ākāśasya parabrahmatāṃ sādhayati \| setutva-sarvaloka-vidharaṇatva-ādayaḥ āvirbhūta-svarūpasya api na saṃbhavanti iti jagad-vyāpāra-varjanīya-sūtra-pāde ’pi āmaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

yad etad dahara-vākye atha ya eṣa samprasāda ityādinā jīva-prastāvaḥ kriyate iti cet; tatra āha—

## \|\| 1.3.19 \|\|

*anyārthaḥ ca parāmarśaḥ*

**Комментарий:**
