dahara-ākāśa-śabda-upabṛṃhaṇa-patva-jagad-vidhāraṇatva-ādivat anantasya tad-upasaṃhāra-upabṛṃhaṇa-phalatā-ādi-kalyāṇa-guṇa-viśiṣṭhatvam āvikarūpa-pratipādanena tadvattva-rūpaṃ parampuruṣa-upāsādhāraṇaṃ guṇam upadeṣṭuṃ prajāpatī-vākya-uktasya jīvasya atra parāmarśaḥ \| prajāpatī-vākye ca mukta-ātma-svarūpa-yāthātmya-vijñānaṃ dahara-vidyā-upayogitayā uktam \| brahma-upāsakaiḥ hi jīva-ātmanaḥ svaṃ svarūpaṃ ca jñātavyam eva; svayam api kalyāṇa-guṇa eva sarva-vikāra-nirāsa-asaṃkhyeya-kalyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇaṃ paraṃ brahma anubhaviṣyati iti brahma-upāsana-phala-antargatatvāt svaṃ svarūpa-yāthātmya-vijñānasya \| sarvān ca lokān āpnoti sarvān ca kāmān yas taṃ paryeti jakṣat krīḍan ityādikaṃ prajāpatī-vākye kīrtyamānaṃ phalam api dahara-vidyā-phalam eva \|\| 19 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.20 \|\|

*alpa-aśruteḥ iti cet tad uktam*

**Комментарий:**

dahara-ukteḥ imaṃ iti alpa-parimāṇa-śruteḥ ārāgra-upamitasya jīvasya eṣa ucyate, na tu sarvasmāt jyāyasaḥ brahmaṇaḥ iti cet; tatra yad uttaraṃ vaktavyam, tat pūrvasmin eva nirṇayatvāt eva nirṇītam \| ato dahara-ākāśaḥ ajāgratā-avidyā-ādi aśeṣa-doṣa-āspadaḥ svābhāvika-aniratiśaya-jñāna-bala-aiśvarya-vīrya-śakti-tejaḥ-prabhṛti-aparimita-udāra-guṇa-sāgaraḥ puruṣottama eva \| prajāpatī-vākya-nirdiṣṭuḥ anena svenaṃ vicchādyanti iti evam-ādi-nirapākarma-nimitta-deha-parigrahaḥ paścāt arciṣaḥ atirūpa-saṃpattau āvibhūta-upahata-pāpmatva-ādi-viguṇaka-svarūpaḥ iti na dahara-ākāśaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

itarat cet evam—

## \|\| 1.3.21 \|\|

*anukṛteḥ tasya ca*

**Комментарий:**

tathā—dahara-ākāśasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ; anukārāt apahata-pāpmatva-ādi-muktātmatva-ādi-viṣayakaḥ vimukta-saṃbandhaḥ pratyag-ātmā na dahara-ākāraḥ \| tad uktāraḥ—tatsāmānyam \| tathā hi pratyag-ātmanaḥ vimuktasya

parabrahmatukāra-śrutyaiśvarya padhyaḥ padhyate svayaṃvaraṃ kartāram īśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahma-yonim \| tathā vidyā-puṇya-pāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyam upaiti \|\| iti \| ataḥ ’yuktaḥ prajāpatitva-ākya-nirdeśaḥ \| anukāraṃ brahma dṛṣṭa-ākāraḥ \|\|21\|\|

## \|\| 1.3.22 \|\|

*api smaryate*

**Комментарий:**

saṃsāriṇaḥ api mukta-avasthāyāṃ parama-sāmya-āpatti-lakṣaṇaḥ parabrahmatukāraḥ smaryate “idaṃ jñānam upāśritya mama sādharmyam āgatāḥ \| sarge api na upajāyante pralaye na vyathanti ca \|\|” iti \|\| kaiścit “anukṛteḥ tasya api smaryate iti sūtra-dvayam adhikaraṇa-antaram “saiva bhāti anubhāti sarvaṃ tasya bhāsā sarvam idaṃ vibhāti iti asyāḥ śruteḥ parabrahma-paratva-niṣṭhāpya pravṛttaṃ varṇitam \| tat tu “adṛśyatva-ādi-guṇakaḥ dharma-uktiḥ “dyubhu-ādi-āyatanaṃ sva-śabdāt iti adhikaraṇa-dvayena tasya prakaraṇasya parabrahma-viṣayatva-pratipādanāt eva “jyotiḥ-caraṇa-abhidhānāt iti ādi-bhu parasya brahmaṇaḥ bhā-rūpatva-avagateḥ ca pūrva-pakṣa-anupasthānāt ayuktam; sūtra-akṣara (vaikalyaṃ) vailakṣyaṃ ca \|\|22\|\| iti dṛṣṭa-adhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|

**atha pramita-adhikaraṇam \|\|6\|\|**

## \|\| 1.3.23 \|\|

*śabdāt eva pramitaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

kaṭha-vallīṣu śrūyate “aṅguṣṭha-mātraḥ puruṣaḥ madhya ātmani tiṣṭhati \| īśānaḥ bhūta-bhavyasya na tataḥ vijugupsate \|\| etat vai tat” “aṅguṣṭha-mātraḥ puruṣaḥ jyotiḥ iva adhūmakaḥ \| īśānaḥ bhūta-bhavyasya saḥ eva adya saḥ u śvaḥ \| etat vai tat” “aṅguṣṭha-mātraḥ puruṣaḥ antarātmā sadā janānāṃ hṛdaye saṃni-viṣṭaḥ \| taṃ svāt śarīrāt pravṛhet muñjāt iva iṣikāṃ dhairyeṇa \| taṃ vidyāt śukram amṛtam iti \| tatra saṃdihyate—kim ayam aṅguṣṭha-mātra-pramitaḥ pratyagātmā, uta paramātmā iti \| kiṃ yuktam? pratyagātmā iti \| kutaḥ? jīvasya atyayāṅguṣṭha-mātrātva-śruteḥ “prāṇa-adhipaḥ saṃcarati sva-karmabhiḥ; aṅguṣṭha-mātraḥ ravi-tulya-rūpaḥ saṃkalpa-ahaṅkāra-samanvitaḥ yaḥ” iti \| tathā nyāya-upāsanārthatayā api paramātmanaḥ aṅguṣṭha-mātratvaṃ śrūyate \| evaṃ niścīte jīvatve īśānatvaṃ śarīra-indriya-prauḍha-yoga-karaṇa-apekṣayā api bhaviṣyati \| iti prāpte brūmaḥ—śabdāt eva pramitaḥ—aṅguṣṭha-pramitaḥ paramātmā; kutaḥ? “īśānaḥ bhūta-bhavyasya” iti śabdāt eva; na ca bhūta-bhavyasya sarveśvaratvaṃ karma-vaśyasya jīvasya upapadyate \|\|23\|\| kathaṃ tarhi paramātmanaḥ aṅguṣṭha-mātratvam iti atra āha—

## \|\| 1.3.24 \|\|

*hṛdi apekṣayā tu manuṣya-adhikāratvāt*

**Комментарий:**

paramātman upāsanārtham upāsaka-hṛdaye vartamānātvat upāsaka-hṛdaya-sthaḥ pramāṇatvattadapekṣyaitamatraḥ pramitattvam upapadyate \| jīvasya api aṇuḥ pramitattvaṃ hṛdi antarvartitvāt tadapekṣam eva; tathā api premāśrayatva-śruteḥ \| manuṣyāṇām eva upāsakatva-saṃbhāvanayā śāstrasya manuṣya-adhikāratvāt manuṣya-hṛdayasya ca tanutvaṃ pramitattva-aviruddhatvam ubhaya-aṅgīkṛtinām anubhaya-deśe’pi na kaścit doṣaḥ \| sthitaṃ tāvat uttarasya samāparyavasāne \|\| 24 \|\| iti pramita-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha etadarthe devatā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.3.25 \|\|

*tadupari api bādarāyaṇaḥ saṃbhavāt*

**Комментарий:**

parasya brahmaṇaḥ aṅguṣṭha-pramitatva-upapatteḥ manuṣya-adhikāraṃ brahma-upāsana-śāstram iti uktam \| tat sarvajñena etasmin brahmavidyāyāṃ deva-ādīnām api adhikāraḥ asti na vā iti vicāryate \| kiṃ tāvat yuktam ? na asti deva-ādīnām adhi̱kāra iti \| kutaḥ ? sāmarthya-abhāvāt \| na hi aśarīrāṇāṃ deva-ādīnāṃ viveka-vimoka-ādi-sādhana-satkāra-guṇa-upeta-brahma-upāsana-upasaṃhāra-sāmarthyam asti \| na ca deva-ādīnāṃ śarīratve pramāṇam upalabhyate \| yadyapi pariniṣpanne’pi vastuni vyutpatti-saṃbhavanayā vedānta-vākyāni pare brahmaṇi pramāṇa-bhāvam upagacchanti; tathā api deva-ādīnāṃ vigrahattva-vyatirikta-ahaṃ-padaṃ na kiñcit api vācyaṃ upalabhyate \| mantra-arthavādāḥ tu karma-vidhi-śeṣatayā anyaparatvāt devatā-ādi-vigraha-sādhane prabhavanti \| karma-vidheyaḥ ca sva-apekṣita-deśa-prakāra-kartṛtva-ādiḥ eva devatā-gataṃ kim api na sādhayati \| ataḥ eva tadanusmārttika-karma api na saṃbhavati \| ataḥ sāmarthyāt ca eva upāya-abhāvāt deva-ādīnām anadhikāra iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—tadupari api bādarāyaṇaḥ saṃbhavāt \| tadupari—tatva—brahma-upāsanam, upari—deva-ādiṣu api, saṃbhavati iti bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ manyate, teṣām arthitva-sāmarthyayoḥ saṃbhavāt \| arthitvaṃ tāvat adhyātmika-ādi-durvighahad-duḥkha-abhisaṃtāpa-aparimita-bṛhan-niratatiśaya-nikhila-doṣa-gaṇḍanavadhika-atiśaya-asādhya-eka-kalyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇe niratatiśaya-bhogyatva-ādi-vijñānena saṃbhavati \| sāmarthyam api paṭutara-deha-indriya-ādimattayā saṃbhavati \| deha-indriya-ādimattvaṃ ca brahma-ādīnāṃ sakala-upaniṣatsu sṛṣṭi-prakaraṇeṣu prāyaḥ tatra prakaṭaḥ bhūyate \| tathā hi saḥ eva somya idam agre āsīt...tadā aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tat tejaḥ asṛjata iti ārabhya sarva-sac-etanaṃ tejovabrahmapurussarīraviśeṣabahulīkṛtya anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi iti sūkṣmatya brahma-ādi sthāvara-antaṃ caturvidhaṃ bhūtajātaṃ tejaḥ-karma-ādi-daśā-śarīraṃ taduccita-nāma-saṃcaya-prakaroti iti uktam \| evaṃ sarvaṃ samīhitam etadṣu deva-tiryaṅ-

unmukha-śāvara-tamatā catur-vidhā buddhiḥ ucyate \| deva-ādi-bhedaḥ ca tat-karma-anuguṇa-brahma-loka-paryanti-caturdaśa-loka-stha-phala-bhoga-ayogya-deha-indriya-ādi-yoga-āpattiḥ; ātmanaṃ stutaḥ deva-kṛtvā abhāvāt \| tathā tad-ubhaye devā-asurā ātmabuddhiḥ te ha ūvuḥ ... indraḥ ha eva devānām abhipravavrāja virocanaḥ asurāṇām tau ha asaṃvidānau eva samit-pāṇī prajāpati-kāśam ājagmatuḥ tau ha dvā-triṃśataṃ varṣāṇi brahmacaryam ūṣatuḥ tau ha prajāpatiḥ uvāca yasya ahimā spaṣṭā eva śarīra-indriyatvaṃ deva-ādīnām pratīyate \|\|

karma-viśeṣa-bhūta-brahma-artha-vādaiḥ eva api vajra-hastaḥ purandaraḥ tena indraḥ vajram udyacchat ity-ādibhiḥ pratīyamānāṃ vigraha-ādi-mattvaṃ pramāṇa-antara-aviruddhaṃ tathā eva upameyam \| na ca anupatteḥ artha-avakāśatva-nuti-paratvāśaṅkāṃ pratīyamāna-artha-antara-avīkṣā śakyate vaktum; stutyā hi upayogitvāt tena vidhā-stuti-avadhūpaśleṣaḥ \| guṇa-kathanena hi stutiḥ ittham \| guṇānām asattve stutiḥ eva na hi bhavet \| na ca asatā guṇena kathitena prarocanā jāyate \| ataḥ karma prarocayantaḥ guṇāḥ tadṛśaṃ bodhayanti eva artha-vādāḥ \| mantrāḥ ca karmasu viniyuktāḥ tatra eva kiñcit-kartavyatā-anugraha-arthaṃ prakāśayantaḥ devatā-ādi-gata-vigraha-ādi-guṇa-viśeṣam abhidadhata eva tatra liṅgaiḥ bṛṃhitā, api āhendriya-samutra-yupavataḥ \| na ca nirviśeṣā devatā dhiyam adhirohati \| tatra, pramāṇa-antara-apratipanna-guṇāḥ svayam eva bodhayitvā taiḥ karma prarocayanti; guṇa-viśiṣṭā vā prakāśayanti; prāk īśvāt ca hi etaiḥ prarocana-prakāśane kurvanti; viruddhe tu tad-abhaviṣyat-śabde aviruddha-anuguṇā lakṣaṇā kurvanti \|\|

karma-viśeṣaḥ ca devatāyāḥ aiśvaryam apekṣata eva \| kāmitaḥ kartavyatayā karma vidhīyamānaṃ svayaṃ kṣaṇa-pradhvaṃsi kālāntara-sambhavināḥ phalasya svābhāvita-sādhakam apekṣate \| mantra-artha-vādyoḥ ca vāyuḥ kṣepiṣṭhā devatā vāyum eva svena bhāga-dheyena upadhāvati saḥ eva enam bhūtiṃ gamayati yat ena haviṣā āśāste tat skuryāt tad-devatyā anantaraṃ devaṃ rādhnoti ity-ādiṣu devatāyāḥ karma-aṇḍa-ārādhitāyāḥ phala-arpitvaṃ tad-guṇāḥ ca aiśvaryaṃ pratīyamānam apekṣitvena vākyārthe samanvīyate \| deva-pūjā-abhidhāyinaḥ yaji-dhātoḥ ca yāga-ākhyaṃ karma sva-ārādhya-devatā-ārādhanaṃ pratīyate \|\|

tad evaṃ kṛtsna-vākya-paryālocanayā vākyyāt eva viṣaye viśiṣṭaṃ sarveśvaratām iti na pūrva-adhikaraṇa-niyutpatti-samapāna-avatāṃ karma-vidhi-vibhedyatvā kartavyatvā vā apratītavyam \| tathā ṣaḍ-guṇya-bāhulya-mantra-arthavat-sūtreṣu dharma-śāstra-itihāsa-purāṇeṣu brahma-ādīnām deva-asura-manuṣya-ādīnām ca deha-indriya-ādi-asta-svabhāva-bhedāḥ sthānāni bhogāḥ kṛtyāni ca ekatvam-ādya saṃyuktāḥ pratipādyante \| ataḥ vigraha-ādi-matvādi vānām adhyakṣikaro asti eva \|\| 25 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.26 \|\|

*virodhaḥ karmaṇi iti cet na aneka-upapatteḥ darśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

deva-ādīnām vigraha-ādi-mattvādi-upagame karmaṇi virodhaḥ prasañcyate, bahuṣu yāgeṣu yugapad ekasya eva devasya vigrahavat ca agnimanta āvaha indra āgaccha hariva āgaccha iti-ādinā āhūtasya tasya

sa-vidhānupapatteḥ \| darśayati ca agni-ādīnāṃ tat-tad-abhāvanam kasya vā ha devā yajamānasya icchanti kasya vā na bahūnāṃ yajamānānāṃ yo vai devatāḥ pūrvaḥ parigṛhṇāti sa enān aśvabhūte yajate iti \| ato vigraha-ādimattve karmaṇi virodhaḥ prasajyata iti cet, tatra; aneka-pratipatti-deśanād—dṛśyate hi saubhari-prabhṛtīnāṃ śaktimatāṃ yugapad aneka-śarīra-pratipattiḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.27 \|\|

*śabda iti cet na ataḥ prabhavāt pratyakṣa-anumānābhyām*

**Комментарий:**

virodha iti vartate \| mā bhūt karmaṇi virodhaḥ aneka-kārīra-pratipatteḥ \| śabde tu vaidike virodhaḥ prasajyate, anitya-artha-saṃyogāt \| vigrahavattve hi sāvayavatvena deva-ādeḥ arthasya anityatvam anivāryam \| tato devatādi-sabdavat indrādi-śabdasya janmanaḥ prāk vināśāt ūrdhvaṃ ca indrādi-kāla-bhāvī vaidikānām artha-samanvayasya vā vedasya smṛta-anityatvaṃ vā bhavet iti abhisaṃdhiḥ; tatra, ataḥ prabhāvāt—asmāt indrādi-śabdāt eva punaḥ punaḥ indrādi-arthasya prabhavāt \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—ya hi devatādi-śabdavat indrādi-śabdā vaidikā vyakti-viseṣa-mātre saṅketa-pūrvakāḥ pravṛttāḥ; api tu svabhāvata eva gavādi-śabdavat ākṛti-viśeṣa-vācitvena \| tatra ca kalpāminndra-vyakto vinasyatyām eva vaidikā indra-śabda-smṛtiḥ viparivartamānāt tad-avāgata-tad-āśrita-indrādi-arthakāro dhātā tad-ākāram eva aparām indraṃ sṛjati; yathā kulālo ghaṭa-śabda-smṛtiḥ viparivartamānam tad-āśraya-kāram eva ghaṭam iti \| katham idam avagamyate? pratyakṣa-anumānābhyām—śrutiḥ sṛṣṭityānityārthaḥ \| śrutiḥ tāvad vedena rūpe vyakarot sā asatī prajāpatiḥ iti; tathā sa bhūḥ iti vyāharat sa bhūmim asṛjata sa bhuvaḥ iti vyāharat saḥ antarikṣam asṛjatetyādi \| vācaka-śabda-pūrvakaṃ tat-tad-artha-saṃsthānaṃ smarataḥ tat-tat-saṃsthāna-viśiṣṭaṃ tat-tad-arthaṃ sṛṣṭavān iti arthaḥ \| smṛtiḥ api anādi-nidhanā hi eṣā vāk utsṛṣṭā svayaṃbhuvā \| ādau vedamayī divyā yataḥ sarvāḥ pravṛttayaḥ \|\| iti; sarveṣāṃ tu sa nāmāni karmāṇi ca pṛthak pṛthak \| veda-śabdebhyaḥ eva ādau pṛthak saṃsthāḥ ca nirmame \|\| iti \| saṃsthāḥ—saṃsthānāni, rūpāṇi iti yāvat \| yathā nām rūpaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ kṛtyānāṃ ca prapañcanam \| veda-śabdebhyaḥ eva ādau devādīnāṃ cakāra saḥ \|\| iti \| ato devādīnāṃ vigrahavattve api vaidika-śabdānām ānarthyaṃ, vedasya anityatvaṃ ca na prasajyate \|\| 27 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.28 \|\|

*ataḥ eva ca nityatvam*

**Комментарий:**

yataḥ eva indra-vasiṣṭhādi-śabdānāṃ deva-viśeṣiṇāṃ tat-tad-ākāra-vācitvaṃ, tatra icchadena tat-tad-artha-smṛti-pūrvikā ca tat-tad-buddhiḥ; tataḥ eva mantra-kṛto vṛṇīte namo ṛṣibhyaḥ mantra-kṛdbhyaḥ ayaṃ saḥ agniḥ iti viśvāmitrasya sūktaṃ bhavati ityādi-vinirviṣṭṛṇāṃ mantra-kṛddakāṇḍa-kṛtva-ṛṣitvādau pratīyamāne api vedasya nityatvam upapadyate \| ṛṣiḥ eva mantra-kṛto vṛṇīte ityādi-vinirdeśa-anantaraṃ tat-tat-kāṇḍa-sūkta-mantrakatva-pūrṇāśakti-tātkaraṇa-adhikaṃ parama-puruṣaṃ tat-tad-ākārāraṃ tatra icchā-liṅgitāṃś ca sṛṣṭvā prajāpatiḥ tataḥ eva tatamantra-adhikaraṇe niyuṅkte \| te api prajāpatinā

āhita-aśakyaḥ tat-svarūpaṃ tapasaḥ tvayā nitya-siddhatva-pūrva-pūrva-adhisiddhānu tān eva mantra-akṣīna-ghorā iva svarataḥ varṇa-āśrāvasthita-ātparyanti \| ataḥ ca vedānāṃ nityatvam eva atra mantrakṛtvam upapadyate \|\| 28 \|\|

atha syāt—naimittika-pralayādici vajagadudbhavo vedādi-devaḥ pūrva-pūrva-smṛti-mātreṇa prajāpati nā devādi-vyakti-rūpa-padārthān nāmaḥ prākṛta-pralaye tu śabdaḥ prajāpati-bhūta-gahī-rūpa-pariṇāma-śabdasya vinaṣṭatvāt akaṃ prajāpatiḥ eva śabda-pūrvikā dṛṣṭiḥ upapadyate ? kasya tvaṃ vinaṣṭasya vedasya nityatvam ? ataḥ veda-nityatva-vādinā devādīnāṃ viṣṇu-vacana-anugamāt api loka-vyavahārasya prabāha-gṛhīta-āśrayaṇīyā iti \| atra uttaraṃ paṭhati—

## \|\| 1.3.29 \|\|

*samāna-nāma-rūpatvāt ca āvṛttau api avirodhaḥ darśanāt smṛteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

vṛttānte paṃhāre jagad-utpattāvṛttau api pūrva-ākāra-samāna-nāma-rūpatvāt eva na kaścit virodhaḥ \| tathā hi—sa bhagavān puruṣottamaḥ pratyavasāna-samaye pūrva-saṃsthānaṃ jagat samastaṃ brahma sthāyīti saṃcintya, bhedya-bhoktṛ-jātaṃ svārṇasūta śakti-mātra-avaśeṣaṃ pralīnaṃ vibhajya, mahadādi-vrahmāṇḍaṃ hiraṇya-garbha-paryantaṃ yathā-pūrvaṃ sṛṣṭvā, vedān ca pūrva-pūrvī-viśeṣa-sthitān āviṣkṛtya, hiraṇyagarbhāya upadiśya, pūrva-vat eva devādhikāra-jāta-saṃjñaṃ taṃ niyujya, svayam api tad-antarātmantayā avasthye \| ataḥ yathoktaṃ sarvam upapannam \| evaṃveda ca vedasya api īśvaratvaṃ nityatvaṃ ca, yad pūrva-pūrvīccāraṇa-krama-janita-saṃskāreṇa tad eva krama-viśiṣṭaṃ smṛtvā tena eva krameṇa uccāryam \| tadrsamākhu sarveśvare api samānam \| iti astu viśeṣa—saṃskārānapeḍam eva svayam eva anusandhatte puruṣottamaḥ \|\|

kutaḥ idaṃ yathoktam avāmyata iti cetaḥ; tathā hi—darśanāt smṛteḥ ca \| darśanaṃ tāvat śrūyo brahmāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrvaṃ yo vai vedān ca prahiṇoti tasmai iti \| smṛtiḥ api mānavī āsīt idṃ tamobhūtam iti ārabhya saḥ abhidhyāya śarīrāt svāt sisṛkṣuḥ vividhāḥ prajāḥ \| apaḥ eva sasarja ādau tāsu vīryam apāsṛjat \|\| tad aṇḍam abhavad dhemaṃ sahasra-aṃśu-sama-prabham \| tasmin jajñe svayaṃ brahmā sarva-loka-pitāmahaḥ \|\| iti \| tathā paurāṇikī asya mukhya devasya nābhaḥ padmam ajāyata \| tasmin praje mahābhāgā veda-vedāṅga-pāragaḥ \|\| brahma utpattasya tena uktaḥ prajāḥ sṛja mahāmate \|\| tathā paraḥ nārāyaṇaḥ devaḥ tasmāt jātaḥ caturmukhaḥ iti \| tathā ahi dam agraṃ vedaṃ, iti ārabhya ucyate—sṛṣṭvā nāraṃ toyam anantaḥ tisyātode yena sthāne nāma nārāyaṇa iti \| kalpe kalpe tvayā brāhmi bhava-sṛṣṭasya me nābhiṃjā sthāyathaṇḍajasya \|\| evaṃ-bhūtasya me devi nābhi-padme caturmukhaḥ \| utpannasa mayā ca uktaḥ prajāḥ sṛja mahāmate \|\| iti \| ataḥ devādi-nām paricchinna-sāmarthya-yogāt brahma-vidyāyām adhikāraḥ asti iti siddham \|\| 26 \|\| iti devatādhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|

**atha pratimā-adhikaraṇam adhya-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.3.30 \|\|

*madhv-ādiṣu asaṃbhavāt anadhikāraṃ jaiminiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

brahma-vidyāyāṃ deva-ādīnām api adhikāraḥ asti iti uktam \| iha cintyate—yeṣām upāsyeṣu yā devatā eva upāsyāḥ teṣu tāsām adhikāraḥ asti na iti \| kiṃ prāptam ? na asti adhikāraḥ teṣu madhv-ādiṣu iti jaiminiḥ manyate \| kutaḥ ? asaṃbhavāt—na hi āditya-vasv-ādīni upāsyā āditya-vasv-ādayaḥ anye saṃbhavanti \| na ca vasv-ādīnāṃ satāṃ vasv-āditvaṃ prāpyaṃ bhavati, prāptatvāt \| madhu-vidyām uddiśya etat viditvā avakīrṇiṣṭapācyaḥ raśmīkṣoṇa prāptasya satyāśrathasya labdha-madhu-upadeśasya ādityasya raśmīnāṃ vasv-ādibhiḥ bhuñjyamānānām upāsyatvaṃ vasv-āditvaṃ ca prāpyaṃ śrūyate asau vā ādityaḥ deva-madhu iti upakramya tat yat prathamam amṛtaṃ tat vasavaḥ upajīvanti iti uktatā sa yaḥ etat evam ṛtaṃ veda vasūnām eva ekaḥ bhūtvā agninā eva mukhena etat evam ṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā tṛpyati iti iti-ādinā \|\| 30 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.31 \|\|

*jyotiṣi bhāvāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

te devāḥ jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ iti upakramya etat bhūrtinita jyotiḥ iti–parasmin brahmaṇi upāsanaṃ devānāṃ śrūyate \| deva-manuṣya-ubhaya-sādhāraṇe para-brahma-upāsane devānām ubhaya-phalakatvena devānām iśvarorośāsananiyuktaṃ hotṛtviti \| ataḥ eva vasv-ādīnām anadhikāraḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

iti prāpte abhidhīyate—

## \|\| 1.3.32 \|\|

*bhāvaṃ tu bādarāyaṇaḥ asti hi*

**Комментарий:**

āditya-vasv-ādīnām api teṣu adhikāra-bhāvaṃ bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ manyate \| asti hi āditya-vasv-ādīnām api sva-avasthya-brahma-upāsanena vasv-āditva-prāpti-pūrvaka-brahma-upasaṃbhavaḥ \| idānīṃ vasv-ādīnām api satāṃ kalpa-antare api vasv-āditva-pratikṣā-yogitā bhavati \| atha hi kārya-kāraṇa-ubhaya-avastha-brahma-upāsanaṃ vidhīyate asau vā ādityaḥ deva-madhu iti ārabhya atha tataḥ ūrdhvaṃ udetya iti ataḥ , prāk āditya-vasv-ādi-kārya-viśeṣa-avasthaṃ brahma upāsyam upadiśyate; atha tataḥ ūrdhvaṃ udetya iti-ādinā āditya-antarātmatayā avasthitaṃ kāraṇa-avastham eva brahma upāsyam upadiśyate \| tat evam kārya-kāraṇa-ubhaya-avasthaṃ brahma upāsītaḥ kalpa-antare vasv-āditvaṃ prāpya tat-kṛtaṃ kāraṇaṃ paraṃ brahma eva āpnīti \| na ha vai asmā udeti na nimlocati sakṛt divā ha eva asmai bhavati yaḥ etām eva brahma-upaniṣadaṃ veda iti kṛtsnāyā madhu-vidyāyā, brahma-upaniṣattva-śravaṇāt brahma-prāpti-paryanta-vasv-āditya-phalasya śravaṇāt ca vasv-ādi-bhogya-bhūta-āditya-aṃśasya vidheyamānām upāsanaṃ tat-avasthasya eva brahmaṇaḥ iti avagamyate \| ataḥ evaṃvidhi-upāsanam āditya-vasv-ādīnām api saṃbhavati \| evam ca brahmaṇaḥ eva upāsyatvāt te devā

jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ iti upakramyate \| tathā hi vṛtti-kāraḥ asti hi madhvādiṣu saṃbhavaḥ brahma ca sarvatra niścāyyatvāt iti \|\| 32 \|\| iti madhvādhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|

**atha apasūtrādhikaraṇam \|\| 9 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.3.33 \|\|

*śubhasya tad-anādara-śravaṇāt tad-ādravaṇāt sūcyate hi*

**Комментарий:**

brahma-vidyāyāṃ śūdrasya api adhikāraḥ asti na vā iti vicāryate; kiṃ yuktam ? asti iti kutaḥ ? arthitva-sāmarthyayoḥ śūdratva-avirodhasya, śūdrasya api tat-saṃbhavāt \| yady api agni-vidyā-sādhye karmasu agni-vidyāvat śūdrasya anadhikāraḥ; tathā api mano-vṛtti-sādhyatvāt brahmopāsanasya tatra adhikāraḥ asti eva \| jñātṛtva-kriyā-apekṣatve api upāsanasya tat-karma-āśrayaḥ cit-kriyā eva apekṣitatvāt śūdrasya api svarga-phalita-pūrva-varṇa-āśrameṣu ye kriyā bhaviṣyati \| śrūyate mānaśūdraḥ yajñotsavamūlaḥ ityādi api vidyā-sādhyasya arthi-kartṛ-nirdeśa-kāra eva nyāya-siddhaḥ asti eva \| nanu adhīta-vedasya aśruta-vedāntasya brahma-svarūpa-padārtha-vivaraṇa-jñānasya kathaṃ brahmopāsanaṃ saṃbhavati ? ucyate—anadhīta-vedasya aśruta-vedānta-vākyasya api itihāsa-purāṇa-āśravaṇena api brahma-svarūpa-tad-upāsana-jñānaṃ saṃbhavati \| asti ca śūdrasya api itihāsa-purāṇa-śravaṇa-jñānāt śrāvayet caturo varṇān kṛtvā brāhmaṇam agrataḥ ityādi \| hṛdayaṃ ca etad āsmṛti-grāhyaṃ vidura-ādyau brahma-niṣṭhāḥ \| tathā upaniṣatsu api saṃvarga-vidyāyāṃ śūdrasya api brahma-vidyā-adhikāraḥ pratīyate—śuśrūṣāṃ hi jānaśrutim ācaryaṃ raikvam udgamya amantraya tasmai brahma-vidyām upadideśa iti—ājahāra imāḥ śūdrān aiva mukhena ālāpayiṣyathāḥ ityādinā \| ataḥ śūdrasya api adhikāraḥ saṃbhavati \|\|

iti prāpte ucyate—na śūdrasya adhikāraḥ saṃbhavati, sāmarthya-abhāvāt \| na hi brahma-svarūpa-tad-upāsana-prakāram ajānataḥ tad-uddhṛtya vedānta-vacana-dhārikhā-anadhikṛtasya upāsana-upasaṃhāra-sāmarthya-saṃbhavaḥ \| asāmarthyaṃ ca adhīta-vedasya adhikāraḥ na saṃbhavati \| asāmarthyaṃ ca veda-adhyayana-abhāve \| yathā eva hi traivarṇika-viṣaya-adhyayana-vidhi-siddha-svādhyāya-saṃpāda-jñāna-lābhena karma-vidhi-ajñāta-tad-upāya-ādi-niṣṭhāḥ svīkṛtyante; tathā brahmopāsana-vidhayaḥ api \| ataḥ adhyayana-vidhi-siddha-svādhyāya-adhigata-tattvasya eva brahmopāsana-upāyatvāt śūdrasya brahmopāsana-saṃsāmarthya-asaṃbhavaḥ \| itihāsa-purāṇe api veda-upabṛṃhaṇaṃ kurvati eva upāsana-bhāvam anubhavataḥ; na svātantryeṇa \| śāstratve api itihāsa-purāṇa-śravaṇa-ajñānaṃ, bhāṣya-arthi-hita-phalārthaṃ; na upāsana-artham \| vidura-ādayaḥ tu bhagavattarā-adhigata-jñānāḥ pramāṇa-bhūtāḥ jñānavantaḥ śāstra-prārabdha-karma-vaśāt ca iha ajjanma-yoginaḥ iti teṣāṃ brahma-niṣṭhatvam \|\|

nanu saṃvarga-vidyāyāṃ śūdra-āśrutiḥ saṃbodhane śūdrasya adhikāraṃ sūcayati iti; tatra api āha—śubhasya tad-anādara-śravaṇāt tad-ādravaṇāt sūcyate hi \| śuk-bhūyaḥ jānaśruteḥ pautrāyaṇasya brahma-jñāna-āvedaka-alpene

haṃsādi-jana-darśanāt śravaṇāt deva brahmavidau raikvasya sakāśaṃ pratyāśravaṇāt upanayasya sadṛśā iti hi sūcyate; ataḥ śūdra iti āmantryate, na caturtha-varṇena \| śocati hi śudraḥ; śuceḥ daś ca iti śucy-arthe dhātoḥ ṣṭran ca akārasya ca dakāre śudra iti bhavati \| ataḥ śocitṛtvena asya śūdra-śabda-prayogeṇa sūcyate; na jāti-yogaḥ \| jānaśrutiḥ kila pautrāyaṇo bahu-pradeyo bahv-anna-prasūḥ ca babhūva \| tasya dhārmikatvasya dharmeṇa prītasyaḥ kayāci brahma-hāsamānor asya brahma-jijñāsām udīpayituḥ haṃsa-rūpeṇa niśāyām asya avidūre gacchato ’sya itara ittham uvāca—aho bhāyi bhallākṣa bhallākṣa jānaśruteḥ pautrāyaṇasya samaṃ divā jyotiḥ ātatam tat mā prasaṅkṣīḥ tat tvā mā pradhākṣīt iti \| evaṃ jānaśruti-praśaṃsā-rūpaṃ vākyam upaśrutya paro haṃsaḥ pratyuvāca—kam vara enam etat santaṃ sayugvānam iva raikvam ātthe iti \| kaṃ santaṃm jānaśrutiṃ sayugvānaṃ raikvaṃ brahma-jñam iva guṇasya bhettāraṃ; sa brahma-jño raikva eva loke guṇavattaraḥ \| mahatā dharmeṇa saṃyuktasya api asya jānaśruteḥ brahmārhasya ko guṇaḥ, yat guṇa-gaṇitaṃ tejo raikva-teja iva māṃ dahet ity arthaḥ \| evam uktena pareṇa ko ’sau raikva iti pṛṣṭhaḥ loke yat kiñcit sādhu-kṛtyaṃ karma, yat ca sarva-veda-anugataṃ vijñānam, tad ubhayam adeśa-jñāna-karma-vidbhyaḥ, sa raikva iti āha \|

tat etat dṛṣṭānta-vākyaṃ brahma-jñāna-vidhurasya prātaḥ nidrā-bhaṅge tadvattayā ca raikva-praśaṃsā-rūpaṃ jānaśrutiḥ upaśrutya tadakṣaṇāt eva kṣattāraṃ raikva-anveṣaṇāya preṣya tasmin nivṛtte āgate svayam api raikva-samīpam gatvā ṣaṭśataṃ niṣkam aśvatarī-rathaṃ ca raikvāya āhṛtya raikvaṃ prārthayām āsa anu etāṃ bhagavo devatāṃ śādhi yāṃ devatām upāsse iti \| tvag-upāyāṃ parāṃ devatāṃ mama anugrahāya arthaḥ \| sa ca raikvaḥ tu ayoga-mahima-vividita-loka-trayo jānaśruteḥ kṣatrāṇi vidhuratā-nimitta-anādara-vyāmiśra-vākya-śravaṇena śoka-āviṣṭatāṃ tadanantaram eva brahma-jijñāsā-yogaṃ ca viditvā asya brahma-vidyā-yogyatām abhiprāya cira-kāla-labdhaṃ vinā dravya-pradānena śuśrūṣamāṇasya asya yācñā-ucita-pravadane brahma-vidyā pratiṣṭhitā bhavati iti matvā tam upaguhya tasya śoka-āvidhatām upadeśa-yogyatā-abhiprāyakaṃ śūdratā-udīnāmantraṇena jñāpayan idam āha—aho hāre tvā śūdra tava eva saha gobhiḥ astu iti \| saha gobhiḥ ayaṃ rathaḥ tava eva astu; na etāvatā mahyaṃ dattena brahma-jijñāsāyā śoka-āviṣṭasya tava brahma-vidyā pratiṣṭhitā bhavati ity arthaḥ \| sa ca jānaśrutiḥ tūsṇīṃ yogipi sva-āśayasya anuguṇam eva gārdabhikaṃ dhanaṃ kanyāṃ ca pradāya upasasāda \| sa raikvaḥ punar api tasya yogyatām eva sthāpayan śūdra-śabdena āmantrya āha—ājahāra imāḥ śūdra anena eva mukhena ālāpayiṣyathāḥ iti \| imāni dhanāni śāstrasya anuguṇāni ājahṛḥ; anena eva dvāreṇa cira-sevyayā vinā api māṃ tvad-vijñaptitaṃ brahmopadeśa-rūpa-vākyam ātalāpayiṣyathā ity uktvā tasmai upadideśa \| ataḥ śūdra-śabdena vidyā-upadeśa-yogyatā-sthāpanārthaṃ śoka eva asya sūcitaḥ; na caturthe varṇatvam \|\|33\|\|

## \|\| 1.3.34 \|\|

*kṣatriyatva-gatiḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

bahu pācī iti vākya-nirdeśena bahu-bhāṣyaḥ iti acintā sarveṣām eva ātma-dṛśyāditvena bahutva-upapattiḥ abādhita-pratīteḥ \| sa ha śiśuḥ eva kṣattāram uvāca iti kṣattṛ-preṣaṇāt bahu-grāma-pradāna-varāta-janapada-adhipatyādibhyaḥ ca jānaśruteḥ kṣatriyatva-pratītiḥ ca caturthe varṇake \|\| 34 \|\| tat evam upakramāta ādhyadhikāryāṃ kṣatriyatva-pratītiḥ uktā; upasaṃhāra-gata-ādhyadhikāryām api kṣatriyatvasya pratīyate iti āha—

## \|\| 1.3.35 \|\|

*uttaratra caitrarathyena liṅgāt*

**Комментарий:**

asya jānaśruteḥ para-vidyā-upāyanāya samyak eva saṃvarga-vidyām anantarasya kṣatriya-mānena abhipratāriṇāmnā caitrarathyena kṣatriyeṇa asya kṣatriyatvaṃ gamyate \| katham ? ayam u śaunakaṃ ca kāpeyam abhipratāriṇaṃ ca kāpisenim pariviṣyamāṇau brahmacārī bibhikṣe iti ādinā brahmacāriṇo dame upāsamāhe iti etena kāpeyam abhipratāriṇaḥ abhipratārasya brahmacāriṇaḥ ca saṃvarga-vidyā-saṃbandhitvaṃ pratīyate \| teṣu ca abhipratārī kṣatriyaḥ, itarau brāhmaṇau \| ataḥ asyāṃ vidyāyāṃ brāhmaṇasya tad-itarasya ca kṣatriyasya anvayaḥ dṛśyate; na śūdrasya \| ataḥ asyāṃ vidyām anvitaḥ caitrarathya-brāhmaṇa-antarasya jānaśruteḥ api kṣatriyatvam eva yuktam; na caturtha-varṇatvam \|\| nanu viruddha-karṇaḥ abhipratāriṇaḥ caitrarathatvaṃ kṣatriyatvaṃ ca na śrutam; tat katham asya abhipratāriṇaḥ caitrarathyatvam ? kathaṃ vā kṣatriyatvam ? tatra āha—liṅgāt iti \| ayaṃ hi śaunakaṃ ca kāpeyam abhipratāriṇaṃ ca kāpisenim iti abhipratāriṇaḥ kāpeya-pāṭhāt pariliṅgita-tadvaya-abhipratāriṇaḥ kāpeya-saṃbandhaḥ pratīyate \| anyatra ca śrutena vai caitrarathaṃ kāpeyāḥ ayājayan iti kāpeya-saṃbandhina-caitrarathatvaṃ śrūyate; tathā caitrarathasya kṣatriyatvaṃ tasmāt caitrarathaḥ nāma ekaḥ kṣatrapatiḥ jāyate iti \| ataḥ abhipratāriṇaḥ caitrarathatvaṃ kṣatriyatvaṃ ca gamyate \|\| 35 \|\| tat evaṃ nyāya-virodhini śūdrasya adhikāre liṅgaṃ na upapadyate iti uktam \| idānīṃ nyāya-siddha-śūdrasya anadhikāra-śruti-smṛtiḥ nirguṇatvāt iti āha—

## \|\| 1.3.36 \|\|

*saṃskāra-parāmarśāt tad-abhāva-abhilāpāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

brahma-vidyā-upadeśa-vaideśyena yata-saṃskāraḥ parāmṛśyate—rūpa tvā nediṣṭhaṃ ha upaninye iti ādi tu \| śūdrasya ca upanayanādi-saṃskāra-abhāvaḥ abhilapyate—na śūdre pātakaṃ kiṃcit na saṃskāram arhati caturthaḥ varṇaḥ eka-jātīnām ca saṃskāra-sahitaḥ bhavet iti \|\| 36 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.37 \|\|

*tad-abhāva-nirdhāraṇe ca pravṛtteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

satya-vacanaṃ brahmaṇaḥ vivakṣamāṇam iti samidhaṃ somya āhara iti śūṣroca nivāsasya śūdratva-abhāva-nirdhāraṇe sati eva vidyā-upadeśa-pravṛtteḥ ca na śūdrasya adhikāraḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.38 \|\|

*śravaṇa-adhyayana-artha-pratiṣedhāt*

**Комментарий:**

śūdrasya veda-āśravaṇa-adhyayana-tad-artha-anuṣṭhānāni pratiṣidhyante pad u ha vā etat śmaśānaṃ yat śūdraḥ tasmāt śūdra-samīpe na adhyetavyam tasmāt śūdraḥ bahu-paśuḥ ayajñiyaḥ iti \| bahu-paśuḥ paśu-sadṛśaḥ iti arthaḥ \| anuśravaṇe’dhyayana-tad-artha-jñāna-tad-anuṣṭhānāni na saṃbhavanti; atas tāni api pratiṣidhyante \|\| 38 \|\|

## \|\| 1.3.39 \|\|

*smṛteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

smarante ca śravaṇa-ādi-niṣedhaḥ: atha ha asya vedam upaśṛṇvataḥ tapujatu śrotra-pratipūraṇam, udāharaṇe jihvā-cchedaḥ dhāraṇe śarīra-bhedaḥ iti, na ca asya upadiśet dharmaṃ na vā asya vratam ādiśet iti ca \| ataḥ śūdrasya anadhikāraḥ iti siddham \|\|

ye tu nirviśeṣa-cin-mātraṃ brahma eva paramārthaḥ; aparatvaṃ mithyā-bhūtam, bandhaḥ ca pāramārthikaḥ, sa ca vākya-janya-etat-suyāthātmya-jñāna-mātra-nivartyaḥ, tat-nivṛttiḥ eva mokṣaḥ iti vadanti; taiḥ brahma-jñāne śūdra-ādeḥ adhikāraḥ vaktuṃ na śakyate; anupnītasya anadhīta-vedasya aśruta-vedānta-vākyasya api yathāmārkamārti nirviśeṣa-cin-mātraṃ brahma eva paramārthaḥ anyat sarvaṃ tasmat-nivartakatayā mithyā-bhūtam iti vākyāt etat-suyāthātmya-jñāna-utpatteḥ, tāvat eva bandha-nivṛtteḥ ca \| na ca tadvamasyādi-vākyena eva jñāna-utpattiḥ kāryā, na vākya-antareṇa iti niyataṃ śakyam; jñānasya puruṣa-tantratvāt, satyāṃ sāmagrayām anicchataḥ api jñāna-utpatteḥ \| na ca veda-vākyaiva vastu-yāthātmya-jñāne sati bandha-nivṛttiḥ bhavati iti śakyaṃ vaktum, yena kena api vastu-yāthātmya-jñāne sati bhrānti-nivṛtteḥ; pauruṣeyāt api nirviśeṣa-cin-mātraṃ brahma paramārthaḥ anyat sarvaṃ mithyā-bhūtam iti vākyāt jñāna-utpatteḥ tāvattveva bhrama-nivṛtteḥ ca \| yathā pauruṣeyāt vākya-ātmaka-śuktikā-rajata-viśvāsa-nivṛttiḥ ghaṭasya śūdra-ādeḥ api nivartate, tathā eva idaṃ śūdrasya api veda-viruddha-saṃvāda-āgata-tattvamasyādi-vākya-artha-suyāthātmya-jñānena jagat-bhrama-nivṛttiḥ api bhaviṣyati \| na ca asya upadiśet dharmam ityādinā veda-viruddha-śruti-smṛtibhyaḥ na vadasi iti ca na śakyaṃ vaktum, tanmatasthādi-vākya-gata-brahma-asambhāvanāṃ veda-śiraṣi vartamānatayā dṛḍha-akhila-adhikāratvena niṣedha-śaṅkā-kiñcid-rūpa-abhāvāt; atikrānta-niṣedhe vā kenacid uktāt vākyāt śūdra-ādeḥ jñānam utpadyate eva \|\|

na ca vācyaṃ—śuktikāraḥ rajata-ādi-sambandhi-yukta-virodhi-vākya-janya-tacchāsana-sambandhaṃ śūnyaṃ jagat-bhramaḥ na nivartate iti; tanmatasthādi-vākya-āśravaṇa-samanantaraṃ bāhya-pratyayāpi jagat-bhrama-nivṛtteḥ \| nirdiśyamānena dvaita-vāsanāyāṃ nirastāyām eva tanmatasthādi-vākyaṃ nivartakaṃ jñānam utpādayati iti cet; pauruṣeyam api vākyaṃ śūdra-ādeḥ tathā eva iti na kaścid viśeṣaḥ \| nirdiśyamānaṃ hi nāma brahma-ātma-satva-avidyāpi vākyaṃ yad-artha-pratipādaka-grantham, tad-artha-bhāvanataḥ sā eva viparīta-vāsanāṃ

nivartayanti iti draṣṭavyaṃ nirviśeṣa-āsana-vidhi-dhvaṃse \| veda-anuvacana-ādīni api vividiṣā-upātta-aviśeṣa-guṇyante iti sūtrasthā api vividiṣāyāṃ jātāyāṃ pārśva-vedana-prasiddhi-asamādhi-viṣaya-itara-vāsanāyāṃ nirastāyāṃ jñānam upapadyate; tena eva paramārthiko bandho nivartata iti \|\|

athavā tarka-anugṛhīta-tatva-yathātmya-anumāna-adhigataṃ nirviśeṣa-svaprākāśa-cinmātra-dravya-vastubhūtaṃ jñāna-amṛtatvaṃ, tarka-anatividha-vivicitra-adṛṣṭa-ādi-avidyā-kalpa-rūpaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagad vāyastam iti niścīyamānaṃ bhūta-viśuddha-pratyagvastu-yathātmya-bhāvanayā viparīta-vāsanāṃ nirasya tad eva pratyagvastu sākṣātkṛtya śūdra-dyogo api vimokṣyanta iti mithyābhūta-vicitra-artha-vicitra-duḥkhi-ālokika-ananta-viśeṣa-anvitena vedānta-vākyena na kiñcit prayojanam iha dṛśyata iti śāstra-dṛṣṭiḥ eva brahmavidyāyām adhikāra-sprayojanaḥ \| anena eva nyāyena brāhmaṇa-ādīnām api brahmavedana-siddhe rūpa-nibandha tad-arthitvau hasta-jālaṃ vilīyate \|\|

na ca vācyam nairāñjalya-loka-vyavahāre bhrāntatodya kenacitavyaṃ laukika-vyavahāro bhramaḥ; paramārthaḥ ca iti animīti samarthite sati eva pratyakṣa-anumāna-anuguṇatayā jāyanta iti tat-samarthikā śrutiḥ iti api acyate; yato bhavabhūtānāṃ śāstrīyasya eva pratyakṣa-anumānābhyāṃ vastuniṣṭhatvaṃ kṛtaḥ pratyakṣa-anumāna-vyatibhūtā janavṛttiḥ; buddhi-ādīnāṃ ca jātāyāṃ pratyakṣa-anumānābhyām eva vivikta-svabhāvatvaṃ nitya-śuddha-svaprākāśa-hitatva-upahitastva iti eva setu, anyat sarvaṃ tasmin adhyastam iti suvicetrasam \| evaṃbhūte svaprākāśe vastuni śruti-samarthyaṃ viśeṣa-antaram ca na abhyupagamyate; adhyastatātva-anivṛttau hi śrutiḥ api svamate \| na ca mata ātmāna-ānanda-rūpatā-jñāna-yoga-nivṛtta-sthityā, cidrūpatayā eva sakala-itara-adrūpatayā ānanda-rūpatvāt \|\|

yasya tu mokṣa-sādhanatayā vedānta-vākya-avihitaṃ jñānam upāsanā-rūpam, tad ca parabrahmabhūta-paramapuruṣa-prījanam, tad ca śāstra-eka-samadhigamyam, upāsanā-śābdaṃ codanayā nirdiṣṭatā-adhīna-svādhyāya-arjitaṃ jñānaṃ viveka-vimokṣa-ādi-sādhana-anugṛhītena eva svopāyatvayā svīkṛtā iti, evaṃlopaśāmnāśīteḥ puruṣeṣam upāsakaṃ svābhāvika-anavadya-ananta-jñāna-ādīnāṃ karma-ajanita-ajñāne na agranthavāne vakti iti pakṣaḥ; tasya yathoktayā līlayā śruta-vedārtha-adhikāra upapadyate \|\| 36 \|\|

iti apaśūdra-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

**atha pramita-adhikaraṇa-śeṣaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|**

tad evaṃ prastuta-upāsanā-adhikāra-kathāṃ parisamāpya prakṛtatayā aṅguṣṭha-mitasya bhūta-bhavya-bhartṛtva-abhāvāt parabrahma-bhāva-anupapattiṃ devāntara-sādha—

## \|\| 1.3.40 \|\|

*kampanāt*

**Комментарий:**

ajaḍaḍumārāḥ puruṣaḥ madhye ātmāni tiṣṭhati iti śrutiḥ ajaḍaḍumārāḥ puruṣe antarātmā iti anyoge viṣayaḥ madhye hṛdayaṃ kiñ ca jagat sarvaṃ prāṇaḥ ejati niḥsṛtam \| mahat bhayaṃ vajram udyatam yaḥ etat viduḥ amṛtāḥ te bhavanti \|\| bhayāt asya agniḥ tapati bhayāt tapati sūryaḥ \| bhayāt indraḥ ca vāyuḥ ca mṛtyuḥ dhāvati pañcamaḥ \|\| iti kampanasya jagataḥ agni-sūryādīnāṃ ca asmin chruḍumārākhye puruṣe prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭe sthitānāṃ sarveṣāṃ tataḥ niḥsṛtānāṃ tasmāt taj-jñāna-mahā-bhaya-nimittam ejanaṃ—kampanaṃ śrūyate \| tat-śāsana-ativṛttau kiṃ bhaviṣyati iti mahataḥ bhayāt vajra-vat udyatasya jagat kampata iti arthaḥ \| bhayāt asya agniḥ tapati iti anena etat kāryā iti mahad-bhayaṃ vajram udyatam iti pañcamy-arthe prathamā \| ayam arthaḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ svabhāvaḥ etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi sūrya-candramasau vidhṛtau tiṣṭhataḥ śroṣāḍaśamātrātaḥ pavate, bhīṣā udeti sūryaḥ; bhīṣā asmāt agnīś ca indraś ca, mṛtyuḥ dhāvati pañcamaḥ iti parasya brahmaṇaḥ puruṣottamasya eṣa viśeṣaṇa-vigītaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

iti ajaḍḍumārādhikaraṇam ṣaṣṭham: puruṣottamaḥ—

## \|\| 1.3.41 \|\|

*jyotir-darśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

tam eva etam ajaḍumārādhikaraṇa-viṣayayoḥ madhye para-brahma-sādhāraṇaṃ sarva-tejasāṃ dhārakaṃ sarva-tejasāṃ kāraṇa-bhūtam anugrāhakaṃ ca aḍaḍumārābhimatasya jyotiḥ iti ete— na tatra sūryaḥ bhāti na candra-tārakam na imā vidyutaḥ bhānti kutaḥ ayam agniḥ \| tam eva bhāntam anubhāti sarvaṃ tasya bhāsā sarvam idaṃ vibhāti \|\| iti \| asmeva śloka ātharvaṇe paraṃ brahma adhikṛtya śrūyate \| para-jyotiḥ ca sarvatra parasya brahmaṇaḥ śrūyate \| yathā paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate śrutaṃ devā jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ āyuḥ upāsate amṛtam atha yataḥ paraḥ divaḥ jyotiḥ dīpyate ity-ādiṣu \| ataḥ ajaḍuḍuḍumārābhimataḥ paraṃ brahma \|\| 41 \|\| iti pramitādhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha arthāntaratvādi-vyapadeśādhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.3.42 \|\|

*ākāśaḥ arthāntaratvādi-vyapadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

chāndogye śrūyate ākāśaḥ ha vai nāma-rūpayoḥ nirvahitā te yat antarā tat brahma tat amṛtaṃ sa ātmā iti \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kiṃ-prabhā-ākāśa-śabda-nirdiṣṭaḥ muktātmā, uta paramātmā iti \| kiṃ yuktam? muktātmā iti \| kutaḥ? aśvaḥ iva romāṇi vidhūya pāpaṃ candraḥ iva rāhoḥ mukhāt pramucya \| dhūtvā śarīram akṛtaṃ kṛtātmā brahma-lokam abhisaṃbhavāmi \|\| iti muktasya anantara-śrutatvāt, te yat antarā iti ca nāma-rūpa-nirvahitṛtayā tasya abhidhānāt \| ātmā-rūpayoḥ nirvahitā iti ca sa

evaṃ pūrvatra api aupādhika-lakṣaṇa-vidhitaḥ; sa eva hi deva-ādiṣu api nāmāni ca pūrvam avidhṛta \| tasya eva nāma-rūpa-nirvaktā sāṅketika-vyavasthā śruti-baddhā tadartham iti ucyate \| ākāśa-śabdasya tasmin brahmaṇi mukhya-vṛttitva-prakāra-yogaḥ upapadyate \| nanu dahara-vākya-śeṣatvāt tasya sa eva dahara-ākāśaḥ arthaḥ iti pratīyate, tasya ca paramātmatvaṃ nirṇītam \| mā evam, prājāpatya-ativākya-vyavadhānāt \| prājāpatya-ativākye ca pratyagātmanaḥ mukta-vyavasthāntaṃ rūpam abhihitam \| anantaraṃ ca vidhūya pāpmānam iti sa eva mukta-avasthaḥ prastutaḥ \| ataḥ atra ākāśaḥ mukta-ātmā \|\|

iti prāpte ucyate—ākāśaḥ arthāntaratva-ādi-vyapadeśāt iti \| ākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma; kutaḥ? arthāntaratva-ādi-vyapadeśāt \| arthāntaratva-vyapadeśāt tāvat ākāśaḥ ha vai nāma-rūpayoḥ nirvahitā iti nāma-rūpayoḥ nirvoḍhatvaṃ baddha-mukta-ubhaya-avasthā-pratyagātmanaḥ arthāntaratvam ākāśasya upapādayati \| baddha-avasthasya karma-vaśya-nāma-rūpe bhajamānaḥ na nāma-rūpe nirvoḍhuṃ śaknuyāt; mukta-avasthasya jagad-vyāpāra-asaṃbhavāt na nitarāṃ nāma-rūpa-nirvoḍhatvam; īśvarasya tu sakala-jagad-viśiṣṭasya nāma-rūpayoḥ nirvoḍhatvaṃ śrutaṃ ca pratipannam anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi sarvaṃ vai sarvavidaḥ jñānamayaṃ tapaḥ \| tasmāt etad brahma nāma-rūpam annaṃ ca jāyate sarvāṇi rūpāṇi vicitya dhīraḥ nāmāni kṛtvā abhivadan yad āste ityādiṣu \| ataḥ nirvāhya-nāma-rūpa-pratyagātmanaḥ nāma-rūpayoḥ nirvoḍhā ayam ākāśaḥ arthāntara-bhūtaḥ paraḥ eva brahma \| tad deva-apādya iti te yad antarā iti \| yasmāt ayam ākāśaḥ nāma-rūpe antarā—tābhyām aspṛṣṭaḥ arthāntara-bhūtaḥ; tasmāt tāni evaṃ-vidhā—apahata-pāpmatva-ādi-satya-saṅkalpatvavat ca nirvāhite arthaḥ \| ādi-śabdena brahmatva-ātmatva-amṛtatvāni gṛhyante \| niṣprapañca-bṛhatvāt ca ayaṃ hi paramātmani eva saṃbhavanti; tena ākāśaḥ paraḥ eva brahma \| yad uktaṃ hṛdayaṃ śarīram iti muktaḥ anantara-prakṛtaḥ iti, tatra, brahmalokam abhisaṃbhavāmi iti parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ anantara-prakṛtatvāt \| yat punar abhisaṃbhavitṛ-muktasya abhisaṃbādhyatayā paraṃ brahma nirdiṣṭam, tat hi abhisaṃbhavitṛ-muktasya nāma-rūpa-nirvoḍhatvāt śreṣṭha-saṃvāda-artha-saṃbādhyam paraṃ brahma tatra pratīyeta eva \| kiñca ākāśa-śabdena prakṛtasya dahara-ākāśasya atra pratyabhijñānāt, prājāpatya-ativākyasya api upāsakatva-upakalpana-arthatvāt upāsyaḥ eva dahara-ākāśaḥ prāpyatvena upasaṃhriyate iti yuktam \| ākāśa-śabdaḥ ca pratyagātmani na kvacid dṛṣṭacaraḥ \| ataḥ atra ākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma \|\|

ākāśaḥ talliṅgāt \|\| 1.1.22 \|\|

atha syāt—pratyagātmanaḥ arthāntara-bhūta-mātra-anantaram eva nāsti, aiccha-upadeśāt, hanta-pratiṣedhāt ca; śuddha-avasthaḥ eva hi pratyagātmā paramātmā paraṃ brahma parameśvaraḥ iti ca vyapadiśyate \| ataḥ prakṛta-mukta-ātmanaḥ abhisaṃbandhi-guṇa-nirdhāraṇa-samisaṃbādhyaḥ brahmalokaḥ; ataḥ nāma-rūpayoḥ nirvahitā ākāśaḥ api sa eva bhavitum arhati iti \|

ataḥ uttaraṃ paṭhati—

## \|\| 1.3.43 \|\|

*suṣupti-utkrāntyoḥ bhedena*

**Комментарий:**

vyapadeśāt iti vartate, suṣupti-utkrāntyoḥ pratyagātmanaḥ arthāntaratvena paramātmanaḥ vyapadeśāt pratyagātmanaḥ arthāntara-sūtraḥ paramātmā eva \| tathā hi—bālāki-upākhyāne ajātam āha—vidhyaṃ vijñānamayaḥ prāṇeṣu iti prakṛtasya pratyagātmanaḥ suṣupti-avasthāyām avikriya-jñasya sarvajñena paramātmanā pariṣvaṅga āmnāyate—prājñena ātmanā sampariṣvaktaḥ na bāhyaṃ kiṃcana veda na antaram iti; tathā utkrāntau api prājñena ātmanā anvāroḍha utsarjan yāti \| na ca svaprati utkrāntau bālaki-vijñasya tadānīm eva hṛdena eva sarvajñaṃ matā pariṣvaṅga-anvārohe saṃbhavataḥ; na ca kṣetrajña-antareṇa, tasya api sarvajñatva-asambhavāt \|\| 43 \|\|

ity atra pratyagātmanaḥ arthāntara-bhūtaḥ paramātmā ity āha—

## \|\| 1.3.44 \|\|

*pati-ādi-śabdebhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

ayaṃ pariṣvaṅgakaḥ paramātmā uttaraṃ pati-ādi-śabdena upadiśyate—sarvasya vaśī sarvasya īśānaḥ... sa na sādhunā karmaṇā bhūyān no eva asādhunā kanīyān, eṣa sarveśvara eṣa bhūta-adhipatiḥ eṣa bhūta-pālaḥ eṣa setuḥ vidharaṇa eṣāṃ lokānām asaṃbhedāya \| taṃ etaṃ veda-anuvacanena brāhmaṇā vividiṣanti...etam eva viditvā muniḥ bhavati, etam eva pravrājinaḥ lokam icchantaḥ pravrajanti sa vā eṣa mahān aja ātmā ajaraḥ amaraḥ amṛtaḥ abhayaḥ brahma iti \| evaṃ ca patitva-jagad-vidharaṇa-sarveśvaratva-ādayaḥ pratyagātmani mukta-avasthye api na kathañcit saṃbhavanti \| ataḥ muktātmanaḥ arthāntara-sūtraḥ nārāyaṇaḥ nirdihitā āha \| aupaniṣada-śabdu sarvasya cidacidātmakasya brahma-kāryatvena tadātmakatayā ca iti sarvaṃ khalv idaṃ brahma tajjalān ity apṛthagbhāva-vṛttyaḥ pratipādyate iti pūrvam eva samarthitam \| daivastra-viṣaye atra tata aikya-anvayaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

iti arthāntaratva-ādi-vyapadeśa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 10 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye prathamasya adhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

śrīḥ astu

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**prathama-adhyāye—caturthaḥ pādaḥ—ānumānika-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Pada 4

## \|\| 1.4.1 \|\|

*ānumānikam api ekeṣām iti cet na śarīra-rūpaka-vinyasta-gṛhīteḥ darśayati ca*

**Комментарий:**

uktaṃ parama-puruṣārtha-lakṣaṇam īkṣa-sādhanatvena jijñāsyaṃ jagajjanmādi-kāraṇaṃ brahma avidyasmṛtaḥ pradhānādeḥ cetanācu brahmuktobhayāvasthāhitarakṣaṇaṃ nirasta-samasta-doṣa-gandhaṃ sarvajñaṃ sarvaśakti satya-saṅkalpaṃ samasta-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ātmakam sarva-antarātmatayā nirduṣkṣmārthim iti \| idānīṃ kāpila-tantra-

siddha-brahmātmaka-pradhāna-bhūta-cin-miśritācid-vastu-mukhena pradhāna-kāraṇatva-pratipādana-icchayā-anusārīṇi api kāni cid vākyāni kārya-viśeṣāvāptu sat iti ajñāḍhyaṃ brahma kāraṇatva-vyapadeśena tarkitarām iti \|\|

kaṭha-vallī-śākhā āmnīyate indriyebhyaḥ parā hi arthāḥ arthebhyaś ca paraṃ manaḥ \| manasaḥ tu parā buddhiḥ buddheḥ ātmā mahān paraḥ \|\| mahataḥ param avyaktaṃ avyaktāt puruṣaḥ paraḥ \| puruṣāt na paraṃ kiṃcit sā kāṣṭhā sā parā gatiḥ \|\| iti \| tatra sandehaḥ—kiṃ kāpila-tantra-siddha-brahmātmake pradhānābhihāvyaktābdena ucyate, uta na iti \| kiṃ yuktam ? pradhānam iti \| kutaḥ ? mahataḥ param avyaktaṃ avyaktāt puruṣaḥ paraḥ iti tantra-siddha-tattva-prakriyā-madhya-patitācena tathaiva pratīteḥ; puruṣāt na paraṃ kiṃcit sā kāṣṭhā sā parā gatiḥ iti pañca-viṃśaka-puruṣātirikta-tattva-niṣedhāc ca \| ataḥ avyaktaṃ kāraṇam iti prāptam \| tad idam uktam—ānumānikam api ekeṣām iti ca udeti \| ekeṣāṃ śākhināṃ śākhāsu ānumānikaṃ pradhānam api kāraṇam āmnāyata iti cet \|\|

atra uttaraṃ—na iti \| na ayaṃ kaṭha-vallī-gata-brahmātmake pradhānābhidhīyate \| kutaḥ ? śarīra-rūpaka-vinyasta-gṛhīteḥ—śarīrākhya-rūpaka-vinyastasya avyakta-śabdena gṛhīteḥ \| ātma-śarīra-buddhi-mana-indriya-viṣayeṣu rathya-rathādi-śabdena rūpiteṣu ratha-śabdena vinyastasya śarīrasya śāstrāvyakta-śabdena grahaṇa-darśanāt \|\|

etad uktaṃ bhavati—pūrvatra hi ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi śarīraṃ ratham eva ca \| buddhiṃ tu sārathiṃ viddhi manaḥ pragraham eva ca \| indriyāṇi hayān āhuḥ viṣayāṃs teṣu gocarān \| ity-ādinā, saḥ adhvanaḥ pāram āpnoti tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam iti antyena saṃsārādhvanaḥ pāraṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ padaṃ prepsatātmopāsakaṃ rathitvena tac-charīra-rūpiṇī ca ratha-sthānīyatvena rūpayitvā, yathā ete rathādhyo vaśaṃ tiṣṭhanti, saḥ eva adhvanaḥ pāraṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ padam āpnoti iti uktvā, teṣu rathādi-sthita-saṃrīrādiṣu yāni jñeyo vaśī-kāryāṇi iti āṃ pradhānāni, tāni ucyante indriyebhyaḥ parāḥ iti ādinā \| tatra hi arthebhyo rūpitebhya indriyebhyo gocaratvena rūpitā viṣayāḥ vaśī-kāryatve parāḥ, arṇavendriyasya api viṣaya-sannidhāv indriyāṇāṃ durnigrahatvāt \| tebhyaḥ api paraṃ pragraha-rūpitaṃ manaḥ, manasi viṣaya-pravaṇe viṣaya-saṃnidhānasya aśakya-śaṅkatvāt \| tasmāt api sārathi-vat-sthāpitā buddhiḥ parā, adhyavasāyābhāve manasaḥ api akiñcitkaratvāt \| tathyā api rathitva-sthāpita ātmā kartuḥ tvena prādhānyāt paraḥ; sarveṣāṃ ca artha-ātmana-icchāyattatvāt, ātmāna eva mahān iti ca viśeṣyate \| tasmāt api ratha-sthāpitaṃ śarīraṃ param, tad-ayattatvāt jīvātma-saṃkalpa-puruṣārtha-sādhana-pravṛttīnām \| tasmāt api para-sarva-niyantaram abhūtoḍravya-ādi-madhyavān pāramārthaḥ parama-puruṣaḥ; prayo kartṛtām āpannasya samastasya tad-adhīnatvāc ca pravṛttatvāt \| sa ca kaṇṭakāpitastathopāsanasya api niyāmakaḥ \| parā tu tat-śrutir iti hi jīvātmanaḥ kartuḥ tvaṃ parama-puruṣāyattam iti vakṣyate \| vaśī-kāryopāsanīyaṃ rūpyāṇakatā-bhūtaḥ param-

prāpya atra te eva \| tad vidat śrūyāmānaṃ paraṃ kiñcit asti kāṣṭhā sā parā gatiḥ iti \| tathā ca antaryāmi-brāhmaṇe, ya ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmā antaraḥ yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ ya ātmāni viddhyāṇyadhisṭhā sarvaṃ sākṣāt kurvan sarvaṃ niyamayati iti uktvā na anyaḥ ataḥ asti draṣṭā iti draṣṭṛtva-niyamanatvaṃ nirviśyate \|\| bhagavad-gītāsu ca adhiṣṭhānaṃ tathā kartā karaṇaṃ ca pṛthag-vidham \| vividhā ca pṛthak-ceṣṭā daivaṃ ca eva atra pañcamam \|\| iti \| daivam atra puruṣottama eva sarvasya ca ahaṃ hṛdi sanniviṣṭaḥ mattaḥ smṛtiḥ jñānam apohanaṃ ca iti vacanāt \| tasya ca vaśīkaraṇaṃ tat-śaraṇāgatiḥ eva \| yathā āha īśvaraḥ sarva-bhūtānāṃ hṛd-deśe arjuna tiṣṭhati \| bhrāmayan sarva-bhūtāni yantra-ārūḍhāni māyayā \|\| tam eva śaraṇaṃ gaccha iti \| tat evam ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi ityādinā rathi-adhidevatva-viśiṣṭatā indriyādayaḥ indriyebhyaḥ parā hi arthāḥ iti atra svabhāvata eva pratyabhijñāpyante, na ratha-sthitiṃ śarīram iti pariśeṣāt tad-vyatirikta-devatā ucyate iti nirṇīyate \| ataḥ kāpila-tantra-siddhasya pradhānasya prasaṅgaḥ eva na asti \| na ca atra tat-tantra-siddha-prakriyā-pratyabhijñā, indriyebhyaḥ parā hi arthāḥ iti ādi indriyebhyaḥ arthānāṃ śabda-ādīnāṃ paratva-ukteḥ nāma \| na hi śabdasya indriyāṇāṃ kāraṇabhūtatā īdṛśī \| arthāt ca paraṃ manaḥ iti api na tat-tantra-smṛtatvam, akāraṇatvāt eva \| tathā buddheḥ ātmā mahān paraḥ iti api asaṅgatam, buddhiḥ iti etena mahat-tattvasya abhidhānāt upagamāt \| na hi mahataḥ mahat-tattva-saṃbhavati \| mahataḥ ātma-śabdena viśeṣaṇaṃ ca na sañjaghate \| ataḥ rūpaka-vinyastānām eva grahaṇam \|\| dṛśyati ca tad eva śreyaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu gūḍhaḥ ataṃ na prakāśate \| dṛśyate tu agryayā buddhyā sūkṣmayā sūkṣma-darśibhiḥ \|\| yacchet vāk manasī prājñaḥ tad yacchet jñāne ātmani \| jñānam ātmani mahati niyacchet tad yacchet śānte ātmani \|\| iti abhigata-agamya-antara-karaṇasya parama-puruṣasya durdarśatva-avagamāt hṛdi-ādi-sthitānām artīndriya-devatānāṃ vaśīkāra-sahakāraḥ ayam ucyate \| yacchet vāk manasī—vācaṃ manasi niyacchet, vāg-dukāni karmendriyāṇi jñānendriyāṇi ca manasi niyacchet iti arthaḥ \| vāk ucyate dvitīyayā: supāṃ suluk iti luk \| manasi iti saptamyā aṣṭhānasaḥ dīrghaḥ \| tad yacchet jñāne ātmani—tat manaḥ buddho niyacchet \| jñāna-śabdena atra pūrvoktaḥ buddhiḥ abhidhīyate \| jñāne ātmani iti vyadhikaraṇe saptamyau \| ātmani vartamāne jñāne niyacchet iti arthaḥ \| jñānam ātmani mahati niyacchet—buddhiṃ kartari mahati ātmani niyacchet \| tad yacchet śānte ātmani—taṃ kartāraṃ paramātma-rūpiṇi sarvāntaryāmiṇi niyacchet \| vyatyayena tad iti napuṃsaka-liṅgatā \| evaṃ-bhūtena rathinā vaiṣṇavaṃ padaṃ gantavyam iti arthaḥ \|\| 1 \|\| atha ākāśa-ādeḥ na kathaṃ vyaktasya śarīrastha-abhidhānam? tatra āha—

## \|\| 1.4.2 \|\|

*sūkṣmaṃ tu tad-arhatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

bhūta-sūkṣmam avyākṛtaṃ hi avyakta-viśeṣa-mātrasya śarīre bhavati; tad-vyatireka-arthaṃ śarīra-avastha-

samyaktvād eva ucyate \| tad ahaṃvād—tasyāyāḥ kṛtsnasya avicchedena eva vikārāpannasya ratha-caturu-pārtha-saṃghāta-pratyaya-hetutvāt \|\| 2 \|\|

yadi bhūta-sūkṣmam adhyākṛtam abhyupagamyate; kapila-tantra-siddhe pradhāne kaḥ praveśaḥ? tatra api hi bhūta-kāraṇam eva avyaktam iti ucyate; tatra āha—

## \|\| 1.4.3 \|\|

*tad-adhīnatvād arthavat*

**Комментарий:**

parama-kāraṇa-bhūta-paramapuruṣa-adhīnatvāt tad-ayojanavat ca bhūta-sūkṣmam \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—na avyaktam tat-tat-pariṇāma-viśeṣa-śūnyaṃ svarūpeṇa na abhyupagamyāmaḥ; api tu paramapuruṣa-śarīratayā tat-samasta-kārya-virodheṇa \| tad-ātmakatvena eva hi prakṛty-avastha-prayojanaṃ sādhayati; anyathā svarūpa-sthiti-pravṛtti-bhedāḥ tasya na syuḥ \| tathā ajām upagamya eva tan-mantrita-kārya-kāritvam iti \| śruti-smṛtyoḥ hi jagad-utpatti-samaya-viśeṣu paramapuruṣam abhimukhīkṛtya eva ca prakṛti-vikṛti-puruṣāḥ tatra tatra samākṛṣyante; yathā pṛthivyam u pralīyate iti ārabhya tanmātrāṇi bhūtādau līyante, bhūtādiḥ ca iti līyate, mahān avyakte līyate, avyaktam akṣare līyate, akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate, tamaḥ pare deve ekībhavati \| tathā yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ ... yasya āpaḥ śarīraṃ ... yasya tejaḥ śarīraṃ ... yasya vāyuḥ śarīraṃ ... yasya ākāśaḥ śarīraṃ ... yasya ahaṅkāraḥ śarīraṃ ... yasya buddhiḥ śarīraṃ ... yasya avyaktam śarīraṃ ... yasya akṣaraṃ śarīraṃ ... yasya mṛtyuḥ śarīram ... eṣa sarva-bhūta-antarātmā apahata-pāpmā divyaḥ devaḥ eko nārāyaṇaḥ \| tathā bhūmiḥ āpaḥ analaḥ vāyuḥ khaṃ manaḥ buddhiḥ eva ca \| ahaṅkāraḥ iti iyaṃ me bhinnā prakṛtiḥ aṣṭadhā \|\| aparā iyam itas tu anyāṃ prakṛtiṃ viddhi me parām \| jīva-bhūtāṃ mahābāho yayā idaṃ dhāryate jagat \|\| etad-yonīni bhūtāni sarvāṇi iti upadhāraya \| ahaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ pralayaḥ tathā \|\| mattaḥ parataraṃ na anyat kiñcid asti dhanañjaya \| mayi sarvam idaṃ protaṃ sūtre maṇi-gaṇāḥ iva \|\| iti, vyaktaṃ viṣṇuḥ tathā avyaktam puruṣaḥ kālaḥ eva ca iti, prakṛtiḥ yā mayā ākhyātā vyakta-avyakta-svarūpiṇī \| puruṣaḥ ca api ubhau etau līyete paramātmani \| paramātmā ca sarveṣām ādhāraḥ parameśvaraḥ \| viṣṇu-nāmā sa vedeṣu vedānteṣu ca gīyate \|\| iti ca \|\| 3 \|\|

## \|\| 1.4.4 \|\|

*jñeyatva-avacanāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

yadi tantra-siddham idam akṣaram avidiviṣiṣyeta, tadā asya jñeyatvam avakṣyat; vyakta-avyakta-jñānavān mokṣaṃ bhavati iti ādiṣu kaivalyaṃ jñeyatva-abhyupagamāt \| na ca asya jñeyatvam ucyate; ataḥ na tantra-siddam idaṃ grāhyam \|\| 4 \|\|

## \|\| 1.4.5 \|\|

*vadati iti cet na prājñaḥ hi prakaraṇāt*

**Комментарий:**

akṣaratma-sparśa-niṣṭhaṃ mantraṃ tathā aparaṃ nityam āha yat ca yat \| anādi-anantaṃ mahataḥ param

dhruvaṃ nicāyya taṃ mṛtyu-mukhāt pramucyate \|\| iti avyaktasya īśvaram anantaram eva vaktavyaṃ śrūtiḥ iti cet, tatra; prāha:—parama-puruṣa eva hi atra śloke nicāyyatvena pratipādyate vijñāna-sārathiḥ yaḥ tu manaḥ-pragrahavān naraḥ \| saḥ adhvanaḥ pāram āpnoti tat viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam eṣa sarveṣu bhūteṣu gūḍha-ātmā na prakāśate \| dṛśyate tu agryayā buddhyā sūkṣmayā sūkṣma-darśibhiḥ \|\| iti prāk tasya eva prakṛtatvāt \| ata eva puruṣāt na paraṃ kiñcit iti na pañcaviṃśa-kṣetra-atiriktatva-niṣedhaḥ \| tasya ca parama-puruṣasya aśabdatvādayaḥ dharmāḥ yat ca ādeḥ śubham ādhyāna-yogīśuṣirāḥ \| mahataḥ param avyadi buddheḥ ātmā mahān paraḥ iti pūrva-pakṣatāt jīva-canaḥ paratvena eva ucyate \|\|4\|\|

## \|\| 1.4.6 \|\|

*trayāṇām eva ca evam upanyāsaḥ praśnaḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

asmin prakaraṇe hi upāya-upeya-bhūtānāṃ trayāṇām eva ca evam upanyāsaḥ—īyattvena upanyāsaḥ, tad-viṣayaḥ ca praśnaḥ dṛśyate, na anyasya avyaktādeḥ \| tathā hi—naciketā mumukṣuḥ tat mṛtyuṃ (śreyo) vṛṇīte varṇye prathamena vareṇa asamaḥ pitṛ-sauśasyatā-pāditnīm ātmani pitṛ-sumanaskatāṃ pratītya dvitīyena vareṇa mokṣa-upāya-bhūtaṃ nāciketa-agni-vidyāṃ yayau—saḥ tvam agnim svargyam adhyeṣi mṛtyo prabrūhi taṃ śraddadhānāya mahyam \| svarga-lokāḥ amṛtatvaṃ bhajanta etat dvitīyena vṛṇe vareṇa \|\| iti \| svarga-śabdena atra parama-puruṣārtha-lakṣaṇaḥ mokṣaḥ abhidhīyate, amṛtatvaṃ bhajanta iti tad-madhyasya janma-maraṇa-abhāva-śravaṇāt tat ca kṣipakarma-kṣaya-anidarśanāt ca; triṇāciketaḥ tribhiḥ etya sandhiṃ trikarma-kṛt tarati janma-mṛtyū iti ca prativacanāt \| tṛtīyena vareṇa mokṣa-svarūpa-prakṣāra-upeya-svarūpa-mumukṣu-svarūpa-prāpya-bhūta-karma-anuṣṭheya-upāsana-tat-kartavyaṃ ca pṛṣṭaṃ—yā iyam prete vicikitsā manuṣye asti iti eke na ayam asti iti ca eke \| etat vidyām anuśiṣṭaḥ tvayā ahaṃ varāṇām eṣa varaḥ tṛtīyaḥ \|\| iti \|\|

evaṃ mokṣe pṛṣṭe tad-upadeya-upāyatāṃ parīkṣya upadideśa—śataṃ dūrdarśaṃ gūḍham anupraviṣṭaṃ guhāhitam gahvareṣṭhaṃ purāṇam \| adhyātma-yoga-adhigamena devaṃ matvā dhīraḥ harṣa-śokau jahāti \|\| iti \| tat eva sāmānyena upadiṣṭaṃ nāciketāḥ śrotum icchan devaṃ matvā iti upāsyatayā nirdiṣṭasya prāpya-bhūtasya devasya adhyātma-yoga-adhigamena iti veditavyatayā nirdiṣṭasya prāptaḥ pratyag-ātma-tattva matvā dhīraḥ harṣa-śokau jahāti iti nirdiṣṭasya brahma-upāsanasya ca svarūpa-viśodhanāya punaḥ papraccha—anyatra dharmāt anyatra adharmāt anyatra asmāt kṛta-akṛtāt \| anyatra bhūtāt ca bhavyāt ca yat tat paśyasi tat vada \|\| iti \| evaṃ sakala-itara-hita-anāgata-vartamāna-sādhya-sādhana-sādhaka-vilakṣaṇe traye pṛṣṭe prathamaṃ praṇavaṃ praśasya tad-ālambyaṃ prāpya-svarūpaṃ tad-antaragataṃ ca prākṛta-svarūpaṃ vācaka-svarūpaṃ ca upāyaṃ punaḥ api sāmānyena sthāpayitvā tāvat upadideśa—sarve vedāḥ yat padam āmananti tapāṃsi sarvāṇi ca yat vadanti \| yat icchantaḥ brahmacaryaṃ caranti tat te padaṃ saṅgraheṇa bravīmi om iti etat \|\| iti \| evam upadiśya punaḥ api praṇavaṃ praśasya prathamaṃ tāvat prāhuḥ pratyag-ātma-svarūpam āha—na jāyate mriyate vā

vipratiṣedha iti āha \| prāpyasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ viṣṇoḥ svarūpam aṇoḥ aṇīyān ityādīn ā ka ity ā veda yatra saḥ ity antaiḥ na upadiśā madhye na ayam ātmā pravacanena labhyaḥ na medhayā na bahunā śrutena ityādinā upaprabṛhya tasya upāsanasya bhakti-rūpatām āyāha \| śraddhā vivatsāvit ca etopāyasya upāsanā-eka-sahāyasya anāsūsāratā-anupapattau ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi ityādikān atuṃ pathaḥ tat-kṣayaḥ vadanti ityantena upāsana-prakāra-supāsanasya ca vaiṣṇava-parama-pada-prāptim ādhiṣṭhāya aśabdam asparśam ityādinā upasaṃhṛtam \| avasthātrayaṇām eva atra jñeyatvena upanyāsaḥ praśnaḥ ca; tasmāt iha tāntrikasya vyaktasya grahaṇam \|\|6\|\|

## \|\| 1.4.7 \|\|

*mahat vat ca*

**Комментарий:**

yathā buddheḥ ātmā mahān paraḥ ity atra asam avagatam ātma-adhikarathyāne tattvac cintyaṃ mahattvaṃ gṛhyate; evam vyaktam api asmanaḥ varṇena abhidhānāt na kāpila-tantra-siddhaṃ gṛhyate iti sthitam \|\|7\|\| iti ānumānika-adhikaraṇam \|\|1\|\|

**atha camasa-adhikaraṇam \|\|2\|\|**

## \|\| 1.4.8 \|\|

*camasa-vat aviśeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

atra api tantra-siddha-prakriyā nirasyate, na brahma-ātmakānāṃ prakṛti-mahat-ahaṅkārādīnāṃ svarūpam, śruti-smṛtibhyāṃ brahma-ātmakānāṃ teṣāṃ pratipādanāt \| yathā ātharvaṇikāḥ adhīyate— vikāra-jananīm ajñām aṣṭa-rūpām ajāṃ dhruvām \| dhyāyate’dhyāpitā tena tanyate preryate punaḥ \| sūyate puruṣārthaṃ ca tena eva adhiṣṭhitā jagat \| gauḥ anādi-antavatī sā janitrī bhūta-bhāvinī \| sita-asitā ca raktā ca sarva-kāma-dhuk vibhoḥ \| pibanti enām aviṣam aviṣaya-asitāḥ kumārakāḥ \| ekaḥ tu pibate devaḥ svacchandaḥ atra vaśānugām \|\| dhyāna-kriyā-sparśa-bhāva-saṃyutaiḥ sa prasannaiḥ viṣṇuḥ \| sarva-indriya-dhāraṇī ; doṣaiḥ ca etaiḥ mānātāṃ tu prakṛtiḥ \|\| ajām viśati-rūpa-ātmakaṃ vyakrāmadhyate iti \| atra prakṛtyādīnāṃ svarūpa-abhihiteḥ \| yadātmakaḥ ca ās te prakṛtyādayaḥ; sa parama-puruṣaḥ api śraddhā ca āditya-varṇaṃ tamasaḥ parāre \| puruṣaṃ nityaṃ puruṣaṃ sāṅkhya-madhya-īśvaraṃ viduḥ \|\| iti pratipādyate \| apare ca ātharvaṇikāḥ aśṭau prakṛtayaḥ ṣoḍaśa vikārāḥ ity adhīyate; śvetāśvatarāḥ ca prakṛti-puruṣa-īśvara-svarūpam āmnanti saṃyuktam etat kṣaram akṣaraṃ ca vyakta-avyaktaṃ bharate viśvam īśaḥ \| anīśaḥ ca ātmā badhyate bhoktṛ-bhāvāt jñātvā devaṃ mucyate sarva-pāśaiḥ \|\| jña-ajñau dvau ajau īśa-anīśau ajā hi ekā bhoktṛ-bhogya-artha-yuktā \| anantaḥ ca ātmā viśva-rūpaḥ hi akartā trayaṃ yadā vindate brahma etat \|\| kṣaraṃ pradhānam amṛta-akṣaraṃ haraḥ kṣara-ātmānau īśate devaḥ ekaḥ \| tasya abhidhyānāt yojanāt tattva-bhāvāt bhūyaḥ ca ante viśva-māyā-nivṛttiḥ \|\| iti; tathā chandāṃsi yajñāḥ kratavaḥ vratāni bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ yat ca vedā

yadiha \| asmāsmāyo mṛjyate viśvebhyaḥ tīrtham ṛcābhyā māyayā sanniruddhaḥ \| māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāt māyinaṃ tu maheśvaram \| tasya avayava-bhūtaiḥ tu vyāptaṃ sarvam idaṃ jagat \|\| iti; tathā uttaratra api pradhāna-kṣetrajña-patiḥ guṇeśaḥ saṃsāra-mokṣa-sthiti-bandha-hetuḥ iti \|\|

smṛtiḥ api prakṛtiṃ puruṣaṃ ca eva viddhi anādī ubhau api \| vikārān ca guṇān ca eva viddhi prakṛti-saṃbhavān \|\| kārya-kāraṇa-kartṛtve hetuḥ prakṛtiḥ ucyate \| puruṣaḥ sukha-duḥkhānāṃ bhoktṛtve hetuḥ ucyate \|\| puruṣaḥ prakṛtisthaḥ api bhuṅkte prakṛtijān guṇān \| kāraṇaṃ guṇasaṅgaḥ asya sad-asad-yoni-janmasu \|\| sattvaṃ rajaḥ tamaḥ iti guṇāḥ prakṛti-saṃbhavāḥ \| nibadhnanti mahābāho dehe dehinam avyayam \|\| tathā sarva-bhūtāni kaunteya prakṛtiṃ yānti māmikām \| kalpādau visṛjāmi aham \|\| prakṛtiṃ svām avaṣṭabhya visṛjāmi punaḥ punaḥ \| bhūta-grāmam imaṃ kṛtsnam avaśaṃ prakṛteḥ vaśāt \|\| mayā adhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate sa-carācaram \| hetunā anena kaunteya jagat viparivartate \|\| iti \| tasmāt abrahmam akṛtena kapila-tantra-asiddhāḥ prakṛtyādayaḥ nirasyante \|\|

śvetāśvataropaniṣadi śrūyate ajām ekaṃ lohita-śukla-kṛṣṇāṃ bahvīḥ prajāḥ sṛjamānāṃ sarūpāḥ \| ajaḥ hi ekaḥ juṣamāṇaḥ anuśete jahāti enāṃ bhukta-bhogām ajaḥ anyaḥ \|\| iti \| tatra sandehaḥ— kim asmin mantre kevalā tantra-asiddhā prakṛtiḥ abhidhīyate, uta brahmātmikā iti \| kiṃ yuktam ? kevalā iti \| kutaḥ ? ajām ekām iti asyaḥ prakṛteḥ akāryatva-śravaṇāt, bahvīḥ prajāḥ sṛjamānāṃ sarūpāḥ iti svātantryeṇa saha guṇāṃ bahvīnāṃ prajānāṃ sṛṣṭatva-śravaṇāt ca—iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate—camasavat aviśeṣāt \| na jāgataḥ iti ete vākya-śeṣa-pratipādya-na-ātma-siddhā-brahmātmaka-jāyamāṇa-viśeṣa-pratīteḥ; camasavat—yathā arvākbilāḥ camasaḥ ūrdhva-budhnaḥ iti asmin mantre camasatva-lakṣaṇa-sādhanatva-mātraṃ camasa-saṃśabdena pratīyate iti na tāvat-mātreṇa camasa-viśeṣa-pratītiḥ, yaugika-aśabdanām artha-prakaraṇa-ādibhiḥ vinā artha-viśeṣa-niścaya-ayogāt \| tatra yad idaṃ tarhiṣṭhaḥ eṣaḥ hi arvākbilāḥ camasaḥ ūrdhva-budhnaḥ iti ādinā vākya-śeṣeṇa śirastrāṇa-sadṛśa-niścayaḥ \| tathā atra api artha-prakaraṇa-ādiḥ viśeṣāḥ ca nirṇetavyāḥ; na ca atra tantra-siddhāyām ajā eva ahita-tattva-artha-prakaraṇa-ādayaḥ dṛśyante; na vākya-antara-avāntara-artheṇa spaṣṭatvaṃ pratīyate, bahvīḥ prajāḥ sṛjamānām iti spaṣṭatva-mātra-pratīteḥ \| ataḥ anena mantreṇa na abrahmātmikā ajā abhidhīyate \|\|5\|\|

**brahmātmaka-ajā-pratipādane eva viśeṣa-hetuḥ darśitaḥ ayam—**

## \|\| 1.4.9 \|\|

*jyotiḥ upakramā tu tathā hi adhīyate eke*

**Комментарий:**

tṛjā-hetu-avadhāraṇārthaḥ; jyotiḥ upakramām eva ajām \| jyotiḥ—brahma taṃ devāḥ jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ atha yat ataḥ paraḥ divaḥ jyotiḥ dīpyate ityādi-śruti-prasiddheḥ \| jyotiḥ upakramā—brahma-kāraṇikā ityarthaḥ \| tathā hi adhīyate eke—hi iti hetoḥ, yasmāt asya ajāyāḥ brahma-kāraṇatvam eva

śrāhitaḥ taiḥ anireva arthīyate—apāṇerṇopamanvato mahīyān ātmā guhāyāṃ nihitaḥ asya jantoḥ iti hṛdaya-guhyām uparyadvena sannihitaṃ brahma abhidhāya yasya prāṇaḥ prabhavanti tasmāt ityādinā sarveṣāṃ lokānāṃ brahma-adhīnāṃ ca tataḥ utpattim abhidhāya sarva-kāraṇībhūta-aṇḍajā tataḥ utpattau abhidheye ajaḥ ekaṃ lohita-śukla-kṛṣṇāṃ bahvīḥ prajāḥ janayantīṃ sarūpāḥ \| ajaḥ hi ekaḥ juṣamāṇaḥ anuśete jahati enāṃ bhukta-bhogām ajaḥ anyaḥ \|\| iti \| sarveśya-tattva-atirikttasya vastu-jātasya tataḥ utpattau tathāsmakalopadeśe prakṛta-prāṇa-abhidhīya-upamānatva-trāṇa-samupavartanādi-vadeśāt yajā bahvīnāṃ sajjarpaṇāṃ prajānāṃ ghaṭṭī karmavaśyena ātmanā bhuṅjamānā anena viduṣāḍanena tyajyamānā ca brahmaṇā utpannā brahma-ātmikāḍanantavaryāḥ \| ataḥ vākya-upagamyasya viśeṣavacchābāt tarīyate tasmākṣa-trayatmāyām anāthahīya-upādhiyāmitāṇḍaṃ brahma-ātmikā iti niścīyate \| iha api prakaraṇa-krame kiṃ kāraṇaṃ brahma tasya asya te dhyāna-yoga-anugatāḥ apaśyan devātma-śaktiṃ svaguṇaiḥ nigūḍhām iti parabrahma-sarūpayā ajayā avagateḥ, upaniṣat ca asmān māyī sṛjate viśvam etat niṣmāyayaḥ māyayā sanniruddhaḥ māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāt māyinaṃ tu maheśvaram yaḥ yoniyoniniṃ-kṣititadvivekaḥ iti ca tasyāḥ eva pratītinismin mantre tantrisiddhavat tantra-prakṛti-pratipattiḥ na aṅgīḥ \|\|

karyaṃ tarhi jyotir-upakramāya lohita-śukla-kṛṣṇa-rūpayā asyāḥ prakṛteḥ rajastvam, ajayā vā karyaṃ jyotir-upakramatva-niśrāha—

## \|\| 1.4.10 \|\|

*kalpanopadeśāt ca madhvādivat avirodhaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

prastuta-artha-kṛta-nirūpya-arthavādaḥ \| asyāḥ prakṛteḥ ajatvaṃ jyotir-upakramatvaṃ ca na virudhyate; kutaḥ ? kalpanopadeśāt \| kalpanaṃ kḷptiḥ sṛṣṭiḥ; jagat-sṛṣṭyu padeśāt ityarthaḥ \| yathā sūryācandramasau dhātā yathāpūrvaṃ akalpayat iti kalpanaṃ sṛṣṭiḥ \| asyāḥ api asmāt māyī sṛjate viśvam etat iti jagat-sṛṣṭiṣu vidhiyate \| śvetāviśvatarasmāsūkṣma-avasthā-kāraṇa-māyī sarveśvaraḥ sarvaṃ jagat sṛjati ityarthaḥ \| anena kalpanopadeśena asyāḥ prakṛteḥ kārya-akāraṇa-rūpeṇa avasthā-dvaya-anugamaḥ avagamyate \| sā hi pralaya-velāyāṃ brahmatopannā avibhakta-nāma-rūpa sūkṣma-rūpeṇa avatiṣṭhate; sṛṣṭi-velāyāṃ bṛdbhūtasvasthaviguṇa-vibhakta-nāma-rūpa-avyaktādi-śabda-vācyā tejomadhādi-rūpeṇa ca pariṇatā lohita-śukla-kṛṣṇa-ākārā jñāyate \| ataḥ kāraṇa-avasthā ajā, kārya-avasthā jyotir-upakrama iti na virodhaḥ \| madhvādivat—yathā īśvaronirīśvarasya kāraṇa-avasthāyām ekasya iva avasthitasya kārya-avasthāyām ṛgyajussāmātharvaśirvāṅmayakanipātarasāśrayatayā vasvādī-divatā-bhogyatayā madhutvakalpita madhvākāra-madhvadatvaṃ ca na virudhyate \| tat uktaṃ madhu-vidyāyām asau vai ādityaḥ deva-madhu iti ārabhya atha tataḥ ūrdhvaṃ udetya na eva udetā na astam etā ekaḥ eva madhye sthātā iti antena \| ekalā-ekatva-bhāvaḥ \| ataḥ anena mantreṇa brahma-ātmikāḍaiva abhidhīyate, na kāpila-tantra-siddhā iti siddham \|\|

atra raśmimātre tejo-leśa-lakṣaṇāt jāka-āśraya-yogāt iti brūte \| te śākhāḥ—kiṃ tejo-brahma eva tejo-brahma-abhimānakaṃ? uta tejo-brahma-rūpaṃ brahma eva? kiṃ vā tejo-brahma-kāraṇabhūtā kācit? iti \| prathame kalpe tejo-brahma-nāmnā ekatvāt ajām ekām iti virudhyate \| na ca vācyaṃ tejo-brahma-nāmnā ekatve api trivṛt-karaṇaikatā-pariniriti \| trivṛt-karaṇe api bahutva-avagamāt, imāḥ tisro devatāḥ tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtam ekaikaṃ karavāṇi iti pratyekaṃ trivṛt-karaṇa-upadeśāt \| dvitīyaḥ kalpo vikalpaḥ—kiṃ tejo-brahma-rūpeṇa vidyutaṃ brahmājo’kā? kiṃ vā svarūpeṇa avasthitam avidyutam iti \| prathamaḥ kalpo bahutvāt apapādy eva nirastaḥ \| dvitīyaḥ api lohita-śukla-kṛṣṇam iti virudhyate \| svarūpeṇa avasthitaṃ brahma tejo-brahma-lakṣaṇam iti vaktuṃ api na śakyate \| tṛtīye kalpe ajajā-śabdena tejo-brahmāni nirdiśya taiḥ kāraṇāvasthā-upasthāpanīyā asti iyam \| tataḥ varṇa-samāja-śabdena tejo-brahma-kāraṇāvasthāyāḥ śruti-siddhayā eva abhidhānam \|\| yat punaḥ atraḥ prakṛteḥ aja-śabdena cchāgātvaṃ parikalpanam upadiśyate iti; tat api asaṅgatam, niṣprayojanatvāt \| yathā ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi ityādiṣu brahma-prāpti-upāya-tathā-adhyāpanāya śarīra-indriya-ratha-ādi-rūpaṇaṃ kriyate; yathā ca āditye vasvādīnāṃ bhogyatva-rūpaṇāya madhutva-kalpanaṃ kriyate; tadvat evaṃ prakṛte cchāgātvaṃ parikalpyaṃ kiṃ prayujyate? na kevalam upayoga-abhāva eva, virodhaḥ ca; kṛtsna-jagat-kāraṇabhūtāyāḥ sarvasmin avikalā-saṃbaddhānāṃ sarveṣām eva cetanānāṃ nikhila-sukha-duḥkha-upabhoga-varṇādhānatvāt; acetanāyāḥ atyanta-prajñā-saṃkaraṇa-karaṇam acetana-viśeṣa-kalpatvasya prayojana-anādanena parityāga-ahetubhūtatva-saṃvidhya parityāga-asamarthyaṃ cetana-viśeṣa-śabda-cchāgāsaṃbhavāt adhyāpanāya tadvattva-kalpanaṃ viruddham eva \| ajām ekām ajaḥ hi ekaḥ ajāḥ hi anyāḥ ityatra aja-śabdasya hiṃsā-paryāya-raṅkavaṃ ca na śobhanam \| sarvatra cchāgātvaṃ parikalpyate iti cet, ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate iti viduṣaḥ ātmābhimukha-śruti-parityāgaṃ kurvataḥ anena vākyena api punar api saṃbandha-yogya-cchāgātvaṃ parikalpanam atyanta-viruddham \|\| 10 \|\| iti camasādhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha saṃkhyopasaṅgrahādhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.4.11 \|\|

*na saṃkhyopasaṅgrahāt api nānābhāvāt atirekāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

vājasaneyinaḥ samāmananti yasmin pañca pañca-janā ākāśaḥ ca pratiṣṭhitaḥ \| tam evam manya ātmānaṃ vidvān brahma amṛtaḥ amṛtam \|\| iti \| kim ayaṃ mantraḥ kāpila-tantra-siddha-tattvātiriktaparaḥ; uta na iti saṃdihyate \| kiṃ yuktam? tantra-siddha-tattvātiriktaparaḥ iti \| kutaḥ? pañca-śabda-viśeṣitāt pañca-jana-śabdāt pañcaviṃśati-tattva-pratīteḥ \| evaṃ yuktaṃ bhavati pañca-janāḥ iti samāse samāhāra-viṣayaḥ \| pañcānāṃ janānāṃ samūhaḥ pañca-janāḥ; pañcatvam iti atra \| pañca-janāḥ iti taddhitasya

yat chandasaḥ \| te ca samūhāḥ kati iti apekṣāyāṃ pañcajana-śabda-viśeṣaṇena prathamena pañca-saṃkhyena samūhāḥ pañca iti pratīyante; yathā pañca pañca-pūlya iti \| ataḥ pañca pañcajanāḥ iti pañcaviṃśati-padārtha-vācī te katama iti apekṣāyāṃ mokṣa-adhikāratvāt mumukṣubhiḥ jñātavyatayā smṛti-siddhāḥ prakṛti-ādayaḥ eva jñāyante \| mūla-prakṛtiḥ avikṛtiḥ mahad-ādyāḥ prakṛti-vikṛtayaḥ sapta \| ṣoḍaśakaś ca vikāro na prakṛtir na vikṛtiḥ puruṣaḥ \|\| iti hi kāpilānāṃ prasiddhiḥ \| ataḥ tattva-pañcaviṃśatitva-pratipādana-paraḥ \|\|

iti prāpte pratyucyate—na saṃkhya-upasaṃgrahād api \| pañca pañcajanāḥ iti pañcaviṃśati-saṃkhya-upasaṃgrahād api na tattva-siddhiḥ iti arthaḥ \| kutaḥ? nānā-bhāvāt—evaṃ pañca-saṃkhyayā viśeṣitānāṃ pañcajanānāṃ tattva-siddheḥ pṛthag-bhāvāt \| yasmin pañca pañcajanā ākāśaś ca pratiṣṭhitaḥ iti eteṣāṃ yat-śabda-nirdiṣṭa-brahma-āśrayatayā brahma-ātmakatvaṃ hi pratīyate, tam eva manya ātmānaṃ vidvān brahma amṛtaḥ amṛtam iti asya tam iti paramarśena yat-śabda-nirdiṣṭaṃ brahma upagamyate \| ataḥ tadīyāḥ pṛthag-bhūtāḥ pañcajanā iti na tattva-siddhā ete \| atirekāc ca—tattva-siddhi-arthatve api atra tattva-atirekaḥ api bhavati; yat-śabda-nirdiṣṭaḥ ātmā ākāśaś ca atra atiricyate \| ataḥ taṃ ṣaḍviṃśakam iti āhuḥ iti śruti-siddha-sarva-tattva-abhyabhūtasya īśvaraḥ parama-puruṣaḥ abhyupagamyate \| na saṃkhya-upasaṃgrahād vaiśabdasya pañca pañcajanāḥ iti atra pañcaviṃśati-tatva-niṣpattiḥ eva na saṃbhavati iti abhiprāyaḥ \| kasmāt? pañcaviṃśati-saṃkhyā-samūha-pañcakatvāt \| na hi tattva-siddhānteṣu pañcasu pañca-saṃkhyāṃ tat-saṃkhyā-upaniṣṭhiti-nimitaṃ jātyā asti \| tat ca vācyaṃ—pañca karma-indriyāṇi, pañca jñāna-indriyāṇi, pañca mahābhūtāni, pañca tanmātrāṇi, avaśiṣṭāni pañca ekatra antara-saṃkhyā-niveśa-nimitam na syāt iti; ākāśasya pṛthag-nirdeśena, pañcaviṃśati-saṃkhyā-mahābhūta-samūha-atirekaḥ \| ataḥ pañcajanāḥ iti ayaṃ samāso na samāhāra-viṣayaḥ; ayaṃ tu dik-saṃkhye saṃjñāyām iti saṃjñā-viṣayaḥ; anyathā pañcajanā iti liṅga-vyatyayaś ca \| pañcajanā nāma kecit santi; te ca pañca-saṃkhyayā viśeṣyante, pañca pañcajanāḥ iti; sat sarvaṃ ca iti evam \|\| 11 \|\|

ke punaḥ te pañcajanā iti ata āha—

## \|\| 1.4.12 \|\|

*prāṇa-ādayo vākya-śeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

prāṇasya prāṇam uta cakṣuṣaḥ cakṣuḥ uta śrotrasya śrotram annasya manaso ye mano viduḥ iti vākya-śeṣād brahma-āśrayāḥ prāṇa-ādayaḥ eva pañca pañcajanāḥ iti vijñāyante \|\| 12 \|\|

atha syāt—kāṇvānāṃ mādhyandinānāṃ ca yasmin pañca pañcajanāḥ iti ayaṃ mantra-

rasmātaḥ; prāṇasya prāṇam iti-ādi-vākya-madhye kārya-nāma-sattya pāṭho na vidyate; teṣāṃ pañca pañca-janāḥ prāṇādaya iti na śakyaṃ vaktuṃ smṛtiḥ; atra eva uttaram—

## \|\| 1.4.13 \|\|

*jyotiṣā ekeṣām asati anne*

**Комментарий:**

ekeṣāṃ—kāṇvānāṃ pāṭhe asati anne jyotiṣā pañca-janāḥ indriyāṇi iti jñāyante, teṣāṃ vākya-śeṣaḥ pradarśanārthaḥ \| evam uktaṃ bhavati—yasmin pañca pañca-janāḥ iti asmin pūrvasmin mantre taṃ devā jyotiṣāṃ jyotir āyur hopāsate tāmṛtam iti jyotiṣāṃ jyotir-jñena brahmaṇi abhidhīyamāne brahmādhīnatva-kāryāṇi kānicit jyotīṃṣi pratīyante; tāni ca viṣayāṇāṃ prakāśakatvāt indriyāṇi iti yasmin pañca pañca-janāḥ iti adhikṛta-viśeṣa-nirdeśena avagamyate iti \| prāṇasya iti prāṇa-śabdena sparśana-indriyaṃ gṛhyate, vāyu-sambandhitvāt sparśana-indriyasya; mukhya-prāṇasya jyotiḥ-śabdena pradarśanāyogāt \| cakṣuṣa iti cakṣur-indriyam, śrotrasya iti śrotra-indriyam, akṣīya iti ghrāṇa-rasanayoḥ etat netroṇopādānam, akṣa-śabdena itarat pṛthivī-sambandhitvāt ghrāṇa-indriyam etena gṛhyate; adyate anena iti annam iti rasana-indriyam api gṛhyate \| manasa iti manaḥ \| prāṇa-rasanayoḥ etat netroṇopādānam iti pañcatvam aviroddhum \| prakāśakāni manaḥ-paryantāni indriyāṇi pañca-jana-śabda-nirdiṣṭāni; tad-virodhāya prāṇa-rasanayoḥ etat netroṇopādānam \| tad evam yasmin pañca pañca-janā ākāśaś ca pratiṣṭhitaḥ iti pañca-jana-śabda-nirdiṣṭāni indriyāṇi ākāśa-śabda-pradarśitāni mahā-bhūtāni ca brahmaṇi pratiṣṭhitāni iti sarvātmatvānāṃ brahma-śabda-samanvaya-pratipādanāya tat-saṅkhyopasaṅgraha-siddhānta-sūtram \| ataḥ sarvatra vedānte saṅkhyopasaṅgrāhe lakṣaṇāve vā na kāpila-tantra-siddha-tattvātireka iti siddham \|\| 13 \|\| iti saṅkhyopasaṅgrahādhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha kāraṇatvādhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.4.14 \|\|

*kāraṇatvena ca ākāśādiṣu yathā-vyapadiṣṭokteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

punaḥ pradhāna-kāraṇa-vādī pratyavatiṣṭhate—na vedānteṣu ekasmat sṛṣṭiratnātmyata iti jagato brahma-eka-kāraṇatvaṃ na yujyate vaktuṃ \| tathā hi sat eva somya idam agre āsīt iti sat-pūrvikā sṛṣṭiḥ āmnāyate; ātmā idam eka āsīt iti ātma-sat-pūrvikā ca; anyatra asat eva idam agre āsīt tat sat āsīt tat sambhavat iti ca \| ato vedānteṣu sṛṣṭi-utpatti-sthiteḥ jagato brahma-eka-kāraṇatvaṃ na niśceṣu śakyam; prastuta-pradhāna-kāraṇatvam eva niśceṣu śakyate, tadā idaṃ tarhi avyākṛtam āsīt iti avyākṛte pradhāne jagat-pralayam abhidhāya tat nāma-rūpābhyām eva vyākriyata iti avyākṛtāt eva jagat-sṛṣṭy-abhidhīyate \| avyākṛtaṃ hi avyaktam, nāma-rūpābhyāṃ na vyākriyata—na vyajyata ity arthaḥ \| athavākta

pradhānam eva \| asya ca svarūpa-nirūpaṇena pariṇāma-āspadatvena ca jagat-kāraṇatvādi-vāraṇato sat-asat-śabdau brahma-upāsana-nimittaṃ virodhyete \| evam anya-grahaṇa-kāraṇatve nirṇīte sati īkṣaṇa-anvayaḥ kāraṇatā-śrutiḥ ca mukhya-abhidhānena yojitavyāḥ \| brahma-akṣara-śabdau api bṛhatva-acchāparicchidyatvaṃ pradhāne eva vartete \| ataḥ sṛṣṭi-upakrama-śrutiḥ pradhānam eva jagat-kāraṇaṃ vedānta-tattvaṃ pratipādayate \|\| iti prāpte pracakṣmahe—kāraṇatvena ca ākāśādibhu yathā-vyapadiṣṭa-ukteḥ \| cara-acarasya sarvajñatva-bandharāntara-ātmā-rūpa-avirasthā-nikhila-doṣa-anadhyaparastamāḍbrahmakṣaṇa eva jagat-udbhavata iti nirṇetuṃ śakyate \| kutaḥ? ākāśādibhu kāraṇatvena yathā-vyapadiṣṭasya ukteḥ;—sarvajñatvādi-viśiṣṭatvena janmādyasya yataḥ iti evam-ādibhu pratipāditaṃ brahma yathā-vyapadiṣṭam iti ucyate, tasya eva kārya-ākārādibhu kāraṇatvena ukteḥ tasya eva etasmāt ātman ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ tatajo’gniḥ iti ādibhu sarvaṃ brahma eva kāraṇatvena ucyate \| tathā hi satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma saḥ aśnute sarvān kāmān saha brahmaṇā vipaścitā iti prakṛtaṃ vipaścit eva brahma tasmāt vā etasmāt iti parāmṛśyate \| tasmin dṛṣṭaṃ bahu syām iti nirīkṣya sarvaṃ brahma eva tatejo’sṛjata iti parāmṛśyate \| evaṃ sarvatra sūcayitavyam \| ataḥ brahma kāraṇaṃ jagat iti niścīyate \|\| 14 \|\| nanu asat vā idam agre āsīt iti atra eva kāraṇatvena vyapadiśyamānaṃ tarka-saṃgati sarvajñasya satyasya brahmaṇa eva kāraṇatvaṃ niścīyate iti ataḥ āha—

## \|\| 1.4.15 \|\|

*samākarṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

asat vā idam agre āsīt iti atra api vipaścit-cara-ānanda-rūpaṃ satya-saṅkalpaṃ brahma eva samākṛṣyate \| katham? tanmāhā etasmāt vijñānamayāt, anyaḥ antara ātmā ānandamayaḥ saḥ akāmayata, bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti... idaṃ sarvam asṛjata, yat idaṃ kiñca, tat sṛṣṭvā, tat eva anuprāviśat, tat anupraviśya, sat ca tyat ca abhavat iti ādinā brāhmaṇena ānandamayaṃ brahma satya-saṅkalpaṃ sarvasya sraṣṭṛ sarva-anupraveśena sarva-ātmasu bhūma abhidhāya tat eva eṣa ślokaḥ bhavati iti uttarasya asya sarvasya sākṣitvena dṛśyataḥ ayaṃ ślokaḥ asat vā idam agre āsīt iti \| tathā uttarasya bhīṣā asmāt vātaḥ pavate iti ādinā tat eva brahma samākṛṣya pūrvasya prakāśitatvāt nirasta-nikhila-ānandatvādi abhidhīyate \| ataḥ ayaṃ mantraḥ tad-viṣaya eva \| tadānīṃ nāma-rūpa-vibhāga-abhāvena tattva-abhidheyasya astitva-abhāvāt iti ucyate vā sat-asat-śabdena ucyate \| asat vā idam agre āsīt iti atra api evam nirvāhaḥ \| yuktaṃ tat ha idam tarhi avyākṛtam āsīt iti pradhānam eva jagat-kāraṇatvena abhidhīyate iti na iti ucyate, tatra api avyākṛta-śabdena avyākṛta-nāma-rūpaṃ brahma eva abhidhīyate; sa eṣa iha praviṣṭaḥ ānakhāgrebhyaḥ paśyan cakṣuḥ śṛṇvan śrotraṃ manvānaḥ manaḥ ātmā iti eva upāsīta iti atra, sa eṣaḥ iti tat-śabdena avyākṛta-śabda-abhihitasya ānataḥ praviśya praśāsitṛtvena anukampati, tasmṛtyā, tam devān anupraviśat anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe

vyākarttāṇīti ghaṭadussnairāsya parasya brahmaṇaḥ kāryānupaveśāt asrūpākāraraṇasiddheś ca \| antaḥ praviṣṭaśāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā iti niyamanārthatvāt anupaveśasya pradhānam api acetanatvād eva rūpeṇa upaveśo na saṃbhavati \| ataḥ adhyākṛtam—adhyākartāraṃ brahma hatāmahāpāthyāṃ vyāpikayā iti tad eva adhimutanāmarūpaṃ brahma sarvaṃ satyamūlaṃ svena eva vibhakta-nāmarūpaṃ svayam eva vyākriyata iti ucyate \| evaṃ ca sati īkṣaṇādayo mukhyā eva bhavanti \| brahmātmaśabdād api niraticāraguharaciniyamanārthyādhipatibhāvena pradhāne na kathañcid upapadyate \| ato brahmaika-kāraṇaṃ jagad iti sthitam \|\| 14 \|\| iti kāraṇatvādhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha jagadācitvādhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.4.16 \|\|

*jagadācitvāt*

**Комментарий:**

punar api sāṃkhyaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—yady api vedānta-vākyāni cetanaṃ jagat-kāraṇatvena pratipādayanti; tathā api tanniṣiddha-pradhāna-puruṣātiriktaṃ vastu jagat-kāraṇaṃ vedayatyā na tebhyaḥ pratīyate \| tathā hi—bhoktāram eva puruṣaṃ kāraṇaṃ veda-vākyādhīyate kovitikito bālākhyajātaśatru-saṃvāde yaccha te bravāṇi ity upakramya yo vai bālaka eteṣāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ kartā yasya (ca) vai etat karma sa vai veditavyaḥ iti \| upakrame vaktavyatayā bālākinopāsitaṃ brahmajātane tasya eva upāsitṛguṇā sa vai veditavyaḥ iti brahma upadiśyate \| asya vai etakarma iti karma-saṃbandhāt prakṛtyadhyakṣo bhoktā puruṣo veditavyatayā upadiśiṣṭo brahma iti niścīyate, na athāntarasya, tasya karma-saṃbandhānupagamāt \| karma eva puṇyāpuṇya-lakṣaṇaṃ kṣetrajñasya eva saṃbhavati \| na ca vācyam, kriyata iti karma iti vyutpattyā pratyakṣādi-samāṇopasthāpitaṃ jagad etat karma iti nirdiśyate, yasya etat kṛtsnaṃ jagat-karma, sa veditavya iti kṣetrajña-vyatiriktam eva pratīyate iti; yo vai bālaka eteṣāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ kartā yasya vai etakarma iti pṛṣṭha-nirdeśād vyarthā iti, karma-saṃbandhasya ca lokavedyayoḥ puruṣa-śabdasya eva karmaṇi prasiddheḥ; tatas tu śaukra-karma-nirmitāyājñāmūrtaparivaiṣaṃ puruṣaṇāṃ kartā iti ca bhoktṛtvena eva upapadyate \| tad ayyam arthaḥ—eṣām āditya-maṇḍalādhikaraṇānāṃ kṣetrajña-bhogopayogikaraṇabhūtānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ yaḥ kāraṇabhūtaḥ; etat-kāraṇabhāvahetubhūtaṃ puṇyāpuṇya-lakṣaṇaṃ ca karma yasya, sa vai veditavyaḥ—tat kartṛtvaṃ prakṛtiviviktam veditavyam—iti \| tathosrasya sautā ha mumūḥ puruṣam iyutuḥ...tāṃ yathā cikṣepa iti supta-puruṣa-gamanaiḥ indriyāṇi ity apavānād api ca bhoktuḥ pratipādana eva liṅgāni \| tathā upariṣṭād api bhokteḥ pratipādyate tad yathā śreṣṭhī svaiḥ bhuṅkte yathā vā svāṅkṣibhiḥ bhuṅkte evam eva prajñātmanā etarāmāṇi bhuṅkte evam etad ātmānaṃ enaṃ bhuñjanti iti \| tathā tvaṃ ca etatāloke puruṣo ’śayiṣy eva vā etad abhūtam etad āgāt iti pṛṣṭam arthaṃ jānate tasmai svapn̄e vā jātaśatrur uvāca hita nāma

hṛdayasya nāḍyaḥ.... tāsu tadā bhavati yadā suptaḥ svapnaṃ na kaṃcana paśyati atha asmin prāṇaḥ eva ekadhā bhavati tadā enaṃ vāk sarvaiḥ nāmabhiḥ saha apyeti manaḥ sarvaiḥ dhyānaiḥ saha apyeti sa yadā pratibudhyate yathā agneḥ jvalataḥ sarvā diśaḥ vipraliṅgāḥ visphuliṅgāḥ vipratiṣṭheran evam eva etasmāt ātmanaḥ prāṇāḥ yathā-yatanaṃ vipratiṣṭhante prāṇebhyaḥ devāḥ devebhyaḥ lokāḥ iti \| mukhya-prāṇasya svataḥ suṣupti-jāgarita-avasthāḥ ca vartamāne vāk-ādi-karaṇa-grāma-apītam-sthānām eva jīvātmanaḥ tu asya asmin prāṇaḥ ekadhā bhavati iti uktānām \| asmin jīvātmani prāṇa-mukhāni-vandhanānāṃ prāṇa-śabdaḥ; sa yadā pratibudhyate iti prāṇa-śabda-anirdiṣṭasya prabodha-śravaṇāt; mukhya-prāṇasya aiśvaryasya ca suṣupti-prabodhayor asaṃbhavāt \| athavā asmin prāṇe iti vyadhikaraṇe saptamyau; asmin ātmani vartamāne prāṇaḥ ekadhā bhavati vāk-ādi-karaṇa-grāmaḥ iti \| prāṇa-śabdasya mukhya-prāṇa-paratve api jīvaḥ eva asmin prakaraṇe pratipādyate, svataḥ prāṇasya jīva-upakaratvāt \| ataḥ bālya-daya-upakrāntaṃ brahma pucchaṃ prati tad-vya tirikta-śarīra-siddhiḥ \| kāraṇatvāt na kṣaṇatvāt ca tatra arthāḥ asmin eva vākya-vṛnde iti etat-siddhitaṃ pradhānam eva nirākaraṇam \|\|

iti prāpte pracakṣmahe—jagad-adhitvāt \| atra puṇya-apuṇya-paratvaḥ śuddhaḥ kṣetrajñatvābhimataḥ prakṛti-viṣaya-dhyāsena tat-pariṇāma-hetu-bhūtaḥ puruṣaḥ na abhidhīyate; api tu nirasta-samasta-avidyā-ādi-doṣa-gandhaḥ ananta-vibhūtikatitvān tādṛśasya cakṣurāṇāṃ guṇa-gaṇa-tirikta-vikāra-jagat-kāraṇa-bhūtaḥ puruṣottamaḥ abhidhīyate \| kutaḥ ? asya vai tat karma iti etena śabditasya karma-śabdasya param-puruṣa-kārya-bhūta-jagad-adhitvāt \| etat-śabdaḥ hi artha-karaṇa-ādi-niḥsṛṣṭaṃ vicitritatva-viśeṣeṇa pratyakṣa-ādi-māṇa-upasthāpita-nikhila-cid-acin-miśra-jagad-vipayaḥ \| na ca puṇya-apuṇya-lakṣaṇaṃ kasyacid karma-śabdam iddheyam, brahma te bravāṇi iti upakrama brahma eva enaṃ vakṣyāmi iti vicchānām-ādibhyaḥ ca sāmarthikaraṇānāṃ puruṣāt paraṃ brahma eva etena yūyaṃ vai khalu mā saṃvādayiṣṭhāḥ iti tasmāt dharma-vinirmokṣa tena aviviktaṃ brahma nānāyājatvādīyuṣaṃ vākyam avataritaṃ yaṃ vai bālake iti-ādi \| puṇya-apuṇya-lakṣaṇa-karma-saṃbandhinam āditya-ādi-adhikaraṇa-arasattva-sajātīyaḥ ca puruṣaḥ tena eva vihitā iti tad-vadita-puruṣa-viśeṣa-aparaparayoṃ karma-śabdaḥ na puṇya-apuṇya-mātra-vācī; kriyā-mātra-vācī vā; api tu kṛtsnasya jagataḥ kāryatva-vācī \| evam eva khalu abhihitatārtha upadiṣṭaḥ bhavati \| mukhyasya karma-saṃbandhy api-lakṣitasya abhāva-vikāra-śūnyasya ajātasya vaidikatva-pradeśaḥ ca lakṣaṇam; karma-saṃbandha-mātrasya eva vaidikatva-ārūḍha-lakṣaṇatvāt asya karma sa vaidikatvaṃ hṛti etāvataiva caritārthe; asya vai tat karma iti etat-śabda-vaiparyyaṃ ca \| yaḥ yaḥ eva evaṃ kartā asya vai tat karma iti pṛṣṭa-nideśasya ca ayam abhiprāyaḥ—ye tvayā brahma eva enaṃ nirdiṣṭāḥ puruṣāḥ, teṣāṃ yaḥ kartā, te prakārya-bhūtāḥ; kiṃ vividhyā abhidhīyate, kṛtsnasya jagadhasya kāryam, utkṛṣṭā apakṛṣṭāḥ ca tanā acetanāḥ ca sarve padārthāḥ prakāra-bhūte tulyāḥ; sa parasma-kāraṇa-bhūtaḥ puruṣaḥ teṣāṃ vaidikatvyaḥ—iti \| jagad-utpatteḥ jīva-kamāni-vaśatve api na jīvasya-

bhautya-bhoga-karaṇa-pādaiḥ svayam upādakaḥ, api tu svakarma-anuguṇa-īśvara-prasūtaiḥ sarvaṃ bhuṅkte; ato na tasya puruṣārtha iti kartṛtvam upapadyate \| ataḥ sarva-vedānta-vikhyātaṃ parama-kāruṇatayā prasiddhaṃ paraṃ brahma eva ca atra vedhitavyatvena upadiśyate \|\|16\|\|

## \|\| 1.4.17 \|\|

*jīva-mukhya-prāṇa-liṅgāt na iti cet na upāsā-traividhyāt āśritatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

atha yad uktam, jīva-villiṅga-mukhya-prāṇayoḥ devatānāṃ liṅgāt ubhayasmikaraṇe pratīyate, na paramātmeti; tad api ayuktam—tasya nivāhaḥ pradarśana-vidyāyām abhihitaḥ \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—pratyakra-karma-upasaṃhāra-paryālocanayā brahma-paraṃ vākyam iti niścitam, tatra anya-liṅgānāṃ tad-anurodhena varṇanīyatvāt iti tatra pratipāditam \| ajāyamāno bahudhā te babāṇi iti brahma-upabhogimaḥ; madhye ca asya vedat karmaṇi iti nirdeśo na puruṣa-mātra-arthe, api tu nikhila-jagad-eka-kāraṇaṃ brahma vettham \| upasaṃhāre ca sarvāṇi padanīyasya ānandaḥ ’pahaty sarveṣāṃ ca bhūtānāṃ śreṣṭhaṃ svārājyam ādhipatyaṃ paryaiti yaḥ evaṃ veda iti brahma-upāsanā-ekakāṃ sarva-pāpa-apahati-vūrvaṃ svārājyaṃ ca phalaṃ śrutam; ataḥ asya vākyasya brahma-paratva-niścayena jīva-mukhya-prāṇa-liṅgāni api tat-paratayā varṇanīyāni iti \| śrātattane hi āsāṃ traividhyena jīva-mukhya-prāṇa-liṅgānāṃ brahma-paratva-yuktam; atrāpi arthe asmiṃtrāṇāṃ evaṃ kathā bhavati iti sāmānādhikaraṇya-sambhave vaiyadhikaraṇya-samāśrayaṇa-ayogāt brahmaṇaḥ eva prāṇa-ātmada-prayoga-abhidhānena ca prāṇa-śarīraka-brahma-upāsanatāṃ prāṇa-tad-uttīrṇaṃ liṅgaṃ yujyate \|\|17\|\|

jīva-liṅgānāṃ punaḥ kathaṃ brahma-paratvam iti atra āha—

## \|\| 1.4.18 \|\|

*anyārthaṃ tu jaiminiḥ praśna-vyākhyānābhyām api ca evam eke*

**Комментарий:**

tac-chabdaḥ jīvaḥ tad-uttīrṇena vākyasya tat-paratva-saṃbhavāt nyāya-dṛṣṭyā \| anyārthaṃ jīvaḥ tad-uttīrṇena jīva-atirikta-brahma-svarūpa-pratipādana-artham iti jaiminiḥ ācāryaḥ manyate \| kutaḥ? praśna-vyākhyānābhyām; praśnāt tāvat ko ha eva antaṃ puruṣaḥ yaṇḍiṣṭhādisu sutasya prabuddha-prāṇasya eva prāṇānām anumiśrāmantrāṇa-śravaṇāt biṣīrātīty apanandyaṃ prāṇādi-yadvatiriktam jīvaṃ pratibodhya punaḥ jīva-ādi-atirikta-brahma-pratibodhana-paraḥ dṛśyate \| etad eva abālāke pu rurodaṣṭram eva vā etad abhūt etad agāt iti \| vyākhyānām api etad ghumatsu antaṃ na paśyeta etad yathā asmiṃ prāṇam eva ekadhā bhavati etasmāt ātmanaḥ prāṇāḥ yathā-ayatanaṃ vipratiṣṭhante prāṇebhyaḥ devāḥ devebhyaḥ lokāḥ iti jīva-ādi-atītarabhūta-paramātma-parā eva \| supuṣyasya hi jīvasya yaṣṭyopitasya jāgaritatvavadan daśā-saṃbandhi-vicitra-duḥkhaḥ svānuṣbhava-kālūṣya-rahitaṃ saṃsārasya svasya anāpattiḥ, punaḥ utthāya yathā mṛtyogaṃ nigamam, saḥ ayaṃ paramātmā \| tathā hi satā saumya tadā saṃpannaḥ bhavati prājñena ātmanā saṃpariṣvaktaḥ na bāhyaṃ kiñcana veda na antaram iti suṣupta-sthāna-gatatayā prasiddhaḥ jīva-ādi-atītarabhūtaḥ prājñaḥ

paramātmā \| ataḥ praśna-prativacanābhyāṃ jīva-saṃjñitena eva jīva-avasthita-sūtra-paramātma-pratipādanārtham iti niścīyate \| yad uktaṃ praśna-vyākhyāne jīva-kare, suṣupti-sthānaṃ ca nāḍyaḥ eva, karaṇa-grāmasya prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭe jīva eva ekadhā bhavati iti, tad ayuktam, nāḍīnāṃ svapana-sthānatvāt, uttareṣāṃ brahmaṇa eva suṣupti-sthānatvāt, prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭe brahmaṇi eva jīvasya tad-upakrama-bhūta-vāg-ādi-karaṇa-grāmasya ca ekatā-āpatti-vibhāvakatvāt \| api ca evam eke—vājasaneyinaḥ asmin eva bālāki-jātaśatru-saṃvāde suṣupta-vijñāna-maryādvena tad-āśraya-bhūtaṃ paramātmānam amananti yatra eṣa vijñānamayaḥ puruṣaḥ kva eṣa tadā abhūt kutaḥ etad āgāt eṣa eva etasmin ākāśe prāṇānāṃ vijñānena vijñānam ādāya ya eṣaḥ antar-hṛdaya ākāśaḥ tasmin śete iti \| ākāśa-śabdasya ca paramātmani prasiddhaḥ daharaḥ asmin antar-ākāśaḥ iti \| ataḥ atra jīva-saṃjñitena tasmād vyatirikta-sūtrasya prānasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ pratipādanārtham iti avagamyate \| tasmād asmin vākye puruṣārtha-antara-bhūtasya nikhila-jagad-kāraṇasya parasya eva brahmaṇo veditavyatayā abhidhānāt tad-vyatiriktasya puruṣasya tad-vidhi-ratasya vā pradhānasya kāraṇatvaṃ sarva-cit-śruti vedānte pratīyata iti siddham \|\|17\|\| iti jagad-vācitva-adhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|

**atha vākya-anvaya-adhikaraṇam \|\|6\|\|**

## \|\| 1.4.19 \|\|

*vākyānvayāt*

**Комментарий:**
