atra api kāpila-tantra-siddha-puruṣatva-vedana-paraṃ vākyaṃ kvacid dṛśyate iti tad-uktaḥ īśvaraḥ nāma na kaścit asaṃsārī ca iti āśaṅkya nirākriyate \| bṛhad-āraṇyake maitreyī-brāhmaṇe śrūyate na vā are patyuḥ kāmāya patiḥ priyaḥ bhavati iti ādi asya na vā are sarvasya kāmāya sarvaṃ priyaṃ bhavati ātmanas tu kāmāya sarvaṃ priyaṃ bhavati ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ śrotavyaḥ mantavyaḥ nididhyāsitavyaḥ; maitreyi ātmani khalu are dṛṣṭe śrute mate vijñāte idaṃ sarvaṃ viditam iti \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim asmin vākye brahma-ādi-upadiśyamānatvena siddhaḥ puruṣa eva? athavā sarva-antaratayā anubhūta-sarveśvaraḥ? iti \| kiṃ yuktam? puruṣaḥ iti \| kutaḥ? ādi-madhya-avasāna-anu puruṣasya eva pratīteḥ \| upakrame tāvat pati-jāyā-putra-vitta-paśu-ādi-priya-yogāt jīva-ātmanā eva pratīyate; madhye api vijñātāram are kena vijānīyāt iti sa eva jñātā kṣetrajñaḥ eva pratīyate, na īśvaraḥ \| ataḥ tantra-siddha-puruṣa-pratipādana-param idaṃ vākyam iti niścīyate \| nanu amṛtatvasya tu na āśā asti vittena iti upakramya amṛtatva-prāpti-upāya-upadeśa-param idaṃ vākyam iti avagamyate \| tarhi arthaṃ puruṣa-pratipādanam asyā vākyasya? tad ucyate—atra eva hi atra puruṣa-pratipādanam \|

tan-nayāmbud-bhrad-hiyā-siddhi-yuktaṃ puruṣa-vacanam upāyārthasya eva jñānān-medā-hetutvena ucyate \| ato jīvātmanaḥ prakṛti-viyuktaḥ svarūpa-mahā-bhūtatvād ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ ity-ādiṣu upadiśyate \| sarvopāsana-mānī prakṛti-viyuktaṃ svarūpam eka-rūpam iti prakṛti-viyukta-svātma-yāthātmya-vijñānena sarvaṃ eva asmāno viditaṃ bhavatīti ātma-vijñānena sarva-vijñānam upapannam; deva-ādhi-sthāvara-anteṣu sarveṣu kṣetrajñātmasu ekasya jñānaika-ratvāt idaṃ sarvaṃ yad ayam ātmā iti ekātmya-upadeśaḥ; deva-ādhy-ākārāṇām anātmakāratvāt sarvaṃ taṃ parādāt ity-ādinā anyatva-niṣedhaś ca; atra hi dvaitam iva bhavatīti ca nānā-darśana-niṣedhena ekatvasya hi ātmani deva-ādiṣu pratiparyāṇa-bhedena nānātvam mithyā iti ucyate; tasya ha vā etasya mahato bhūtasya niśśvasitam eva ṛg-vedeḥ ity-ādhy api prakṛtādhi-puruṣa-bhūtena puruṣa-nimittatvāj jagad-udbhavatvena rūpyate \| evam abhinna-vākye puruṣa-pare niścite sati tad-ekākhyātitve devānām antar-ātma-siddhiṃ puruṣam etābhi dadhātīti tad-adhīnatā prakṛteś ca jagad-upādānam u, na īśvaraḥ iti \|\| evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—vākya-anvayāt iti \| sarveśvara eva abhinna-vākye pratīyate \| kutaḥ ? evam eva hi vākya-avayavānām anonya-anvaya-samanjaso bhavati \| amṛtatvasya tu na āśā asti vittena iti yājñavalkyena abhihite yena ahaṃ na amṛtā syāṃ kim ahaṃ tena kuryāṃ yad eva bhagavān veda tad eva me brūhi iti amṛtatvānupāyayā vittyā dārāgamutva-phala-puruṣam eva prārthyamānāyai maitreyeyāṃ tad-upāyayā draṣṭavyena upadiśyamāna ātmā paramātmā eva, sa eva viditvā ati mṛtyum eti tam eva vidvān amṛta iha bhavati na anyaḥ panthāḥ iti avibhinnavṛttatvasya parama-puruṣa-vedanaika-upāyatvayā pratipādanāt \| parama-puruṣa-viśrūti-mṛtasya śrāddhu-rasa-manantara-svarūpa-yāthātmya-paryanta-sādhana-paramu-puruṣa-vedanopayogitayā jñānatavyam; na svata eva upāyatvena \| ato ’tra paramātmā eva amṛtatvopāyayā draṣṭavyaḥ ity-ādinā upadiśyate \| tathā tasya ha vā etasya mahato bhūtasya niśśvasitam itad-vedeḥ ity-ādinā kṛtsnasya jagataḥ kāraṇatva-mukham ānaṃ parama-puruṣād anyasya karma-vayatrasya muktasya nityāṇārasya ca puruṣa-mātrasya na saṃbhavati \| tathā ātmano vā are darśanena ity-ādinā eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñānam abhidhīyamānaṃ sarva-nirmite paramātmani eva avakalpate \| yatra tu idam arūpatvād hāra-nāmikāna-vijñānena sarvārtha-vijñānam ucyata iti; tat sulabhu, acetana-prapañca-jñāna-sabhāvena sarva-vijñāna-saṃbhavāt \| pratina-upapādanāya ca idaṃ brahma idaṃ kṣatram iti upakramya idaṃ sarvaṃ yad ayam ātmā iti pratyakṣādi-siddhaṃ cid-acin-miśraṃ prapañcam idam iti nirdiśya, etad-yāthātmya-ekāntya-upadeśāś ca paramātmani eva upapadyate \| na hi idaṃ-śabda-vācyaṃ cid-acin-miśraṃ jagat puruṣeṇa avidita-saṃsṛṣṭhena rahi-yuktena svakena vā dhayiṣyate vaiyadhy-upagacchati \| ata eva sarvaṃ taṃ parādāt anyatra ātmanaḥ sarvaṃ veda iti vyatiriktatvena sarva-veda-nindā ca; tathā prathamo ca maitreyī-brāhmaṇe mahā-bhūtam anantam apāram iti śrutā mahattvādayo guṇāḥ paramātmani eva saṃbhavanti \| atas tam eva atra pratipādyate \|\|

yat uktaṃ pratijñāyā-putra-vitta-paśv-ādi-viṣayā-vināśe jīvātmani upakrame tad-vaidhurya-thā pratipādanāt siddhyam eva idaṃ vākyam iti; tat ayuktam, ātmanaḥ tu kāmāya iti asmād eva jīvātma-saṃśabdane tasya ātmā vā are draṣṭavya iti anena anvaya-prasaṅgāt, ātmā vā are draṣṭavya iti asmin na u brahma-vyata-upayogitayā ātmanaḥ tu kāmāya iti avadhṛtam iti pratīyate \| ātmanaḥ tu kāmāya—ātmanaḥ kāma-saṃbandhe; kāmyata iti kāmaḥ; ātmani iṣṭa-saṃbandha iti yāvat \| na ca jīvātmani iṣṭa-saṃbandhe paty-ādayaḥ priyā bhavanti iti ukte sati tasya jīvasya svargāpavarga-ceṣṭyaṃ bhavati \| priyam eva hi abhiceṣṭavyam; nanu priyaṃ prati śeṣiṇa priyaviyuktaṃ svargāpam \| yasmāt ātmani iṣṭa-saṃbandhe paty-ādayaḥ priyā bhavanti, tasmāt paty-ādi priyaṃ parityajya tadīya-uttamātma-svarūpam anveṣṭavyam iti asaṅgataṃ bhavati \| prakṛta na paty-ādi-śeṣatayā paty-ādīnāṃ priyatvaṃ; api tu ātman-śeṣatayā paty-ādīnāṃ priyatvam iti uktaṃ svargīyatvā tu tu upapadeśāt arṣuḥ \| ātmanaḥ tu kāmāya sarvaṃ priyaṃ bhavati iti asya pareṇa anvaye vākya-bhedaḥ prasaṅgate \| atha upāsana-mātre’pi vākya-bhede pūrva-vākyasya na kiñcit-prayojanaṃ dṛśyate \| ataḥ paty-ādi sarvaṃ priyaṃ parityajya ātmani eva abhiceṣṭavyaṃ yathā pratīyate, tathā vākyārtha-varṇanīyaḥ \| śoṣyam ucyate—amṛtatvasya tu nāśaḥ asti vittena iti vitt-ādīnāṃ nityānirdiśaniratīyānandaḥ-svarūpāmṛtatvāsamucaya-thā-mukhyā vittu-pratijñāy-ādīnāṃ sāhitrya-daḥkha-bhiśaṅkā-viniścayitvadasaṃbhāvamānaṃ na paty-ādi-svarūpa-prayuktam, api tu niratiśayānanda-svabhāva-paramātma-prayuktam \| ataḥ yaḥ eva svayaṃ niratiśayānanda-satsaṃbhogī priyaḥ ca īśāpadaṃ māyākhyāti, sa paramātmā eva draṣṭavya iti upadiśyate \|\|

tat ayam arthaḥ—na vā are patyuḥ kāmāya patiḥ priyaḥ bhavati—na hi pati-jñāyā-putra-vitt-ādayaḥ mat-prayojanāya aham asya priyasthām iti svātma-dṛḍhatayā priyā bhavanti; api tu ātmanaḥ kāmāya—paramātmanaḥ tad-ārādhaka-priyam atilambhana-hetutvena teṣāṃ iti arthaḥ \| paramātmā hi karmabhiḥ ārādhitaḥ san saṃkalpitān pratinivṛtteśa-kāla-svarūpa-pariṇāma-sādhakāṃs tad anarhantuṃ priyatvam āpādayati eṣaḥ hi eva ānandayāti iti śruteḥ \| na tu tattvaṃ tu svarūpeṇa priyam apriyaṃ vā \| yathoktaṃ—tad eva prītaye bhūtvā punaḥ duḥkhāya jāyate \| tad eva kopāya yataḥ prasādāya ca jāyate \|\| tasmāt duḥkhātmakaṃ na asti na ca...sukhātmakam \|\| iti \|\|

ātmanaḥ tu kāmāya iti asya jīvātma-paratve’pi ātmā vā are draṣṭavya iti tu paramātma-viṣayam eva \| tatra ayam arthaḥ—paramātma-prayojanābhimukhyaṃ saṃbhavet tat-para-darśinaḥ paty-ādayaḥ priyaveśan na upādeyante, api tu adṛṣṭa-saṃbandhe svataṃtreṇa sva-prayatnena upādīyante; tasmāt ca eva asmin niṣpādita-nirdeśitātiśaya-priyaḥ paramātmā; sa eva hi draṣṭavyaḥ; na duḥkha-bhiśātmakasukha-duḥkhaḥ ca sarvaḥ parāyatta-sattā-svarūpa-bhāvaḥ pati-jāyā-putra-vitt-ādayaḥ viṣayāḥ iti \| asmiṃs tu prakaraṇe

jīvātmasvaviśiṣṭe na api paramātmani eva vidhānāt ātmānam eva kāmāya, ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ iti pūrvokta-prakriyopanyāsa-ātma-śabda-āvedya-viṣayo \|\|19\|\|

matāntareṇa api jīvābhedena paramātma-vidhānopapādanāya āha—

## \|\| 1.4.20 \|\|

*pratijñā-siddheḥ liṅgam āśmarathyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñā-siddhy-arthaṃ liṅgaṃ, prājīvātmādi-śabdaiḥ paramātmanaḥ abhidhānam iti āśmarathya ācāryo manyate sma \| yadi ayaṃ jīvaḥ paramātma-kāryatayā paramātmā eva na bhavet, tadā tad-vyatiriktatayā paramātma-vijñāna-jñāte etad-vijñānaṃ na setsyati \| ātmā vā idam eka eva agre āsīt iti prāk sṛṣṭeḥ ekatvāvadhāraṇāt, yathā su-dīptāt pāvakāt visphuliṅgāḥ sahasraśaḥ prabhavante sa-rūpāḥ \| tathā akṣarāt vividhāḥ somya bhāvāḥ prajāyante tatra ca eva api yanti \|\| iti-ādiṣu jīvānām utpatti-pralaya-anasmṛteḥ ca lakṣaṇayā jīvānāṃ brahma-kāryatvena brahmaṇi apṛthaktvam avagamyate \| ataḥ jīvābhedena paramātmābhidhānam iti \|\|20\|\|

## \|\| 1.4.21 \|\|

*utkramiṣyata evaṃ-bhāvāt iti auḍulomiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

yad uktaṃ jīvasya brahma-kāryatvāt brahmaṇa eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñopapādanārthaṃ brahmaṇi jīvābhedena pratipādanam iti, tad ayuktaṃ na jāyate mriyate vā vipaścit iti-ādinā ajanya-śruteḥ jīvasya anāditvāt prācīna-karma-phala-bhogāya janmāśrayaṇopapādāt, anyathā viśva-sṛṣṭam utpadyeta, brahma-kāryatvaṃ jīvasya brahmātāpattiḥ lakṣaṇo mokṣa ākāśādi-vat vyañjanīya iti tad-upāya-vidhānānuguṇārthatvāt ca, ghaṭādi-vikāraṇa-grāmaḥ cit-nāśatvena mokṣasya apuruṣārthatvāt ca \| jīvātmanaḥ utpatti-vilaya-vādopapattiḥ itaratra prapañcayiṣyate \| ataḥ eṣa saṃprasādaḥ asmāt charīrāt samutthāya paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate yathā, nadyaḥ syandamānāḥ samudre astaṃ gacchanti nāma-rūpe vihāya \| tathā vidvān nāma-rūpād vimuktaḥ parāt-paraṃ puruṣam upaiti divyam \|\| iti utkrānti-niṣṭhaḥ paramātma-bhāvāt jīvābhedena paramātmanaḥ abhidhānam iti auḍulomir ācāryo manyate sma \|\|21\|\|

## \|\| 1.4.22 \|\|

*avasthiteḥ iti kāśakṛtsnaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

yad uktam utkramiṣyataḥ jīvasya brahma-bhāvāt brahmaṇaḥ tat-śabdena abhidhānam iti; tad api ayuktam, vikalpāsahatvāt \| asya jīvātmanaḥ utkrānteḥ pūrvam evaṃ-bhāvaḥ kiṃ svābhāvikaḥ, uta upādhikaḥ; tatra api pāramārthikaḥ, apāramārthikaḥ vā iti \| svābhāvikaḥ cet brahma-bhāvaḥ na upapadyate, bhedasya svabhāva-prayuktatvena svabhāve vidyamāne tat-anapāyāt \| atha bhedena saha svabhāva-pravṛttiḥ iti, tathā sati vināśatvāt eva tasya na brahma-bhāvaḥ; apuruṣārthatvāt doṣa-prasaṅgaḥ ca \| pāramārthikopādhikatve

prāk api brahma eva iti utkṣipyate evaṃ-bhāvāt iti viśeṣaḥ na yujyate vaktum \| asmin pakṣe hi upādhi-brahma-vyatirekeṇa vastu-antara-abhāvāt charīra-vat tasya brahmaṇaḥ upādhi-naṣṭe chandoga-vākya-upapāditaḥ eva bhedaḥ iti ukta-anteḥ prāk api brahma eva \| aupādhikasya bhedasya apāramārthikatve katham ayukta-antaḥ brahma-bhāvaḥ iti vaktavyam \| brahmaṇaḥ eva avidyā-upādhi-tirohita-svarūpasya iti cet; nitya-mukta-sva-prakāśa-aniruddha-svarūpasya avidyā-upādhi-tirodhāna-asaṃbhavāt \| tirodhānaṃ nāma vastu-svarūpe vidyamāne tat-prakāśa-nivṛttiḥ \| prakāśaḥ eva vastu-svarūpam iti aṅgīkāre tirodhāna-abhāva-vastu-bhaṅga-nāśaḥ vā syāt \| ataḥ nitya-avimukta-svarūpa-pracyānasya aśakya-antaḥ brahma-bhāve na kaścit viśeṣaḥ iti utkrānti-mukhyatā iti viśeṣaṇam ayathā eva \| asmāt charīrāt samutthāya iti pūrvam eva brahmatvasya na tatra antar-brahmatā-pratipattiḥ; api tu pūrva-siddha-svarūpasya āvirbhāvaḥ \| tathā hi vakṣyate saṃpatti-āvirbhāvaḥ svena śabdāt ity-ādibhiḥ \| ataḥ anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya ya ātmāni tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaraḥ yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīram ya ātmānam antaraḥ yamayati sa te ātmā antaryāmī amṛtaḥ yaḥ akṣaram antare sañcaran yasya akṣaraṃ śarīram yam akṣaraṃ na veda evaṃ sarva-bhūta-antar-ātma-prabhṛtatvāt paramātmā divyaḥ devaḥ ekaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ antaḥ praviṣṭaḥ śāstā janānām sarv-ātmā iti sva-śarīra-bhūta jīvātmakayā samastayā avasthayā sthitvā iti āḍhvena brahma-pratipādanam iti kāśakṛtsna ācāryaḥ manyate sma \| jīva-śabdaḥ ca jīvasya paramātma-paryantatayā eva vācakaḥ na jīva-mātrasya iti pūrvam eva uktam nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi iti ca \| evam āśmarathya-abhāvena tādātmya-upapattau parasya brahmaṇaḥ api ahata-pāpattva-sarvajñatva-ādi-yogataḥ jīvasya avidyā-prayojita brahmopāsana-upadeśa-virodhaḥ; jāgara-svapna-suṣupti-abhimāna-adhīnatvaḥ, jagataḥ brahmatā-tātparya-upadeśa-paraḥ ca sarva-śruta-para-samyak-upapāditā bhavanti iti kāśakṛtsnena eva mataṃ sūtra-kārasya eva īkṛtatvāt \|\| ayam atra vākya-arthaḥ:—amṛtatva-upāye mantavye pṛṣṭe yājñavalkyaḥ—ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ ity-ādinā paramātma-upāsana-smṛtatva-upāyam uktvā, ātmani khalu are dṛṣṭe ity-ādinā upāsya-lakṣaṇam uktum āviṣṭhāna-antareṣu upāsana-upakarāṇāṃ bhūtānāṃ pravṛtti-karaṇa-niyamanaṃ ca samādhinā avideyam, sa yathā ārdrendha-agneḥ ity-ādinā sa yathā sarvāsām apām samudraḥ eka-ayanam ity-ādinā ca upāsya-bhūtasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ nikhila-jagat-eka-kāraṇatvam sakala-viṣaya-śravaṇa-niṣṭhūlikaraṇa-kṣamatvam ca vistareṇa upadiśya sa yathā saindhavaḥ ity-ādinā amṛtatva-upāya-pravṛtti-rodhāya jīvātma-svarūpeṇa avasthitasya paramātmanaḥ api pariśchinna-jñāna-kākāratvam upapādya, tasya eva apariśchinna-jñāna-ākārasya saṃsāra-daśāyām bhūta-pariṇāmatva-cintya vijñāna-ghanaḥ eva etebhyaḥ bhūtebhyaḥ samutthāya tāni eva anu vinaśyati iti abhidhāya, na pretya saṃjñā asti iti mokṣa-daśāyām svābhāvika-apariśchinna-jñāna-saṅkoca-abhāvena bhūta-saṅghātena eka-sthalam api deva-ādi-rūpa-jñāna-abhāvam uktvā, punar api atra hi dvaitam iva bhavati ity-ādinā avidyātmakaṃ dvaitam nānā-bhūta-vastu-darśanam saṅkoca-hetum iti nirasta-nikhilā-

jñānasya brahmātmakaṃ kartṛtvaṃ jagad-vyāpakatve brahma-vyatirikta-pratyaktvatva-asambhavena bheda-darśanaṃ nirasy, “anena sarvaṃ vijānāti taṃ kena vijānīyāt iti ca jīvātmā svātmatayā-avasthitena yena paramātmanā adhiṣṭhātṛ-sannihitaṃ sarvaṃ vijānāti, ayaṃ taṃ kena vijānīyāt, na kena api iti paramātmano duravabodhatva-prāyaḥ, “sa eṣa neti neti iti-ādinā ayaṃ sarveśvaraḥ vedana-samānatva-vicchittṛ-vilakṣaṇa-svarūpa eva sarveśvara-aiśvarya-saṃsthātmatayā-avasthita iti sva-aśarīra-bhūta-cid-acad-vastu-gataṃ bandhanaṃ saṃspṛśyata iti-abhiprāya, “vijñātāram are kena vijānīyād iti-ukta-anusāsana-ādiṣṭi maitreyy-anta-vāde khatva-pratyayāḥ” iti samasta-vastu-vijñātṛtayaṃ nikhila-jagad-eka-kāraṇa-bhūtaṃ sarveśya vijñātāraṃ puruṣottam-ukta-prakārād upāsanārho kena vijānīyād iti asambhava-upāsanatvam ucyata iti arthaḥ; brahma-prāptir eva ca amṛtatva-phalam iti āha—ityukta-vād \| ataḥ paraṃ brahma vāsan-vācyaṃ pratipādyata iti param eva brahma jagat-kāraṇam, na puruṣaḥ tad-adhiṣṭhitā vā prakṛtir iti sthitam \|\|22\|\|

**iti vākyānvaya-adhikaraṇam \|\|6\|\|**

**atha prakṛti-adhikaraṇam \|\|7\|\|**

## \|\| 1.4.23 \|\|

*prakṛtiś ca pratijñā-dṛṣṭānta-anuparodhāt*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ nirīśvara-sāṅkhyaṃ niraste sati seśvara-sāṅkhyaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—yady api īkṣaṇa-ādi-guṇa-yogāt sarvajña-īśvaraṃ jagat-kāraṇatvena vedāntāḥ pratipādayanti \| tathā api vedāntaiḥ eva jagad-upādānatayā pradhānam eva pratipādyata iti pratīyate \| na hi vedāntās sarvajñasya apariṇāmino 'dhiṣṭhātur īśvarasya adhiṣṭhīyamāna-acetanena pariṇāminā pradhānena vinā jagataḥ kāraṇatvam avagamayanti \| tathā hi apariṇāmino nimittaṃ prakṛtiṃ ca etad-adhikāṃ pariṇāminīm adhīyate—“niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāntaṃ niravadyaṃ nirañjanam” “sa vā eṣa mahān aja ātmā 'jaro 'maraḥ” “vikāra-jananīm ajñāna-rūpām ajāṃ dhruvām \| dhyāyate 'dhyāsitā tena tanvate prerate punaḥ \| sūyate puruṣārthaṃ ca tena eva adhiṣṭhitā jagat \| gaur anādi-antavatī sā janitrī bhūta-bhāvinī \|\|” iti \| tathā prakṛtim upādāna-bhūtām adhiṣṭhātrī ca īśvaro viśvaṃ jagad utpatati iti śrūyate “asmān māyī sṛjate viśvam etat” “māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyān māyinaṃ tu maheśvaram” iti \| smṛtir api “pradhāna-kṣetrajña-patir guṇeśaḥ saṃsāra-vṛkṣam” iti \| evam aśruter api pradhāna-upādānatve brahmaṇo jagat-kāraṇatva-śruteḥ yathā-anupapattiś ca pradhāna-svarūpaṃ tasya īśvara-adhiṣṭhitatvaṃ jagad-upādānatvaṃ ca siddhyati \| evam eva hi loke nimitta-upādānayoḥ anya-bhede dṛśyate \| sūkṣma-varṇa-ādir acetanasy ghaṭa-kaṭaka-ādi-upādānatvaṃ cetanasya kulāla-suvarṇakāra-āder nimittatvaṃ ca niyatam upalabhyate \| kārya-niṣpattis tu niyamena aneka-kāraka-apekṣā dṛṣṭā \| evaṃ nimitta-upādānayoḥ kṛtiyaṃ kārya-niṣpattau rese-karaka-samavadhūti-niyamaṃ carita-krama eva brahma-upādānaṃ nimittaṃ ca pratipāda-

tṛtuṃ na prabhavanti vedānta-vākyāni \| ato brahma nimitta-kāraṇam eva, na upādānam \| upādānaṃ tu tad-dhitam pradhānam eva iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte ’bhidhīyate—prakṛtiḥ ca pratijñā-dṛṣṭānta-anuparodhāt \| prakṛtiḥ ca—upādānaṃ ca \| na nimitta-kāraṇa-mātraṃ brahma, upādāna-kāraṇaṃ ca brahma iti arthaḥ \| kutaḥ ? pratijñā-dṛṣṭānta-anuparodhāt \| evam eva hi pratijñā-dṛṣṭāntau na uparudhyete \| pratijñā tāvat sat eva somya idam agre āsīt; ena aśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavati amataṃ matam avijñātaṃ vijñātam iti avijñānena sarva-vijñāna-viṣayā \| dṛṣṭāntaḥ ca yathā somya ekena mṛt-piṇḍena sarvaṃ mṛn-mayaṃ vijñātaṃ syāt yathā somya ekena loha-maṇinā yathā somya ekena nakha-nikṛntanena iti kāraṇa-vijñānāt kārya-vijñāna-viṣayaḥ \| yadi nimitta-kāraṇam eva jagataḥ brahma, tadā tat vijñānāt samastaṃ jagat vijñātaṃ syāt \| na hi kulāla-ādi-vijñānena ghaṭa-ādiḥ vijñāyate \| ataḥ pratijñā-dṛṣṭāntau etau eva \| brahmaṇaḥ eva upādānatve upādāna-bhūta-mṛt-piṇḍa-loha-maṇi-nakha-kṛntana-vijñānena ghaṭa-maṇi-kaṭaka-mukuṭa-vāsī-parśva-ādi-kārya-vijñānavat vikara-jāgrat-upādāna-bhūte brahmaṇi vijñāte tat-kāryaṃ nikhilaṃ jagat vijñātaṃ syāt \| kāraṇam eka-avasthāntara-āpannaṃ kāryaṃ, na dravya-antaram iti kārya-kāraṇa-rūpeṇa avasthita-mṛd-vikāra-ādi-nidarśanena pratijñā-samarthanāt brahma jagad-upādānaṃ vai iti niścīyate \|\|

astu nimitta-upādānayoḥ bheda-dṛṣṭeḥ eva pratīyata iti; tad asat, nimitta-upādānayoḥ aikya-pratīteḥ uta tam ādeśam aprākṣyaḥ? yena aśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavati iti \| ādiśyate praśiṣyate ’nena iti ādeśaḥ; utasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi iti ādi-śruteḥ \| śāsakātmanasya eva kartā vivakṣitaḥ \| tam ādeśam aprākṣyaḥ; yena aśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavati—yena ādeṣṭuḥ vidhiṣṭā śrutena aśrutam api śrutaṃ bhavati iti nimitta-upādānayoḥ aikyaṃ pratīyate; sat eva somya idam agra āsīt ekam eva advitīyam iti avadhāraṇāt dvitīya-padena adhiṣṭhātr-antara-nivāraṇāt ca \| nanu evaṃ sati vikāra-jananīṣu gauḥ anādi-antavatī iti ādibhiḥ prakṛteḥ ādhāna-viraheṇa nityatvaṃ jagad-upādānatvaṃ ca śrūyamāṇaṃ katham apavadyate ? tad ucyate—jñāna-svarūpa-vikāratvam kāraṇa-āvasthaṃ brahma eva prakṛti-śabdena abhidhīyate, brahma-vyatirikta-vastu-antara-abhāvāt \| tathā hi śrūyate; sarvaṃ taṃ parādāt yaḥ anyatra ātmanaḥ sarvaṃ veda yatra tu asya sarvam ātmā-abhūt tat kena kam paśyet iti ādyāḥ; sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma aitad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvam iti kārya-avasthaṃ kāraṇa-avasthaṃ ca sarvaṃ jagat brahma-ātmakam iti śravaṇāt ca \|\|

etat uktaṃ bhavati—śrayaḥ pṛthivyām antare sañcaramayasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yam pṛthivī na veda iti ārabhya yaḥ akṣaram antare sañcaramayasya akṣaraṃ śarīraṃ yam akṣaraṃ na veda yaḥ akṣaram antare sañcaran yam akṣaraṃ na veda yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivyāḥ antaraḥ yam pṛthivī na veda yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yaḥ pṛthivīm antaraḥ yamayati iti ārabhya ya ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaraḥ yam ātmā

na veda yathā ātmanā śarīraṃ ya ātmānam antaro yamayati sa ta ātmā antaryāmi amṛtaḥ iti ca sarva-cid-acid-vastu-śarīratayā sarvadā sarva-niyantṛtvaṃ paraṃ brahma kathitam iti bhakta-nāma-rūpam, kathita-vicitra-bhakta-nāma-rūpam; yathā bhakta-nāma-rūpatvaṃ tadā tad eva bahutvena kāryatvena ca ucyate; yadā ca avidya-bhakta-nāma-rūpaṃ tad e karma-dvitīyaṃ kāraṇam iti ca \| evaṃ sarvadā cid-acid-vastu-śarīrasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ abhakta-nāma-rūpatayā yā kāraṇa-avasthā sā śrī-raṅganātha-vratī śrī-vikārananambhājī śrī-ajñānekāś ityādi-viśiṣṭiḥ abhidhīyate iti \|\|

nanu ca śrī-mahān adi ante līyate avyaktam akṣare līyate iti pralaya-prakriyā-avasara-ukti-pralayo pratīyate; tathā ca mahābhārate śrī-tasminn adhyakta-sukṣmaṃ viṣṇuṃ dṛṣṭvā tena śrī-avyaktaṃ puruṣe brahmaṇi kriyate saṃpralīyate iti \| na evaṃ doṣaḥ; acid-vastu-śarīrasya brahmaṇaḥ avyakta-śabda-vācyayā tri-guṇa-avasthāyāḥ kāryatvāt \| śrī-yadā tamas tan na divā na rātriḥ iti kṛtsnasya caturmukha-kāryasya api brahmātmakasya ati-sūkṣmatayā acid-vastuni sthiti-abhidhānāt jña-kāraṇasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ prakāśa-bhūtam ati-śukṣmaṃ ca acid-vastu nityam eva iti tat-prakāraṃ brahma eva śrī-raṅganātha-vratītyādibhiḥ abhidhīyate \| ata eva ca śrī-akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate tamaḥ pare deva ekī bhavati iti tamasa ekī-bhāva-mātram eva śrūyate; na tu layaḥ \| ekī-bhāva iti tamo-tamobhidāna-atisūkṣmācid-ākārasya brahmaṇaḥ abhakta-nāma-rūpatayā avasthānam abhidhīyate \| śrī-tam āsīn na sadā gūḍham agre praketaṃ tamas tan mahi majāyatakam iti ādi-ṛg-veda eva vadati \| tathā ca mānavaṃ vacaḥ śrī-āsīd idaṃ tamo-bhūtam aprajñātam alakṣaṇam \| apratarkyam avijñeyaṃ prasuptam iva sarvataḥ \|\| iti \| śrī-asann āsamādhi dṛśyate viśva-bhettat iti ādy-anantaram eva upapādyante; brahmaṇaḥ apariṇāmitva-śruty-arthaś ca \|\|

yat ca ekasya nirvikāratv-upādānatvaṃ ca na saṃbhavati, eka-kāraṇa-niṣṭha-pratyakṣaṃ ca kāryasya; loke tathā niyama-darśanāt \| pratoditā siddhaṃ dṛṣṭi-vibhedānavayavādhyāpeka-sādevo api pratipādayituṃ na prabhavati iti \| atra ucyate—sakala-itara-vilakṣaṇasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ sarva-śaktaiḥ sarvajñasya ekarasya eva sarvaṃ prapadyate \| mṛd-antarvate nasya jñānāmātreṇa vidhi-śrutas tathāgava-vidhi-śrutiḥ kulālād eva vicitra-variṇāma-saktibhir ahat-satya saṅkulapyataya ca tathā darśanīyamaḥ \| ato brahma eva jagato nimittam upādānaṃ ca \|\| 23 \|\|

## \|\| 1.4.24 \|\|

*abhidhyā-upadeśāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

itakṣo ayaṃ brahma eva, śrī-so akāmayata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti śrī-tad aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti saṅkalpa-pūrvakaṃ bahu-bhavanaṃ śruty-upadeśāt \| vividha-cid-acid-rūpeṇa asau eva bahu syāṃ tathā prajāyeyeti saṅkalpa-pūrvikā hi sṛṣṭiḥ upadiśyate \|\| 24 \|\|

## \|\| 1.4.25 \|\|

*sākṣāt ca ubhaya-āmnānāt*

**Комментарий:**

na kevalaṃ pratijñā-hānāt nimitta-upādeśa-ādibhiḥ ayam artho niścīyate, brahmaṇaḥ eva nimittatvam upādānatvaṃ ca sākṣāt āmnāyate kiṃ svid vanaṃ ka u sa vṛkṣa āsīt yataḥ dyāvā-pṛthivī niṣṭatakṣuḥ \| manīṣiṇaḥ manasā pṛcchata idu tat yat adhyatiṣṭhat bhuvanāni dhārayan \| brahma vanaṃ brahma sa vṛkṣa āsīt yataḥ dyāvā-pṛthivī niṣṭatakṣuḥ \| manīṣiṇaḥ manasā vibudhyadhvaṃ yaḥ brahma adhyatiṣṭhat bhuvanāni dhārayan \|\| iti \| atra hi samudra-bandhanaḥ kiṃ upādānaṃ kāni ca upakaraṇāni iti loka-ghaṭṭanā-puṣṭe sakala-itara-vilakṣaṇasya brahma-prasāda-kriyo na viruddha iti brahma eva upādānam upakaraṇāni ca iti parihṛtam \| ataḥ ca ubhayam brahma \|\| 25 \|\|

## \|\| 1.4.26 \|\|

*ātma-kṛteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

saḥ akāmayata bahu syām prajāyeya iti sidhyudbhavena prakṛtasya brahmaṇaḥ tat ātmānaṃ svayam akuruta iti sṛṣṭeḥ kartuṛtvaṃ karyatvaṃ ca pratīyata iti ātmanaḥ eva bahu-karaṇāt svasya nimittatvam upādānatvaṃ ca pratīyate \| śruti-vākyam āha ātmā kartā, sa eva viśva-karmākhyaḥ kāryam iti kṛtveva karmantr̥tayoḥ virodhaḥ \| svayam eva ātmānaṃ tathā akuruta iti nimittam upādānaṃ ca \|\| 26 \|\|

satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma ānando brahma apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ avijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāntaṃ niravadyaṃ nirañjanam sa vā eṣa mahān aja ātmā ajaraḥ amaraḥ iti sva-bhāvataḥ nirasta-samasta-cetana-acetana-vastu-vartitva-ayogyasya niratisaya-śrī-ānanda-ekatānasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ vividha-vicitra-ananta-puruṣārtha-prada-vicitra-viniṣṭa-prapañca-rūpeṇa ātmanaḥ bahu-bhavana-saṅkalpa-pūrvakaṃ bahu-karaṇaṃ katham upapadyata iti āśaṅkya āha—

## \|\| 1.4.27 \|\|

*pariṇāmāt*

**Комментарий:**

pariṇāma-svabhāvatvāt; na atra upariśyamanasya pariṇāmasya parasmin brahmaṇi doṣa-āvahatvaṃ saṃbhāvaḥ, pratyuta niraṅkuśa-aiśvarya-vṛhatvam eva eṣa viśeṣaḥ \| ekam eva hi pariṇāma upadiśyate \| aśoheyamadhyāno 'kalyāṇakatānaṃ sva-itara-samasta-vastu-vilakṣaṇaṃ sarvaṃ satya-saṅkalpam avāpta-samasta-kāmam anavadhika-atiśaya-ānandaṃ sva-vilop-karaṇa-bhūta-samasta-cid-acid-vastu-jāta-śarīratayā tad-ātmaghūtaṃ paraṃ brahma sva-śarīra-bhūte prapañce sūkṣma-avasthā-anupacikīrṣāṇa-para-śabdayā tamas-śabd-vācya-atiśūkṣma-acit-svarūpeṇe sati, tasmin ca sva-śarīratayā api pṛthaktva-niṣṭhāna-hīna-sūkṣma-daśā-āpannayā svakīyena cetanā-mātreṇa sati, tathā-bhūta-tamaḥ-śarīraṃ brahma pūrva-vadibhakta nāma-rūpa-cid-acid-miśra-prapañca-śarīraṃ sthitam iti sāṅkultyā avyakrameṇa jagac-charīrasya ātmānaṃ pariṇāmayati iti sarvaṃ vedānteṣu

pariṇāma-upadeśaḥ \| tathā eva bṛhad-āraṇyake kṛtsnasya jagato brahma-śarīratvaṃ brahmātmakatāṃ ca pratipādayate śreyaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivyā antaro yaṃ pṛthivī na veda yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yaḥ pṛthivīm antaro yamayati eṣa ta ātmā antaryāmī amṛtaḥ iti ārabhya śreyasy apa-śarīrasya śreyasy agninā-śarīrasya śreyasy antarikṣa-śarīrasya śreyasya vāyu-śarīrasya śreyasya dyauḥ-śarīrasya śreyasy āditya-śarīrasya śreyasya diśaḥ śarīrasya śreyasya candra-tārakaṃ śarīrasya śreyasya tamaḥ-śarīrasya śreyasya tejaḥ-śarīrasya śreyasya sarvāṇi bhūtāni śarīrasya śreyasya prāṇa-śarīrasya śreyasya vāk-śarīrasya śreyasya cakṣuḥ-śarīrasya śreyasya śrotraṃ śarīrasya śreyasya manaḥ-śarīrasya śreyasya tvak-śarīrasya śreyasya vijñānaṃ śarīrasya śreyasya retaḥ-śarīrasya iti evam antena kāṇva-pāṭhe; mādhyandine tu pāṭhe vijñāna-sthāne śreyasy ātmā śarīram iti viśeṣaḥ; loka-yajña-vedānāṃ paramātma-śarīratvam adhikṛtya \| su bāla-upaniṣadi ca pṛthivy-ādīnāṃ tattvānāṃ paramātma-śarīratvam adhidhāya vājasaneyake antaryāmi-tatvāni śarīratvaṃ brahmaṇa ātmatvaṃ ca śrūyate śreyasya buddhiḥ śarīrasya śreyasy ahaṅkāraḥ śarīrasya śreyasya cittaṃ śarīrasya śreyasy avyaktaṃ śarīrasya śreyasy akṣaraṃ śarīrasya śrayo mṛtyum antare sañcaran yasya mṛtyu-śarīraṃ yaṃ mṛtyuḥ na veda eṣa sarva-bhūtāntar-ātmā apahata-pāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ iti \| atra mṛtyu-śabdena parama-sūkṣmam acij-vastu tamaḥ-śabda-vācayam abhidhīyate, śrey akṣaram akṣare līyate akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate iti tasyām eva upaniṣadi krama-pratyabhijñānāt \| sarveṣām ātmanāṃ jñānāvaraṇārthaṃ mṛtyutvena tad eva hi tamo mṛtyu-śabda-upadeśyam \|\| subāla-upaniṣad eva brahma-śarīratayā tadātmakatāṃ tattvānāṃ brahmaṇā eva pratyag-ātmatayā śreyas ṛṣṭavyam evaṃ līyate āpaḥ tejoṣi līyate tejo vāyau līyate vāyuḥ ākāśe līyate ākāśam indriyeṣu indriyāṇi tanmātreṣu tanmātrāṇi bhūtādau līyante bhūtādiḥ mahati līyate mahān avyaktaṃ līyate avyaktam akṣare līyate akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate tamaḥ pare deva ekī bhavati iti \| avibhāga-pratiniṣṭhāyam api viśva-cin-mātratvāti-śukṣmaṃ sa-karma-saṃskāraṃ tirodhīyur asya brahmate śreyan karma-vibhāgādi-virahitā veśān nātivattv upapatte ca āyuḥ upalabhyate ca iti \|\| evaṃ svasmād vibhāga-avyapadeśāc ca hetunā paramātmanādeśa-bhūtātmatva-sūkṣma-cid-acid-vastu-śarīratādeka-smād eva ahita-upādhi-rahitriyām anantāt sarva-vyāsa-satyasya duḥkhāpada-brahmaṇo nāma-rūpa-vibhāgārha-sthūla-cid-acid-vastu-śarīratayā bahu-bhavana-saṅkalpa-pūrvako jagad-ākāreṇa pariṇāmaś ca śrūyate \| śrayaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma śretasmād \| etasmād ātmana ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥ śrey eṣa hi eva ānandayāti śrī-lokāmayata, bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti, sa tapaḥ atapyata, sa tapaḥ taptvā, idaṃ sarvam asṛjata, yad idaṃ kiṃ ca, tat sṛṣṭvā, tat eva anuprāviśat, tat anupraviśya, sat ca tyat ca abhavat, niruktaṃ ca aniruktaṃ ca, nilayanaṃ ca anilayanaṃ ca, vijñānaṃ ca avijñānaṃ ca, satyaṃ ca anṛtaṃ ca satyam abhavat iti \| atra tapaḥ-śabdena

prācīna-jagad-ākāra-paryālocanavat jñānam abhidhīyate, “yasya jñāna-mayaṃ tapaḥ” ity-ādi-śruteḥ \| prāk sṛṣṭeḥ jagat-saṃsthānam ālocayataḥ anādir api tat-sṃsthānaṃ jagad-brahma-dvitīyārthaḥ \| tathā eva hi brahma sarveṣu kalpavekāna-rūpam eva jagad iti “sūryā-candramasau dhātā yathā-pūrvam akalpayat, divaṃ ca pṛthivīṃ ca antarikṣam atho suvaḥ” ity-artha-anuvṛtti-liṅgāni nānā śrūyāṇi parye \| dṛśyante tāni tāni eva tathā bhāvā yugādiṣu \|\| iti śruti-smṛtibhyaḥ \|

tad ayam arthaḥ— svayam aparicchinna-jñāna-ānanda-vaibhava-udaya-nasthūlasya ca asakala-sthala-olopakara-vividha-vicitra-śarīratayā tasyaḥ paramātmā vividha-ananta-koṭi-jagad-koṭi-sthasya sthāvarīra-bhūta-prakṛti-puruṣa-samūha-dharasya mahā-bhūta-parṇatamāsaṃ tatra tatra choraika-pariṇāmasya tasyaḥ punas tasya cchādanācya-vivitra-vicitra-vinimna-śvedādi-sthāvarāntas jagad-bhoga-vardhati \| “tad aikṣata bahu syām” ity-ādi-śrutāt, tad-upūrveṣu api kāraṇa-avasthāyām ātmas tayā avasthitaḥ paramātmā kārya-rūpeṇa vikriyamāṇāsu apracchāyāsu eva sarvāsu avasthāsu tat-tad-bhāvid ucyate \| evaṃ paramātma-vicitra-cit-acit-prāṇā-rūpa-jagad-ākāra-pariṇāme paramātma-śarīra-bhūta-vicchinnāta-sarvaṃ eva apuruṣārthaḥ; tathā-bhūta-vicchinnatāḥ ca sarve vikārāḥ; paramātmani kāryatvaṃ tad-avasthā-yogāt ca anya-niḥsṛtavat na bhavet; paramātmā tu tayoḥ sva-śarīra-bhūtayoḥ nirapekṣatayāṣṭūlas tasmāt puruṣārtha-kāraḥ ca na sṛjyate; aparicchinna-jñāna-ānanda-mayaḥ sarva-śeṣa-rūpa eva jagat-parivartanena līlayā vibhute \| tad etad āha “satyaṃ ca anṛtaṃ ca satyam abhavat” iti \| vividha-vicitra-cit-acit-prāṇa-vikārasya api api brahma satyam eva abhavat—nitya-sthiti-nibaddha-upādāna-bhūtam aparicchinna-jñāna-ānandam eka-rūpeṇa abhavad ity arthaḥ \| sarvāṇi vicitra-sthūlāni sūkṣma-daśā-pannāni sthūla-daśā-pattāni ca parasya brahmaṇaḥ līlā-upakaraṇāni; sṛṣṭi-ādi-avasthā līlā iti bhagavad-gītāyām avatara-avatariṣyatasya “avyaktādīni bhūtāni” pariṇāma-hiṃsā-yuktam \| krīḍā hareḥ idaṃ sarvaṃ kṣaram iti upadhāryatām \|\| “krīḍataḥ bālakasya iva ceṣṭā tasya niśāmaya” ity-ādiṣu \| vakṣyati “lokavat tu līlā-kaivalyam” iti \| “asmānmāyī sṛjate viśvam etat tasmiṃś ca anyo māyayā sanniruddhaḥ” iti brahmaṇi jagad-rūpatayā vikriyamāṇe api tat-prakāra-bhūta-cidaśgalāt tas sarvaṃ vikāraras tat-prakāra-bhūta-kṣetra-jagatāḥ ca puruṣārthāḥ iti vivekena prakṛti-puruṣayoḥ brahma-śarīra-bhūtatve asatatāṃ tathā nirdeśāt na hi sūkṣma-daśā-pannasya brahmaṇaḥ kaḥ api bhedaḥ vyapadeśaḥ; “tadātmānaṃ svayam akuruta” ity-ādi-nirākaraṇena tu \| tathā eva mānavaṃ vacaḥ: “saḥ abhidhyāya śarīrāt svāt sisṛkṣuḥ vividhāḥ prajāḥ \| apaḥ eva sasarja ādau tāsu vīryam apāsṛjat \|\|” iti \| ata eva brahmaṇaḥ nirdeśavat-nidhikāratva-śravaṇa-yoga-upapannaḥ \| prāṇaḥ brahma eva jagataḥ nimitta-upādānatvam \|\| 27 \|\|

## \|\| 1.4.28 \|\|

*yoniḥ ca hi gīyate*

**Комментарий:**

itatra jagataḥ nimitta-upādānatvaṃ brahma, yasmāt śāstra-antareṣu api gīyate “kartāram īśaṃ puruṣaṃ

brahma-yoniḥ iti, śabda-bhūta-yoniṃ paripaśyanti dhīrāḥ iti ca \| yogi-śabdāc ca upādāna-vacanaṃ iti yathā urṇa-nābhiḥ sṛjate gṛhṇate ca iti vākya-śeṣa-darśanāt \|\| 28 \|\| iti prakṛti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|

**atha sarva-vyākhyāna-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|**

## \|\| 1.4.29 \|\|

*etena sarve vyākhyātā vyākhyātāḥ*

**Комментарий:**

etena—pradhāna-puruṣayoḥ jagat-kāraṇatva eva, sarva-vedānteṣu jagat-kāraṇa-pratipādana-parā sarvaṃ vākyaviśeṣāś ca tat-tat-cetana-lakṣaṇa-sarvaiśvarya-sthiti-brahma-pratipādana-parā vyākhyātāḥ \| vyākhyātā iti pada-abhyāsaḥ adhyāya-parisamāpti-dyotanārthaḥ \|\| 26 \|\| iti sarva-vyākhyāna-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye prathamasya adhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 4 \|\| \|\| samāptaḥ ca adhyāyaḥ \|\|

śrīḥ astu

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ \|

**dvitīyaḥ adhyāyaḥ—prathamaḥ pādaḥ—smṛti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Adhyaya 2

## Pada 1

## \|\| 2.1.1 \|\|

*smṛti-anavakāśa-doṣa-prasaṅga iti cet na anya-smṛti-anavakāśa-doṣa-prasaṅgāt*

**Комментарий:**

prathame adhyāye pratyakṣādi-pramāṇāgocara-acetanānta-saṃsṛṣṭa-nikhila-cid-acid-vastu cetanātvādi-nikhila-dharmānirasta-nikhila-avidyādi-apuruṣārtha-gandham-ananta-jñānānanda-ekatāna-parimita-udāra-guṇa-sāgaraṃ nikhila-jagat-eka-kāraṇaṃ sarvāntarātma-rūtaṃ paraṃ brahma vedānta-vedyam iti uktam \| anantaram arthasya saṃbhāvanīya-samasta-prakāra-durdharva-pratipādanāya dvitīyaḥ adhyāya ārabhyate \| prathamaṃ tāvat smṛti-samati-virodhād eva vedāntānām atatparatvam āśaṅkya vaikarṇyīkriyate \| kathaṃ smṛti-virodhaḥ cet, netarasyaratvam? uktaṃ hi virodhe tu anapekṣaṃ syāt iti śruti-vihṛddhayā smṛteḥ anādaraṇīyatvam \| satyam, audumbarīṃ spṛṣṭvā udgāyet iti apiṣu stvā evaṃ-vidyaya-saṃbhavāt tiṣṭhata smṛti ca anārṇīyau; śruttu vedānta-vedya-tattva-dhukhavodhyena parama-āpta-praṇītas smṛti-virodhe satyam artha iti niścayāyogāt smṛtyā śruteḥ tatparatva-upapādanam aviruddham \|\|

evaṃ uktaṃ bhavati—prācīna-śākhā-gata-nikhilāśrudayaśadhana-vratānicita-śraddhā-paripūrṇa-samyo atikramādhikarmibhiḥ yathāvat anuṣṭhātā śruti-smṛti-itihāsa-purāṇeṣu ṛṣiṃ prasūtaṃ kapilam iti-ādi-vākyaiḥ anekena sandṛtītena para-saṃvidā kalpitena para-paniḥśreyasa-sādhana-bodhayedve-

toṣa-nibaddha-mṛtyutvaṃ heyena vinā alpa-śrutair manda-matibhir vedanārthi-niḥśreya-yoga-adhyakṣa tāthyena yāvaj-jīvitayāsmāś ca sūtreṣu sakalayā pūrva-pakṣa-prasaṅgaś ca smṛti-viruddha eva artho vedānta-vedya iti balād aśrūyamāṇopīti \| na ca vācyaṃ manvādi-smṛtīnāṃ brahma-kāraṇatvādi-jñāninām eva mata-prakāśatva-doṣa-prasaṅgaḥ iti; dharma-trivipādana-para-prācīna-māgata-upavyūhana eva sāvakaśatvāt \| astu astu kṛtsnāyās tatra pratipādana-paratva-sthāneṣv apy ame niravakāśatvam eva syāt \| tad idam ārāḍhūne smṛti-anavakāśa-doṣa-prasaṅga iti coditam \|\|

tatra uttaraṃ—na anyasya hṛdaya-anavakāśa-doṣa-prasaṅga ity ādi \| anyā hi manvādi-smṛtayo brahma-kāraṇatāṃ vadanti \| yathā āha manuḥ: āsīd idaṃ tamo-bhūtam iti ārabhya, tataḥ svayaṃ-bhūr bhagavān avyakto vyaktayann idam \| mahā-bhūtādibhir vṛttaḥ prādur āsīt tamo-nudaḥ \|\| saḥ abhidhyāya śarīrāt svāt sismṛkṣur vividhāḥ prajāḥ \| apa eva sasarja ādau tāsu bījam avāsṛjat \|\| iti \| bhagavad-gītāsu ca ahaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ pralayaḥ tathā ahaṃ sarvasya prabhavaḥ mattaḥ sarvaṃ pravartate iti ca \| mahā-bhārate kutastham idaṃ sarvaṃ jagat sthāvara-jaṅgamam \| pralaye ca kam abhyeti tan me brūhi pitāmaha \|\| iti pṛṣṭa āha nārāyaṇo jagat-mūrtir ananta-ātmā sanātanaḥ iti \| tathā tasmāt avyaktam utpannaṃ tri-guṇaṃ dvija-sattama iti, avyaktaṃ puruṣe brahma-niṣkriye sampralīyate iti ca \| āha ca bhagavān parāśaraḥ: viṣṇoḥ sakāśād udbhūtaṃ jagat tatra eva ca sthitam \| sthiti-saṃyama-kartā asau jagato ’sya jagac ca saḥ \|\| iti \| āha ca āpastambaḥ: bhūḥ prajñāta-sarva eva guhāśayasya ahanyamānasya vikarma-bhaya ity ārabhya tasmāt kāyāḥ prabhavanti sarve sa mūlaṃ śāśvatikaṃ sa nityaḥ iti \| yadi kapila-smṛtyā vedānta-avagatārtha-vyavasthā syāt, tad etāsāṃ sarvāsāṃ smṛtīnāṃ manvakāśatva-khyo mahān doṣaḥ prasajyeta \|\|

api arthaḥ—yady api vedānta-avagatānām avijñāna-traya-pakṣaiḥ sakala-itara-prāmāṇya-saṃbhāvanā-smṛti-sūtrārtha-pratipādana-paratvān artha-bādhāyogyāḥ, tāni pratisannutāṃ tat api evaṃpekṣitam \| tathāpi tad-arthānusāriṇām āgamatva-praṇītānāṃ bahvīnāṃ smṛtīnāṃ tad-apekṣayā pravṛttānām anavakāśatvaṃ mā prasāṅkṣīd iti śruti-viśeṣārthaṃ kapila-smṛtiḥ upekṣaṇīyā \| upapadyate ca śruti-smṛti-vyāghāta-virodha-karaṇam; tat ca viruddhārthayā smṛtyā na śakyate kartuṃ \|\|

na ca etāsāṃ smṛtīnāṃ prācīna-mātrodita-dharma-viśeṣopadeśa-karaṇena sāvakāśatvam; para-brahma-bhūta-parama-puruṣa-ārādhanena dharmāntarvatīnāṃ cetanāsām ārādhya-bhūta-parama-puruṣa-pratipādana-abhāve sati tad-ārādhana-bhūta-dharma-pratipādana-asaṃbhavāt \| tathā hi parama-puruṣa-ārādhanatā sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ smaryate—yataḥ pravṛttir bhūtānāṃ yena sarvam idaṃ tatam \| sva-karmaṇā tam abhyarcya siddhiṃ vindati mānavaḥ \|\| yajñair ārādhanaṃ devaṃ snānādibhiś ca karmabhiḥ \| brahma-lokam avāpnoti na ca iha āvartate punaḥ \|\|

bhīṣā asmāt vātaḥ pavate bhīṣā udeti sūryaḥ \| bhīṣā asmāt agniś ca indraś ca mṛtyuḥ dhāvati pañcamaḥ \|\| iti \| na ca adhikāri-abhimukha-saṃsāra-rika-phala-sādhana-karma-pratipādanena etāsāṃ sāvakāśatvam \| yat tatra eva māpi karmāṇi parama-puruṣa-ārādhanatvena eva svarga-phalaṃ; yathoktaṃ dṛṣṭa-pūrṇābhyāṃ svarga-kāmaḥ yajeta śraddhayā anvitaḥ \| taiḥ api mām eva kaunteya yajanti avidhi-pūrvakam \|\| ahaṃ hi sarva-yajñānāṃ bhoktā ca prabhuḥ eva ca \| na tu māṃ vijānanti tattvena ataḥ cyavanti te \|\| iti \| tathā grahaiḥ vāmiṃjyase nityaṃ sarva-deva-mayaḥ acyuta \| havya-kavya-bhogī ca tvaṃ pitṛ-deva-svarūpa-dhṛk \|\| iti \|\|

yat uktaṃ ṛṣibhiḥ prasūtaṃ kapilam iti kapilasya aikṣatyā saṅkīrtana-anantarya-anusāreṇa vedānta-artha-vyavasthāpanaṃ nyāyyam iti; tat sat, brahma-pradeśa-śruti-sthitu sarvopādhi-śaśvitanānāṃ nirdeśāntareṇa saṅkīrtanāntara-prāptena loka-gatyena śruti-artha-vyavasthāpana-prasaṅgāt \|\|1\|\|

atha syāt, kapilasya sva-yoga-mahimnā vastu-yāthātmya-upalabdhena etat-smṛti-anusāreṇa vedānta-arthaḥ vyavasthāpanīya iti; ata uttaraṃ paṭhati—

## \|\| 2.1.2 \|\|

*itareṣāṃ ca anupalabdheḥ*

**Комментарий:**

tat-śabdaḥ tu śaṣṭha-arthe abhihitatyābhūnigṛhyārthaḥ \| itareṣāṃ—manv-ādīnāṃ bahūnāṃ sva-yoga-mahima sākṣāt-kṛta-pārāvara-tattva-yāthātmyānāṃ likhila-jagad-bhaja-bhūta-tattva-vākyatayā śraddhe kiñca manur-vad-indrair-vacanaiḥ iti śruti-prasiddhānāṃ kapila-pakṣa-karaṇe tattva-anupalabdheḥ śruti-vihuddhā kapila-upalabdhir abhiniṅmūlā iti na tayā yojyo vedānta-artha-śābdhiṃ śakya iti siddham \|\| 2 \|\| iti smṛti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha yoga-pratyukty-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.1.3 \|\|

*etena yogaḥ pratyuktaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

etena—kapila-smṛti-nirākaraṇena, yogaḥ smṛtiḥ api pratyuktā \| kā punaḥ asya adhikā-śaṅkā, yat-pariharaṇāya nyāyātideśaḥ ? yoga-smṛtau api īśvarasya upamānasya kṣamā-dhamatvayā vedānta-vihita-yogasya carita-dhyāna-ādi-parihāryasya sarva-vedānta-pravṛtta-adhikṛtatvāc ca tat-smṛtyā vedānta-upeyaṃ hūṇaṃ nyāyyam iti \| parihāraḥ tu—akṣayātmaka-pradhāna-kāraṇa-vālitvāt mita-kāraṇa-mātra-īśvarasya upamā-śraddhā-ātmakasya yogasya dhyeyā-kaniṣṭha-rūpeṇa adhyeya-dhyeya-bhūtayoḥ viśeṣayoḥ aikya-rūḍhi-vācām avagatatvāt tadita-saṅkalpānuguṇātmakatva-virodhā-vaidhikattvāt aparihārya-sattvam api kṣetrajña-bhūtasya kadācid viśeṣa-saṃbhinna eva saṃbhavāc ca yoga-smṛtiḥ api taraṇī-gata-rajasamīmuludrāgaṇa-dṛṣṭānta-smṛtiḥ iti na tayā vedānta-upeyaṃ hūṇaṃ nyāyyam iti \|\| 3 \|\| iti yoga-pratyukty-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha vilakṣaṇatvādhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.1.4 \|\|

*na vilakṣaṇatvāt asya tathātvaṃ ca śabdāt*

**Комментарий:**

punar api smṛti-virodha-vādī tarkam avalambamānaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—yat asmṛti-upabṛṃhita-nirākaraṇena jagataḥ brahma-kāryatvam uktam, tat na upapadyate; asya pratyakṣa-ādibhiḥ cetana-acetanatvena anubhūyamānasya eva na ādeḥ cetana-duḥkhātmakatvena ca upalabhyamānasya vicitra-vicitrātmakasya jagataḥ bhavat-samya-etat-sarvajña-sarveśvara-ādheya-prapañcī-kāraṇa-ānanda-ekānta-ālaṅkṛhano vilakṣaṇatvāt \| na kevalaṃ pratyakṣa-ādibhiḥ eva jagataḥ vailakṣaṇyam upalabhyate; śabdaḥ ca tathātvaṃ—vilakṣaṇatvam, upalabhate—vijñānaṃ ca avijñānaṃ ca evaṃ eva etā bhūta-mātrāḥ prajñā-mātrāsu arpitāḥ prajñā-mātrāḥ prāṇe arpitāḥ samāne vṛkṣe puruṣaḥ nimagnaḥ anīśayā śocati muhyamānaḥ anīśaḥ ca ātmā badhyate bhoktṛ-bhāvāt īśana-ādibhiḥ kāryasya hi jagataḥ acetanatva-duḥkhitva-bādhaḥ nirdiśyate \| yadi yat akāryam, tat sattamāha-vilakṣaṇam, yathā mṛt-suvarṇa-ādi-kāryaṃ ghaṭa-kaṭaka-ādi \| ataḥ brahma-vilakṣaṇasya asya jagataḥ tat-kāryatvaṃ na saṃbhavati iti sāṅkhya-smṛti-anudṛṣṭena kārya-lakṣaṇaṃ pradhānam eva kāraṇaṃ bhavitum arhati \| avagataṃ ca śāstrasya ananya-apekṣayā atīndriya-artha-gocarataḥ api tarka-iḍamparīṇaiḥ; yataḥ sarveṣāṃ pramāṇānāṃ hṛta-vikati-viddhiṣu api tarka-anugṛhītānām eva artha-niścaya-hetutvam \| tarkaṃ hi nāma—artha-svabhāva-viṣayeṇa vā sāmagrī-viṣayeṇa vā nirūpaṇena artha-viśeṣe pramāṇaṃ vyavasthāpayati iti tat-kṛta-vyatā-rūpa-dhūta-prapañcaṃ jñānam \| tat-apekṣā ca sarveṣāṃ pramāṇānāṃ samānā \| śāstrasya tu viśeṣa-grāhakatvāt ahita-yogyatā-nātādhi-pramāṇa-bhāvasya sarveṣu eva tarka-anugraha-apekṣā \| uktaṃ ca manunā yaḥ tarkeṇa anusandhatte sa dharmaṃ veda na itaraḥ iti \| tat eva hi tarka-anugṛhīta-śāstra-artha-śrī-viṣṇu-purāṇe śrutyā ca mantavyaḥ iti ucyate \|\|

atha ucyeta—śrutyā jagataḥ brahma-eka-kāraṇatve niścite sati tat-kāryasya api jagat-caitanya-anuguṇa-nirastva-upapadyate \| yathā cetanasya suṣupti-mūrchā-ādiṣu caitanya-anupalabdhiḥ; tathā ghaṭa-ādiṣu api sat eva caitanyam anudbhūta-taram; ataḥ eva caitanya-acetana-vibhāgaḥ—iti \| na etat upapadyate, yataḥ nitya-anupalabdhiḥ asad-bhāvam eva sādhayati \| ataḥ eva caitanya-śakti-yogaḥ api teṣu nirastaḥ \| yasya hi kadācit kā api icchā api yat-kārya-upalabdhiḥ, tasya tat-kāraṇa-śaktiḥ bṛṃ vāṇo vandhyā-suta-sanniviṣu tat-saṃbandhaḥ na prajāyate iti brūyāt \| kiñca vedānte jagataḥ brahma-upādānatā-mātra-pratipādana-niścaye sati ghaṭa-ādīnāṃ caitanya-śaktiḥ caitanyasya vā anudbhūtasya saṃbhavet; tat-niścaye sati vedānte jagataḥ brahma-upādānatā-mātra-pratipādana-niścayaḥ iti itaretara-āśrayatvam \| vilakṣaṇayoḥ hi kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ pratipādayitum eva na śakyate \|\|

kiṃ punaḥ prakṛti-vikārayoḥ sālakṣaṇyam abhipretam, yat-abhāvāt jagataḥ brahma-upādānatā-pratipādana-asaṃbhavaṃ brūṣe ? na tāvat sarva-dharma-sālakṣaṇyam, kārya-kāraṇa-bhāva-anupapatteḥ \| na hi mṛt-piṇḍa-kāryaṃ

ghaṭārāva-ādīṣu, piṇḍatva-bāhu-bhujatvādi-dharma-mate \| ayaṃ yena ekena chinna-mūlaṃ sālakṣyam, tad-rūpānupraveśe api santati-lakṣaṇaṃ saṃbhavati \| tad ucyate—yena svabhāvena kāraṇa-bhūtaṃ vastu vastu-antara-ārambha-yogyam, tasya svabhāvasya tat-kāryāvasthā-buddhiḥ kāryasya kāraṇa-sālakṣaṇyam \| yena hi ākāreṇa mṛd-ādibhyo hiraṇyaṃ vyāpatte, tad-ākārānupraveśita-kāryaṃ kuṇḍalādiṣu dṛśyate \| brahma ca heya-pratyanīka-kalyāṇānandaiśvarya-svabhāvam, jagat ca tat-pratyānīka-bhāvam iti na tad-rūpatānam \|\|

nanu ca vailakṣaṇye api kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvo dṛśyate, yathā cetanāt puruṣāt cetanāni keśa-nakha-danta-lomāni jāyante; yathā ca acetanāt gomayāt cetano vṛściko jāyate; cetanāc chepanatāruś cetanas tantuḥ \| na etad evam, yatas tatra api acetanāṃśa eva kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

atha syāt—acetanatvena abhimatānām api caitanya-yogya-śrutiṣu śrūyate śruteḥ pṛthivya brabīta, āpo vā akāmayanta śreyo hame prāṇā ahaṃ-śreyase vivadamānā brahmāṇo jagmuḥ iti \| na deśa-bhūta-preta-ātmanām api cetanatvaṃ paurāṇikā ātiṣṭhante; ato na vailakṣaṇyam iti \| ata uttaraṃ paṭhati—

## \|\| 2.1.5 \|\|

*abhimānin-vyapadeśas tu viśeṣa-anugati-bhyām*

**Комментарий:**

tṛṇa-ādi-vat ca udita-tākhyā-nivṛtty-arthaḥ; pṛthivy-ādi-abhimāninyo devatāḥ taṃ pṛthivy abravīt ity-ādiṣu pṛthivy-ādi-pada-vācyeṣu upadiśyante \| kutaḥ ? viśeṣa-anugati-bhyām \| viśeṣaḥ—viśeṣaṇam, devatā-śabdena viśeṣya-pṛthivy-ādi-yogādhiyecyate; haṃtāhṛyamārpitayo devatāḥ iti tejo-bannāni devatā-śabdena viśeṣyante; sarvā ha vai devatā ahaṃ-śreyase vivadamānāḥ me devāḥ prāṇe niḥśreyasaṃ vividiṣuḥ iti ca \| anugatiḥ—anupraveśaḥ; agnir vāg-bhūtvā mukhaṃ prāviśat, ādityaś cakṣur bhūtvā akṣiṇī prāviśat, vāyuḥ prāṇo bhūtvā nāsike prāviśat ity-ādinā vāg-ādi-abhimāni-devatānām āyatana-anupraveśa-śruteḥ \| ato jagataḥ acetanatvena vilakṣaṇarahād brahma-kāryatvānupapattes tarkānugṛhīta-smṛtyupetarodhit jagataḥ pradhāna-upādānatvaṃ vedāntaiḥ pratipadyate \|\| 5 \|\|

**iti prāpte abhidhīyate—**

## \|\| 2.1.6 \|\|

*dṛśyate tu*

**Комментарий:**

tṛṇandad-pakṣo viparivartate; yad uktaṃ jagato brahma-vilakṣaṇatvena brahma-upādānatvaṃ na saṃbhavati iti; tad ayuktam, vilakṣaṇayoḥ api kārya-kāraṇa-bhāva-darśanāt \| dṛśyate hi māhikā-ādi-vilakṣaṇasya krimi-dehenasabhāṣam-utpattiḥ \| mṛtaka-sacetāṃśa eva kārya-kāraṇa-bhāve atra sālakṣaṇyam \| tatra yuktam; na tāvatā kārya-kāraṇayor bhinna-sattā-lakṣaṇa-siddhiḥ \| yathā kṣetrajña-sālakṣaṇye sarvasya sarva-sālakṣyeṇa sarva-saṃtaty-vaiparīty-unimiṣaḍ-rūpatādbhuta-rajamo vastu-antara-ārambha-yogyatve hetu-bhūtasya kārasya tādṛśānu

hṛdi-stilakṣaṇyaṃ bhavatā api upetam \| sa tu niyamo mokṣa-kartṛṣu kriyāṣu adyapaso na hṛdyata iti brahma-vilakṣaṇasya api jagato brahma-kāryatvaṃ na upapadyam \| na hi mṛd-viracita-ghaṭa-mukuṭādi-vilakṣaṇa-vastv-antara-vyāhṛti-hetu-bhūta-sādhāraṇākāra-anugatiḥ mokṣa-karma-upayogi-kriyādiṣu hṛdyate \|\| 6 \|\|

## \|\| 2.1.7 \|\|

*asad iti cet, pratiṣedha-mātratvāt*

**Комментарий:**

yadi kārya-bhūtāt jagataḥ kāraṇa-bhūtaṃ brahma vilakṣaṇam, tarhi kārya-kāraṇayoḥ aikyātyantarekheṇā kāraṇe parasmin brahmaṇi kāryaṃ jagat vidyate iti asat eva jagat utpattiḥ prasajyata iti cetaḥ, na eva idam evam, kārya-kāraṇayoḥ saṃlakṣaṇa-niyama-pratiṣedha-mātram eva hi pūrva-sūtre abhihitam, na tu kāraṇa-kāryasya dravyāntaratvam \| kāraṇa-bhūtaṃ brahma eva svarūpādi-lakṣaṇānyathākāreṇa pariṇamati iti etat tu na parityaktam \| kṣīra-madhikāro api hi sati ca vailakṣaṇye kuṇḍala-hiraṇyayor iva dravyaikatvam atyeva \|\| 7 \|\|

**atra vocyate—**

## \|\| 2.1.8 \|\|

*apītau tadvat-prasaṅgād asamañjasam*

**Комментарий:**

upītau vicchaya-viśiṣṭa-kaṇṭhavadbhiḥ pradarśitārtham, sa eva somya idam āsīd, ātmā vā idam eka eva agra āsīd ityādi-vākya-upapādita-upadeśa-pūrvaka-khalikṛta-svarūpa-deḥ \| yadi kārya-kāraṇayoḥ vaicitryam asmyupetam, tadā kāryasya jagato brāhmaṇa-aikyātmakatva-prakṛti-aikyaṃ satyaṃ brāhmaṇa eva tattva-vyarthatvaṃ iti kāryatā sarvaṃ eva apurṇārthaḥ; brāhmaṇi pralayet, suvarṇa iva kuṇḍalatātāviśeṣaḥ \| tatra eva vedānta-vākyāṃ sarvemasyaivaṃ syāt, yat edaṃ sarvaṃ tat viditam apahāta-pāpmā vijaro vimṛtyuḥ na tasya kāryaṃ karaṇaṃ ca vidyate na tat-samaḥ ca abhyadhikaḥ ca dṛśyate tamyotrasya pipalaṃ svādit iti anīśayā ātmā badhyate bhoktṛ-bhāvāt anīśayā śocati muhyamānaḥ iti eka-rūpasya eva vastu-dvayā paraspara-viruddha-dharmā prasaṅgaḥ \|\|

atha ucyeta—cid-acid-vastu-śarīrakasya parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvāt śarīra-bhūta-cid-acid-vastu-gata-tattvāt ca doṣoṇās, na śarīriṇi brahmaṇi kāryāvasthye kāraṇāvasthye ca prasaṅgaḥ iti \| tad ayuktam, jagad-brahmaṇoḥ śarīra-śarīri-bhāvasya etat-sambandhāt; sambandhe ca brahmaṇi śarīra-saṃbandha-nibandhana-doṣāṇāṃ nirvartanāt \| na hi cid-dacid-vastu-metre hūmupasarīratvaṃ sambhavati \| śarīra hi nāma karma-phala-upabhoga-bhūtaṃ kṣetrajña-upabhoga-sādhana-bhūta-indriya-āśrayaḥ pañca-vṛtti-prāṇa-ādhāraṇaḥ pṛthivyādi-mūta-bhūta-saṅghāta-viśeṣaḥ; tathā-vidhasya ca laukika-deva-adhi-śarīratva-prasiddhiḥ \| parasmin brahmaṇi ca apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ antar-bahiḥ ca tat sarvaṃ vyāpya nārāyaṇaḥ sthitaḥ apāṇi-pādo javano grahītā paśyati acakṣuḥ sa śṛṇoti akarṇaḥ aprāṇo hi amanāḥ iti evaṃ-vidhaiḥ karma-tat-phala-bhoktṛ-tad-upabhoga-kṣetra-ādi-siddha-vyatīta-śarīratva-bādha-pramāṇa-

vastu-abhāvāc ca na taṃ prati cetana-acetanayoḥ īśvara-śarīratvam \| na ca acetana-vyatirikta-guṇaka-ādīnāṃ samṛddhi-rūpasya bhūta-sūkṣmasya ca indriya-āśrayatva-ādi saṃbhavati \| bhūta-sūkṣmasya pṛthivī-ādi-visajātīyatvaṃ ca na vidyate \| cetanasya tu jñāna-eka-ākāratayā sarva-avasthāsu saṃbhava iti tatra śarīratva-saṃbhavaḥ \| na ca bhoga-āyatanaṃ ca śarīratvam iti śarīratva-saṃbhavaḥ; bhoga-āyataneṣu vaiṣamyāt eva śarīratva-aprasiddheḥ \| yatra vartamānatvena sukha-duḥkha-upabhogaḥ, tat eva bhoga-āyatanam iti cet; para-kāya-praveśa-janu-sukha-duḥkha-upabhoga-āyatanasya para-kāyasya pratiṣṭhā-śarīratva-aprasiddheḥ \| īśvarasya tu svābhisiddha-nitya-nirvikriyānandasya bhogaṃ prati siddha-citta-ur-āyatanatva-niyamo na saṃbhavati \| etena bhoga-asādhanamātrasya śarīratvaṃ pratyuktam \|\|

atha matam, yat icchādhīna-svarūpa-sthiti-pravṛtti yat tat asya śarīram iti, sarvasya īśvara-icchādhīna-svarūpa-sthiti-pravṛttitvena śarīratvaṃ saṃbhavatīti; tad api na sādhīyaḥ; śarīratayā prasiddheṣu tat-tat eva etana-icchāyatta-svarūpatva-abhāvāt, karṇa-śarīrasya tat-icchādhīna-pravṛttitva-abhāvāt, mṛta-śarīrasya tat-aṅga-ceti-sthititva-abhāvāc ca, śālagrāmika-ādiṣu cetana-icchādhīna-svarūpa-sthiti-pravṛttiṣu tat-śarīratva-aprasiddheś ca \| cetanasya nityasya īśvara-icchāyatta-svarūpatva-abhāvāc ca, na tat-śarīratva-saṃbhavaḥ \| na ca yat deha-eka-nigūḍham, yat ekaṃ-dharyam, yasya eva śeṣa-bhūtam, tat tasya śarīram iti vācyam; hriy-ādiṣu vyabhicārāt \| aśarīraṃ śarīreṣu aprāṇi-vāccho javano grahītā iti-ādi-śrutibhyaḥ īśvarasya śarīra-abhāvaḥ pratipādyate \| ataḥ jagad-brahmaṇoḥ śarīra-śarīri-bhāva-dhyāna-saṃbhavāt tanmave ca brahmaṇi doṣa-saṅgāt brahma-kāraṇa-vāde vedānta-vākyānām asāmañjasyam iti \|\|5\|\|

ayogam—

## \|\| 2.1.9 \|\|

*na tu dṛṣṭānta-abhāvāt*

**Комментарий:**

na evam asāmañjasyam, ekaś cet avasthā-ākāratve 'pi guṇa-doṣa-vyavasthiteḥ śrutatvasya vidyamānatvāt \| bṛhad-āraṇye hi asaṃbaddha-bhāvasya saṃbhavanīyatāṃ śocayati \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—cid-acid-vastu-śarīratayā tad-ātmaka-bhūtasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ sadoṣa-vikāsa-ātmakakārya-kāraṇa-bhāva-avasthā-dhyānayoḥ api na kaścid virodhaḥ; parasya sadoṣa-vikāśaḥ para-brahma-śarīra-bhūta-cid-acid-vastu-gataḥ; śarīra-gatās tu doṣā na ātmani prasajyante; ātma-gatāś ca guṇā na śarīre; yathā deva-manuṣya-ādīnāṃ rasa-rūpāṇāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ śarīra-gatā bālatva-yauvatva-avasthā-vikāra-ādayo na asmin saṃbadhyante; ātma-gatāś ca jñāna-sukha-ādayo na śarīre \| atha ca devaḥ jātaḥ, manuṣyaḥ jātaḥ; tathā sa eva bālaḥ yuvā sthaviraś ca iti vyapadeśasya mukhyaḥ \| bhūta-sūkṣma-śarīrasya eva kṣetrajñasya deva-manuṣya-ādi-bhāva iti tat-anarha-vitanto iti vakṣyate iti \|\|

muktānantaṃ cid-acid-ātmakasya jagad-avasthālasya sūkṣmasya ca paramātmānaṃ prati śarīra-

bhāvaḥ na upapadyate iti; tad-ukta-kālita-smṛty-antara-nyāya-mukhya-vedānta-vākya-gaṇasya svarūpa-tirohita-kutarka-vitṛmbhitasya \| sarva eva hi vedāntāḥ sthūlasya sūkṣmasya cetanasya acetanasya samastasya ca paramātmānaṃ prati śarīratvaṃ śrāvayanti \| vājasaneyake tāvat kāṇva-śākhāyāṃ mādhyandina-śākhāyāṃ bālāntyāmina-brāhmaṇe śrayaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan... yasya pṛthivī śarīram iti ārasya pṛthivy-ādi-samastam abhihatya, yaḥ vijñāne tiṣṭhan... yasya vijñānaṃ śarīram śraya ātmani tiṣṭhan... yasya ātmā śarīram iti cetanaṃ ca pṛthak upasthāpya tasya tasya paramātma-śarīratvam abhidhīyate \| subālopaniṣadi ca yaḥ pṛthivīm antare sañcaran yasya pṛthivī śarīram iti ārasya tad eva cid-acitoḥ tat-savikasayoḥ paramātma-śarīratvam abhidhāya eṣa sarva-bhūta-antarātmā apahata-pāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ iti tasya sarva-bhūtāni praty-ātmatvam abhidhīyate \| smaranti ca jagat sarvaṃ śarīraṃ te yat ambu vaiṣṇavaḥ kāyaḥ tat sarvaṃ vai hareḥ tanūḥ tāni sarvāṇi tad-bapuḥ sarvo hi sthāya śarīra-grasta-vāstho ity-ādi \| bhūta-sūkṣmātmatvāc ca śarīratvārthaḥ \|\|

loke ca śarīra-śabdo ghaṭādi-śabdavat ekākāra-dravya-niyataḥ kṛmi-koṭa-pataṅga-sarpa-nara-paśu-prabhṛtiṣv atyanta-vilakṣaṇākāreṣu buddhebhagunaḥ prayujyamāno dṛśyate \| tena tasya pravṛtti-nimitta-vyavasthāpane sarva-prayogānugameṇa eva kāryam \| laghu-kṛtaṃ ca karma-phala-bhoga-hetutvādikaṃ pravṛtti-nimitta-lakṣaṇaṃ na sarva-prayogānuguṇam, yatheṣṭa-ceṣṭā-śarīratvādibhiḥ eva pṛthivy-ādi-vyavacchidyateḥ \| kiñca īśvarasya iṣṭa-vicchetuḥ, muktānāṃ ca sa ekadhā bhavatīty-ādi-vākyānavibu-vipheṣu tat-lakṣaṇam avyāptam, karma-phala-bhogi-nimittatvābhāvāt teṣām \| parama-puruṣecchā-vighraś ca na pṛthivy-ādi-sūtra-sūtrita-viśeṣaḥ; na bhūta-saṅgha-saṃsthāno dehaḥ asya paramātmanaḥ iti smṛteḥ \| ataḥ bhūta-saṅghāta-rūpatvaṃ ca śarīrasya vyāptam \| pañca-vṛtti-prāṇādi-dhāraṇatvaṃ ca sthāvara-śarīreṣu avyāptam \| sthāvareṣu hi prāṇa-vṛtteḥ api tasya pañcadhā avasthāya śarīrasya dhārakatvena avasthānaṃ nāsti \| aharyādīnāṃ karma-nimita-himakarābhiniśarīrendriyāśrayatvaṃ sukha-duḥkha-hetutvaṃ ca avyāptam \|\|

ataḥ yasya cetanasya yat dravyaṃ sarvātmanā svārthe niyantuṃ dhārayituṃ ca śakyaṃ, tat-śeṣataika-svarūpaṃ ca, tasya śarīram iti śarīra-lakṣaṇam āśrayam \| iṣṭa-śarīrādi-viṣu niyamanādi-adarśanaṃ vidyamānayā eva niyamana-śakteḥ pratibandha-kṛtam, anyādṛśa-śakti-pratibandhādoṣa-darśanavat \| mṛta-śarīraṃ ca cetana-viyoga-samaya eva viśīryitum ārabdhaṃ; kṣaṇāntare ca viśīryate \| pūrvaṃ śarīratayā parikḷptatama-dravya-eka-deśatvena ca tatra śarīratva-vyavahāraḥ \| ataḥ sarvaṃ paraspuruṣeṇa sarvātmanā svārthe niyāmyaṃ dhāryaṃ tat-śeṣataika-svarūpam iti sarvaṃ cetana-acetanaṃ tasya śarīram \| akṣaraṃ śarīreṣu ity-ādi ca karma-nimitita-śarīra-pratibodhakam, yathokta-sarva-śarīratva-lakṣaṇāt \| upari-

tam adhikaraṇeṣu ca etad upapādayiṣyate \| apīto tadvat-prasañjād asamañjasam na tu dṛṣṭānta-bhāvāt iti sūtra-dvayena itara-vyapadeśāt tu iti adhikaraṇa-siddhaḥ arthaḥ smāritaḥ \|\|9\|\|

## \|\| 2.1.10 \|\|

*sva-pakṣa-doṣāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

na kevalaṃ brahma-kāraṇa-vādasya nirdokṣatva-hetu-samāśrayam; pradhāna-kāraṇa-vādasya duṣṭatvāt ca tat-parityājyatvam eva samāśrayaṇīyam \| pradhāna-kāraṇa-vāde hi jagad-utpattiḥ na upapadyate \| tatra hi nirvikārasya cin-mātraika-rasasya puruṣasya prakṛti-saṃvidhānena prakṛti-vidhy-āsa-nibandhanā jagat-sṛṣṭiḥ \| nirvikārasya cin-mātra-rūpasya prakṛti-vadhy-āsa-hetu-bhūtaṃ prakṛti-vidhyānaṃ kiṃ rūpam iti vivecanīyam \| kiṃ pradhāne asya draṣṭṛtvam eva ? uta tad-ūhāḥ kartṛ-dhikāraḥ ? atha puruṣa-gata eva kathañcid vikāraḥ ? na tāvat puruṣa-gataḥ, anabhyupagamāt \| na api prakṛti-vikāraḥ, tasya adhyāsa-kāryatayā puruṣa-itarasya adhyāsāhetutvā-sambhavāt; rajju-samānātve su-piṣṭatvāt muktasya adhyāsa-prasaṅgaḥ iti tvat-pakṣe jagad-utpattiḥ na upapadyate \| ayam arthaḥ sāṅkhya-pakṣa-pratisṣepara-samaye abhyupagame api arthābhāvāt u iti atra pratipādayiṣyate \|\|10\|\|

## \|\| 2.1.11 \|\|

*tarka-apratiṣṭhānāt api*

**Комментарий:**

tarkasya apratiṣṭhitatvāt api śruti-mūlo brahma-kāraṇa-vāda eva samāśrayaṇīyaḥ; na pradhāna-kāraṇa-vādaḥ \| śāstra-yoni-adhyākṣa-pada-dvaya-parikalpa-tat-pratitarka-guṇa-madhyo-rathyāghāta-sārkaryā-apratiṣṭhitatvaṃ gamyate \|\|11\|\|

## \|\| 2.1.12 \|\|

*anyathānumeyam iti cet evam api animokṣa-prasaṅgaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

etāni vidyamānānāṃ śāstra-yonīnāṃ tarka-anugrāhyatayā pradhāna-kāraṇa-vāda-samudbhāvitāni duṣaṇāni guṇatve na anumantavyāni iti cet; evam api puruṣa-buddhi-mūla-tarkāvalambanasya tathaiva deśāntara-kālāntareṣu evam avadhikṛta-tarka-kuśala-puruṣair dūṣita-tarkābhyutthāpana-sambhavanayā tarka-apratiṣṭhāna-doṣāt nirmokṣo durvāraḥ \| ataḥ atīndriyārthe śāstram eva pramāṇam; tad uktaṃ hṛṇāṃ ca tarke upādeyaḥ; tathā ca āha ārṣaṃ dharmopadeśaṃ ca veda-śāstra-avirodhinā \| yas tarkeṇa anusandhatte sa dharmaṃ veda na itaraḥ iti \| veda-śāstra-avirodhinā iti arthaḥ \| ataḥ veda-virodhitvena vedārtha-sahakāritva-rūpa-veda-aṅgatvād apādatayā sāṅkhya-smṛtir nirastanīyā \|\|12\|\| iti vilakṣaṇatvādhikaraṇam \|\|3\|\|

**atha śiṣṭāparigrahādhikaraṇam \|\|4\|\|**

## \|\| 2.1.13 \|\|

*etena śiṣṭāparigrahā api vyākhyātāḥ*

**Комментарий:**

śiṣṭāḥ—pariśiṣṭāḥ; na vidyate veda-parigrahaḥ yeṣām iti aparigrahāḥ; śiṣṭāḥ ca aparigrahāḥ ca śiṣṭāparigrahāḥ; etena—veda-parigṛhīta-sāṅkhya-pakṣa-kṣapaṇena, pariśiṣṭāḥ ca veda-parigṛhītāḥ kaṇ-

bhavābādhya-svarūpa-karmiśra-padārthaḥ kṣaṇikā vaidikāś ca \| paramāṇu-kāraṇa-vāde 'pi mokṣaṃ sarveṣāṃ saṃvāda-lakṣaṇa-vastu-vivakṣasya tarka-śāstra-apatiṣṭhitatvaṃ na śakyate vaktum iti adhikaraṇa-dvayam \| tāvan-mātra-saṃvāde 'pi tarka-mūlatva-aviśeṣāt paramāṇu-svarūpe 'pi śūnyatva-pakṣatvāt śūnyātmakatva-vijñānātmakatva-arthitakatva-kṣaṇikatva-anityatva-ekāntatva-anekatva-saṃsāra-tātmakatvādi-viśeṣa-avedarśanāc ca apratiṣṭhitatvam eva iti parihāraḥ \|\| 13 \|\| iti śiṣṭa-aparigraha-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha bhoktāpatti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.1.14 \|\|

*bhoktāpatter avibhāgaś cet syāt lokavat*

**Комментарий:**

punar api sāṅkhyaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—yuktam, sthūla-sūkṣma-cid-acid-vastu-śarīrasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ kārya-kāraṇa-avasthayoḥ jīva-brahmaṇoḥ tat-svabhāva-vibhāgaḥ upapadyata iti; sa tu vibhāgo na saṃbhavati, brahmaṇaḥ śarīratve tasya bhoktṛtva-āpatteḥ, śarīratve jīvasya eva īśvarasya api śarīritva-prayukta-sukha-duḥkha-abhoktṛtvasya varjanīyatvāt \| nanu ca śrutyā bhoga-prasaṅga-parihāraḥ uktaḥ; na itaraḥ tatra hi upāsanatayā hṛdi āyatane saṃhitasya śarīra-antar-dhṛtatva-mātreṇa bhoga-prasaṅgo na vidyata iti uktam, iha tu jīva-vastuṣu api tāvat etat-sukha-duḥkha-abhoktṛtva-prasaṅgo duṣkara iti ucyate \| hṛdyate hi sa-śarīrāṇāṃ jīvānāṃ śarīra-gata-bālatva-yuvatva-vṛddhatvādi-vikāra-saṃsarge 'pi śarīra-dhātu-maya-vaiṣamya-kṛta-nimita-sukha-duḥkha-yogaḥ \| śrutiś ca śnau ha vai sa-śarīrasya sataḥ priyāpriyayoḥ apahatiḥ asti a-śarīraṃ vā va santaṃ na priyāpriye spṛśataḥ ś iti \| ataḥ śarīra-brahma-kāraṇa-vāde jīveśvara-svabhāva-vibhāga-asaṃbhavāt kevala-brahma-kāraṇa-vāde mṛt-suvarṇādi-vat jaḍa-upādāna-apuruṣārtha-siddhi-virodha-prathitava-brahma-jñānaṃ ca pradhāna-kāraṇa-vāda eva upapadyān iti cet;

atra uttaram—syāt lokavat iti \| syāt eva vibhāgo jīveśvara-svabhāvayoḥ \| na hi jīvasya śarīra-dhātu-sāmya-vaiṣamya-nimittaṃ sukha-duḥkha-abhoktṛtvaṃ sa-śarīratva-kṛtam, api tu puṇya-pāpa-rūpa-karma-kṛtam \| śnau ha vai sa-śarīrasyety api karma-ārabdhe deha-viṣayam, śsa ekadhā bhavati tridhā bhavati śsa yadi pitṛ-loka-kāmo bhavati śsa tatra paryeti jakṣat-krīḍan-ramamāṇaḥ śiti karma-deha-avinirmuktasya api śrutiḥ tat-svarūpasya śarīra-saṃyoga-pūrva-artha-gandha-abhāvāt \| apahata-pāpmānaṃ tu paramātmanaḥ sthūla-sūkṣma-sakala-puruṣa-tanajacchūrīratve 'pi karma-saṃbandha-abhāvo nāsti iti na tarām prākṛta-puruṣārtha-gandha-prasaṅgaḥ \| lokavat tu—yathā loke rāja-śāsana-anuvartināṃ tad-vivartināṃ ca rāja-puruṣaiḥ prabhūta-sukha-duḥkha-yogaḥ api na śarīratva-mātreṇa śāsake rājñā api śāsana-anuvṛtti-anivṛtti-nimitta-sukha-duḥkha-yoga-bhoktṛtva-prasaṅgaḥ \| yathāha dramiḍa-bhāṣya-kāraḥ śyathā loke rājā prabhṛta-dṛśyuke dhāraṇa-yakṣus tair api

pradeśo vartamāno vyajyamāna-dhṛta-deho doṣaṃ na spṛśyate, abhinetāṃś ca lokān paripālayati, bhogāṃś ca gandhādi-vicitra-nepathyanādyādhārṣati, tathā asau lokeśvaro bhramas tu asāmarthya-mātre doṣaṃ na spṛśyate, rakṣati ca lokān sva-lokādīn, bhogāṃś ca vicitra-nepathyādy-ādhārayati iti \| mṛt-suvarṇādi-vad brahma-svarūpa-pariṇāmas tu na eva abhyupagamyate, avikāratva-nirdeśanyādi-śruteḥ \|\| yat u paraiḥ hi aṅkāra-pāde bhoktṛ-bhogya-vibhāga-śāstram āśritya samudra-phena-taraṅgāghātetana-vibhāga-pratipādana-paraṃ sūtraṃ vyākhyātam; tat ayuktam, ananta-abhivistara-cana-viyogādhikād brahmaṇas tṛtīyāṃśāvacchannocchitāneka-praveśa-parihārayor asya sūtratvāt, kāraṇān na antara-śabda-viyogādy-upahitasya bhoktṛtvād upādheś ca bhogyatvāt; vilakṣaṇayoḥ iti ayoḥ paraspara-svabhāva-vṛttir hi na saṃbhavati \| svarūpa-pariṇāmas tu taiḥ api na abhyupetaḥ, na karma-vibhāgād iti caitrādi-navād iti kṣetrajñānāṃ brahma-gata-karmāṇāṃ caitrāditya-pratipādanāt \| svarūpa-pariṇāmābhyupagame api bhettṛ-bhogya-vibhāga-śāstrānuguṇyaṃ kathaṃ-cid api na jāyate, mṛt-suvarṇādi-pariṇāma-spaṣṭa-dṛśarāvaka-kaṭaka-mukuṭādi-vibhāga-dṛṣṭi-vaibhodya-vibhāgopapatteḥ \| svarūpa-pariṇāme ca brahmaṇa eva bhoktṛ-bhogyatvāpatitir iti punar api asāmañjasyam eva \|\|14\|\| iti bhoktṛ-pādy-adhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|

**atha ārambhaṇādhikaraṇam \|\|6\|\|**

## \|\| 2.1.15 \|\|

*tad-ananyatvam ārambhaṇa-śabdādibhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

arpita iti caitra pratiṣedha-mātratvāt ityādibhiḥ kāraṇa-bhūtād brahmaṇaḥ kārya-bhūtasya jagato anyatvam abhyupagamya brahmaṇo jagat-kāraṇatvam upapāditam, idānīṃ tat eva ananyatvam ākṣipya samādhīyate \|\| tatra kāṇādāḥ prāhuḥ—na kāraṇāt kāryasya ananyatvaṃ saṃbhavati, vilakṣaṇa-buddhi-bodhyatvāt—na khalu tantu-paṭa-dṛṣṭi-puṣpavad iṣu kārya-kāraṇa-viṣayā buddhir eka-rūpā \| śābda-bhedāc ca—na hi tantavaḥ paṭa iti ucyante, paṭo vā tantava iti \| kārya-bhedāc ca—na hi mṛt-piṇḍena udakam āhriyate, ghaṭena vā kuḍyaṃ nirmīyate \| kāla-bhedāc ca—pūrva-kālaṃ ca kāraṇam, apara-kālaṃ ca kāryam \| ākāra-bhedāc ca piṇḍākāraṃ kāraṇam, kāryaṃ ca pṛthu-budhno-darākāram \| tathā satyām eva mṛdi ghaṭo naṣṭa iti vyavahriyate \| saṃkhyā-bhedaś ca dṛśyate—bahavas tantavaḥ, ekaś ca paṭaḥ \| kāraka-vyāpāra-bhedaś ca—kāraṇam eva cet kāryaṃ kiṃ kāraka-vyāpāra-sādhyaiḥ syāt ? satya-kārye kāryeṣa-yogitayā kāraka-vyāpāreṇa bhavitavyaṃ ced, sarvadā kāraka-vyāpāreṇa na uparataṃ syāt \| sarvasya sarvadā sattvena nityānitya-vibhāgaś ca na syāt \| atha kāryaṃ sadaiva pūrvam anirvṛttaṃ kāraka-vyāpāreṇābhi-

vyajyate, ataḥ kāraṇa-vyāpāratvaṃ, nitya-anitya-vibhāga-śocyaṃ; tat-satvam, agnīṣomādeḥ ca vyavasthā-antara-apekṣatve anavasthānāt anapekṣatve kāryasya nitya-upalabdha-prasaṅgāt udaya-vyaya-dharmam e ca asta-kārya-vāda-prasajñatvāt \| kiṃ ca kāraṇa-vyāpārasya abhivyañjakatve ghaṭārthaṃ kāraṇa-vyāpāreṇa kṛta-tarpaṇa-abhivyaktiḥ prasajyate, saṃtati-vyābhivyañjaka-mātreṣu dīpādi-abhivyañja-viśeṣa-niyama-darśanāt; na hi ghaṭārtham ārpitāḥ pradīpāḥ karakaṭa-ādi-abhivyanakti \| ataḥ asatḥ kāryasya utpatti-hetutvena eva kāraṇa-vyāpāra-arthavatvam, ataḥ ca sat-kārya-vāda-atiriktiḥ \| na ca niyatam kāraṇa-upādānaṃ sat eva kāryatve sādhayati; kāraṇa-śakti-niyamaḥ eva tat-upapatteḥ \| nanu sat-sakārya-vādinaḥ api kāraṇa-vyāpāraḥ na upapadyate; prāk upatteḥ kāryasya asattvāt kāryatve ca kāraṇa-vyāpāreṇa bhavitavyam; tasya anyathā-viśeṣāt tat-guṇa-kāraṇa-vyāpāreṇa ghaṭa-utpattiḥ api prasajyate \| na evaṃ, yathā-kārya-utpādana-śaktaṃ yat kāraṇam, tat-guṇa-kāraṇa-vyāpāreṇa tat-kārya-niṣpattiḥ \|\|

atra āhuḥ—kāraṇāt ananya-kāryaṃ \| na hi paramārthaḥ kāraṇa-vyatiriktaṃ kāryaṃ nāma vastu asti, avidyā-nibandhana-nāma-kalā-kāryatva-vyavahārayaḥ \| ataḥ yathā kāraṇa-bhūtāt mṛd-vyatirekeṇa vikārebhyaḥ paramārtha-abhyatiriktaṃ ghaṭa-śarāva-ādi-kāryaṃ vyavahāra-mātra-ālambanaṃ mithyā, kāraṇa-bhūtaṃ mṛd-dravyam eva satyam; tathā nirviśeṣa-san-mātra-kāraṇa-bhūtāt brahmaṇaḥ api iti-ādi-vyavahāra-ālambanaṃ kṛtsnaḥ prapañcaḥ mithyā, kāraṇa-bhūtaṃ san-mātraṃ brahma eva satyam \| tasmāt kāraṇa-vyatiriktaṃ kāryaṃ nāsti iti kāraṇāt ananya-kāryaṃ \| na ca vācyam śuktikā-rajata-ādīnām iva ghaṭa-ādi-kāryāṇām asatyatva-prasiddheḥ dṛṣṭānta-upapattiḥ iti \| yataḥ tāni api yuktyā mṛd-dravya-mātram eva satyatvena vyavasthāpyante; tat-atiriktaṃ hi yuktyā bādhyate \| kā punaḥ atra yuktiḥ ? mṛd-dravya-mātra-rūpa-anuvartamānatvam, tat-atiriktasya ca vyāvartamānatvam \| rajju-sarpa-ādiṣu hi anuvartamānasya adhiṣṭhāna-bhūtasya rajjvādeḥ satyatā, vyāvartamānasya ca sarpa-bhūta-lāñchana-dhārādeḥ asatyatā dṛṣṭā; tathā eva anuvartamānam adhiṣṭhāna-bhūtaṃ mṛd-dravyam eva satyam, vyāvartamānāḥ tu ghaṭa-śarāva-ādayaḥ asatya-bhūtāḥ \|\|

kiñ ca sat ātmanaḥ vināśa-abhāvāt tatra śakti-vigaṇādiṣu labhyamānāt upalabdhi-vināśayoḥ api kāryaṃ sat-anad-dravya-atirekeṇa eva iti gamyate \| agni-vecanīyaṃ ca śuktikā-rajata-ādīnām eva \| tasya ca agni-vecanīyatvaṃ pratīti-bādhābhyāṃ siddham \| kiñ ca kārya-mṛd-upādāna-dravya-ādi-kāraṇabhyaṃ kismit vikṛtam eva kāryam utpadyati, uta kṛtsnaṃ viśeṣa-mātrasya ? na tāvat vikṛtam utpadyati, sarvadopaśyadavyaprasaṅgāt \| na api viśeṣāntaram api asyaṃ, viśeṣāntaram atra api viśeṣāntara-āpatti-pūrvakatvena bhavitavyam, tasyā api tathā eva anavasthānāt \| adhikṛtam eva deśa-kāla-nimitta-viśeṣa-saṃbandhaṃ kāryam iti upapadyate iti cet \| deśādi-viśeṣa-saṃbandhaḥ api hi avikṛtasya viśeṣāntara-mātrasya ca pūrvavat saṃbhavati \|\|

na ca vācyaṃ mṛt-suvarṇa-dundubhy-ādibhyo ghaṭa-rucaka-kṛṣṇāyodha-āsuptiḥ śruteḥ; śuktikā-rajat-ādi-vat īśa-kāla-ādi-tritva-yogāt dhīḥ bādhyam na hṛṣyate, ataḥ pratīti-śaraṇānāṃ kāraṇa-kāryayoḥ ita-svarūpa-apraṇītiḥ; vikalpa-sahatvāt—kiṃ hema-ādi-mātram eva svarūti kādeśoḥ ārambhakam? uta rucaka-ādi? atha rucakādy-anyaḥ hema-ādi? na tāvat hema-ādi-mātra-ārambhakam; hema-vyatiriktasya kāryasya abhāvāt, svarūmānaṃ pratyāsanna ārambhakatva-saṃbhavāc ca \| hema-vyatiriktaṃ svarūti kaṃ hṛṣyata iti cet; na hema-vyatiriktaṃ tat, hem-pratyabhijñānāt tad-vyatiriktatva-antarānupalabdheś ca \| buddhi-śabdāntar-ādibhiḥ vaiśvarūpa-vat nana-rūpatvaṃ sādhyam iti cet, abhihitataratva-bala-vannānāṃ buddhi-śabdāntar-ādīnāṃ śuktikā-rajata-śabda-buddhy-ādi-vat brahmāti-mūlatvena vastutva-rūpa-sadbhāvasya aśakyatvāt \| na api rucakādi-svarūti kādeḥ ārambhakam, svarūti ke hi rucakaṃ paṭa iva tantavo bhavanto ’pi nopalabhyate \| na api rucakādy-abhūtaṃ hem, rucakādy-ākāreṇa hema-svarūti ke ’ndupalabdheḥ \|\|

ataḥ mṛd-ādhikaraṇātiriktasya kāryasya asatyatva-darśanāt brahma-vyatiriktaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat kāryatvena mithyā-bhūtam \| tad-vad brahma-vyatirikta-mithyātva-sukha-pratipattaye kālpanika-mṛd-ādi-satyatvam āśritya kāryasya asatyatvaṃ pratipāditam \| paramārthatas tu mṛt-suvarṇādi-kāraṇam api ghaṭa-rucakādi-kārya-vat mithyā-bhūtam, brahma-kāryatva-viśeṣāt \| eṣa tat ātmyaṃ idaṃ sarvaṃ tat satyam neha nānā asti kiṃcana mṛtyoḥ sa mṛtyum āpnoti yaḥ iha nānā iva paśyati yatra hi dvaitam iva bhavati tat itaraḥ itaraṃ paśyati yatra tu asya sarvam ātmā abhūt tat kena kaṃ paśyet indro māyābhiḥ puru-rūpaḥ īyate iti evam-ādibhiḥ śrutibhiḥ ca brahma-vyatiriktasya mithyātvam avagamyate \| na ca āgama-bādhitārthasya pratyakṣa-virodhaḥ aśaṅkanīyaḥ; yathokta-prakāreṇa kāryasya sarvasya mithyātvāvaghamāt, pratyakṣasya sat-mātra-viṣayatvāc ca \| virodhe sati api aśambhivatsvāṣaṣya-varasamāhiti-narva-bala-padarśana-vāde pratyakṣāt ceppekṣitavyā api pramito niravakāśasya niravakāśasya śāstrasya balīyastvāt \| ataḥ kāraṇa-bhūtāt brahmaṇaḥ anyat sarvaṃ mithyā \|\|

na ca prapañca-mithyātvena jīva-mithyātvam āśaṅkanīyam, brahmaṇaḥ eva jīva-bhāvāt \| brahma eva hi sarveśoreṣu jīva-bhāvam anubhavati anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya eko devaḥ sarva-bhūteṣu gūḍhaḥ eko devaḥ bahudhā niviṣṭaḥ eṣaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu gūḍhaḥ ātmā na prakāśate na anye ato ’sti draṣṭṛ iti evam-ādibhyaḥ \| nandv ekam eva brahma sarveśoreṣu jīva-bhāvam anubhavati ca iti; pādo me vedanā, śiro hi me sukham iti sarva-śarīreṣu sukha-duḥkha-nisandhānaṃ syāt; jīveśvara-baddha-mukta-śiṣyācāryatva-jñātvādi iti vyavasthā ca na syāt \|\|

atra kecid vidhitoratvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ aupupannam evaṃ samādhate—ekasya eva brahmaṇaḥ pratibimba-bhūtānāṃ jīvānāṃ buddhi-vad dhuḥśitvāt tad-vad ekasya eva mukhyasya pratibimbānāṃ maṇi-kuṇḍāṇ-

darpaṇādi-dṛṣṭa-lasya māna-nānātva-paramahattva-malīnatva-vimalatvādi-vat tad-upādhi-kṣayāt vyavasthāpyate \| nanu anena jīvātma-samāśrita-vidyādy-aiśvaryeṇa eva jīvāḥ brahmaṇaḥ bhidyante iti uktaṃ; satyam, paramārthataḥ; kālpanikaṃ tu bhedam āśritya iyaṃ vyavasthā ucyate \| kathaṃ punaḥ kalpanā ? na tāvat brahmaṇaḥ; tasya pariśuddha-jñāna-manaḥ-kalpanā-sambhavāt \| na api jīvānām, itaretarāśrayatva-prasaṅgāt—kalpanādhīnaḥ hi jīva-bhāvaḥ, jīvāśrayā ca kalpanā iti \| nanu evam, avidyā-jīva-bhāvayoḥ bījāṅkura-nyāyena anāditvāt \| kiñca—śāstrādi-anigaraṇādi-vat tu paramārtham eva ayam vastu-bhūtāyām avidyāyāṃ na itaretarāśrayatvādayaḥ vastu-doṣāḥ prabhavanti \| vastutaḥ brahmātiriktānāṃ jīvānāṃ svataḥ vidyuktatve api kṛpaṇādi-tatsubhrati-nibandhana-mātrāvidya-dehopādhi-kārya-dṛṣṭānta-bhāva-vidyāśrayatvopapatteḥ kalpanika-kartṛtvopapattiḥ \| pratibimba-vat syām-atādṛśa-jñeyatām uddiśya api śāntiḥ eva, anyathā-jñāna-moha-ekṣa-prasaṅgāt \| jīvānāṃ brahma-pravāhānāditvāt atra tad-uddhṛtve varṇīyaḥ—iti \|\|

tad etad-vid-dṛṣṭānta-nyāyāstyānāṃ bheda-vādya-brahmānujñanas-tad-upamāna-balokena-rihastā-avicitram tasya \| tathā hi—jīvasya kalpita-vat-sabhāva-vikalpopāvidyāśrayatve brahmaṇa eva avidyāśrayatvam uktaṃ syāt \| tad-atiriktasya harinma-kalpitena kāreṇa avidyāśrayatve jaḍasya avidyāśrayatvam uktaṃ syāt \| na khalu dehatvādi-masa-mudāya-madhyātirikta-mātra-samudghuṇāvacchinnam \| kalpitākāra-viśiṣṭena svarūpeṇa eva avidyāśrayatvam iti cet, tatra svarūpadhyāvachaṅkarasadya vidyām antareṇa viśiṣṭa-rūpatā-siddheḥ; avidyāśrayākāra eva hi nirūpyate \|\|

kiñca brahma-mokṣābhidheyārtha-siddhy-arthaṃ hi jīvājñāna-vādāśrayaṇam, sā tu vyavasthā jīvājñāna-pakṣe api na sidhyati \| avidyā-vināśa eva hi mokṣaḥ \| tatra ekasmin mukte avidyā-vināśāt itareṣu api vimucyeran \| anyathā mukta-vād avidyā tiṣṭhati iti cet, tarhi ekasya api amuktir eva syāt, avidyāyāḥ avināśāt \| prati-jīvam avidyā-bhedaḥ kalpyate, tatra yasya avidyā vinaśet, sa mokṣyate, yasya tu avinaṣṭā, sa bhantsyate iti cet, tatra, prati-jīvam iti jīva-bhedam āśritya brūṣe, sa jīva-bhedaḥ kiṃ svābhāvikaḥ ? uta avidyā-kalpitaḥ ? na tāvat svābhāvikaḥ; anabhyupagamāt; bheda-siddhy-arthasya ca avidyā-kalpanasya vyarthatvāt \| atha avidyā-kalpitaḥ; tatra eva jīva-bheda-kalpikā avidyā kiṃ brahmāṇaḥ, iti cet, āgataḥ asi madīyaṃ mārgaṃ \| atha jīvānām, kim asya jīva-bheda-kalpita-siddhy-arthānāṃ vismṛtiḥ ? atha prati-jīvaṃ baddha-mukta-vyavasthā-siddhy-arthaṃ yāḥ avidyāḥ kalpyante, tābhiḥ eva jīva-bhedaḥ api iti manyase, jīve bheda-siddhau tāsāṃ siddhiḥ iti, tāsu siddhāsu jīva-bheda-siddhiḥ iti itaretarāśrayatvam \| na ca atra bījāṅkura-nyāyaḥ sidhyati \| bījāṅkura-heṣu hi anyatva-cchinna-sattva-sthāyi anyāṅkuraḥ saṅga-upādakaḥ \| iha tu yābhiḥ avidyābhiḥ jīvāḥ kalpyante,

tāni ca adhiṣṭhāya tāsāṃ siddhiḥ tiryak-kaniyatā \| atha bīja-aṅkura-rūpyaina pūrva-pūrva-jīva-abhyāgatiḥ avidyā-abhirataḥ itara-jīva-kalpanaṃ manyase, tathā sati jīvānāṃ bhṛśaṃ svayaṃ-mukhatā-adhyāsa-kṛta-vijñāna-adhikriya-pratyūhaḥ \| ata eva brahmaṇaḥ pūrva-pūrva-jīva-abhyāgatiḥ avidyā-abhirataḥ itara-jīva-bhāva-klpanam iti api nirastam \| avidyā-pravāhaḥ api upādhyamāne tat-kalpita-jīva-bhāvasya api tad-hṛdaya-avagāhana-ahitā syāt; na hi apekṣate \| āmokṣāt ca jīvasya api abandha-siddhiṃ na sidhyet \|\|

yat ca uktam avidyāyāḥ avastu-rūpatvena anupapatteḥ na eka-vidyayā na itaretara-āśrayatvādayaḥ vastu-doṣāḥ anavakalpim āvahanti iti, tathā sati muktāt paraṃ brahma-abheda-vidyā \| śuddha-vidyā-svarūpatvāt aśuddhi-rūpā na tatra prabhavati iti cet, kiṃ pūrva-tanu-mūrtiḥ vidyā? evaṃ tarhi kārtsnya-pūrtiḥ api jīvān ādi na āśrayet \| kiṃ jīva-āśrayā avidyā tat-tvāmānīyam-āśraye sati jīvaḥ na sidhyet? na vā? yadi na syet, sva-saṃkalpita-sa-lakṣaṇo mokṣaḥ syāt; no cet avidyā-nāśaḥ api animokṣaḥ, brahma-daśā-apracyuti-rikta-jīvatva-avasthānāt \|\|

yat ca uktaṃ maṇi-kṛpāṇa-darpaṇa-ādi-dṛṣṭa-phalabhyamāna-sukha-malinatva-vikalatvādi-vat śuddhasya śuddhādi-avasthā-upapattiḥ iti; tat evaṃ vimarśanīyam—alpatva-malinitatvādyāḥ aupādhikā doṣāḥ kathaṃ na syuḥ iti? kṛpāṇa-ādi-upādhi-upagamaḥ iti cet, kiṃ tad-āśrayatvāt āśrayaḥ pratibimbaṃ vibharti vā, na vā? vibharti cet, tat-sthānīyasya jīvasya api sthitatvāt animokṣa-prasaṅgaḥ \| naśyati cet, tat eva jīva-nāśa-svarūpa-ucchitti-lakṣaṇo mokṣaḥ syāt \| kiṃ ca madhye hi upaśamyet doṣa-timāsaḥ, tasya bandha-ucchedaḥ puruṣārthaḥ; tatra kim upādhi-kṛta-pratibhāsaḥ viśva-vyathā-nīyasya brahmaṇaḥ? uta pratibimba-sthānīyasya jīvasya? uta asya eva kasyacit? ādyayoḥ kalpayoḥ dharma-anuvādaḥ na saṅgacchate, mukhasya mukha-pratibimbasya vā lakṣaṇā-ādi-dṛṣṭa-pratibhāsa-abhūtvāt na hi mukhaṃ tat-pratibimbaṃ vā taikṣṇye \| brahmaṇaḥ doṣa-pratibhāse brahma-vidyā-prasaṅgaḥ ca \| dvitīyaḥ api kalpaḥ na kalpate, jīva-brahma-bheda-atiriktasya dṛṣṭa-svarūpa-abhāvāt \|\|

kiṃ ca avidyā-kalpitasya jīvasya kalpakaḥ kaḥ iti nirūpaṇīyam \| na tāvat avidyā, acetanatvāt \| na api jīvaḥ; ātma-āśraya-doṣa-prasaṅgāt; śukti-rajata-ādi-vat avidyā-kalpatvāt ca jīva-bhāvasya \| brahma eva kalpakam iti cet, brahma-jñānam eva āpātam \| kiṃ ca brahma-jñānam anupapannam iti brahma jīvān asṛjati vā? na vā? na paśyati iti cet, īśa-aśaktikatva-vicitra-buddhi-nirūpaṇa-kāraṇa-mithyā-ādi brahmaṇaḥ na syāt \| atha paśyati, akhaṇḍa-eka-rasaṃ brahma na avidyā-mantreṇa jīvān paśyati iti brahma-jñāna-prasaṅgaḥ \| ata eva māyā-avidyā-vibhāga-vādaḥ api nirastaḥ \| ajñāna-mantreṇa hi māyinaḥ api brahmaṇaḥ jīva-darśitvaṃ na syāt \| nanu māyāyāḥ para-sadṛśā mohitum alam; na api māyā māyāvinaḥ darśana-āśayam, hṛdayeṣu pareṣu tasyāḥ sādhana-mātra-vat sthāḥ \|

atha brahmaṇaḥ māyā tattvaṃ jīva-dūṣitatvaṃ kurvatī jīva-mohasya hetuḥ iti matayāḥ; tarhi pariśuddha-svabhāvaika-rasa-sarva-prakārasya brahmaṇaḥ para-darśanaṃ kurvatī māyā māyā-paryāyā avidyā eva syāt \|\|

atha matam—viparīta-darśana-hetuḥ avidyā, māyā tu mithyā-bhūtaṃ brahma-vyatiriktaṃ mithyātvena darśayantī na brahmaṇaḥ viparīta-darśana-hetuḥ \| asataḥ tayā na avidyātvam asti iti \| na evam—candramase jāyamāne dvi-candra-darśana-hetoḥ api avidyātvāt \| yadi ca brahma mithyātvena eva sva-vyatiriktaṃ jānāti, na tarhi tat-mohāt iti \| na hi ajānataḥ mithyātvena jñātāt mohitum īhate \| atha apuruṣārtha-paramārtha-darśana-hetuḥ avidyā, māyā tu brahmaṇaḥ māyā-puruṣārtha-darśana-hetuḥ; ataḥ asyāḥ na avidyātvam asti iti mataṃ; tatra, dvi-candra-jñānasya duḥkha-hetutva-abhāve api tad-durita eva, tamirasm eva prasarati \| yadi ca na apuruṣārtha-darśana-karī māyā; tarhi anucchidyatayā nityayā brahma-svarūpānubandhinī syāt \| prastu kaḥ doṣaḥ iti cet, hanta evam eva doṣaḥ \| śreyaḥ ca hi hanta evaṃ bhavati... yatra tu asya sarvam ātma eva abhūt kena kaṃ paśyet iti-ādyaiḥ ca śrutayaḥ prakupyeyuḥ \| paramārtha-viṣayāhaṃtā-śūnyataḥ; jñānāya as tat-svarūpārthatvāvirodha iti cet, aparicchinna-ānandaika-rasa-svarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ aparamārtha-bhūta-māyā-darśanaṃ tadvatā caritavyam mantreṇa na upapadyate \|\|

kiñca aparamārtha-bhūtayā nityayā māyayā kiṃ prayojanaṃ brahmaṇaḥ? jīva-mohaḥ iti cet, apuruṣārthena mohena kiṃ prayojanam? krīḍā iti cet, aparicchinna-ānandasya kiṃ krīḍayā? paripūrṇa-bhogātasya eva krīḍā puruṣa-śabdena loke dṛṣṭā iti cet, na evam iha upapadyate; na hi aparamārtha-bhūtaiḥ krīḍopkaraṇaiḥ aparamārthayā pratibhāmānatva-niṣprapañca-aparamārtha-bhūtayā krīḍayā aparamārtha-bhūtena ca tad-darśibhiḥ anta-anukūlanatāṃ krīḍārathaḥ niṣpadyate \| māyā-āśrayatayā abhimata-brahma-vyatirekeṇa avidyā-āśrayasya jīvasya kalpanāt saṃbhavaḥ ca pūrvam eva uktaḥ brahmyaḥ \| ataḥ brahma ca anādi-avidyā-āśrayatvaṃ svātāntrānānatvaṃ paśyatīti ahitīyatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ aśuddhatvāt dūṣṭatvaṃ ca dṛṣṭeyam \|\|

yat tu bandha-mokṣa-vyavasthā na upapadyata iti, na tat brahma-ajñāna-vādinā prayojyam, ekasya eva brahmaṇaḥ anasya svātantra-anityasya mokṣaṇāgatvādi-baddha-mukta-viṣaya-vyavasthāyāḥ eva abhāvāt, vyavahriyamāṇāyāḥ ca baddha-mukta-ādhyācārtha-viṣaya-vyavasthāyāḥ kālpanikatvāt, svatantra-hīnaḥ iva ca ekasya icchā-viṣayā sarva-kalpanā-upapattiḥ; svataḥ ca hi eṣām hṛdi hiṅkhyācārtha-vyasta-dviṣaya-kartṛtva eva \| ata eva brahma-vidyā-kalpanam api na yuktimat \| pāramārthikī bandha-mokṣa-vyavasthā sva-para-vyavasthā ca jīva-ajñāna-vādinā api na abhyupeyate \| apāramārthikī deha-karṣyāvidyayā upapadyate \|\|

prayogaḥ ca—bandha-mokṣa-vyavasthā sva-para-vyavasthāḥ ca sva-avidyā-kalpitāḥ; apāramārthikatvāt, svatantra-indra-vyavasthā-vat iti \| śarīrāntaratve api madyaṃ bandham iti, śarīratvāt, evañ ca-

rekhat \| śarīra-antarāpattau sarva-indriya-ākalpitāni, śarīra-snehāt, kāryatvāt, jaḍatvāt, kalpitatvataḥ; evaṃ ca śarīravat \| vivāda-adhyāsitaṃ cetana-jātam aheyaṃ, caitanyavat, yat na ahaṃ tat cetanaṃ dṛṣṭam, yathā ghaṭaḥ \| ataḥ tvat-paribhāgaḥ baddha-mukta-āśiṣya-ācārya-ādi-vyavasthāyāṃ kalpa-avidyā-kalpitāḥ \|\|

etat-vāditā api baddha-mukta-vyavasthā duḥkha-prahāḥ; atītānāṃ kalpa-prāmāṇatyādeḥ kalpisma-kalpe ekaika-muktāv api sarveṣāṃ mokṣa-saṃbhavān mukta-anupapatteḥ \| anantatvāt āsmanām uktāś ca santi iti cet, kim idam anantatvam? asaṅkhyeyatvam iti cet, bhūtatvād vartṛr asyaṅkhyeyavat keṣāṃcit saṅkhyeyā eva \| tasyā api aparicchinnatve sarveśitvaṃ na syāt; ātmanāṃ nirasaṅkhyatvāt īśvarasya avidyamānasyaṅkhyā-vedanā-abhāvo nāma aṅgīkṛtam āvahati iti cet, bhinnatve saṅkhyā-vidyutvaṃ na upapadyate \| ātma-antara-saṅkhyāvattaḥ, bhinnatvāt, māṣa-parva-dhaṭa-paṭa-ādivat \| bhinnatve ca ātmasu ghaṭa-ādivat jaḍatvam anātmatvaṃ kṣaṇikatvaṃ ca prasajyate \| brahmaṇo anantatvaṃ na syāt \| anantatvaṃ nāma pariccheda-rahitatvam \| bheda-vāde ca vastu-antara-āhita-lakṣaṇatvena brahmaṇo vastutaḥ pariccheda-rahitatvaṃ na śakyate vaktum; vastu-antara-abhāva eva hi vastutaḥ paricchedaḥ \| vastutaḥ paricchinnasya deśataḥ kālataś ca aparicchinnatvaṃ ca na yujyate; vastu-antara-āhita-lakṣaṇatvena vastutaḥ paricchinnā eva ghaṭādayaḥ deśataḥ kālataś ca paricchinnā hi dṛṣṭāḥ; tathā sarve cetanāḥ brahma ca vastutaḥ paricchinnāḥ; deśa-kālābhyām api paricchidyante \| evaṃ ca satyaṃ jñānam anantam iti ādibhiḥ sarva-prakāra-pariccheda-rahitatvaṃ vada-dṛṅ-virodhaḥ \| utpatti-vināśa-ādayaś ca jīvānāṃ brahmaṇaś ca prasajyeran \| kāla-pariccheda eva hi utpatti-vināśa-abhigatvam \| ata ekaś cet aparicchinnasya brahmaṇo avidyā-ādi-vibhinnaṃ bāhya-hita-svarūpantaṃ kathaṃ jagat \| sukha-duḥkha-pratisandhāna-vyavasthāyoge api svāpaṇa-vyavasthād vividyā-ādi-ābhāṣyāt vyupapadyante \| tasmāt eka eva nitya-mukta-sva-prakāśa-svabhāvam anādi-avidyā-dṛśya-jagad-ākāraṇa vivarttata iti paramārthaḥ brahma-pratiriktabhāvāt adananyatvaṃ jagataḥ—iti \|\|

atra ucyate—nirviśeṣa-svaprakāśānāṃ brahmānāṃ vidyā-tatiriktasvarūpaṃ svagata-anānātvaṃ parasparataś ca prakāśa-svarūpasya nirūpasya prakāśa-nivṛtti-rūpa-tirodhāne svarūpa-nāśa-prasaṅgena virodhāna-saṃbhavāt iti asakala-pramāṇa-viruddhaṃ sva-vacana-viruddhaṃ ca iti pūrvam eva uktam \| yat anuktaṃ kāraṇa-vyatiriktaṃ kāryaṃ yuktyā-bādhitatvena yuktyā-kāraṇatā-ādi-naśvaraṃ iti; tat asyuktam; yukteḥ abhāvāt \| yat ca durvartamānasya kāraṇa-āśraya satyatvam, vyavartamānānāṃ ghaṭa-śarāva-ādi-kāryāṇām asatya-darśan iti; tat api atra dṛṣṭānta-tra vyavartamānatā na bādhitā iti ādibhiḥ pūrvam eva parihṛtam \| yathā-upalabhyamānatva-vināśitvābhyāṃ sad-brahma-anirvacanīyatvena kāryasya mṛṣātva-siddhiḥ iti; tat asat, upalabdhi-vināśa-yoge hi na mithyātvaṃ sādhyati, kiñca nityatvam \| yaiḥ sva-kāla-sambandhitayā yat upa-

lakṣyam, tad-deśa-kāla-saṃbandhitayā bādhitatve eva hi tasya mithyātve hetuḥ \| deśāntara-kālāntara-saṃbandhita-upalabdhi-asthāna-deśa-kāla-saṃbandhitvena bādhitatvaṃ deśāntara-kālāntara-asaṃbhavā-mātraṃ sādhayati, na tu mithyātvam \| pratiyogya—ghaṭādi-kāryaṃ satyam, deśa-kāla-aviśratti-pratyogitva-bādhitatvāt, ātmavat \|\|

brahma-vettaṃ kāraṇatva-rūpa-bādhita-atiriktatāha kāryāpatter saṃbhavati iti; tad-avasthā, deśa-kālādiṣu ahaṅkāra-saṃbandhitāhaṅkāraṇā-kāryāpatti-saṃbhavāt \| tat-saṃbandhāṃś ca vikṛtasya avikṛtasya ca na saṃbhavati iti yuktatvam; tad ayuktam; pūrvam avikṛtasya eva kālādi-sambandha-saṃbhavāt \| śruti-kṛtatva-viśeṣāt pūrvam api deśa-kālādi-sambandhaṃ prasañjata iti cet; deśa-kālādi-sambandhasya kāraṇāntara-ayatna-saṃvedyatvābhāvāt \| ato deśa-kālādi-sambandha-rūpa-viśeṣāpattiṃ kāraṇaṃ kārya-mṛdyatayatīti na kiñcid vahinvam \| kāraṇasya ca kāryaṃ pratyārambhakatva-saṃbandhitaṃ hṛdyamānaṃ na kenāpi prakāreṇa apahnotuṃ śakyate \|\|

yas tu hema-ādi-mātrasya, rucaka-ādi-kāryeṇa itathāprakasya vā hema-ādeḥ ārambhakatvaṃ na saṃbhavati iti; tad ayuktam, hema-ādi-mātrasya eva yathokta-pariṇata-yuktasya ārambhakatva-saṃbhavāt \| na ca ārambhakatva-icchā-viśiṣṭaṃ kāryaṃ na hṛdyata iti vaktuṃ śakyam; hema-ādi-nirmitasya svastikasya darśanāt, śuddha-śabda-rantarita-virūpatva-antarasya sādhitatvāc ca \| na ca ayaṃ śuktikā-rajata-ādivat bhramaḥ; utpatti-vināśayoḥ antarāle upalabdhyamānasya tad-deśa-kāla-saṃbandhitayā bādhādarśanāt \| na ca asya upalabdheḥ bādhikā kācid api yuktiḥ śakyate \| prāpta-anupalabdhuḥ asiddhi-kalpa-adhika-velāyām api hematva-bhinnā svastikākhyātayā hemnaḥ anuvṛtteḥ avirodhāt \|\|

śruti-śirassu padārtha-mithyātva-sādhanaṃ pūrvam eva nirastam \| yat ca atyanta pratyakṣa-avirodhādi pratipattavyam, tad api sarvaṃ pūrvam eva dūṣitam \|\|

yat ca eka-rasa-aneka-rasa-tā sarvāṇi śarīrāṇi ātmavanti iti; tad-avasthā, ekaṃ ca eva sarva-śarīra-prayukta-sukha-duḥkha-spratisaṃdhāna-prasaṅgāt \| sopādhiyabhūmiṣu hi ātmaikatvena aneka-sarīra-prayukta-sukha-ādi-pratisaṃdhānam ekasya hṛdyate \| na bāhyārthasya jñātṛtva-ānanda-śāstra-tisaṃdhāna-abhāvo, na ātma-sattvā iti vaktuṃ śakyam; ātmā jñātā eva, sa bāhyārthaṃ eva, antaḥkaraṇa-bhūtatva-bheda-dvāreṇa jaḍatvāsaṃkaraṇatvāc ca bāhya-indriya-ādivat jaḍa iti upapāditatvāt \| yat ca śarīra-vat jaḍatva-kāryatva-kalpita-deha-sarva-śarīra-gaṇam ekasya avidyā-kalpitam uktam; tad api sarveśvarair api avidyā-kalpitatva-dvaya-icchā-abhāvād ayuktam \| tathā-bhāvyatvāviditasya satyatva-upapādanāt \| yat ca caitanyasya jaḍatva-darśanāt tat-sarva-caitanyānām anṛtatvam uktam, tad api sukha-duḥkhy-avasthayā, bheda-upapādanā eva nirastam \|\|

yat tu maty etat sarva-cit, sarva-vidyā-kalpitāni ahaṃ eva sarvaṃ caitanya-jāta-anirvācya-saṃbandha-

m upapāditam, tad-vyatāna-cchid-ānandasya śāstri-vicchinnasya; ahaṃtvamātrārtha-cin-lakṣaṇaṃ cin-mātraṃ jñāna-sātvamte \|\|

kiñca nirviśeṣa-cin-mātrātireki sarvaṃ mithyā iti vadataḥ mokṣārtha-śravaṇādi-prayatnaḥ niṣphalaḥ; śrī-codya-karma-bandhāt; śuktikā-rajatādi-vad rajatādi-upādāna-avidyaty-avat \| mokṣārtha-prayatnaḥ vyarthaḥ; kalpita-ācāryādi-sajñāna-kāryatvāt, śuktikādi-vādhyadādi-viprayatna-vat \| tat-svarūpādhikārya-jana-jñānaṃ na bandhi-vartakam, avidyā-kalpita-vākya-janyatvāt, svapna-avidyāsmaka-vat, avidyā-kalpita-saṃprāya-catvāt, kalpita-ācārya-saśravaṇa-janyatvābhyāṃ, svāpya-artha-nirmocana-vākya-janya-jñāna-vat \| kiñca nirviśeṣa-cin-mātraṃ brahma mithyā, avidyā-kārya-jñāna-gamyatvāt, avidyā-kalpita-jñānādhyāna-gamyatvāt, avidyāsmaka-jñāna-gamyatvād dhā, yat evaṃ tat mṛṣā, yathā svātma-svādy-anagarādiḥ \|\|

na ca nirviśeṣa-cin-mātraṃ brahma svayaṃ prakāśate, yena na pramāṇāntaram apekṣate \| pratyastama-sākṣikaṃ svayaṃ-prakāśa-jñānaṃ hṛdyate, tat tu jñeya-viśeṣa-siddhi-rūpaṃ jñānānuguṇam eva hṛdyata iti pūrvam eva uktam \| yāni ca tasya nirviśeṣatva-sādhakāni yaugikāni jñāna-mukhya-vacāni, tāni ca anantokta-ividyaka-kāryatva-vidyādibhiḥ upamūlitāni \| na ca nirviśeṣasya cin-mātratva-yajñāna-sākṣitvam ahantārādi-gajāśva-bhra-procyate \| sākṣitva-bhramādyoḥ api hi jñāna-viśeṣatā grāhyaḥ; na iti-mātra-gatāḥ \| na ca tasya prakāśatvaṃ sva-anyataḥ-prakāśatā vā sidhyati \| prakāśaḥ hi kasyacid ūṣasya kasyanā artha-viśeṣaṃ prati siddhi-rūpo hṛdyate \| tat eva hi tasya svayaṃ-prakāśatā upapādyate bhaved indreḥ api \| na ca avādhārasya nirviśeṣasya prakāśatā saṃbhavati \|\|

yaḥ punaḥ sva-gocarūpatva-paramārtho ’pi paramārtha-kāryaṃ hṛdyata iti udghoṣaḥ; saḥ api tāni kāryāṇi sarvāṇi avidita-kalanāni vyavahārika-satyāni; vastutas tu avidyāsmakāni eva iti sva-abhyupagamāt eva nirastaḥ \| asmābhiḥ api sarveṣāṃ paramārthā eva kāraṇātma-svaika-kāryatva-nirūpaṇāya-dṛṣṭaḥ pūrvam eva nirastaḥ \| na ca tvam-padānugamānāṃ śruti-virodhaḥ vaktuṃ śakyate, śruteḥ tu avidyāsmakatā eva ca ukṣāghātenabhyaḥ viśeṣābhāvāt \|\|
