yat tu brahmopāsanādhikātāyām ayadṛṣṭe ’pi paścāt-tana-bādha-darśanād ugraha satyam eva iti; tat s astu; dṛṣṭānta-rajat-sthana-agamyatve nivṛtte sati paścāt-tana-bādha-darśanasya akiñcitkaratvāt; yathā śruty eva tattvam iti vākya-janya-jñānasya paścāt-tana-bādha-darśane ’pi doṣa-mūlatva-niścayād eva tat-dheyatvam ucyate \|\|

kiñca na iha nānā asti kiñcana vijñānam ānandaṃ brahma iti vijñāna-mātrātiriktasya dṛśyasya vastu-jātasya niṣedhakatvena sarva-bhedātirikta-vākya-paścāt-tana-bādha-darśana-mukhye; śruty eva

tarhi am iti tatra api akṣaraṃ vadataḥ samasta-pratyavekṣaṇam paścāt na abādhaḥ dṛśyate \| sarveṣu ucyatātireki-nirveda-asaṃbhavāt sarve ca paścāt na abādha-darśanāt \| doṣa-mūlatvaṃ tu pratyakṣa-ādīnāṃ vedānta-janmanaḥ sarvekṣuḥ-ajñāna-sthāpya-viśeṣam \|\|

ataḥ sarve vijñāna-jātaṃ pāramārthikatā-śūnyam, svayaṃ ca paramārtha-bhūta-artha-viśeṣa-nirdiṣṭa-rūpaḥ; tatra kiñcit jñānaṃ doṣa-mūlam; deśaḥ ca paramārthaḥ; kiñcid nirvāhaṃ pāramārthikatā-mātreṇa iti am iti grāvya-abhyupete, na tāvat tasya mithyārthatva-vyavasthā loka-vyavahāraḥ ca setsyati \| loka-vyavahāraḥ hi pāramārthiko bhrānti-rūpaḥ ca pāramārthika-jñāna-dvāra-artha-viśeṣa-nirdiṣṭa-rūpa-prakāra-apūrvaḥ; nirviśeṣa-san-mātrasya tu pāramārthikatayā pāramārthikasya ca pratibhāsamāna-hetutva-asaṃbhavāt loka-vyavahāraḥ na saṃbhavati \|\|

yat ca taiḥ nirviśeṣa-cin-mātra-saṃbhavāt sarva-vidyā-samādhiṣṭhānasya san-mātrasya pāramārthikatvam uktam, tat api doṣa-doṣāśrayatva-anubhūyamānānām paramārthye api bhrama-upapannatvāt vijñāna-pāramārthyasya api bhrama-upapattiḥ nirastam \| atha adhiṣṭhāna-pāramārthyasya api na karhicit bhramaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti san-mātrasya pāramārthikatvam avasthāpyate, hanta tarhi doṣa-doṣāśrayatva-anubhūyamānānām paramārthye api api na karhicit bhramaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti darśa-anurūpeṇa teṣām api pāramārthyam avasthāpyanīyam iti na karhicit dṛṣṭo goḍyatra tattva-saṃbhāvaḥ \|\|

yat tu bheda-pakṣa iti ati-kalpanānām ānantyāt sarva-dharma-asanmāno muktatvena bandha-saṃbhavāt baddha-mukta-vyavasthā na saṃbhavati iti, tatra asamānatvena parihṛtam \| yat ca asmānāṃ bhinnatve bhāṣya-bandha-paṭha-datta-vicitra-saṃsthānavat madhye ca ayam iti, tatra ghaṭa-ādīnāṃ ekatvā-bṛhat-nānā-rasa-samānādhikaraṇasya abhāvāt \| daśa ghaṭāḥ samatulaṃ bhāṣā iti saṃkhyā-vācastvaṃ dṛśyate iti cet, satyaṃ, tātu na ghaṭādi-vika-rūpatām, api tu deśa-kāla-adhyadhika-bhu ghaṭādinām; tāsāṃ tu saṃkhyā-vacanā asmānām abhyupagamyatvāt \| na ca tāvatā sarva-mukta-prasaṅgaḥ; ātma-svarūpa-ānantyāt \|\|

yat tu ātmanāṃ bhinnatve ghaṭādivat jaḍatvān ātma-svarūpa-kaṅka-prasaṅgaḥ iti; tat ayuktam, eka-jātīyānāṃ bhedasya taj-jātīyānāṃ jāḍya-narīryatva-anāpādakatvāt na hi ghaṭānāṃ bhedaḥ teṣāṃ paṭatvam āpādayati \|\|

yat tu bhinnatve vastutaḥ pariccheda-adyuṣkhalābhyām api paricchedaḥ brahmaṇaḥ prasajyeta iti anantatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ na sidhyati iti; tat ayuktaṃ, vastutaḥ paricchinnānām api deśa-kāla-paricchedasya nyūnādhika-bhāvena niyama-darśanāt; deśa-kāla-sambandhy-antayāḥ pramāṇāntara-yatnirṇayatvena brahmaṇaḥ sva-deśa-kāla-sambandhasya api pramāṇāntara-āpatato virodha-abhāvāt \| vastutaḥ pariccheda-mātrāt eva sarva-prakāra-pariccheda-rahitatva-abhāvāt nityā-siddhiḥ iti cet, tatra brūtaḥ api avidyā-nivṛkṣyāṃ

brahmagorḍasthupavatat-samānatvam \| antaḥ-sattvo ’vidyā-vilakṣaṇatvād aṇu-bhūmābhyāṃ brahma-goḥ api mithyetvena bheda-muktau doṣa-sāmyaṃ tathā api prasañjayeran \| yathā avidyā-vilakṣaṇatvaṃ na abhyupayate; tathā vidyātmakam eva brahma syāt \| satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti lakṣaṇa-vākyam api tata eva apārthakaṃ syāt \| bhedetarannābhyupagame hi sva-pakṣa-rakṣā-sādhakatvābhāvād vicikitsāyāṃ sarva-nirākaraṇaṃ syāt \| ānandam api siddhendra deśa-kāla-pariccheda-rahitatva-mātreṇa; na vastutaḥ api pariccheda-rahitatvena, tathā-vidhasya śāstra-vipraṇigamana-samanupalabdheḥ \| bhedāviditaḥ tu sarva-viccheda-svarūpatvena brahmaṇaḥ sarva-prakāratvāt svataḥ parataḥ api paricchedo na vidyate \| tad evaṃ kāraṇa-vastunasya kāryasya satya-tvād brahma-kāryaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat brahma-goḥ anyac ca \|\|

iti prāpte pratyabhidhe—nadanatva-yogād ārambhaṇa-śabd-ādiḥ syaḥ \| tasmāt parama-kāraṇād brahmaṇaḥ, ananyatvaṃ jagataḥ ārambhaṇa-śabd-ādiḥ syaḥ tad-upapādyatvam upapādyate \| ārambhaṇa-śabdaḥ ādyaḥ ca vākya-śeṣaḥ, tāni ārambhaṇa-śabd-ādīni—vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛttikā iti eva satyam sad eva somya idam agra āsīt ekam eva advitīyaṃ tad aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tat tejaḥ asṛjata anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya san-mūlāḥ somya imāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ sad-āyatanāḥ sat-pratiṣṭhāḥ... etad-ātmam idaṃ sarvaṃ tat satyaṃ sa ātmā tat tvam asi śvetaketo iti etāni; prakaraṇāntara-sthāni api etad-anusāri eka-arthatvād viśeṣavanti \| etāni hi vākyāni cid-acid-ātmakasya jagataḥ paramātma-brahmaṇaḥ ananyatvam upapādayanti \|\|

tathā hi—uta tam ādeśam aprākṣyo yena aśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavaty amataṃ mataṃ vijñātam avijñātam iti kṛtsnaṃ jagato brahma-eka-kāraṇatvam, kāraṇa-kāryasya ananyatvaṃ ca hṛdi nidhāya kāraṇa-bhūta-mṛd-vijñānena kārya-bhūtasya sarvasya vijñāne pratijñāte sati kṛtsnasya brahma-eka-kāraṇatām ajānatā śiṣyeṇa kathaṃ nu bhagavaḥ sa ādeśaḥ iti apṛcchatā śravaṇāt ārambhaṇaṃ codiṭo, jagato brahma-kāraṇatām upadeśya, laukika-dṛṣṭānta-siddhaṃ kāraṇa-kāryayoḥ ananyatvaṃ tāvat yathā somya ekena mṛt-piṇḍena sarvaṃ mṛn-mayaṃ vijñātaṃ syāt iti darśayati \| yathā kṛtsna-mṛt-piṇḍa-ārabdhānāṃ ghaṭa-śarāva-ādīnāṃ tasmād anātirikta-dravyatayā tat-jñānena jñātatetarthaḥ \|\|

atha kāraṇa-vādena kāraṇa-kāryasya dṛṣṭānta-stha-sāmānyād dṛḍhībhavitena kāraṇa-kāryasya ananyatām upapādayati vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛttikā iti eva satyam iti \| ārabhyate ālabhyate spṛśyate iti ārambhaṇaḥ; kṛtya-lyuṭo bahulam iti karmaṇi lyuṭ \| vācā—vāk-yuktena vyavahāreṇa hetunetyrthaḥ \| ‘ghaṭo ’yamāhāra’ ity-ādi-vācām mukhyo hi ukārāṭ-ṭhādi-vyavahāraḥ; tasya vāc-vyavahārasya siddhe, tena eva mṛd-rūpeṇa pṛthu-budhna uttaracchadī-lakṣaṇo

vikāraḥ—saṃsthāna-vīśeṣaḥ; tatra mṛktaṃ ca ghaṭa iti ādinām ādhyam, spaṣṭyate—udakāharaṇa-ādi-vyavahāra-viśeṣa-siddhy-arthaṃ mṛd-unmay eva saṃsthānāntara-nāmādeyāntaram ārabhate \| ato ghaṭāt api mṛttikā eva satyam—mṛttikātvyam iva satyam, pramāṇena upalabhyata iti arthaḥ; na tu dravyāntaratvena \| asataḥ tu eva dṛṣṭānta-bhūte dehyaṇa saṃsthānāntara-bhāva-mātreṇa buddhi-śabdāntarādhya upapadyante; yathā ekatve eva devadattasya avasthā-bhedaiḥ bālaḥ yuvā sthavira iti buddhi-śabdāntarādhyaḥ kārya-viśeṣaḥ ca dṛśyate \|\|

yuktaṃ satyam eva mṛdi ghaṭo nṛtu iti vyavahārākāra-ghaṭya kāryam iti, tat-anukṛti-vināśayoḥ kāraṇa-bhūtasya eva dravyasya avasthā-viśeṣatvam upapāditam eva parihṛtam \| tataḥ tat-vyarthakaṃrṣyeḥ hadaṭasya ete śabdāḥ sannitāni ca kāryāṇi iti yuktam \| dravyasya tat-tad-avasthatvaṃ kāraka-vyāpārāyattam iti tat-kāryatvaṃ ca \|\|

abhivyaktam uktaṃ ca iti na ucitam \| tasya ananya-upanāmatve eva parihṛtāni \| udakya-bhṛt-yupame api sat-kārya-vādaḥ na virudhyate; sata eva upatteḥ \| vismṛti-vivṛddhi-nṛtya abhidhīyate—pūrvaṃ eva sat, tat utpadyate ca iti \| ajñāta-utpatti-vināśā yathā-svam ete na ucyaḥ; dravyayoḥ antaroḥ asaṃsthāna-yogaḥ pūrva-pūrva-saṃsthāna-āsthititasya vināśaḥ, sva-avasthyasya tu utpatttiḥ \| antaḥ-savineyasya dravyasya sat-citasa-kārya-vādaḥ na virudhyate \| saṃsthānasya asat utpattau as-kārya-vāda-prasaṅga iti cet, asat-kārya-vādinaḥ api utpatteḥ tat-utpattimattve sat-kārya-vādaḥ ca; utpattimattve ca ananyathā \| asmākaṃ tu avasthānaṃ pṛthak-dravya-tipatti-kṣaṇe yogo mahā-vāda-vyasthāvat evam eva uccaikaṃ sarvam iti nirvadhyam \| kapālatva-cūrṇatva-piṇḍatvāvasthā-graheṇa ghaṭatvāvasthā-dekatvāvasthā-graheṇa bahutvāvasthā, tat-graheṇa ekatvāvasthā ca iti na kaścid virodhaḥ \|\|

tathā sa deva somya idam āsīt ekam eva advitīyam iti sat eva idaṃ idānīṃ vibhakta-nāma-rūpatvena nānāhaṃ jagat agre nāma-rūpa-vibhāgābhāvena ikam eva āsīt, sarva-śaktitvena ādhīyuḥnantara-śrutatvāt advitīyaṃ cetana-anyatva-acetana-upādhitam \| tathā tat aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti sakṣamaṇānejaḥ-prabhṛti-vividha-vicitra-sthira-trasahapa-jagad-bhedātmano bahu-bhavanaṃ saṅkalpya jagat-sṛṣṭi-pidhānantaraṃ kāryasya jagataḥ parama-kāraṇāt parasmāt brahmaṇaḥ anyatvam abodhīyate \| suṣṭha-dvācchyasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ sarvaiśasya satyasya sthūla-rūpasya nityaṃ caitveva ra vevameva iti nirdeśāṅgaṇatvāt, sat-śabda-vācyasya eva jagato nāma-rūpa-vibhāgābhāvena ekatvam abhidhāya anantaraṃ nirapekṣasya, punar api tasya eva vicitra-sthira-trasaha-jagatvena bahu-bhavanam anurukta-lakṣaṇam, yathā āha śrutiṃ saumya katham apaghāta iti āśaṅkhyā āha saumya deva iti hata iti tārki-bhimata-siddho devatāḥ anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaraṇavāṇi iti tāvatāṃ śrūyate śruty-utram iti ādi \| tisro devatā iti kṛtsnam abhihitaṃ nirdiśya

svātmaka-jīvātma-praveśena iti hi dṛśyata-nāma-rūpa-bhāktva-vāgiyuktasya \| anena jīvena ātmanā—mad-ātmaka-jīvena ātmatayā anupraviśya ca etad-dvicitra-nāma-rūpa-bhāk aravāṇi iti arthaḥ \| svātmano jīvasya ca ātmatayā anupraveśārhaṃ nāma-rūpa-bhāktvam iti uktaṃ bhavati \| tad akṣyā, tad eva anuprāviśat, tad anupraviśya, sat ca tyat ca abhavat iti śruty-antareṇa spaṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ ca jagataḥ tena brahmaṇā ātmatayā anupraviṣṭam iti \| tad eva tat-tat-kāryāvasthasya ca kāraṇāvasthasya ca cid-acid-vastunaḥ sthūlasya sūkṣmasya ca para-brahma-śarīratvam, parasya ca brahmaṇa ātmatvam antaryāmitvāt iti etat-siddhaṃ smāritam \| anena pūrvoktā śākhā nirastā \| acid-vastuni sañjīve brahmaṇi ātmatayā avasthite nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇa-vacanāt cid-acid-vastu-śarīrakaṃ brahma eva jagac-chabda-vācyam iti sūtra-pratipāditasya \| śarīra-bhūta-cid-acid-vastu-gata-ātmatā-sarvaṃ vikārāspṛṣṭa-rūpārthaś ca brahmaṇo nirvikāratvaṃ kalyāṇa-guṇākaratvaṃ ca sūcitam \| tad etat adhikaṃ tu bheda-nirdeśāt iti anantaram eva vakṣyati \| tathā aitadātmyam idaṃ sarvam iti kṛtsnasya cetanācetanasya brahmātmakatvam upadiśati \| tad eva ca tat tvam asi iti nigamayati \|\|

tathā prakārāntarasthe’pi vākyeyu sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma ātmani khalu are dṛṣṭe śrute mate vijñāta idaṃ sarvaṃ viditam idam sarvaṃ yad ayam ātmā brahma eva idaṃ sarvam ātmā eva idaṃ sarvam iti anupadaṃ pratīyate \| tathā agryaṃ ca niṣidhyate sarvaṃ taṃ parādāt yaḥ anyatra ātmanaḥ sarvaṃ veda neha nānā asti kiñcana, mṛtyoḥ sa mṛtyum āpnoti yaḥ iha nānā iva paśyati iti \| tathā atra hi dvaitam iva bhavati tad itara itaraṃ paśyati yatra tu asya sarvam ātmā eva abhūt tat kena kaṃ paśyet ity-ādivat dvaita-darśane vidyupāyaṃ tad-darśane pratipādyatvān nyatvam eva tātvikam iti pratipādayati \| tad eva amārasaprāṇāśabdādibhyo jagataḥ parama-kāraṇāt parasmād brahmaṇaḥ anyatvam anupapadyate \|\|

atra idaṃ tattvam—cid-acid-vastu-śarīratayā tat-prakāraṃ brahma eva sarvadā sarvair-vedāvidheyam \| tat-kāraṇāvastheśarīratayā api pṛthag-upadeśānārhaṃ sūkṣmāvasthāpracid-acid-vastu-śarīram \| tat-kāraṇāvasthaṃ brahma \| kadācit ca vibhakta-nāma-rūpa-vyavahārārhaṃ sthūlāvasthāpracid-acid-vastu-śarīram \| tat ca kāryāvastham iti kāraṇāt parasamād brahmaṇaḥ kāryatvaṃ jagad anyat \| śarīra-bhūta-cid-acid-vastunaḥ śarīriṇo brahmaṇaś ca kāraṇāvasthāyāṃ kāryāvasthāyāṃ ca bhūta-jāti-siddhayā svabhāva-vyavasthayā guṇa-doṣa-vyavasthā ca na anu hīyanta iti uktā \|\|

ye tu kārya-kāraṇayoḥ ananyatvaṃ kāryasya mithyātvāśrayaṇena varṇayanti, na teṣāṃ kārya-kāraṇayoḥ ananyatvaṃ sidhyati, satya-mithyārthayoḥ aikyānupapatteḥ; tathā sati brahmaṇo mithyātvaṃ jagataḥ satyatvaṃ vā syāt \|\| (brahmājñāna-vādi-mata-dūṣaṇam idam \|\|)

na eva kāryam api pāramārthikam asamyujyanta eva jīva-brahmaṇoḥ eka-adhikaraṇya-vastvam, svābhāvikaṃ

jñānatvam, acit-brahmayoḥ tu hṛdi api svābhāvikam iti vadanti; teṣām upādhi-kṛta-bheda-vivikta-vastv-antara-abhāvāt śruti-avarodhitasyā brahmaṇaḥ eva upādhi-saṃbandhi-dṛṣṭa-svarūpasya eva heya-ākāra-pariṇāmāt, śakti-pariṇāma-abhyupagame śakti-brahma-bheda-aṅgīkārāt ca jīva-brahmaṇoḥ karma-vaśyatva-apahata-ānandatva-ādi-bheda-vyavasthā-virodhāt jīva-brahmaṇoḥ ca pariṇāma-apariṇāma-vādinī aśrutatayo vyākuleyuḥ \|\| (bhāskara-smṛti-dūṣaṇa-siddham \|\|)

ye punaḥ nirasta-nikhila-bheda-mukhya-adhikāra-svabhāvaṃ sarva-indriya-guṇaṃ samagra-dravyasya eva kāraṇaṃ brahma; tat ca pralaya-velāyāṃ śānta-śeṣa-sukha-duḥkha-anubhava-aviśeṣaṃ sva-prakāśam api suṣupta-ātma-sva-cic-chakti-lakṣaṇa-avasthitam; sṛṣṭi-velāyāṃ mṛttikā-kāryam iva ghaṭa-śarāva-ādi-rūpam, samudra iva ca phena-taraṅga-budbuda-ādi-rūpaṃ bhoktṛ-bhogya-niyantṛtvena aṃśa-traya-avastham avatīrṇam; ataḥ bhoktṛ-bhogya-niyantṛtvāni tatra yuktāni guṇa-doṣāḥ śarīra-avaccheda-vaśa-nimittatva-danuśaya-anākāśa-bhedavat ca vyavasthitāni; bhoktṛ-bhogya-niyantṝṇāṃ sadātmanaḥ ekatvaṃ ca ghaṭa-śarāva-mṛttikā-divat mṛd-ātmanā indratvavat upapadyate; ataḥ samagra-dravyasya eva sarva-avasthā-avasthitam iti brahmaṇaḥ jagaj-janma-ādi-avirodhāt; teṣāṃ sakala-smṛti-smṛti-itihāsa-purāṇatva-artha-virodhaḥ \|\|

sarvā hi śrutayaḥ smṛti-itihāsa-purāṇāḥ sarveśvara-īśvaraṃ sarveṣāṃ sarva-daṃ sarva-avarjitaṃ satya-saṃkalpaṃ niravadyaṃ deśa-kāla-anavacchinna-anavadhika-ātiśaya-ānandaṃ paramaṃ kāraṇaṃ brahma pratipādayanti; na punaḥ īśvarasya api param īkṣa-riśi samagra-ātma \| tathā hi— sad eva saumya asīt ekam eva advitīyam tat aikṣata bahu syām prajāyeya iti brahma vai idam agra āsīt ekam eva tat ekam san na vyabhavat tat śreyaḥ rūpam atyajanat kṣatram prāvedayati devatrā kṣatrāṇi indraḥ varuṇaḥ somaḥ rudraḥ parjanyaḥ yamaḥ mṛtyuḥ īśāna iti ātmā vai idam ekaḥ eva agre āsīt na anyat kiñcana miṣat sa aikṣata lokān nu sṛjā iti ekaḥ ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt na brahmā na īśānaḥ na ime dyāvā-pṛthivī na nakṣatrāṇi na āpaḥ na agniḥ na somaḥ na sūryaḥ sa ekākī na rameta tasya dhyāna-antasthasya iti-ādi-śrutiḥ paramaṃ kāraṇaṃ sarveśvara-īśvaraḥ nārāyaṇaḥ eva iti avagamyate \| sat-brahma-ātma-śabdāḥ hi tasya upakramaṇa-pratyas-tulya-ākāra-gaṇa-sthena nārāyaṇa-śabdena viśeṣitātma-samaveta-arthānti \| sarveśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśvaram sa kāraṇaṃ karaṇa-adhipa-adhipaḥ na ca asya kaścit janitā na ca adhipaḥ iti īśvarasya eva kāraṇatvaṃ śrūyate \|\|

smṛtiḥ api mānavī atataḥ tvaṃ-bhū-bhāvanam iti pratyasya, saḥ abhidhyāya śarīrāt svāt sissiṣuḥ vividhāḥ prajāḥ \| āpaḥ eva sasarja ādau tāsu vīryam apāsṛjat \|\| iti \|

itihāsa-purāṇam api puruṣottamam eva paraṃ kāraṇam abhidadhati— nārāyaṇaḥ jagan-mūrtiḥ ananta-ātmā sanātanaḥ ... sa sisṛkṣuḥ sahasra-aṃśāt utsasarja puruṣāt vidyā viṣṇoḥ sakāśāt udbhūtaṃ jagat tatra eva ca sthitam iti-ādi u \|\|

na ca evam asan-mātram eva iti vaktuṃ śakyam, tasya tad-aṃśatvābhyupagamāt sa-viśeṣatvāt \| na ca tasya jñāna-ānanda-ādi-ananta-kalyāṇa-guṇa-yogaḥ kādācitka iti vaktuṃ śakyate, teṣāṃ svābhāvikatvena sadātanatvāt \| parāsya śaktir vividha eva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca satyasarvaṃ sarvavid ity-ādi-śrutiḥ \| jñāna-ānanda-ādi-śakti-yogaḥ eva asya svābhāvika iti ma vo caḥ, śaktiḥ svābhāvikī, jñāna-bala-kriyā ca svābhāvikī iti pṛthag-nirdeśa-aśravaṇa-prasaṅgāc ca \| na ca vācaka-ādivat sarva-jñaḥ ity-ādiṣu śakti-mātre rūḍhyatyaya iti vaktuṃ śakyam, kṛtsnatyayamātrasya śaktatva-smaraṇāt, śataṃ hṛścana hradaḥ ity-ādiṣu kevāśvadeva hṛdayatyayānāṃ śakti-viṣayatva-smaraṇāt \| vācakādiṣu tu anyathā lakṣaṇā samāśrīyate \|\|

kiñca īśvarasya tad-aṃśa-viśeṣatvāt tasya cāhiheda taraṅga-samudra-yodhārāgni-taḍid-adhiṣṭhāna-vat, tad-miśrāṇāṃ paraṃ maheśvaram na tat-samaś ca abhyadhikaś ca dṛśyate ity-ādibhir īśvara-viṣayāṇi paraśatāni vacāṃsi bādhyeraṇ \| kiñca san-mātrasya sarvātmakatve aṅgīkṛte ca īśvarasya tad-aṃśa-viśeṣatvāt tasya sarvātmakatva-aṅgīkṛtāv api eṣa vyāhanyeta \| yadi maṇḍūka-matatvaṃ tad-aṃśatvaṃ vā ghaṭa-śarāva-ādeḥ \| svāṃśaṃ sarvaṃ san-mātrasya pūrva-uktena prakāreṇābhyupetya tasya pūrṇatvāt tad-ātmakatāṃ tad-aṃśaḥ śvetarāṇi vastu nīti ca atra, ghaṭe api san-mātrasya pūrṇatvād īśvarasyāpi ghaṭa-ātmakavat tad-aṃśatva-prasaṅgāt \| na ca san-mātrasya ghaṭe asti, paṭe na asti iti vastu-dharmatayāgatasya dravyatvaṃ kāraṇatvaṃ vā upapadyate \| vyavahāra-yogyatā hi sattvam \| virodhi-vyavahāra-yogyatā tad-vyavahāra-upayogya-sattvam \| dravyam eva sādityayugapme kriyādeśīnām sattva-prasaṅgaḥ \| kriyādiṣu kāla-kuṭāvalambanena api sarvatra kathā sattā duṣprapādā \| sad-ātmatvaṃ ca sarvasyābhimatve sarvatra eva sarvadā sarva-dharma-bhāva-prasaṅgāt tat sarvaṃ guṇa-doṣa-prasaktaṃ prasannaṃ ca pūrvam eva uktaḥ \| ato yatnaḥ atra prakaraṇevānapadam \|\| (yādavya-prakāśa-tantra-dūṣaṇārtham) \|\| 152 \|\|

adha ucyate—eka-smṛtyāvasthāntarayor api buddhi-sabda-tatarādayo bālātmapurastād viṣu dṛśyante, mṛd-ādiḥ hiraṇya-ādiṣu dravyāntareṣv api dṛśyante, tatra mṛd-ghaṭa-ādiṣu kārya-kāraṇeṣu buddhi-sabda-tantarādayo avasthānivṛddhatā eva iti kuto nirṇīyata iti \| tatra antarasu—

## \|\| 2.1.16 \|\|

*bhāve ca upalabdheḥ*

**Комментарий:**

kuṇḍala-ādi-kārya-smṛtaye ca kāraṇa-bhūta-hiraṇya-sthopalabdheḥ—‘idaṃ kuṇḍalaṃ hiraṇyam’ iti hiraṇyatvena pratyabhijñānāt iti arthaḥ \| na ca evam hiraṇyādiṣu dravyāntareṣu mṛd-ādaya upalabhyante \| ato bālya-yuvatva-vikārāṇabhūtasyaiva havyam avasthāntarāpattaṃ kāryam iti gīyate; dravyāntara-pariṇāma-dravya-bhedenāvasthāntara-yogeṇa buddhi-śabdāntarādiṣu pakṣeṣv anupalabdher dharmāntara-kalpanā-anupapatteś ca \| na ca jāti-nibandheyaṃ pratyabhijñā, jāti-āśraya-bhūta-dravyāntara-anupalabdheḥ; ekam eva hem-jātīyaṃ dravyaṃ

kārya-karaṇa-ubhayatvaṃ dṛśyate \| nanu brahma-bhede samavādhikaraṇa-anuguṇasya kāryaṃ pratijñāyā animita vakṛtuṃ śakyam, brahma-anantatve sati āśraya-anuguṇam ājñena tad-āśrita-dravya-antare pratisaṃdhāna-anupapatteḥ \| gomaya-ādi-kārye vṛścika-ādau gomayādi-dravya-sthatā na dṛśyata iti cet, tatra api adhikaraṇa-bhūta-dṛṣṭa-vidya-dravya-bhinnālāt \| agni-kārye dhūme agni-pratyabhijñānaṃ na dṛśyata iti cet, bhavatu na tatra pratyabhijñānam; tathā api na doṣaḥ, anenimita-kāraṇa-mātratvāt \| agni-saṃyukta-indhana-ādi-dūmo jāyate \| gandhasya ghrāṇendhana-kārye iva dhūmaḥ \| ataḥ kārya-bhāve ca tat eva idam iti upalabdhā eva hi śabda-antarādyodavyāsaila-mātra-nibandhanā iti avagamyate \| tasmāt kāraṇa-tattva-kāryam \|\|156\|\|

**itaś ca—**

## \|\| 2.1.17 \|\|

*sattvāc ca aparasya*

**Комментарий:**

aparasya—kāryasya kāraṇe sattvāc ca kāraṇāt kāryasya ananyatvam \| loke vede ca api kāryam eva kāraṇatayā vyapadiśyate; yathā loke sarvam idaṃ ghaṭa-śarāva-ādikaṃ pūrvāhṇe mṛttikā eva āsīt iti; vede ca sat eva somya idam agre āsīd iti \|\|17\|\|

## \|\| 2.1.18 \|\|

*asad-vyapadeśāc ca iti cen na, dharma-antareṇa vākya-śeṣa-ādi-yukteḥ śabda-antarāc ca*

**Комментарий:**

yad uktaṃ kāraṇe kāryasya sattvaṃ loke-veda-abhyām avagamyata iti; tad ayuktam, asad-vyapadeśāt—asat eva idam agre āsīt asat vā idam agre āsīt idaṃ vā agre na eva kiñcana āsīt iti; loke ca sarvam idaṃ ghaṭa-śarāva-ādikaṃ pūrvāhṇe na āsīd iti \| ato yathoktaṃ na upapadyata iti cet; tat na, dharma-antareṇa tathā vyapadeśāt \| sa khalu asad-vyapadeśaḥ tatkasya kārya-dravyasya pūrva-kāle dharma-antareṇa—saṃsthāna-antareṇa; na bhavadbhiḥ iva tucchatvena \| sattva-asattve hi dravyasya abhivyaktasya \| tatra sattva-dharma-viruddham asattvam \| idaṃ-śabda-anirdiṣṭasya jagataḥ sattva-dharmo nāma-rūpe; asattva-dharmas tu tad-birodhino sūkṣma-avasthā \| ato jagato nāma-rūpa-yuktasya tad-birodhi-sūkṣma-daśā-pannitvasattvam \| katham idam avagamyate ? vākya-śeṣa-ādi-yukteḥ; śabda-antaraṃ ca—vākyasthīkatāvattu idaṃ vā agre na eva kiñcana āsīt iti atra tat-sadeva sanmano’kuruta syāmin iti iti \| anena vākya-śeṣa-gatena manaskāra-liṅgena asac-chabda-arthaṃ tucchatva-nirāse niścite tadvakāryānivi asat eva idam agre viśvayam sad-anavat syām eva artha iti niścīyate \| yuktaiś ca asattvasya dharma-antaratvam avagamyate \| yuktiḥ hi sattva-asattve padārtha-dharmau iti āha \| mṛd-dravyasya pṛthu-budhodara-ākāra-yogo ghaṭaḥ asti iti vyavahāra-hetuḥ; tat sa eva tad-birodhy-avasthāntara-yogo ghaṭaḥ na asti iti vyavahāra-hetuḥ \| tatra kapālādi-avasthāpātāt tad-birodhitvena eva ghaṭāvasthasya na asti iti vyavahāra-hetuḥ \| na ca tadacyatiriktaṃ ghaṭākhyo nāma

kaścit upalabhyate; na ca kalpyate, tāvat etābhāva-vyavahāra-upapatteḥ \| tathā śabdāntarāḍ pūrva-kāle dharmāntara-yogaḥ eva avagamyate \| śabdāntarasya pūrva-vihitāt sā eva somidamya āsīt iti adhikṛtam \| tatra hi kṛtsnaṃ khalu sauryaṃ syāt iti tucchattvam ākṣipya sā eva somidamya āsīt iti adhikṛtaṃ sthāpitam taddhedaṃ tarhi avyākṛtam āsīt annāma-rūpābhyāṃ vyākriyata iti sūkṣma-sūkṣmam \|\|15\|\| idānīṃ kāryasya kāraṇād ananyatve nirdeṣṭavyaṃ hāsyaṃ sūtrābhyāṃ darśayati—

## \|\| 2.1.19 \|\|

*paṭavat ca*

**Комментарий:**

yathā tantavaḥ eva vyatiṣajya-viśeṣam ājaḥ paṭa iti nāma-rūpa-kāryāntarādhikaṃ bhajante; tadvat brahma api \|\| 19 \|\|

## \|\| 2.1.20 \|\|

*yathā ca prāṇādiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

yathā ca vāyuḥ ekaḥ eva āśarīre vṛtti-viśeṣaṃ bhajamānaḥ prāṇāpānādi-nāma-rūpa-kāryāntarāṇi bhajate; tadvat brahma ekaṃ vicitra-cit-svarūpaṃ jagad-rūpatīti parama-kāraṇāt paramsādṛśyopajananatvaṃ jagat-siddham \|\| 20 \|\| iti ārambhaṇādhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha itara-vyapadeśādhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.1.21 \|\|

*itara-vyapadeśād dhitākaraṇādi-doṣa-prasaktiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

jagato brahmānanyatvaṃ pratipādya dṛḍhaṃ tat tvam asi ayam ātmā brahma iti-ādhibhir jīvasya api brahmānanyatvaṃ vyapadiśyata iti uktam \| tatra idaṃ codyate—cetana-rasya jīvasya brahma-bhāvāṅgī-bhāvyaṃ ca upadiśyate, tadā brahmaṇaḥ sārvajñya-satya-saṅkalpatvādhuktasya ātmanaḥ hita-rūpa-jagad-akaraṇam-ahita-rūpa-jagakaraṇa-nityādyā doṣāḥ prasajyeran \| ādhyātmikādi-bheda-viṣa-vidhyabhikānanda-duḥkhākare ca idaṃ jagat; na eśaḥ asya svārthaṃ sva-dhīraḥ buddhimān pravartate \| jīvānubrahmaṇo bhedāc cittayeśu-bhūtatyo jagad-brahma-gaur-ananyatvaṃ vadatā tvayaiva parihṛtāḥ; bhede sati ananyatva-viddheḥ \|\| aupādhika-bheda-viṣayā bheda-śrutayaḥ; svābhāvika-abheda-viṣayāś ca abheda-śrutaya iti cet; tatra idaṃ vaktavyam—svabhāvataḥ dvam-asādṛśyaṃ jīvaṃ kim akurvantaṃ jagat-kāraṇaṃ brahma jānāti vā ? na vā ? na jānāti iti cet, sarvajñatva-hāniḥ; jānāti cet, svabhāva-bhinnasya jīvasya duḥkhaṃ sva-duḥkham iti jānato brahmaṇo hitākaraṇāhitakaraṇādi-doṣa-prasaktir anivāryā \| jīva-brahmaṇoḥ jñāna-kṛto bhedaḥ, tad-vidyayā bheda-śrutir iti cet, tatra api jīvājñāna-pakṣe pūrvokto vikalpas tatkaraṃ ca tadvavastham \| brahmājñāna-pakṣe sva-prakāśa-ekasya brahmaṇo ajñāna-sākṣitvaṃ yathā iha jagadṛṣṭyaṃ na saṃbhavati \| ajñānena prakāśa-tirohita-brahmatvaṃ; tirodhānasya prakāśa-nivṛtti-karattvena prakāśaikyaṃ

svarūpatvāt svarūpa-nirūpaṇe ca iti svarūpa-nāśādi-dopasaṃhṛtaṃ prāptaṃ ca udīritum \| ata idam ucyate brahmaṇo jagat-kāraṇatvam \|\| 21 \|\| iti prāpte ’bhidhīyate—

## \|\| 2.1.22 \|\|

*adhikaṃ tu bheda-nirdeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

uktaḥ pakṣo vyāvartyaḥ; ākhyātikataditthaṃkhyogāhṛtaratnagānamaḥ adhikam—artha-antara-bhūtaṃ brahma \| kutaḥ? bheda-nirdeśāt—pratyag-ātmano hi bhedena nirdiśyate paraṃ brahma ya ātmāni tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaro yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ ya ātmānam antaro yamayati sa ta ātmā antaryāmi amṛtaḥ, pṛthag ātmānaṃ preritāraṃ ca matvā juṣṭaḥ tataḥ tena amṛtatvam eti sa kāraṇaṃ karaṇa-adhipa-adhipaḥ tayoḥ anyaḥ pippalaṃ svādv atti anaśnan anyaḥ abhicākaśīti jña-ajñau dvau ajau īśa-anīśau pradhāna-kṣetrajña-patiḥ guṇa-īśaḥ nityaḥ nityānāṃ cetanaḥ cetanānām ekaḥ bahūnāṃ yaḥ vidadhāti kāmān yaḥ avyaktam antare sañcaran yasya vyaktaṃ śarīraṃ yam avyaktaṃ na veda yaḥ akṣaram antare sañcaran yasya akṣaraṃ śarīraṃ yam akṣaraṃ na veda eṣa sarva-bhūta-antar-ātmā apahata-pāpmā divyaḥ devaḥ ekaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ ity-ādibhiḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## \|\| 2.1.23 \|\|

*aśmādivac ca tat-anupapattiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

aśma-kāṣṭha-loṣṭṛṇa-ādīnām acetanānāṃ satatab-vikārāspadānām artha-viccheṣaṇāṃ niravayava-nirvikāra-nikhila-heya-pratyanīka-kalyāṇa-ekatāna-cetana-samasta-vastu-vilakṣaṇa-anantaṃ jñāna-ānanda-eka-rūpa-sahaja-anāvidyān tamahādi-vikṛti-nibandhana-vaddheyaṃ yathā na upapadyate, tathā cetanasya api ananta-duḥkha-yoga-āhṛtasya svarūtaikalyasya apahata-pāpmā iti-ādi-vākya-avagata-sakala-heya-pratyanīkatva-adhyadhikaraṇatāyā- saṃsṛṣṭe yak-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākara-brahma-bhāva-anupapattiḥ \| sāmānādhikaraṇya-nirdeśaḥ yasya ātmā śarīraṃ niyantṛ-kṣetrajña iti ca evaṃ brahma-śarīratvād brahmaṇo jīva-karīratayā tathā ātmatvena vā sthiteḥ jīva-prakāra-brahma-sthiti-paratantrasya ena-dvirodhī, pratyuta tasya arthasya upapādakaḥ ca iti avastiteḥ iti kāśakṛtsnaḥ ity-ādibhiḥ uktam upapāditam \| atas sarva-vicyaṃ brahma cid-acit-sukha-duḥkhādīram iti sthūla-cid-acit-sukha-duḥkha-śarīraṃ brahma kāraṇam; tad eva brahma sthūla-cid-acit-sukha-duḥkha-śarīraṃ jagad-ākhyaṃ kāryam iti jagad-brahmaṇoḥ-sāmānādhikaraṇya-opapattiḥ, jagato brahma-kāryatvam, brahmaṇo jagan-natvam, acit-satato jīvasya ca brahmaṇaś ca pariṇāmitvaḥ; nirvikalyāṇa-guṇākaratva-brahma-bhāvānudruṣṭa-samadeśuyati-virodhaḥ ca bhavati \| sat eva somya idam agre āsīt ekam eva advitīyam iti-ādi-samadhigataṃ jīvasya brahma-śarīratayā sūkṣma-rūpeṇa avasthānam avasthāsyuḥ punas tatvam, vaidharmyāt ca na svapna-ādavat \| na karma-avibhāgāt iti

caitrāvitratādupapatthe ca apālasyate ca iti sūtra-yogitva-vādatābhāpi sūkṣmaḥ ceṇa eva vyākhyānasya \| avibhāgaḥ tu nāma brahma-vibhāga-bhāvāt upapadyate \| ataḥ brahma-kāraṇatvaṃ saṃbhavati eva \|\|

ye punaḥ asya eva jīvasya vidyā-avidyā-kṛtāvasthābhidyeyaṃ bhedaṃ varṇayanti, teṣām idaṃ sarvam asaṃgataṃ syāt; na hi tadvad yasya sarva-ekatvaṃ, sarveśvaratvaṃ, samasta-kāraṇatvaṃ, sarva-niyantṛtvam ity-ādīni santi \| anena eva rūpeṇa hi ābhiḥ śrutibhiḥ pratyag-ātmano bhedaḥ pratipādyate \| (tasya sarvasya avidyā-parikalpitatvāt \|) tat saṃvaṃ hi avidyā-parikalpitaṃ sva-māte \| na ca avidyā-parikalpitasya vidyāvasthāyāṃ śuktikā-rajatādi-vibhedavat para-pratyakṣa-śeṣatvaṃ sūtra-kāreṇa adhikaṃ tu bheda-nirdeśāt ity-ādi-sūtraṃ pratipadyate \| brahma-jijñāsā kartavyā iti jijñāsyatayā prakrāntasya brahmaṇo janmādi-adhikaraṇasya vedānta-vedyatvam, tasya ca smṛti-bhagavad-gītādy-air-oṣadhariā-śāstraṃ kriyate \| apītaḥ tadvat-prasaṅgāt asamañjasam na tu dṛṣṭānta-bhāvāt iti sūtrayam etat adhikaraṇa-siddham anuvartate; tatra hi vilakṣaṇayoḥ kārya-kāraṇa-bhāva-saṃbhavaḥ eva adhikaraṇārthaḥ \| apīti caitra-pratibheda-mātra-vyādīti ca pūrvādhikaraṇārtham anuvartate \|\|23\|\| iti itara-vyapadeśādhikaraṇam \|\|7\|\|

**atha upasaṃhāra-darśanādhikaraṇam \|\|8\|\|**

## \|\| 2.1.24 \|\|

*upasaṃhāra-darśanāt na iti cet na kṣīravat dhi*

**Комментарий:**

parasya brahmaṇaḥ sarveśasya satya-saṅkalpasya sthūla-sūkṣmāvasthasya cetanācetana-vastu-śarīratayā sarva-prakāratvena sarvasmātvaṃ sakaletara-vilakṣaṇatvaṃ ca upādhiḥ miti sthāpitam \| idānīṃ satya-saṅkalpasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ saṅkalpa-mātreṇa vicitra-jagad-utpatti-yogo na viṣṇu iti sthāpyate \| nanu ca parimitāśaktīnāṃ kāraka-kalāpāpekṣā-rahita-saṅkṣetavya-śaṅkena sarveśakteḥ hṛṇaḥ kāraka-kalāpānupasaṃhāreṇa jagat-kāraṇatva-virodhaḥ katham āśaṅkhyate ? ucyate—loke tantu-kārya-janana-śaktir api tantūpakaraṇāpekṣitva-darśanāt sarva-śaktimattasya parasya brahmaṇo’pi tantūpakaraṇa-virahaḥ śabdatvaṃ na upapadyata iti kasyacin manda-dhiyā āśaṅkā jāyata iti sā nirākriyate \| ghaṭa-paṭādi-kāraṇa-bhūtānāṃ kulāla-tantu-vādināṃ taj-janana-sāmarthye saty api kāścit upakaraṇāni upasaṃhṛtya eva janayitṛtvaṃ dṛśyate \| taj-janana-śaktāḥ kāraka-kalāpopasaṃhāre’pi janituṃ na śaknuvanti; śaktāḥ punaḥ kāraka-kalāpopasaṃhāre janayanti eva etāvān eva viśeṣaḥ \| brahmaṇo’pi sarveśakteḥ sarvasya janayitṛtvaṃ tad-upakaraṇānām anupasaṃhāre na upapadyate \| prākṛṣṭa-śrāma-hadṛtvaṃ śaśvad dhīyate śrī-hayerasya āśrita eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt ity-ādiṣu pratīyate \| atas tattvaṃ na upapadyata iti evaṃ prāptam \| tad-vikṣaṇam āhūte—upasaṃhāra-darśanāt na iti cen neti \|\|

pariharati—na kṣīravat dhi \| na sarveṣāṃ kārya-janana-śaktānām upasaṃhārāpekṣatvam asti;

yathā śrī-raṅganātha-aidaihika-janana-śatasya tat-janane \| evaṃ brahmaṇaḥ api svayam eva sarva-janana-śakti-sambandhaḥ janani-vṛtta-samutpadyate \| hi iti prasiddha-bṛhiraṅga-kṣodasya manda-tādhyāpanāya \| śrī-raḍhikā-tattva-jñānāpekṣā na daśyādi-mātrayaḥ; api tu śuśrūṣārthaṃ rasa-viśeṣārthaṃ ca \|\|24\|\|

## \|\| 2.1.25 \|\|

*deva-ādi-vat api loke*

**Комментарий:**

yathā deva-ādayaḥ sve sve loke saṅkalpa-mātreṇa sva-apekṣitāni sṛjanti; tathāhaṣo puruṣottamaḥ hastena jagat-saṅkalpa-mātreṇa sṛjati \| deva-ādīnāṃ devatātma-śaktīnāṃ hṛdaya-tattva-upādānam, brahmaṇo veda-apagata-śakti-saṅgrahaṇāni pratipattavyam \|\|25\|\| iti upasaṃhārāt darśanādhikaraṇam \|\|10\|\|

**atha kṛtsna-prasaṅgādhikaraṇam \|\|6\|\|**

## \|\| 2.1.26 \|\|

*kṛtsna-prasaṅgo niravayavatva-śabda-kopo vā*

**Комментарий:**

sat eva somya idam agra āsīt idaṃ vā agre na eva kiñcana āsīt ātmā vā idam eka eva agra āsīt iti-ādiṣu kāraṇa-avasthāyāṃ brahma eva niravayavam āsīt iti kāraṇa-avasthāyāṃ nirasta-cit-acid-vibhāgatayā niravayavaṃ brahma vā āsīt iti gamyate \| tat-vibhāgena niravayavena eva brahmāṇḍaṃ sthāyīti sakalasya ākāśa-ādi-acid-vibhāgaṃ brahmādi-tattva-caryanta-kṣetrajña-vibhāgaṃ ca apavadati cetas \| evaṃ sati tat eva paraṃ brahma kṛtsnaṃ kārya-dehena yuktaṃ mithyā-bhūtaṃ bhavet \| atha cid-vargaḥ kṣetrajña-vibhāga-viśvastaḥ, acid-vaṃśāśā-ākāśa-ādi-vibhāga-vibhaktaḥ iti ucyate, tathā sat eva somya idam agra āsīd ekam eva advitīyam brahma eva ātmānaṃ eva iti evaṃ-ādayaḥ kāraṇa-bhūtasya brahmaṇo niravayavatva-vādinaḥ śabdāḥ kupyeyuḥ—bādhitā bhaveyuḥ \| yady api sūkṣma-cid-acid-vastu-śarīraṃ brahma kāraṇam, sthūla-cid-acid-vastu-śarīraṃ brahma kāryam iti abhyupagamyate; tathā api śarīraṃ-śarīri kāryatābhyupagamāt ukta-doṣo durvāraḥ \| tasya niravayavasya bṛhat-bhavanaṃ ca na upapadyate \| kāryatvābhyupagata-tacchithitvra na upapadyate \| tasmāt asamanvayam iti \| ataḥ brahma-kāraṇatvaṃ na upapadyate \|\|26\|\|

iti ākṣipte samādhāne—

## \|\| 2.1.27 \|\|

*śruteḥ tu śabda-mūlatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

yuktaṃ ukta-doṣaṃ vyāvartayati \| na evam asāmañjasyam; kutaḥ ? śruteḥ \| śrutiḥ tāvat itara-vaiṣamyatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ tataḥ vicitrāśiṃ ca āha \| śrotena arthaṃ yathā-śruti pratipattavyam iti arthaḥ \| na tu śrutiḥ api agninā siñcet iti-vat paraspara-vādyāyogamya-arthaṃ pratipādayituṃ na samarthā; ata āha—śabda-mūlatvāt iti \| śabda-eka-pramāṇakatvena sakala-itara-vastu-vilakṣaṇatopapāditasya arthasya vicitra-kāritā-yogo na virudhyata iti na śāstra-yato dṛṣṭaṃ sādhanaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ vā arhīti brahma \|\|27\|\|

## \|\| 2.1.28 \|\|

*ātmani ca evaṃ vicitrāḥ ca hi*

**Комментарий:**

kiñca—evaṃ vastu-antara-saṃbandhinaḥ dharmasya vastu-antare ca āropaṇe sati, acetane ghaṭa-ādau dṛṣṭaṃ dharmam ati-sṛjat-jātīye cetane nitye ātmani api prasajyate \| tatra śaktīḥ ca bhāva-svabhāva-vaicitryāt eva—vicitrāḥ ca hi iti \| yathā agni-jala-taṭa-ādīnām anālocya-vijātīya-upādāna-upāya-ādi-śaktayaḥ ca vijātīyā dṛśyante; tadvat ekaika-vijātīye pare brahmaṇi tantra-tantra-aśeṣa-sahakāraḥ śaktayaḥ saṃbhavinī na kiṃcit viruddha-pakṣam \| yathā uktaṃ bhagavatā parāśareṇa—nirguṇasya aprameyasya śuddhasya api amala-ātmanaḥ \| kathaṃ sarga-ādi-kartṛtvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ abhyupagamyate \|\| iti sāmānya-dṛṣṭyā paricodya śaktayaḥ sarva-bhāvānām acintya-jñāna-gocarāḥ \| yataḥ ataḥ brahmaṇaḥ tāḥ tu sarga-ādyā bhāva-śaktayaḥ \| bhavanti tapatāṃ śreṣṭha pāvakasya yathā uṣṇatā \|\| iti \| śrutayaḥ ca kiṃ svid vanaṃ ka u sa vṛkṣa āsīt yataḥ dyāvā-pṛthivī niṣṭatakṣuḥ \| manīṣiṇaḥ manasā pṛcchate du tat yat atiṣṭhat bhuvanāni dhārayan \|\| brahma vanaṃ brahma sa vṛkṣa āsīt yataḥ dyāvā-pṛthivī niṣṭatakṣuḥ \| manīṣiṇaḥ manasā vibruvīmi vaḥ brahma adhyatiṣṭhat bhuvanāni dhārayan \|\| iti \| sāmānyataḥ dṛṣṭaṃ codyaṃ sarva-vastu-vilakṣaṇe pare brahmaṇi na avataṣṭe iti arthaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

itatra—

## \|\| 2.1.29 \|\|

*sva-pakṣa-doṣāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

sva-pakṣe—pradhāna-ādi-kāraṇa-vāde, laukika-vastu-vijātīyasya abhāvena pradhāna-ādeḥ loka-dṛṣṭa-doṣātmatvaṃ bhavet iti sakala-itara-vilakṣaṇaṃ brahma eva kāraṇam abhyupagantavyam \| pradhānaṃ ca niravayavam; tasya niravayavasya kathaṃ iva mahat-ādi-vividha-jagad-ārambha upapadyate ? sattvaṃ rajas tama iti tasya avayavā vidyante iti cet, tredhā vicchedaḥ ayam; kiṃ saṃcaya-avastha-samūhaḥ pradhānam ? uta saṃcaya-janitābhir avasthaṃ pradhānam u ? prathama-uttare kathaṃ pradhānaṃ kāraṇam iti svābhāvya-avirodhaḥ; sva-aṅga-bhūta-samūha-virodhaḥ ca; teṣām api niravayavatānāṃ kārya-ārambha-virodhaḥ ca \| samūha-pakṣe ca teṣāṃ niravayavatvena pradeśa-bhedam anapekṣya saṃyujyamānānāṃ na sthūla-dvya-ārambhakatva-siddhiḥ paramāṇu-kāraṇa-vāde api tathaiva aṇavaḥ niraṃśāḥ niṣpradeśāḥ pradeśa-bhedam anapekṣya parasparaṃ saṃyujyamānāḥ api na sthūla-kārya-ārambhāya prabhaveyuḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## \|\| 2.1.30 \|\|

*sarvopetā ca tat-darśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

sakala-itara-vastu-vijātīyā parā devatā sarveśvaratva-upetā ca \| tathā eva parāṃ devatāṃ darśayanti hi śrutayaḥ—parāsya śaktiḥ vividha eva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca tathā, apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ avijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ iti sakala-itara-vijātīyatvaṃ parasya devatāyāḥ pratipādya satya-kāma-satya-saṅkalpaḥ iti sarva-śaktitvaṃ pratipādayanti \|

tathā śrīmatomayaḥ prāṇa-śarīro bhārūpa-satya-saṅkalpa ākāśātmā sarva-karmā sarva-kāmaḥ sarva-gandhaḥ sarva-rasaḥ sarvam idam abhyāttaḥ avāk anādaraḥ iti ca \|\| 30 \|\|

## \|\| 2.1.31 \|\|

*vikaraṇatvāt na iti cet na uktām*

**Комментарий:**

yad api ekam eva brahma sakala-itara-vilakṣaṇaṃ sarva-śakti; tathā api na tasya kāryaṃ karaṇaṃ ca vidyate iti karaṇa-virahitasya na kāryārambha-saṃbhavaḥ iti cet, atra uttaraṃ śabda-smūlatvāt vicitrāś ca hi iti uktam \| śabda-eka-pramāṇakaṃ sakala-itara-vilakṣaṇaṃ tat-karaṇa-virahaḥ api tat-kāryārambhārhata iti arthaḥ \| tathā ca śrutiḥ—apāṇi-pādo javano grahītā iti evam-ādyā \|\| 31 \|\| iti kṛtsna-prasakti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha prayojanavattva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 10 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.1.32 \|\|

*na prayojanavattvāt*

**Комментарий:**

yad api īśvaraḥ prāpta-aśeṣaka eva san sakala-itara-vilakṣaṇa-cetana sarvārtha-siddhi-yuktaḥ svayam eva vicitraṃ jagat labdhaṃ śaknoti \| tathā api īśvara-kāraṇatvaṃ na saṃbhavati, prayojanavattvāt vicitra-buddheḥ; īśvarasya ca prayojanābhāvāt \| buddhi-pūrvakāriṇām ārambhe hi viṣayaṃ hi prayojanaṃ—svārthaḥ parārthaḥ vā \| na hi parasya brahmaṇaḥ tat-svabhāvāt eva āpta-samasta-kāmasya jagal-lakṣaṇaṃ kiñcana prayojanam avāptum avāpyate \| na api parārthaḥ; avāpta-kāmasya puruṣārthatā hi para-puruṣeṇa bhavati; na ca eteṣām artha-janma-prabhṛti-itarasya antaraskhihinānā-vidhā-nanta-duḥkha-bahulaṃ jagat-kāraṇyāt sṛjati; prastuta sukhakṛtānām eva jaṃtyaijjaṅgala-rūpayā sṛṣṭen \| ataḥ prayojanābhāvāt brahmasyaḥ kāraṇatvaṃ na upapadyata iti \|\| 32 \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pratyāha—

## \|\| 2.1.33 \|\|

*lokavat tu līlā-kaivalyam*

**Комментарий:**

avāpta-samasta-kāmasya paripūrṇasya svacchanda-rūpa-vikārya-vividha-vicitra-acid-vargi-nimiśra-jagat-sarga-līlā eva kevalaṃ prayojanam, lokavat tu—yathā loke sarveśinaḥ api medinībhārthaniṣṭhata-samupūrṇa-aiśvarya-vīrya-parākramasya api mahārājasya kevala-līlā-eka-prayojanāḥ kandukādi-ārambhā hṛdyante; tathā eva parasya api brahmaṇaḥ svacchanda-rūpa-mātra-vat līlā-sargasya asti iti eṣā eva līvā prayojanam iti niravadyam \|\| 33 \|\|

## \|\| 2.1.34 \|\|

*vaiṣamya-nirghṛṇye na sāpekṣatvāt tathā hi darśayati*

**Комментарий:**

yady api paras-puruṣasya sakala-itara-cid-vicitraḥ tu vilakṣaṇasya acintya-śakti-yogasya avāpta-samasta-aiśvarya-ekasya, niravadyasya api vividhāvidhi-vimiśra-jagat-sṛṣṭi-sāmarthyam abhyeti; tathā api deva-tiryaṅ-manuṣya-

sthāvarāsamanokta-brahmadhyamāpakarṣa-prasaṅgāt pakṣapātaḥ prasajyeta \| atiniṣṭhura-duḥkha-yoga-karaṇāḥ ca ghṛṇāṃ ca apajñaneyam iti \| tatra uttaram— na sāpekṣatvāt iti \| na prasajyeyātāṃ vaiṣamya-nirghṛṇye \| kutaḥ? sāpekṣatvāt—sṛjyamāna-devādi-viśeṣajña-karma-sāpekṣatvādivamṛṣṭeḥ \| deva-ādīnāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ deva-tiryaṅ-manuṣyatvāpattiṃ darśayati hi śruti-smṛtyoḥ— sādhukārī sādhuḥ bhavati pāpakārī pāpaḥ bhavati puṇyaḥ puṇyena karmaṇā bhavati pāpaḥ pāpena karmaṇā \| tathā bhagavatā parāśareṇa api devādi-dehenaiteḥ sṛjyamānānāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ prācīna-karma-śaktiḥ eva uktā— nimitta-mātram eva asau sṛjyānāṃ sarga-karmaṇi \| pradhāna-kāraṇī-bhūtā yato vai sṛjya-śaktayaḥ \|\| nimitta-mātraṃ muktvaiva na anyat kiñcit apekṣate \| nīyate tapatāṃ śreṣṭha svaśaktyā vastu vastutām \|\| iti \| svaśaktyā—svakarmṇā eva devādi-vastutām avir iti \|\| 34 \|\|

## \|\| 2.1.35 \|\|

*na karma-vibhāgāt iti cet na anāditvāt upapadyate ca api upalabhyate ca*

**Комментарий:**

prāk sṛṣṭeḥ kṣetrajñā nāma na santi; kutaḥ? avibhāga-śravaṇāt, sat eva somya idam āsīd iti; asat-sadānīṃ tad-bhāvāt karma na vidyate; kathaṃ tat-apekṣaṃ sṛṣṭi-vaiṣamyam ucyata iti cet; na, anāditvāt kṣetrajñānāṃ tat-karma-pravāhāṇāṃ ca \| tad-anāditvaikyavibhāga upapadyate ca; yathā-santaṃ kṣetrajña-vastu parityakta-nāma-rūpaṃ brahma-śarīratayā api pṛthag-upadeśānārham iti sūkṣmam \| tathā janma-purāṇe avṛttāvasthānām ukta-vibhāga-śabda-prayogaḥ ca \| upalabhyate ca teṣām anāditvaṃ— na jāyate mriyate vā vipaścituḥ iti \| sṛṣṭi-pravāhānāditvaṃ ca sūrya-candramasau dhātā yathā-pūrvam akalpayat iti-ādi \| tad idaṃ tarhi avyākṛtam āsīn nāma-rūpābhyāṃ vyākriyata iti nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇānārhāvasthānāt kṣetrajñānāṃ svarūpānāditvaṃ siddham \| smṛtau api prakṛtiṃ puruṣaṃ ca eva viddhi anādī ubhāv api iti \| atas sarva-vilakṣaṇatvāt sarva-śaktitvāt ca līlaika-prayojanatvāt, kṣetrajña-karmānuguṇena vicitra-vṛṣṭi-yogāt akṣa eva jagat-karaṇam \|\| 35 \|\|

## \|\| 2.1.36 \|\|

*sarva-dharmopapatteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

pradhāna-paramāṇv-ādīnāṃ kāraṇatve yat ubhaya-kalpyam, brahmaṇaḥ ca tasya sarvasya dharma-jātasya kāraṇatvopapattitaḥ brahmaṇi upapattiḥ ca brahma eva jagat-kāraṇam iti sthitam \|\| 36 \|\|

iti prayojanatva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 10 \|\| iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye dvitīyasya adhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 1 \|\| śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ \| dvitīya-adhyāye—dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ—racanā-anupapatty-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

## Pada 2

## \|\| 2.2.1 \|\|

*racanā-anupapatteḥ ca na anumānaṃ pravṛtteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

uktaṃ jagaj-janmādi-kāraṇaṃ paraṃ brahma iti; tatra paraiḥ udbhāvitāḥ ca doṣāḥ parihṛtāḥ \|

itānīṃ sva-pakṣa-rakṣāya para-pakṣāḥ pratiṣidhyante \| itarathā kasyacit cin-mata-vidyate iva pakṣāṇāṃ mukhyamāsūtratām ajānataḥ prāmāṇika-brahma-vidyā vaidika-pakṣe kiñcit dṛṣṭvā kartavyaṃ jāyeta api \| ataḥ para-pakṣa-pratiṣedhān anantaraḥ pādaḥ pravartate \| tatra prathamaṃ tāvat kāpila-mataṃ nirasyate, vaidikānugatas kārya-vādārtha-saṃgraheṇa asya sat-pakṣa-niṣedha-saṃbhāvanā-mātra-hetutvātiriktāt \| īkṣateḥ na aśabdam ity-ādibhiḥ vaidika-nyāyānāṃ tattva-paratva-mātra-yuktatvaṃ; api cet tat-pakṣa-svarūpa-pratiṣedhaḥ kriyata iti na paunaruktyāśaṅkā \|\|

evaṃ hi sāṅkhyānāṃ darśana-sthitiḥ— mūla-prakṛtiḥ avikṛtiḥ mahad-ādyāḥ prakṛti-vikṛtayaḥ sapta \| ṣoḍaśakas tu vikāraḥ na prakṛtiḥ na vikṛtiḥ puruṣaḥ \|\| iti tattva-saṃgrahaḥ \| mūla-prakṛtir api sukha-duḥkha-mohātmakāni laghu-prakāśa-calatva-phala-prabhṛtīni yathāsaṃbhavaṃ kārya-karaṇa-rūpāṇi vivekālambyūnānī rekhāṇi samatāyuccāni sattva-rajas-tamāṃsi dravyāṇi \| sā ca sattva-rajas-tamasāṃ sāmyāvasthā prakṛtiḥ ekā svayam acetanā eka-cetana-bhogāpavargārthā nityā sarvagatā satata-vikriyā na kasyacit vikṛtiḥ; na tu puruṣa-kārya eva \| mahad ākhyāt tad vikāra-yogyāṃ ca prakṛty-avastham-mahān ahaṅkāraḥ śabdas tan-mātraṃ sparśa-tan-mātraṃ rūpa-tan-mātraṃ rasa-tan-mātraṃ gandha-tan-mātram-

iti \| tatra ahaṅkārāsthāt— vaikārikas taijaso bhūtādir ityā kramāt sāttviko rājasas tāmasaś ca \| tatra vaikārikāt sātvikā indriyāṇi; bhūtādis tāmaso mahā-bhūta-hetu-bhūta-tan-mātra-hetuḥ; taijasau rajas-tadrūbhayor anugrahakaḥ \| ākāśādīni pañca mahā-bhūtāni śrotrādīni pañca jñānendriyāṇi vāg-ādīni pañca karmendriyāṇi mana iti kevala-vikārāḥ ṣoḍaśa \| puruṣas tu nirapariṇāmatvena na kasyacit prakṛtiḥ; na kasyacit vikṛtiḥ; tata eva nir-marma-kartr̥-caitanya-mātra-vṛttitvāt niṣkriya-sarvagataḥ prati-śarīraṃ bhinnaś ca \| nirvikāratvān niṣkriyatvāc ca tasya kartṛtvaṃ bhoktṛtvaṃ ca na saṃbhavati \|\|

evaṃ-bhūte api tattve mūḍhaḥ prakṛti-puruṣa-sannidhi-mātreṇa puruṣasya caitanyaṃ prakṛtau adhyasya prakṛteś ca kartṛtvaṃ sphaṭika-karaṇādi-vat japā-kusuma-sthāruṇimānaṃ puruṣe adhyasya ‘ahaṃ kartā bhoktā’ iti manyate \| evaṃ sattvānāṃ yogaḥ; tattva-jñānād apavargaḥ \| tade etat pratyakṣānumānābhyāṃ sādhayati \| tatra pratyakṣa-siddheṣu padārtheṣu na atīva vivādam arhati \| āgamā api kapilādi-vaṃśa-jñāna-mūlā iti saḥ api prathame kāṇḍe pramāṇa-lakṣaṇe nirasta-prāyaḥ \| yadi ca pradhānam eva jagat-kāraṇam iti anumānam, tarhi parasmin tamasī sarvaṃ nirastaṃ bhavatīti tad eva nirasayate \|\|

te ca evaṃ varṇayanti— kṛtsnasya jagataḥ eka-mūlatvam anvayānuguṇam anopapad, anekasya kāryasya atyanta-vibhāge kāraṇānavasthānāt \| tantu-prabhṛtayo hi avayavāḥ svarūpeṇa ekatva-bahubhiḥ pārthaiḥ parasparaṃ saṃyujyamānā avayavini utpādayanti; teṣāṃ tantu-avayavāḥ sva-avayavair bhedyamānā rūpam utpādayate; te ca

tathā-bhūtaṃ svāvyarhaiḥ iti paramāṇu-śarīri sva-kārya-brahmaṇaḥ pāraṃsa-saṃyoga-mātre eva sva-kārya-utpādana-sāmarthyata eva iyam; anyathā prativādhi-upapatteḥ \| paramāṇavaḥ api hi dṛṣṭena sva-aṃśaiḥ saṃyoga-utpādyante, te ca sva-aṃśaiḥ iti na kvacit na kāraṇa-vyavasthitiḥ \| ataḥ kāraṇa-vyavasthā-siddhi-artham dṛśyaṃ vividha-vicitra-kārya-pariṇāma-śakti-yuktaṃ svayam acyuta-svarūpam eva mahat-ādi-nānā-avasthā-āśrayaḥ kāraṇam ādhyasthīyam \| tat ca ka kāraṇaṃ guṇa-traya-sāmyaṃ pradhānam iti tarkyante hetutvena vyavasthita—bhedānāṃ pariṇāmatvam avayava-cchititaḥ pradhāneṣu \| kāraṇa-kārya-vibhāgāt vibhāgāt vaiśvarūpyasya \|\| kāraṇam asti avyatantram iti \| ayarthaḥ—viśva-rūpam eva vaiśva-rūpyam, vicitra-saṃniveśaṃ tat-mukhena hi kurute jagat \| tasya jagat-vicitra-saṃniveśena kārya-bhūtaṃ tat-saṃsthāpyaka kāraṇam \| kutaḥ ? kāryatvāt \| kāryaṃ hi sarveṣya tat-rūpa-kāraṇa-viśeṣāt vibhāgaḥ tasmin eva avibhāgaḥ ca dṛśyate \| yathā ghaṭa-mukuṭa-ādeḥ kāryasya tat-rūpa-tantu-mṛd-ādiḥ kāraṇa-dvibhāgaḥ tasmin eva ca avibhāgaḥ \| ataḥ viśva-rūpasya jagataḥ tat-saṃsthāta-pradhānāt upaniṣṭataḥ tasmin eva layaḥ iti pradhāna-kāraṇakam eva jagat \| guṇa-traya-ātmakaṃ rūpaṃ pradhānam eva jagataḥ rūpaṃ kāraṇam; sattva-rajas-tobhya-sukha-duḥkha-moha-ātmakatvāt jagataḥ \| yathā mṛd-ātmane ghaṭasya mṛd-dravyam eva kāraṇam \| tat eva hi tat-rūpatayā pravartam avicchinnam, tathā darśanāt \| avyaktasya guṇa-sāmya-rūpasya ca itaraḥ kāla-tathā-pariṇatasya eva kāraṇatvaṃ bhedānāṃ mahat-ahaṅkāra-tanmātra-ādīnāṃ pariṇatatva-avagamyate \| mahat-ādīni ca ghaṭa-ādivat pariṇatāni kṛtsna-jagat-upayogī na prabhavanti, atīndriyaṃ jagat-guṇa-caya-asāmānya-rūpa-pradhāna-eka-kāraṇam iti niścīyate \|\| adhiuccyate—racanā-anupapatteḥ ca na anumānaṃ pradhāneṣu \| anumīyata iti anumānam \| na bhavat-uktaṃ pradhānaṃ vicitra-jagat-racanā-asamartham, acetanatve sati tat-tat-svabhāva-vijñāna-adhiṣṭhitatvāt; yat evaṃ tat tathā, yathā rathyā-sāda-ādi-vinirmitiḥ api kevalatva-vardhakam \| bādhita-cetanasya tat-anādhiṣṭhitasya kārya-ārambha-anupapatteḥ īrṇāt, tat-adhiṣṭhitasya kārya-ārambha-pravṛtteḥ dṛṣṭatvāt ca na prajñāna-adhiṣṭhitaṃ pradhānaṃ kāraṇam iti uktaṃ bhavati \| ca-kārāt anvaya-vyatirekābhyāṃ samuccinoti \| na hi ativataṃ śaila-kūṇīlāt-ādi kāraṇatva-vyāsam \| na ca vācyam mā bhūt vivata-ātmā api śailasya adhiṣṭhātrīṇāṃ kāraṇatvam, tasya tu hema-ādeḥ kārya-aniṣṭhitasya kāraṇatva-vyapadeśa-sthādeḥ; śāstra-ādau api dṛṣṭāni kāryāṇi kārya-aniṣṭhānī kāraṇatva-vyāsanāni iti \| yat tat sarva-vācyaḥ brahma-paryāḥ; na tu brahma-vat rūpam \| sattvādayaḥ hi pṛthivī-ādi-vyatala-mukta-prakāśa-ādi-hetu-bhūtāḥ tat-svabhāva-viśeṣāḥ eva \| na tu mṛdit-pātra-ādivat dravyatayā kārya-anvitatā upalabhyante \| guṇāḥ iti eva ca sattva-ādīnāṃ prasiddhiḥ \|\| yat kāraṇa-vyavasthā-siddhaye jagataḥ eka-mūlatvam uktam, tatra api sattva-ādīnāṃ na ekatva-aśeṣa-pratyaye \| ataḥ eva kāraṇa-vyavasthā ca na sidhyati \| sāmya-avasthā-sacivā ayam eva hi pradhāna-

m iti cet na tasu \| ataḥ kāraṇa-brahma-vāda-ananyathā tad-vad-arthe eva \| na ca teṣām aparimitatvena vyavasthā-sthitiḥ; aparimitatve hi śravaṇāgāmi sarvagatvena vyakta-adhika-bhāva-abhāvād bṛhat-sāmya-siddhiḥ kārya-ārambha-asaṃbhavāt, kārya-ārambhe ca parimitatva-saṃkhyā-śravaṇāyam \|\| 1 \|\|

yatra rathyādi-gu-stṛṇam cetana-adhiṣṭhitatvaṃ dṛṣṭam, tad aparihṛtaṃ sarvaṃ pakṣīkṛtam iti āha—

## \|\| 2.2.2 \|\|

*payaḥ-ambu-vat cet tatrāpi*

**Комментарий:**

yat uktaṃ pradhānasya prājña-anādhiṣṭhitasya vividha-jagat-rachanā-anuguṇa-pariṇatiḥ; tatra, yataḥ payaḥ-ambu-vad-vṛtti-rūpa-pakṣe \| payaḥ tāvat dhimāvena pariṇamamānasya na anya-apekṣasya madhuriki-rūpaṃ pravṛtti-pariṇāma-pradarśanāt svata eva payaḥ upalabhyate \| yathā ca vāri-stambhu-muktasya ambunaḥ eka-rasasya nārikalāla-tāla-drūta-niryūta-nirmathita-vicitra-guṇaiḥ vividha-rasa-rūpeṇa pariṇāma-pravṛttiḥ svata eva dṛśyate \| tathā pradhānasya api pariṇāma-svabhāva-sthasya anabhihita eva pratisarga-avasthāyāṃ sahakāri-pariṇāmena avasthitasya sārgika-sthāyāṃ guṇa-vaiṣamya-nimitta-vicitra-rūpa-pariṇāma upapadyate \| yathoktaṃ pariṇāmataḥ salila-vat śakti-viśeṣāt iti \| tad evaṃ anyatra ananya-apekṣaṃ pravartata iti cet, atra uttaram—tatra api iti \| yadi kṣīra-jala-ādi dṛṣṭānta-tayā nirdiśyate, tatra api prājña-anādhiṣṭhāne pravṛttiḥ upapadyate; tat api pūrvavat pakṣīkṛtam iti abhiprāyaḥ \| upasaṃhāra-darśanāt na iti cet chīra-vat dhi iti atra dṛṣṭa-parisara-anantara-rahitasya api sva-asādhāraṇa-pariṇāma upapadyata iti etāvat uktam, na prājña-adhiṣṭhitatvaṃ parāhṛtam, yoneḥ ca hi iti-ādi-śruteḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.3 \|\|

*vyatireka-anavasthiteḥ ca anapekṣatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

īśasya satya-saṃkalpatva-īśvara-adhiṣṭhāna-apekṣa-pariṇāmitve sāṃkhya-atirekeṇa pratisaṃgā-avasthāyāṃ na vyaktita-prasaṅgaḥ ca na prājña-adhiṣṭhitaṃ pradhānaṃ kāraṇam \| prājña-adhiṣṭhitatve tasya satya-saṃkalpatvena sarva-aṅgīṇa-vicitra-vṛtti-vyavasthā-siddhiḥ \| na ca vācyaṃ prājña-adhiṣṭhitatve api tasya eva ātma-samasta-kāmasya paripūrṇasya anavadhika-atiśaya-ānandasya niravadyasya nirmalasya sarva-pritarvya-vyavasthā-hetu-abhāva-dviṣama-vṛtteḥ nirhetvatva-prasaṅgaḥ ca samānaḥ ayaṃ doṣa iti \| paripūrṇeṣu api līlā-śruti-upapatti-saṃbhavāt, sarveśasya tasya pariṇāma-virodha-apatra-kṛt-nirdeśa-anukūlam aṅgīkṛti-sarva-viśeṣa-hetoḥ asaṃbhavāt, kṣetra-karmaṇā eva viṣama-mṛddhi-daridrya-yathāpākavatāt ca \| nanu evaṃ kṣetrajña-puṇya-apuṇya-rūpa-karmaṇā eva sarvā vyavasthā siddhyati iti kuta īśvara-anādhiṣṭhā \| puṇya-apuṇya-rūpa-anubhavi-karma-saṃskṛtā prakṛtiḥ eva puruṣārtha-rūpaṃ yathā-yathaṃ vyavasthayā pariṇamate \| yathā viṣapati-dūṣita-tāmra-bhāṇḍa-ādena āmoda-viśeṣa-vyakti-citānāṃ ca mugdha-dugdhebhyaḥ pariṇāma-viśeṣaḥ deśa-kāla-vyavasthayā dṛśyate \| avasanna-prati-karma-vyavasthā, deva-ādi-viṣama-mṛddhiḥ, karma-vyavasthā ca sarva-prakāra-pariṇāma-śakti-pūrṇasya pradhānasya eva upapadyata iti \|\|

anabhisaṃhito bhagavān puṇya-apuṇya-karma-svarūpaḥ \| puṇya-apuṇya-svarūpo hi śāstre karma abhīyate \| śāstraṃ ca anādi-vihita-anādi-vicchinna-pāṭha-saṃpradāya-anādhātṛ-mātra-ādi-deva-bhāṣita-veda-vākya-akṣara-rāśiḥ \| tat ca parama-puruṣa-ārādhana-tad-vidhi-paryaṅkaṃ karmaṇi puṇya-apuṇye, tad-anuṣṭhāna-tad-aprādhāne ca tat-phale sukha-duḥkhe iti vadati \| tathā āha dramiḍācāryaḥ phala-saṃdhi-bhinnatayā hi karma-īśvara-satāṃ viproṣita sa pretośate phalāya iti śāstra-maryādā iti \| tathā eva śrutiḥ iṣṭā-pūrtaṃ bahudhā jātaṃ jāyamānaṃ viśvaṃ bibharti bhuvanasya nābhiḥ iti \|\|

tathā ca bhagavatā svayam eva uktaṃ— yataḥ pravṛttir bhūtānāṃ yena sarvam idaṃ tataṃ \| sva-karmaṇā tam abhyarcya siddhiṃ vindati mānavaḥ \|\| iti, jñānāhaṃ dviṣataḥ krūrān saṃsāreṣu nara-adhamān \| kṣipāmi ajasram aśubhān āsurīṣu eva yoniṣu \|\| iti ca \| sa bhagavān puruṣottamo upāsana-svarūpa-sarveśvara-tadīya-sarvātma-satkṛtaḥ; sva-māhātmya-anuguṇa-līlā-pravṛttaḥ, etāni karmāṇi sarva-cetana-acetara-anya-samīcīnāni karma-ādi-vaśye saṃvidhāya, tad-anudāna-aucitya-deha-indriya-ādikaṃ tat-kriyamanaś-śakti ca sarveṣāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ sāmānyena pradāya, sva-śāsana-tad-avadhi śāstraṃ ca pradṛśya, tad-upasaṃhāra-artham ca antar-ātmatayā anupareṇa anumantṛtayā ca niyacchati iti sthiti \| kṣetrajñāḥ tu tad-āhita-śāstra-tat-pravṛtti-karaṇa-kalevara-ādikas-tad-ādhārāś ca svayam eva sva-icchā-anuṣṭheyena puṇya-apuṇya-rūpe karmaṇi upādadate \| tataḥ puṇya-rūpa-karma-kāriṇaṃ sva-ajñāna-anuvartinaṃ jñātvā dharma-artha-kāma-mokṣair vardhayate \| śāstra-anativartinaṃ ca tad-viparyayair yojayati \| ataḥ svātantrya-cārvīdaṃkalpādye api na avakāśaṃ labhate \| dayā hi nāma sva-artha-nirapekṣā para-duḥkha-asahiṣṇutā \| sā ca sva-āśrita-aviṣayita-vyavasthāpikā api vartamānā na guṇāya kalpate; pratyuta apuruṣārtham eva āvahati \| tad-viṣaha eva tasya guṇaḥ; anyathā jñatvi-niṣṭā-hīnatām aguṇatva-prasaṅgāt \| sva-āśritāv iti ucyasya artha-anuvartina-mātreṇa anācchandana-kalpa-upacita-duṣṭi-bahulān aparādhān dṛśīkaraṇa nirtiśaya-mukhāṃbudhau svayam eva plavate \| yathoktaṃ— teṣāṃ satata-yuktānāṃ bhajatāṃ prīti-pūrvakam \| dadāmi buddhi-yogaṃ taṃ yena mām upayānti te \|\| teṣām eva anukampa-artham aham ajñāna-jaṃ tamaḥ \| nāśayāmi ātma-bhāva-stho jñāna-dīpena bhāsvatā \|\| iti \| ataḥ pradhāna-adhīhiṃ pradhānaṃ na kāraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

atha syāt— yadi api pradhāna-adhiṣṭhātṛtvāyaḥ prakṛteḥ pariṣyandana-pravṛttir api na saṃbhavati iti uktam, tathā api anapekṣayā eva pariṇāma-pravṛttiḥ saṃbhavati, yathā dṛṣṭāntaḥ; dhenuvatsitoṣṭukaṃ hi tṛṇa-udaka-ādi svayam eva kṣīra-tayā ākāreṇa pariṇamamānaṃ dṛśyate \| ataḥ prakṛteḥ api svayam eva jagad-ākāreṇa pariṇameta iti \| tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.2.4 \|\|

*anyatra-abhāvāt ca na tṛṇa-ādi-vat*

**Комментарий:**

na etad upapadyate, tṛṇa-ādeḥ pradhāna-adhiṣṭhitusya pariṇāma-abhāvād dṛṣṭānta-asiddheḥ \| katham asiddhiḥ?

anyathā-abhāvāt—yadi hi kṛṣṇa-udaka-ādi-kṣaṇa-tad-vāhya-yuktaṃ bahiḥ vā śarīra-kāreṇa paryañcarataḥ; tataḥ pradhāna-siddhiḥ eva parihanyata iti vaktum aśakyata \| na evam avasthite; ataḥ vaitāla-yuktaṃ prāpta eva śarīra-kāreṇa \| prayojyatvāt cetana-adhiṣṭhitam eva atra pravṛttaṃ tantraṃ vyabhicāra-darśanāya \|\| 4 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.5 \|\|

*puruṣa-aśmavat iti cet tathā api*

**Комментарий:**

atha ucyeta—yathā hi caitanya-mātra-vapuḥ puruṣo niṣkriyaḥ pradhānam api dṛṣṭa-śakti-vikalam; tathā api puruṣa-sannidhānāt cetanaṃ pradhānaṃ pravartate, yathā darśanāt \| gamana-śakti-vikala-dṛk-śakti-yukta-upaṅguḥ, sa-vidhāna-racanā-caturyaḥ api dṛk-śakti-vikalaḥ pravṛtti-śakta-aśvaḥ pravartate; ayaskānta-adharmāsidhānāt ca āyaḥ pravartate \| evaṃ prakṛti-puruṣa-saṃyoga-kṛto jagat-sargaḥ pravartate \| yathoktaṃ puruṣasya darśana-arthaṃ kaivalya-arthaṃ tathā pradhānasya \| paṅgu-andha-vat ubhayoḥ api saṃyogaḥ tat-kṛtaḥ sargaḥ \|\| iti \| puruṣasya pradhāna-upabhoga-arthaṃ kaivalya-arthaṃ ca puruṣa-sannidhānāt pradhānaṃ sarga-ādi pravartata iti arthaḥ \| atra ucyate—tathā api iti \| evam api pradhānasya pravṛtti-asaṃbhavaḥ tad-avasthā eva, paṅgu-vandha-aśva-śakti-vikalasya api mārga-darśanāt puruṣeśāvayaḥ kācā-vicitrā viśeṣāḥ sahasra-saṃkhyāḥ; ayasaḥ api cetana-saṃskṛtam upekṣā-dharmam eva pravartate; tathā ayaskānta-maṇeḥ ca āyaska-sambandha-yogyatvam asti \| puruṣasya tu niṣkriyasya na tādṛśā vikārāḥ saṃbhavanti \| sa-vidhāna-mātrasya nityatvena nitya-sarga-prasaṅgaḥ; nitya-yuktatvena bandha-abhāvaḥ apavarga-abhāvaḥ ca \|\| 5 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.6 \|\|

*aṅgitva-anupapatteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

guṇānāṃ sukha-duḥkha-mohānām anyonya-aṅga-aṅgi-bhāva-ādi jagat-utpattiḥ pratipanna-triguṇa-āśraya-viśeṣāt iti vadadbhir abhyudaya-sṛjyata \| pratisaṃsthāyāṃ tu sāmya-avasthānāṃ sarva-parasparāmaṅgonyāṅgi-bhāva-pūrva-nyāya-abhāvāt aṅga-aṅgi-bhāva-anupapatteḥ jagat-sargaḥ na upapadyate \| tad-arthi kāryasya ayuktatvena nitya-sarga-prasaṅgaḥ \| ataḥ ca na pradhāna-siddhiḥ pradhānaṃ kāraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.7 \|\|

*anyathā-anumitau ca jña-śakti-viyogāt*

**Комментарий:**

dṛṣṭa-prakāra-atirikta-prakāra-antareṇa pradhāna-anumitau ca pradhānasya jñātṛtva-śakti-viyogāt eva doṣaḥ prāduṣyet \| ataḥ na kathaṃcid acyutānumānena pradhāna-siddhiḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.8 \|\|

*abhyupagame api artha-abhāvāt*

**Комментарий:**

anumānena pradhāna-siddhi-abhyupagame api pradhānena prayojana-abhāvāt tad-anumānatvam \| puruṣasya darśana-arthaṃ kaivalya-arthaṃ tathā pradhānasya iti pradhānasya prayojanaṃ puruṣa-upabhoga-apavargau nimittau,

to ca na saṃbhavataḥ \| puruṣasya caitanya-mātra-svarūpo niṣkriyasya nirvikārasya nirmalasya tata eva nitya-mukta-svarūpasya prakṛti-darśana-hetuḥ bhogaḥ tadīya-yoga-rūpo 'pavargaś ca na saṃbhavati \| evaṃ-rūpasya eva prakṛti-saṃvidhi-mātrāntara-pariṇāma-viśeṣa-mukhaḥ sva-darśana-hetu-bhoga-sambhavanāyāṃ prakṛti-saṃvidhānasya nityatvena kadācid api apavargo na setsyati \|\| 8 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.9 \|\|

*vipratiṣedhāc ca asamañjasam*

**Комментарий:**

vipratiṣiddhaṃ ca idaṃ sāṅkhyānāṃ darśanam \| tathā hi—prakṛteḥ parārthatvena heyatvena bhogyatvena ca prakṛteḥ bhoktāram adhiṣṭhātāraṃ jñaṃ sākṣiṇaṃ ca puruṣam abhyupagamya prakṛtyā eva sādhana-bhūtayā tasya kaivalyam api prāpyaṃ vadatas tasya nitya-nirvikāra-caitanya-mātra-svarūpatayā akartṛtvaṃ, kaivalyaṃ ca svarūpam eva iti; tata eva bandha-mokṣa-sādhana-anuguṇaṃ mokṣaṃ prakṛteḥ eva ca āhuḥ; evaṃ-bhūta-vikāro 'sau puruṣa-viṣayānatva-prakṛteḥ iti ce tarādhyakṣeṇa sarga-ādi-vyavṛttiṃ puruṣa-bhoga-apavarga-arthaṃ ca āhuḥ—sāddhyatā-parārthakvād dvi-guṇādhi-paryaya-vidhānāt \| puruṣo 'sti bhoktṛ-bhāvāt kaivalya-arthaṃ pravṛtteś ca \|\| tasmāc ca viparyāsa-siddhaṃ sākṣitvam asya puruṣasya \| kaivalyaṃ mādhyasthyaṃ draṣṭṛtvam akartṛ-bhāvaś ca \|\| iti \| puruṣa-vimokṣa-nimittaṃ tathā pravṛttiḥ pradhānasya hetum evaṃ ca āhuḥ—śrutasya badhyatve 'pi na mucyate na api saṃsarati kaścit \| saṃsarati badhyate mucyate ca nānā-āśrayā prakṛtiḥ \|\| iti \|\|

tathā śretasa-mānasa-saṃyogāc cetanaṃ caitanya-artham iva liṅgam \| guṇa-kartṛtve ca tathā kartā iva bhavati udāsīnaḥ \|\| puruṣasya darśana-arthaṃ kaivalya-arthaṃ tathā pradhānasya \| paṅgu-andha-vad ubhayor api saṃyogaḥ tat-kṛtaḥ sargaḥ \|\| iti \| sākṣitva-draṣṭṛtva-bhoktṛtva-ādyo nitya-nirvikārasya cetanasya akartuḥ brahmāno 'pi kaivalya-eka-rūpa-svarūpasya na saṃbhavati \| evaṃ-rūpasya tat-sādhyam ānuma-śramo 'pi na saṃbhavati, adhyāsa-āśramayor api vikāratvāt \| prakṛteś ca to na saṃbhavataḥ, tasyāḥ cetana-dharmatvāt \| adhyāso hi nāma cetanasya anyasmin anya-dharma-anusandhānam \| sa ca cetana-dharmo vikāraś ca \| na ca puruṣasya prakṛti-saṃvidhi-mātreṇa adhyāsa-tadayor saṃbhavati, nirvikāratvād eva \| saṃbhavati cet, nityaṃ prasajyeta \| saṃvideḥ aśuddvi-kāratvaṃ na vijñāṇa-ghana eva iti atra pratipāditam \| prakṛtiś ca saṃsarati badhyate mucyate ca iti, kathaṃ nitya-muktasya puruṣasya upakāriṇī setsyatyate ? badhnāti hi anādi-viṣaya-yopariṇāmam upakāriṇaḥ puṃsaḥ \| guṇavatva-guṇasya sataḥ tasya artham apārthakaṃ carati \|\| iti \|\|

tathā prakṛti-vene puruṣeṇa prayatyamānā hṛṣṭā, tasmāt puruṣaḥ tasyā na eva niyacchanti iti ca āhuḥ—raṅgasya darśayitvā nivartate nartakī yathā nṛtyāt \| puruṣasya tathā ātmānaṃ prakāśya vinivartate prakṛtiḥ \|\| prakṛteḥ sukumāra-taraṃ na kiñcit asti iti me matir bhavati \| yā dṛṣṭā asmi iti

punar-darśana-anupapattiḥ puruṣasya \|\| iti \| tat ayam ajñaḥ tāṃ puruṣaḥ hi nityamuktatvāt nirvikāratvāt ca tāṃ kadācit api paśyati, na adhyasyati ca \| svayaṃ ca sva-ātmānaṃ na paśyati, acetanatvāt \| puruṣasya sva-ātma-darśanaṃ sva-darśa-nivṛttiḥ na adhyasyati ca, svayam acetanatvāt; puruṣasya ca darśana-rūpa-vikāra-asaṃbhavāt \| atha sannidhi-mātreṇa eva darśana-nimittatve; sannidheḥ nityatvena nitya-darśana-prasaṅgaḥ iti etat \| svarūpa-tiraskāra-ādi-nikara-vidhaiḥ api nitya-nirvikārasya na upapadyate \|\|

kiñca mokṣa-hetutvaṃ sa-vidhānasya eva darśanaṃ vedaḥ; bandha-hetuḥ api tat eva iti nitya-bandhaḥ mokṣaḥ ca syātām \| anyathā-darśanaṃ bandha-hetuḥ, yathā antar-ātma-rūpa-darśanaṃ mokṣa-hetuḥ iti cet; ubhaya-vidhayā api darśanasya sa-vidhāna-rūpatvāt nikāra-ādeḥ doṣa-prasaṅgaḥ eva \| sa-vidheḥ anityatve tasya hetuḥ alveṣaṇīyaḥ, tasya api tad-avasthā \| atha eteṣāṃ pariṇāmitayā svarūpa-nūtanatve eva sa-vidhiḥ iti, tadā svarūpasya nityatvena nitya-bandha-mokṣau \| ataḥ evam-ādeḥ prati-vedānta-siddhāntānāṃ darśana-matam asaṅjasam \|\|

yady api kūṭastha-nitya-cid-rūpa-brahma-prakāśa-cin-mātrā brahma-vidyā-siddhāntena apāramārthika-bandha-mokṣa-bhāk iti vadanti, teṣām anukta-mīṃsā-vidyā-siddhāntayā sādharmya-avakāśa-samāśrayam eva; idaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ—śāstrīyāḥ janma-maraṇa-pratiniyama-avidyā-avasthā-siddhy-arthaṃ puruṣa-bahutvam icchanti, te tu tat api na icchanti iti śruta-rasāmāñjasyam \| yaḥ tu prakṛteḥ pāramārthika-sāmarthya-bhāgena vaidharmyam uktam, tat ayuktam; pāramārthika-cid-ekya-pāramārthikatve api nitya-nirvikāra-sva-prākāśaka-rūpa-cin-mātrasya sva-ccha-tiraskāra-sākṣitva-anupapatteḥ \| apāramārthikatve tu tattvataḥ hasti-tvabādhyatva-anupagamāt sundaram asaṅjasam \| aupādhika-bheda-vāde api upādhi-saṃnidhānaḥ brahmaṇaḥ api evaṃ svabhāvaḥ iti aupādhika-saṃbandha-anupapatteḥ asāmañjasyaṃ pūrvam avoktam \|\| 9 \|\| iti racanā-anupapatti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha mahat-dīrgha-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.2.10 \|\|

*mahat-dīrghavat vā hrasva-parimaṇḍalābhyām*

**Комментарий:**

pradhāna-kāraṇa-vādasya yukty-ābhāsa-mūlatayā vipratipatti-vattvāt ca asāmañjasyam uktam; saṃprati paramāṇu-kāraṇa-vādasya api asāmañjasyaṃ pratipādyate—mahat-dīrghavat vā hrasva-parimaṇḍalābhyām iti \| asamañjasam iti vartate \| bāla-agra-śata-aṃśaḥ \| hrasva-parimaṇḍalābhyāṃ—dvyaṇuka-paramāṇubhyāṃ, mahat-dīrghaṃ ca—tryaṇuka-utpatti-vāde vadet, anyat ca tat-rūpa-guṇatāṃ sarvam asamañjasam; paramāṇubhyaḥ dvyaṇuka-ādi-śrameṇa jagat-utpatti-vāda-vadana yat api asamañjasam iti arthaḥ \| tathā hi—tantu-prabhṛtayaḥ hi avayavāḥ sva-aṃśavat-dṛṣṭaiḥ pārśva-saṃyujyamānāḥ avayavi-niṣpattau anti, paramāṇavaḥ api svakīyaiḥ bahubhiḥ

pārthe saṃjñā-prasātā eva brahma-aśva-vīṇām upāsakā bhaveyuḥ; anyathā paramāṇūnāṃ pradeśa-bhedābhāve sati sahasra-paramāṇu-saṃyoge api eka-paramāṇu-samāna-saṃsthitiḥ iti upari-māṇasya aṇutva-hetutva-mahattva-dīrghatva-dy-asiddhiḥ syāt \| pradeśa-bhedābhyupagame paramāṇavaḥ api sāṃśāḥ svīkāryāḥ, te ca svāṃśaiḥ iti anavasthā \| nanu vācyam avayavatva-lakṣaṇa-mahattvābhyaṃ hi sarva-prasiddhayoḥ anubhyamāḥ; paramāṇoḥ upacita-lāghavacve avayavānta-tyakṣābhyāsa-bandha-mahīyasaḥ vaiṣamya-asiddheḥ avayavāpaka-śaka-ākāśavat ca abhyupagamanīya iti \| paramāṇūnāṃ pradeśa-bhedābhāve sati eka-paramāṇu-parisaṃsthānādikiḥ prathimā na jāyate iti sūtreṇa mahodaya-ukta-asiddheḥ \| kiṃ bhūma iti hetuḥ vaidikaḥ pakṣaḥ parigṛhītāt \|

vastu paraḥ brahma-kāraṇa-vāda-duṣaṇa-parihāra-param idaṃ sūtraṃ vyākhyātam; tat asaṅgatam, punaruktaṃ ca; brahma-kāraṇa-vāde paroktan doṣān pūrvāsmin pāde parihṛtya para-pakṣa-nirīkṣaṇo hi asmin pāde kriyate \| cetana-anu-brahmaṇo jagad-utpatti-saṃbhavaḥ ca na vilakṣaṇatvāt iti eva prapañcitaḥ \| ataḥ hi aṇḍa-parimaṇḍalābhyaṃ mahad-dīrgha-pṛthutva-utpatti-vat acandra tad-abhyupagataṃ sarvam asamañjasam iti eva sūtrārthaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

**kim atra api ad-anusāraḥ tikṣata āhu—**

## \|\| 2.2.11 \|\|

*-5. ubhayathā api na karmātaḥ tad-abhāvaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

paramāṇu-kāraṇa-vāde hi paramāṇu-gata-karma-nimita-tat-saṃyoga-pūrvaka-dvyaṇukādi-krameṇa jagad-utpattiḥ nirūpyate \| tatra nitya-niravayava-dravyatvānusāreṇa paramāṇūnām ādhyaṃ karma-adhyakāritavinityaṃ yujyate anīśvara-icchānaṃ vāyoditādi-arambhana-puṇya-anāpāśrayaiḥ karma-adhyakāritavini iti \| tarhi paramāṇu-gataṃ karma sva-gata-adṛṣṭa-kāritam, ātma-gata-adṛṣṭa-kāritaṃ vā? ubhayathā api na saṃbhavati; kṣetrajña-upadhyāyāttubhāna-janita-tarastha-adṛṣṭasya paramāṇu-tattva-saṃbhavāt \| saṃbhave ca sadā-utpādakatva-prasaṅgaḥ \| ātma-gatasya ca adṛṣṭasya paramāṇu-gata-karma-utpatti-hetutvaṃ na saṃbhavati \| aya-adṛṣṭavat-dhārmayoḥ karma-utpattiḥ, tadā tat syāt adṛṣṭa-vāheṣu nityatvena nitya-saṃyoga-prasaṅgaḥ \| na adṛṣṭaṃ vipāka-apekṣaṃ phalāya alam \| kānicit addarśani tadā eva eva vipacyante, kānicit janmāntare, kānicit kalpāntare \| ataḥ vipāka-apekṣatvena sadā-utpādakatva-prasaṅga iti \| na etat, anantaiḥ ātmabhiḥ asṛṣṭat-pūrva-karmābhyupadattujjhitāneka-vidha-karma-nirjita-samasta-bhuvana-sebhir īśa-kāle ekadā-utpatti-prāk-upapādani-katvāt \| kṣata eva yugapat-saṃhāro dvyarādhakāla-samavipākena avasthānaṃ ca na saṃśiṣyate \| na ca īśvara-icchādi-tad-vīśiṣṭa-adṛṣṭa-saṃyogāt aṇu karma, āgamānika-īśvara-siddheḥ śāstra-yonitvāt iti atra upapāditatvāt \| ataḥ jagad-utpatteḥ aṇu-karma-pūrvakatvābhāvaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.12 \|\|

*samavāya-abhyupagamāt ca sāmyāt anavasthiteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

samavāyābhyupagamābhāsañjasam \| kutaḥ ? sāmyāt anavasthiteḥ—samavāyasya api avayava-vijātīya-guṇavat-upakāra-antara-apekṣā-sāmyāt upakāra-antarasya api tathā-ittham-avasthiteḥ asamañjasam eva \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—ayutasiddhānām ādhāra-ādheya-bhūtānām iha pratyaya-hetuḥ sambandhaḥ; sa samavāya iti samavāyaḥ abhyupagamyate \| ayutasiddheṣu upalabhyamānaḥ jāti-ādīnāṃ tathā-bhāvasya nirvāhaka-tvena cet samavāyaḥ abhyupagamyate, samavāyasya api tat-sādhyān tathā-bhāva-hetutvena eva upagamyaḥ; tasya api tathā-ittham-avasthitiḥ \| samavāyasya tat-pratyaya-siddhatvaṃ svabhāvaḥ iti parikalpyate cet, jāti-guṇānām eva svabhāvaḥ parikalpanīyaḥ, na punaḥ dṛṣṭa-caram samavāya-abhyupagamaḥ tasya eva svabhāvaḥ iti kalpayituṃ yuktam iti \|\| 12 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.13 \|\|

*nityam eva ca bhāvāt*

**Комментарий:**

samavāyasya sambandhatva-sambandhasya nityatve sambandhinaḥ jagataḥ ca nityatvena bhāvāt sama-dūṣaṇam \|\| 13 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.14 \|\|

*rūpādi-mattvāt ca viparyayaḥ darśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

paramāṇūnāṃ pārthivatyena rasavat-tva-yogāt catur-vidhānāṃ rūpa-rasa-gandha-sparśa-vattva-abhyupagamāt abhinna-nityatva-sūkṣmatva-vināśa-vyavahāra-ādi-viparyayeṇa anityatva-sthūlatva-sāvayavatva-ādi prasajyate, rūpādi-mattvāṃ ghaṭa-ādīnām anityatva-adhyadhikaraṇa-antara-ārabdhatva-ādīḥ darśanāt \| na hi dṛṣṭa-anugame nādoṣaḥ kalpyamānaḥ sva-abhimata-viśeṣe vyavasthāpayituṃ śakyaḥ \| dṛṣṭa-anugupyet hi paramāṇūnāṃ rūpādi-mattvaṃ tvayā kalpyate \| ataḥ api asamañjasam \|\| 14 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.15 \|\|

*ubhayathā ca doṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

na kevalaṃ paramāṇūnāṃ rūpādi-mattva-abhyupagame eva doṣaḥ; rūpādi-virahe api kāraṇa-guṇa-pūrvakatvāt kārya-guṇānāṃ pṛthivī-ādayaḥ rūpādi-śūnyāḥ syuḥ \| tat-parijihīrṣayā rūpādi-mattva-abhyupagame bhūyān doṣaḥ iti ubhayathā ca doṣāt asamañjasam \|\| 15 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.16 \|\|

*aparigrahāt ca atyantam anapekṣā*

**Комментарий:**

kapila-pakṣasya śruti-nyāya-virodha-parihṛtasya api sat-kāryatva-ādīnāṃ kaiścit aṃśaḥ vaidikaiḥ parigṛhītaḥ asti, asya tu kāṇāda-pakṣasya kena api aṃśena aparigrahāt upapannavāt ca atyantam anapekṣā eva niḥśreyasa-arthibhiḥ kāryā \|\| 16 \|\|

iti mahad-dīrgha-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha samudāya-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.2.17 \|\|

*samudāye ubhaya-hetuke api tat-aprāptiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

paramāṇu-kāraṇa-vādinaḥ vaiśeṣikāḥ nirastāḥ; saugatāḥ ca jagataḥ paramāṇu-kāraṇatvam abhyupagacchanti iti anantaraṃ tat-mate api jagat-utpatti-sad-bhāva-hārddikaṃ na upapadyata iti ucyate \| te catur-vidhāḥ—kecit māyātv-ajñāna-vyatireka-paramāṇu-mūhātmakaṃ bhūtam bhautikaṃ ca bāhyam aicchan vaijānanāntara-arthān pratyakṣa-anumāna-siddhān abhyupayanti; anye tu bāhya-arthān sarvān pṛthivī-ādi-jñāna-anumeyān vadanti; apare svayaṃ-sūryaṃ vijñānam eva paramārtha-sat, bāhya-arthaḥ tu svātana-artha-kalpaḥ iti āhuḥ; caturthe mate svātma-prabhāṃ vastu kṣaṇikam icchate, ukta-sva-sādhika-citta-caitta-vyatiriktam ātmānam īśa-ādikaṃ svargeṣu eva na abhyupaiti; anye tu sarva-śūnyatvam eva saṃgirante \| tatra ye bāhya-artha-astitva-vādinaḥ; te na abhyupagamyante \| te ca evaṃ manyante—rūpa-rasa-sparśa-gandha-svabhāvāḥ pārthivāḥ paramāṇavaḥ; rūpa-rasa-sparśa-svabhāvāḥ āpyāḥ; rūpa-sparśa-svabhāvāḥ ca taijasāḥ; sparśa-svabhāvāḥ ca vāyavīyāḥ pṛthivī-ādau jāyamāneṣu saṃhatyante, taiḥ ca pṛthivī-ādi-mayaḥ śarīra-indriya-viṣaya-rūpa-samūhānāṃ bhavanti; tatra ca śarīra-indriye prāṇa-karma-nimitta-rūḍhaḥ vijñāna-santānaḥ eva ātma-dṛṣṭena abhigṛhītaḥ; tataḥ eva sarvaḥ laukikaḥ vyavahāraḥ pravartate iti \|\|

tatra abhidhīyate—samudāye ubhaya-hetuke api tat-aprāptiḥ \| yaḥ ayam apūrvekaḥ pṛthivī-ādi-bhūta-ātmakaḥ samudāyaḥ, yaḥ ca pṛthivī-ādi-saṃhataka-kārair indriya-viṣaya-rūpa-samudāyaḥ; tasmin ubhaya-hetuke api samudāye tat asmin na upapadyate—jagat-ātmakaḥ samudāyaḥ api cit na upapadyate iti arthaḥ \| paramāṇūnāṃ pṛthivī-ādi-bhūta-gataṃ ca kṣaṇikatva-abhyupagamāt, kṣaṇavattaḥ paramāṇavaḥ bhūtāni ca kadā saṃhatāni vyāpriyante? kadā vā saṃhatyante? kadā vijñāna-viṣaya-bhūtāḥ? kadā ca hāna-upādāna-viṣaya-vyavahāra-āspadatāṃ bhajante? kaḥ vā vijñāna-ātmā kam ca viṣayaṃ īrayati? kasya vijñāna-ākāraṃ kadā vedyate? kam vā vividita-rūpaṃ kasya hāna-upādāne? kṣaṇaḥ hi naḥ; spaṣṭaḥ ca naḥ; tathā veditā viditaḥ ca naṣṭaḥ; kasya ca ayam spaṣṭam anyaḥ vedyate? kasya ca anyena viditam bhi anyaḥ ucyate? santānānāgata-katve api santata-abhyupetenaṃ vastutaḥ vastu-antaratvāt anubhava-mātra-tiryambandhena vyavahāraḥ iti upapadyate \| ahaṃ pūrvaṃ ajñāsam, tadvat śāstre api ca upapāditam purastāt \|\| 17 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.18 \|\|

*itaretara-pratyayatvāt upapattiḥ iti cet na saṃghāta-bhāva-animittatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

avidyā-ādīnām itaretara-hetutvena upapakṣaṃ saṃghāta-bhāvāt iti cet \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—yathā kṣaṇikāt sarvaṃ bhāvaḥ, tathā api avidyā-ādeḥ santateḥ upapadyate \| avidyā hi nāma viparyānuviddhiḥ kṣaṇikam tu sthiratva-ādi-yoga-carā \| tathā saṃskārāḥ syā rāga-ādi-baddhā jāyante; tataḥ vijñānam

jvalana-rūpaṃ vijñānam; tat tac ca nāma-ākhyāti-saṃjñena eva; tataś ca pṛthivī-ādikaṃ ca rūpi-dravyam; tad-anusthānānālayābhinirvṛttakam; tataḥ sparśa-ākhyaḥ kāyaḥ; tato vedanā-ādayaḥ tataś ca punar upa-vidyā-ādayo yathettham iti anādi iyam avidyā-ādikā adho ’dhomukhā cakravat pravṛttiḥ \| etac ca sarvaṃ pṛthivī-ādi-bhūta-bhautika-saṃghātam antareṇa na upapadyate \| antara-saṃghāta-bhāva-ādikam upapāditam iti \| tatra antareṇa saṃghātam apavarga-nimittam abhihitam \| na etad upapadyate, eṣām avidyā-ādīnāṃ pṛthivī-ādi-bhūta-bhautika-saṃghāta-bhāve pratyayimittatvāt \| na khalu asthiratva-ādīṣu sthira-svabhāva-buddhi-ādikā avidyā tasminn itā rāga-dveṣa-ādayo bādhyante tasya kṣaṇikatvasya saṃhati-hetutvaṃ pratipadyante \| mṛttikārakṣatṛvuddhir hi na mṛt-kārya-artha-saṃhati-hetur bhavati \| kiñca yasya kṣaṇike sthira-sva-buddhiḥ; sa tad eva naṣṭa iti kathaṃ rāga-ādi utpadyate? saṃskāra-āśraye sthirasmin dravyam anubhūya paścāt taṃ saṃskāra-anuvṛttir api na śakyā kalpayitum \|\|15\|\|

## \|\| 2.2.19 \|\|

*uttara-utpāde ca pūrva-nirodhāt*

**Комментарий:**

itas ca kṣaṇikatva-pakṣe jagad-utpattir na upapadyate; uttara-kṣaṇa-utpatti-velāyāṃ pūrva-kṣaṇasya vinaṣṭatvāt, tadyoḥ uttara-kṣaṇaṃ prati hetutvam upapannam \| abhāvaś ca hetutve sarvaṃ sarvatra sarvadā utpadyeta; atha pūrva-kṣaṇa-antimakena hetutvam iṣyate, evaṃ tarhi kaścid eva ghaṭa-kṣaṇas tad-uttara-kāla-sthāvināṃ sarveṣām eva go-mahiṣa-aśva-kuśa-cūṣā-pāṇa-ghaṭānāṃ trailokya-vartināṃ hetuḥ syāt \| atha eka-jātīya eva pūrva-kṣaṇa-vartino hetutvam iṣyate, tathāpi sarva-deśa-vartimān uttara-kṣaṇa-bhāvināṃ ghaṭānām eka eva pūrva-kṣaṇa-vartti-ghaṭo hetuḥ syāt \| atha eka eva hetur eka iti manye, tathāpi kasya ekasya ko hetur iti na jñāyate \| atha yasmin deśe yo ghaṭa-kṣaṇaḥ sthitaḥ; tad-deśa-saṃbandhita eva uttara-ghaṭa-kṣaṇasya sa hetur iti, kiṃ deśasya sthiratvaṃ manye? kiñca cakṣus-ādi-saṃprayuktasya arthasya jñāna-utpatti-kālo bhava-sthiti-tva-mātraṃ kalpayitavyasya jñāna-viṣayatvaṃ saṃbhavati \|\|16\|\|

## \|\| 2.2.20 \|\|

*asati pratijñā-uparodho yaugapadyam anyathā*

**Комментарий:**

asaty api hetau kāryam utpadyate cet; sarvaṃ sarvatra sarvadā utpadyeta \| na kevalam utpatti-virodha eva; pratijñā ca bhavatām upahanyeta, adhipati-sahakāri-ālambana-samanantara-pratyayāś catvāro vijñāna-utpattau hetava iti vaḥ pratijñā; adhipatiḥ indriyam \| atha pratijñā-uparodhāya ghaṭa-kṣaṇe sthita eva ghaṭa-kṣaṇāntara-utpattir iṣyate, tathā ca sati dvayoḥ kārya-kāraṇayor ghaṭa-kṣaṇayor yugapad dvayor upalabdhiḥ prasajyeta, na eva tathā upalabhyate; kṣaṇikatva-pratijñā ca evaṃ hīyeta \| kṣaṇikatvaṃ sthiratvam eva iti cet, indriya-saṃprayoga-jñāna-yaugapadyam prapadyeta \|\|20\|\|

## \|\| 2.2.21 \|\|

*pratisaṃkhyā-apratisaṃkhyā-nirodha-aprāptir avicchedāt*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ tāvad asata utpattir nirastā; sataḥ niravayava-vināśo ’pi na upapadyata iti ucyate \|

kṣaṇikatva-vādi-nirmita-dugdha-abhidhātādi-anantara-sambhāvite yaḥ abhyupeta-cchadma-upanyasa-sahakāra-santāna-antara-svarūpaḥ sthūlaḥ yaḥ, sahasāntatane prati-kṣaṇa-bhāvī ca upalabhyamānaḥ sūkṣmaḥ ca yaḥ niravayavaḥ vināśaḥ pratisaṃkhyā-nirodha-apratisaṃkhyā-nirodha-śabdābhyām abhidhīyete, tau na sambhavata iti arthaḥ \| kutaḥ ? avicchedāt—sataḥ niravayava-viccheda-asaṃbhavāt \| asaṃbhavaḥ ca, sataḥ utpatti-vināśau nāma avasthā-antara-āpattiḥ eva; avasthā-yoginaḥ tu dravyam ekam eva sthiram iti kāraṇatanyatvaṃ kāryasya upapādayadbhiḥ er asmābhiḥ śaiva-tan-mata-vimintram pratipāditaḥ \| niravayavasya dīpasya niravayava-vināśa-darśane tasya api vināśaḥ niravayavaḥ anubhūyata iti cet, ghaṭa-śarāva-ādyau mṛt-ādi-avasthā-ananyatva-palabdhyā sataḥ dravyasya avasthā-antara-āpattiḥ eva vināśaḥ iti niścite sati pradīpa-ādeḥ sūkṣma-sāra-api asya api upalambha-yogyataḥ tatra api avasthā-antara-āpatti-kalpanam eva yuktatvāt \|\|21\|\|

## \|\| 2.2.22 \|\|

*ubhayathā ca doṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

kṣaṇikatva-vādi-nirabhyupetā tucchād urpattiḥ utpannasya tucchatā-āpattiḥ ca na saṃbhavati—yuktam; tad-abhyupaprakāra-abhyupagataḥ doṣaḥ ca bhavati \| tucchād urpatto tucchātmakam eva kāryaṃ syāt; yadi paramāṇuḥ ucyate, tat-sad-ātmakam dṛṣṭaṃ; yathā mṛt-suvarṇa-ādi-kāryaṃ maṇi-makuṭa-ādi mṛt-suvarṇa-ādy-ātmakam dṛṣṭaṃ; na ca jagat-tucchātmakam bhavadbhiḥ abhyupagamyate; na ca pratīyate \| sataḥ niravayava-vināśaḥ sati eka-kṣaṇāt ūrdhvaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ tucchatā-āpattiḥ eva syāt; paścāt tucchāt jagad-utpatto anantara-ukta-tucchātmakatvam eva syāt \| ataḥ ubhayathā api doṣāḥ bhavad-ukta-prakāra-abhyupagati-nirodhaḥ \|\|22\|\|

## \|\| 2.2.23 \|\|

*ākāśe ca aviśeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

bāhya-abhyantara-vastuṣu sthiratva-vipratipattaye pratisaṃkhyā-apratisaṃkhyā-nirodhyetu tuccha-svarūpatā nirākṛtā \| tat-saṃbandhena tābhyāṃ saha tucchatvena śūnyataḥ pariṇatasya ākāśasya api tucchatā pratiṣidhyate \| ākāśe ca nirupākhyatā na yuktā, bhāva-rūpatvena abhyupagamāt pṛthivī-ādi-vat ākāśasya api avabodhitatvena \| na ca pṛthivī-ādeḥ bhāva-mātram ākāśa iti vaktuṃ śakyam, vikalpa-asahatvāt \| pṛthivī-ādiḥ prāk-bhāvaḥ; pradhvaṃsa-abhāvaḥ; itaretara-abhāvaḥ; atyanta-abhāvaḥ vā ākāśaḥ ? sarvathā api ākāśatva-etad anupapattiḥ tathāt \| prāk-bhāva-pradhvaṃsa-abhāvayoḥ ākāśatve pṛthivī-ādi-vastu vartamāneṣu ākāśa-pratīteḥ ayogāt nirākāśaṃ jagat syāt \| itaretara-abhāvatvam ākāśatve api itaretara-abhāvasya tantu-sugatatvena deśa-antarāle ākāśa-pratītiḥ na syāt \| atyanta-abhāvaḥ tu pṛthivī-ādīnāṃ na saṃbhavati \| abhāvasya vidyamāna-padārtha-viśeṣa-doṣa-pādanābhāvaḥ ca ākāśasya abhāva-rūpatve api na nirupākhyatvam \| avacchinna-vṛttīnāṃ ākāśasya cidrūkaraṇa-upadeśa-bheditāt pakṣīkareṇa rūpavat-tābhiyukta-deśa-pratirodhaḥ \|\|23\|\|

## \|\| 2.2.24 \|\|

*anumiteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

pūrvatra asmṛtaṃ bahuṣuḥ sthiratvam eva upapāditam \| anusaraṇe—pūrva-niṣpratipanna-vastu-viṣayaṃ jñānaṃ pratyabhijñānam iti arthaḥ \| tat eva idam iti sarvaṃ vastu-jātam atīta-kālavat bhūtaṃ pratyabhijñāyate \| na ca bhavad-dṛṣṭānta-āliṅgitve eva sādṛśya-nibandhā pratyabhijñā-avamohaḥ iti vaktuṃ śakyam; vyāghṛtya u na anūpekṣyānām anupapatteḥ \| na hi anubhūtena eva sādṛśyaṃ vā sthāṇu-bhūtasya nyāyya-upapadyate \| ataḥ bhikṣu-kalpita-vastu-āśraya-sādṛśya-āśraya-anibandhanam eka-vastu-avamohaṃ yat dṛṣṭāntena eva sthāpayitum aśakyam \| tatra lokeṣu api ghaṭa-ādiṣu jvālā-ādiṣu iva bheda-sādhaka-pramāṇa-prabalam āhe, yena sādṛśya-nibandhanāṃ pratyabhijñāṃ kalpayema \| yat api vaitathyam iti etat ghaṭa-ādeḥ kṣaṇikatvaṃ sidhyati \| pratyakṣaṃ tāvat asthi-mātra-viṣayam āvartamāna-anusandhānāt vyāptuṃ sthūla-viṣayam avagamyate, nīlatvāt pītāvat \| evaṃ ca bhūta-viṣaya-sūtraṃ varṇa-mātrasya vastu-santaratva-vattāṃ bhavati \|\|

anumānam api—artha-kriyā-kāritva-rūpaṃ sattvaṃ ca ghaṭa-ādiḥ kṣaṇikaḥ, yat akṣaṇikaṃ śaśa-śṛṅga-ādi, tat artha-kriyā-kāryaṃ na \| tathā akṣaya-deśa-kṣaṇatvāt aṇubhyaḥ kṣaṇa-sambandhāni vināśīni, ghaṭa-kṣaṇatvāt, anta-kṣaya-deśa-kṣaṇa-saṃbandhavat iti \| rakṣaḥ krāvya-kāraṇa-bhāva-anupapattyā vidhi-pūrvaṃ ca eva nirastam \|\|

kiñca pratyakṣam agrā vartamānasya vartamānatva-bodhāt bhinnaṃ vartamānasya vastu-sattvam avagamyati; atītaṃ vartamāna-kāla-yogitā-mātram \| na ca tāvatā vastu-santaratvaṃ sidhyati, tarhi ca kālāntara-yoga-saṃbhavāt \| yat u sattva-artha-kriyā-kāritvāt cet hi kṣaṇikatve hetum uktam, tat asminn eva viṣaye saṃbaddhana-sādhitukam \| sattva-artha-kriyā-kāritvatā ghaṭa-ādiḥ sthāsnuḥ, yat asthāsnu tat asat artha-kriyā-kāri ca, yathā śaśa-viṣāṇam iti api hi vaktuṃ śakyam \| kiñca artha-kriyā-kāritvam eka-kṣaṇikatvena eva sādhyate \| kṣaṇa-vyatireke hi vyāpāra-asaṃbhavāt artha-kriyā-kāritvaṃ na saṃbhavati iti uktam \| tathā asti aghaṭa-kṣaṇasya hetutā na indraśana-ādi-itareṣu api ghaṭa-kṣaṇā hetv-apekṣā-vināśāsti asya iti atha mudgara-ādinā hetutva-vināśānāṃ sthāsnutvena eva \| tatra vāryaṃ na mudgara-ādayaḥ vināśa-hetavaḥ, api tu kapāla-ādi-saṃśleṣa-saṃtāna-uddhūnana-hetavaḥ iti; kapālatva-vyavasthāpitasya eva ghaṭa-śreṇī vināśaḥ iti upacariyate \| kapāla-ādi-niṣpatty-atiriktatvāt mudgare api vināśasya vināśa-hetutvam eva mudgara-ādeḥ aṅgīkṛtaṃ hetutaram \| ataḥ pratyabhijñā sthirattvam-bakāra-mānaṃ na kena api prakāreṇa apānetuṃ śakyam \| pūrvapakṣa-kāla-sambandha-vyavasthiti-viṣaya-yogaḥ pratyabhijñāyā artha-viṣayatvaṃ ca vakṣyate \| tasmāt siddhāntara-viṣayatvaṃ draṣṭavyam \| kiñca pramāṇam anupayogaḥ kṣaṇikatvaṃ vada-dṛṣṭāntitva-dhāraṇāt smaraṇa-pūrvaka-anumāna-āśrayatvam api duḥśakam \| tathā idaṃ kṣaṇika-nirākṛtiḥ vistṛtā pūrva-

hetvanāsādhikaṃ karma api na upapadyate bhavatām, pratijñā-upakrama-kṣaṇa eva brahma-vicchedāt; na hi etan na upakrāntam ajānān nirūpya etat samāpayituṃ śakyam \|\|24\|\|

## \|\| 2.2.25 \|\|

*na asataḥ adṛṣṭatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ tāvat bhāṣika-bodha-anvita-kṣaṇa-bhaṅgyā dṛṣṭatva-vādinaḥ asādhāraṇāni dūṣaṇāni uktāni \| tatra yat uktaṃ saṃyuktasya arthasya jñānatva-sikatā-leśavat avasthitatvāt atra kṣaṇa-vicchedasya jñāna-viṣayatvaṃ saṃbhavati iti; tatra sautram-antikaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—na jñāna-kalevara-avasthānam arthasya jñāna-viṣayatva-hetuḥ; jñāna-utpatti-hetutvam eva hi jñāna-viṣayatvam \| na ca etāvatā cakṣur-ādeḥ jñāna-viṣayatva-prasaṅgaḥ; sva-ākāra-samarpaṇena jñāna-hetoḥ eva jñāna-viṣayatvābhyupagamāt \| jñāne sva-ākāraṃ samarpya vinaṣṭaḥ api arthaḥ jñānena nīlākārīṇā anubhūyate \| na ca pūrva-pūrva-jñānena uttarottara-jñāna-ākāra-siddhiḥ; nīla-jñāna-santato pīta-jñāna-anupapatti-sthira-sattvāt \| ataḥ arthasya eva jñāne vaicitryam arpiti \|\| ajñocyate—na asataḥ adṛṣṭatvāt iti \| yādṛśaṃ jñāne nīlādi-vidha-ākāram upalakṣyate, sa vinaṣṭasya asataḥ arthasya ākāraḥ bhavituṃ na arhati \| kutaḥ? adṛṣṭatvāt—na khalu dharmiṇi vinaṣṭe tat-dharmasya artha-antare saṅkramaṇaṃ dṛṣṭam \| pratibimba-vādikam api sthirasya eva bhavati \| tatra api na dharma-mātrasya \| ataḥ artha-vaicitrya-kṛtaṃ jñāna-vaicitryam arthasya jñāna-kāle avasthānāt eva bhavati \|\|25\|\|

punar api sādhāraṇaṃ dūṣaṇam āha—

## \|\| 2.2.26 \|\|

*udāsīnānām api ca evaṃ siddhiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ kṣaṇikatva-asad-utpatti-ahetuka-vināśa-śūnyopāne udāsīnānām asmad-ādīnām api sarva-artha-siddhiḥ syāt \| iṣyamāṇa-siddhi-vidhuratvāt prayatnādi-visādhyate \| kṣaṇa-bhaṅge hi sarvaṃ bhāvānāṃ pūrva-pūrvaṃ vastu tadṛśato vā viśeṣaḥ saṃskāra-viśeṣo vidyādi-vary uttarasya na kiñcit upakurvata iti prayatnādi-sādhyaṃ na kiñcit asti \| evaṃ sati ahetu-sādhyatvāt sarva-siddhīnām udāsīnānām artha-nihitāmujjhita-kṛtaṃ mokṣaḥ ca sidhyec ca \|\|26\|\| iti samudāya-adhikaraṇam \|\|3\|\|

**atha upalabdhy-adhikaraṇam \|\|4\|\|**

## \|\| 2.2.27 \|\|

*na abhāvaḥ upalabdheḥ*

**Комментарий:**

vijñāna-mātra-astitva-vādinaḥ yogācārāḥ pratyavatiṣṭhante—yuktam artha-vaicitrya-kṛtaṃ jñāna-vaicitryam iti; tathā api ucyate, arthavat jñāna-mātram eva sākārāṇāṃ svayam eva vicitravat \| tat ca svarūpa-vaicitryaṃ vāsanā-vaśāt eva upapadyate \| vāsanā ca vilakṣaṇa-pratyaya-pravāha eva \| yat ākāraṃ jñānaṃ kapālālākāra-asahāya-utpadakam, tasya tathā-vidhasya utpādakaṃ tat-pūrva-ghaṭa-jñānam; tasya ca

tathā-vidha-yogya-pādakaṃ tataḥ pūrva-vat ajñānam iti evaṃ-rūpaḥ pravāhaḥ eva vāsanā iti ucyate \| kathaṃ bahiḥ-saṃbandhaḥ adhīyate? iti uktam—artha-stha api vyavahāra-yogyatvaṃ jñāna-prakāśyatvam, anyathā sva-para-vedyayoḥ aviśeṣa-prasaṅgāt \| prakāśamānatvaṃ jñānasya sa-ākāratvam avasthā-viśeṣaḥ, nir-ākārasya prakāśa-ayogāt \| ekasyām ākāraḥ upalabdhamānaḥ jñānasya eva \| tasya ca bahiḥ-vedya-bhāvaḥ api bhrama-kṛtaḥ \| jñāna-arthaḥ asaha-upalambha-niyamāt ca jñānāt avyatiriktaḥ arthaḥ \| kiñ ca bāhya-arthasya sphuṭa-viduḥ api ghaṭa-paṭa-ādi-vijñāneṣu jñānasya tat-tat-artha-sādhāraṇaṃ tat-tat-artha-sāhityam anusareṇa na upapadyate iti avaśyaṃ jñāneṣu artha-rūpaṃ rūpam āstheyam \| tāvatā eva bāhya-vedya-vyavahāra-upapattiḥ; tat-atirikta-āśaṅkanā mithyā-mātrikā ato vijñāna-mātram eva tattvam, na bāhyaḥ arthaḥ asti iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—na abhāvaḥ upalabdheḥ iti \| jñāna-atiriktasya arthasya abhāvaḥ vaktuṃ na śakyate; kutaḥ? upalabdheḥ—jñātuḥ ātmanaḥ artha-viśeṣa-vyavahāra-yogyatā-pādana-rūpeṇa jñānasya yoga-labdheḥ \| evam eva hi sarve laukikāḥ pratīyanti ghaṭam ahaṃ jānāmi iti \| evaṃ rūpeṇa samakṣaṃ sakṛt eva jñātatva-arthaṃ sarva-loka-sākṣikam aparīkṣam avabhāsamānam eva jñānamātram eva paramārthaḥ iti sādhyamānasya vaitoṣika-upahāsa-āspada-karaṇaṃ bhavanti iti veda-vāda-sacchadma-pracchanna-bauddha-nirākaraṇe nipuṇataraṃ prapañcitam \| yat tu saha-upalambha-niyamāt abhedaḥ nīla-tad-dhiyoḥ iti, tat tu sva-vacana-viruddham, sāhityam anya-apekṣatva-hetukatvāt \| tad-arthaṃ vyavahāra-yogyatā-kara-rūpasya jñānasya tena saha-upalambha-niyamaḥ tasya tat-eka-lakṣya-saṃbandham iti ca hāsyam \| niratyaya-vināśiṣu jñānānām anuvartamāna-sthira-ākāra-viniṣṭhāhāsanā ca duṣprapādā \| vināśena pūrva-kṣaṇena anuvṛtta-smṛta-saṃjñāṃ vādhyate? ato jñāna-indriya-grāhya-prathy-artha-viṣaya-kṛtam eva \| tat-artha-vedya-vyavahāra-upeta-tattā-pādana-rūpatayā sākṣāt-kartṛ-mānasya jñānasya tat-sad-artha-saṃbandhitvaṃ tat-sad-ādhāratvam \| saṃvedyasya saṃyoga-lakṣaṇaḥ \| jñānam api hi dravyam eva \| prabhāvāt asya pradeśopama-bhūtatvaṃ jñānasthāpatya-ātmanubhūtasya dravyatvam aviśiṣṭam \| ato na bāhya-artha-abhāvaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

yat-paraḥ: svapna-jñāna-dṛṣṭena jāgarita-jñānānām api nirālambanatvam vaktum; tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.2.28 \|\|

*vaidharmyāt ca na svapna-ādi-vat*

**Комментарий:**

svapna-jñāna-vaidharmyāt jāgarita-jñānānām artha-śūnyatvaṃ na yujyate vaktum \| svapna-jñānāni hi nidrā-ādi-doṣa-upahṛta-karaṇa-janyāni, bādhitāni ca; jāgarita-jñānāni tu tat-viparītāni iti teṣāṃ na tat-sāmyam \| sarveṣāṃ ca jñānānām artha-śūnyatve bhavadṛṣṭa-sādhya-upādhi-arthaṃ na sidhyati, nirālambanatva-anumānasya tu artha-śūnyatvāt \| tasya arthaḥ ca etat jñānatva-mātra-ekāntyāt sutārām artha-śūnyatvā-siddhiḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

**dvitīya-adhyāye—dvitīya-pāde—sarvathā-anupapatti-adhikaraṇam \|**

## \|\| 2.2.29 \|\|

*na bhāvaḥ anupalabdheḥ*

**Комментарий:**

na kevala-sthāyi-īśvarasya jñānasya bhāva-saṃbhavati; kutaḥ ? kvacid api upalabdheḥ \| na hi antya-kṣaṇa-mātrasya vā jñānasya kvacid upalabdhiḥ \| svapna-jñāna-ādiṣu api na artha-śūnyatvam iti sthāpiti-nirūpaṇe pratipāditam \|\| 29 \|\| iti upalabdhi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha sarvathā-anupapatti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.2.30 \|\|

*sarvathā-anupapatteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

atra sarva-śūnya-vāde mādhyamikaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate \| śūnya-vādaḥ eva hi sugata-mata-kāṣṭhā \| śiṣya-buddhi-yogyatā-anuguṇyena arthi-bhava-bhinnāḥ kṣaṇikatvādayaḥ uktāḥ \| vijñāne bāhya-artha-āśraye sarve na santi, śūnyam eva tattvam; abhāva-upaniś ca eva ca mokṣaḥ iti eva buddhasya abhiprāyaḥ \| tat eva hi yuktam, śūnya-svabhāva-hetutā-adhyayatā svataḥ-siddheḥ \| sat eva hi hetutvam evaṇīyam \| tat ca sat, māhātma-bhāvāt ca na utpadyate; bhāva-ātmātram kvacid utpattiḥ dṛṣṭā; na hi ghaṭa-ādiḥ utpadyate piṇḍa-ādike jāyate \| na api abhāvāt utpatti-saṃbhavati, naṣṭe piṇḍa-ādike hi abhāvāt utpadyamānaṃ ghaṭa-ādikam abhāvātmakam eva syāt \| tathā svataḥ parataḥ ca utpattiḥ na saṃbhavati \| svataḥ sva-utpattau ātma-saṃśraya-doṣa-prasaṅgāt, prayojana-abhāvāt ca \| parataḥ para-utpattau paratva-aviśeṣāt sarveṣāṃ sarvathā utpatti-prasaṅgaḥ \| janma-abhāvāt eva vināśasya api abhāvaḥ \| ataḥ śūnyam eva tattvam \| ataḥ janma-vināśa-sad-bhāvayoḥ bhrānti-mātram \| na ca niradhiṣṭhāna-bhrama-saṃbhavāt bhrama-ādhiṣṭhānaṃ kiñcid vāstāvikaṃ tattvam āśritavyam, doṣataḥ api artha-vattvāt tucchatva-vyavahāra-sambandhe api bhrama-upapatteḥ niradhiṣṭhānatayā api saṃbhavāt \| ataḥ śūnyam eva tattvam \|\|
