iti prāpte ucyate—sarvathā-anupapatteḥ ca iti \| sarva-śūnya-pavatre sarveṣāṃ śūnyatvaṃ ca bhavadbhiḥ bruvāṇaiḥ na saṃbhavati \| kiṃ bhavān sarvaṃ sahitam vā pratijānīte, asahitam vā, anyathā vā ? sarveṣāṃ tattva-bhedāt tucchatvaṃ na saṃbhavati; loke bhāva-abhāva-śabdayoḥ tat-sattva-atiriktayoḥ ca vidyamānatvaṃ vastuno dvayasya viśeṣayoḥ ca etat-svasya pratipāditatvāt \| ataḥ sarvaṃ śūnyam iti pratijānānā sarvaṃ sat iti pratijānate eva sarvasya vidyamānatvāt avasthā-viśeṣa-upahitā eva pratijñātā bhavati iti bhavat-abhimatā tucchatā na kutraścid api sidhyati \| kiñca kutaścit pramāṇāt śūnyatvam upalabhya śūnyatvaṃ siṣādhayiṣasā tasya pramāṇasya sat-asattvam abhyupetavyam; tathā sati eva sarvaṃ satyaṃ sthāpitaṃ sarveṣāṃ sarva-śūnyatvaṃ ca anupapannam \|\| 30 \|\| iti sarvathā-anupapatti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

**atha ekasmin asaṃbhava-adhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|**

## \|\| 2.2.31 \|\|

*na ekasmin asaṃbhavāt*

**Комментарий:**

nirastāḥ tīrthikāḥ \| jaināḥ api paramāṇu-kāraṇa-vādikaṃ jagataḥ vadantaḥ ayam antaraṃ jaina-pakṣaḥ pratiṣidhyate \| te kila manyante—jīva-ajīva-ātmakaṃ jagat aśeṣam; tat ca brahmady-ātmakam \| tāni ca dravyāṇi jīva-dharma-adharma-pudgala-kāla-ākāśa-ākhyāni \| tatra jīvāḥ—baddhāḥ, yoga-siddhāḥ, muktāḥ ca iti tri-vidhāḥ \| dharmaḥ nāma gati-matāṃ gati-hetu-bhūtaḥ dravya-viśeṣaḥ jagad-vyāpī \| adharmaḥ api sthiti-nibandhanaḥ vyāpī \| pudgalaḥ nāma varṇa-gandha-rasa-sparśa-vattva-bṛddhatvam \| tat ca dvi-vidham—paramāṇu-rūpaṃ, tata-saṅghāta-rūpaṃ ca pavana-jvalana-salila-dharaṇī-tanu-bhavana-ādiṣu \| kālaḥ tu abhūtatad-bhaviṣyat iti vyavahāra-hetuḥ aṇu-rūpaḥ dravya-viśeṣaḥ \| ākāśauḍyakoḍa-nanta-pradeśaḥ ca \| teṣu ca aṇuḍy-atirikta-dravyāṇi pañca-astikāya iti ca saṃgṛhyante—jīva-astikāyaḥ; dharma-astikāyaḥ, adharma-astikāyaḥ; pudgala-astikāyaḥ; ākāśa-astikāyaḥ—iti \| aneka-deśa-varttīni dravyāṇi astikāya-śabdaiḥ prayujyante \|\|

jīvānāṃ mokṣa-upayoginam aparam api saṃgrahaṃ kurvanti—jīva-ajīva-āsrava-bandha-nirjarā-saṃvara-mokṣāḥ iti \| mokṣa-sādhyena mokṣa-upāyaḥ ca gṛhītaḥ \| sa ca samyag-jñāna-darśana-caritrasyaḥ \| tatra jīvaḥ tu jñāna-darśana-sukha-vīrya-guṇaḥ \| ajīvaḥ ca jīva-bhogya-bhūta-jātam \| āsravaḥ tat-tat-yogya-karaṇa-bhūta-anirjīyādikaḥ ca \| bandhaḥ ca avidyā—dhārmikaṃ catur-mukham, adhārmikaṃ catur-mukham iti \| tathā arthaṃ jīva-guṇānāṃ svābhāvikānāṃ jñāna-darśana-vīrya-sukhānāṃ pratighāta-karam \| aparaṃ śarīra-saṃsthāna-tad-vimānatā-sthiti-tat-prakṛta-sukha-dduḥkha-upeśā-hetu-bhūtam \| nirjaraṃ mokṣa-sādhanam ahetu-padeśa-avāptaṃ tapaḥ \| saṃvaraḥ nāma indriya-nirodhaḥ sama-dṛṣṭi-rūpaḥ \| mokṣaḥ tu nivṛtta-rāga-ādi-vakleśasya svābhāvika-ātma-svarūpa-vibhūtiḥ \| pṛthivī-ādi-hetu-bhūtāḥ ca aṇavaḥ vaiśeṣika-ādīnām iva na catur-vidhāḥ \| antike svabhāvāḥ \| pṛthivī-ādi-viśeṣaḥ tu pariṇāma-kṛtaḥ \|\|

sarvaṃ ca vastu-jātaṃ sad-asattva-nityatva-ādityatva-bhinnatva-abhinnatva-ādi-saṃkīrṇa-anekāntakam icchanti—syāt asti, syāt nāsti, syāt asti ca nāsti ca, syāt avaktavyam, syāt asti ca avaktavyaṃ ca, syāt nāsti ca avaktavyaṃ ca, syāt asti ca nāsti ca avaktavyaṃ ca iti sarvatra sapta-bhaṅgī-naya-avatāraḥ \| sarvaṃ vastu-jātaṃ kathaṃ paryāya-ātmakam iti dravya-ātmanā sattva-katva-nityatvāt vā dharmitvaṃ; paryāya-ātmanā ca tat viparītam \| paryāyāḥ ca dravyasya avasthā-viśeṣāḥ \| teṣāṃ ca bhāva-abhāva-rūpatvāt tat-saṃsarga-vādikaṃ sarvam upapannam iti \|\|

atra abhidhīyate—na ekasmin asaṃbhavāt iti \| na etat upapadyate; kutaḥ? ekasmin asaṃbhavāt—ekasmin avastuni astitva-nāstitva-ādi-aikāntikasya chāyā-tapavatv gapad asaṃbhavāt \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—

dravyasya tad-viśeṣaṇa-bhūta-pṛthivī-ādi-śabda-vidheya-avasthā-viśeṣasya ca pṛthag-bhāvaḥ cet tat na ekasmiṃś cid dharmi-samāveśa-saṃbhavataḥ api \| tathā hi—ekena astitva-ādinā avasthā-viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭasya tadānīm eva na tat-virodhena nāstitva-ādi-viśiṣṭatvaṃ saṃbhavati \| utpatti-vināśa-ākhya-pariṇāma-viśeṣa-āspadatvaṃ ca dravyasya anityatvam, tad-viparītaṃ ca nityatvaṃ tasmin kathaṃ samaveti iti? virodhi-dharma-āśrayatvaṃ ca bhinnatvam, tad-viparītaṃ ca abhinnatvaṃ kathaṃ vā tasmin samaveti iti? yathā-śravam ahi-guṇa-nyāyena ekasmāt dvayaṃ bhavaḥ \| ayam arthaḥ pūrvam eva bhedābhedādy-anirvacanatā-samaye santu sāmastyādinā atra prapañcitāḥ \| kālasya padārtha-viśeṣaṇatva eva pratīyamānasya pṛthak astitva-nāstitva-ādayo na vaktavyāḥ; na ca parihartavyāḥ \| kālaḥ asti iti nāsti iti vyavahāraḥ vyavahṛtaṃ pāṃ jāti-ādi-astitva-nāstitva-vyavahāra-tulyaḥ \| jāti-ādayo hi dravya-viśeṣaṇatvena pratīyante iti pūrvam eva uktam \| kathaṃ punar etat eva brahma sarva-karma iti śrutiḥ ca ityate? sarva-cetana-acetana-rūpa-avāntara-cetanasya sarva-jñasya puruṣottamasya iti uktam \| śarīra-śarīriṇoḥ tat-dharmāṇāṃ śāstra-antare lakṣaṇam uktam, kiṃ ca jīvātanoḥ kṣaṇaṃ dravyaṇām eka-dravya-paryāyatva-lābhān na eṣu dravyatvena paryāyāsannā ca ekatva-anekatva-vādaḥ durupapādaḥ \| atha ucyeta—ghaṭa-ādīni dravyāṇi svakīyaiḥ paryāyaiḥ svena svena ca ātmanā tathā bhavanti iti \| evam api sarvam ekāntikatvam iti abhyupagama-virodhaḥ, anyonyatā-taratamya-abhāvāt \| ataḥ na yuktimat jaina-matam \| īśvara-asiddhi-hetu-paramāṇu-kāraṇa-vāde pūrvokta doṣāḥ tathā eva avatiṣṭhante \|\| 31 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.32 \|\|

*evaṃ ca ātma-akārtsnyam*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ bhavat-abhyupagame sati ātmanaḥ ca akārtsnyaṃ prasajyate \| jīvaḥ asaṅkhyāta-pradeśaḥ deha-parimāṇa iti hi bhavatāṃ sthitiḥ \| tatra hasta-ādi-śarīra-avasthitasya ātmanaḥ tataḥ nyūna-parimāṇe pipīlikā-ādi-śarīre praviśataḥ alpa-deśa-vyāpitvena eka-deśasya asaṃprasajyate—aparipūrṇatā prasajyate iti arthaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

atha sūkṣma-icchā-vikāsa-bhedena ātmanaḥ paryāyāt śakti-bhedya-avasthāntara-āpattiḥ virodhaḥ parihriyata iti ucyate; tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.2.33 \|\|

*na ca paryāyāt api avirodhaḥ vikāra-ādibhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

na ca sūkṣma-icchā-vikāsa-rūpa-avasthāntara-āpattau virodhaḥ parihartuṃ śakyate, vikāratvasya ukta-anityatva-ādi-doṣa-prasaṅgāt iti tulyatva-prasaṅgaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.34 \|\|

*antya-avasthiteḥ ca ubhaya-nityatvāt aviśeṣaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

jīvasya yat dehaṃ parimāṇaṃ mokṣa-avasthā-lakṣaṇam, tasya paścāt dehāntara-parigraha-abhāvād vardhiṣṇutvāvādāt manaḥ ca mokṣa-avasthasya tat-parimāṇasya ca ubhyoḥ nityatvāt ca ātmanaḥ svābhāvikaṃ

parimārtha-sthiti pūrvam api tasmād viśeṣaḥ syāt \| ato deha-parimāṇatvāt ātmanaḥ na syād iti asaṃgatam eva idam āha nanu \|\| 34 \|\| iti ekasminn asaṃbhava-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha paśupati-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.2.35 \|\|

*paśupatya-asāmañjasyāt*

**Комментарий:**

kapila-kaṇāda-sugata-ahita-matānām asāmañjasyena veda-bāhyatvāc ca nirasta-prasādhita-siddhānta-dṛḍhīkaraṇam uktam; idānīṃ paśupati-matasya api veda-virodhād asāmañjasyāc ca anādaraṇīyatā ucyate \| tatra tatra smaraṇa-āśruti-vidhāḥ—kapālāḥ kālamukhāḥ pāśupatāḥ śaivāḥ ca iti \| sarve ca ete veda-vihitaṃ tattva-āśrayikam aihika-āmuṣmika-nirśreyasa-prasādhana-karma-mātraṃ kalpayanti \| nimitta-upādānayoḥ dvandvaṃ, nirvikāratvaṃ ca paśupati-vādi-akṣate \| tathā nirśreyasa-prasādhanam api mudrikā-ṣaṭka-dhāraṇa-ādi-vikam \| yathā āhuḥ kapālāḥ—mudrikā-ṣaṭka-kṛta-tv-āsaḥ paramudrā-vicāradaḥ \| bhagāsana-stham ātmānaṃ dhyātvā nirvāṇam ṛcchati \|\| kaṇṭhikā rucakaṃ ca eva kuṇḍalaṃ ca śikhā-maṇiḥ \| bhasma yajñopavītaṃ ca mudrā-ṣaṭkaṃ pracakṣate \|\| abhimudita-dehaḥ tu na bhūyaḥ iha jāyate \|\| ity ādikam \| tathā kālāmukhāḥ api kapāla-pātra-abhojana-āśava-smasnāna-antara-śrī-kānala-gu-dhāraṇa-aduraṅkuśam asthāpana-tat-pādārtha-deva-pūjā-ādikaṃ mudrikā-muktikam akṣakala-phala-sādhanam abhidadhati \| dhyāta-kuṭa-kuṭīṃ haste jaṭā ca ekā ca mastake \| kapālaṃ bhasmanā snānam iti ādi ca prasiddhaṃ śaiva-āgameṣu \| tathā kaucidī-kriyā-viśeṣeṇa vijātīyānām api brāhmaṇya-kṣatriyatva-nābham abhīti ca āhuḥ—dīkṣā-praveśa-mātreṇa brāhmaṇaḥ bhavati kṣaṇāt \| kapālaṃ vratam āsthāya yadi jīvet iti mānavaḥ \|\| iti \|\|

tatra evaṃ ucyate—paśupatya-asāmañjasyād iti \| na ekasminn asaṃbhavad iti ataḥ na iti ucyate \| pratyuḥ—paśupateḥ; mataṃ na ādaraṇīyam; kutaḥ? asāmañjasyāt \| asāmañjasyam ca aneka-upapadyamānatāt veda-virodhāc ca \| mudrikā-ṣaṭka-dhāraṇa-bhasma-snānāsthāna-laya-śayana-suraṅkuśam asthāpana-tad-artha-devatā-arcanā-gūḍhācāra-samāna-bhava-sanānā-vratād apūrva-abhyudaya-aniṣṭayocaya-viśruti \| veda-vihiteṣu vaide-tantva-parikalpanam uktānām ācāraḥ ca \| vedaḥ khalu paraṃ brahma nārāyaṇam eva jagan-nimittam upādānaṃ ca bravīti—nārāyaṇaṃ paraṃ brahma tattvaṃ nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ \| nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ jyotiḥ ātmā nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ tad aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti saḥ akāmayata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tad ātmānaṃ svayam akuruta iti ādayaḥ \| parabrahma-bhūtāt puruṣa-vedanena eva ca mokṣa-sādhanam upavataṃ vadanti—vedāham etaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntam, āditya-varṇaṃ tamasas tu pāre... tam evaṃ vidvān amṛta iha bhavati, na anyaḥ panthā ayanāya vidyate iti ādinā \| ekāṃ gata-āsārthaṃ vedānāṃ \| tad-viviktaṃ dvayam abhūtaṃ karma ca veda-vihita-varṇāśrama-saṃbandhi prajā-ādikam eva vadanti—tam evaṃ veda-anuvacanena brāhmaṇā vividiṣanti yajñena

dānena tapaś ca anāśakena ... evam eva pravarjitaḥ loka-niṣṭhalatāḥ pravrajanti iti ādayaḥ \| kevala-paratattva-pratipādana-paratārṇāṇuvāka-siddhatva-carāḥ kaiścit upāsanārtha-vidhīyireṣu vākyepu śrutāḥ prajāpati-śvetakāśvataraprāṇādi-śabdāḥ iti āha brahmacaryaṃ tu am eva vāmatheva hṛdayatra pratiṣṭhitam \| tathā eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt na brahmā na īśānaḥ; iti āś ca sa ekākī na rameta iti dṛṣṭa-vākyoditaṃ sādhāraṃ nārāyaṇam eva samāntra-karaṇasthaḥ tat eva saumya idam iti-ādi-bhu sādharāṇaḥ sad-brahmātmādi-śabdaḥ pratipādyanta iti janmādy asya yataḥ iti atra pratipāditam \| ataḥ vaidika-vṛttanveṣaṇa-sanuguṇānāṃ vidhyānarūpa-guṇita-samanantara-goyam eva \|\| 35 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.36 \|\|

*adhiṣṭhānānupapatteś ca*

**Комментарий:**

veda-bāhyānām anumānādi kevalānicinīṣeṣa-kalpanā \| tathā sati hasta-turagāre kulāla-vivakṣādhānaṃ kartavyam; na ca kulālādyeṣu dāyādhiṣṭhānavat anupaveśini-smṛtasya pradhānādhiṣṭhānam upapadyate, aśarīratvāt; saśarīrāṇām eva hi kulālādīnām adhiṣṭhāna-śaktiḥ dṛṣṭā \| na ca aiśvarasya saśarīratvam upagantavyam; tac-charīrasya sādhavacasya niravaye anityatve ca śāstra-yonitvādi-tyatra doṣasya uktavat \|\| 36 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.37 \|\|

*karaṇavat cen na bhogādibhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

yathā bhūta-jīvasya karaṇa-kalevarādhiṣṭhānam aśarīrasya eva hṛdyate; tadvat maheśvarasya api aśarīrasya ca pradhānādhiṣṭhānam upapadyata iti cet; bhogādibhyaḥ—puṇya-pāpa-rūpa-karma-phala-bhogārthaṃ puṇya-pāpa-rūpāhaṅkāritaṃ hi tat-adhiṣṭhānam; tat-ananupateḥ api puṇya-pāpa-saṃbandhatayā tat-phala-bhogādi sarvaṃ prasajyeta; ataḥ na adhiṣṭhāna-saṃbhavaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.38 \|\|

*antavattvam asarvajñatā vā*

**Комментарий:**

vā-śabdaḥ ca arthe; paśupateḥ puṇya-pāpa-sahāyatve jīvavat antavattvaṃ sṛṣṭi-saṃhārādharmadarśana-masarvajñatā ca syāt iti avādaragohyam evaṃ matam \| virodhe tu anapekṣaṃ syāt iti-āditā veda-viruddhayānādaragoyatve siddhe’pi paśupati-matasya a-veda-viruddhatākhyāpanārthaṃ patyuḥ asāmañjasyāt iti punar-ārambhaḥ \| yady api pāśupatāḥ tu ayoveda-bāhirocita iva kaścana dharmaḥ pratīyate; tathā api veda-viruddha-nimittopādāna-bheda-kalpanā-parāśaratarhavyayoga-kalpanāśrīlatvāt sarvam asamañjam eva iti asaṃśraya iti uktam \|\| 38 \|\| iti paśupaty-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|

**atha utpatty-asaṃbhavādhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|**

## \|\| 2.2.39 \|\|

*utpatty-asaṃbhavāt*

**Комментарий:**

kapilādi-tantra-sāmānyād dravya-ādi-vihita-parama-niḥśreyasa-sādhanatvābodhini pañca-rātra-tantrābhyupānam asādhu iti nirākriyate \| tatra evam āśaṅkyate—parama-kāraṇatva-paraṃ brahma-bhūtāt tasudeva-saṃkarṣaṇo nāma jīvo jāyate, saṃkarṣaṇād yumnasaḥ mano jāyate, tasmād aniruddha-saṃjño 'haṃkāro jāyata iti hi bhāgavata-prakriyā \| atra jīvasya utpattiḥ śruti-viruddhā pratīyate; śrutayo hi jīvasya anāditvaṃ vadanti na jāyate mriyate vā vipaścit iti ādyāḥ \|\|39\|\|

## \|\| 2.2.40 \|\|

*na ca kartuḥ karaṇam*

**Комментарий:**

saṃkarṣaṇākhyāj junasaḥ mano jāyate iti kartuḥ jīvāt, karaṇasya manasa utpattiṃ saṃbhavati, etasmāj jāyate prāṇo manaḥ sarva-indriyāṇi ca iti paramātmā eva brahmaṇo manaso 'pi utpatti-śruteḥ \| ataḥ śruti-viruddha-grantha-pratipādanatvād api tantrasya aprāmāṇyaṃ pratibiddham iti \|\|40\|\|

**evaṃ prāpte prabrūmahe—**

## \|\| 2.2.41 \|\|

*vijñānādi-bhāve vā tad-apratiṣedhaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

vāsudeva-pakṣo viparivartate; vijñānaṃ ca asti ca iti para-brahma vijñānādi \| saṃkarṣaṇa-pradyumna-aniruddhānām api para-brahma-bhāve sati tat-pratipādana-parasya śāstrasya prāmāṇyaṃ na pratiṣidhyate \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—bhāgavata-prakriyābhijñātatm idaṃ codaṃ pañca-jīve-devatā-viruddhā api hi iti \| vāsudevākhyāṃ paraṃ brahma iti ca abhiśravat sattvaṃ svābhistrsamāśrayaṇoktyāya svecchayā caturdhā sthitam iti; hi tatra prakriyā \| yathā pauṣkara-saṃhitāyāṃ kṛtsnasya yena vai yajñaṃ cātur-ātmasya mūṣyate \| kramāt tair aśva-saṃjñābhiḥ brāhmaṇānāṃ tu tattva \|\| iti ādi \| tac ca cātur-ātmyopāsanaṃ vāsudevākhya-para-brahmopāsanam iti sārtha-saṃhitāyām uktaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ hi sarveṣāṃ vāsudevākhya yājinām \| viveka idaṃ paraṃ śāstraṃ brahmopaniṣadaṃ mahat \|\| iti \| yadi vāsudevākhyam paraṃ brahma saṃpūrṇa-ṣāḍguṇya-vapuḥ sūkṣma-vyūha-vibhava-bhedair miśraṃ yathādhikāraṃ bhaktaḥ jñāna-pūrvaṃ karmaṇābhyarcita samyag āpyate \| viśva-prācenaikyaṃ prāpya vyūhārcanena aparaṃ brahma vāsudevākhyam akṣaraṃ prāpyata iti vadanti \| viśvasya hi nāma rāma-kṛṣṇa-ādi-ādyavibhavāḥ \| vyūho vāsudeva-saṃkarṣaṇa-pradyumna-aniruddha-rūpa-vibhūhaḥ \| sūkṣmaṃ tu kevala-ṣāḍguṇya-vigrahaṃ vāsudevākhyam para-brahma \| yathā pauṣkare parasmāt satya-paraṃ brahma vāsudevākhyam avyayam \| asmād avāpte śāśvataṃ jñāna-pūrveṇa karmaṇā \|\| iti ādi \|

atas sāṅkhya-pāṭhīnāṃ api param eva brahmaṇaḥ sva-icchāti-vigraha-rūpatvāt ajāyamānaḥ bahudhā vijāyate iti śruti-siddhatvenādhigata-tattva-pratiniṣedhya-iccheti viśada-sukha-rūpa-janmanaḥ abhidhānāt tasmin adhāya adhyāhāra-trāmāṇyasya aprativedha iti \| tatra jīva-mano-hṛḍhū tattva-nāmābhidhātāraḥ saṅkarṣaṇa-pradyumna-aniruddhā iti teṣām eva jīvādi-śabdena abhidhānam aviruddham; yathā ākāśa-prāṇādi-śabdaiḥ brahmaṇaḥ abhidhānam \|\| 45 \|\|

## \|\| 2.2.42 \|\|

*vipratiṣedhāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

vipratiṣiddhā hi jīva-utpattiḥ tad-abhinna api tantraiḥ; yathoktaṃ parasaṃhitāyām— acetanaḥ parārthā ca nityā satata-vikriyā \| tri-guṇā karmiṇāṃ kṣetraṃ prakṛte rūpam ucyate \|\| vyāmiśreṇa saṃsargeṇa eva puruṣasya ca \| sa hi anādiḥ anantaḥ ca paramārthaḥ niścitaḥ \|\| iti \| evaṃ sarveṣu api saṃhitāsu jīvasya nityatva-vacanair jīva-saṅkalpa-utpattiḥ pañcarātra-tantre pratibiddhā \| janma-maraṇādi-vyavahāraḥ tu loka-vedayoḥ jīvasya yathopapadyate, tathā ātmā śruteḥ iti atra vakṣyate \| ataḥ jīvasya anityatvā api pratipiddhā iti jīva-utpattyādi-vihitāśrāpaṇya-kāṅkṣā dūrotsāritā \|\| yat ca evaṃ keṣāñcit udvegaḥ: sāṅkhyeṣu vedeṣu nibaddhata-lakṣaṇāḥ śāṇḍilyaḥ pañcarātra-śāstram adhītavān iti sāṅkhye vede-bhyaḥ puruṣārtha-niṣṭhā na labheti vacanāt vaidika-dharma-vedaṃ tantram iti, saḥ api ātta-veda-vacanam anākalita-tad-guḍhārtha-kalpanānāṃ śraddhā-mātra-vijṛmbhitaḥ \| yathā bhāraḥ prātastaraṃ te vadanti puroḍāśaṃ juhvati kenitahomaḥ iti anudita-homa-nindā udita-homa-praśaṃsārtham uktam; yathā ca bhūmi-vidyā-prakrame nāradena ṛgvedaṃ bhagavaḥ adhyemi yajurvedaṃ sāmavedaṃ mātharvaṇaṃ caturtham itihāsa-purāṇaṃ pañcamaṃ iti ārabhya sarvaṃ vidyā-sthānam abhidhāya saḥ ahaṃ bhagavaḥ mantra-vit eva asmi na ātma-vit iti bhūma-vidyā-vyatiriktāsu sarvāsu vidyāsu ātma-saṃvedanālābha-vacanaṃ brahmātma-bhūma-vidyā-praśaṃsārthaṃ dṛṣṭam \| athavā asya nāradasya sāṅkhyaṃ vede-bhyaḥ yad aratatvaṃ pratipādyate, tad-dharma-nimittaḥ ayaṃ vādaḥ; evam eva śāṇḍilyasya iti, pañcarātrāntara-veda-vākya-virodhi aparama-brahmāntara-abhidhānāt na dūṣaṇam yate \| tathā vedārthasya durjñānatayā su-bodhana-upāya-bhūta-adhikaramaḥ parasaṃhitāyām ucyate \| arthataḥ bhagavān vedāt sāṅkhya-upāṅgata-rasa-vistaraḥ \| śrutāni ca mayā aṅgāni vākya-vākya-yutāni ca \| na ca etaiḥ samastaiḥ ca śakyaḥ ahaṃ vinā kvacit \| śreyo-mārgaṃ prapaśyāmi yena siddhiṃ gacchāmi \|\| iti \| vedāntaveṣu yathā sāraṃ saṃgṛhya bhagavān hariḥ \| bhaktānukampayā vidvān saṃkṣepaṃ kathayāsu-bham \|\| iti ca \| ataḥ asya bhagavad-veditvāt ācāryaḥ para-brahmābhidhānaḥ yāju-dvedī nikhilaiḥ prayatnaiḥ karcayāṃ-cakrānāmanta-nāmānuvādha-pratimodahā-guṇa-sāgara-stava-sa-sūdraprabhṛtu— īśvara-caturātma-prabhava-vyūhādiḥ iti ādyāt dharmārtha-kāma-mokṣa-puruṣārtha-nimittatvān muktānanda-vaidya-apārakīrṇa-śeṣa-śeṣitvātmatva-udyadharma-hetubhiḥ sva-saṅkalpa-vibhūtitvāt rādhāntara-phalāyātakatvāt

bodhinaḥ vedāḥ tat-anuṣṭhāna-arthaṃ śaiva-vaiṣṇava-parimita-āśayāt vidhi-artha-vāda-samartha-rūpatvāt sva-itara-saṃkalpa-mukhara-ḍuḍu-vagāhā-śrāddhārthyaṃ tad-yathāvasthi-avabodhi pāñcarātra-śāstraṃ svayam eva nirmimīti iti niravadyam \|\|

yas tu paraṃ sūtra-api-ukhyaṃ kasyacit rahasya-prāmāṇya-niṣedhakaṃ vyākhyātam; tatra sūtra-akṣara-anuguṇam, sūtra-kāra-abhiprāya-viruddham \|\|

tathā hi—sūtra-kāreṇa vedānta-tattva-apāyibhyaḥ api sūtrāṇi abhidadhyau veda-upabṛṃhaṇāya ca bhārata-saṃhitāṃ jñāna-sahakṛtiṃ kurvatā mokṣa-dharma-jñāna-kāṇḍe abhihite granthyo brahmacārī ca vānaprasthaḥ atha bhikṣukaḥ \| yaḥ icchet atimātmānaṃ devatāṃ kāṃ yajeta saḥ \|\| iti ārasya mahatā prabandhena pāñcarātra-śāstra-kriyā pratipādya, idaṃ śata-sahasrāt dhi bhārata-ākhyāna-vistarāt \| ādidhya mati-manthānaṃ dadhnaḥ ghṛtam iva uddhṛtam \| navanītaṃ yathā dadhnaḥ dvi-padāṃ brāhmaṇaḥ yathā \| āraṇyakaṃ ca vedebhyaḥ auṣadhi-bhyaḥ yathā amṛtam \|\| idaṃ mahā-upaniṣadaṃ catur-veda-samanvitam \| sāṅkhya-yoga-kṛtāntena pāñcarātra-anugṛhitam \|\| idaṃ śreyaḥ idaṃ brahma idaṃ hitam anuttamam \| ṛg-yajuḥ-sāmabhiḥ juṣṭam atharvāṅgirasaiḥ tathā \| bhaviṣyati pramāṇaṃ vai etat veda-anuśāsanam \|\| iti \| sāṅkhya-yoga-śabdābhyāṃ jñāna-yoga-karma-yogau abhihitau; yathoktaṃ—jñāna-yogena sāṅkhyānāṃ karma-yogena yoginām iti \| bhīṣma-parvaṇi api—brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriya-vaiśyāḥ śūdrāḥ ca kṛta-lakṣaṇāḥ \| arcayitvā arcyaḥ sevyaḥ ca pūjanīyaḥ ca mādhavaḥ \| sātvataṃ vidhim āsthāya gīta-saṅgṛhītena yaḥ \|\| iti \| kam evaṃ buddhoṇī vādarāyaṇo veda-vit-variṣṭhaḥ vedānta-vedya-para-brahma-bhūta-vāsudeva-upāsana-ananta-avirbhāvāt anantarasya śānta-tā-sahasya-aprāmāṇyaṃ brūyāt ?

nanu ca sāṅkhyaṃ yogaḥ pāñcarātraṃ vedāḥ pāśupataṃ tathā \|...kim etāni eka-niṣṭhāni pṛthak-niṣṭhāni vā mune iti-ādinā sāṅkhya-ādīnām adyādarśaṇīyata ucyate \| śārīrake tu sāṅkhya-ādoṣi pratiṣidhyante \| ataḥ idam api tantraṃ tat-tulyam \| na iti ucyate, yataḥ tatra api ime eva śārīraka-uktaiḥ nyāyaiḥ avatarati ati \| kim etāni eka-niṣṭhāni pṛthak-niṣṭhāni ca iti praśna-tathyārthaḥ—kiṃ sāṅkhya-yoga-pāśupata-veda-pāñcarātrāṇi eka-tattva-pratipādana-parāṇi, pṛthak-tattva-pratipādana-parāṇi vā ? yadi eka-tattva-pratipādana-parāṇi, kiṃ tat ekaṃ tattvam ? yadā tu pṛthak-tattva-pratipādana-parāṇi, tadā evaṃ parasparaṃ viruddha-artha-pratipādana-paratvāt vastuni vikalpa-asaṃbhavāt ca kam eva pramāṇam āgīkāraṇīyam, kiṃ tat ekam ? iti \| atha uttaraṃ munayaḥ jñānāni etāni rājarṣe viddhi nānā-matāni vai \| sāṅkhyasya vaktā kapilaḥ iti ārabhya sāṅkhya-yoga-pāśupatānāṃ kapila-hiraṇyagarbha-paśupati-kṛtatvena pauruṣeyatvaṃ pratipādya avāntaratvavā nāma veda-ācāryasya ucyate iti vedānām apauruṣeyatvam abhidhāya pāñcarātrasya kṛtsnasya vaktā nārāyaṇaḥ svayam iti pāñcarātra-tantrasya vaktā nārāyaṇaḥ svayam eva iti bruvāṇāt \|\|

evaṃ vadatsu ayam āśayaḥ—pauruṣeyāṇāṃ hi tantrāṇāṃ paraspara-viruddha-vastuṣu avitatayā apauruṣeyatvena nirasta-samasta-doṣa-gandha-nikhila-laukika-veda-vedya-vastu-suniścitatva-vidhāyikānāṃ yathāvasthita-vastuni prāmāṇyaṃ durlabham; veda-vedyaḥ ca para-brahma-bhūtaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ; ataḥ tat-tantra-abhihita-spradhāna-puruṣa-paśupati-prabhṛtīnāṃ tasya vedānta-vedya-para-brahma-bhūta-nārāyaṇa-samakatvaṃ ca vastutvam asthūlam anīyam iti \| tat idam āha ca sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma viśvaṃ nārāyaṇaḥ ity-ādinā sarvasya brahmātmakatā-anusandhānasya nārāyaṇaḥ eva niṣṭhā iti pratīyata iti arthaḥ \| ataḥ vedānta-vedyaḥ para-brahma-bhūtaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ svayam eva pañca-rātrasya kartṛtvasya vakṣati, tat-svarūpa-pratipādana-abhidhāyi tantram iti ca tasmin itara-tantra-sāmānyaṃ na kenacit duṣṭatva-viduṃ śakyam \| ataḥ ca veda-mūlatve—evaṃ-vettā sāṅkhya-yogaṃ veda-āraṇyakam eva ca \| paraspara-aṅgāni etāni pañca-rātraṃ tu kathyate \|\| iti \| sāṅkhyaṃ ca yogaḥ ca sāṅkhya-yogam, vedāḥ ca āraṇyakāni ca veda-āraṇyakam, paraspara-aṅgāni etāni eka-tattva-pratipādana-paratva-ekī-bhūtāni iti evaṃ pañca-rātram iti kathyate \| evam uktaṃ bhavati—sāṅkhya-oktyā pañca-viṃśati-tattvāni, yoga-uktaṃ ca yama-niyama-adhyātmakaṃ yogam, veda-udita-karma-svarūpāṇi aṅgīkṛtya tattvānāṃ brahmātmakatvaṃ, yogasya ca brahma-upāsana-prakāratvaṃ karmaṇāṃ ca tat-ārādhana-rūpatā-abhidhāyi brahma-svarūpaṃ pratipādayan nyāya-vacanāni \| evam eva pareṇa brahmaṇā nārāyaṇena svayam eva pañca-rātra-tantraṃ viśadīkṛtam iti \|\|

śārīrake ca sāṅkhya-ukta-tattva-mātra-brahmātmakatā-mātraṃ nirākṛtam; na svarūpam \| yoga-pāśupatayoḥ ca īśvarasya kevala-nimitta-kāraṇatā, para-ukta-tattva-viparīta-kalpanā, veda-bahir-bhūta-ācāraḥ nirākṛtaḥ; na yoga-svarūpam, paśupati-rūpaṃ ca \| ataḥ sāṅkhyaṃ ca yogaḥ pañca-rātraṃ vedāḥ pāśupataṃ tathā \| ātma-pramāṇāni etāni na hantavyāni hetubhiḥ \|\| iti api tat-tat-abhihita-tat-tat-svarūpa-mātra-prāñjīkāryam; jina-mata-abhihitatva-vat sarvaṃ na bahīkāryam iti ucyate, yathāgam yathānyāyaṃ niṣṭhā nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ iti anena antyāntam \|\| 42 \|\| iti utpatti-asambhava-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye dvitīyasya adhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**dvitīya-adhyāye—tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ—viyat-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Pada 3

## \|\| 2.3.1 \|\|

*na viyat aśruteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

sāṅkhya-ādi-veda-bāhya-tantrāṇāṃ nyāyābhāsa-mūlatayā viprati-veda-añcasāmūlam uktam;

idaṃ hi sva-pakṣasya viprati-vedi-dviśeṣa-jñāna-abhāva-khyāpanāya brahma-kāryatvādibhiḥ ati-vicitra-cid-acit-maka-prapañcasya kāraṇa-prakāro viśodhyate \| tatra viyat-utpattau, na vā iti saṃśayate \| kiṃ yuktam? na viyat utpadyata iti \| kutaḥ? aśruteḥ; saṃbhavitasya hi śravaṇa-saṃbhavaḥ; asaṃbhavitasya tu gagana-kusuma-vat viyat-utpatteḥ śabda-mithyātvaṃ na saṃbhavati \| na khalu niravayavasya sarva-gatasya ākāśasya ātmana iva īśvareṇa anirūpya-kṛtuṃ śakyate \| ata eva caitanya-saṃbhavāt chāndogye sṛṣṭi-prakaraṇe tejaḥ-prabhṛtīnām eva utpattiḥ āmnāyate—tad aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tat tejaḥ asṛjata iti \| taittirīyakāḥ ca tasmāt vā etasmāt ātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ; pṛthivīmajjāyate prāṇo manaḥ sarva-indriyāṇi ca, khaṃ vāyuḥ jyotiḥ āpaḥ ity-ādiṣu śruti-smṛtīḥ artha-virodhāt bādhyata iti \|\|1\|\|

**evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate—**

## \|\| 2.3.2 \|\|

*asti tu*

**Комментарий:**

asti tu ākāśasya utpattiḥ \| atīndriya-artha-viṣayā hi śrutiḥ pramāṇa-antara-avirodhi viyat-utpatti-pratipādayituṃ samarthā \| na ca śruti-pratipanne arthe tad-itara-vidhi-niravayavatvādi-hetukam anutpatti-anumānam udeti; ātmanaḥ api utpattiḥ iti niravayavatva-prabhṛtiḥ iti vakṣyate \|\|2\|\|

**punar āśaṅkyati—**

## \|\| 2.3.3 \|\|

*gauṇī saṃbhavāc chabdāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

tasmāt vā etasmāt ātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ ity-ādi-viyat-utpatti-śrutiḥ gauṇī iti kalpayituṃ yuktam, tat tejaḥ asṛjata iti sisṛkṣoḥ brahmaṇaḥ prathamaṃ teja utpannam iti tejas-utpatti-nyāyena viyat-utpatti-śruti-apāta-saṃbhavāt, vāyuḥ ca antarikṣaṃ ca etad amṛtam iti viyataḥ amṛtatva-śravaṇāt ca \|\|3\|\|

katham etasya saṃbhūta-śabdasya ākāśāpekṣayā gauṇatvam, agnyādyapekṣayā mukhyatvam iti cet, tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.3.4 \|\|

*syāt ca ekasya brahma-śabda-vat*

**Комментарий:**

ekasya eva tasmāt vā etasmāt ātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ iti ākāśe mukhyatva-asaṃbhavāt gauṇatve prayuktasya saṃbhūta-śabdasya vāyoḥ agniḥ ity-ādiṣu api uktasya mukhyatvaṃ syāt eva; brahma-śabda-vat—yathā brahma-śabdaḥ; tasmāt etat brahma nāma rūpam annaṃ ca jāyate ity atra pradhāne gauṇatayā prayuktasmin eva prakaraṇe brahma ca agre brahma tataḥ asṛjyata iti brahmaṇi mukhyatayā prayujyate; tad-vat \| anupapannai ca śravaṇāhitātivādhinānupatti-vidhi-vat eva etad-arthaḥ \|\|4\|\|

**parihṛtiḥ—**

## \|\| 2.3.5 \|\|

*pratijñā-hāniḥ avyatirekāt*

**Комментарий:**

chāndogya-śruti-upanyāsena nyāsaṃ vidyut-prabhṛti-cāhinīnānāṃ śrutīnāṃ gauṇaṃ kalpayituṃ na yujyate \| yataḥ chāndogya-śrur eva viyat-utpattiḥ iti uktā; evam aśrutaṃ śrutim iti ādinā brahma-vijñānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñānāt \| tathā hi pratijñāyāḥ ahāniḥ ākāśasya api brahma-kāryatvena tad-avyatirekād eva bhavati \|\| 5 \|\|

## \|\| 2.3.6 \|\|

*śabdebhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

itaś ca viyat-utpattiḥ chāndogye pratīyate, “sat eva saumya idam agra āsīt ekam eva advitīyam” iti prastutya-eka-vijñāna-avadhāraṇa-para-brahmāt; “aitadātmyam idaṃ sarvam iti etac-chabde-madhyasya kāryatvena brahmaṇo ’vyatireka-smṛtaḥ \| na ca “tat tejaḥ asṛjata” iti tejasaḥ utpatti-śrutiḥ viyat-utpattiṃ vārayati \| viyat-utpatti-vacana-mātreṇa tejasaḥ prāg evamānaṃ prāthamyam iti ānantarya-pratiṣedhaṃ viyat-utpattiṃ na nivārayitum alam \|\| 6 \|\|

## \|\| 2.3.7 \|\|

*yāvad vikāraṃ tu vibhāgo lokavat*

**Комментарий:**

tūktaṃ ca etat; “aitadātmyam idaṃ sarvam” iti avibhāga-mukhasya vikāra-vacanena tasya ākāśasya brahmaṇi vibhāgaḥ—utpattiḥ iti ukta eva \| lokavat—yathā loke ete sarve devadatta-putrāḥ iti avidhāya teṣu kecid vañcitā utpatti-vacanena sarveṣām utpatti-kartā syāt, evaṃ ca sati “vāyuḥ ca antarikṣaṃ ca etat amṛtam” iti purāṇa-ādi iva cira-kāla-sthāyitva-mātram \|\| 7 \|\|

## \|\| 2.3.8 \|\|

*etena mātariśvā vyākhyātaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

anena eva hetunā mātariśvanaḥ vāyoḥ api utpattiḥ vyākhyātā \| viyat-mātariśvanoḥ pṛthag yoga-karaṇaṃ “tejaḥ asṛjata” tathā hi āha iti mātariśva-prabhava-āśaṅkṣe \|\| 8 \|\|

## \|\| 2.3.9 \|\|

*asaṃbhavaḥ tu sataḥ anupapatteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

tu uktau ca udāharaṇa-arthaḥ \| asaṃbhavaḥ—anutpattiḥ; sataḥ—brahmaṇa ekaḥ; tad-vyatiriktasya kasyacid api anutpattiḥ na saṃbhavati, anupapatteḥ \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—viyat-mātariśvanoḥ upapatti-pādanam udāharaṇa-artham; utpatti-saṃbhavaḥ tu sataḥ parama-kāraṇasya parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ \| tad-vyatiriktasya kṛtsnasya api ātma-mahat-ahaṃkāra-tanmātra-indriya-viṣaya-bhuvana-ādikasya prapañcasya aneka-vidhātena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñā-ādi-viruddha-avakāśe api avasthāy anutpattiḥ na upapadyata iti \|\| 9 \|\| iti viyat-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha tejo-'dhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.3.10 \|\|

*tejaḥ ataḥ tathā hi āha*

**Комментарий:**

brahma-kṣatiriktasya kṛtsnasya brahma-kāryatva-bhuktam \| idānīṃ vyavasthita-kāryāṇāṃ kiṃ kevalānantadananata-kāraṇa-bhūtā-hetum utpattiḥ, āhosvid ananta-pada-brahmaṇa iti cintyate \| kiṃ yuktam ? kevalānantad-abhūtam iti kutaḥ ? tejas-tattvādeḥ ataḥ—śāstra-viśrute eva utpadyate \| “sayogoraciḥ” iti hi āha \|\| 10 \|\|

## \|\| 2.3.11 \|\|

*āpaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

āpaḥ api ataḥ—tejasaḥ eva utpadyante, “agner āpaḥ” “tad āpaḥ agṛhṇata” iti hi āha \|\| 11 \|\|

## \|\| 2.3.12 \|\|

*pṛthivī*

**Комментарий:**

pṛthivī adbhyāḥ utpadyate—“adbhyāḥ pṛthivī, “tā akṣasamṛjanta” iti hi āha \|\| 12 \|\| nandatra śabdena kathaṃ pṛthivī abhidhīyate ? ata āha—

## \|\| 2.3.13 \|\|

*adhikāra-rūpa-śabdāntarebhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

mahā-bhūta-dṛṣṭy-adhikārāt pṛthivī eva akṣara-śabdena ucyata iti pratīyate \| adanīyasya sarvasya pṛthivī-vikāratvāt kāraṇe kārya-śabdaḥ \| tathā vākya-śeṣe bhūtānāṃ rūpa-saṃśabde, “yad agne rohitaṃ rūpaṃ tejasas tad rūpaṃ yat śuklaṃ tat apāṃ yaḥ kṛṣṇaṃ tat annasya” ityatra jarosannajātiyena bādhya-śabdasya eva pratīyate \| śabdāntaraṃ ca samāna-prakaraṇe “agner āpaḥ; adbhyāḥ pṛthivī” iti śrūyate \| ataḥ pṛthivī eva akṣara-śabdena ucyata iti adūṣya eva pṛthivī jāyate \| udāhṛta-vākyena prabhṛtayaḥ pradarśitārthāḥ \| mahādi-utpādā api sthānāntara-vacanena eva utpadyate, yathā śruti-upagāma-avirodhāt \| “etasmāj jāyate prāṇo manaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca \| khaṃ vāyuḥ jyotiḥ āpaḥ pṛthivī viśvasya dhāriṇī “tasmād vā etasmād ātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ; ākāśād vāyuḥ” iti ādyau brahmaṇaḥ paramparayā kāraṇatvodyupapadyata iti \|\| 13 \|\| evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—

## \|\| 2.3.14 \|\|

*tad-abhidhyānād eva tu tat-liṅgāt saḥ*

**Комментарий:**

tulyavat tad-akṣo jāyate \| mahat-ādi-kāryāṇām api tad-anantatva-vasthūrīkatvam eva puruṣottamaḥ kāraṇam; kutaḥ ? tad-abhidhyāna-rūpa-tal-liṅgād ava \| abhidhyānaṃ—bahu syām iti saṅkalpaḥ, “tat teja aikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya” iti “tā āpa aikṣanta bahvyaḥ syāmaḥ prajāyemahi”

iti uktāni brahma-bhavana-durlabha-cchadeśa-śravaṇāt mahad-aṇḍūra-rākāśādyānām api kāraṇānāṃ tathā-viśeṣa-pūrvakaṃ sva-kaṇṭha-gṛhīta iti gamyate; tathā divyaṃ veṣaṃ tat-tac-chrotṛ-kartṛtva-paratvaṃ brahmaṇa upapadyate \| śrūyate ca sarva-śarīrakatvena sarvātmakatvaṃ parasya brahmaṇaḥ anyatra abhihāyate yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan yaḥ apsu tiṣṭhan yaḥ tejasi tiṣṭhan yaḥ vāyau tiṣṭhan yaḥ ākāśe tiṣṭhan iti-ādi \| subālopaniṣadi ca asya pṛthivī śarīram iti ārabhya asya ahaṅkāra-śarīrasya asya buddhi-śarīrasya asya avyaktaṃ śarīram iti-ādi \|\| 14 \|\|

yat ekam etasmat ājāyate prāṇaḥ manaḥ sarva-indriyāṇi ca iti-ādiṣu śrūyamāṇā brahmaṇaḥ prāṇa-ādi-buddhiḥ paramparayā upapadyata iti; atra api ucyate—

## \|\| 2.3.15 \|\|

*viparyayeṇa tu kramaḥ ataḥ upapadyate ca*

**Комментарий:**

tu-śabdaḥ avadhāraṇārthaḥ \| avyakta-mahad-aṅkūra-rākāśādi-kramāt viparyayeṇa yat sarveṣāṃ kāryāṇāṃ brahma-anantaratva-rūpaḥ kramaḥ etasmat ājāyate prāṇa iti-ādiṣu pratīyate, sa ca kramaḥ tat-tat-mūla-pāḍa-brahmaṇaḥ tat-kāryatvena eva upapadyate \| paramparayā kāraṇatve hi ānantaryya-śravaṇam upahanyate \| ataḥ etasmat ājāyate iti-ādi-kramaḥ api sarvasya brahmaṇaḥ sākṣāt-saṃbhava-yogam abhanat \|\| 15 \|\|

## \|\| 2.3.16 \|\|

*antarā vijñāna-manasī krameṇa tat-liṅgāt iti cet na aviśeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

vijñāna-sādhanatvāt indriyāṇi vijñānam iti ucyante \| yat uktam etasmat ājāyate iti-ādinā sarvasya brahmaṇaḥ anantara-kāryatvaṃ śākyate; ataḥ śrānena vākyena sarvasya sākṣāt-brahmaṇo utpattiḥ abhidhāna-liṅga-avagata-utathyata iti; tat na upapadyate, krama-viśeṣa-paratvāt asya vākyasya; atra api sarveṣāṃ krama-prātīteḥ \| kha-ādi-vat tāvat śruty-antara-siddhaḥ kramaḥ atra api pratīyate; taiḥ saha pāṭha-liṅgāt abhūta-prāṇayoḥ antarāle vijñāna-manasī api krameṇa ucyete iti pratīyate \| atas sarvasya sākṣāt-brahmaṇa eva saṃbhava-yoga-abhidhau vākyaṃ na bhavati iti cet; tat na, aviśeṣāt— etasmat ājāyate prāṇa iti anena aviśeṣāt \| vijñāna-manasoḥ khādīnāṃ ca etasmat ājāyate iti anena sākṣāt-saṃbhava-phala-saṃbandhasya aviśeṣasya sarveṣāṃ prāṇa-ādi-buddhy-antanām aviśiṣṭatvāt sa eva vidheyaḥ, na kramaḥ \| śruty-antara-siddha-krama-virodhāt ca na eva krama-param, pṛthivy apasu pralīyate iti ārasya kramāḥ pare deva ekī-bhavati iti anena kramāntara-prātīteḥ \| ataḥ avyakta-ādi-śarīrāt parasya brahmaṇa eva sarva-kāraṇāt mūrtiḥ iti \| tejaḥ-prabhṛti atra śabdaḥ trastāt bhūtaṃ brahmopānirudham \|\| 16 \|\|

nanu ca idaṃ sarva-śabdānāṃ brahma-vācitve sati tat-saṃśabdaḥ tat-tat-bhūta-yupadeśaḥ yuktān iti siddham upakṛṣyate; tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.3.17 \|\|

*cara-acara-vyapāśrayaḥ tu syāt tad-vyapadeśaḥ bhāktaḥ tad-bhāva-bhāvitvāt*

**Комментарий:**

tuhuḥ-śobhita-śākhāḍūna-nityārthaḥ \| nikhila-jaḍa-madhyātmasya āśrayaḥ tanu-cchanda-vyapadeśaḥ bhāktaḥ—cara-acara-vyapāśraye bhavyata iti arthaḥ \| samasta-vastu-prakāśiṇaḥ brahmaṇaḥ prakāśa-bhūtatvam upahita-pratyakṣādi-pramāṇa-viṣayavād iti na atraṇāḥ yama-kārya-smṛteḥ, prakāśi-śrīti-vibhāga-bhāvitvāt ca tatra eva sampratyayaḥ, loke tantu-samāve vāraṇeṣu iva ete śabdāḥ bhaṅktvā bhaṅktvā vyapadiśyate \| athavā tejaḥ-prabhṛti-miśra-śabdaḥ tan-mātratva-bhāvitayā vyutpannaṃ brahmaṇaḥ vyapadeśaḥ bhāktaḥ syāt—ātmopasthā-vidyāḍūnaḥ ca, cara-acara-vyapāśrayaḥ tu iti ucyate \| cara-acara-vyapāśrayaḥ tat-tat-upadeśāt—tat-tat-viśeṣaḥ; cara-acara-viśiṣṭaḥ brahmaṇaḥ bhāktaḥ—mukhyaḥ eva; kutaḥ? brahmātmanā-vidita-tat-saṃbandhānāṃ vācaka-bhāvasya; nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇa-sthā hi tathā-bhāvasya \|\| 17 \|\|

iti tejo-dhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha ātmādhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.3.18 \|\|

*na ātmā śruteḥ nityatvāt ca tābhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

viyad-ādeḥ kṛtsnasya para-samād brahmaṇaḥ utpattiḥ uktā \| idānīṃ jīvasya api utpattiḥ asti iti na iti saṃśayyate \| kiṃ yuktam? asti iti, kutaḥ? eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñā-upanateḥ; prāk utpatteḥ ekatva-avadhāraṇāt ca \| viyad-ādeḥ iva jīvasya api utpatti-vādinaḥ śrutayaḥ tu bhavanti—yataḥ prasūtā jagataḥ prasūtī toyena jīvān vyasasarja bhūmyām prajāpatiḥ prajāḥ asṛjata san-mūlāḥ somya imāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ sat-āyatanāḥ sat-pratiṣṭhāḥ sataḥ vai imāni bhūtāni jāyante iti \| evaṃ sa-cetanasya jagataḥ utpatti-śravaṇāt jīvasya api utpattiḥ pratīyate \| na ca vācyaṃ brahmaṇaḥ nityatvāt tan-madhyastha-abhinna jīvasya brahmatva-avagamāt jīvasya nityatva-siddhiḥ; etad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvam sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma iti evam-ādi-vidhi-vacaneṣu api brahmatva-avagamāt tasya api nityatvam astu; ataḥ jīvaḥ api viyad-ādi-vat utpadyata iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate—na ātmā śruteḥ iti \| na asau utpadyate; kutaḥ? śruteḥ—saḥ na jāyate mriyate vā vipaścit ajñātaḥ dhruvaḥ jātaḥ iti-ādibhiḥ jīvasya utpatti-niṣedhaḥ hi śrūyate \| ātmanaḥ nityatvaṃ ca tābhyaḥ—śrutibhyaḥ eva avagamyate nityaḥ nityānām cetanaḥ cetanānām ekaḥ bahūnāṃ yaḥ vidadhāti kāmān ajaḥ nityaḥ śāśvataḥ ayaṃ purāṇaḥ na hanyate hanyamāne śarīre iti-ādibhyaḥ \| ataḥ ca na ātmā utpadyate \| kathaṃ tarhi eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñāna-pratijñā upapadyate? iti atra āha—jīvasya api kāryatvāt kārya-kāraṇayoḥ ananyatvāt ca \| evaṃ tarhi viyad-ādi-vat

dur-nivāram anadṛṣṭaṃ syāt; na iti ucyate \| kārakaṃ hi nāma-ākhyā-pratyaya-vācyatva-sthita-artha-viśeṣaḥ tat-tādhyavasthasya eva \| iyaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ—vidyadeḥ eva cetanasya grāhya-śeṣyathā-abhāvaḥ na tādṛśaḥ jīvasya; jñānatvaḥ ca vikāsa-lakṣaṇaḥ jīvasya anyathā-bhāvaḥ, vidyadeḥ tu svarūpa-anyathā-bhāva-lakṣaṇaḥ \| sā iyaṃ svarūpa-anyathā-bhāva-lakṣaṇaḥ vikāritve pratibadhyate \|\|

evaṃ uktaṃ bhavati—bhogya-bhoktṛ-niyantṛ-vidhi-kṛt-svabhāvāt pratipādya bhogyatā-mukhya-pratipādakaṃ bhoktari pratibadhya tasya nityatāṃ ca pratipādya, bhogyatā-mukhya-pratipādakaṃ bhokṛtāṃ bāhya-karaṇa-artha-vyatvaṃ niyantari pratibadhya, tasya nityatvaṃ, nirañjanatvaṃ, sarvadā sarvajñatvaṃ, satya-saṅkalpatvaṃ, karaṇa-adhipa-adhipatvaṃ, viśvasya patitvaṃ ca pratipādya, sarva-viṣaya-tri-viśiṣṭa-cittatvaṃ prati śarīratvaṃ, tasya ca ārānatvaṃ pratipāditam \| atas tad veda ā cid-acid-vastu-śarīrasya tattva-prakāraṃ brahma \| tat kvacit cittatvam āhiṣva-rūpya-deśa-māhitya-sūkṣma-daśā-āpanna-cid-vastu-śarīraṃ tiṣṭhati; tat kāraṇa-avasthaṃ brahma \| kadācit ca vibhakta-nāma-rūpa-sthūla-cid-acid-vastu-śarīram; tac ca kārya-avastham \| tatra kāraṇa-avasthasya kārya-avasthā-āpannatva-videśasya kāraṇa-avasthāyāṃ śabda-ādi-kṛtsnasya bhogyatva-vācya-śabda-abhimatayā svarūpa-anyathā-bhāva-rūpa-vikāraḥ bhavati \| cid-vastuṣya ca karma-phala-viśeṣa-bhoktṛtvāya tat-tat-anukūla-jñāna-vikāsa-rūpa-vikāraḥ bhavati \| ubhaya-prakāra-vikṛtau niyantre tattva-avasthita-dṛśya-viśiṣṭa-ātma-svarūpa-vikāraḥ bhavati; kāraṇa-avasthāyāḥ avyavahita-nāma-pati-rūpa-vikāraḥ prakṛta-dvaye prakāriṇi ca samānaḥ \| ata evai kārya-avasthā-antara-āpati-rūpa-vikāro 'pe kṣayā netā śrutī śrūyate—nityaḥ nityānāṃ cetanaḥ cetanānām ekaḥ bahūnāṃ yaḥ vidadhāti kāmān iti eka-vijñānena sarva-vijñānaṃ pratijñāya mṛttikā-dṛṣṭāntaḥ yathā somya ekena mṛt-piṇḍena sarvaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vijñātaṃ syāt iti-ādinā nirdiṣṭaḥ \| īśvara-anata-drūpa-vikāra-skara-tantu-dṛḍha-saṃbandha-yoga-niṣṭhāyāḥ jīvasya uparita-mṛga-vyādikyaḥ; prajāpatiḥ prajāḥ asṛjata iti-ādyāḥ śrutayaḥ \| acid-īśvarāt saṃjñā-anyathātva-abhāva-abhiprāyā utpatti-antra-vidhāya-vidyaḥ nityatva-vādinyaḥ na jāyate mriyate iti-ādyāḥ; nityaḥ nityānām iti-ādyāḥ ca śrutayaḥ \| svarūpa-anyathā-bhāva-ātma-jñāna-vikāsa-rūpaḥ yathā-vidhā-niṣṭha-vikāra-samaviprāyāḥ sadā eva mahān aja ātmā ajaraḥ amaraḥ amṛtaḥ brahma nityaḥ nityānām iti-ādyāḥ para-viṣayāḥ śrutayaḥ \| evaṃ sarva-vedāḥ cid-acid-vastu-viśiṣṭhasya brahmaṇaḥ prakṛṣṭa-deśa-kāla-vacāraṇe ca nāma-rūpa-vibhāga-abhāvāt prapadyate \| tad idaṃ tarhi avyākṛtam āsīt tat nāma-rūpābhyāṃ vyākriyata iti hi nāma-rūpa-vibhāga-bhāva-abhāvābhyāṃ nānātvam ekatvaṃ vadanti iti \|\|

ye tu aviccha-upādhikaṃ jīvatvaṃ vadanti, ye ca pāramārthika-upādhi-kṛtam, ye ca san-mātra-svarūpaṃ brahma svayam eva bhogya-bhojya-niyantṛ-rūpeṇa tri-dhā-avasthitaṃ vadanti; sarveṣu ete avidyā-śarīra-upadhi-kṛta-kṣetra-śrita-bhogya-niyantṛ-śaktīnāṃ ca pralaya-kāle 'pi anena eva tad-anīma-kartavya-ādhāraṇaṃ nāma-rūpa-vibhāga-bhāvāt iti upapādyanti \| bandha-mokṣau ca na sāpekṣatvāt na karma-vibhāgāt iti vaivaraṇād iti anupapadyate cāṭu-phalatve ca iti sūtrābhyāṃ jīva-bhedasya tat-karma-pravāhasya ca anā-

ditvādhyupagamac ca \| iti-ādi-viśeṣaḥ—ekasya anādi-avidyayā brahma svayam eva muhyati; atha asya pāramārthikatvābhyupagacchitā brahma-svarūpam eva badhyate, upādhiḥ brahma-vyatiriktaḥ cet tarhi tat-antarbhāvāt; aparasya brahma eva vicitra-ākāreṇa pariṇamate, karma-phalāni ca anubhūya bhūtte; niraṃśasya eva bhoktṛtva-abhāve api sarva-kartṛtvasya samāhitaṃ bhoktāram anuvidadhāti iti svayam eva bhuṅkte \| asmākaṃ tu sthūla-sūkṣma-avasthā-cid-acid-vastu-śarīraṃ brahma kārya-kāraṇobhāyāvasthā-sthitam api pūrvoktā nirasta-nikhila-doṣa-gandhaṃ satya-saṃkalpatva-ādi-aparimita-udāra-guṇa-sāgaram avatiṣṭhate \| prakāra-bhūta-cid-acid-vastu-gatāḥ alpa-vyayāḥ svarūpa-anyathā-bhāvāḥ ca iti sarvaṃ samañjasam \|\| 18 \|\|

iti ātma-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha jñā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.3.19 \|\|

*jñaḥ ata eva*

**Комментарий:**

cidvastv cidacjīvo nodyata iti uktam, tatrasaj jñena jīva-svarūpaṃ nirūpyate \| kiṃ suṣupti-kṛt-ādiṣu avicchinnāvastho adhyātma-saṃbandham; uta kādācitkābhimata-vyāpāraika-lakṣaṇa-svarūpa-ciritsvabhāvenānuvartamānaṃ caitanya-guṇakam; atha jñātvam eva asya svarūpam iti \| kiṃ yuktam? cin-mātra-vṛttiḥ; kutaḥ? tathā śruteḥ \| ananya-adhīna-prakāśo hi ayam ātmāni tiṣṭhati mādhyandodīya-paryāyasya sthāne yo vijñāne tiṣṭhati kāṇvā adhīyate \| tathā vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanute, karmāṇi tanute api ca iti kartṛtvamno vijñānam eva svarūpaṃ śrūyate \| smṛtiṣu ca jñāna-svarūpam ati-anirmalaṃ paramārthataḥ iti-ādi-vacanamno jñāna-svarūpatvaṃ pratīyate \|\|

aparas tu—jīva-ātmano jñānatve jñātṛtve ca svābhāvikeṣu upapadyamāne tasya sarva-gatasya sarvadā sarvatrāpalabdha-prasaṅgāt, karaṇānāṃ ca vaiyarthyati, supti-mūrcchālikṣu sataḥ api ātmanaś ca tatyānupalabdheś caritra-sāmagrīyāṃ satyāṃ jñānopacchindarśanād asya na jñānaṃ svarūpam; na api jñātṛtvam; āgantukam eva caitanyam; sarvagatatvaṃ ca ātmanaḥ dravyatvāt pṛthvyādyatulyam, sarvatra kāryopalabdhiḥ sarvatra sva-saṃnikarṣāvidhānābhūṣaṇamānecchārogam anena eva kārya-saṃbhave sati gati-kalpanāyāṃ pramāṇa-abhāvāc ca \| śrūtiḥ api supti-privelāyāṃ jñāna-abhāvaṃ darśayati—na aha khalv ayam evaṃ saṃpraty ātmānaṃ jānāti ayam aham asmi iti no eva imāni bhūtāni iti \| tathā mokṣa-daśāyāṃ jñāna-abhāvaṃ darśayati—na pretya saṃjñā asti iti iti \| jñāna-svarūpatādyavikrayogas tu jñānasya tat-asādhāraṇa-guṇatvena lākṣaṇika iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—jñaḥ ata eva \| jña eva—ayam ātmā jñātṛ-svarūpa eva, na jñānamātram; na api jaḍa-svarūpaḥ; kutaḥ? ata eva—śruteḥ evaṃ-vācyāḥ \| jña ātmā śruteḥ iti prakṛtā

śrutiḥ ata iti śabdena parāmṛśyate \| tathā chāndogye prajāpati-vākye mukta-amukta-ātma-svabhāva-prakaraṇe atha yaḥ vedaṃ jighrāṇīti sa ātmā manasā etān kāmān paśyan ramate ya ete brahma-loke satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpaḥ na upajanaṃ smaran idaṃ śarīram \| atha yathā na prayataḥ mṛtyuṃ paśyati; tathā vājasaneyake katamaḥ ātmā iti pūrvaṃ yaḥ ayaṃ vijñāna-mayaḥ prāṇeṣu hṛdi antar-jyotiḥ puruṣaḥ iti; tathā vijñātāram are kena vijānīyāt janāti eva ayaṃ puruṣaḥ; tathā eṣa hi draṣṭā śrotā ghrātā rasayitā mantā boddhā kartā vijñāna-ātmā puruṣaḥ; eṣa eva asya paridraṣṭur imābhogau kālaḥ iti \|\|16\|\|

yat tu uktaṃ jñānatve svābhāvike sati sarva-gatasya tasya sarvadā sarva-viṣaya-labdhiḥ prasajyeta iti; tatra ucyate—

## \|\| 2.3.20 \|\|

*utkrānti-gati-āgatīnām*

**Комментарий:**

na ayaṃ sarva-gataḥ; api tu aṇu-parimāṇaḥ; kutaḥ? utkrānti-gati-āgatīnāṃ śruteḥ \| utkrāntiḥ tāvat śrūyate tena pradyotena eva ātmā niṣkrāmati cakṣuṣaḥ vā mūrdhnaḥ vā anyebhyaḥ vā śarīra-deśebhyaḥ iti \| gatiḥ api ye vai ke ca asmāt lokāt prayanti candramasam eva te sarve gacchanti iti \| āgatiḥ api tasmāt lokāt punaḥ eti asmai lokāya karmaṇe iti \| tasmāt etāḥ utkrānti-ādayaḥ na upapadyeran \|\|20\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.21 \|\|

*sva-ātmanā ca uttarayoḥ*

**Комментарий:**

śabda-vat ca adhāraṇe \| yady api śarīra-viyoga-deśa-utkrāntiḥ sthita-sthāna-apādānamanaḥ kañcid upapadyate; gati-āgatī tu na kathañcid upapadyete \| ataḥ te sva-ātmanā eva saṃbandhye \|\|21\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.22 \|\|

*na aṇuḥ ata-cchruter iti cet na itara-adhikārāt*

**Комментарий:**

yaḥ ayaṃ vijñāna-mayaḥ prāṇeṣu iti jīvaṃ prakṛtya sa vā eṣa mahān aja ātmā iti mahattva-śruteḥ na aṇuḥ jīva iti cet na \| itara-adhikārāt—jīva-atiriktasya prājñasya tatra adhikārāt \| yady api upakrame jīvaḥ prakṛtaḥ; tathāpi prasādāt viniḥ pratibuddha ātmā iti madhye paraḥ pratipādyata iti tat-sambandhi idaṃ mahattvam; na jīvasya \|\|22\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.23 \|\|

*sva-śabda-unmānābhyāṃ ca*

**Комментарий:**

sākṣāt aṇu-śabda eva śrūyate eṣaḥ aṇuḥ ātmā cetasā veditavyaḥ yasmin prāṇaḥ pañca-dhā saṃviveśa iti \| udāhṛtya māṇḍūka-manāmaḥ; āpāduśaraṃ vastu-sūkṣmatvaṃ tan-mātratvaṃ jīvasya śrūyate bāla-agra-śata-bhāgasya śata-dhā kalpitasya ca, bhāgaḥ jīvaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ iti; ārāgra-mātraḥ hi avaraḥ api dṛṣṭaḥ iti ca \| ataḥ aṇu-parimāṇa-ātmā \|\|23\|\|

atha syāt—ātmanaḥ aṇutve sakalā-śarīra-vyāpinī vedanā na upapadyata iti; tat matāntareṇa pariharam āha—

## \|\| 2.3.24 \|\|

*avirodhaḥ candana-vat*

**Комментарий:**

yathā hari-candana-bindur deha-eka-deśa-vartī api sakala-dehaṃ vyāpinīm āhlādaṃ janayati, tadvat ātmā api deha-eka-deśa-vartī sakala-deśa-vartinīṃ vedanām anubhavati \|\|24\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.25 \|\|

*avasthiti-viśeṣyāt iti cet na abhyupagamāt hṛdi hi*

**Комментарий:**

hari-candana-bindoḥ aikadeśa-viśeṣa-vasthiti-viśeṣāt tasyā abhāvaḥ; ātmanas tu tatra vidyata iti cet; ātmanaḥ api deha-deśa-viśeṣe sthiti-abhyupagamāt \| hṛdaya-deśe hi ātmanaḥ sthitiḥ śrūyate hṛdi hi ayam ātmā tat-saṃkrānto nāḍībhiḥ iti; tathā kaṇṭham āmeti pratyak yogyaṃ vijñānamayaḥ prāṇeṣu hṛd-antar-jyotiḥ iti \| ātmano deha-viśeṣa-sthiti-apekṣayā candana-dṛṣṭāntaḥ pradarśitaḥ; na tu candanasya deśa-viśeṣa-apekṣā \|\|25\|\|

eka-deśa-vartinaḥ sakala-deha-vyāpi-kārya-karatva-avakāśaṃ sva-matena āha—

## \|\| 2.3.26 \|\|

*guṇāt vā āloka-vat*

**Комментарий:**

bāla-stho maṇis tat-sva-prabhā-cayaḥ \| ātmā sva-guṇena jñānena sakala-dehaṃ vyāpya avasthitaḥ; āloka-vat—yathā maṇi-prabhā-maṇi-bhūtānibhikadeśa-vartinām ālokena eka-deśa-vyāpī hṛdyate, tadvat hṛdaya-sthasya ātmanaḥ jñānaṃ sakala-dehaṃ vyāpya vartate \| jñātuḥ prabhā-sthānīyasya jñānasya svābhāvikatvaṃ vṛtti-nimitta-prabhavatva-vacchata iti prathama-sūtre sthāpitam \|\|26\|\|

nanu uktaṃ jñāna-mātre caitanya iti; tat kathaṃ jñānasya svarūpa-vyatirikta-guṇatvam ucyate; tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.3.27 \|\|

*vyatirekaḥ gandha-vat tathā ca darśayati*

**Комментарий:**

yathā pṛthivyāḥ gandhasya guṇatvena upalabdhamānasya tato vyatirekaḥ, tathā jñānāgati jñātṛ-guṇatvena pratīyamānasya jñānasya ātmanaḥ vyatireka-siddhaḥ \| darśayati ca śrutiḥ jñātāradevāyaṃ puruṣa iti \|\|27\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.28 \|\|

*pṛthak-upadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

svarūpaś ca itva vijñānaṃ vijñātuḥ pṛthak upadiśyate na hi vijñātuḥ vijñāteḥ viparilopo vidyate iti \|\|28\|\|

yad uktaṃ yo vijñāne tiṣṭhan vijñānaṃ yaṃ tanute jñāna-svarūpam avantar-nirmalam ityādiṣu jñānam eva ātmā iti vyapadiśyata iti, tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.3.29 \|\|

*tad-viguṇa-sāravāt tu tad-vyapadeśaḥ prājña-vat*

**Комментарий:**

tu-śabdaḥ codyaṃ vyāvartayati \| tad-viguṇa-sāravāt—vijñāna-guṇa-sāravāt ātmanaḥ vijñānam iti vyapadeśaḥ \| vijñānam eva asya sāra-rūpaḥ guṇaḥ; yathā prājñasya ānanda-sāra-rūpaḥ guṇaḥ iti prājñaḥ ānanda-śabdena vyapadiśyate—yad eṣa ākāśa ānandaḥ na syāt ānandaḥ brahma iti vyajānāt iti \| prājñasya hi ānanda-sāra-bhūtaḥ guṇaḥ—sa ekaḥ brahmaṇa ānandaḥ ānandaṃ brahmaṇaḥ vidvān, na bibheti kutaḥ-cana iti \| yathā vā satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti vipascitaḥ prājñasya jñāna-śabdena vyapadeśaḥ \| saha brahmaṇā vipaścitā iti yaḥ sarva-jñaḥ iti-ādiṣu prājñasya jñānaṃ sāra-bhūtaḥ guṇaḥ iti vijñāyate \|\| 29 \|\|

## \|\| 2.3.30 \|\|

*yāvad-ātma-sambhāvitvāt ca na doṣaḥ tad-darśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

vijñānasya yāvad-ātma-sambhāvitvāt iha tad-vyapadeśaḥ na doṣaḥ; tathā ca khaṇḍatva-yāvacchūlatva-bhāvitva-goḥ vādi-vyapadeśena goḥ iti vyapadiśyamānā hṛṣyante; svarūpa-rūpa-dharmatva-hṛdayaḥ \| ca-kārāt jñāna-vattamaḥ api sva-prakāśatvena vijñānam iti vyapadeśaḥ na doṣaḥ iti samuccinoti \|\| 30 \|\|

yat ca uktaṃ suguptvādi-gu jñāna-svabhāvāt jñānasya na svarūpānubandhi-dharmatvam iti, tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.3.31 \|\|

*puṃstvādi-vat tvasya sataḥ abhivyakti-yogāt*

**Комментарий:**

tu-śabdaḥ ca āhitāśaṅkā-nivṛtti-arthaḥ \| ayam—jñānasya suguptvādau api vidyamānasya jāgaritva-abhivyakta-jñāna-saṃsarga-abhāvāt svarūpānubandhi-dharmatva-niṣopattiḥ \| puṃstvādi-vat—yathā puṃstvādiṣṭha-sādhāraṇasya dhātau cet-vyāvasthāyāṃ sataḥ api anabhivyaktasya yuvater vibhaktau puṃskṛtvattā na kadācit nivartako bhavati \| samabhyupagataṃ hi śarīrasya svarūpānubandhi—etaṃ ṣaṇḍhaṃ tri-palaṃ dvi-pādi catur-vidha-āhāramayaṃ śarīram iti śarīra-svarūpa-vyapadeśāt \| suguptvādi-cidvadarthyaḥ prakāśata iti pratipoktam \| tasya vidyamānasya jñānasya viṣaya-gocarattvaṃ jāgar-ādiṣu upalabhyate \| ca ete ca ātmanaḥ jñātṛtvādayaḥ dharmāḥ prāk eva upapāditāḥ \| ataḥ jñātṛtvam eva jīvātmanaḥ svarūpam \| saḥ ca ayam ātmā aṇu-purisarāṇaḥ \| na pretya saṃjñā asti iti iti etat api na muktasya jñāna-abhāvaḥ ucyate; api tu etebhyaḥ bhūtebhyaḥ samutthāya tāni eva anuvinaśyati iti iti saṃsāra-daśāyāṃ yad-bhūta-vṛddhi-vyatirikta-prayuktaṃ janma-nāśādi-ka-darśanam, tat muktasya na vidyate; na paśyaḥ mṛtyuṃ paśyati na rogaṃ, na uta duḥkhatām \| sarvaṃ ha paśyaḥ paśyati sarvam āpnoti sarvaśaḥ \|\| na upajanaṃ smaran idaṃ śarīram saṃsaṃcāp kāmān paśyan ramate iti-ādi-śruti-ukty-abhyupati \|\| 31 \|\|

saṃśṛti-jñāna-samavāde tasya sarvāgatatve dṛṣṭapramāhuḥ—

## \|\| 2.3.32 \|\|

*nitya-upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ anyatara-niyamaḥ vā anyathā*

**Комментарий:**

anyathā—sarvāgatatva-pakṣe tasya jñāna-samānatva-pakṣe ca nitya-upalabdhi-anupalabdhī saheta prasajyeyātām; anyatara-niyamaḥ vā—upalabdhiḥ eva vā nityaṃ syāt, anupalabdhiḥ eva vā \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—loke tāvad-vartamānayoḥ ātmopalabdhi-anupalabdhyoḥ ayaṃ jñānātmā sarva-gataḥ hetuḥ asti yataḥ; upalabdhiḥ eva vā, anupalabdhiḥ eva vā \| ubhaya-hetutve sarvadā sarvatra api prasajyeta \| yat upalabdhiḥ eva, sarvasya sarvadā sarvatra anupalambhaḥ na syāt \| atha anupalabdhiḥ eva, sarvadā sarvatra apalabdhiḥ na syāt iti \| asmākaṃ śarīra-sthānatad-evānityatvād āmanasa-saṃyoga-upalabdhi-nimittā iti vyavasthā-siddhiḥ \| karaṇa-mano-palabdhaiḥ api sarveṣām ātmānāṃ sarvāgatatvena sarvaiḥ karaṇaiḥ sambandhāt saṃyuktatvāt dṛṣṭādeḥ api aniyama-kṛta-doṣa-sāmānam \|\| 32 \|\| iti tṛtīya-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha kartṛ-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.3.33 \|\|

*kartā śāstra-arthavattvāt*

**Комментарий:**

ayam ātmā jñātā, sa vāṇādi-śravaṇa-icchukaḥ; idānīṃ kiṃ sa eva kartā; uta sva-guṇa-kartṛtva-sacetanānāṃ guṇānāṃ kartṛtvam ātmani adhyasyati iti cintyate \| kim atra yuktam? akartṛtvam iti \| kutaḥ? ātmanaḥ hi akartṛtvaṃ, guṇānām eva ca kartṛtvam adhyātma-śāstreṣu eva śrūyate \| tathā hi kaṭha-vallīṣu jīvasyainaṃ jāyate mriyate iti-ādinā janma-jarā-maraṇa-ādikaṃ sarvaṃ prakṛti-dharmaṃ pratiṣidhya hantṛtva-viṣayāmukriyāsu kartṛtvam api pratiṣidhyate—hantā cet manyate hantum hataḥ cet manyate hatam \| ubhau tau na vijānīto na ayaṃ hanti na hanyate \|\| iti \| hantāram ātmānaṃ jñātvā jāgatādhyātma-samarpitārthaḥ \| tathā ca bhagavatā svayam eva jīvasya akartṛtvaṃ svarūpa-kṛta-kartṛtva-abhimānaḥ tu vyāmohaḥ iti ucyate—prakṛteḥ kriyamāṇāni guṇaiḥ karmāṇi sarvaśaḥ \| ahaṅkāra-vimūḍhātmā kartā aham iti manyate \|\| na anyaṃ guṇebhyaḥ kartāraṃ yadā draṣṭā anupaśyati kārya-kāraṇa-kartṛtve hetuḥ prakṛtiḥ ucyate \| puruṣaḥ sukha-duḥkhānāṃ bhoktṛtve hetuḥ ucyate \|\| iti ca \| ataḥ puruṣasya bhoktṛtvam eva, prakṛteḥ eva tu kartṛtvam iti \|\| evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—kartā śāstra-arthavattvāt iti \| ātmā eva kartā; na guṇāḥ \| katham? śāstra-arthavattvāt \| śāstrāṇi hi yajne ta svargakāmaḥ; mumukṣurbopāsītetyavamādīni svarga-mokṣa-ādi-niratasya bhoktā eva kartṛtve niyuñjate \| na hi acetanasya kartṛtve dehyo niyujyate \| śāstra-ākhyaṃ śāsanaṃ ca pravartanam; śāstrasya ca pravartakatvaṃ bodhya-jana-dvāreṇa; acetanaṃ ca pradhānaṃ na bodhayituṃ śakyam \| ataḥ śāstrāṇām arthavattvaṃ bhoktaṛvyacetanasya eva kartṛtve bhavet \|

tat uktaṃ śāstra-phalaṃ prayoktar iti \| yat uktaṃ hṛtvā devāsaye ity-ādinā hanta-kriyāyām asmat-kartṛtvam ātmanaḥ śrūyate iti; tat-samāna-nirdeśena hantavyatva-abhāvāt ucyate \| yac ca pratītiḥ kriyamāṇāni ity-ādinā guṇānām eva kartṛtvaṃ smaryata iti; tatra sāttvika-pravṛtti-kartṛtvāt sattva-rajas-tamo-guṇa-saṃjñakāḥ; na svarūpa-pracyutiḥ iti prādhānya-avivakṣeṇa guṇānām eva kartṛtvam ucyate \| tathā ca tatra eva ucyate kāraṇaṃ guṇa-saṅgaḥ asya sad-asad-yoni-janmasu iti \| tathā tatra eva ātmanaḥ kartṛtvam ucyate tatra eva sati kartāram ātmānaṃ kevalaṃ tu yaḥ \| paśyati akṛta-buddhitvāt sa paśyati durmatiḥ \|\| iti \| adhiṣṭhānaṃ tathā kartā karaṇaṃ ca pṛthag-vidham \| vividhā ca pṛthak ceṣṭā daivaṃ ca eva atra pañcamam \|\| iti adhiṣṭhāna-ādi-daiva-yatnata-sāpekṣe sati ātmanaḥ kartṛtve yaḥ ātmānam eva kevalaṃ kartāraṃ manyate, na sa paśyati ity-arthaḥ \|\|33\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.34 \|\|

*upādānāt vihāra-upadeśāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

sa yathā mahā-rājaḥ iti prakṛtya, evam eva etat ātman prāṇān gṛhītvā sve śarīre yathā-kāmaṃ parivartate iti prāṇānām upādāne vihāre ca kartṛtvam upadiśyate \|\|34\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.35 \|\|

*vyapadeśāt ca kriyāyāṃ na cet nirdeśa-viparyayaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanute, karmāṇi tanute api ca iti laukika-vaidika-kriyāsu kartṛtva-vyapadeśāt ca kartā \| vijñāna-śabdena na ātmanaḥ vyapadeśaḥ; api tu anantaḥkaraṇasya buddhiḥ iti cet; evaṃ sati nirdeśa-viparyayasya syāt—buddheḥ karaṇatvāt vijñāna iti karaṇa-vibhakti-nirdeśasya syāt \|\|35\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.36 \|\|

*upalabdhivat aniyamaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

ātmanaḥ kartṛtve doṣa ucyate \| yathā asya ātmanaḥ viśuddhe nitya-upalabdhṛtvam upalabdha-prasaṅga ity-ādinā upalabdheḥ niyama uktaḥ; tathā ātmanaḥ kartṛtve prakṛteḥ ca kartṛtve tasya sarva-puruṣa-sādhāraṇatvāt sarvāṇi karmāṇi sarveṣāṃ bhogāya syuḥ; na eva vā kasya cit \| ātmanaḥ viśuddhatva-yugapat-sarva-siddhānām api sarveṣām aviśiṣṭam \| ata eva śāntaḥkaraṇa-hīnānām api niyamaḥ na upapadyate, yat āyattā vyavasthā syāt \|\|36\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.37 \|\|

*śakti-viparyayāt*

**Комментарий:**

buddheḥ kartṛtve kartṛtvasya bhoktṛtvāt upapatteḥ moktṛtva-kartṛtvam api tasya eva syāt ity ātmanaḥ bhoktṛtva-śrutiḥ hīyeta \| bhoktṛtvaṃ ca buddheḥ eva saṃbhavata iti ātma-sambandhe pramāṇa-abhāvaḥ ca syāt \| puruṣaḥ asti bhoktṛ-bhāvāt iti hi teṣām abhyupagamaḥ \|\|37\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.38 \|\|

*samādhi-abhāvāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

buddheḥ kartṛtve mokṣa-sādhana-bhūta-samādhy-ādir devaṃ kartā syāt \| sa ca samādhiḥ prakṛteḥ svātma-atyanta-vaikaṭyam; na ca prakṛteḥ tayoḥ smṛtiḥ prakṛti-samādhānulam \| ataḥ api ātmānaṃ va kartā \|\| 38 \|\|

nanu vā asmataḥ kartṛtveṣy upapadyamāne sarvadā kartṛtvaṃ, sa uparame tasyāha—

## \|\| 2.3.39 \|\|

*yathā ca takṣā ubhayathā*

**Комментарий:**

vasv-adhikāra-sampad-udyamā yad eva icchati, tadā karoti; yadā tu na icchati, tadā na karoti \| yathā takṣā vāsī-ādi-karaṇa-sannidhāne api icchā-anuguṇyena karoti, na karoti ca \| buddhes tattvetanīyāḥ kartṛtve tasyāḥ śāstrīyā-icchā-viṣaya-prakaraṇa-abhāvāt sambandhā kartṛtvam eva syāt \|\| 39 \|\| iti kartr-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

**atha parāyatta-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.3.40 \|\|

*parāt tu tat-śruteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

idaṃ jīvasya kartṛtvaṃ kiṃ svātantryeṇa, uta paramātma-āyattam iti \| kiṃ prāptam? svātantryeṇeti \| paramātma-āyattatve hi vidhi-niṣedha-śāstra-anarthakyaṃ prasajyeta \| yaḥ hi sva-buddhyā pravṛtti-nivṛtti-ārambhakaḥ; sa eva niyojyo bhavati \| ataḥ svātantryeṇa asya kartṛtvaṃ \|\| iti prāpte abhidhīyate—parāt tu tat-śruteḥ iti \| tumadoḥ pakṣe vyatyayaḥ smṛtiḥ; tataḥ ca kartṛtvaṃ asya jīvasya parātu—paramātmana eva hetoḥ bhavati \| kutaḥ? śruteḥ—antaḥ praviṣṭa-śāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā śraya ātmāni tiṣṭhan yasma ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ ya ātmānam antaro

yamayati sa ta ātmā-antaryāmy-amṛtaḥ iti \| smṛtir api sarvasya ca ahaṃ hṛdi sanniviṣṭho mattaḥ smṛtiḥ jñānam apohaṃ ca īśvaraḥ sarva-bhūtānāṃ hṛd-deśe arjuna tiṣṭhati \| bhrāmayan sarva-bhūtāni yantra-ārūḍhāni māyayā \|\| iti \|\| 40 \|\|

nanv evaṃ vidhi-niṣedha-śāstra-anarthakyaṃ prasajyetety uktam; tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.3.41 \|\|

*kṛta-prayatna-apekṣas tu vihita-pratiṣiddha-avaiyarthya-ādibhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

sarveṣu kriyāsu puruṣeṇa kṛtaṃ prayatnam—udyogam apekṣya antaryāmī paramātmā tat-anumati-dānena pravartayati \| paramātma-anumati-mantareṇa asya pravṛtti-nivṛttau hi ayogyaḥ \| kutaḥ evam? vihita-pratiṣiddha-avaiyarthya-ādibhyaḥ—adhyātma-cetanā-prahitatvāt vacanebhyo gamyate \| yathā dyoti-sādhāraṇe

dharme para-sattvāpādana-samyag-avagama-nimittatvena lopa-sakheḥ; athāpi doṣānumateḥ snehena hatam iti tat-kālaṃ svargam eva bhavati \| pāpa-karmasu nirvarta-niṣṭa-kāryasya api anumantṛtvaṃ na nirdeśyatvāvahaṃtīti sādhu-samyag-jñāna-rūpeṇa pratipāditam \| na ca eṣa puṇyaḥ svayaṃ sādhu karma kārayati taṃ yamobhyo lokebhya unninīṣati evam eva asādhu karma kārayati taṃ yam adho ninīṣati iti ukta-nitya-sādhvīni-tīcyā ca svayam eva sādhya-sādhuṇe karmaṇā kārayati iti etad-doṣa-padye \| ucyate—puruṣaḥ sarva-sādhāraṇaḥ, yat-svīkṛti-saṃmātra-parama-puruṣārtha-kṛtye vyavasthitaḥ pravartate; tam anugṛhṇan bhagavān svayam eva sva-prāpty-upāya-vitati-kalyāṇeṣu karmasu eva ruciṃ janayati \| yathā ati-mātrātikūlye vyavasthitaḥ pravartate taṃ nigṛhṇan sva-prāpti-virodhiṣu adho-gati-sādhaneṣu karmasu rājayati \| yathoktaṃ bhagavatā svayam eva—ahaṃ sarvasya prabhavo mattas sarvaṃ pravartate \| iti matvā bhajante māṃ budhā bhāva-samanvitāḥ \|\| icchā-dveṣaḥ śreyaḥ satyā-yuktānāṃ bhajatāṃ prīti-pūrvakam \| dadāmi buddhi-yogaṃ taṃ yena mām upayānti te \|\| teṣām eva anukampārtham aham ajñāna-jaṃ tamaḥ \| nāśayāmi ātma-bhāva-stho jñāna-dīpena bhāsvatā \|\| iti \| tathā asad-grāha-matibhiḥ te jagad āhuḥ anīśvaram iti-ādi, tān ahaṃ dviṣataḥ krūrān saṃsāreṣu nara-adhamān \| kṣipāmi ajasram aśubhān āsurīṣu eva yoniṣu \|\| iti uktaṃ \|\| 41 \|\| iti parāyatta-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha aṃśa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.3.42 \|\|

*aṃśo nānā-vyapadeśād anyathā ca api dāśa-kitava-vāditvam adhīyata eke*

**Комментарий:**

jīvasya kartṛtvaṃ para-sukhāyatam iti uktam; idānīṃ kim ayaṃ jīvaḥ paramātma-dravyāntara-bhūtaḥ; uta parasya eva brahmaṇo bhrāntaḥ, uta brahma-viśeṣādhyavacchinnaḥ, atha brahmāṃśa iti saṃśayate \| śruti-vipratipattis saṃśayaḥ \| nanu tat tvam asi iti paramātma-bhāvāvabodha-viṣayaḥ; adhikaṃ tu bheda-nirdeśāt iti anyena mārgeṇa nirṇītaḥ siddhāntaḥ, sa eva nānātvaikyañcāśruti-vipratipattayā āhitya jīvasya brahma-śarīratvopādhānena viśeṣato nirṇīyate \| yāvad dhi jīvasya brahma-śarīratvaṃ na nirṇītam, tāvaj jīvasya brahmaṇaḥ anaanyatvaṃ, brahmaṇas tat-samādhikatvaṃ ca na pratitiṣṭhati \| kiṃ tāvat syām atra? atyanta-bhinna iti \| kutaḥ? jñājñau dvāv ajāv īśanīśau iti-ādi-bheda-nirdeśāt \| jñātreśvara-bheda-śrutyas tu, abhinnaṃ siddhaṃ dvitīya-brahma-vāda-pratimā-stānopapatti-viṣayaḥ \| brahmopādhyo jīva iti etad api na sādhīyaḥ, eka-śabdena eka-deśa-vācī hi śrūyate \| jīvasya brahma-eka-deśatve tad-dhṛtā doṣā brahmaṇi bhaveyuḥ \| na ca brahmaṇḍo jīva iti aṃśa-doṣa-śrutiḥ; kṣudra-nānā-hṛdayāśraya-brahmaṇaḥ, prājñasya eva prabhū-śabdaḥ \| tasmād atyanta-bhinnasya ca tat-saṃsthaṃ duṣṭopapattim \| yathā śāstraṃ brahma eva jīvaḥ, kutaḥ ?

tat-śaktiḥ ayam ātmā brahma iti-ādi-brahmātma-bhāva-upadeśāt \| nānātvavat tv iha tu pratyakṣādi-siddhārtha-anuvādi-darśanāni yathā-sthūla-hetv-adeśa-parābhiḥ śrutibhiḥ pratyakṣādy-iṣṭāvidyā-nivṛttitaḥ sthāpyante \| athavā brahmaṇo vā aṃśa-mātraṃ pāda-cchittraṃ jīvaḥ \| kutaḥ? tata eva brahmātma-bhāva-upadeśāt \| na ca amukha-artha-prādhānyena upakalpita iti vaktuṃ śakyam, bandha-mokṣādi-vyavasthā-anupapatter iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte ’bhidhīyate brahma-aṃśaḥ iti \| kutaḥ? nānā-vyapadeśād, anyathā ca—ekatvena vyapadeśāt \| ubhayathā hi vyapadeśo dṛśyate \| nānātvavyapadeśaḥ tāvat spaṣṭa-laghucittva-acinityatva-niyāmyatva-satyatva-svādhīnatva-parādhīnatva-śuddhatvāśuddhatva-kalyāṇa-guṇākaratva-vidyāpara-tattva-patitva-śokādi-vinirmitiḥ śrūyate \| anyathā ca abhedena vyapadeśaḥ api tat-śaktiḥ ayam ātmā brahma iti-ādibhiḥ śrūyate \| api dārśanikatvādi-sambandhi yat eke—brahma-dāśā brahma-dāsā brahma ime kitavāḥ iti ātharvaṇikā brahmaṇo dārśanikatvādi-sambandhi adhīyate \| tatra sarva-jīva-vyatirekeṇa abhede vyapadiśyata ity arthaḥ \| evam ubhaya-vyapadeśa-mukhyatvā-siddhaye jīva-aṃśaḥ brahmaṇaḥ iti aṃśi-upagantavyam \| na ca bheda-vyapadeśānāṃ pratyakṣādi-siddhatva-devatā-mukhyādi-sadṛśam; brahmātma-vyakta-viśramāyatana-cchāyīratva-cchaitatvat-sarva-dhāratvat-satyatvat-sarīratvena anupāsakaṃ tat-tvena prasādita-dharmārtha-kāma-mokṣa-rūpa-phalārthi-bhāvavādy-aprakṛtatvāt atra jīva-brahmaṇoḥ bhedaḥ pratyakṣādy-agocara-devatā-ananyathā-siddhaḥ \| ato na

jāgara-svapnādi-vādinīnāṃ pramāṇāntara-siddha-bheda-anuvādena mithyārtha-upadeśa-paratvam \| na ca akhaṇḍa-ekarasa-cin-mātra-svarūpeṇa brahmaṇā ārambha-noḍdra-bhāva-tu-sādhānas, bahu-bhavana-saṅkalpa-pūrvakaṃ viyat-tvi-vṛddhi, jīva-bhāvena tat-tat-praveśaṃ, vivicira-nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇaṃ, tad-ahāntatva-viṣaya-anubhava-nimita-sukha-dduḥkha-śāritvam, abhībhūtvena tatra sthitvā tat-niyamanetarasyāmitatvaṃ, jīva-bhūtasya svasya kāraṇa-

brahmātma-bhāva-anusandhānaṃ, saṃsāra-mokṣaṃ, tad-upadeśāṃś ca kurvījet iti vaktavyam iti upadiśyate; tathā sati unmatta-pralapita-vāk-patāt \| upādhi-avicchinnaṃ brahma jīvaḥ iti api na sādhīyaḥ; pūrvokta-advaita-niyantṛtva-niyāmyatvādi-vyapadeśa-bādhāt eva \| na hi devadattāt eka eva gṛhāt ca pādyaṃ devāpriyanta-nigamana-sthāna-artha-bhedaḥ \| ataḥ ubhaya-vyapadeśa-upapattaye jīva-aṃśaḥ brahmaṇaḥ iti abhyupeyam \|\|43\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.43 \|\|

*mantra-varṇāt*

**Комментарий:**

pādaḥ asya viśvā bhūtāni tri-pādasya amṛtaṃ divi iti mantra-varṇāt ca brahma-aṃśaḥ jīvaḥ \| aṃśa-vācaḥ hi pāda-śabdaḥ \| viśvā bhūtāni iti jīvānāṃ bahutvābahu-bhavanam mantre \| sūtre ’pi aṃśaḥ iti eka-vacanaṃ jāti-abhiprāyam; na ātmā śruteḥ iti ācārya-eka-vacanaṃ jāti-abhiprāyam; nityaḥ nityānāṃ cetanaḥ cetanānām ekaḥ bahūnāṃ yaḥ vidadhāti kāmān iti-ādi-śrutiṣu īśvarād dehātmanoḥ

bahutva-niyatva-yathāśrāyi-vidhiyamānatvāt \| evaṃ nityānām āśramāṇāṃ bahutve śrāmaṇādhike sati jñāna-rūpatvena sarveṣām e karūpatve 'pi bhedaka-ākāraḥ ātma-yāthātmya-vedana-kṣamair avagamyate \| akṣarateśrāvaṇa-nikaraḥ iti ānantaryam eva cāturmāhūtyaṃ vakṣyati \|\|43\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.44 \|\|

*api smaryate*

**Комментарий:**

mama eva aṃśo jīva-loke jīva-bhūtaḥ sanātanaḥ iti jīvasya puruṣāṃśatvaṃ smaryate; atakṣāyāṃśaḥ \|\|44\|\|

aṃśatve 'pi jīvasya brahma-eka-deśatvena jīvagatā doṣā brahmaṇy eva iti āśaṅkyāha—

## \|\| 2.3.45 \|\|

*prakāśādivat tu na evaṃ paraḥ*

**Комментарий:**

tu-śabdaś codyaṃ vyāvartayati \| prakāśādivat jīvaḥ paramātmano 'ṃśaḥ, yathā maṇy-ādity-ādibhis sthito bhāsraḥ prakāśo 'ṃśo bhavati, yathā navāṃśu-kalagṇānāṃ go-stana-divitiṣṭhānāṃ vastūnāṃ gauratvādīni viśeṣaṇāni aṃśāḥ; yathā vā dehino deva-manuṣy-ādi-dehe 'ṃśaḥ; tadvat \| eka-vastu-aika-deśatvaṃ hi aṃśatvam \| viśiṣṭasya ekasya vastuno viśeṣaṇam aṃśa eva \| tathā ca vivekakāḥ viviktaṃ vastuni viśeṣaṇāṃśo 'yam, viśeṣyāṃśo 'yam iti vyapadiśanti \| viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣyayor aṃśitve 'pi svabhāva-vailakṣaṇyaṃ dṛśyate \| evaṃ jīva-prayogi-viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣyayor aṃśitvaṃ, svabhāva-bhedo 'papadyate \| tad idam ucyate—na evaṃ para iti \| yathā-bhūto jīvaḥ, na tathā-bhūtaḥ paraḥ \| yaṃ ca, hi prameyaḥ pramāṇāntara-yābhūtaḥ tathā pramāṇasyopāttaṃ svāṃśa-jīvāṃśī parocayati-niścaya-bhūta ity arthaḥ \| evaṃ jīva-prayogi-viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-vakṛtaṃ svabhāva-vailakṣaṇyam āśritya bheda-nirdeśāḥ pravartante; abheda-nirdeśās tu pṛthag-avasthānārha-viśeṣaṇānāṃ viśeṣya-paryantatvam āśritya mukhyayā eva upapadyante \| tat tvam asi ayam ātmā brahma iti-ādiṣu, tat-śabda-braḥma-śabdavat tvam-padam ātmā iti-śabdā api, jīvāvacchārīraka-brahma-vācakatvena sādhita-vākyārtham adhikāritavād ity asamarthaḥ prāg eva prapañcitaḥ \|\|45\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.46 \|\|

*smaranti ca*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ pramā-pramātṛtveṇa śakti-sthiti-mūtreṇa śarīrātmatvena ca aṃśāṃśi-bhāvaṃ jagad-brahmaṇoḥ parāśarādayaḥ smaranti ekaika-sthitasya yāneyeṃnā vistāriṇī yathā \| parasya brahmaṇaḥ śaktiḥ tathā idam akhilaṃ jagat \|\| viṣṇu-sthitaṃ yatra sarvam utam vai dvija \| tasya sṛjyasya saṃbhūto tat sarvaṃ vai hareḥ tanuḥ \|\| ity-ādinā \| vakṣārājuṣṭatyo 'pi—asya ātmā śarīram iti-ādinā ātma-śarīra-bhāvenāṃśāṃśitvaṃ vadanti iti ucyate \|\|46\|\|

evaṃ brahmaṇo 'kartve, brahma-pravartakatve, sattve ca sarveṣāṃ samāne, keṣāñcid avādhyapratyayanatva-danuṣṭhātṛtva-duḥkhā, keṣāñcit svargi-hāraḥ, keṣāñcid artha-anartha-anugatṛtvā, keṣāñcit parihāraś ca, śāstreṣu katham upapadyata iti āśaṅkyāha—

## \|\| 2.3.47 \|\|

*anuśāpa-parihārau deha-saṃbandhāt jyotir-ādi-vat*

**Комментарий:**

sarveṣāṃ brahma-upāsanādinā eka-rūpye saty api brāhmaṇa-kṣatriya-vaiśya-śūdra-ādi-rūpa-uccyāvaci-deha-saṃbandha-nibandhana-anujñā-parihārāḥ ca upapadyante \| jyotir-ādi-vat—yathā agner nimitta-naika-rūpatve api śrotriya-āgārāt nirarhriyate; śmaśānāt hetoḥ parihriyate; yathā ca atra ādi śrotriyādeḥ śunā spṛśyate; abhimataḥ tu ahetoḥ parihriyate \|\|47\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.48 \|\|

*asantateḥ ca avyatikaraḥ*

**Комментарий:**

brahma-upāsanādinā eka-rūpye saty api jīvānām madhyocca-bhedāvadyutena prati-sārīraṃ bhinnatvāt ca bhoga-vyatikaraḥ api na bhavati \| śānta-brahma-jīva-vāde ca upahita-brahma-jīva-vāde ca jīva-prayojīvatāṃ ca bhoga-vyatikarāt ayam eva doṣaḥ santi iti abhiprāyeṇa sva-pakṣe bhoga-vyatikara-abhāva uktaḥ \|\|48\|\|

nanu śānta-brahma-jīva-vāde api avidyā-kṛta-upādhi-bhedāt doṣam adhyasyatvāt upapadyate; ata āha—

## \|\| 2.3.49 \|\|

*ābhāsaḥ eva ca*

**Комментарий:**

akhaṇḍa-eka-rasa-prakāśa-mātra-svarūpasya svarūpa-tirodhāna-pūrvaka-upādhi-bheda-upapādana-hetuḥ ābhāsaḥ eva \| prakāśa-eka-rūpasya prakāśa-tirodhānaṃ prakāśatā apayati iti prāk eva upapāditam \| ābhāsāḥ eva iti vā pāṭhaḥ; tathā sati haitavo abhāsāḥ; cakārāt dharmātmānaṃ prerītāraṃ ca matvā jñau dvau ajo hī tapo asyāḥ niṣkalaṃ svādātni iti-ādi-sthiti-virodhaḥ ca \| avidyā-upādhi-kalpita-upādhi-bhede api sarva-jñādi-niṣedha-viruddha-svaccha-īśvaratva-kalpanam bhoga-vyatikara-vat tadvat asya eva \|\|49\|\|

pāramārthika-upādhi-upahita-brahma-jīva-vāde api upādhi-bhede hetu-bhūta-anādhyadhyavasthā bhaviṣyatī iti āśaṅkya āha—

## \|\| 2.3.50 \|\|

*adṛṣṭa-aniyamāt*

**Комментарий:**

upādhi-svarūpa-hetu-bhūtātayā dṛṣṭāyā api abrahma-svarūpa-kṣatvena niyama-hetu-abhāvāt tad-vyavasthānaṃ, upādhi-saṃbandhaḥ ca sva-saṃbandhena brahma-svarūpa-cheda-asaṃbhavāt \|\|50\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.51 \|\|

*abhisandhy-ādiṣu api ca evam*

**Комментарий:**

adṛṣṭa-hetu-bhūta-abhisandhy-ādi-jñapyatvā deva hetoḥ niyamaḥ eva \|\|51\|\|

## \|\| 2.3.52 \|\|

*pradeśa-bhedāt iti cet na antar-bhāvāt*

**Комментарий:**

madhyama ekena eva brahma-eka-rūpam, bāla-deśānāṃ nānātra-upādhi-niṣṭhatvaṃ vakṣyate; tathā api upādhi-saṃbaddha-viśva-brahma-pradeśa-ekatā ca upapadyata eva bhoga-vyavasthati ca iti cet; tathā, upādhīnāṃ tatra tatra gamanāt sarva-

pradeśānāṃ sarvāpidhyana-niṣevitu-kartṛtva-vyavasthā eva \| pradeśābhedena saṃbaddho 'pi sarvaṅga-brahma-pradeśa-gata-tat-tad-brahmopāṃvidhiḥ duḥkhaṃ brahmaṇa eva syāt \| pūrvaṃ śrinityopalabhyatvopalabdhṛmasaṃjñeyatvarnimayo vākyārthaḥ upalabdhṛtva-nīyamaḥ iti asmin sūtrāsyaṃ veda-bāhyānāṃ saṃgāta-jīvādīnāṃ doṣa uktaḥ; atra tu ābhāsa eva ca ityādi-sūtraiḥ vedānta-virodhinām ādhyātma-kurvādīnāṃ doṣa ucyate \|\| 52 \|\| iti aṃśādhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye dvitīyasya adhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**dvitīyādhyāye—caturthaḥ pādaḥ—prāṇotpattaya-dhikaraṇam \|\|**

## Pada 4

## \|\| 2.4.1 \|\|

*tathā prāṇāḥ*

**Комментарий:**

brahma-vyatiriktasya viyad-ādeḥ kṛtsnasya kāryatvena upāttatvāt ayaṃ jīvasya kāryatve 'pi svarūpa-anyathā-bhāva-lakṣaṇa-utpattiḥ na adhitā \| tat-prasaṅgena jīva-svarūpaṃ śodhitam \| saṃprati jīva-upakaraṇānām indriyāṇāṃ prāṇasya ca utpatti-ādi-prakāro viśodhyate \| tatra kim indriyāṇāṃ kāryatvaṃ jīvavat; uta viyad-ādi-vat iti cintyate \| kiṃ yuktam ? jīvavat eva iti pūrvapakṣī tathā prāṇāḥ iti \| prāṇāḥ—indriyāṇi \| yathā jīvo na utpadyate; tathā indriyāṇi api na utpadyante \| kutaḥ ? śruteḥ \| yathā jīvasya anutpattiḥ śruteḥ eva gamyate; tathā prāṇānām api anuṣpattiḥ śruteḥ eva avagamyate \| tathā prāṇā iti pramāṇam api anirdiśyate \| kā punaś ca śrutiḥ ? asad vā idam agre āsīt tasyaḥ kiṃ tad āsīti śraddhayo vā va te agre medhāvīti tasyaḥ ke te śraddhaya iti prāṇā vā va śraddhayaḥ iti jagad-utpatteḥ prāk indriyāṇāṃ sad-bhāvaḥ śrūyate \| prāṇa-śabde bahuvacanāt indriyāṇi eva iti niścīyate \| na ca śrutiḥ vāyuḥ antarikṣaṃ caitanyam rathāntarīnatā devatā yad āhuḥ iti vicitra-kāla-avayava-artha-viśeṣeṇa pariṇatuṃ śakyā, asad vā idam agre āsīt iti kṛtsna-prapañca-pralaya-velāyām avyavasthitatva-abhāvāt \| udgīthādi-vyavahṛ jīvotpatti-ādīnāṃ ca dṛṣṭa naikyā iti \|\| evaṃ prāpte 'bhidhīyate—viyad-ādi-vat eva prāṇāś ca utpadyante \| kutaḥ ? sad eva itad agre āsīt ātmā vā idam eka eva agre āsīd ityādi-kha prākṣṭhve vaikārāṇāṃ, punas tmājāyate prāṇo manas-sarvendriyāṇi ca iti indriya-prāṇam-utpatya-viśeṣaṇāṃ ca, prāg-avat-sthāna-saṃkhyā-vat \| na eṣāṃ sotpatti-vāda-vidhi-niṣedho sotpatti-vādaḥ pariṇetuṃ śakyaḥ, ātma-vṛttyeva-tritva-viśeṣa-mūrtīnāṃ nityatvavat sūtrāṃ vyādharṇanāt \| asad vā idam agre āsīt ityādi-vādyo 'pi prāṇāṇāṃ taṃdaṃ pariśeṣeṇa nirdiśyate, sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni prāṇam eva abhisaṃviśanti prāṇam abhyujjihate iti

prāṇāt ca paramātmasya api pratīteḥ \| śrotrā vāva prajñayā iti śarīra-indriyasya sarvasya tasmin eva prajñate; na tu etat nirdiśyate \|\| 1 \|\| prajñā; prāṇā iti ca bahu-vacana-anugatiḥ katham upapadyata iti cet, tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.4.2 \|\|

*gauṇya-saṃbhavāt tat-srāvaṇyas tat-prakṛteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

bahu-vacanasya nirgoṇī, brahma-artha-saṃbhavāt; tasya eva paramātmanaḥ śruteḥ prāṇa-sthāna-śruteḥ ca \|\| 2 \|\|

## \|\| 2.4.3 \|\|

*tat-pūrvakatvād vācaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

itthaṃ prāṇa-śabdaḥ paramātma-vacanaḥ; vācaḥ—paramātma-jyotis-rikta-viṣayasya nāmadheyasya vāk-viṣaya-bhūta-vijñātṛ-jīva-jñānakatvāt \| tat evaṃ tasya vyākṛtam āsīt tat nāma-rūpābhyāṃ vyākriyata iti nāma-rūpājyām abhivyañjanī vāṇo’nitya-kārya-bhāvāt ca tāni na santi iti arthaḥ \|\| 3 \|\| iti prāṇa-utpatti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha sapta-gati-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.4.4 \|\|

*sapta gater viśeṣitatvāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

tāni indriyāṇi kiṃ sapta eva syuḥ, atha ekādaśa iti cintyate \| śruti-vipratipatti-saṃśayaḥ \| kiṃ prāptam; sapta iti \| kutaḥ? gater viśeṣitatvāt ca \| hastau agṛhyamānena yriyamāṇena tu jīvena saha lokebhyaḥ samutkrāmanta eva hi ete tasmād ime lokā vepuś caranti prāṇā guhā-śayā nihitāḥ sapta-sapta iti \| boddhā puruṣaḥ ca abhiprāyaḥ \| viśeṣitāḥ ca te gati-mantaḥ prāṇāḥ saptatvataḥ yadā pañca avatiṣṭhante jñānāni manasā saha \| buddhiḥ ca na viceṣṭate tām āhuḥ paramāṃ gatim \|\| iti \| śarīra-anantara-saṃcaraṇaṃ vihāya mokṣa-ākhya-gamane paramā gatiḥ \| evaṃ jīvena saha janmāntara-gaṇe’samānām eva gati-śravaṇāt yoga-daśāyāṃ jñānāni iti viśeṣitatvāt ca jīvasya karaṇāni śrotra-tvag-akṣi-jihvā-ghrāṇāni uttamāni iti sarvaṃ veti gamyate \| yāni tu itarāṇi viṣayāṇāṃ grāhakatvena śrotre tvak rūhaḥ sapta vai śīrṣaṇyāḥ prāṇāḥ dvau avāñcau iti-ādiṣu caturdaśa-paryantāni prāṇa-pratipādaka-vākyeṣu vāk-pāṇi-pāda-pāyu-upastha-ahaṃkāra-citta-ākhyāni indriyāṇi pratīyante, teṣāṃ jīvena saha gati-śravaṇa-abhāvāt jīvastha-āśraya-āloka-kārakatva-mātreṇa upacārikāḥ prāṇa-śabda-ādeśāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

iti prāpte pracakṣmahe—

## \|\| 2.4.5 \|\|

*hasta-ādayaḥ tu sthite ataḥ na evam*

**Комментарий:**

na sarve va-indriyāṇi, api tu ekādaśa; hasta-ādīnām api śarīre sthite jīvaiḥ tasya bhoga-upakaraṇatvāt, kārya-bhedāt ca \| dṛśyate hi śrotra-ādīnām iva hasta-ādīnām api kārya-bhedaḥ ādāna-ādikaḥ; atas te api santi eva \| ataḥ na evam—ataḥ hasta-ādayaḥ na santi iti eva na mantavyam ity arthaḥ \| adhyavasāya-abhimāna-cintā-dhī-niṣedhānām eva buddhi-aṅkāra-citta-ākhyatve vyavasthāyām indriyatve api indriyāṇi \| ataḥ śruti-smṛtī puruṣe prāṇāḥ ātmana ekādaśa iti ātma-saṃbandhena manobhiḥ dhyāyate \| indriyāṇi daśa ekaṃ ca pakṣaḥ ca indriya-gocaraḥ \| etasmāt indriyāṇi udyena vaikārikaṃ daśa \| ekādaśaṃ manaḥ ca atra iti-ādiṣu nis-smṛti-siddha-indriya-saṃkhyā sthitā \| adhika-saṃkhyā-vādāḥ mano-buddhi-abhimātrāṇāṃ nyūnatva-upadeśāḥ ca tattva-tra vivakṣita-mānādi-kārya-viśeṣa-prayuktāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

iti samānya-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha prāṇa-aṇutva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.4.6 \|\|

*aṇavaḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

etat evaṃ sarve eva samāḥ sarva-jñatāḥ iti ānantya-śravaṇāt dheyatvaṃ prāṇānām; iti prāpte abhidhīyate—prāṇa-sūkṣmānatvaṃ sarve prāṇā aṇu-ākārāḥ ti na iti ukta-anyathā-ādi-śravaṇāt aparimitatve siddhe sati ukta-anyathā-ādiṣu pārvaṇena yugapat-vartamānatvāt ca prāṇāḥ \| anantatva-śrutiḥ tu atha yaḥ ha etān anantān upāste iti upāsana-śravaṇāt upāsya-prāṇa-viśeṣaṇa-bhūta-kārya-bāhulya-abhidhāyā \|\| 6 \|\|

## \|\| 2.4.7 \|\|

*śreṣṭhaḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

prāṇa-saṃvāde śarīra-sthiti-hetutvena śreṣṭhatayā nirṇītaḥ mukhya-prāṇaḥ anantya-vat ataḥ saṃkhyayā tebhyaḥ iti mahattva-avagame sva-kārya-bhūta-prāṇa-sthūla-asthūla-bhāvāt, etasmāt jāyate iti janma-śravaṇasya jīva-jarma-śravaṇavat upacare nīyate iti āśaṅkya prādhṛṣṭa-eka-kārya-ādhāraṇādi-virodhāt, etasmāt jāyate prāṇaḥ iti pṛthivī-ādi-vat sṛṣṭatva-śravaṇāt, utpatti-aniṣedha-bhāvāt ca, jāyate eva śreṣṭhaḥ prāṇaḥ iti ucyate \| anityatā-vāditu tu na jīve śreṣṭha-prāṇe abhipretya ucyate; api tu parasya brahmaṇaḥ ekasya eva vidyamānatvam ucyate, avatāritaḥ tasmāt eva prāṇāt \| pūrvaṃ ca tulya-nyāyatve api pṛthak-yojana-karaṇam uttara-cintā-artham \|\| 7 \|\|

iti prāṇa-aṇutva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha vāyu-kriyā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.4.8 \|\|

*na vāyu-kriye pṛthak-upadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

saḥ ayaṃ śreṣṭhaḥ prāṇaḥ kiṃ mahā-bhūta-dvitīya-pāyu-bhāvaḥ; tasya vā spanda-rūpa-kriyā; athavā vāyuḥ eva kaścana viśeṣaḥ prāṇaḥ ? iti vicārya, vāyuḥ eva iti prāptam, yaḥ prāṇassa vāyuḥ iti upadeśāt \| yathā vāyu-mātre prāṇatva-prasiddha-smaraṇāt, mukhyāsitiny-āśritāsu kriyāyāṃ prāṇa-śabda-prasiddheḥ tat-kriyaiva \|\| iti prāpte, na vāyu-mātram, na eva tat-kriyā iti ucyate \| kutaḥ ? pṛthak-upadeśāt—etasmāt jāyate prāṇaḥ manaḥ sarva-indriyāṇi ca, khaṃ vāyuḥ iti \| tata eva pṛthak-upadeśāt vāyu-kriyā api na bhavati prāṇaḥ; na hi tejaḥ-prabhṛtīnāṃ kriyā tebhyaḥ pṛthak ucyate iti upadiśyate \| yaḥ prāṇassa vāyuḥ iti tu vāyu-deva-avasthāntara-sapannaḥ prāṇaḥ; na tejaḥ-prabhṛti-vat tattvāntaratvam iti jñāpana-artham \| ucchvāsa-niśvāsa-ādau api prāṇaḥ spandana iti kriyāvati dravya eva prāṇa-śabda-prasiddhiḥ; na kriyā-mātre \|\| 8 \|\|

kim ayaṃ prāṇo vāyo-vikāraḥ sañcārin ivaḍa-bhūta-antaraṃ? na iti āha—

## \|\| 2.4.9 \|\|

*cakṣur-ādi-vat tu tat-saḥśiṣṭy-ādibhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

na ayaṃ bhūta-viśeṣaḥ; api tu cakṣur-ādi-vat jīva-upakharaṇa-viśeṣaḥ \| tattva-upakharaṇatvam upakaraṇa-bhūtaiḥ indriyaiḥ saha śiṣṭy-ādiṣu vyapadeśāt \| cakṣur-ādibhiḥ saha yadā prāṇaḥ śiṣyate prāṇa-saṃvāda-ādiṣu tatra jñātavyaṃ hi tat-saḥ śāsanaṃ yujyate \| prāṇa-śabda-parityāgaḥ api karaṇatvasya vijñāpana-artham abhidhāna-saṃvādene gṛhyate; atha ha ya etad vaid mukhyaḥ prāṇaḥ yaḥ ayaṃ madhyamaḥ prāṇaḥ iti ādiṣu vijñeyam abhidhānāt \|\| 9 \|\|

cakṣur-ādi-vat tathā api karaṇatve deha-dhāraṇe api jīvasya pratyupakāra-viśeṣa-rūpa-kriyāṃ pratipadyamaḥ; sā tu na dṛśyate; ataḥ na ayaṃ cakṣur-ādi-vat indriya-bhūta iti cet, tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.4.10 \|\|

*akaraṇatvāt ca na doṣaḥ tathā hi darśayati*

**Комментарий:**

akaraṇatvāt—karaṇaṃ kriyā, akriyatvāt, asya prāṇasya jīvaṃ prati upakāra-viśeṣa-rūpa-kriyā-rahitatvāt ca yaḥ doṣaḥ udbhāvyate, saḥ na asti; yata upakāra-viśeṣa-karaṃ śarīra-indriya-dhāraṇā-ādi-rūpaṃ kriyāṃ darśayati śrutiḥ—śreṣṭham anukrāmante, hṛdaṃ śarīraṃ pāpiṣṭhataraṃ iva dṛśyate sa vaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ iti uktvā vāk-ādi-utkramaṇe api śarīra-sthiti-prāṇāṃ ca sthitiṃ darśayitvā prāṇa-utkramaṇe śarīra-indriya-sthiti-abhidhānāt \| ataḥ prāṇānatdyānava eva ātma-bhāpakāreṇa pakṣmāśravat iti ataḥ ayaṃ prāṇaḥ śarīra-indriya-dhāraṇānvitā jīvasya upakrotena iti cakṣur-ādi-vat karaṇatvam \|\| 10 \|\|

madhye nāma-bheda-kārya-bhedāc ca prāṇāmānād rasatva-antarāṇi syuḥ; tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.4.11 \|\|

*pañca-vṛttir mano-vad vyapadiśyate*

**Комментарий:**

yathā kāmādi-citti-bhede tat-kārya-bhede ’pi na kāmādikaṃ manas tattva-antaram, kāma-saṅkalpo vicikitsā śraddhā aśraddhā dhṛtir adhṛtir hrīr dhīr bhīr iti etat sarvaṃ mana eva iti vacanāt \| evaṃ prāṇaḥ apāno vyāna udānaḥ samāna iti etat sarvaṃ prāṇa eva iti vacanād apāna-ādayo ’pi prāṇasya eva vṛtti-viśeṣāḥ; na tattva-antaram iti abhyagamyate \|\| 11 \|\| iti vāyu-kriyā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha śreṣṭha-aṇutva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.4.12 \|\|

*aṇuś ca*

**Комментарий:**

aṇuḥ ca asau, pūrva-vad utkrānti-ādi-śravaṇāt tam utkramantaṃ prāṇaḥ anūtkramati iti-ādiṣu \| adhikāḍhyaṃ tu sam ebhir vilīnair asmin ojena sarveṇa prāṇe sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ sarvaṃ hi idaṃ prāṇena abhūtaṃ iti-ādi-śravaṇāt mahā-parimāṇa iti \| parihāras tu—utkrānti-ādi-śravaṇāt tarhi niścīyate sarvasya prāṇi-jātasya prāṇasya atisthitivena vaibhava-vāda-upapattir iti \|\| 12 \|\|

iti śreṣṭha-aṇutva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

**atha jyotir-ādi-adhiṣṭhāna-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.4.13 \|\|

*jyotir-ādi-adhiṣṭhānaṃ tu tat-āmananāt prāṇa-vatā śabdāt*

**Комментарий:**

sakṣīṇāṃ prāṇānāṃ svarūpa utpattiḥ iyattā-parisaṅkhyānaṃ ca uktam \| teṣāṃ prāṇānāṃ manasy-ādi-devatā-adhiṣṭhitatvaṃ ca pūrvam eva abhimāninī-vyapadeśās tu viśeṣānugatibhyām iti anena sūtreṇa prasaṅga-dṛḍhīkṛtam \| jīvaiḥ ca sva-bhoga-asādhanatvena bhūtānāṃ loka-siddhaḥ; evam eva etad ātmanaḥ prāṇā gṛhītvā sve śarīre yathā-kāmaṃ parivartante iti-ādi-śruti-siddhaṃ ca \| tarhiṃ jīvasya anna-ādi-vedhanatāṃ ca prāṇa-viṣayam adhiṣṭhānaṃ kiṃ sva-āyattam, uta paramātma-yattam iti vicārya nirddeśyatādhyayanam \|\| iti prāpta ucyate—jyotir-ādi-adhiṣṭhānam iti \| prāṇa-vatā—jīvena saha jyotir-ādīnāṃ agny-arka-ādi-devatānāṃ prāṇa-viṣayam adhiṣṭhānam, tat-āmananāt—tasya paramātmanaḥ āmanana-śruteḥ \| āmananam—abhimukhyena mananam, paramātmanas saṅkalpa eva bhavati iti arthaḥ \| kuta evam ? śabdāt—indriyāṇāṃ sādhimāni-devatānāṃ jīvātmanaḥ ca sva-kārye parama-puruṣa-mano-'nuyattatva-

śāstrāt \| yathā tv apitri-brāhmaṇa-vidu śreyo'so niṣkramate rastaro yam arminnaṃ veda yasya aniśarīraṃ yojinam astaro yasyati sa ta ātmā antaryāmi-amṛtaḥ śreyo vācyaḥ viṣṇuḥ śreya āditye tiṣṭhan śreya ātmani tiṣṭhan śreya-pṛthivy api viṣṇuḥ ity-ādi \| yathā ca śrī-bhāṣya-mahātāḥ pavate, bhīṣā udeti sūryaḥ, bhīṣā asmād agniḥ ca indraḥ ca, mṛtyuḥ dhāvati pañcamaḥ iti \| tathā etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi sūrya-candramasau vidhṛtau tiṣṭhataḥ ity-ādi \|\|13\|\|

## \|\| 2.4.14 \|\|

*tasya ca nityatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

sarveṣāṃ paramātmā-adhiṣṭhitatvasya nityatvāt, svarūpa-pravṛtti-bhedena niyantṛtvaṃ ca tat-sadṛśyād eva prāṇādi-bhūta-tuparamarjanīyam \| śrī-talpakṣa, tat eva anupaviśat, tat anupaviśya, sat ca tyat ca abhavatū iti-ādinā parama-puruṣasya niyantṛtvena sarva-cid-acit-pravṛtta-praveśaḥ svarūpa-pravṛttī śrūyate \| smṛtau ca śrī-viṣṇu-vādi idam kṛtsnam eka-aṃśena sthito jagatī iti \|\|14\|\|

iti jyotir-ādi-adhiṣṭhāna-adhikaraṇam \|\|6\|\|

**atha indriya-adhikaraṇam \|\|7\|\|**

## \|\| 2.4.15 \|\|

*tāni indriyāṇi tat-vyapadeśāt anyatra śreṣṭhāt*

**Комментарий:**

kiṃ sarve prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭāḥ indriyāṇi, uta, śreṣṭha-prāṇa-vyatiriktāḥ eva iti viṣaye prāṇa-śabda-vācyatvāt, karaṇatvāt ca sarve eva indriyāṇi \| iti prāpte ucyate—śreṣṭha-vyatiriktāḥ eva prāṇāḥ indriyāṇi \| kutaḥ? śreṣṭhāt ekadā eva prāptuṃ tat-vyapadeśāt \| śrī-indriyāṇi daśa ekaṃ ca pañca ca indriya-gocarāḥ ity-ādi-virddhi catura-vidhyu samanaska-ekādaśa indriya-saṅkhyā vyapadiśyate \|\|15\|\|

## \|\| 2.4.16 \|\|

*bheda-śruteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

śrī-etasam ājāyate prāṇo, manaḥ sarva-indriyāṇi ca śrī ity-ādi-vidhiḥ ca indriyebhyaḥ prāṇasya pṛthak-vyapadeśāt prāṇa-vyatiriktāni eva indriyatvam avagamyate \| manasaḥ pṛthak-vyapadeśe'pi tasyā api atra indriyatvam uktam; śrī-manuṣyaḥ cakṣur-ādibhiḥ driyāṇi śrī ity-ādau \| veda-śravaṇaṃ ca cakṣur-ādibhiḥ; śreṣṭha-prāṇa-yoge, phala-vyakteḥ; suṣuptau hi prāṇasya vṛttiḥ phala-vyakte, cakṣur-ādīnāṃ tu vṛttiḥ na phala-vyakte \| kāryaṃ ca cakṣur-ādīnāṃ samanaskānāṃ jñāna-karma-sādhanatvam; prāṇasya tu śarīra-indriya-dhāraṇam \| prāṇa-adhīna-dhāraṇatvāt indriyāṃśu prāṇa-śabda-upadeśaḥ \| tathā ca śrutiḥ: śrī-te etasya eva sarve rūpam abhavan tasmāt ete ete na ākhyāyante śrī iti \| rūpam abhavan—śarīra-sambandha, tat-adhīna-pravṛttāḥ ca nityāḥ \|\|16\|\| iti indriya-adhikaraṇam \|\|7\|\|

**atha saṃjñā-mūrti-kḷpti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|**

## \|\| 2.4.17 \|\|

*saṃjñā-mūrti-kḷptis tu tri-vṛt-kurvata upadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

bhūta-indriya-ādīnāṃ sama-sṛṣṭiḥ, jīvānāṃ kartṛtvaṃ ca paramātmat brahmaṇa iti uktaṃ purastāt \| jīvānāṃ sva-indriya-adhiṣṭhānaṃ ca para-āyattam iti ca anantaraṃ sthirī-kṛtya atra samarthitam \| yā divyaṃ nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇa-ātmikā prapañca-vya-sṛṣṭiḥ; sā kiṃ samaṣṭi-jīva-rūpasya hiraṇyagarbhasya eva karma ? uta tejaḥ-prabhṛti-śarīrakasya paramātma-antar-yāmi-hiraṇyagarbha-śarīrakasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ ? iti iha anīṃ cintyate \| kiṃ yuktam ? samaṣṭi-jīvasya eva iti; kutaḥ ? anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi iti jīva-kartṛkaṃ kartavyam aśravaṇāt \| na hi parā devatā svena rūpeṇa nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi iti īhate; api tu sva-aṃśa-bhūtena jīvena rūpeṇa, anena jīvena ātmanā iti vacanāt \| na ca evaṃ cāreṇa anupraviśya para-kālaṃ saha-kulya-apānīya-vat haṭhā karavāṇi iti uktam a-puruṣaḥ kartṛtva-viśeṣa pratiṣṭhita lakṣaṇikasya syāt \| na evam, tatra rāja-vāryaḥ svacchanda-śete iti ā-lākṣaṇikatvāt, iha tu jīva-rūpa-ādi sva-aṃśatvena svarūpa-vācitvena rūpeṇa praveśo vyākaraṇaṃ ca ātmana eva iti na lākṣaṇikatva-prasaṅgaḥ \| na ca saha-yoga-lākṣaṇye tṛtīyā, kārakatva-vivakto saṃbhavati upapada-vibhakteḥ svarūpāt \| na ca karaṇe tṛtīyā, brahma-kartṛtve karmaṇaḥ praveśa-vyākaraṇayoḥ jīvasya sādhakatva-saṃbhavāt \| na ca jīvasya kartṛtve praveśa-mātre paryavasyati; nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇaṃ tu brahmaṇa eva iti śakyaṃ vaktum; kartṛ-śabda-yogena samāna-kartṛkaṃ ktvayoḥ iti \| jīvasya sva-aṃśatvena svacchandatve api para-svarūpa-vyākṛti-arthaḥ anena jīvena iti parāmarśaḥ \| ataḥ hiraṇyagarbha-kartṛkā eva nāma-rūpa-vyākriyā \| astu evaṃ ca smṛtiṣu caturmukha-kartṛ-kṛt-sṛṣṭi-karaṇe nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇaṃ saṃdṛśyate nāma rūpaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ kṛtyānāṃ ca prapañcanam \| veda-śabdebhya eva ādau deva-ādīnāṃ cakāra saḥ \|\| iti ādi \|\|

evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate—saṃjñā-mūrti-kḷptis tu iti \| durbalaḥ pakṣo vyāvartyate \| saṃjñā-mūrti-kḷptiḥ—nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇam \| tat tri-vṛt-kurvataḥ parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ, tasya eva nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇa-upadeśāt \| tri-vṛt-karaṇaṃ kurvata eva hi nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇam upadiśyate, sā iyam devatā aikṣata hanta aham imāḥ tisro devatāḥ anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi tāsāṃ tri-vṛtaṃ-tri-vṛtam eka-ekaṃ karavāṇi iti samāna-kartṛkatva-śruteḥ \| tri-vṛt-karaṇaṃ tu caturmukha-aṇḍa-antar-vartino na saṃbhavati, tri-vṛt-kṛta-tejo-bannasya eva aṇḍa-utpādakatvāt \| caturmukhasya ca aṇḍe saṃbhavaḥ smaryate—tasmin aṇḍe abhavat brahmā sarva-loka-pitāmahaḥ iti \| atas tri-vṛt-karaṇaṃ parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ; tat-samāna-kartṛkaṃ nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇaṃ ca tasya eva iti vijñāyate \| kathaṃ tarhi anena jīvena iti bhinna-kartṛtvaṃ ? ātmā vā jīvena iti sāmāna-adhikaraṇyāt jīva-śarīrakaṃ paraṃ brahma eva jīva-śabdena abhidhīyate; yathā tat teja aikṣata tad apaḥ asṛjata tā āpa aikṣanta tā annam asṛjanta iti
