tejaḥ-prabhṛti-śarīrakaṃ parasya eva brahmaṇo vidhīyate \| ato jīva-samṛddhi-bhūta hiraṇyagarbha-śarīrakasya parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ karma nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇam \| evaṃ ca śraviṣya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi iti pratijñāti-sthāna-puruṣa-pravilaye mukhya-artha-viṣayaḥ bhavati \| praveśa-vyākaraṇayoḥ samāna-deśaṃ kartavyatvam upapadyate \| caturmukha-śarīrakasya parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ karma deva-ādi-vicitra-buddhiḥ iti caturmukha-hetuṃ kartuḥ karaṇe nāma-ākhyā-kriyā-upadeśaḥ ca upapadyate \| ataḥ satyaṃ devatā iti ādi-vākyasya ayam arthaḥ—iyaṃ—tejo’ba-rūpa-sthitayo devatāḥ, anena jīvena—jīva-samṛddhi-viśiṣṭena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi—deva-ādi-vicitra-buddhi tan-nāmadhyāni ca karavāṇi \| tadyaṃnacayoḥ sarva-vipradhāna-apekṣaṃ tejo’bbanāṃ viśeṣa-buddhy-ātmakānāṃ tan-nāmadhyāmakānāṃ trivat-trivṛttvaṃ karavāṇi iti \| ataḥ parasya eva brahmaṇaḥ karma idaṃ nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇam \|\|17\|\|

atha syāt—nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇasya trivṛt-karaṇena eka-kartṛkatvāt paramātma-kartṛkam iti na śakyate vaktum, trivṛt-karaṇasya api jīva-kartṛkatva-saṃbhavāt \| aṇḍa-sṛṣṭi-uttara-kālaṃ hi caturmukha-sṛṣṭa-jīvāṣu trivṛt-karaṇa-prakāra upadiśyate—sā iyaṃ tu khalu somya imāḥ tisro devatāḥ puruṣaṃ prāpya trivṛt-trivṛt ekaikā bhavati tāsāṃ vijānīhi iti annam aśitaṃ tredhā vidhīyate tasya yaḥ sthaviṣṭho bhāgaḥ tat-purīṣaṃ bhavati yaḥ madhyamaḥ tan-māṃsaṃ yaḥ ’ṇiṣṭhaḥ tan-manaḥ ity-ādinā \| tathā pūrvasminn api vākye anena jīhitaṃ rūpaṃ tejasas tadvaṃ yat śuklaṃ tad apāṃ yat kṛṣṇaṃ tad akṣarasya ity-ādinā caturmukha-sṛṣṭāni āditya-candra-vidhutsu trivṛt-karaṇaṃ pradarśyate \| nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇa-uttara-kālaṃ ca trivṛt-karaṇaṃ śrūyate sā iyaṃ devatā aikṣata hanta jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi iti tāsāṃ tisṛt-trivṛtam ekāmako ’nu iti \| tatrāha—

## \|\| 2.4.18 \|\|

*māṃsādi bhaumaṃ yathā-śabdam itarayoḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

yat kṛṣṇam anna-sṛṣṭyuuttara-kālaṃ caturmukha-sṛṣṭa-devatā-viṣayatvāt chrutisāṃ trivṛttiṃ trivṛtam ekām akarot iti trivṛt-karaṇopadeśa iti; tatra api akṣobhaḥ; annam aśitaṃ tredhā vidhīyata iti asya soma-nagareḥ purodhāturtenāṇ-upadeśatvena ca vyavasthiteḥ kāraṇa-anuvividhātvivenāpya-tejas-svatra-prastutatvāt; āpaḥ pītāḥ iti atra api sūtrāgraṇeḥ sthaviṣṭāṃśayoḥ pārthivatva-tājasatva-pradarśanāt ca \| na evaṃ manyete; māṃsādi hi bhaumam iti ete—purīṣavat-māṃsa-manasī api bhaume—pārthive icchete, annam aśitaṃ tredhā iti prakramāt \| yathā-śabdam itarayoḥ ca—itarayoḥ api āpaḥ pītāḥ tejo’śitam iti pañca-yogyathā-śabdaṃ vikāra iṣyate \| āpaḥ pītāḥ tredhā vidhīyanta iti atra amedya tredhā pariṇāmaḥ śabdāt pratīyate; tathā tejo’śitaṃ tredhā vidhīyata iti api taijasa eva tredhā pariṇāmaḥ śabdāt pratīyate \| ataḥ purīṣa-māṃsa-manāṃsi pṛthivy-adhikārāḥ, mūtra-lohita-prāṇāḥ abadhikārāḥ, asthi-majjā-vācaḥ tejo’vikārā iti pratipattavyam; annamayaṃ hi somya manaḥ āpomayaḥ

prāṇa-sena-ujjhayī vākyaṃ iti vākya-śeṣa-virodhaḥ ca \| ataḥ śraddhāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtam ekaikam akarot iti arciḥ-trivṛt-karaṇa-prakāraḥ aśarmā-aśita-nityādinā na pradarśyate; tathā sati manaḥ-prāṇa-vācāṃ nayanam annāyīṣṭatvena tejas-tvena tejas-tvāt aśarmyaṃ hi somya manaḥ iti-ādi-virudhyate \| prāñci trivṛt-kṛtānāṃ pṛthivy-ādīnāṃ puruṣa-prāśanāt aśarmā-aśitānityādinā ekasya eva pariṇāmaḥ ucyate \| annāt sṛṣṭeḥ prāñci ca tejo-lambānāṃ trivṛt-karaṇena bhāvitavyam, adhikṛtatānāṃ teṣāṃ kārya-ārambha-asāmarthye \| anyonya-saṃyuktānām eva hi kārya-ārambha-sāmarthyaṃ; tad eva ca trivṛt-karaṇam \| tathā ca smaryate annānāṃ bījāṃ pṛthak-bhāva-anantaraṃ saṃhṛti vinā \| nāśaknuvaṃ prajāḥ sraṣṭum asamāgamya kṛtsnaśaḥ \|\| sametya anyonya-saṃyogaṃ paraspara-samāśrayāḥ \| mahat-ādyā viśeṣāntāḥ hi aṇḍam utpādayanti te \|\| iti \| ata eva ca anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi tatrāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtam ekaikam akarot iti pāṭha-krame 'pi kameśa bādhyate \| aṇḍāntar-vartini-khila-padārthādi-vyu trivṛt-karaṇa-pradarśane śvetaketu-upāsya-bhedāṇḍa-nirvartitena tasya bahu-bubhutsuṃ trivṛt-karaṇa-pradarśana-ayogāt trivṛt-kṛtānāṃ kāryatvam ārambhitavyāniṣu kriyate \|\| 18 \|\|

syāt etat; aśarmā-aśitam āpaḥ pītāḥ tejaḥ-aśitam iti trivṛt-kṛta-mātra-ādīnāṃśekarasya tejo-lambānam etena triṣu asya katham asamāpatet iti eka-śeṣeṇa vyapadeśaḥ upapadyate iti; tatra āha—

## \|\| 2.4.19 \|\|

*vaiśeṣyāt tu tad-vādaḥ tad-vādaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

vaiśeṣyaṃ—viśeṣa-bhāvaḥ \| trivṛt-karaṇena triṣu api ekeṅkarimabhaya-ādhikyāt tatra-bhāsa-ādi-vādaḥ \| dviruktiḥ adhyāya-samāptiṃ dyotayati \|\| 16 \|\| iti saṃjñā-mūrti-kḷpti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīrika-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye dvitīyasya adhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

**samāpra-ādhyāyaḥ \|\|**

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**tṛtīyaḥ adhyāyaḥ—prathamaḥ pādaḥ—tad-anantara-pratipatti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Adhyaya 3

## Pada 1

## \|\| 3.1.1 \|\|

*tad-anantara-pratipattau raṃhati saṃpariṣvaktaḥ praśna-nirūpaṇābhyām*

**Комментарий:**

atikrānta-ananta-adhyāya-dvayena nikhila-jagat-eka-kāraṇaṃ nirasta-nikhila-doṣa-gandham aparimita-udāra-guṇa-sāgaraṃ sakala-itara-vilakṣaṇaṃ paraṃ brahma mumukṣum upāsyatayā vedāntaiḥ pratipādyam iti ayam arthaḥ smṛti-nyāya-virodha-parihāra-para pakṣa-nirīkṣya vedānta-vākya-paraspara-virodha-parihāra-rūpa-kārya-svarūpa-saṃśodhanena suṣṭhu pūrva-pakṣa-hetubhiḥ api saṃsthāpitaḥ \| atha atra adhyāya-dvayena brahmopāsanasyaṃ prati-

bāhitum \| uttareṣāṃ tad-apānudyair eva prati-prakāraḥ ca nivarttum ucyate \| tatra dvitīya-adhyāye upāya-bhūta-upāsana-viṣayā cintā vartate \| upāsanā-samāpti-arthita-upāsya-prāpya-vastu ucyate tikta-bhedaḥ ca, prāpya-tṛṣṇā ca iti, tat-siddhy-arthaṃ jīvasya loka-antareṣu saṃcarato jāgat-svapna-suṣuptasya mūrchitaḥ ca doṣaḥ, parasya ca brahma-gatatvāhitā, kalyāṇa-guṇa-karatvaṃ ca prathama-dvitīyayor pādayoḥ pratipādyate \| tatra dehāt dehāntaraṃ gacchato jīvo dehāntara-ārambha-hetu-bhūta-sūkṣma-bhūta-saṃpariṣvakta eva gacchati, uta na iti cintāyāṃ yadi ayaṃ jīvo yāti tatra ca bhūta-sūkṣmāṇāṃ sulabhatvāt asaṃpariṣvaktaḥ yāti iti prāptam \| pañcāgni-vidyāyām vā iti prāptam \|\|

tatra siddhāntam āha—tad-anantara-pratipattau raṃhati saṃpariṣvakta iti \| saṃgṛhīta-sūkṣma-bhūtair iti mūrddhanvena dehaḥ prastutaḥ; sa tat-śabden parāmṛśyate \| tad-anantara-pratipattau—dehāntara-ārambhe bhūta-sūkṣmi-saṃpariṣvakto jīvo raṃhati—gacchati iti arthaḥ \| kutaḥ ? praśna-nirūpaṇābhyāṃ—praśna-prativacanābhyām \| pañcāgni-vidyāyām eva praśna-prativacane āmnāyete—vetthaikatāṃ kitarāyāṃ pāñcālaḥ pravāhaṇaḥ karmaṇā gantavyaṃ deśaṃ, punar-āvṛtti-prakāraṃ, deva-yāna pitṛ-yāṇa-patha-vyāvarttane,

amum lokasya pradhāraṃ ca veda iti pṛṣṭheṣu api papraccha—vettha yathā pañcamyām āhutau āpaḥ puruṣa-vacaso bhavanti iti \| tatra imaṃ paricchinnaṃ praśnaṃ pratibudhya dyu-loka-paryantena rūpitvā tasmin etasmin agnau devāḥ śraddhāṃ juhvati tasyā āhuteḥ soma-rājā sambhavati iti-ādinā deva-ādhyāḥ jīvasya prāṇaḥ agniḥ devena rūpite dyu-loke śraddhā-ākhyaṃ vastu prakṣipanti; sā ca śraddhā soma-rāja-ākhya-amṛta-atmaya-deha-rūpeṇa pariṇamate \| taṃ ca abhūta-mayaṃ dehaṃ ta eva prāṇaḥ parjanye agnitvena rūpite prakṣipanti; sa ca dehaḥ tatra prakṣīṇo varṣaṃ bhavati \| tat ca varṣaṃ ta eva prāṇaḥ pṛthivyām agnitva-rūpitāyāṃ prakṣipanti; tat ca tatra prathimam annaṃ bhavati \| tat ca annaṃ ta eva puruṣe agnitva-rūpite prakṣipanti; tat ca tatra retaḥ bhavati \| tat ca ta eva yoṣāyām agnitva-rūpitāyāṃ

prakṣipanti; tat ca tatra prathiṣṭhaṃ garbho bhavati iti uktvā āha—iti tu pañcamyām āhutau āpaḥ puruṣa-vacaso bhavanti iti \| evaṃ pañcamyām āhuto hutāyām āpaḥ puruṣa-śabda-abhilapyā bhavanti iti arthaḥ \| evam ukte pūrvāḥ catasraḥ huti-bindutva-trimānanām eva āpaṃ sūkṣma-bhūtānām itarāṇāṃ puruṣa-ākāratvaṃ bhavati iti uktaṃ bhavati \| ata eva praśna-prativacanābhyāṃ dehāt dehaṃ bhūta-sūkṣma-sah tatra-tatra yāti iti gamyate \|\| 1 \|\|

nanu āpaḥ puruṣa-vacasa iti ukte apāṃ puruṣa-ākāra-pariṇāma-matrotair ūcyatāṃ jīvena tāsām eva pariṣvaṅgaḥ pratīyate \| ataḥ kathaṃ sarveṣāṃ bhūta-sūkṣmāṇāṃ pariṣvaṅga iti ? tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.1.2 \|\|

*try-ātmakatvāt tu bhūyas-tvāt*

**Комментарий:**

tu-śabdaḥ codyaṃ vyāvartayati \| dehārambha-kāraṇam apaḥ kevalānāṃ na dehārambha-saṃbhavaḥ \|

dehāt ca ātmā ayaṃ hi na arasam citra-vṛtti-citva-vivekena am iti citra-upakaraṇam \| kevalānām ayāṃ śravaṇaṃ tu tatra eva bhūyas-tvāt \| deve ca lohita-ādi-bhūyas-tvena ārambhakāṇāṃ bhūyas-tvaṃ gamyate \|\|2\|\|

## \|\| 3.1.3 \|\|

*prāṇa-gateḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

itaḥ ca bhūta-saṃbhava-parigrahasya gamanam iti gamyate \| utkrāmati jīve prāṇānāṃ tat-anu gati-śruteḥ—tam utkrāmantam prāṇaḥ anūtkramati prāṇam anūtkramantam sarve prāṇāḥ anūtkrāmanti iti \| smaryate ca—manasaḥ ṣaṣṭhāni indriyāṇi prakṛti-sthāni karṣati \| śarīraṃ yat avāpnoti yat ca api utkrāmati īśvaraḥ \| gṛhītvā etāni saṃyāti vāyuḥ gandhān iva āśayāt \|\| iti \| na ca nirāśrayaṇāṃ gati-rūpā upapadyata iti tat-āśraya-bhūtānāṃ bhūta-sūkṣmāṇām api gatiḥ abhyupagantavyā \|\|3\|\|

## \|\| 3.1.4 \|\|

*agni-ādi-gati-śruteḥ iti cet na bhāktatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

yatra asya puruṣasya mṛtasya agniṃ vāk apyeti, vātaṃ prāṇaḥ cakṣuḥ ādityam iti-ādinā prāṇānāṃ jīva-karaṇa-kāle acanacādidravya-prayabhavānām teṣāṃ jīvena saha gamanam iti gati-śrutiḥ uktā na iti cet, na, bhāktatvāt acanacādidravya-prayabhavasya \| kathaṃ bhāktatvam ? yoṣā vai annāni vanaspatīn keśāḥ iti-ādi pṛthivī-loma-ādibhiḥ saha śravaṇāt \| ataḥ śrutārtha-prayukteḥ acetanatvāt devatā-prakaraṇa-parā \|\|4\|\|

## \|\| 3.1.5 \|\|

*prathame aśravaṇāt iti cen na tāḥ eva hi upapatteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

yat uktaṃ indriya-saṃbhavibhiḥ bhūta-antara-saṃsṛṣṭaiḥ parivṛtaḥ jīvaḥ gacchati iti praśna-trativacanābhyām avagamyata iti; tat na upapadyate, dyuloka-āti-niviṣaye prathame home apāṃ homyatva-aśravaṇāt \| tasmin etasmin agnau devāḥ śraddhāṃ juhvati iti śraddhā eva homyatvena śrutā \| śraddhā nāma jīvasya mano-vṛtti-viśeṣatvena prasiddhā \| ataḥ na āpaḥ tatra homyāḥ iti cet, na, yataḥ tāḥ—āpaḥ eva śraddhā-śabdena tatra abhidhīyante; kutaḥ ? praśna-prativacana-upapatteḥ \| vettha yathā pañcamyām āhutau āpaḥ puruṣa-vacasaḥ bhavanti iti praśnasya prativacana-upakrāme hi śraddhā dyuloka-agnau homyatvena śrutā \| tatra yadi śraddhā-śabdena āpaḥ na ucyeraṃs, tataḥ anyathā praśnaḥ anyathā prativacanam iti asaṃbaddhaṃ syāt \| iti tu pañcamyām āhutau āpaḥ puruṣa-vacasaḥ iti prativacana-niṣkarṣaṃ ca śraddhā apatyam eva sūcayati \| vettha paryeti hi praśnānāṃ prakāraḥ, iti tu pañcamyām iti nirvādena parihāre nigamyate \| śraddhā-somi-rājanv abhi etā oṣadhi-anna-apāṃ pariṇāma-mukhyā hi āpaḥ puruṣa-vacasaḥ iti nigamyate \| śraddhā-śabdasya ca apsu vaidika-prayogaḥ dṛśyate—apaḥ praṇayati

śraddhā vā āpaḥ iti \| śraddhāṃ juhvati tasyāḥ āhuteḥ somaḥ rājā saṃbhavati iti soma-ākāreṇa pariṇāma-prāptam eva upapadyate \| ataḥ bhūta-antara-saṃsṛṣṭa-śarīra-indriya-saṃparivṛttaḥ jīvaḥ raṃhati iti upapannam \|\| 5 \|\|

## \|\| 3.1.6 \|\|

*aśuddhatvād iti cet na śabdāt*

**Комментарий:**

raṃhataḥ sa-śarīra-indriya-saṃparivṛtakaḥ jīvaḥ yāti iti ayam arthaḥ pūrvam ākāśādi-vākyāt iti; tat na upapadyate, agnau api jīvasya aśravaṇāt \| atra hi śraddhātvam eva ambuda-avasthā-viśeṣo hotavyena śrutaḥ; na tu jīvaḥ tat-parivṛtaṃ iti cet; tat na, iṣṭādi-kāriṇāṃ pratītiḥ—agnau eva ca ayam etac ca brahmaṇāṃ vidyota-pūrta-datta-kāriṇo dyu-lokaṃ prāpya soma-rājānaḥ bhavanti; puṇya-karma-avasāne ca punar āgatya garbhaṃ prāpnuvanti iti ucyate atha ya ime grāme iṣṭā-pūrte dattam iti upāsate te dhūmam abhisaṃbhavanti iti ārabhya vṛṣṭeḥ annam annaṃ puruṣaḥ sa yoṣiti retaḥ siktaṃ bhavati iti \| atha api dyu-lokam api śraddhāṃ juhvati tasya āhuteḥ somaḥ rājā saṃbhavati iti tad eṣṭavyāc chuddhatvasyaiṣṭa-vidyā-somaḥ bhūtvā deva-vidyaḥ bhavati iti uktam iti gamyate \| dehasya jīva-viśeṣaṇatva-rūpasya vāyavaḥ śabdaḥ viśiṣṭe jīva eva pūrva-vat ca iti \| ataḥ saṃparivṛtakaḥ jīvaḥ yāti iti upapadyate \|\| 6 \|\| nanu ca te devā bhakṣayanti iti deva-bhakṣyamāṇatva-vacanāt somaḥ rājā iti na jīvaḥ ucyate, jīvasya ca ānandīyatvāṅ; tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.1.7 \|\|

*bhāktaṃ vā anātma-vittvāt tathā hi darśayati*

**Комментарий:**

vāg-ādibhyaḥ ca vyāvartyate \| iṣṭādi-kāriṇaḥ asmin vicāre daivānāṃ bhogya-upakaraṇatvena iha amutra vā vartante \| iha iṣṭikānāṃ kṣavārthaṃ kuṭumbam upakurvanti \| ārādhana-prītaiḥ devaiḥ hṛṣṭa-manāṃ loke prāpya tatra tat-samāna-bhoga-upakaraṇaṃ bhavati \| yathā paśuḥ eva saḥ devānām iti anyatām avidvo devānām upakaraṇatvaṃ darśayati śrutiḥ \| śrutiḥ iti ātmavidāṃ brahma-prāptim anātma-vidāṃ ca deva-bhogyatvaṃ darśayati—devān deva-yajaḥ yānti mad-bhaktā yānti mām api iti \| ataḥ jīvasya devānāṃ bhogya-upakaraṇatva-abhimātram akṣya-vacanam; ataḥ tad-rūpaḥ tat-saṃparivṛttaḥ jīvaḥ raṃhati iti siddham \|\| 7 \|\| iti tat-antara-pratipatti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha hṛtāyati-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.1.8 \|\|

*kṛta-atyaye anuśaya-vān dṛṣṭa-smṛtibhyām yathā-itam anevam ca*

**Комментарий:**

kevala-iṣṭā-pūrta-saṃskāriṇām dhūma-ādinā pitṛ-yāṇena pathā gatvā karma-phala-avasāne punaḥ-āvartanam ca āmnātam śrāvayat sampātam upajīva atha enam iti pratinivartante iti \| tatra pratyavaroha-jīvaḥ kim asmāt āvār pratyavarohati, uta na iti saṃśayyate \| kim yuktam ? karmaṇaḥ kṛtsnasya upabhuktatvāt na anuśaya-vattvam iti prāptam \| anuśayaḥ hi upabhukta-śiṣṭam karma \| tat ca kṛtsna-phala-upabhogāt sati na avaśiṣyate \| yāvat-sampātam upajīva iti vacanāt kṛtsna-upabhogaḥ ca jñāyate \| sampatati anena svargam lokam iti sampātaḥ karma ucyate \| śruti-antaram ca prāpya antam karmaṇaḥ tasya yat-kiṃcit iha karoti ayam \| tasmāt lokāt punaḥ eti asme lokāya karmaṇe \|\| iti \|\|

evam prāpte abhidhīyate—anuśaya-vān pratyavarohati iti \| kutaḥ ? dṛṣṭa-smṛtibhyām—śruti-smṛtibhyām iti arthaḥ \| śrutiḥ tāvat—atha iha ramaṇīya-caraṇāḥ abhyāśaḥ ha yate ramaṇīyām yonim āpadyeran brāhmaṇa-yonim kṣatriya-yonim vaiśya-yonim vā atha ye iha kapūya-caraṇāḥ abhyāśaḥ ha yate kapūyām yonim āpadyeran śva-yonim vā sūkara-yonim vā caṇḍāla-yonim vā iti pratyavarohataḥ prati śrūyate \| asmin ukte lokāḥ traya-vataḥ praṇayoka-karmaṇaḥ ramaṇīyām brāhmaṇa-ādi-yonim pratipadyante; kapūya-caraṇāḥ kutsita-karmabhiḥ kutsitām śva-sūkara-caṇḍāla-ādi-yonim pratipadyanta iti pratyavarohatām puṇya-pāpa-karma-yogam darśayati \| smṛtiḥ api—varṇāḥ āśramāḥ ca sva-karma-nibhiḥ pretya karma-phalam anubhūya tataḥ śeṣeṇa viśiṣṭa-deśa-jāti-kula-sāyur-śruta-vitta-vṛtta-sukha-medhasaḥ-hi janma pratipadyante viparyaye viparītā naśyanti iti \| tathā tataḥ pariśeṣam karma-phala-śeṣeṇa jātim rūpam varṇam balam medhām prajñām dravyāṇi dharma-anuṣṭhānam iti pratipadyante tat cakra-vat ubhayoḥ lokayoḥ sukhe eva vartante iti \| yāvat-saṃpātam iti phalita-ārambha-kṛta-karma-viśeṣa-viṣayam; prāpya antam ca karoti ayam iti idam api tat-viṣayam eva \| apūrṇa-phala-dāna-saṅkuṭā-praviṣṭānām ca karmaṇām karma-antara-phala-anubhava-sthāra-udiprayuktam \| ataḥ asmiṃ loke gatasya anubhūya eva yathā-itam anevam ca punaḥ nivartante—ārohaṇa-prakāreṇa ca punaḥ nivartanta iti arthaḥ \| ārohaṇam hi dhūma-rātri-apara-pakṣa-dakṣiṇāyana-avapakāśa-candra-krameṇa; avarohaṇam tu candramasaḥ sthānāt ākāśa-vāyu-dhūma-abhra-megha-krameṇa \| tathā ākāśa-avarohaṇa-ādi iṣṭataḥ vācya-ādi-sthāneḥ pitṛ-loka-āghrātatva-itra-vet \|\| 5 \|\|

## \|\| 3.1.9 \|\|

*caraṇāt iti cet na tat-upalakṣaṇa-arthe iti kārṣṇājiniḥ*

**Комментарий:**

ramaṇīya-caraṇāḥ kapūya-caraṇāḥ iti nanu caraṇa-śabdena puṇya-pāpa-hetum karma abhidhīyate, caraṇa-śabdasya laukika-vedayoḥ ācāre prasiddheḥ \| laukikaḥ khalu caraṇam ācāraḥ śīlam vṛttam iti paryāya-nibandhane; vede ca yāni anavadyāni karmāṇi, tāni sevitavyāni yāni asmākam

su-caritrāṇi, tāni tvayā uparjyanti iti caraṇa-karmaṇī bhedena vyapadiśyete \| ataḥ caraṇāt—śīlāt tu yoni-viśeṣa-prāptiḥ; na anuṣṭhānāt iti cet; tatra, caraṇa-śrutiḥ karma-upalakṣaṇa-arthā kāraṇijināsārthā manyate, kevala-ācārāt sukha-duḥkhādi-asaṃbhavāt \| sukha-duḥkhe hi puṇya-pāpa-rūpa-karma-phale \|\|8\|\|

## \|\| 3.1.10 \|\|

*ānarthakyam iti cet na tad-apekṣatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ tarhi ṛtvarthāt ācārasya smṛti-vihita-snānārtham eva iti cet; tatra, tad-apekṣatvāt puṇyasya karmaṇaḥ \| ācāravataḥ eva puṇya-karmādhikāraḥ; sandhyā-hīnaḥ aśuciḥ nityam anarhaḥ sarva-karmasu ācāra-hīnaṃ na punanti vedāḥ iti-ādi-vacanebhyaḥ \| ataḥ caraṇa-śrutiḥ karma-upalakṣaṇa-arthā kāraṇijināsārthā bhavati \|\|10\|\|

## \|\| 3.1.11 \|\|

*sukṛta-duṣkṛte eva iti tu bādariḥ*

**Комментарий:**

puṇyaṃ karma ācarati, pāpaṃ karma ācarati iti karmaṇi carateḥ prayogāt, pṛthag-nirdeśasya ca pratyakṣa-śruti-siddha-ācāra-anumita-śruti-siddhatva-viṣayatvena gauṇyā vṛtteḥ anyathā-upapatteḥ; mukhye saṃbhavati na lakṣaṇā nyāyyā iti sukṛta-duṣkṛte eva caraṇa-śabda-abhidheye iti bādarir ācāryaḥ manyate \| atra bādari-matam eva sva-matam \| ācāra-anumita-śruti-vihita-sandhyā-vandanādeḥ karmiṇā adhikāra-saṃpādanaṃ phalam iti tu sva-śreṣṭhatam \| atas tathaiva praty-avarohaṇāt \|\|11\|\| iti kṛtātyayādhikaraṇam \|\|2\|\|

**atha aniṣṭa-adhikāri-adhikaraṇam \|\|3\|\|**

## \|\| 3.1.12 \|\|

*aniṣṭādhikāriṇām api ca śrutam*

**Комментарий:**

kevalaiḥ eva darśa-pūrṇamāsaiḥ mṛtyuṃ taranti evaṃ nitarām taranti iti uktam \| iṣṭāniṣṭa-kāriṇaḥ api candramasaṃ gacchanti, na iti cintyate \| ye vihitaṃ na kurvanti, niṣiddhaṃ ca kurvanti, te ubhaye api pāpa-karmāṇaḥ aniṣṭādhikāriṇaḥ \| kiṃ śrutam ? te api candramasaṃ gacchanti iti \| kutaḥ ? teṣām api hi tad-ūgamanaṃ śrutam, ye vai keca asmāl lokāt prayanti candramasam eva te sarve gacchanti iti aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣām eva gati-śravaṇāt \|\|12\|\|

evaṃ tarhi sukṛta-duṣkṛta-kāriṇaḥ asmy arohasya aviśeṣeṇa gati-śravaṇāt; na iti āha—

## \|\| 3.1.13 \|\|

*saṃyamane tu anubhūyeta itareṣām ārohāvarohaū tad-gati-nidarśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

tu hā duḥkhaṃ vyāvartyati \| itareṣām aniṣṭādhikāriṇāṃ candra-ārohāvarohaū saṃyamane—yama-sāsane tat-sayugmaty-ātmanā anubhūyeta, na upapadyate; kutaḥ ? tad-gati-nidarśanāt—dṛśyate hi pāpaka-

maṇāṃ pramadhvaratvā tad-vṛttam anyatra ayaṃ lokaḥ na asti na paraḥ iti mānī punaḥ punaḥ vaśam āpadyate me vañcayanti saṃgamate janānāṃ yamaṃ rājānam ityādikṣu \|\|13\|\|

## \|\| 3.1.14 \|\|

*smaranti ca*

**Комментарий:**

smaranti ca sarveṣāṃ yama-vaśyatāṃ parāśara-ādayaḥ—sarve ca ete vaśaṃ yānti yamasya bhagava-liṅgam ityādikṣu \|\|14\|\|

## \|\| 3.1.15 \|\|

*api sapta*

**Комментарий:**

pāpa-karmaṇāṃ gantavyatvena raurava-ādīni sapta narakān api smaranti \|\|15\|\| nanu samyag lokṣu gacchatāṃ kathaṃ yama-sadana-prāptiḥ; atra āha—

## \|\| 3.1.16 \|\|

*tatrāpi tad-vyāpārād avirodhaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

teṣu api samyag yamaś ca eva gamanād avirodhaḥ \| ataḥ agniṣvādikariṇām api yama-lokaṃ prāpya sva-karma-anurūpaṃ yātanā-prāptamukha paścāc candra-ārohaṇa-avaroho staḥ \|\|16\|\| iti prāpta ucyate—

## \|\| 3.1.17 \|\|

*vidyā-karmaṇoḥ iti tu prakṛtatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

tu avadhūḥ pakṣa-vyāvṛtti-arthaḥ \| agniṣvādikariṇām api candra-prāptiḥ asti iti etat apapadyate; kutaḥ ? vidyā-karmaṇoḥ iti—vidyā-karmaṇoḥ phala-bhoga-arthatvād yāna-pitṛ-yānayoḥ \| evaṃ sūktaṃ bhavati—agniṣvāttikariṇāṃ yathā vidyā-vidhuratvād eva yānena yathā gamanaṃ na saṃbhavati; tadvad deṣāpūrta-tat-vidhura-svahiṣṭhyānena candra-gamanam api na saṃbhavati iti \| deva-yāna-pitṛ-yānayoḥ vidyā-viṣayatvaṃ puṇya-karma-viṣayatvaṃ ca katham avagamyata iti cet; prakṛtatvāt anayoḥ \| prakṛtā hi deva-yāne vidyā, pitṛ-yāṇe ca karma; tat ya itthaṃ viduḥ ye ca ime araṇye śraddhā tapaḥ iti upāsate iti uktvā te arcir abhiśaṃbhavanti ityādinā; pitṛ-yāṇa-vacanāc ca \| ye vai ke ca asmāl lokāt prayanti candramasam eva te sarve gacchanti iti etat pūrva-vacanaṃ ca iṣṭādi-kāriṇaḥ te sarve iti pariṇeyam \|\|17\|\| nanu pāpa-karmaṇāṃ candramasa-mārgye pañcamāhuti-saṃbhavāt śarīra-ārambha eva doṣaḥ syāt; pañcamyām āhutau āpaḥ puruṣa-vacasaḥ bhavanti iti hi śarīra-ārambha upyate, sā ca āhutiḥ strī-prāpti-pūrvikā iti darśitam; ati-śarīra-ārambhaś ca teṣām api candra-ārohaṇa-avarohaṇa-caraṇa-apekṣayā iti āviṣat āha—

## \|\| 3.1.18 \|\|

*na tṛtīye tathā upalabdheḥ*

**Комментарий:**

tṛtīya-sthānasya śarīra-ārambhāya na pañcama-āhuti-apekṣā; kutaḥ? tathā-upalabdheḥ—tṛtīya-sthāna-ārabdhena kevala-prakaraṇi ucyate, teṣāṃ deha-ārambhe pañcama-āhuti-apekṣatvam upalabhyate etad yathā kena asau lokaḥ na saṃpūryate iti asya praśnasya prativacane atha eteyoḥ pathoḥ na katareṇa ca tāni imāni kṣudrāṇi asakṛt-āvartīni bhūtāni bhavanti jāyasva mriyasva iti evaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sthānaṃ tena asau lokaḥ na saṃpūryate iti tṛtīya-sthānasya dyu-loka-ārohaṇa-virodha-mātreṇa dyu-loka-saṃpūrti-vacanāt tasya tṛtīya-sthānasya śarīra-ārambhāya na pañcama-āhuti-apekṣā \| pañcamyām āhutau iti ca apāṃ pañcama-āhuti-saṃbandhasya puruṣa-vacas-tva-hetutva-mātraṃ pratipādyati, na anyat nivārayati, avadhāraṇā-śravaṇāt \|\|18\|\|

## \|\| 3.1.19 \|\|

*smaryate api ca loke*

**Комментарий:**

puṣpa-karṇābhiḥ keṣāṃcit pañcama-āhuti-apekṣayā deha-ārambhaḥ loke smaryate draupadī-dhṛṣṭadyumna-prabhṛtīnām \|\|19\|\|

## \|\| 3.1.20 \|\|

*darśanāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

śrutau api dṛśyate keṣāṃcit pañcama-āhuti-apekṣayā deha-ārambhaḥ: teṣāṃ khalu eṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ trīṇi eva bījāni bhavanti āṇḍajaṃ jīvajaṃ udbhijjam iti \| evaṃ tri-jagad-dehajayoḥ bhūtayoḥ pañcama-āhuti-nirapekṣatva-niścayaḥ \|\|20\|\|

nanu svedajānām atra na saṃgṛhītatvam asti trīṇi eva bījāni iti vacanāt; tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.1.21 \|\|

*tṛtīya-śabda-avarodhaḥ saṃśoka-jasya*

**Комментарий:**

saṃśoka-jasya—svedajasya api āṇḍajaṃ jīvajaṃ udbhijjam iti asya tṛtīyena udbhijja-śabdena avarodhaḥ saṃgrahaḥ vidyate iti arthaḥ; ataḥ kevala-prakaraṇāṃ candra-prāptiḥ na saṃbhavati \|\|21\|\|

iti agnihotra-ādi-adhikaraṇam \|\|3\|\|

**atha tat-svabhāvya-āpattaya-adhikaraṇam \|\|4\|\|**

## \|\| 3.1.22 \|\|

*tat-svābhāvya-āpattiḥ upapatteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

iṣṭhādhikāriṇo bhūta-sūkṣma-pariṣvaktāḥ soma-bhāvam āpadya brahma-lokam ārohanti iti uktam; avaroha-prakāraḥ ca atha etam eva adhvānaṃ punar nivartante yathā etam ākāśam ākāśād vāyuṃ vāyuḥ bhūtvā dhūmaḥ bhavati

bhūmā bhūtvā anyaḥ bhavati akṣaraṃ bhūtvā meyaḥ bhavati meyaḥ bhūtvā prabardhati iti vacanāt; śreyas-tma-nivedya-hetutvam \| tantra-ākhyākṣā-āśrayitra-tipanno deva-manuṣya-ādi-śabda-bādhaka-ākāśa-ādi-śabdaḥ; uttat sādhāraṇa-cin-nāma-bhiniti viṣaye śraddhāvasthasya śeṣamāvacabdar-viśeṣāt ākāśa-ādi-śabdāḥ \|\| iti prāpte tat-tathā-bhāvya-siddhi-hetutvam uccate \| tat-svabhāvya-āpattiḥ—tat-sādhāraṇa-āpattiḥ iti arthaḥ \| kutaḥ evam ? upapatteḥ—soma-bhāva-manuṣya-bhāvādau hi sukha-ḍuḥkha-upabhogāya tat-rūpatvaṃ; atha tu ākāśādo sukha-ḍuḥkha-upabhoga-abhāvāc candrānam upacarita-dvāpa-nicayaṃ tat-saṃsṛta-tat-sādhyāparyabhiprāyam \|\| 22 \|\| iti tat-svabhāvya-āpatty-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha nātivicira-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.1.23 \|\|

*nātivicireṇa viśeṣāt \| 3 \| 1 \| 23 \|\|*

**Комментарий:**

ākāśa-ādi-apithivī-bhūti yāvat-vṛhy-ādi-śruti kiṃ tatra eva na ati-viciṃraṃ tiṣṭhati, uta aniyam iti vicārye niyama-hetv-abhāvāt aniyama iti prāpte ucyate—na ati-vicireṇeti \| kutaḥ ? viśeṣāt—uttara śrīy-ādi-śrāvī ato vai khalu durniṣpataram iti vihiṣya kṛcchra-niṣkramaṇatva-abhidhānāt pūrvatra hi ākāśa-ādi-śrāvānā-vicira-niṣkramaṇaṃ gamyate \| durniṣpataram iti cchāndasatva-śabda-lopaḥ; durniṣpa(ta)-taraṃ—duḥkha-niṣkramaṇa-taram iti arthaḥ \|\| 23 \|\| iti nātivicira-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

**atha anya-adhiṣṭhita-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.1.24 \|\|

*anya-adhiṣṭhite pūrvavat abhilāpāt \| 3 \| 1 \| 24 \|\|*

**Комментарий:**

avarohaṇo jīvāḥ vrīhy-ādi-bhāvena jāyante iti śrūyate vrīhayo bhūtvā prabardhati te iha vrīhītvā oṣadhi-vanaspati-ādi-stambhā iti jāyante iti \| te kiṃ svayaṃ vrīhy-ādi-śarīra-adhiṣṭhitā anu vrīhy-ādi-utpatti-sthānyanti, uta te bhoktāro vrīhy-ādi-śarīroto jāyanta iti viśayaṃ jāyanta iti vacanāt devī jāyate manuṣyo jāyata iti ukta-vrīhy-ādi-śarīra eva; iti prāpte ucyate—anya-adhiṣṭhite iti \| jīvāntaraiṇa-adhiṣṭhite vrīhy-ādi-śarīre devāṃ saṃśleṣa-mātram eva \| kutaḥ ? pūrvavad-abhilāpāt—ākāśa-ādir-deva-paryanta-vakṣemavat vrīhy-abhilāpāt \| yatra hi bhoktṛtva-sambandhitvam ; tatra tat-sādhana bhūtaṃ karma abhilapyate ramaṇīya-caraṇāḥ... kapūya-caraṇāḥ iti \| iha ākāśādivad abhilapyate karma; phala-pradāne pradhānasya svāpi-yogya-phala-śekheḍeḥ karaṇaṃ; svāpi-bhogād eva samānastvāt, anārabhasya ramaṇīya-caraṇāḥ... kapūya-caraṇāḥ iti brahma-yānāt, naṣṭe karman-antara-abhāvāc ca \| ata ākāśādisamavacavat-svavrīhy-ādi-bhāvena janma-vacanam אופacārikam \|\| 24 \|\|

## \|\| 3.1.25 \|\|

*aśuddham iti cen na śabdāt \| 3 \| 1 \| 25 \|\|*

**Комментарий:**

netarasmāt iti atra adhihite vrīhy-ādihite brāhmādi-śarīre saṃśleṣa-mātram, bhoktṛtva-hetu-bhāvāḥ ca brāhmādibhāvena janma, iti; bhoktṛtva-hetu-sañjñānāt \| svargāpavarga-phalopāsṭy-adhi-karma-śrāddham—pāśi-hiṃsam, anīṣādhikṛta-hiṃsā-yuktatvāt \| hiṃsā ca na hiṃsyāt sarvā bhūtāni iti niṣiddhatvāt pāpam eva \| na ca atra pahuyajanīyādi-kṛta-pāpa-kṣayavat sambhavati, bhinna-viṣayatvāt \| anīṣādhīya-hiṃsā-vidhi-hiṃsāyāḥ kratu-upakārakatvaṃ bodhayati; na hiṃsyāt iti tu hiṃsāyāḥ pratyavāya-phalatvam \| atha ucyeta—anīṣādhīyāhiṣu vihitaḥ pravṛttena vahiṣyaṃ nivṛtta-vidyā-rakṣakati, rāga-prāpta-viṣayatvāt sthiti; na evam, iha api rāga-prāter aviśiṣṭatvāt \| svargaṃ kāmo yajeta evam-āde hi kartimnaḥ karttavyatayā yāga-ādau pradeśādhāveḥ svargādi-sādhanatvam avagamya phala-rāgāt eva yāga-ādau pravarttate \| anīṣādhīyādikā api teṣāṃ phala-sādhana-bhūtasya yāgādeḥ prakārakatvaṃ śāstrāt avagamya rāgāt eva pravarttate \| loka-sthānām api hiṃsāyāṃ kevalitarāṇena hiṃsāyāḥ sva-bhoga-hitupādhanatvam avagamya rāgāt pravarttata iti na kaścid viśeṣaḥ \| tathā nityeṣv api karmaṣu sad eva agnīnāṃ svādhīna-sukhena parampararhimitā sukham iti-ādi-vacana-phala-sādhanatvāvagamya rāgāt eva pravṛttir iti teṣām api aśuddhi-yuktatvam \| ata iṣṭa-ādīnāṃ pāp-miśratvena aśuddhy-utpannānāṃ svarga-anubhavaḥ phalaṃ svarga-anubhūya hiṃsādeḥ asya phalaṃ brāhyādi-vyāvṛttsanāvena anubhūyate \| sthāvaratvaṃ ca pāpa-phalaṃ smarati śarīrajaḥ karma-doṣair yāti sthāvaratāṃ naraḥ iti \| ataḥ brāhmādibhavena bhogāya anuśayino jāyanta iti cet; tat na \| kutaḥ? śabdāt—anīṣādhībhir yaded saṃyuktasya svarga-lokeṣṇātir hetutayā hiṃsātmaka-avacchabdāt \| paśoḥ hi saṃjñapana-nimittaṃ svarga-lokaṃ prativedayatantaṃ śabdam āmananti hiraṇya-śarīra ūrdhvaḥ svargaṃ lokam eti ity-ādikam \| aniṣṭhitāśuddhy-asādhana-bhūto vyāpāro bhṛtyukhaṇḍo ’pi, na hiṃsā; pratyuta rakṣaṇam eva \| tathā ca mantra-varṇaḥ—śaṃ vā u ce tāmisrayate na riṣyati, devo devir parimissutehiḥ; yatra yanti sukṛto na api duṣkṛtaḥ tatra tvā deva-savitā dadhātu iti \| cikitsakaṃ ca tātakālikālpaduḥkha-kāraṇam api rakṣakam eva vadanti, pūjayanti ca taj-jñāḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## \|\| 3.1.26 \|\|

*retasi saṃyogaḥ atha*

**Комментарий:**

itaḥ caritvārikṣaṃ vrīhy-ādi-janma vacanam, vrīhy-ādi-bhāva-vacanānantaraṃ yo yo hi annam atti yo retaḥ siñcati tat bhūya eva bhavati iti retassiṅ-bhāvāgraṇāt yajamānāṃ śūramāṇe yathā tat-yoga-mātraṃ pratipādayati; tathad vrīhy-ādi-bhāvaḥ api iti arthaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## \|\| 3.1.27 \|\|

*yoneḥ śarīram*

**Комментарий:**

yoni-prāpteḥ pañca-āhutīnāṃ śarīra-arpitaḥ, tanmaiva sukha-duḥkha-upabhoga-sthūnatvāt \| tataḥ prāṇa-kārya-vibhāgitvam api tat-yoga-sāpekṣatva-artham \|\| 27 \|\| iti anya-adhiṣṭita-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye tṛtīyasya adhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**tṛtīya-adhyāye—dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ—sandhyā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Pada 2

## \|\| 3.2.1 \|\|

*sandhye sṛṣṭiḥ āha hi*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ karma-anurūpa-prabhavana-āgamana-janma-ādi-yogena jāgrataḥ jīvasya duḥkhitvaṃ vyapadiṣṭam; idānīm asya svapna-avasthā parīkṣyate \| svapnam upakramya śrūyate na tatra rathā na ratha-yogā na panthānaḥ bhavanti atha rathān ratha-yogān pathaḥ sṛjate na tatra ānandā mudaḥ pramudaḥ bhavanti atha ānandān mudaḥ pramudaḥ sṛjate na tatra veśāntāḥ puṣkariṇyaḥ sravantyaḥ bhavanti, atha veśāntān puṣkariṇīḥ sravantīḥ sṛjate sa hi kartā iti \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim iyaṃ ratha-ādi-sṛṣṭiḥ jīvena eva kriyate, āhosvit ca īśvareṇa iti \| kiṃ yuktam ? sandhye sṛṣṭiḥ jīvena iti \| kutaḥ ? sandhyaṃ svapna-sthānam ucyate, sandhyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ svapna-sthānam iti vacanāt; sā tu jīvena eva kriyate; sṛjate sa hi kartā āha hi \| svapna-sṛjīva eva tatra pratīyate \|\| 1 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.2 \|\|

*nirmātāraṃ ca eke putra-ādayaḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

kiñca, evaṃ jīvaṃ svapne kāmānāṃ nirmātār eke śākhinaḥ adhīyate sa eṣu supteṣu jāgarti kāmaṃ kāmaṃ puruṣaḥ nirmimāṇaḥ iti \| putra-ādayaḥ ca tatra kāmyamānatayā kāma-śabdena nirdiśyante; na icchā-mātram; pūrvaṃ hi sarvān kāmāṃś chandataḥ prārthayitvā śata-āyuṣaḥ putra-pautrān vṛṇīṣva iti putra-ādayaḥ eva kāmāḥ prakṛtāḥ \| ataḥ ratha-ādīn jīvaḥ svapne sṛjati \| jīvasya ca satya-saṅkalpatvaṃ prajāpati-ti-vākye śrutam; ata upakaraṇa-abhāvāt eva sṛṣṭiḥ upapadyate \|\| 2 \|\|

iti prāpte abhidhīyate—

## \|\| 3.2.3 \|\|

*māyā-mātraṃ tu kārtsnyena anabhivyakta-svarūpatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

tu-śabdaḥ pakṣaṃ vyāvartayati \| svapne ratha-puṣkariṇy-ādi-artha-jātaṃ māyā-mātraṃ—parama-puruṣa-sṛṣṭa-mithyārtham \| māyā-śabdaḥ hi āścarya-vācī \| janakasya kule jātā deva-māyā eva nirmitā iti-ādiṣu tathā varṇanāt \| atha api, na tatra rathā na ratha-yogā na panthāḥ—saṅkalate ratha-yogān subhāvyatayā

na bhavanti iti arthaḥ; atha sthānu khayoga-anvayaḥ sṛjate—svatanu-hānubhavamayā tatkāla-mātrāvasānān sujata iti āśaṅkya āha padmena iva āha \| pūrva-vidyā-pratibaddhā tu hṛta-smṛti-asaṅkucitasya parama-puruṣasyaivaśvaryaṃ, na jīvasya iti \| tasya satya-saṅkalpatvāt iti yuktasya api saṃsāra-daśāyāṃ kātsnyena abhivyakta-svarūpatva-abhāvāt jīvasya tathā-vidha-aśvaryaṃ niṣidhyate \| kāmān kāmī puruṣaḥ nirmimāṇaḥ iti ca parama-puruṣam eva nirmātāram āha, sa eṣu supteṣu jāgarti tad eva śukraṃ tad brahma tad eva amṛtam ucyate \| tasmin lokāḥ śritāḥ sarve tat u nātyeti kaścana iti upakrama-upasaṃhārayoḥ parama-puruṣa-asādhāraṇatva-bhāva-pratīteḥ \| atha yeśānāthāḥ puruṣa-karṣiṇaḥ svapnantaḥ sṛjate sa hi kartā iti ca tayā śrutyā kaścid anaraḥ parama-puruṣa eva kartā iti āha \|\| 3 \|\|

svābhāvikaṃ caijīva-vyāpṛtatvāt tad-vadikam, kṛtsna-saṃsāri-bhivyajyata iti ata āha—

## \|\| 3.2.4 \|\|

*parābhidhyānāt tu tirohitaṃ tataḥ hi asya bandha-viparyayau*

**Комментарий:**

tu-śabdaḥ śrutādi-yukta-arthaḥ; parābhidhyānāt—parama-puruṣa-saṅkalpāt, asya jīvasya svābhāvikaṃ rūpaṃ tirohitam; anādi-karma-paramparayā kṛta-aparādhasya hi asya svābhāvikaṃ kalyāṇa-rūpaṃ paramukulatātirodhāyati; tataḥ tat-saṅkalpāt eva hi asya—jīvasya bandha-mokṣau śrutau yadā hi eva eṣa etasmin adṛśye anātmye anirukte anilayane abhayaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ vindate, atha saḥ abhayaṃ gataḥ bhavati, yadā hi eva eṣa etasmin udaram antaraṃ kurute, atha tasya bhayaṃ bhavati eṣa hi eva ānandayāti śoṣādamāhataḥ pavate iti ādiṣu \|\| 4 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.5 \|\|

*deha-yogāt vā saḥ api*

**Комментарий:**

saḥ api—tirodhāno deha-yoga-dvāreṇa vā bhavati, sūkṣma-acid-ucchriti-yoga-dvāreṇa vā; dṛṣṭi-kāle dehāntareṇa acid-vastunā saṃyoga-abhāvīti, pralaya-kāle nāma-rūpa-vibhāga-anarhita-cid-vastu-yogāt \| ataḥ anabhivyakta-svarūpatvāt svapne jīvaḥ na sṛṣṭi-ādīn saṅkalpa-mātreṇa spaṣṭaṃ śaknoti \| tasmin lokāḥ śritāḥ sarve tat u nātyeti kaścana iti sarveṣu mukteṣu jāgareṣu sarva-loka-āśrayatva-niyantṛtve hi parama-puruṣasya eva saṃbhavataḥ \| ataḥ jīvānānanda-pradaikatamṛguṇakatvānubhāvārthaṃ tāvan-mātra-kāla-avasānāt tad-ekānubhāvānārthaṃ nṛṇām arhati iti \|\| 5 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.6 \|\|

*sūcakaḥ ca hi śruteḥ ācakṣate ca tat-vidaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

itasya svapna-arthā na jīva-prabuddha-pūrvakāḥ; yataḥ svapnaḥ api upamāna-bhūtayoḥ sūcakaḥ śrutair avagamyate—yadā karmaṣu kāmyeṣu striyaṃ svapneṣu paśyati \| samṛddhiṃ tatra jānīyāt tasmin

svapna-darśane \|\| iti; atha svapne puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇa-dantaṃ paśyati sa enaṃ hanti ity-ādiṣu \| svapna-adhyāya-vidiś ca svataṃ śubha-aśubhayoḥ sūcaka-mātra-darśate \| sūcakatvaṃ ca svānuṣṭhātṛ-āyattasya na upapadyate; tathā ca aśubha-aniṣṭha-anubandhasya sūcakena eva dṛṣṭaṃ paryeti \| ataḥ svapne sṛṣṭiḥ īśvareṇa eva kṛtā \|\|6\|\| iti sandhyā-adhikaraṇam \|\|1\|\|

**atha tad-abhāva-adhikaraṇam \|\|2\|\|**

## \|\| 3.2.7 \|\|

*tad-abhāvo nāḍīṣu tac-chruter ātmani ca*

**Комментарий:**

idānīṃ suṣupti-sthānaṃ parīkṣyate \| idam āmnāyate atha ya etat suptaḥ samastaḥ saṃprasannaḥ svapnaṃ na vijānāti āsu tadā nāḍīṣu sṛtaḥ bhavati iti; tathā atha yadā suṣupto bhavati yadā na kasya cana veda hitā nāma nāḍyaḥ dvā-saptati-sahasrāṇi hṛdayāt purītatam abhi-pratiṣṭhante tābhiḥ prati-avasṛpya purītati śete iti; tathā yatra etat puruṣaḥ svapiti nāma satā somya tadā sampanno bhavati iti \| evaṃ nāḍyaḥ purītat brahma ca suṣupti-sthānatvena śrūyate; kim eṣāṃ vikalpaḥ mucchannayo veti viṣaye nirapekṣatva-prāpteḥ yugapat-eka-sthāna-antara-saṃbhavāc ca vikalpaḥ \|\| iti prāpta ucyate—tad-abhāva iti \| tad-abhāvaḥ—svapna-abhāvaḥ suṣuptiḥ nāḍīṣu purītatayātmani ca bhavati; eṣāṃ sthānānāṃ samuccaya iṣyate \| kutaḥ ? tac-chruteḥ—trayāṇāṃ sthānatva-śruteḥ \| na ca kārya-bhedena samuccayo saṃbhavati pāli-krama-adhārmiti-kalpo nyāyyaḥ \| saṃbhavati ca prāsāda-khaṭvā-paryaṅka-brāhmaṇa-ādīnāṃ kārya-bhedaḥ \| tatra nāḍī-purītatī prāsāda-khaṭvā-sthānīyau; brahma tu paryaṅka-sthānīyam \| ato brahma eva sākṣāt suṣupti-sthānam \|\|7\|\|

## \|\| 3.2.8 \|\|

*ataḥ prabodhaḥ asmāt*

**Комментарий:**

yato brahma eva sākṣāt suṣupti-sthānam, ataḥ asmāt brahmaṇaḥ evaṃ jīvānāṃ prabodhaḥ śrūyamāṇa upapadyate sata āgamya na viduḥ sata āgacchāmahe ity-ādiṣu \|\| 8 \|\|

**iti tad-abhāva-adhikaraṇam \|\|2\|\|**

**atha karma-anusmṛti-śabda-vidhibhyaḥ adhikaraṇam \|\|3\|\|**

## \|\| 3.2.9 \|\|

*sa eva tu karma-anusmṛti-śabda-vidhibhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

kiṃ suptam eva prabodha-samaye uttiṣṭhati, uta anya iti saṃśaye, asya sakala-upādhi-vinirmuktasya brahmaṇi sampannasya mukta-vat viśeṣa-adarśanāt prācīna-zarīra-indriya-ādi-sambandha-abhāvād anyaḥ \|\|

iti prāptaḥ ucyate—sa eva tu—iti \| tṛtīyaḥ pakṣaḥ vyāvartyate; sa eva agniḥ; kutaḥ ? karma-anusmṛti-śabda-vidhibhyaḥ \| karma tāvat—mumuṣute pūrva-kṛtaṃ puṇya-pāpa-rūpaṃ tat-saṃcāra-anugrāhakam eva bhoktavyam \| anusmṛtiḥ api—yaḥ eva ahaṃ suptaḥ; sa eva prabuddhaḥ asmi iti \| śabdaḥ api śruta-prabuddhasya eva agni darśayati ta iha vyāghraḥ vā siṃhaḥ vā vṛkaḥ vā varāhaḥ vā kīṭaḥ vā pataṅgaḥ vā daṃśaḥ vā maśakaḥ vā yat yat bhavanti tathā bhavanti iti \| vidhy-artha mokṣārtha-mumuṣutasya muktvā eva idha-artha-kāryaḥ \| na ca asau sarva-upādhi-vinirmukta ātmabhūta-tat-svarūpaḥ tad yatra etaṃ suptaḥ iti suptaṃ prakṛtya, tāha khalv ayaivaṃ saṃprati ātmānaṃ jānāti ayam aham asmi iti no eva imāni bhūtāni vināśam eva apīto bhavati na aham atra bhogyaṃ paśyāmi iti vacanāt \| muktasya ca paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate sa tatra paryeti jakṣat-krīḍan ramamāṇaḥ saḥ svarāṭ bhavati tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu kāma-cāraḥ bhavati sarvaṃ ha paśyaḥ paśyati sarvam āpnoti sarvaśaḥ iti sarva-jñatva-ādi śrūyate \| atasmukta-saṃsaraṇe vā āyata-tat-sarva-karaṇo jñāna-saṃkoca-śāstrī viśrama-sthānaṃ paramātman upasaṃpadya āśrataḥ punar-bhogāya itiṣṭhati iti \|\| 9 \|\|

iti karma-anusmṛti-śabda-vidy-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha mugdha-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.2.10 \|\|

*mugdhe ardha-saṃpattiḥ pariśeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

mugdham adhītya cintyate; kim iyaṃ mūrchā suṣupti-ādi-vyavasthā-sama-avasthā, uta avasthā-antaram iti viśaye suṣupti-ādīnām anyatama-avasthāyām eva mūrchitasya api puṃsatva-avasthā-antara-kalpane pramāṇa-abhāvāt avastham avasthā \| iti prāptaḥ ucyate—mugdhe ardha-saṃpattiḥ iti \| mugdhe puṃse yā tasya avasthā, sā maraṇāya ardha-saṃpattiḥ \| kutaḥ ? pariśeṣāt—na tāvat asyā jāgaraḥ, jñāna-abhāvāt; niḥśeṣa-indriya-vyāpāra-abhāvāt ca na suptiḥ maraṇe \| nimittaṃ hi mūrchāyām abhighāta-ādiḥ \| pariśeṣyā maraṇāya ardha-saṃpattiḥ tu icchā \| maraṇaṃ hi sarva-prāṇa-deha-saṃbandha-uparatiḥ; sūkṣma-prāṇa-deha-saṃbandha-avasthitiḥ tu icchā \|\| 10 \|\|

iti mugdha-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha ubhaya-liṅga-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.2.11 \|\|

*na sthānataḥ api parasya ubhaya-liṅgaṃ sarvatra hi*

**Комментарий:**

doṣa-darśanāt rāma-ādi-upāya jīvasya avasthā-viśeṣā liṅgitaḥ \| īdṛśaṃ brahma api taturaṇājananāṃ prāptyasya brahmaṇo nirdoṣatva-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ātmakatva-vipratipattana-yā ārabhe \| tatra jāgara-svapna-

**tṛtīya-adhyāye—dvitīya-pāde—ubhaya-liṅga-adhikaraṇam \|**

mukta-guṇa-mukti-uktāni tu sthāneṣu tat-sthānām uktā jīvasya ye doṣāḥ, te tat-anantara-bhinnāḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ api tantra-tantra-avasthitasya santi iti, na iti vicāryate \| kiṃ yuktam? santi iti \| kutaḥ? tattva-vat-śarīra-īśvara-sthānāt \| nanu saṃbhoga-prāptiḥ iti cet na vaiśeṣyāt sthitiḥ adanatābhyāṃ ca iti evaṃ-vidhu para-sthāna-arma-bandhavatvena doṣa-abhāva uktaḥ; tat katham akarma-vaśyasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ tattva-sthāna-saṃbandhāt doṣa ucyate? iti ucyate—karmaṇi api deha-saṃbandham āpadyamāna-puruṣārthānānāti bhavati iti deha-yogāt iti atra uktam \| tat ca deha-saṃbandha-puruṣārtha eva bhavati \| itarathā karma-upacaye duḥkhaṃ janayiṣyati iti; kiṃ deha-saṃbandhena? ataḥ akarma-vaśyatve sati api nānā-vidhā-mukti-veha-saṃbandho mukhasya eva \| antaḥ tat-dhiyam anārthaṃ sva-icchayā tattva-arkośya-puruṣārtha-saṃbandhaḥ avajjanīyaḥ \| pūryopṇitādi-majjane hi sva-icchā-kāritam apuruṣārtha eva \| ataḥ yadi api jagat-eka-kāraṇam, sarva-jñatvādi-vikalyāṇa-guṇa-ākaram ca brahma; tathā api yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan ya ātmani tiṣṭhan akṣaram iti ca yaḥ retasi tiṣṭha iti ādi-vacanāt tantra-tantra-avasthitasya tat-tat-saṃbandha-rūpa-puruṣārthiḥ santi iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—na syāt ataḥ api parasya iti \| na pṛthivy-ādi-aniṣṭhānataḥ api parasya brahmaṇaḥ apuruṣārtha-gandha-saṃbhavati \| kutaḥ? ubhaya-liṅgaṃ sarvatra hi—yataḥ sarvatra śruti-smṛtiṣu paraṃ brahma ubhaya-liṅgam—ubhaya-lakṣaṇam ṛṣīyate; nis-samasta-nikhila-heya-pratyanīka-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākaratva-lakṣaṇa-upetam atyarthaḥ \| apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ avijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṃkalpaḥ samasta-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ātmakaḥ asau sva-śakti-leśād yata-bhūta-nutām agaḥ tejo-bala-aiśvarya-mahā-vabodha-suvīrya-śakty-ādi-guṇa-kara-rāśiḥ \| paraḥ parāṇāṃ sakalā na yatra kleśādayaḥ santi parāvara-īśe \|\| samasta-heya-rahitaṃ viṣṇv-ākyaṃ paraṃ padam iti ādi-śruti-smṛtibhyaḥ ubhaya-lakṣaṇaṃ hi brahma avagatam \|\| 11 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.12 \|\|

*bhedāt iti cet na pratyekam atat-vacanāt*

**Комментарий:**

yathā jīvasya prajāpati-vivakṣyāvātāmanṛtatva-matsyā-abhaya-liṅgasya api deva-ādi-deha-yoga-rūpa-avastha-bhedāt puruṣārtha-yogaḥ; tathā anantara-bhinnāḥ parasya api svataḥ apahata-pāpmatvādi-ubhaya-liṅgasya tat-tat-deva-ādi-śarīra-yoga-anurūpa-avasthā-bhedāt puruṣārtha-yoge go-dohana-nyāyaḥ iti cet, tat na; pratyekam atat-vacanāt—yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan ya ātmani tiṣṭhati iti-ādibhiḥ prati-paryāyaṃ yaṃ ta ātmanaḥ anvācyaṃ mṛtaḥ iti antara-bhinna-udbhūta-tattva-vacanena tantra-tantra sva-icchayā niyamanaṃ kurvataḥ tat-tat-saṃbandham uktā-puruṣārthatiḥ vidhāt \| jīvasya tu tat-tat-svarūpaṃ tirohita iti para-abhidhyānāt tu tirohitaṃ tirokṣam \| nanu sva-icchayā kurvataḥ api tatad-duḥkha-sambhavāt puruṣārtha-saṃbandhaḥ avajjanīya iti uktam; na etat dṛṣṭam; na hi aviduṣaḥ api sva-bhāvataḥ apuruṣārtha-svarūpam; karma-vaśyānāṃ tu karma-svabhāva-tulyena

parama-puruṣa-sukha-duḥkha-upādāne eke eva vastu kāla-bhedena puruṣa-bhedena ca sukhāya duḥkhāya ca bhavati; vastu-svarūpa-muktaṃ tu tat-tulye sarvaṃ sarvadā sarvasya sukhāya eva duḥkhāya eva vā syāt; na ca evaṃ dṛśyate \| tathā ca uktam—naraka-ravāse vai pāpa-puṇye dvija-uttama \| vastu eva eka eva duḥkhāya sukhāya iṣṭāniṣāya ca \| kopāya ca prasādāya ca jāyate \| tat eva prītyai bhūtvā punaḥ duḥkhāya jāyate \| tat eva kopāya yataḥ prasādāya ca jāyate \| tasmāt duḥkha-ātmakaṃ nāsti na ca kiñcit sukha-ātmakam \|\| iti \| ataḥ jaivyasya karma-vaśya-vāsta-karma-anuguṇena tat-tat-sukha-duḥkha-hetuḥ parama-puruṣārtha-saṃyogaḥ; parasya tu brahmaṇaḥ svādhīnasya sa eva sambandhaḥ tat-siddhi-vicitra-niyamana-hetutayā ila-artha-mātraṃ syāt \|\| 12 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.13 \|\|

*api ca evam eke*

**Комментарий:**

api ca eke śākhinaḥ ekasmin eva deha-saṃyoge jīva-parama-puruṣārthaṃ parasya tu tat-abhāvaṃ niyamana-rūpa-varya-gata-tat-indriya-yogaṃ ca svā-śabdaiḥ adhīyate dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte \| tayoḥ anyaḥ pippalaṃ svādu atti anaśnan anyaḥ abhicākaśīti \|\| iti \|\| 13 \|\| atha syāt—anena jīvātmanām ā-puruṣasya nāma-ākhye vyākaravāṇi iti brahma-ātmaka-jīva-anupraveśa-pūrvakaṃ nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇam iti brahmaṇaḥ api tat-ātmaka-bhūtasya deva-manuṣya-ādi-śarīpatvaṃ tathā samākhyatvaṃ ca asti, tatra ca brāhmaṇaḥ yajeta iti-ādi-vidhi-niṣedha-śāstra-ucchavena karma-vaśyatva-varjanīya-gam iti; tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.2.14 \|\|

*arūpa-vat eva hi tat-pradhānatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

deva-ādi-śarīra-anupraveśe tena tena rūpeṇa yuktam api arūpa-vat eva tat u—brahma rūpa-rahita-tulyam eva; jīva-vat śarīriḥ vacani-bandhanaṃ karma-vaśyatvam asya na vidyate ity arthaḥ \| kutaḥ? nirdehiṃkukṛtena pradhānatvāt \| ākāśaḥ ha vai nāma-rūpayoḥ nirvahitā te yat antarā tat yat brahma iti sarva-anupraveśaḥ api nāma-rūpa-kāryi-rūpeṇa nāma-rūpayoḥ voḍhṛtvam eva brahmaṇaḥ pratipādayati \| nanu tat-śarīratvena tat-atvarśitvaṃ katham arūpa-vat iti rūpa-saṃbandha-rahita-tulyatvam ucyate? iti atra—yathā jīvasya tat-tat-janma-sukha-duḥkha-oga-bhāreṇa tat-sūtraṃ-sambandhaḥ, tathā tat-akṣatvāt tat-parya-rūpa-vaccatvam \| vidhi-niṣedha-śāstra-arthaṃ karma-vaśyam eva adhikṛtam iti \| tasmāt arūpakam eva paraṃ brahma \| tat-tat-prānatvābhi-rūpeṇa avasthitam api brahma nirasta-nikhila-doṣatva-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākaratva-rūpa-moryādi-liṅgam eva \|\| 14 \|\| nanu ca satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti avidhi-niviśeṣa-prakāśa-eka-rūpaṃ brahma avagamyate, anyatra sarva-jñatva-satya-saṅkalpatva-jagat-kāraṇatva-sarva-niyamana-sva-avasthūkūla-mata-vārtikaṃ neti neti iti-ādibhiḥ pratividdhyamānatvena mithyā-bhūta-miśra-vāntatva iṣyam \| tat kathaṃ kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākaratva-nirasta-nikhila-doṣatva-rūpa-maryādi-liṅga-rūpaṃ brahmaṇaḥ? iti ata āha—

## \|\| 3.2.15 \|\|

*prakāśavat ca avaiyarthyāt*

**Комментарий:**

yathā satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti ādi-vākya-avaiyarthya-prakāśa-svarūpatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ abhyupagamyate tathā sat-saṅkalpatva-saṃvittva-jagat-kāraṇatva-sarva-niyantṛtva-nirasta-nikhilāvidyādi-dopādhi-vidhāyi-vākya-avaiyarthyāt upama-liṅgam eva brahma \|\| 15 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.16 \|\|

*āha ca tan-mātram*

**Комментарий:**

kiñca satyaṃ jñānam anantam iti ādi-vākyaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prakāśa-svarūpatā-mātraṃ pratipādayati, na anyat satyatva-buddhatvādikaṃ vākyāntara-avagataṃ niṣedhati \| iti neti iti ca niṣedhasya viṣayaḥ anantaram eva vakṣyate \|\| 16 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.17 \|\|

*darśayati ca atho api smaryate*

**Комментарий:**

darśayati ca vedānta-gaṇaḥ kalyāṇa-guṇākara-tvaṃ nirasta-nikhila-doṣatvaṃ ca tam īśvarāṇāṃ paraṃ maheśvaraṃ taṃ devatānāṃ paraṃ ca daivataṃ \| sa kāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipādhipo na ca asya kaścit janitā na ca adhipaḥ \| na tasya kāryaṃ karaṇaṃ ca vidyate na tat-samaḥ ca abhyadhikaḥ ca dṛśyate \| parā asya śaktiḥ vividhā eva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca \|\| satya-saṅkalpa-saṃvit yadya jñānamayaṃ tapaḥ bhīṣā asmāt vātaḥ pavate, bhīṣā udeti sūryaḥ sa eko brahmaṇa ānandaḥ yato vāco nivartante, aprāpya manasā saha, ānandaṃ brahmaṇaḥ vidvān, na bibheti kutaścaneti niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāntaṃ niravadyaṃ nirañjanam iti ādi \| smaryate ca yo māmājanmādi ca vetti loka-maheśvaram viṣṭabhya aham idaṃ kṛtsnam ekāṃśena sthito jagat mayā adhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate sa-carācaram \| hetunā anena kaunteya jagat-viparivartate \|\| janmaḥ puṣkara-sambhavaḥ parama-kṣetrajñaḥ ahaḥ \| yo loka-trayam āviśya bibharti avyaya īśvaraḥ sarvasya ca ahaṃ hṛdi sanniviṣṭo mattaḥ smṛtiḥ jñānam apohanaṃ ca \| vedaiḥ ca sarvaiḥ aham eva vedyo vedānta-kṛt veda-vit eva ca aham \| asṛjyaḥ ca prabhaviṣṇuḥ ca svādhīnaḥ ajastram āśrayaḥ \| kṣetra-jñaḥ sarva-bhūtānāṃ paraḥ śāstā niradhiro'dharaḥ kramaḥ iti ādi \| ataḥ sarvaiḥ avasthitasya api brahmaṇa upama-liṅga-svāgata-tathya-mātram uktā doṣā na paraṃ brahma spṛśanti iti \|\| 17 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.18 \|\|

*ata eva ca upamā sūrya-kādivat*

**Комментарий:**

yato nānā-vidheṣu sthāneṣu sthitasya api parasya brahmaṇo na tatra yukta-doṣa-sambandhaḥ, ata eva jala-indvādi-pratibimba-sūryādi-vat upamānā tatra-tatra-avasthitataḥ api nirdoṣa iti śāstreṣu upamā kriyate—ākāśam ekaṃ hi yathā ghaṭādiṣu pṛthak bhavet, tathātmako hi aneka-stho jalādhāre-

viśva-aṇumāt \|\| ekaḥ eva hi bhūta-ātmā bhūte-bhūte vyavasthitaḥ \| ekadhā bahudhā ca eva dṛśyate jala-candra-vat \|\| iti-ādiṣu \|\| 18 \|\|

**atra codayati—**

## \|\| 3.2.19 \|\|

*ambu-vat-agrahaṇāt tu na tathātvam*

**Комментарий:**

tulya-śabdaḥ ayam dṛṣṭa-arthe \| ambu-vat iti samanvayāt vyatiḥ \| ambu-darpaṇa-ādiṣu yathā sūrya-mukha-ādayaḥ gṛhyante; na tathā pṛthivī-ādiṣu sthāneṣu paramātmā gṛhyate \| ambu-ādiṣu hi sūrya-ādayaḥ bhrāntyā tat-tathyaḥ eva gṛhyante, na paramārtha-sat-tathyaḥ \| iha tu brahma pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan brahmāṇḍa-sthaṃ tiṣṭhan eva ātmani nikhila-cid-acinā paramārthataḥ eva paramātmā pṛthivī-ādiṣu sthitaḥ gṛhyate \| atas tulya-hetu-ambu-darpaṇa-ādiṣu ukta-udāharaṇa-vastu-tantra sthiti-abhāvāt eva \| ataḥ na tathā tathātvam—śārīrikasya na hi aṇu-tulya-vimitatva-arthaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

**pariharati—**

## \|\| 3.2.20 \|\|

*vṛddhi-hrāsa-bhāktvam antar-bhāvāt ubhaya-sāmañjasyāt evaṃ darśanāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

pṛthivī-ādi-sthāna-nānātva-avasthitinaḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ svabhāvataḥ guṇataḥ ca pṛthivī-ādi-sthānagata-vṛddhi-hrāsa-ādi-doṣa-bhāvavantaṃ sūrya-ādi-cetana-itara-nivartate \| katham itat samāpyate ? ubhaya-sāmañjasyāt evam—ubhaya-dṛṣṭānta-sāmañjasyāt evam iti nirṇīyate \| ākāśam ekam hi yathā ghaṭa-ādiṣu pṛthanm eva jala-ādi-akhila-vastu-niṣṭham iti dāṣṭāntyena eṣa vastuṃ vahati; tad-avasthitatayā ākāśasya, vastutaḥ anavasthitatayā tu sārūpya-sacaitanyasya dṛṣṭānta-dvaya-upādānaṃ hi paramātmanaḥ pṛthivī-ādi-gata-doṣa-bhāva-nivartana-mātre pratipādye samañjasaṃ bhavati \| ghaṭa-karka-ādiṣu tathā vṛddhi-hrāsa-bhāsu pṛthag-bhūta-saṃyamanānavakāśaṃ vṛddhi-hrāsa-ādi-doṣaiḥ na spṛśyate; yathā ca jala-āśāreṣu viṣameṣu hrasva-māna-utoṅga-smāt tad-gata-vṛddhi-hrāsa-timiraiḥ spṛśyate; tathā ayam paramātmā pṛthivī-ādiṣu nānā-ākāra-iccha-tanveṣu caitanyeṣu ca sthita-tat-tad-gata-vṛddhi-hrāsa-ādi-doṣaiḥ asaṃspṛṣṭaḥ samvatra vartamānaḥ api ekaḥ eva-rūpa-doṣa-anvayaḥ kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākaraḥ eva \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—yathā jala-ādiṣu bhṛgutaḥ anavasthitatayā sṛṣṭanaḥ hetu-abhāva-jala-ādi-viśeṣa-nirbhinnaḥ; tathā pṛthivī-ādi-vavasthitasya api paramātmanaḥ doṣa-pracyanikārastayā doṣa-hetu-abhāvāt atra doṣa-saṃbandhaḥ iti \| darśanāt ca—dṛśyate ca evaṃ sarvatra sādharmebhyaḥ api viśiṣṭatā-sādharmya-dṛṣṭānta-upādānaṃ, siṃhaḥ iva māṇavakaḥ iti-ādi \| ataḥ svabhāvataḥ nirasta-nikhila-jñāna-ādi-doṣa-gandhasya samasta-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākarasya pṛthivī-ādi-sthānataḥ api na doṣa-saṃbhavaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

atha syāt—śraddhe vāva brahmaṇo rūpe mūrtaṃ ca eva amūrtaṃ ca iti prakṛtya samastaṃ sthūla-sūkṣma-rūpaṃ prapañcaṃ brahmaṇo rūpatvena paramukhya śrautasya ha etasya puruṣasya rūpaṃ yathā mahā-rajanaṃ vāsaḥ iti ādinā ākāra-viśeṣaṃ ca abhidhāya atha ata ādeśo neti neti na hi etasmāt iti neti anyat param asti iti sarvaṃ prakṛtaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prakārataḥ sthitibhyāṃ paramukhya tattvaṃ pratiṣidhya sarva-viśeṣa-vidhuraṃ san-mātram eva brahma; viśeṣaś ca evaṃ-vidhaṃ svarūpam ajānātā brahmaṇā kalpitā iti darśayati; ataḥ katham ucyate akhaṇḍaṃ brahma iti \| atrāha—

## \|\| 3.2.21 \|\|

*prakṛta-etāvattvaṃ hi pratiṣedhati tato bravīti ca bhūyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

neti neti-ādeḥ—yad brahmaṇaḥ prakṛta-viśeṣavattvaṃ neti neti iti pratiṣidhyata iti, tathā sati śānta-jvalita-āyamānattvāt \| na hi brahmaṇi viśeṣaṇatayā pramāṇāntara-ajñāte sarvaṃ tad-viśeṣatvena upadiśya punas tad eva anunmataḥ pratiṣedhati \| yad api nirdiṣṭam nāmeṣu kaścana padārthaḥ pramāṇāntara-prasiddhaḥ, tathāpi teṣāṃ brahmaṇaḥ prakāratvam aprasiddham eva; itareṣāṃ tu svayaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prakāratvaṃ ca ajñātam \| atas teṣām anuvāda-asaṃbhavād atra eva upadiśyante \| atas tad-viśeṣo nopaśyate \| yasmād evaṃ, tasmāt prakṛta-etāvattvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pratiṣedhati iti vācyam \| ye brahmaṇo viśeṣāḥ prakṛtāḥ; tad-viśiṣṭatayā brahmaṇaḥ pratīyamānatayā neti neti iti pratiṣidhyate \| neti neti—nāyaṃ na itaraḥ, ukta-prakāra-mātra-viśiṣṭaṃ na bhavati brahma; ukta-prakāra-viśiṣṭasya yā brahmaṇi heyatā prakṛtā; sā atra iti-śabdena parāmṛśyata iti arthaḥ \| pratyakṣa niṣedha-anantaraṃ brahmaṇo bhūyo guṇa-jātaṃ bravīti, pratyakṣa prakṛta-viśeṣaṇa-yogitva-mātraṃ brahmaṇaḥ pratiṣedhati \| bravīti hi bhūyo guṇa-jātam—na hi etasmāt iti neti anyat param asti atha nāmadheyaṃ satyasya satyam iti prāṇā vai satyaṃ teṣām eṣa satyam iti \| ayam arthaḥ—iti neti yad uktaṃ pratipāditam, tasmād etasmād apy adhikaṃ paraṃ na hi asti; brahmaṇo’py atu svabhāvato guṇāśrayatvaṃ nāsti iti arthaḥ \| tasya ca brahmaṇaḥ satyasya satyam iti nāmadheyam \| tasya ca nirvacanaṃ prāṇā vai satyaṃ teṣām eṣa satyam iti \| prāṇāś cetana-prāṇa-sāhacarya-jīvāḥ paramṛśyante \| te tāvat satyam, viyad-ādi-vistara-rūpatayā sthāvara-rūpa-pariṇāma-abhāvāt \| teṣām eva satyam—teṣv apy eṣa parama-puruṣaḥ satyam \| jīvānāṃ karma-anuguṇyena jñāna-saṅkoca-vikāsau vidyete; parama-puruṣasya tu apahata-pāpmatvato na vidyete; atato’py asāv eva satyam \| ataś ca vākyāśeṣa-viniguṇajāti-ayogāt neti netīti brahmaṇaḥ saviśeṣatvaṃ na pratiṣidhyate; api tu pūrva-prakāra-ukta-mātram \| ata upapannam eva paraṃ brahma \|\| 21 \|\|

brahmaṇaḥ pramāṇāntara-agocaratvena tat-saṃbandhitayā sūtritramūrti-dṛṣṭānta-anuvādena tad-viśeṣa-asaṃbhavāt prakṛta-etāvattva-pratiṣedha uktaḥ; tad eva pramāṇāntara-agocaratvaṃ draḍhayati—

## \|\| 3.2.22 \|\|

*taddhyaktam āha hi*

**Комментарий:**

tat—brahma pramāṇa-antareṇa na vyajyate; āha hi śāstraṃ—ainaṃ sandhe tiṣṭhati rūpam asya na cakṣuṣā paśyati kaścana enam enaṃ cakṣuṣā gṛhyate na api vācā iti-ādi \|\|22\|\|

daivavatvābhāḥ—

## \|\| 3.2.23 \|\|

*api saṃrādhane pratyakṣa-anumānābhyām*

**Комментарий:**

api ca saṃrādhane—samyag ārādhane bhakti-rūpa-upāsane nirdiśyāsana ekasya sākṣāt-kāraḥ; na anyatra iti śruti-nirdiśyam avagamyate \| na ayam ātmā pravacanena labhyaḥ na medhayā na bahunā śrutena \| yam eva eṣa vṛṇute tena labhyaḥ tasya eṣa ātmā vivṛṇute tanūṃ svām \|\| jñāna-prasādena viśuddha-sattvaḥ tataḥ tu taṃ paśyati (te) niṣkalaṃ dhyāyamānaḥ iti śrutiḥ \| smṛtiḥ api na ahaṃ vedaiḥ na tapasā na dānena na ca ijyayā bhaktyā tu ananyayā śakyaḥ aham evaṃ-vidhaḥ arjuna \| jñātuṃ draṣṭuṃ ca tattvena praveṣṭuṃ ca parantapa \|\| iti \| bhakti-rūpa-upāsanaṃ saṃrādhanaṃ tasya śrotrānmiti pūrvam iva uktam \| ataḥ nirdiśyāsanāya brahma-svarūpa-vṛhattvāt idaṃ vāva brahmaṇaḥ iti-ādi-śāstraṃ brahmaṇo mūrta-amūrta-rūpayā avidhi-vivakṣitaṃ grāhitaṃ na anupapattiḥ iti kṣamam \|\|23\|\|

## \|\| 3.2.24 \|\|

*prakāśa-ādi-vat ca avaiśeṣyaṃ prakāśaḥ ca karma-abhyāsāt*

**Комментарий:**

itaḥ ca prakṛtau ca etat prativedhitam; na mūrta-amūrta-ādi-viśiṣṭhasya; yataḥ sākṣāt-kṛta-para-brahma-svarūpāṇāṃ vāmadeva-ādināṃ darśane prakāśa-ādi-vat—jñāna-ānanda-ādi-vat-svakasmūrtāmūrtādiviśiṣṭatayā api brahma-puṇḍarīka-avekṣyaṃ pratīyate tad dha etat paśyan ṛṣiḥ vāmadevaḥ pratipede ahaṃ manuḥ abhavaṃ sūryaḥ ca iti-ādi \| brahma-svarūpa-bhūta-prakāśa-ānanda-ādiṣu iva teṣāṃ vāmadeva-ādināṃ saṃrādhana-ātmaka karma-abhyāsāt upalabhyate \| tasmāt ca avasthā-antara-saṃrādhānāṃ teṣāṃ mūrta-amūrta-ādi-viśiṣṭha-samadhyāviśeṣeṇa pratīyata ity-arthaḥ \|\|24\|\|

**uktaṃ brahmaṇaḥ ubhaya-liṅgatvam upasaṃharati—**

## \|\| 3.2.25 \|\|

*ataḥ anantena tathā hi liṅgam*

**Комментарий:**

ataḥ—ubhaya-hetubhiḥ brahmaṇaḥ anantena kalyāṇa-guṇa-gaṇena viśiṣṭatvaṃ siddham \| tathā hi sati ubhaya-liṅgaṃ brahma-upāsyaṃ bhavati \|\|25\|\| iti ubhaya-liṅga-adhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|

**atha ahi-kuṇḍala-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.2.26 \|\|

*ubhaya-vyapadeśāt tu ahi-kuṇḍala-vat*

**Комментарий:**

mūrti-amūrti-rūpasya acit-vastu-prakarasya brahmaṇo rūpatvaṃ śrūyate vā va brahmaṇo rūpe iti-ādinā upadiśyate \| śrūyate \| atha ata ādeśo neti neti iti mūrta-amūrta-acit-vastukatvayā brahmaṇo ’yatā prativiṣyate \| na hi etasmāt iti neti neti anya-param asti iti brahmaṇo ’mūrta-kuṇḍaṃ na hi asti iti pratiṣādhyate \| tad-upapādanāya atha nāmadheyaṃ satyasya satyam iti prāṇā vai satyaṃ teṣām eṣa satyam iti prāṇa-śabda-nirdiṣṭebhyaś cetanebhyo ’pi eṣa satyam iti kutaś cic cid-jñāna-ādi-sad-guṇa-ādhāra-bhūtatvam \| tathā ādhānaśaicitra-pariṇuṇeṣuḥ iti viśeṣy-ātmakebhyas nityo nityānāṃ cetanaś cetanānām iti-ādi-śruteś ca ayam arthaḥ avagamyate \| tasmāt cid-vastuno brahma-rūpatva-prakāraḥ idānīṃ cintyate, brahmaṇo nirdeśa-vyatirikta-artham \| kim asya acit-vastuno brahma-rūpatvam ahi-kuṇḍala-nyāyena, uta prabhā-prabhāvatoḥ iva bandana-atiyogena, uta jaivaśya eva viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣyatayā aṃśa-aṃśi-bhāvena iti \| iha sthāvarāṇāṃ viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāvam ālambya prakṛtiś ca pratiṣṭādṛṣṭānta tu purodhāya tad-ananyatvam ārabhya ca ityasya sūkṣma-cid-vicid-vastu-viśiṣṭātmaka-brahmaṇaḥ sthūla-cid-vicid-vastu-viśiṣṭasya upaniṣad-anyatvaṃ ca uktam \| kiṃ yuktam? ahi-kuṇḍala-vat iti; kutaḥ? ubhaya-vyapadeśāt— aham eva idaṃ sarvam ātmā eva idaṃ sarvam iti tādātmya-vyapadeśāt, hanta aham imās tisro devatā anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi iti bheda-vyapadeśāc ca \| ataḥ kuṇḍala-bhāva-śūnya-bhāvāt tathaiva brahmaṇaḥ saṃsthāna-viśeṣaḥ eva acit-vastu iti \|\| 26 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.27 \|\|

*prakāśāśraya-vad vā tejas-tvāt*

**Комментарий:**

vā-śabdaḥ pakṣa-vyāvṛtti-arthaḥ; brahma-svarūpaś cid-acid-ubhaya-avasthāne bheda-śrutayo brahmaṇo ’pariṇāmitva-avirodhi bādhitā bhaveyuḥ \| ato yathā tejas-teva prabhātad-vayogi-niṣprayogi tādātmyam; evam acit-prakarasya brahmaṇo rūpatvam ity-arthaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.28 \|\|

*pūrva-vad vā*

**Комментарий:**

vā-śabdaḥ pakṣa-dvayāhāra-arthaḥ \| ekasya eva dravyasya avasthā-viśeṣa-yoge ’pi brahma-svarūpatva-acid-rūpatva-yukta doṣa-nirmokṣaḥ \| atha prabhātad-vayor iva acid-brahmaṇor brahma-etad-vātiyoga-mātram; evaṃ tarhi īśvara-gocaratva-brahmāpāśraye cic-cid-vastuno ’śrayatva-mātraṃ sāmānyam iti sakala-śruti-smṛti-vyavahāra-virodhaḥ \| pūrva-vad eva—aṅgo nānā-vyapadeśāt prakāśādivat tu na evaṃ paraḥ iti jīva-vat pṛthag-bhūta-brahmaṇor viśeṣaṇatvena cid-vastuno brahma-prakāraḥ \| viśiṣṭasya ekatvād avibhāgena bheda-vyavahāro mukhyaḥ; viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣyayoḥ svarūpaikya-bhāvena abheda-vyavahāro mukhyaḥ; brahmaṇi

nirdeśakatvaṃ ca rakṣitam \| tad evaṃ prakāśa-jāti-guṇa-śarīrāṇāṃ maṇi-jvalana-paśu-padmanaḥ pratyupasthita-cid-vilakṣaṇa-viśeṣaṇatayā tathā-śabdasya, taheva jīvasya acid-vastu-sūkṣma brahma pratyantaram \|\| 28 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.29 \|\|

*pratiṣedhāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

sa vā eṣa mahān aja ātmā ajaro ’maraḥ nāyaṃ jarayā etat jīryati ity-ādibhiḥ brahmaṇaḥ acid-vastu-saṃsarga-pratiṣedhāt ca viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭa-dehena eva āhita-bhāva ity arthaḥ \| atas sūkṣma-cid-acid-vastu-viśiṣṭaṃ kāraṇa-bhūtaṃ brahma, sthūla-cid-acid-vastu-viśiṣṭaṃ kārya-bhūtaṃ brahma iti kāraṇa-kārya-vyananyatvasya, kāraṇa-bhūta-brahma-vijñānena kāryasya jñātatā ity-ādi sarvam upapannaṃ; brahmaṇaḥ nirdoṣatvaṃ ca rakṣitam \| brahmaṇaḥ nirdoṣatvena kalyāṇa-guṇākaratvena ca ubhaya-liṅgatvam api siddham \|\| 29 \|\|

iti ahi-kuṇḍala-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha para-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.2.30 \|\|

*param ataḥ setūnmāna-saṃbandha-bheda-vyapadeśebhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

dṛṣṭānta-upasaṃhāra-prabhañjana-adhimitiḥ pādāntarūpa-prakaraṇāt paraṃ-brahmaṇaḥ param api kiñcit tattvam asti iti kecid-vedānta-bhāsitarāśrūya nirākriyate, adhyopāsyasya nirdeṣatvānadhikātiśayāsahūnve caikatyā-guṇākaratvasthene \| tatra evam āśaṅkā— yad idaṃ paraṃ brahma ubhaya-liṅgam; etasmād ikila jagat-kāraṇāt param api kiñcit tattvam asti \| katham? atha ya ātmā sa setu-vidhṛtiḥ iti asya parasya setutva-vyapadeśāt, setu-śabdasya ca loke kūlāntara-prāpti-hetau prasiddheḥ ato ’nyenaina prāpyam asti iti gamyate \| tathā evaṃ setu tīrtvā ’ndhassanandho bhavati iti taritavyatā ca asyābhidhīyate, antavānatvād avadhivahitāt \| unmāna-vyapadeśāt ca—ucmitaṃ—

parimitaṃ idaṃ paraṃ brahma, catuṣ-pād-brahma ṣoḍaśa-kalaṃ iti unmānatva-vyadeśāt \| sambandham unmāna-vyapadeśāt ena setunā prāpyasya anumitasya sthāpitas tato hetv iti \| tathā sambandha-vyapadeśāt ca setu-setumatoḥ prāpakatva-prāpyatva-lakṣaṇo dṛśyate amṛtasya paraṃ setuṃ dagdha-indhanam iva analam amṛtatvasya setuḥ iti \| ataś ca parāt param asti \| bhedena ca parāt paraṃ vyapadiśyate parāt paraṃ puruṣam upaiti parāt paraṃ yan mahato mahāntam iti ca \| tathā tena idaṃ pūrṇaṃ puruṣeṇa sarvaṃ tato yad uttaratare tat arūpam anāmayam iti \| ata eṣyo hetubhyaḥ paramāt brahmaṇaḥ param api kiñcid asti iti gamyate iti \|\| 30 \|\|

evaṃ prāpte ’bhidhīyate—

## \|\| 3.2.31 \|\|

*sāmānyāt tu*

**Комментарий:**

tu-śabdaḥ pakṣaṃ vyāvartayati \| pratipādukte setutva-vyapadeśāparamātparam asti iti; tan nopapadyate \|

na hi ayam atikrānta-śravaḥ prati setuḥ chidyate, evaṃ lokān asaṃbhedāya iti setu-sāmānyena sarva-lokā-sadṛśa-karatva-śruteḥ \| smarati—brahma iti svargam sarvaṃ viśva-viśva-sṛj-ātma-saṃhṛtau bhavati iti setuḥ chidyate \| evaṃ setu tīrtva iti taratiḥ ca prāpti-vacanaḥ \| yathā vedāntaṃ tarati iti \|\| 31 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.32 \|\|

*buddhy-arthaḥ pādavat*

**Комментарий:**

godyaṃ vana-dhupākhyena gocarākhyam pādokhya viśā bhūtāni iti umanātmaty-apadeśaḥ; sa buddhy-arthaḥ—upāsanārthaḥ; satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti ādi nirjñānākāraṇasya brahmaṇaḥ aparicchinnatvābhimānatvavat darśitana-tavāsaṃbhavāt \| jagat-kāraṇatvaṃ hi tasya eva śrūyate—tanmṛṣṭvā \| etasmāt ātmanaḥ ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ ślokam avat, bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti iti \| ato yathā vāgvādaḥ prāṇaḥ pādaḥ cakṣuḥ pādo manaḥ pādaḥ iti ādinā brahmaṇo braṇādit-pādatva-padeśa upāsanārthaḥ, evam ayam api \|\| 32 \|\|

svayam abhinnasya kathaṃ upāśanārthatā iti āśaṅkyā na saṃbhavaḥ; tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.2.33 \|\|

*sthāna-viśeṣāt prakāśādivat*

**Комментарий:**

pratipannāgādhiṣṭhāna-viśeṣa-rūpopādhi-bhedānatvaṃ tad-vihitopimita-vatāguṇa-sthānaṃ saṃbhavati; yathā prakāśākāśādinatasya vātyayana-ghaṭādi-sthāna-bhedaiḥ paricchedyānuguṇa-sthāna-saṃbhava iti arthaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.34 \|\|

*upapatteḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

yad uktaṃ amṛta-rūpaṃ setuḥ iti pratyag-ātmaka saṃbandhy-avyapadeśāt rūpakāntaṃ prāpyam asti iti; tatra, prāpyasya parasya puruṣasya svātmā eva svayaṃ upāyatvopapatteḥ \| na ayam ātmā pravacanena labhyo na medhayā na bahunā śrutena \| yam eva eṣa vṛṇute tena labhyaḥ tasya eva ātmā vivṛṇute tanuṃ svām \|\| iti anya-upāyatva-śravaṇāt ca \|\| 34 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.35 \|\|

*tathā anya-pratiṣedhāt*

**Комментарий:**

yad punar uktaṃ tato yad uttaraṃ parāparaṃ puruṣam akṣarāt ca tataḥ paraḥ iti ādibhir vedhya-vedeśvarāparāpara-samastādhikaiḥ; tatra upapadyate, tatra eva tatopaghamya parasya pratiṣedhāt, yasmāt paraṃ na aparam asti kiñcid yasmān na aṇīyo na jyāyaḥ asti kaścit iti \| yasmād aparaṃ paraṃ na asti kiñcit—na kena api prakāreṇa parasmādīyarthaḥ \| tathā anyasya api na hi etasmāt iti neti anyat para-

satiś ca iti—iti hi iti nirvidya etad-ātmyaṃ brahma gauṇya-parvaṃ na hi asti iti arthaḥ \| tathā sat tat sṛjya kṣatram tasya nāma mahat-yaśaḥ iti \| tat dhi jagat-upādāna-kāraṇatayā anantara-śrute sat māṃ nimeṣā jānir vidyutaḥ puruṣāt adhi, sat āpaḥ prabhṛti ūme ime iti ādinā \| adbhyaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ hiraṇya-garbhaḥ iti eṣaḥ iti ca jagat-kāraṇaṃ puruṣam eva pratipādayati \| tataḥ yat uttaratarat iti kiṃcit iti ca it; pūrvā eva devāhaṃtaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntam āditya-varṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt \| tam eva viditvā ati-mṛtyum eti na anyaḥ panthā vidyate ayanāya \|\| iti parasya brahmaṇo mahā-puruṣasya veda-gamya-amṛtatva-sādhanam, nārāyaṇa-anuvākasya pathā iti upadiśya tat-upapādanāya yasmāt paraṃ na aparam asti kiñcit yasmāt na aṇīyaḥ na jyāyaḥ asti kaścit \| vṛkṣa iva stabdhaḥ divi tiṣṭhati ekaḥ tena idaṃ pūrṇaṃ puruṣeṇa sarvam \|\| iti puruṣasya paratvaṃ, tat-anvaya-atiriktasya parasya asaṃbhavaṃ pratipādya, tataḥ yat uttarataṃ tat arūpam anāmayam \| ye etat viduḥ amṛtāḥ te bhavanti atha itare duḥkham eva api yanti iti pūrva-uktasya hetutaḥ nigamayati—yat uttarataṃ puruṣa-tattvam, tat eva arūpam anāmayaṃ yataḥ; tataḥ ye etat puruṣa-tattvaṃ viduḥ; te eva amṛtā bhavanti, atha itare duḥkham eva api yanti—iti \| anyathā prakrama-virodhāt anantara-ukti-nivirodhaḥ ca \| parāt-paraṃ puruṣam upaiti divyam iti pūrvaṃ akṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ iti, akṣarāt-avyaktāt yaḥ paraḥ—sarva-adhiṣṭhaḥ; tasmāt paraḥ yaḥ akṣara-vyatirikta-mukta-saṃjñaḥ parama-puruṣaḥ; sa eva iha api parāt-para iti samabhivyāhāraḥ pratyabhijñāyate \|\| 35 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.36 \|\|

*anena sarva-gatatvam āyāma-śabdādibhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

anena-brahmaṇā, sarva-gatatva-sarvasya jagataḥ vyāptatvam, āyāma-śabdādibhyaḥ—sarva-vyāpti-vācibhyaḥ vedyair jagan-mānasam anantaraṃ na asti iti avagamyate \| āyāma-śabdat tāvat, etāvat pūrṇaṃ puruṣeṇa sarvam iti kaścit jagati asmin dṛśyate śrūyate api vā \| antar-bahis tat sarvaṃ vyāpya nārāyaṇaḥ sthitaḥ \|\| nityaṃ vibhuṃ sarva-gataṃ su-sūkṣmaṃ yat bhūta-yoniṃ paripaśyanti dhīrāḥ \| ādi-śabdāt brahma eva idaṃ sarvaṃ ātmā eva idaṃ sarvam iti ādiḥ ca abhyudāhṛtaḥ \| ata eva paraṃ brahma ca sarva-ātmakatvasya \|\| 36 \|\| iti para-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|

**atha phala-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.2.37 \|\|

*phalam ataḥ upapatteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

uktam upāsi-viśeṣa-janānāṃ jīvasya sarva-vidyāsu sarva-veditvaṃ, prāpyasya ca parama-puruṣasya nirdeṣatvaṃ, kalyāṇa-guṇākaratvaṃ, sarvasmāt adhikatvaṃ ca \| ataḥ param-upāsanaṃ viśvam anupāsyānāṃ parama-viśvāsam āduḥkha-nāśam aiśvaryam avāpyam iti phalam iti saṃgṛhīte \| mukta-yāpataya śāstrīya-

mauhūrtikam api phalam ata eva paramātma-puruṣārtha-vartitvāt sāmānyena phalavat iti ucyate \| kutaḥ etat ? upapatteḥ—sa eva hi sarvaiḥ saṃśayakti-hetu-bhūto yāga-ādāna-eha-homa-ādi-vidhy-upāsanena ārādhitaḥ aihika-āmuṣmika-bhoga-jātaṃ svarūpa-avirūpam apavargaṃ ca dātuṃ śaktaḥ \| na hi acetanaṃ karma kṣaṇa-dhvaṃsi kālāntara-bhāvi-phala-śādhanaṃ bhavitum arhati \|\| 37 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.38 \|\|

*śrutatvāc ca*

**Комментарий:**

sa vā eṣa mahān aja ātmā ajaraḥ amaraḥ amṛtaḥ eṣa hi eva ānandayati iti bhoktṛ-bhogya-rūpaṃ phalam ayam eva dadāti iti hi śrūyate \|\| 38 \|\| saṅgatiḥ pūrva-pakṣam āha—

## \|\| 3.2.39 \|\|

*dharmaṃ jaiminiḥ ata eva*

**Комментарий:**

ata eva upapatteḥ, śāstrāt ca, yāga-ādāna-iha-upāsana-sa-dharmam eva phaladaṃ jaiminiḥ ācāryo manyate \| loke hi kṛṣṇi-ādikaṃ maha-ādikaṃ ca karma sākṣāt, paramparayā vā svayam eva phala-sādhanaṃ dṛṣṭam; evaṃ vede api yāga-ādāna-iha-homa-ādīnāṃ sākṣāt-phala-sādhanatva-abhāve api paraṃparayā apūrva-dvāreṇa phala-sādhanatvam upapadyate \| tathā svarga-kāmaḥ iti-ādi-śāstram api tri-pādī-vidhi-vit-svārthyaṃ kartṛ-khyātayā yāga-ādi-vidhi-arthatva-anupattyā apūra-dvāreṇa phala-sādhanatvam avagamayati \|\| 39 \|\|

## \|\| 3.2.40 \|\|

*pūrvaṃ tu bādarāyaṇo hetu-vyapadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

tu-abdaḥ pakṣa-vyāvṛtti-arthaḥ \| pūrva-oktaṃ parama-puruṣam eva phala-pradatvaṃ bhagavān bādarāyaṇo manyate \| kutaḥ ? hetu-vyapadeśāt— yajña-deva-pūjāyām iti devatā-ārādhana-bhūta-yāga-ādi-ārādhya-bhūta-agni-vāyu-ādi-devatānām eva tatat-phala-hetutayā tasmin tasmin karmaṇi vāyavye śvapadeśāt \| vāyavyaṃ śvetam ālabheta bhūti-kāmo vāyum eva śvena bhāga-dheyena upadhāvati sa enaṃ bhūtiṃ gamayati iti-ādīṣu \| kāmini-ssimādhi-viṣa-vaklātādhatva-prakara-apekṣayā api vidhi-apekṣitaḥ eva iti na atparametva-prasaṅgaḥ yuktaḥ \| evam api hi tve api phala-saṃbandha-prakāre śabdāt eva avagate sati tarka-ukta-āgama-śruta-apūrva-ādi-parikalpanaṃ ca prāmāṇikā na sahante \| liṅga-nyāye api devatā-ārādhana-bhūta-yāga-ādeḥ prakṛtyā eva kartṛ-vyāpāra-sādhyatāṃ chadṛśi-niṣiddhaṃ śabdaḥ praśāna-mātram anumātum abhivadanti; na anyathā upadikraniṣiti prayoga-uttaram, tad eva vāyuḥ kṣepiṣṭhā devatā iti-ādi-śabda-sāhacaryāt tataḥ phala-pradatvam avagamyate \| vākyāt asmāt ca parama-puruṣa eva ārādhyatayā phala-pradaḥ cintena ca avarthitaḥ iti śrūyate śaṃbhuṃ baḍvā jātaṃ jāyamānaṃ viśvaṃ bibharti bhuvanasya nābhiḥ; tad eva agniḥ sadā-yuktaḥ tūpara-indṛu candramāḥ iti \| antara-abhimāhāpāṇe ca śro vāyo vigṛhyasya vāyu-śarīrasya yaḥ agno vigṛha ya āditye vigṛha iti-ādi śrūyate \| smaryate ca yo yāṃ yāṃ tanuṃ bhaktaḥ

śraddhayā’cita-śūcchati \| tat-syatasyāṃ balāṃ śraddhāṃ tām eva vidyā-mayaṃ \| sa tathā śraddhayā yuktas tasya ārādhanam īhate \| labhate ca tataḥ kāmān mayā eva vihitān hi tān \|\| iti, ahaṃ hi sarva-yajñānāṃ bhoktā ca prabhuḥ eva ca iti \| prabhu-kṛti—phala-pradāyitva-arthaḥ \| devān deva-yajo yānti mad-bhaktā yānti mām api, yānti mad-yājinaḥ api mām iti ca \| loke ca kṣupya-dibhiḥ vicitrai-rūpāt dravya-viśeṣān saṃpādya taṃ rājānaṃ bhṛtya-dvāreṇa sākṣād-dvārvepi arcanti; arcitaḥ ca rājā ta-tad-vandana-anuguṇaṃ phalaṃ prayacchan dṛśyate \| vedānāṃ sarva-tipatita-tattva-ketat-pramāṇa-saṃbhāvanā-bhūmi nirasta-samasta-avidyādi-rdoṣa-gandhaṃ svābhāvika-anantya-adhika-atijñāna-aiśvarya-vīrya-tejo-rāśayaḥ puruṣottame pratiṣṭhāya, tad-ārādhana-rūpāṇi ca yāgā-saṃdeha-mānaskāni, stuti-namaskāra-kīrtana-evāca-dhyānāni ca tad-ārādhanāni, ārādhitād varam a-puruṣād urogapavarga-aiśvaryaṃ phalaṃ ca vadata iti sarvaṃ samañjasam \|\| 40 \|\| iti phala-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye tṛtīya-adhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**tṛtīya-adhyāye—tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ—sarva-vedānta-pratyaya-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Pada 3

## \|\| 3.3.1 \|\|

*sarva-vedānta-pratyayaṃ codanādi-aviśeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

uktaṃ brahma-upāsisiṣo-pajanāya vaktavyaṃ brahmaṇaḥ phalādi-atiriktatva-paryantam \| idānīṃ brahma-upāsanānāṃ guṇa-upasaṃhāra-vikalpa-nirṇayāya vidyā-bhedābheda-cintā prastūyate \| prathamaṃ tāvat ekasyāṃ śaikhānāra-vidyā-dvikāyāḥ aneka-śākhāsu śrūyamāṇāyāḥ kim eka-vidyātvam, uta vidyā-bhedaḥ iti cintyate \| aviśiṣṭa-punaḥ-śravaṇasya prakaraṇāntarasya ca bhedakatvāt saṃchābdena codryaḥ api eka-rūnīyatvāt vidyā-bhedaḥ iti prāpte \| atra eva vaiśvāmedaṃ brahma-vidyāṃ vedeta śiro-vrataṃ vidhivad yaḥ tu cīrṇam iti śiro-vratatvāmātharvaṇikānām eva vidyopadeśa-niyamaḥ upapadyate \| vidyāṃdye hi vidyā-aṅgasya śiro-vratasya anyeṣām api śākhināṃ prāpti-niyamaḥ na upapadyate \|\|

evaṃ prāpte ucyate—sarva-vedānta-pratyayam eka-rūpam asti iti \| kutaḥ ? codanādi-aviśeṣāt—codanā tāvad upāsīta vidyādhi iti avañjātīyakaḥ dhātv-artha-viśeṣa-vidhiḥ \| ādi-śabdena eṣa vā saṃyoga-rūpa-codanākhyā-aviśeṣāt iti karma-kāṇḍādhikānatra-adhikaraṇa-sūtroktasaṃyoga-rūpākhyā gṛhyante \| evaṃ codanā-vedanām aviśeṣāt saivaṃ vidyā iti śākhāntare pratyabhijñāyate \| tathā hi—chāndogya-vājasaneyakayoḥ vedhānas tupārate iti codanā tāvat eka-rūpā; vaiśvānala-lakṣaṇopāsya-rūpasya vividhya-vidyayor asaṃvedya-bhūta-dyadhaḥ-sthāna-vyūhaṃ phala-viśeṣaḥ; ākhyā ca vaiśvānara-vidyā iti vidhiṣṭhā \| ata eṣāḥ pratyabhijñānāt śākhāntare’pi vidyā ekam \|\| 1 \|\|

yat tu uktam viśeṣa-punarabhyupagāśrayaṇāntarāc ca vidyā-bheda-śaṅkā iti; kiṃ karyaṃ iti, tad aduṣṭam pariharati—

## \|\| 3.3.2 \|\|

*bhedān na iti cet na ekasyām api*

**Комментарий:**

aviśeṣa-punarabhyāsasya prakaraṇāntarāc ca vidyā-bhedasya vidyāikyaṃ iti cet; ekasyām api vidyāyāṃ prativacana-bhedāt punarabhyāsa iti; prakaraṇāntaraṃ ca upapadyate \| yatra hi ekasmin pratipattṛ-punarabhyāsa iti prakaraṇāntaraṃ ca vidyate; tatra anyathānupapattyā vidyā-bhedādivābhyupetam; prativacana-bhede tu tat-sarvopapattiḥ anyathā punarabhyāsa-nyāyaḥ pravarteta iti na, vidyā-bhedāntara-saṃbhavaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

yat ca uktaṃ śirovrata-vatvānātharvaṇikānām iva vidyopadeśa-aniyama-daśanād vidyā-bhedaḥ pratīyata iti, tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.3.3 \|\|

*svādhyāyasya tathātve hi samācāre ’dhikārāc ca sava-vac ca tat-niyamaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

na etad asti—śirovrata-upadeśa-aniyama-darśanāt vidyā-bhedaṃ yojayati, śirovratasya vidyāṅgatvābhāvāt \| svādhyāyasya tathātve hi tat-niyamaḥ—svādhyāyasya tathātva-siddhy-arthaṃ—tat-jaṭya-saṃskāra-sāhacarya-siddhy-arthaṃ hi śirovrata-upadeśa-niyamaḥ; na vidyāyāḥ \| kuta etat? na etat acīrṇa-vrato ’dhīyīta iti tasya adhyayana-saṃyogāt \| samācāre ’dhikārāc ca—samācāra-ākhye granthe idam api veda-vratena vyākhyātam iti atideśāt \| teṣām eva etāṃ brahma-vidyāṃ vadeta veda-vidyā-nirūpaḥ \| sava-vac ca tat-niyamaḥ—yathā hi sava-homaḥ sūryodaya-kṣitodana-paryantā ātharvaṇikānāṃ saṃhitā-vratam eva bhavati; na tretāgniṣu \|\| 3 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.4 \|\|

*darśayati ca*

**Комментарий:**

darśayati ca śrutiḥ upāsanasya sarva-vedānta-pratyayatvam \| tathā hi chāndogye tad imām etasadvedhyam iti uktvā kiṃ tantra vidate yadvediṣyam iti praśna-pūrvakam atharvaṇām svāṅgiraṇātharva-vidhiḥ paramātmā tasmin upāsya iti uktam \| taittirīyake tu cchāndogya-arthe pratihitavyaṃ punar ucya tatra api brahma gāvaṃ vicoktasmin yad antas tad upāsitavyam iti guṇāḥ eka-viśiṣṭasya paramātman upāsanam upadiśyate \| tad-vayatra vidyāṃ kurveta guṇa-upasaṃhāraḥ ca apeṣyate \|\| 4 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.5 \|\|

*upasaṃhāraḥ arthābhedāt vidhi-śeṣavat samāne ca*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ sarva-vedānteṣu samāne sati upāsane vedānta-antara-āmnātānāṃ guṇānāṃ vedānta-antara-upasaṃhāraḥ kartavyaḥ, kutaḥ? vidhi-śeṣavat artha-abhedāt—yathā ekasmin vedānte śrutaḥ vaiśvānara-śirā-ādi-vidhi-śeṣaḥ guṇaḥ tad-vidyā-sambandhāt tad-upakāraka-rūpa-prayojana-siddhy-arthaṃ tatra upaiti; tathā vedānta-antara-uditaḥ api tad-vidyā-sambandhena tad-upakāra-viśeṣāt upasaṃhartavyaḥ iti arthaḥ \| candra-ādi-avadhāraṇe \|\| 5 \|\| iti sarva-vedānta-pratyaya-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha anyathātva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.6 \|\|

*anyathātvaṃ śabdāt iti cet na aviśeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ codanā-ādi-aviśeṣāt vidyā-aikyaṃ vaktavyam, ekatve ca guṇa-upasaṃhāraḥ kartavyaḥ iti uktam \| ataḥ paraṃ kāścana vidyā adhikṛtya pratyabhijñā-hetu-bhūta-codanā-ādi-aviśeṣaḥ asti, na iti nirūpya nirṇīyate \| asti udgītha-vidyā vājināṃ chandogānāṃ ca \| vājināṃ tāvat śrūyā ha prājāpatyā deva-asurāḥ iti ārabhya te ha devā ūcuḥ hanta asurān yajña udgīthena atyayāma iti udgīthena asura-vidhvaṃsanaṃ pratijñāya udgīthe vāk-ādi-manaḥ-paryanta-dṛṣṭyā anudruteḥ aśivam abhyuktaṃ ayaṃ hemā-sāmyaṃ prāṇam ucur iti ādinā udgīthe prāṇa-dṛṣṭyā asura-parābhavam uktvā bhavati ātmanā parāsya dviṣan bhrātṛvyo bhavati yaḥ evaṃ veda iti śatru-pārājaya-phalatya-upasaṃhāreṇa prāṇa-dṛṣṭiḥ vihitā \| evaṃ chandogānām api deva-murā ha vai yatra saṃyetire iti ārabhya tad dha devā udgītham ājahruḥ, tena enān asmiṃ hanyāma iti udgīthena asura-parābhavaṃ pratijñāya tatra eva udgīthe vāk-ādi-dṛṣṭī doṣam abhidhāya ayaṃ ha yaḥ eva ayaṃ mukhyaḥ prāṇaḥ tam udgītham upāsāñcakrire iti ādinā udgīthe prāṇa-dṛṣṭyā asura-parābhavam uktvā abhyāsthānām āśāḥ pramucyante evaṃ ha eva sa vidvān veṣate yaḥ evaṃ-vidi pāpaṃ kāmayate iti śatru-pārājayāya udgīthe prāṇa-dṛṣṭiḥ vihitā \| vedana-viṣaya-vidhi-kriyā-śravaṇe api phala-sādhanatva-śravaṇāt vedana-viṣayaḥ vidhiḥ kalpyate \| udgītha-vidyāyāḥ kratu-aṅgatvena kratu-artha-puruṣa-phalatvena upāsanā-artha-avanirūpaṇam api phalaṃ tatra evaṃ prāpyam veti devatā-adhikaraṇe pratipāditam \|\| tatra saṃśayate—kim atra vidyā-aikyam, uta na iti \| kiṃ yuktam? vidyā-aikyam iti \| kutaḥ? ubhayatra udgītha-aikyaṃ tad-vayataraprāṇa-ākāra-yoga-phala-śravaṇa-codanā-ādi-aviśeṣāt \| phala-saṃyogaḥ tāvat śatru-pārābhava-rūpaḥ na viśiṣyate \| rūpam api astadāṇaśālīdgīthopāsyaḥ apāṃśeyatvāt viśiṣyāḥ \| codanā ca vidyātva-rūpatā aviśiṣṭā \| ākhyā ca udgītha-vidyā iti aviśiṣṭā \| atra rāddhānti-

brahma-vidyā paricodanā parivartini—ananyārthatvaṃ śabdādibhiḥ caitra-aviśeṣāt iti \| yat uktaṃ viśvaṃ kṣaṇīti, tasmā upasattau, rūpa-bhedāt; rūpa-ananyatvaṃ hi śābdadeve pratīyate \| vājasaneyake hi atha hemāsāsyaṃ prāṇam udgīthaṃ na udgāyati tathā iti tasya eva prāṇaḥ udgāyatu iti udgānataḥ tasya kartari prāṇa-śabdasya uparāma-bhūtatvāt yaḥ evaṃ veda iti kartavye prāṇa-dṛṣṭeḥ śabdāt avagamyate \| chāndogye atha ha yaḥ eva ayaṃ mukhyaḥ prāṇaḥ tam udgītham upāsāṃcakrire iti udgānataḥ tasya karma-bhūta-udgīthe prāṇa-śabdasya aṅgīkārāma-bhūtatvāt yaḥ evam vidhi pāpaṃ kāmayate iti evam śabda-lakṣya-bheda-upeta-udgīthe prāṇa-dṛṣṭiḥ vihitā \| ataḥ ekatra kartari prāṇa-dṛṣṭiḥ śabdāt anyatra karmaṇi prāṇa-dṛṣṭiḥ śabdāt anyatra rūpa-ananyatvaṃ spaṣṭam \| rūpa-ananyatve ca vidheya-bhede sati kevala-codanā-śabda-viśeṣāḍḍiṣṭakaḥ ca iti vidyā-bheda iti cet; tarhi, aviśeṣāt—aviśeṣeṇa hi ubhayatra udgītha-sādhana-phala-pratisaṃbandhaḥ upakramaḥ pratīyate; vājasaneyake saḥi ha devāḥ udgīthena asurān atyāyāma iti upakrame śrūyate; chāndogye api tat devāḥ udgītham ājahuḥ, anena enān abhibhaviṣyāmaḥ iti \| ataḥ upakrama-avirodhāt tena eva prāṇaḥ udgāyatu iti adhyasta-prāṇa-bhāvaḥ udgīthe udgāna-karma-bhūtaḥ eva pākṣika-bheda-nivṛtti-karatīti sādhya-vikalpā kartṛ-devatā ucyate; anyathā upakrama-gataḥ udgītha-śabdaḥ kartari lākṣaṇikaḥ syāt \| ataḥ vidyaikyam \|\| 6 \|\|

**iti prāpte prabandham āha—**

## \|\| 3.3.7 \|\|

*na vā prakaraṇa-bhedāt paro-varīyastvādivat*

**Комментарий:**

na eva iti pakṣaṃ vyāvartayati; na evaṃ vaktum, yady api śakyate \| kutaḥ? prakaraṇa-bhedāt \| om iti etat akṣaram udgītham upāsīta iti prakṛta-udgītha-avayavaṃ prāptaṃ prastūya etasya vai akṣarasya api ākhyānaṃ bhavati devāsurā ha vai yatra saṃvire iti ārabhya, atha ha yaḥ eva ayaṃ mukhyaḥ prāṇaḥ tam udgītham upāsāṃcakrire iti udgītha-avayava-bhūta-praṇavam upāsataṃ chandogāḥ adhīyate \| vājinaḥ tu tādṛśa-prastāva-prakaraṇa-abhāvāt hanta asurān yajñe udgīthena atyayāmaḥ iti kṛtsna-udgīthaṃ prastutya atha hemāsyaṃ prāṇaṃ ūcuḥ tvaṃ na udgāya iti abhihita-kṛtsna-udgītha-viṣaya-nirṇayate \| ataḥ prakaraṇa-bhedena vidheya-bhedaḥ; vidheya-bhede ca rūpa-bheda iti na vidyaikyam \| kiñ ca atha ha yaḥ eva ayaṃ mukhyaḥ prāṇaḥ tam udgītham upāsāṃcakrire iti pūrva-prakṛta-udgītha-avayava-bhūtaḥ praṇavaḥ eva ālambana-prāṇa-bhāva-yuktaḥ yogānām upāsyaḥ \| vājināṃ tu kṛtsnasya udgīthasya kartṛ-gāmatā prāṇasya upāsya iti; atha hemāsyaṃ prāṇam ūcuḥ tvaṃ na udgāya iti tathā iti tebhyaḥ eṣaḥ prāṇaḥ udgāyat iti upagāna-giri prāṇādhāraṃ nirṇīya yaḥ evaṃ veda iti upagātṛ-vākyataḥ prāṇa-bhāvaḥ upāsyaḥ vidhīyate \| ataḥ ca rūpa-bhedaḥ \| na ca upāsitṛ-pakṣe vihite udgīthena atyayāma iti ārabhya mṛtyu-vyāpāra-udbhavaḥ; udgātṛ-praṇāme udgītha-pradīyamāna-karma-bhūtasya avayava-

parihṛta-tayā tatra api parāmarśa-bhāvasya phalam iti hetutvāt, ataḥ rūpa-bhedāt vidyā-bhedaḥ iti codanādi-viśeṣe api na vidyā-aikyam \| parīvarī-rūpatvāt vā—yathetyam api śākhāyām udgītha-avayava-bhūte praṇave paramātma-sṛṣṭi-vidyā-samādhye api hiraṇmaya-puruṣa-dṛṣṭi-vidhānāt parīvarītvādi-rūpāviśiṣṭa-hṛṣṭi-vidyānām artha-antara-bhūtam \|\| 7 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.8 \|\|

*saṃjñātaḥ cet tat uktam asti tu tat api*

**Комментарий:**

udgītha-vidhiḥ iti saṃśayāt tat-tva—vidyām gamayati cetaḥ; tat—saṃjñāyām vidyā-bhedasya uktatvāt \| yathā agnihotra-saṃjñā nitya-agnihotre, kuṇḍapāyināmayana-agnihotre ca; yathā codanārtha-vacane iti chāndogye prathama-prapāṭhaka-uditāsu bahvīṣu vidyāsu \|\| 5 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.9 \|\|

*vyāpateḥ ca samañjasam*

**Комментарий:**

chāndogye prathama-prapāṭhake uttaratra api vidyāsu udgītha-avayavasya praṇavasya prathama-prastutasya upāsanā-paratvena vyāptam śrūyate tasmāt gātasya etat devāḥ udgītham ājahuḥ; iti udgītha-śabdasya praṇava-viṣayatvam eva samañjasam \| athavā ca samudāya-śabdaḥ paṭho lakṣa iti abhidhīyate \| śrutāḥ ca udgītha-avayava-bhūtaḥ praṇavaḥ eva udgītha-śabda-nirdiṣṭaḥ iti saḥ eva praṇavaḥ ayam upāsyaḥ chāndogye pratipattavyaḥ \| vājasaṇeyake tu kṛtsna-udgītha-viṣayaḥ udgītha-śabdaḥ iti kṛtsna-udgīthasya kartṛ-devatā-prāṇa-dṛṣṭy-upāsya iti vidhānāt na atra siddham \|\| 6 \|\| iti anyathātva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha sarvābhi-dārtha-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.10 \|\|

*sarvābhidāt anyatra ime*

**Комментарий:**

chāndogya-vājasaṇeyakayoḥ prāṇa-vidyā āmnāyate yo ha vai jyeṣṭham ca śreṣṭham ca veda jyeṣṭhaḥ ca vai śreṣṭhaḥ ca bhavati prāṇaḥ ha eva jyeṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ iti-ādi \| tatra ucaśva-śreṣṭha-guṇakaḥ prāṇam upāsyam pratipādya vāk-cakṣuḥ-śrotra-manassu vasiṣṭhatva-pratiṣṭhatva-saṃpad-āyatanatvākhyān guṇān pratipādya vāg-ādīnām dehasya ca prāṇāya-sthiti-vat tena tat āyatanatva-samarthanena ca prāṇasya śreṣṭham pratipādya vāg-ādi-saṃbandhinām śrāvaṇa-cchilikāḥ guṇān prāṇa-saṃbandhitayā pratipādayati \| evam chāndogya-vājasaṇeyakayor jyeṣṭha-śreṣṭha-guṇakaḥ vasiṣṭhatvādi-guṇakaḥ ca prāṇaḥ upāsyaḥ pratipādyate \| kauṣītakinaḥ tu prāṇa-vidyāyām tathā eva jyeṣṭha-śreṣṭha-guṇakaḥ prāṇaḥ upāsyaḥ pratipāditaḥ; na punaḥ vasiṣṭhatvādayaḥ vāg-ādi-saṃbandhinaḥ guṇāḥ prāṇa-saṃbandhitayā pratipāditāḥ \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim atra vidyā bhidyate, uta na iti \| kim yuktam? bhidyata iti \|

kutaḥ ? rūpa-bhedāt \| yady api ubhayaṃ prāṇeṣu jyeṣṭhatva-śreṣṭhatva-guṇa-upāsyaḥ; tathā api ekatra vasiṣṭhatvādi-viśiṣṭe guṇe yuktāḥ prāṇaḥ upāsyaḥ pratīyate, itaratra tu tat-vidhura indriya-prāṇa-rūpa-bhedāt vidyā-bhedaḥ \|\|

iti prāpte brūmaḥ—sarva-abheda-nayaḥ ca \| na atra vidyā-bhedaḥ; anyatra kauṣītakinaḥ prāṇa-vidyāyām api ime—vasiṣṭhatvādayaḥ guṇāḥ upāsyāḥ santi \| kutaḥ ? sarvatra tadvat—pratiṣṭhā-prāṇa-jyeṣṭhatva-śreṣṭhatva-pādāna-ākāraḥ sarvasya tatra api abhedāt \| tathā hi—chandoga-vājasaneyikinoḥ prāṇa-vidyāyām ‘eṣā ha vai devatā ahaṃ-śreyase vyūdhe’ ‘ahaṃ-śreyase vivadamānāḥ’ iti ca upakrama-vākya-ekārtha-kāraṇe anyeṣāṃ sādhāraṇa-asādhāraṇyāṃ śarīrasya ca sthitiṃ tat-kāryaṃ vācikatvaṃ pratipādya, prāṇa-utkramaṇe sarveṣāṃ vihāra-aśakti-pūrvakaṃ ca aśithilya, sarveṣāṃ prāṇa-adhīna-sthititva-tat-tat-caitana-kāryatva-ākhyāṃ prāṇasya jyeṣṭham upapāditam \| evam upavarṇitaṃ vāk-ādi-kāryasya prāṇa-adhīnatvāt ‘atha ha enaṃ vāk uvāca yad ahaṃ vasiṣṭhā asmi tvaṃ tat-vasiṣṭhaḥ asi’ iti-ādinā vāk-ādibhiḥ nṛhate \| kauṣītakīnāṃ prāṇa-vidyāyām api pratyak-jyeṣṭha-śreṣṭhatva-pratipādanāya vāk-ādiṣu vasiṣṭhatvādayaḥ pratipāditāḥ \| ‘atha ha enā devatāḥ prajāpatim pitaraṃ etya abruvan kaḥ vai naḥ śreṣṭhaḥ’ iti-ādinā vāk-ādi-sattā guṇāḥ vāk-ādyāḥ ca dehasya prāṇa-adhīnāḥ iti prāṇasya jyeṣṭham upapāditam \| vāk-ādibhiḥ sva-karma-guṇatāṃ vasiṣṭhatvādīnāṃ prāṇa-adhīnatva-anuvāda-mātraṃ tu na kṛtam \| na itaravatā rūpa-bhedaḥ; vāk-ādīnāṃ vasiṣṭhatvādi-guṇa-anvitānāṃ prāṇa-adhīna-kārya-ca-upapādanena eva prāṇasya vāk-ādi-vasiṣṭhatvādi-guṇa-hetutvasya siddhatvāt \| tat eva hi prāṇasya vasiṣṭhatvādi-guṇa-yogitvam, yad vāk-ādi-vasiṣṭhatvādi-hetutvam \| ataḥ atra api vasiṣṭhatvādi-guṇa-yoga-lakṣaṇo jyeṣṭhaḥ pratipannaḥ iti na asti vidyā-bhedaḥ \|\| 10 \|\| iti sarva-abheda-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha ānandādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

prāṇa-vidyāsu aviṣayam anyad api nirūpaṇam anantaram eva kariṣyate \| yathā prāṇasya vasiṣṭhatva-jñāna-sthāne vinā jyeṣṭhatva-śreṣṭhatva-jñāna-sthānam api etad-uktānām api vasiṣṭhatvādīnāṃ kauṣītaki-prāṇa-vidyāyāṃ prāptiḥ; tathā brahma-svarūpa-guṇānāṃ ānūrṇihanā na upapadyate, te brahma-vidyāsu sarvatra sambandhyāḥ iti api arthaḥ pratipādyate—

## \|\| 3.3.11 \|\|

*ānandādayaḥ pradhānasya*

**Комментарий:**

atra brahma-svarūpa-guṇānāṃ sarvāsu para-vidyāsu sambandhaḥ asti, na iti vicāryate \| akṣara-anādhītānām upasaṃhāre pramāṇa-abhāvāt prakaraṇa-sthānām eva upasaṃhāraḥ iti evaṃ prāpte brūmaḥ—ānandādayaḥ pradhānasya \| akṣarādīni vartante \| pradhānasya guṇinaḥ brahma-prasatvā-pramāṇa-codyatvāt, guṇa-guṇa-sambandha-guṇānāṃ, sarvajña-ānandādayaḥ tat-guṇāḥ ucyante arhīyāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

evaṃ tarhi guṇy-upabhāvād eva ānanda-ādi-viśiṣṭa-śirastva-ādi api tasya priyam eva śiraḥ ity-ādau brahmaṇa upadeśena śrutāt tat sarvatra prasañceta; na iti āha—

## \|\| 3.3.12 \|\|

*priya-śirastva-ādi-aprāptiḥ upacaya-apacayau hi bhede*

**Комментарий:**

brahma-svarūpa-guṇatāṃ prāptum udyamatayāṃ priya-śirastva-ādīnām aprāptiḥ; teṣāṃ brahma-guṇatva-abhāvāt; brahmaṇaḥ puruṣa-vidha-rūpaṇa-mātra-antargatatvāt priya-śirastva-ādīnām \| anyathā śiraḥ-pakṣa-puccha-ādi-avayava-bhede sati brahmaṇaḥ api upacaya-apacayau prasañceyātām \| tathā ca sati satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti api vihanyeta \|\| 12 \|\|

tan-nivedam eva brahma-saṃnidhi-mātram eva aiśvarya-prāpty-abhīrthitayāṅkāraṇy-ādīnāṃ guṇānām anantānāṃ guṇi-pṛthak-sthitatva-prāṇena tat-tac-chrutānām asukha-saṃcāre sarvaṃ sarvatra prasañcet, ānanda-rāya-puccha-sārā-śakti-kraṃ; tatrāha—

## \|\| 3.3.13 \|\|

*itare tu artha-sāmānyāt*

**Комментарий:**
