tu-śabdaḥ codyaṃ vyāvartayati \| itare tu—ānanda-ādayaḥ artha-sāmānyāt sarvatra anuvartante \| ye svarūpa-samānāḥ—artha-svarūpa-nirūpaṇa-dharmāḥ teṣām artha-pratyabhijñāna-rūpa-bhedaḥ; te artha-svarūpavat saṃdhāna-anuvartante \| te ca guṇāḥ tat-sakāśāt ānanda-malatva-anantalatva-ādi \| yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante iti upā-lakṣite brahma satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma ānandaḥ brahma iti ānanda-rūpa-viśiṣṭaṃ svarūpataḥ nirūpyate \| ata upāsaka-brahma-svarūpopābhāya sarvāsu vidyāsu ānanda-ādayaḥ anuvartante \| ye tu nirūpita-svarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ kārya-pratyayo guṇāḥ pratipannāḥ; teṣāṃ guṇi-pṛthak-sthitatve api pratīti-upayuktitva-abhāvāt, ye yatra śrutāḥ, te tatra upasaṃhāryāḥ iti niravadyam \|\| 13 \|\|

ṣaḍ-upañcāpañca-grantha-saṃnidhiḥ priya-śirastva-ādī brahmaṇaḥ puruṣa-vidha-svarūpaṇa-mātrārthaḥ; na tu brahma-guṇāḥ; tarhi yathā-rūpasya brahmaṇaḥ tathātvena rūpaṇaṃ kim-arthaṃ kriyate? avayava-bhūtasya hi tatra svarūpeṇa kenacit prayojanena bhavitavyam; yathā ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi iti atra antopāsakasya saṃyukaraṇānāṃ ca ratha-sthitavat-rūpaṇam upāsana-upakaraṇa-bhūta-śarīra-indriya-ādi-vaśīkaraṇa-arthaṃ kriyata iti uktam \| tadvat tathā-vidhaṃ kiñcit-prayojanaṃ dṛśyata iti balād brahma-guṇatvaṃ priya-śirastva-ādīnām aṅgīkāryam \| tatrāha—

## \|\| 3.3.14 \|\|

*ādhyānāya prayojana-abhāvāt*

**Комментарий:**

prayojana-antarābhāvād ādhyānāya evaṃ rūpa-upadeśaḥ kriyate \| ādhyānasya anuṣṭhitatvam, upāsanam ucyate \| brahma-vit āpnoti param iti upadiṣṭha-yathā-svarūpa-vedana-siddhaye hi ānandamaya brahma-

pratipaty-arthaṃ mānandaṃ brahma priyamoda-ādi-śabdena vibhajya śiraḥ-pakṣa-ādi-dehena rūpayitvā upadiśyate \| yathā aśvamayaḥ puruṣaḥ ayaṃ dehaḥ śiraḥ-pakṣa-ādibhiḥ śaṃsya dehena eva śiraḥ iti-ādinā buddhau āropyate; yathā ca prāṇa-mano-maya-vijñāna-mayāḥ tasya prāṇa eva śiraḥ iti-ādinā prāṇa-śraya-buddhyā āropyante; evam eyo 'rthinita-sūkṣmatva-narātmanā ānandamayo api priya-moda-ādi-nirekadeśaiḥ śiraḥ-prabhṛti-dehena rūpitvena adhyātmanātha buddhau āropyate \| evam ānandamaya-upalakṣaṇatvāt priya-śirastvā-dīnāṃ na sarvadā ānandamaya-pratītau anuvartante \|\| 14 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.15 \|\|

*ātma-śabdāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

anayo 'ntara ātmā ānandamayaḥ iti ātma-śabdena nirdiśyamānatvaṃ śiraḥ-pakṣa-puccha-saṃbhāvit-priya-śirastvavadya-rasasya mukhya-pratipatti-arthaṃ lakṣaṇā-gam iti gamyate \|\| 15 \|\|

nanu anayo 'ntara ātmā prāṇamayaḥ anayo 'ntara ātmā manomayaḥ iti ātma-śabdasya ātmasv api pūrvaṃ śrutatvāt anayo 'ntara ātmā ānandamaya iti ātma-śabdasya paramātma-viṣayatvaṃ kathaṃ niścīyate ? tatrāha—

## \|\| 3.3.16 \|\|

*ātma-gṛhītir itaravad uttarāt*

**Комментарий:**

anayo 'ntara ātmā ānandamaya iti āśrama-śabdena paramātman eva grahaṇaṃ; itaravat— yathā itaraḥ ātmā vā idam eka eva agre āsīt, sa īkṣata lokān nu sṛjā iti aikhvan-mahā-vākyena paramātman eva grahaṇaṃ; tadvat \| kuta etat ? uttarāt— saḥ akāmayata, bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti ānandamaya-viṣayāt uttaravākyāt \|\| 16 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.17 \|\|

*anvayāt iti cet syād avadhāraṇāt*

**Комментарий:**

pūrvatra prāṇamaya-ādivat na asvayam-ātma-śabdatva-darśanāśaṅkā nirākriyata iti cet; syād avadhāraṇāt—syād eva niścayaḥ; kutaḥ ? avadhāraṇāt—pūrvaṃ api brahmāṣā etasmād ātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ iti paramātman eva buddhyā avadhāritatvāt asannayacchabdentare prāṇamaye prathamaṃ paramātma-buddhir avoṇī; tad-anantaraṃ ca prāṇamayād antare manomaye; tato vijñānamaye; tata ānandamaye prakrāntā paramātma-buddhi-tad-anantarāśrābhāvāc casya saḥ akāmayata iti vākyagata-sṛṣṭi-ciṣṭiteṣu pakṣeṣv eṭa-paramātmeti paramātma-buddhyā ātma-śabdatvayaḥ iti niravadyam \|\| 17 \|\|

iti ānandamaya-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha kārya-ākhyāna-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.18 \|\|

*kārya-ākhyānāt apūrvam*

**Комментарий:**

pūrva-prakṛta-prāṇa-vidyā-śeṣa-bhūtām ināṃ cintyate \| chāndogya-vājasaneyakayoḥ jyeṣṭhaḥ ca śreṣṭhaḥ ca prāṇam upāsya mukhyā prāṇasya vāsiṣṭhena upadiśyante \| chāndogye tāvat “sa ha uvāca kiṃ me vāsaḥ bhaviṣyati iti āpaḥ ha etat-puruṣasamāha \| etad vāva pratyataḥ purastāt ca upariṣṭhāt ca adṛbhiḥ paridadhati lasukaḥ ha vāsaḥ bhavati anagnaḥ bhavati” iti \| vājasaneyake “kiṃ me vāsaḥ” iti prāṇena pṛṣṭhā vāk-ādyāḥ ūcuḥ “āpaḥ vāsaḥ iti tad-vidvāṃsaḥ śrotriyāḥ aśiṣyanta ācāmanti aśiṣyāḥ ācāmanti etam eva tad-anamanam kurvantaḥ manyante “narmade deva vidahiṇi ācāmaye eva aśiṣyāḥ ca ācāmet anena eva tad-ananamanam kurute” iti \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim ācamanam vidhīyate, uta apāṃ prāṇa-vāsastva-anusandhānam iti \| “aśiṣyanta ācāmeṣiṣyāḥ ca ācāmet” iti ācāmane vidhi-pratyaya-śravaṇāt, “etena eva tad-ananamanam kurvata iti ca etena vedy anti asabhāvanam antar-aṅga-bhūtena smṛti-artha-saṃrambha-upapatteḥ ca, bhojana-aṅgasya ācamanasya smṛti-ācāra-prāptatvena vidhi-pratyaya-balāt prāṇa-vidyā-aṅgam ācamana-antaraṃ vidhīyate \|

iti prāpte brūmaḥ—ācamanīyānām apāṃ prāṇasya vāsastva-anusandhānam eva iha apūrvam—apām vidhīyate, kārya-ākhyānāt—apām ākhyānāt; apām ākhyāne śabdasya acchanda-vat iti arthaḥ \| evaṃ ca bhavati— “kiṃ me vāsaḥ āpaḥ vāsaḥ aśiṣyan paridadhati “etena eva tad-ananamanam kurute” iti upakrama-upasaṃhārayoḥ avicchinnayoḥ apāṃ prāṇa-vāsoḥ adhivatva-pratiteḥ ācamanasya smṛti-ācāra-prāptatvāt ācamanam anūdya ācamanīyāsu apsu prāṇa-vāsoḥ adhivatva-pratiteḥ ācamanasya smṛti-ācāra-prāptatvāt ācamanam anūdya ācamanīyāsu apsu prāṇa-vāsastva-anusandhānaṃ vidhīyata iti \| ataḥ eva chāndogye “tasmāt etat arśivantaḥ purastāt ca upariṣṭhāt ca adbhiḥ paridadhati” iti adbhiḥ paridhānam avoktam; na ācamanam \|\| 18 \|\| iti kārya-ākhyāna-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

**atha samāna-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.19 \|\|

*samānaḥ evaṃ ca abhedāt*

**Комментарий:**

vājasaneyake agni-rahasye śāṇḍilya-vidyā-aṅga-āmnātaṃ “satyaṃ brahma” iti upāsīta atha khalu kratu-mayaḥ ayaṃ puruṣaḥ” iti ārabhya “sa ātmānam upāsīta mano-mayaṃ prāṇa-śarīraṃ bhā-rūpaṃ satya-saṅkalpam ākāśa-ātmānam” iti \| tathā tasmin eva bṛhad-āraṇyake punaḥ api śāṇḍilya-vidyāyām āmnāyate “mano-mayaḥ ayaṃ puruṣaḥ bhāḥ-satyaṃ tasmin antar-hṛdaye yathā vrīhiḥ vā yavaḥ vā sa eṣa sarvasya vaśī

sarva-deśān na sarvasya adhipatiḥ sarvānandaḥ praśāsti yat idaṃ kiñca iti \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim atra vidyā bhidyate, uta na iti \| saṃyoga-codanā-ākhyānām aviśeṣe ’pi cariṣyatā lagu-pārama-guruṇā bhedena rūpaṃ veda-vidyā-bheda iti prāptam ucyate—samāna evam iti \| yathā ’gni-rahasye mano-maya-śrāṇīraśmādi-rūpasya eva haityatva-guṇāḥ śrutāḥ; evaṃ bṛhad-āraṇyake ’pi mano-mayatvādike samāne sati adhikasya vaśitvādeḥ ca satyasya sadyaguṇatva-guṇābhedāt rūpaikyaḥ; ato vidyāikyam \|\| 19 \|\| iti samānādhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha saṃbandhādhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.20 \|\|

*saṃbandhāt evam anyatra api*

**Комментарий:**

bṛhad-āraṇyake śrūyate—satyaṃ brahma iti upāsya tat yat tat satyam asau sa ādityaḥ yaḥ eṣa etasmin maṇḍale puruṣaḥ yaḥ ca ayaṃ dakṣiṇe ’kṣini iti upakramya āditya-maṇḍala-sthasya ca satyasya brahmaṇo vyāhṛti-niśarīratvena upāsyatvam uktvā tasya ha etasya brahmaṇo nāma satyam iti dve upaniṣado ’haraha-mānī upāsana-upetayā āmnāyete \| te kiṃ yathā-śruta-sthāna-viśeṣa-niyatatvena vyavasthe, uta ubhayoḥ api aniyamena iti saṃśaye, satyasya vyāhṛti-niśarīratvena upāsyasya brahmaṇo hi etāḥ sthāna-yoga-saṃbandhāt upāsya-aikyena rūpābhedāt saṃyoga-ādi-abhedāt ca vidyāikyam atiniyamena iti prāptam \| tat idam ucyate saṃbandhāt evam anyatra api iti \| yathā mano-mayatvādi-guṇa-viśiṣṭasya ekasya api upāsya-brahmaṇaḥ rūpābhede ’pi hṛdaya-guṇa-upasaṃhāraḥ; evam anyatra āditya-saṃbandhinaḥ brahmaṇaḥ satya-saṃkalpatvena vidheyasya api upāsanasya upasaṃhāraḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

ity evaṃ prāpte pratyakṣa-heto—

## \|\| 3.3.21 \|\|

*na vā viśeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

na ca etat asti yat ihaṃbhyām upasaṃhāraḥ iti \| kutaḥ? viśeṣāt—upāsya-svarūpa-viśeṣāt \| brahmaṇaḥ ekatve ’pi ekatra āditya-maṇḍala-sthatayā upāsyatvam, itaratra akṣi-ādhāratayā upāsyatvam iti sthāna-saṃbandhitva-bhedena rūpa-bhedāt vidyā-bhedaḥ \| na evaṃ śāṇḍilya-vidyāyāḥ upāsya-sthānaṃ bhidyate, ubhayatra hṛdaya-ādhāratvena eva upāsyatvāt \| ato vyavatiṣṭhate iti \|\| 21 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.22 \|\|

*darśayati ca*

**Комментарий:**

darśayati ca akṣi-ādhāra-āditya-ādhārayoḥ guṇa-anupasaṃhāraṃ tasya ha etasya tat eva rūpaṃ yat amuṣya rūpam iti ādinā rūpa-atideśena \| svataḥ hi aprāptau atideśena prāptyapekṣā \|\| 22 \|\| iti saṃbandhādhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|

**atha saṃbhūti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.23 \|\|

*saṃbhṛti-dyu-vyāptiḥ api ca ataḥ*

**Комментарий:**

taittirīyake rāṇāyanīyānāṃ khileṣu ca brahma jyeṣṭhaṃ vīryā saṃbhūtāni brahma agre jyeṣṭhaṃ divam ātatāna \| brahma bhūtānāṃ prathamod ajajñe tena arhati brahmaṇā spardhituṃ kaḥ \|\| iti brahmaṇi jyeṣṭhatvaṃ vīryāṇi saṃbhūtiḥ; dyu-vyāptiḥ ca iti-ādi-guṇa-jāta-āmnānam \| teṣām upāsanā-viśeṣam anārabhya adhītānāṃ guṇānāṃ sarvāsu vidyāsu upasaṃhāraḥ prāptaḥ ucyate \| saṃbhṛti-dyu-vyāptiḥ api \| saṃbhṛtiḥ ca dyābhīti samāhāra-dvandva-ekavad-bhāvaḥ \| saṃyukta-adhikaraṇa-nyāyena api atra eva sthāna-śaika-bandha-vyavasthāpyam; na sarva-upasaṃhṛtavyam \| katham anārabhya adhītānāṃ sthāna-viśeṣa-niyatatvam; svām adhyāhiti brūmaḥ \| dyu-vyāptitvāt ṛte dyāvā-pṛthivyoḥ anyatra api vidyāsu na upasaṃhartuṃ śakyā; saṃyuktatvāt api tat-sahacarita-stuti-rūpā eṣā hṛdaya-stha-sthāna-viṣayāsu vidyāsu anupasaṃhāryāḥ \| śāṇḍilya-dahara-ādi-vidyā-saṃbandha-sthāna-viṣayāsu jāyāmān pṛthivyāḥ prāṇaḥ vā avam-ākāśāt vānepoṣantaṃ divam ākāśaḥ iti-ādi-anta-latatra-śākhīya-guṇa-saṃhāraḥ; mano-mayatva-amṛtatva-mukha-ādi-viśiṣṭa-yogasya māhātmya-prativādana-paraḥ \|\| 23 \|\| iti saṃbhūti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

**atha puruṣa-vidyā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.24 \|\|

*puruṣa-vidyāyām api ca itareṣām anāmnānāt*

**Комментарий:**

taittirīyake puruṣa-vidyā āmnāyate tasyai vaṃ viduṣo yajñasya ātmā yajamānaḥ śraddhā patnī śarīram idhmam uraḥ vediḥ lomāni barhiḥ iti-ādikā \| chāndogye api puruṣa-vidyā āmnāyate puruṣaḥ vāva yajñaḥ tasya yāni caturviṃśati-varṣāṇi iti-ādikā \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim atra vidyā bhidyate; uta na iti \| puruṣa-vidyā hi nāma-ākhyaṃ puruṣa-avayaveṣu yajña-avayava-kalpanā-rūpatvāt taittirīyake phala-saṃyoga-āśravaṇāt sa ha ṣoḍaśaṃ varṣa-śataṃ jīvati iti chāndogye śrutatvaṃ puruṣa-vidyā-phalatvāt phala-saṃyogasya viśeṣādhiḥ ca aikyam \|\| iti prāptaḥ ucyate—upamardanānāṃ vidyayoḥ puruṣa-vidyātve api vidyā-bhedaḥ asti eva \| kutaḥ ? itareṣām anāmnānāt—eka-śākhāyām āmnātānāṃ guṇānām anyatra anāmnānāt \| tathā hi tasya yajñaḥ prātaḥ madhyandinaṃ ca tāni savanāni iti ārabhya taittirīyake āmnātāḥ chāndogye savana-trayeṇa nāmnādrate; śraddhā vibhaktaṃ puruṣa-mukhaṃ chāndogye savana-trayeṇa kalpyate \| chāndogye bhūtānām aśiśiṣā-ādīnāṃ dīkṣā-āditva-kalpanaṃ taittirīyake na śrutam \| yajña-anvaya-prayāśi-

parikalpanaṃ vācyam \|\| ataḥ rūpa-samudayasya sidhyate \| tathā phala-saṃyogaḥ api sidhyet; taittirīyake hi pūrva-anuvāke brahmaṇe tvā mahasa om iti ātmānaṃ yuñjīta iti brahma-vidyām abhidhāya tat-phalatvena brahmaṇaḥ mahimānam āpnoti iti uktvā tasya eṣa viduṣaḥ iti-ādinā ānantā puruṣa-vidyā-rūpā eva brahma-vidyā yantavya-kalpanā iti gamyate \| ataḥ brahma-vidyā-phalatvāt brahma-prāptiḥ eva atra phalam; phala-sambandhāt kalaṃ tat uktam iti nyāyāt taittirīyakāt nātā puruṣa-vidyā brahma-vidyā-aṅgam iti gamyate \| chāndogye tu āyuḥ-prāpti-phalā puruṣa-vidyā iti uktam \| ataḥ rūpa-saṃyoga-phala-vaiḍṛśya-ubhaya-bhedaḥ iti eṣā ananatāṃ guṇānām itaratra anupasaṃhāraḥ \|\| 29 \|\| iti puruṣa-vidyā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha vedha-ādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 10 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.25 \|\|

*vedha-ādi-artha-bhedāt*

**Комментарий:**

atharvaṇikāḥ upaniṣad-ārambhe śukraṃ pravidhya hṛdayaṃ pravidhya iti-ādi-mantrān adhīyate; sāma-āmnāye rahasya-brāhmaṇa-ārambhe devā savitaḥ prasuva yajñaṃ prasuva iti-ādi āmnāti; kāṭhaka-ārambhe taittirīyakāḥ ca śaṃ no mitraṃ varuṇaḥ iti ādhīran; śāṭyāyaninaḥ ca śyaṃ toṛhyo hārinīlaḥ asi iti-ādikam; evaṃ teṣu apiṣu mahāvrata-brāhmaṇam adhīyate indraḥ ha vai vṛtraṃ hatvā mahān abhavat iti-ādiḥ; kauṣītakinaḥ api mahāvrata-brāhmaṇam eva prajāpatiḥ ca saṃvatsaraḥ ca ubhau āstām yat mahāvratam iti; vājasaneyinaḥ tu pravargya-brāhmaṇaṃ devāḥ ha vai satraṃ niṣeduḥ iti-ādi \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim upaniṣad-ārambheṣu adhītāḥ śukraṃ pravidhya śaṃ no mitraḥ iti-ādayaḥ mantrāḥ pravargya-ādīni ca karmāṇi vidyā-aṅgam, uta na iti \| kiṃ yuktam? vidyā-aṅgam iti \| kutaḥ? saṃnidhi-sāmarthyāt vidyā-aṅgatva-prāpteḥ \| yady api śukraṃ pravidhya ity āgnenāṃ mantrāṇāṃ pravargyeṣu karmasu śruti-liṅga-vākyaiḥ sambandhāt granthy api ayaṃ karmasu viniyogaḥ avagamyate; tathā api śaṃ no mitraḥ saha nau avatu iti-ādeḥ mantra-dvayasya viniyoga-śāsana-avidhi-adhikārāt ca vidyā-aṅgatva-pratīty-upamit sarvaṃ vidyāsu ime mantrāḥ upasaṃhartavyāḥ \|\| evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—vedha-ādi-artha-bhedāt śukraṃ pravidhya hṛdayaṃ pravidhya śrutaṃ badhiṣyāmi satyaṃ vadiṣyāmi śrutasya mā adiṣaṃ satyam avadiṣam tejasvinau adhītam astu mā vidviṣāvahai iti-ādi-bhiḥ liṅgaiḥ bhinna-arthatvāt na eteṣāṃ viniyoga-āgamāt vidyā-aṅgatvam \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—yathā hṛdayaṃ pravidhya iti-ādi-mantrasya āmnāti śukraṃ pravidhya iti-ādīnām anvicāra-ādi-śeṣatvam avagamate; evam eva śrutaṃ vadiṣyāmi tejasvinau adhītam astu iti-ādikasya tātparyāt iti eva svādhyāya-prayojane śaṃ no mitra iti-ādibhiḥ mantrāṇām avagamyate; ataḥ na teṣāṃ vidyā-aṅgatvam iti \|

śukraṃ pravidhy-ādīnāṃ pravargya-vidyā-aṅgānāṃ ca iha pāṭhaḥ divā-kīrtyaṃ vārayet anavadya-vaktraḥ \|\|25\|\| iti vaidhya-adhikaraṇam \|\|10\|\|

**atha hāni-adhikaraṇam \|\|11\|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.26 \|\|

*hānau tu upāyana-śabda-śeṣatvāt kuśa-cchandas-stuty-upagāna-vat tad uktam*

**Комментарий:**

chandogāḥ āmananti—aśvaḥ iva romāṇi vidhūya pāpaṃ candraḥ iva rāhoḥ mukhāt pramucya \| dhūtvā śarīram akṛtaṃ kṛtātmā brahma-lokam abhisaṃbhavāmi \|\| iti; ātharvaṇikāḥ ca śvetā vidvān puṇya-pāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paraṃ sāmyam upaiti iti; śāṭyāyaninaḥ tu śatasya putrāḥ dāyam upayanti suhṛdaḥ sādhu-kṛtyāṃ dviṣantaḥ pāpa-kṛtyām iti-ādi; kauṣītakinaḥ tu tat sukṛta-duṣkṛte dhunute tasya priyāḥ jñātayaḥ sukṛtam upayanti apriyāḥ duṣkṛtam iti \| evaṃ kvacit śrūyamāṇa-pāpayoḥ hāniḥ; kvacit priya-apriyeṣu tat-prāptiḥ; kvacit ubhayaṃ ca śrūtam \| tatra ubhayeṣāṃ karma-vidyāyāṃ śrūtatvāt sarva-vidyā-aṅga-bhāvitvāt prakaraṇa-viṣayatvena ca upādānatvam \| tad-vicintanaṃ ca vidhīyamānaṃ sarva-vidyā-aṅgaṃ bhavitum arhati \| tatra evaṃ vicāryate—hāni-vidhitaḥ tasya upāyana-cintanam upāya-cintanaṃ vā vikalpyeta, upasaṃhriyeta vā? kiṃ yuktam? vikalpyeran iti \| kutaḥ? pṛthak-ātmanā-asāmarthyāt \| samuccaye hi sarveṣām bhinna-sthānāṃ sthāyāt, tat ca kauṣītakī-vacanena eva siddham iti anyatra ātmanā-asāmarthyam eva sthāyāt \| ataḥ anekātmanā asya vikalpaḥ eva prayojanam \| na ca upāyaneṣu anena parihartuṃ śakyam aneka-ātmanā-asāmarthyam; aviśeṣa-śravaṇaṃ hāni-upāyaneṣu avirodhām; atra tu hāniḥ eva dṛśeḥ śābdayoḥ; upākhyānaḥ eva ceṅkaśyam \| na ca vidyā-bhedena vyavasthāpayituṃ śakyam, sarveṣām abhinnam anusandhānam iyuktatvāt \|\|

atra idam ucyate—hānau tu upāyana-śabda-śeṣatvād iti \| tu-śabdaḥ pakṣaṃ vyāvartayati \| hānāv iti pradarśanārtham; kevalāyāṃ hānau kevale ca upāyane śrūyamāṇe tayoḥ itaretara-saṃbandhaḥ abhyupagantavyaḥ \| kutaḥ? upāyana-śabda-śeṣatvāt—upāyana-śabdasya hāni-vākya-śeṣatvāt \| upāyana-vākyasya hi hāni-vākya-śeṣeṇa eva anvitaḥ, viduṣāṃ vyaktayoḥ puṇya-pāpayoḥ praveśa-sthāna-cintā-upapannatvād arthasya \| pradeśa-antara-āmnātasya vākyasya pradeśa-antara-āmnāta-vākya-śeṣatve dṛṣṭāntaḥ upanyasyate—kuśa-cchandas-stuty-upagāna-vat iti \| kāṭhakinaḥ kuśāḥ vānaspatyāḥ iti āmananti; śāṭyāyaninaḥ tu audumbarāḥ kuśāḥ iti vākyaṃ sāmānyena vānaspatyatvena avagataḥ kuśāḥ audumbarāḥ iti viśeṣaṇatvād upaśleṣam āpadyate \| tathā deva-asurāṇāṃ chandobhiḥ iti-ādinā

aviśeṣeṇa deva-asurāṇāṃ chandasāṃ prasaṅge daiva-cchandāṃsi pūrvam iti vacanaṃ krama-viśeṣaṃ pratipādayatā madhye pavītāṃ gṛhṇāti \| tathā iha eva boddhṛ-jinaḥ stotram upākaroti iti aviśeṣeṇa prāpte, samayā-viṣite sūrye boddhṛ-jinaḥ stotram upākaroti iti viśeṣa-viṣayaṃ vākyaṃ tat-āśaya-bodhakaṃ bhajate \| tathā śrī-vigra upagāyanti iti aviśeṣa-prāptasya na adhvaryuḥ upagāyet iti vākyam adhvaryu-viṣayatām avagamyamānatva-yogena bādhate \| evaṃ sāmānyena tat-padārthaṃ viduṣe vyavasthāpitan tu kṣamasya vākyasya tat-śeṣatvam anadhyupagacchad dvitīya-artha-ucchitti-kalpanam aśraddhitavyam; sa ca saṃbhavatyaṃ gato na śrūyate \| tat uktaṃ pūrvaismin kāṇḍe api tu vākya-śeṣaḥ syād anyāyatvād vikalpasya vidhīnāṃ ca ekadeśaḥ syāt iti \| tat evaṃ kevala-hāna-upāyana-vattayor eka-nāyakatvāt kevalasya hānasya kevalasya ca upāyanasya abhāvād ubhayor na upapadyate \| kauṣītakinaḥ upāyana-ātma-viśeṣa-guṇa-śravaṇatvena pratipattṛ-saṃvedanatva-virodhaḥ \|\| 26 \|\| iti hāni-adhikaraṇam \|\| 11 \|\|

**atha sāṃparāya-adhikaraṇam \|\| 12 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.27 \|\|

*sāṃparāye tartavya-abhāvāt tathā hi anye*

**Комментарий:**

sukṛta-duṣkṛtayor hāna-upāyanaṃ ca sakṛd vidyāsu cintitopanityukram; tadānīṃ kiṃ deha-viyoga-kāle, dehād utkrāntasya adhvani vā, uta deha-viyoga-kāla eva iti viśaye upasthite pūrvam; ubhayathā śrutatvāt \| evaṃ hi kauṣītakinas samāmananti sa etāṃ deva-yānaṃ panthānam āpadya agni-lokaṃ gacchati iti upakramya sa āgacchati virajāṃ nadīṃ tāṃ manasā eva atyeti tat sukṛta-duṣkṛte dhunute iti \| atra vākye adhvani sukṛta-duṣkṛta-hāniḥ pratīyate \| tāṇḍinas tu aśva iva romāṇi vidhūya pāpaṃ candra iva rāhoḥ mukhāt pramucya \| dhūtvā śarīram akṛtaṃ kṛtātmā brahma-lokam abhisambhavāni \|\| iti \| atra tu deha-viyoga-kāla iti pratīyate \| śāṭyāyanake api tasya putrā dāyam upayanti suhṛdaḥ sādhu-kṛtyāṃ dviṣantaḥ pāpa-kṛtyām iti putreṣu dāya-saṅkrānti-samakālaṃ sukṛta-duṣkṛta-saṅkramaṇaṃ śrūyamāṇaṃ deha-viyoga-kāla iti gamyate \| ataḥ sukṛta-duṣkṛtayor deha-viyoga-kāle hāyate; śeṣaḥ tu adhvani \|\| iti prāpta ucyate—sāṃparāye iti \| sāṃparāye—dehād apakramaṇa-kāla eva viduṣaḥ sukṛta-duṣkṛte niravaśeṣe hāyete \| kutaḥ? tartavya-abhāvāt—viduṣo deha-viyogāt prabhṛti sukṛta-duṣkṛta-ātmakāryaṃ taritavya-bhoga-abhāvāt \| vidyā-phala-bhūta-brahma-prāpti-vyatirekeṇa hi sukṛta-duṣkṛtayoḥ bhoktavye sukha-duḥkhe na vidyete \| tathā hi anye deha-viyogam ūrdhvaṃ brahma-prāpti-vyatirikta-sukṛta-duḥkha-upabhoga-abhāvam adhīyate aśarīraṃ vāva santaṃ na priyāpriye spṛśataḥ eṣa samprasādaḥ asmāt charīrāt samutthāya paraṃ jyotir upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate tasya tāvad eva ciraṃ yāvat na vimokṣye atha sampatsye iti \|\| 27 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.28 \|\|

*chandataḥ ubhaya-avirodhāt*

**Комментарий:**

evam-artha-ekatvābhyāsa-kṛta-dviguṇārtha-nikaleśavad-bhūte sati ubhaya-avirodhena—śruti-artha-abhyāsasya ca avirodhena, chandataḥ—yathecchaṃ pradhāna-anvayaḥ varṇanīyaḥ \| kautītaskotavaṃ sattukṛtatuṣṭate hṛdyate iti caturtha-śrutau vākya-avayavaḥ \| evaṃ devayānaṃ panthānam āpadhya iti prathama-śruti-avayavaḥ atra anugamayitavyaḥ iti arthaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

**atra pūrvapakṣī pratyavatiṣṭhate—**

## \|\| 3.3.29 \|\|

*gateḥ arthavattvam ubhayathā hi virodhaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

sūkṛta-duṣkṛtayoḥ aikaideśasya deha-viyoga-kāle hāniḥ; śeṣasya ca pathyā iti ubhayathā karma-kṣaye sati eva gateḥ arthavattvam—devayāna-ādi-gati-śruteḥ arthaśvattvam iti arthaḥ \| anyathā hi virodhaḥ—deha-viyoga-kāla eva sarva-karma-kṣaye sūkṣma-śarīrasya api vināśa-rūpatvāt; tathā sati kevalasya ātmanaḥ gamanaṃ na upapadyate \| ata utkṛānti-samaye viduṣaḥ niśśeṣa-karma-kṣayaḥ na upapannaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

**atra uttaram—**

## \|\| 3.3.30 \|\|

*upapannaḥ tat-lakṣaṇa-artha-upalabdheḥ loka-vat*

**Комментарий:**

upapannaḥ utkrānti-kāle sarva-karma-kṣayaḥ \| katham ? tat-lakṣaṇa-artha-upalabdheḥ—kṣīṇāśeṣāṇo'pādhi-viśeṣaṃ tat-svarūpasya deha-saṃbandha-lakṣaṇa-artha-upalabdheḥ \| paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate sa tatra paryeti jakṣat-krīḍan-ramamāṇaḥ sa svarāṭ bhavati tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu kāma-cāraḥ bhavati sa ekadhā bhavati tridhā bhavati ityādiṣu deha-saṃbandha-apekṣaḥ arthaḥ hi upalabhyate \| ataḥ kṣīṇa-karmaṇaḥ api sūkṣma-śarīra-yuktasya devayānena gamanam upapadyate \| kathaṃ sūkṣma-śarīra-mātra-ātmaka-karma-vināśaḥ avaniścita iti cet, vidyā-māhātmyāt iti brūmaḥ \| vidyā hi svayaṃ sūkṣma-śarīrasya anārambhikā api prāktana-mukhaḥkhopabhoga-sādhana-sthūla-śarīrasya saṃkarṣaṇaṃ ca niravaśeṣa-kṣaye api sva-kalam-abhūta-sūkṣma-prāpti-sthānāya devayānena panthānaṃ gamayituṃ sūkṣma-śarīraṃ sthāpayati \| loka-vat—yathā loke satyādi-samṛddhy-artham ārabdhe taṭāka-ādike tadde tu nivṛtta-akhila-vinaṣṭeṣu api tat eva taṭāka-ādikam abhilakṣya kurvantaḥ tatra pānīya-pāna-ādi kurvanti; tadvat \|\| 30 \|\|

atha syāt—jñāninaḥ sākṣād-brahma-paratatvānāṃ deha-pāta-samaye karmaṇaḥ niravaśeṣa-kṣayāt deha-pātāt ūrdhvaṃ sūkṣma-śarīra-mātraṃ gati-artham anuvartate, sukhaḥkhānumavo na vidyate iti yat uktam, tat na upapadyate; cariṣyad-vāntaratapaḥprabhṛtīnāṃ sākṣād-brahma-paratatvānāṃ deha-pātāt ūrdhvaṃ dehāntara-saṃśleṣaḥ; puñjanma-vivaraṇye timi-sukhaḥkhānumavaḥ ca dṛśyata iti \| ata uttaraṃ paṭhati—

## \|\| 3.3.31 \|\|

*yāvad-adhikāram avasthitiḥ adhikārikāṇām*

**Комментарий:**

na asarva-niḥsarveṣāṃ jñānināṃ deha-pāta-samaye muktanuktavatkavīnāśa uktaḥ; api tu yeṣāṃ jñānināṃ deha-pātānantaram arciṣ-ādikā gatiḥ prāptā, teṣāṃ deha-pāta-samaye muktanuktavatkavīnāśaḥ \| vasiṣṭha-ādīnāṃ tu adhikārikāṇāṃ na deha-pātānantaram arciṣ-ādi-gati-viśeṣaḥ; prārabdha-adhyadhikāra-samāptatvāt \| teṣāṃ karma-viśeṣaṇa-adhikāra-viśeṣaṃ prāptānāṃ yāvad-adhikāra-samāpti tat-ārambhakaṃ karma na kṣīyate \| prārabdhasya hi karmaṇo bhogāt eva kṣayaḥ \| ataḥ adhikārikāṇāṃ tat-ārambhakaṃ karma yāvad-adhikāram avatiṣṭhate \| atas teṣāṃ na deha-pātānantaram arciṣ-ādi-gati-prāptiḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

iti bhāṣya-ratna-prakāśam \|\| 12 \|\|

**atha aniyama-adhikaraṇam \|\| 13 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.32 \|\|

*aniyamaḥ sarveṣām avirodha-śabda-anumānābhyām*

**Комментарий:**

upakosala-ādiṣu yeṣu upāsaneṣu arcir-ādi-gatiḥ śrūyate; kiṃ tat-niṣṭhānām eva tathā brahma-prāptiḥ; uta anyeṣāṃ brahmopāsana-niṣṭhānām iti saṃśaye, itareṣām anāmnātām, śraye ceme raṇye śraddhā tapa iti upāsate śraddhā satyam upāsate iti itara-śākhā-brahma-vidyopaṣṭhāpaka-rūpe pramāṇābhāvāt ca tat-niṣṭhānām eva iti prāpte abhidhīyate—aniyama iti \| sarveṣāṃ—sarvopāsana-niṣṭhānāṃ taṃ ca gantavyatvāt tat-niṣṭhānām eva iti niyamo nāsti \| sarveṣāṃ taṃ ca gamane hi sati śabdatumānābhyām...śruti-smṛtibhyām avirodhaḥ; anyathā virodha eva syāt \| śrutiḥ tāvat chāndogya-vājasaṇeyakyoḥ pañcāgni-vidyā-prāmavicaradikṣu-mārgā sarve brahmopāsana-niṣṭhānāṃ gamam āha yaḥ evam etad viduḥ ca ye ca amī araṇye śraddhā tatam upāsate te arcisam abhisaṃbhavanti iti vājaseyake; atha ye itthaṃ viduḥ ye ca ime araṇye śraddhā tapa iti upāsate te arcisam abhisaṃbhavanti iti chāndogye; ye itthaṃ viduḥ iti pañcāgni-vidyā-niṣṭhān ye ca ime iti ādinā śraddhā-pūrvakaṃ brahmopāsinī-śraddhā-vidyā-niṣṭhā gati-rūpa-diśyate, satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma satyam tu eva vijijñāsitavyam iti satya-śabdasya brahmaṇi prasiddhiḥ \| tapaḥ-śabdasya api tena ekārthyāt satya-tapaḥ-śabdābhyāṃ brahmābhidhīyate \| śraddhā-pūrvakaṃ brahmopāsanaṃ ca atra śrutaṃ—satyaṃ tu eva vijijñāsitavyam iti upakramya śraddhā tu eva vijijñāsitavyā iti \| smṛtir api agniḥ-jyotiḥ-ahaḥ-śuklaḥ ṣaṇ-māsā uttarāyaṇam \| tatra prayātā gacchanti brahma brahma-vido janāḥ \|\| iti sarveṣāṃ brahma-vidām anena eva mārgeṇa gamanam iti āha \| evaṃ-jātīyakāḥ śruti-smṛtyo anvayyasarati \| evaṃ sarva-vidyā-sādhāraṇīyaṃ gatiḥ prāptā vākyeṇa sannidhi-vādaḥ bādhyate \|\| 32 \|\| iti aniyama-adhikaraṇam \|\| 13 \|\|

**atha akṣara-adhi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 14 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.33 \|\|

*akṣara-dhiyām tu avarodhaḥ sāmānya-tad-bhāvābhyām aupasada-vat tad uktam*

**Комментарий:**

bṛhad-āraṇyake śrūyate etad vai tad akṣaraṃ gārgi brāhmaṇāḥ abhivadanti asthūlam anaṇu ahrasvam adīrgham alohitam asneham acchāyam atamo avāyu anākāśam asaṅgam arasam agandham acakṣuṣkam aśrotram avāk amanaḥ atejaskam aprāṇam amukham amātram anantaram abāhyam na tad aśnāti kiṃcana etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi sūrya-candramasau vidhṛtau tiṣṭhataḥ iti \| tathā ātharvaṇe atha parā yayā tad akṣaram adhigamyate yat tad adreśyam agrāhyam agotram avarṇam acakṣuḥ-śrotraṃ tad apāṇi-pādam iti \| tatra śaṅkāḥ—kiṃ ime akṣara-śabda-nirdiṣṭa-brahma-saṃvidyā-śrutāḥ asthūlatvādayaḥ prapañca-pratyanīkatā-svarūpāḥ sarvāḥ api brahma-vidyāsu anusandheyāḥ, uta yatra śrūyante tatra eva iti \| kiṃ yuktam? yatra śrutāḥ tatra eva iti \| kutaḥ? vidyāntarasya rūpa-bhūtānāṃ guṇānāṃ vidyāntarasya rūpatve pramāṇa-abhāvāt, prati-vidyaṃ hi guṇānām eva mānānandādi-vat-rūpatva-abhāva-upapatti-abhāvāc ca \| ānanda-ādiḥ viśva-ghātatva-khaṇḍe hi brahmaṇi sthūlatvādayaḥ prapañca-dharmāḥ pratiṣidhyante, nirālambana-pratiṣedha-ayogāc ca \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pratyāha—akṣara-dhiyām tu avarodhaḥ iti \| akṣara-brahma-saṃbandhinīnām asthūlatvādi-dhiyāṃ sarva-brahma-vidyāsu avarodhaḥ—saṅgrahaṇam iti arthaḥ \| kutaḥ? sāmānya-tad-bhāvābhyāṃ—sarvopāsanopāsyasya akṣarasya brahmaṇaḥ samānandāstūlatvādināṃ tattva-svarūpa-pratīteḥ śravaṇāt ca \| etad uktam bhavati—asādhāraṇa-ākāreṇa brahma hi vastu no brahmam \| na ca kevalam ānanda-ādi brahmaṇaḥ asādhāraṇam ākāram upasthāpayati, pratyagātmatva-dhyānatvāt vidyāntaratvāt \| heya-pratyanīkaḥ hi ānanda-ādiḥ brahmaṇaḥ asādhāraṇaṃ rūpam \| pratyagātmatvam tu svataḥ heya-tva-virahitaḥ api heya-saṃbandha-yogyatayā asti; heya-pratyanīkatvaṃ ca cit-acit-samasta-prapañca-dharmi bhūta-sthūlatvādi-viparīta-rūpam \| ataḥ asādhāraṇa-ākāreṇa brahmānusandadhātā asthūlatvādi-viśiṣṭānānandādy-ākāraṃ brahmānusandheyam iti asthūlatvādinām ānandādi-vat brahma-svarūpa-pratīti-ātma-viditatvāt brahma-vidyāsu tad eva brahmānusandheyam iti \| guṇānāṃ pradhāna-anuvṛttitve dṛṣṭāntam āha—aupāsada-vat—iti \| yathā jāmadagnya-caturātra-prayoga-upayuktaḥ paśu-puroḍāśaḥ sāma-veda-vihitaḥ agnir vai hotraṃ vetu iti-ādikaḥ mantraḥ pradhāna-anuvartitayā yājur-vedikaiṣṭaiḥ prayujyate prayuñje \| tad uktam prathame kāṇḍe guṇa-mukhya-vyatikrame tadarthatvān mukhyena veda-saṃyogaḥ iti \|\| 33 \|\|

nanu evaṃ sarvāsu brahma-vidyāsu brahmaṇaḥ eva guṇitvāt guṇānāṃ ca pradhāna-anuvartitvāt sarva-karmā sarva-gandhaḥ sarva-rasaḥ iti-ādeḥ api ajātasya prati-vidyaṃ vyavasthitatayā avyavasthā syāt; tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.3.34 \|\|

*idaṃ mananāt*

**Комментарий:**

ātmanas—ābhimukhyena mananam, anucintanam \| ātmanas taddhitīyatvena guṇa-jātaṃ sarvatra anusandheyatvena prāpyam, yat chruta-latā-vihita-viśeṣitam ānandādikam \| yena guṇa-jātena vinā brahma-svarūpasya itara-vyāvṛttasya asusandhānaṃ na saṃbhavati; tat eva sarvatra anuvartanīyam; tat ca iyadeśy-arthaḥ \| itare tu sarva-karmāntavyāḥ pradhāna-anuvṛttitaḥ api cintanīyatvena pratibaddhā vyavasthitāḥ \|\| 34 \|\| iti akṣarādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 14 \|\|

**atha antarātva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 15 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.35 \|\|

*antarā bhūta-grāmavat svātmanaḥ anyathā bheda-anupapattiḥ iti cet na upadeśavat \|*

**Комментарий:**

bṛhad-āraṇyake upasta-prastṛna evam āmnāyate yat sākṣāt aparokṣāt brahma ya ātmā sarvāntaraḥ tam eva vyācakṣva iti \| tasya prativacanaṃ yaḥ prāṇena prāṇiti sa ta ātmā sarvāntaraḥ yaḥ apānena apāniti sa ta ātmā ityādi \| anuvṛtte tena punaḥ pṛṣṭe āha enaṃ draṣṭar-draṣṭāraṃ paśyer na śruteḥ śrotāraṃ śṛṇuyāḥ na mateḥ mantāraṃ manvīthāḥ na vijñātuḥ vijñātāraṃ vijānīyā eva ta ātmā sarvāntaraḥ ataḥ anyat ārtam iti \| tathā tat-anantaraṃ kahola-praśne ca evam āmnāyate yat eva sākṣāt aparokṣāt brahma ya ātmā sarvāntaraḥ tam eva vyācakṣva iti \| prativacanaṃ ca yaḥ aśanāyā pipāse śokaṃ mohaṃ jarāṃ mṛtyum atyeti evaṃ hi etam ātmānaṃ viditvā brāhmaṇaḥ putraiṣaṇāyāḥ ca vittaiṣaṇāyāḥ ca ityādi ataḥ anyat ārtam iti anantaram \| tatra saṃśayate—kim anayoḥ vidyā-bhedaḥ asti, na iti \| kiṃ yuktam? bheda iti \| kutaḥ? rūpa-bhedāt \| prativacana-bhedāt rūpaṃ bhidyate \| praśna-saṃyuktatvāt api prativacana-prakāraḥ hi bhedena upalabhyate \| pūrvatra prāṇanādīnāṃ kartā sarvāntara-ātmavena ucyate; paratra aśanāyā-pipāsādi-rahitaḥ \| ataḥ pūrvatra prāṇitā dehaḥ anya-buddhi-manaḥ-prāṇa-vyatiriktaḥ pratyag-ātmā ucyate; paratra tu tat-atiriktaḥ aśanāyā-pipāsādi-rahitaḥ paramātmā \| ataḥ rūpaṃ bhidyate \| bhūta-grāmavattvasya pratyag-ātma-kartṛsya bhūta-grāmasya sarvasya antar-ātmatvena sarvāntaratvam upapannam \| yady api pratyag-ātmanaḥ sūkṣmataratvaṃ bhūta-grāma-mātra-apekṣā eva upapattikam, tathā api dehe grāhyam, anyathā mukhya-antarātma-parigrahe lokaḥ ayam ātma-svīkāre prativacana-bhedaḥ na upapadyate \| prativacane hi pūrvatra pratyag-ātma-viṣayam, paratra ātmanaḥ prāṇi-janasya api nirūha-āhāra-saṃbhavāt \| paraṃ ca paramātma-viṣayam, aśanāyā-pipāsā-rahitatvāt \| tat idam āha sūtre—antarā bhūta-grāmavat svātmanaḥ anyathā bheda-anupapattiḥ iti cet iti \| antarā—sarvāntaratvena prathama-prativacanaṃ

bhūta-mātratva-vā asmataḥ—bhūta-mātra-vāda—tad-anantaraḥ svātmā, pratyag-ātmā sarvāntaraḥ iti ucyata iti arthaḥ \| anyathā yaḥ prāṇena prāṇiti yaḥ aśanāyā-pipāsābhyām atītaḥ iti prativacana-bheda-anupapattiḥ iti cet \|

atra ucyate—na iti \| na vidyā-bhedaḥ iti arthaḥ \| ubhayatra parivicchidyatvāt prasakta-tivacanayoḥ \| tathā hi—yat sākṣāt aparokṣāt brahma yaḥ ātmā sarvāntaraḥ iti praśnaḥ tāvat sarvātma-viṣaya eva \| brahma-śabdasya paramātma-sādhāraṇatve api pratyag-ātma-śabdaḥ api kadācid upacārita-srayoga-śaṅkā-nivṛtty-arthaṃ paramātma-pratipādana-arthaṃ yat sākṣāt brahma iti viśeṣaṇaṃ kriyate \| aparokṣatvam api sarva-vedya-sarva-karatva-saṃbandhitvaṃ satyaṃ jñānam anantaṃ brahma iti ananta-vedyāvagatasya paramātmana eva upapadyate \| sarvāntaratvam api yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivyā antaraḥ iti ārabhya yaḥ ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaraḥ iti sarvāntaryāmiṇaḥ paramātmana eva saṃbhavati \| prativacane api tam eva paramātma-viṣayam \| yaḥ prāṇena prāṇiti iti nirupādhikaṃ prāṇanasya kartṛtvaṃ paramātmana eva, pratyag-ātmanaḥ tu aśṭatvato prāṇaṃ prati kartṛtvābhāvāt \| evam ajñātatā-upadeśena prāṇe kartṛtva-mātra-śrutau macchānena pratyag-ātmanaḥ api sādhāraṇatvaṃ prativacanasya matvā bhujyate punaḥ pṛcchatāṃ prati pratyag-ātmanaḥ vyāvṛtta-nirupādhikatvena prāṇasya kartari paramātmānam āha—na dṛṣṭeḥ draṣṭāraṃ paśyet iti iṣyādiṣu \| indriyādhīnānāṃ darśana-śravaṇa-manana-vijñānānāṃ kartari pratyag-ātmānaṃ prāṇasya kartṛtvena ukta iti na mantavyam; tasya supti-mūrchādyau prāṇānādeḥ kartṛtvāt \| kaḥ hi eva anyāt kaḥ prāṇyāt, yat eṣa ākāśa ānandaḥ na syāt iti sarveṣām api prāṇa-hetutvaṃ hi paramātmana eva ananyatra śrutam \| ataḥ pūrva-praśna-prativacane paramātma-viṣaye \| evam uttare api, akṣarāyāgītīrthasya paramātma-sādhāraṇatvāt \| ubhayatra ataḥ anyat ārtam iti upasaṃhāraika-rūpāt \| praśna-prativacanayoḥ punas tu kūtsna-prāṇi-prāṇa-hetuḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ ajñāna-apādhato-tattva-pratipādanāya \| tatra dṛṣṭāntam āha—upadeśavat iti \| yathā sad-vidyāyām uta tam ādeśam aprākṣyaḥ iti prakrānte samupadeśe bhagavān tu eva me tat bravītu iti bhūya eva mā bhagavān vijñāpayatu iti praśnasya eṣaḥ aṇimā etad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvaṃ tat satyam iti pravacanasya ca bhūyo bhūya āvṛttiḥ asti brahmāntaratamāhātmya-viśeṣa-pratipakṣāya dṛśyate; tadvat \| ataḥ ekatra eva sarvāntara-bhūtasya brahmaṇaḥ kūtsna-prāṇi-prāṇa-hetutva-ajñāna-apādhato-tattva-pratipādanena rūpaikyam vidyaikyam \|\| 35 \|\|

atha syāt—prabandha-dvaye praśna-prativacane para-brahma-viṣaye; tathā api vidyā-bhedaḥ vakṣyate; ekaṃ sarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ prāṇa-hetutvena upāsyam, itarat jñāna-apādhatotvena na iti upāsyam guṇa-bhedena rūpa-bhedāt, prabhṛti-bhedāc ca; pūrvaṃ hi uṣastaḥ praṣṭā, uttatra kaholaḥ iti; tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.3.36 \|\|

*vyatihāraḥ viśiṃṣanti hi itaravat*

**Комментарий:**

na atra vidyā-bhedaḥ, praśna-prativacanābhyām eka-rūpārtha-viṣayatvābhyām eva vidhīyamānaika-

vākya-vyavahṛteḥ \| prathamaṃ tāvat sarvāntara-rāmasya eva vijñāna-brahma-viṣayam \| dvitīye praśne “ya eṣo sākṣāt aparokṣāt brahma ya ātmā sarvāntaraḥ” iti etat kāryaṃ pūrva-upākhyātena dṛḍha-pūrṇa-viśiṣṭa-brahma-viṣayatvaṃ kaṭhola-praśna-sthāyā ādhārayati \| prativacanaṃ ca api atra “sa na ātmā sarvāntaraḥ” iti sarvāntarātmatva-vivakṣita-brāhma-viṣayam eka-rūpam eva \| vidhi-mukhya-proktāntar eva dṛśyate “asnāmadubāhoṇaḥ pāṇi-pādaṃ nityaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ tiṣṭhanti” iti \| evaṃ sarvāntarātmatva-viśiṣṭa-brahma-viṣayatve dṛḍhokta-ragataṃ satyeka-śāstrīṃ ca sarvāntarātmatva-viśiṣṭe brahmopāsye upasaṃhāro’pastaretat-buddhi-vyatirekaḥ kartavyaḥ \| upastarasya yā sarvāntarātmano brahmaṇaḥ sarvā-prāṇi-janāṃ-hetutva-viṣayā buddhiḥ sā kaṭholena itarā api praṣṭā kāryā; yā ca kaṭholasya tattvena brahmopāsanāyādhītātattva-viṣayā buddhiḥ, sā upastena itarā api kāryā \| evaṃ vyatīhāre kṛte ubhābhyāṃ sarvāntarasya brahmaṇo jīva-vyāvṛttiḥ avagatā bhavati \| evaṃ sarvāntarātmanaḥ pratyag-ātmano vyāpṛtamatvāgativat sarvā-prāṇi-janāṃ-hetutva-jñānāyādhītātattva-pratiyādnena viviṣyanti hi yājñavalkyasya prativacanāni \| ato brahmaṇaḥ sarvāntarātmatvam eva upāsya-guṇaḥ \| prāṇa-hetutvādyaistu tasya upāsakāḥ; na upāsyāḥ \| nanu pāsya-guṇaḥ sarvāntarātmatvam eva cet, prāṇa-hetutvasya akṣānāyādhītātattvasya ca praṣṭaḥ vyatīhṛtya-anusandhānaṃ kim-arthaṃ ? tat ucyate—sarveṣā-prāṇi-janāṃ-hetutvena sarvāntarātmani jīva-brahma-bhūte brahmopastetavya-gate sati kaṭholena jīvasya sarvasmānā asaṃbhavitena svābhāva-viśeṣeṇa sarvāntarātma-vyāvṛtto jñāna-sandha iti kṛtvā punaḥ praśnaḥ kṛtaḥ \| yājñavalkyaḥ api sarvebhyaḥ prāṇibhyaḥ pratyag-ātmano’saṃbhavita-samānāyāvidya-vyanīkattvam uktavān \| ataḥ ca upāsyasya vyāvṛtti-niratīniiti-saṃsiddhayeṣu ubhābhyāṃ parastāt buddhi-vyatīhāraḥ kartavyaḥ \| itaratva—yatra ekaḥ ca siddhārthe bhūyo-bhūyaḥ praśneḥ prativacaneṣa tat eva sad brahma vyavacchidyate; na punaḥ pūrva-adhikapāhāduguṇa-guṇāntara-viśiṣṭasya eva upāsyaṃ pratipādyate; tad-dhetu \|\| 36 \|\|

tatra api prathama-tivacana-bhede sati kārya-madhya-vacārāt iti cet, tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.3.37 \|\|

*sa eva hi satyādayaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

sa eva hi—sac-cid-ānandādi-gītā parama-kāraṇa-bhūtā parā devatā eva “satyaṃ devatā iti “na itaḥ paraṃ devatāṇām” iti prakṛtā “yathā somya madhu madhukṛto nirvikṣipanti” ity-ādiṣu paryāyeṣu sarveṣu upadeśe \| yataḥ “bhūtātmā eva ānandaṃ sarvaṃ tat satyaṃ sa ātmā” iti prathama-paryāyoditāvāratmatvādaya-sambandhu paryāyeṣv upasaṃhriyante \|\| kiñca, “vyatīhāro viśiṣyanti hotravat ca “sa eva hi satyādayaḥ” iti sūtra-dvayam adhikaraṇaṃ varṇayati \| tatra pūrveṇa “idaṃ vā āgrā asmin bhagavo devate’haṃ vai tvam asmi bhagavo devate

tathā 'haṃ ṣoḍaśī ṣoḍaśam iti vākye jīva-parayoḥ aikyārthya-hārāyu-sandhānaṃ śruti-prāptam hetucyata iti āhuḥ \| tat tu sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma aitad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvaṃ tat tvam asi iti avagāt sarvasmin nāmādi-viṣaya-vyāpārasya vākyasya mātra pratipādanīya-prama-pūrvam asti iti anādaraṇīyam \| tatra, vakṣyate ārambhaṇaṃ tu śabdādibhyaḥ iti \| na ca sarvasmin nāma-rūpa-pradhānātirekeṇa parasmin brahmaṇi jīva-vat sukha-duḥkhānubhavaḥ, jīve ca para-brahma-vat susūkṣmatvaṃ, tathyaṃ saṃbhavati \| uttareṇa ca sūtreṇa sa yaḥ ha vai tat paramaṃ brahma veda satyaṃ brahma ity-ādi-vākya-pratipāditasya satyopāsanasya udayāstamayī sa ādityaḥ ya eṣa etasmin maṇḍale puruṣaḥ yaḥ ca ayaṃ dakṣiṇe 'kṣiṇi ity-ādi-vākya-pratipādita-upāsanasya ca evaṃ pratipādyata iti; tat apayuktam, uttaratra api avādhādyasthābhedena vidyā-bhedasya pūrvam eva na vā viśeṣāt iti etena pratipāditatvāt \| na ca hyuṇorasyaiṣāhṛdyādi-dharīraskatvena rūpavataḥ hṛta-pāpmānaṃ jarāti ca yaḥ evaṃ veda iti puṃsaṃyoga-codanāvator hy arūpanayoḥ sa yaḥ ha vai tanmahad-yaśaḥ prathamaṃ veda satyaṃ brahma iti jayati mām lokān iti saṃyogārūpamimnatayā nirapekṣaṃ pūrvoproktena upāsanena abheda-saṃbhavati \| na ca hṛta-pāpmānaṃ jarāti iti guṇa-phala-adhikāratvam, pramāṇābhāvāt \| pūrvokta-vidyātve pramāṇam iti cet, itaretarāśrayatvāt \| eka-vidyātve nirṇīte pūrva-phalasya eva pradhāna-phalatvena anāśrayaḥ phala-yogī guṇa-phalatvam, tapo-guṇa-phalatve nirṇīte sati saṃyoga-bhedābhāvāt pūrveṇa vidyāikyam iti itaretarāśrayatvam iti evam-ādi-samyaṃdyotra-prakāra-bheda sūtra-yugmam \|\| 37 \|\| iti antaratvādhikaraṇam \|\| 15 \|\|

**atha kāmādy-adhikaraṇam \|\| 16 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.38 \|\|

*kāmādi itaratra tatra ca āyatanādibhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

chāndogye śrūyate atha yat idam asmin brahma-pure daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma daharaḥ asmin antar-ākāśaḥ tasmin yat antaḥ tat anveṣṭavyaṃ ity-ādi; vājasaneyake ca sa vā eṣa mahān aja ātmā yaḥ ayaṃ vijñāna-mayaḥ prāṇeṣu ya eṣaḥ antar-hṛdaya ākāśaḥ tasmin śete sarvasya vaśī sarvasya īśānaḥ ity-ādi \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim anayoḥ vidyā-aikyam, uta na iti \| kiṃ yuktam? bheda iti \| kutaḥ? rūpa-bhedāt; apahata-pāpmanvādi-vuṇāṣṭaka-viśiṣṭa ākāśaḥ chāndogye upāsyaḥ pratīyate; vājasaneyake svakāmo 'bhayano vaśitvādi-vuṇa-viśiṣṭa upāsyaḥ pratīyate; ataḥ rūpa-bhedād vidyā-bhedaḥ \|\| iti prāpte prabandha-bheda—na bheda iti \| kutaḥ? rūpābhedāt—itaratra tatra ca kāmasya eva hi havaṃ—vājasaneyake chāndogye ca satyakāmādi-viśiṣṭasya eva brahma upāsyam iti arthaḥ \| kuta

etad avagamyate ? āpātato nirdeśaḥ— hṛdaya-anantyena setutva-vidharaṇatva-vibhiḥ tāvat brahma-paraṃ ca eva vidyā iti pratyabhijñāyate \| vaśitvādayaḥ ca vājaneyake śrutāḥ \| chāndogyasya tasya guṇāḥ; apahata-pāpmatvaṃ satya-saṅkalpatvasya viśeṣaḥ eṣa iti satya-saṅkalpatva-sahacāriṇaṃ satya-kāmatvādīnām apahata-pāpmatva-pratenānāṃ sandṛḍham avagamyanti \| ato rūpaṃ na bhidyate \| saṃyogaḥ api paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate abhayaṃ vai brahma bhavati iti brahma-prāpti-rūpaḥ na bhidyate \| ākāśa-śabdaḥ chāndogye paramātma-viṣayaḥ iti, dahara uttarebhyaḥ ity atra nirṇītam \| vājasaneyake tu ākāśe śayānasya vaśitvādi-viśravaṇāt tasya śayānasya paramātmatve sati tad-ādhāratva-dharmiṇi ākāśa-śabdasya sa eṣa neti neti ātmā gṛhyaḥ na hi gṛhyate iti hṛdayāntar-vartinasya puruṣa-śabda-vācyasya ākāśasya abhidhāyakatvam avagamyate \| ato viśeṣaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

atha syāt— yadi uktaṃ vājasaneyake vaśitvādi-viśiṣṭhe satya-kāmatvādi-sandṛḍhāvagamyata iti; tat na upapadyate, vaśitvādīnām eva tatra paramārthatas sandṛḍhāvabhāvāt \| tad-abhāvaḥ ca manasā eva anudraṣṭavyaṃ na iha nānā asti kiñcana \| mṛtyoḥ sa mṛtyum āpnoti yaḥ iha nānā iva paśyati \|\| ekadhā eva anudraṣṭavyam etat aprameyaṃ dhruvam iti prakṛtena vākyena sa eva neti neti ātmā iti uttareṇa ca upāsayasya brahmaṇo nirviśeṣatva-pratīteḥ ucyate \| ato vaśitvādayaḥ api sthūlatvāpūrṇatvavat niṣedhyāḥ iti pratīyante \| ata eva chāndogye api satya-kāmatvādayaḥ na brahmaṇaḥ pāramārthikā guṇāḥ ucyante \| ato avāstavārthika-vāde vedānta-upakramaṃ guṇānāṃ mokṣārthopāsyeṣu lopaḥ iti \| tatrāha—

## \|\| 3.3.39 \|\|

*ādarāt alopaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

brahma-guṇatvena pramāṇāntarāpratītānāṃ guṇānām evaṃ satya-kāmatvādīnāṃ atiṣṭhan yad antarasto dṛśyeta eṣa ātmā apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ avijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpaḥ sarveśaḥ vaśī sarveśānaḥ eṣa sarveśvaraḥ eṣa bhūtādhipatiḥ eṣa bhūta-pālaḥ eṣa setuḥ vidharaṇaḥ eṣāṃ lokānām asaṃbhedāya iti ādibhiḥ tadyoḥ śrutyoḥ ādarāt tu ca mokṣārthopāsanopāsya-brahma-guṇatvena sādaropadeśāt evam alopaḥ; api tu parihāraḥ eva kāryaḥ \| chāndogye tāvat tasya ha vai etasya mahato bhūtasya niḥśvasitam etat yat ṛg-vedaḥ yajur-vedaḥ sāma-vedaḥ atharvāṅgirasaḥ iti satya-kāmatvādi-nirguṇa-viśiṣṭasya brahmaṇo veda-janmidhāya atha ye iha ātmānam anuvindya vrajanti etāṃś ca satyān kāmān teṣāṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu kāma-cāraḥ bhavati iti satya-kāmatvādi-guṇa-viśiṣṭa-vedanasya ādaraṃ darśayati \| tathā vājasaneyake sarveśaḥ vaśī sarveśānaḥ eṣa sarveśvaraḥ eṣa bhūtādhipatiḥ eṣa bhūta-pālaḥ iti bhūyaḥ bhūyaḥ aiśvarya-upadeśāt guṇavādaraḥ pratīyate \| evam anya-

api \| na ca mātā-pitṛ-sahasrebhyaḥ api vatsalataraṃ śāstraṃ pratāraka-vat apramāṇika-svargādi-sādhana-tṛsanīyān guṇān pramāṇa-antara-apratipannān aśeṣa-upadiśya saṃsāra-cakra-parivartitena pūrvam eva brahma-pramāṇa-anuṣṭhitena bhūyaḥ api bhramayitum arhati \| śrūyate nānā asti kiṃcana ṛtaṃ bandhuḥ vṛddhasya iti tu sarvasya brahma-kāryatvena tad-ātmakatva-loka-bandhutvaṃ vidhāya brahma-ātmakatvena pūrva-siddha-nānātvasyaṃ niṣedhati iti arthaḥ prāk eva prasiddhaḥ \| sa eva neti neti ātmā iti asya ca iti-ādi-vākyena pramāṇa-antara-pratipannaṃ prapañca-ākāraṃ para-sudhya na tathā-vidhaṃ brahma iti sarva-abhimatā-bhūtasya brahmaṇaḥ prapañca-vilakṣaṇatvaṃ pratipadyate \| tat eva ca anantara-vākya-padyati \| agṛhyaḥ na hi gṛhyate agoryaḥ na hi śīryate asaṅgaḥ na hi sajyate avyathitaḥ na vyathate na riṣyati iti \| pramāṇa-antara-grāhya-visaṃjātīyatvāt ramāṇāntareṇa na gṛhyate \| viśaraṇīya-vijātīyatvāt ca viśīryate \| evam uttaratra api anusaṃdheyam \| chāndogye api sat-mūlāḥ prajāḥ sat-āyatanāḥ sat-pratiṣṭhāḥ iti uktvā na vandhaṃ nāma hanvate etat satyaṃ brahma-puram asmin kāmāḥ samāhitāḥ iti sarva-vijātīyatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pratipādya tasmin satya-kāmatvādayaḥ vidhīyante \|\| 39 \|\|

nanu evam api tasya iha akāmān anubhavya prajaretyāṃśa satyān kāmān teṣāṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu kāma-cāraḥ bhavati sa yadi pitṛ-loka-kāmaḥ bhavati iti-ādinā satya-kāmādi-guṇa-viśiṣṭa-vedanasya sāṃsārika-phala-saṃbandha-śravaṇān mukṣaur hatyeṣṭo na jagat brahma-upāsanam \| para-vidyā-phalaṃ ca paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate iti iva \| ata eva vyoma-vatvādayaḥ brahma hetunā upanīyamāḥ iti \| ata uttaraṃ paṭhati—

## \|\| 3.3.40 \|\|

*upasthite’stadvacanāt*

**Комментарий:**

upasthitiḥ—upasthānam, brahma-upasaṃpattau sarva-bandha-vinirmukte svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpannaḥ pratyag-ātmāni ata eva—upasaṃpanna eva hetoḥ tasya bandhu lokeṣu kāma-cāraḥ ucyate paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate sa uttamaḥ puruṣaḥ sa tatra paryeti jakṣan krīḍan ramamāṇaḥ strībhiḥ vā yānaiḥ vā jñātibhiḥ vā na upajanaṃ smaran idaṃ śarīraṃ sa svarāṭ bhavati tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu kāma-cāraḥ bhavati iti \| tad etat na uccārya niṣprapañcatvam upapādayiṣyate \| ataḥ saṃbandhu lokeṣu kāma-cārasya mukta-bhogya-phalatvāt mumukṣoḥ hita-kāmatvādayaḥ guṇāḥ upasaṃhāryāḥ \|\| 40 \|\| iti kāmādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 16 \|\|

**atha tad-vadhāraṇā-niyama-adhikaraṇam \|\| 17 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.41 \|\|

*tad-vadhāraṇā-niyamaḥ tad-dṛṣṭeḥ pṛthak hi apratibandhaḥ phalam*

**Комментарий:**

agni-cayeta-kṣattra-su-gopāsya upāsīta iti-ādīni karma-aṅga-āśrayāṇi upāsanāni karmāṅ-

bhūtādiyo-dharmikena juhvādi-dharmikena paryāpta-avidvat-karmaḍijyena nikhila-anuṣṭhāna-anudyuyodhaya yātanāṃ-vidhiyo śraddhā eva vidhyayā karoti śraddhā-upaniṣadā tadā eva vīryavataraṃ bhavati iti vartamāna-nirdeśasya paryāpta-visaṃbandhi-āpāpa-loka-āśravaṇa-ady-artha-karma-lava-kalanā-yoga-lakṣu niyamena vṛddhāra iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pratyāha—tad-vidhāraṇa-niyama iti \| vidhāraṇaṃ—niścayena matam-odavarthāparam, dhyāna-nimittārthaḥ; tad-vidhāraṇa-niyamaḥ—karmasu dṛṣṭa-ādi-yathā-śruta-santāna-aniyamaḥ; kutaḥ? tad-uddiśe—upalabhyate hi aprāsa-anugraha-aniyamaḥ: nemo kumuto yad etad evaṃ veda yaś ca na veda iti-ādi-viśeṣa-odyuṣṭi-nivacanāt \| na ca śrutve saty-upāsanasya anudāna-aniyama upapadyate \| evaṃ-upāsana-tthāna-dṛṣṭe niścīyate saty-upāsana-vidheḥ phala-ākāṅkṣāyāṃ ratri-satra-nyāyena vīryavattaraṃ karma-phalatva-yathābhūta eva phala-nityayām avate \| kim idaṃ vīryavattaram? karma-kartṛ-saṃbandha-aviśeṣaḥ \| pratibadhyate hi karma-phalaṃ prabala-karma-antara-phalena tāvantaṃ kālam; tat-saṃyoga-pratibandhaḥ \| sa dūrati-bandhaḥ karma-phala-āsvāpādi-liṅgānāṃ tad-yathābhūta eva phalam \| tad-didṛkṣyate—evam api atibandhaḥ phalam iti \| ataḥ karma-aṅga-dhyānām api pṛthak-phalatvād yeteṣāṃ dṛṣṭa-ādivat karmasu upāsanānām aniyamena upehāraḥ \|\| 41 \|\| iti tad-vidhāraṇa-niyama-adhikaraṇam \|\| 17 \|\|

**atha pradhāna-adhikaraṇam \|\| 16 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.42 \|\|

*pradhānavat eva tad uktam*

**Комментарий:**

dahara-vidyāyāṃ atha yad ātmānam anuvidya vrajanti etāṃś ca satyān kāmān iti dahara-ākāśasya paramātman upāsanam uktvā, etāṃś ca satyān kāmān iti guṇānām api pṛthag upāsanaṃ vihitam \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—guṇa-vittve ’pi tad-guṇa-viśiṣṭatayā daharasya ātmani-śritatva-bhāvān na upāsya, uta na iti \| dahara-ākāśasya upahita-tapastvādīnāṃ guṇitvād asya ca samṛddham eva muktatvāṃ śaṅkavā dahara-guṇa-arthaṃ tad-dhittatvaṃ na vartta-niyam \|\|

iti prāpte ucyate—pradhānavat eva iti \| pradhānavat varttanīyam etad arthaḥ \| yady api dahara-ākāśa eka eva upahita-tapastvādi-viguṇānāṃ guṇī; sa ca prathamaṃ vivakṣitaḥ; tathāpi svarūpa-mātra-anudhū-guṇa-viśeṣa-ākārasya bhinnatvāt apaheta-pāpmā vijaraḥ iti-ādinā guṇa-viśiṣṭasya ca upāsyatvena vihitatvāt apūrvaṃ svarūpeṇa anusandhitsayā upahita-tapastvādiyi-viśiṣṭasya anudusandhānārtham āvṛttiḥ karttavyā \| yathā indrāya rājñe puroḍāśam ekādaśa-kapālaṃ nirvapet indrāyādhirājāya indrāya svarāje

iti indratve eva rājatvādi-guṇa-viśiṣṭatve api tatra na guṇa-saṃbandha-mātrasya śiṣṭatvāt pradhāna-adhutiḥ kriyate \| tad uktaṃ śāṇḍilye śrīmān vā devatā pṛthaktvāt iti \|\|4.2\|\| iti pradāna-adhikaraṇam \|\|5\|\|

**atha liṅga-bhūyastva-adhikaraṇam \|\|16\|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.43 \|\|

*liṅga-bhūyastvāt dhi balīyas tad api*

**Комментарий:**

taittirīyā dahara-vidyā-anantaraṃ dhīyate sahasra-śīrṣaṃ devaṃ viśva-akṣaṃ viśva-śambhuvam \| viśvaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ devam akṣaraṃ paramaṃ prabhum \|\| iti ārabhya saḥ akṣaraḥ paramaḥ svarāṭ iti antam \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kiṃ pūrva-prakṛta-vidyā-aṅga-vidyātvena tad-guṇastha-viśeṣa-nirdhāraṇa-mātreṇa kriyate, uta sarva-vedānta-udita-para-vidyā-upāsya-viśeṣa-nirdhāraṇam iti \| kiṃ yuktam ? dahara-vidyā-upāsya-viśeṣa-nirdhāraṇam iti \| kutaḥ ? prakaraṇāt \| pūrvatra anuvāke dahara-vidyā hi prakṛtā daharaṃ vipāpaṃ para-veśma-bhūtaṃ yat-puṇḍarīkaṃ pura-madhya-saṃstham \| tatra api dahaṃ gaganaṃ viśokas tasmin yat antas tad upāsitavyam \|\| iti \| asmin anuvāke ṛtaṃ satyaṃ paraṃ brahma puruṣaṃ iti-ādinā hṛt-puṇḍarīka-vidyā-madhyastha nārāyaṇa-anuvākasya dahara-vidyā-upāsya-nirdhāraṇa-arthatvam upapādayati iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—liṅga-bhūyastvāt iti \| asya nikhila-para-vidyā-upāsya-viśeṣa-nirdhāraṇa-arthatve bhūyāṃsi liṅgāni hi upante; tathā hi—para-vidyā-ānanda-akṣara-viśvāśambhu-para-brahma-paraṃ-jyotiḥ-para-tattva-paramātmā-ādi-śabda-nirdeśa-mukhāni vahīva tāni eva śabda-rūpya tasya nārāyaṇatvaṃ vidhīyate \| bhūyasīṣu vidyāsu śrutānāṃ nārāyaṇatva-vidhāna-bhūyastvaṃ nārāyaṇa eva sarva-vidyāsu upāsyam astu alpa-vidyā-viśeṣita-ānandarīṇāṃ paraṃ brahma iti viśeṣaṇatve bhūyaḥ bahutaraṃ liṅgaṃ bhavati \| atra liṅga-śabdaḥ cihna-paryāyaḥ \| cihna-bhūtaṃ vākyaṃ bahutaram asti iti arthaḥ \| tad dhi prakaraṇāt valīyaḥ \| tad api uktaṃ prathama-kaṇḍe śruti-liṅga-vākya-prakaraṇa-sthāna-samākhyānāṃ samavāye pāra-daurbalyam artha-viprakarṣāt iti \| yat uktaṃ param-ākāśa-śvokāśa-nityā-vidya-vacanaṃ dahara-upāsanasya upetavyamvītih; tatra, balīyasā pramāṇena sarva-vidyā-upāsya-nirdhāraṇa-arthaṃ veda-vākye sati; dahara-vidyāyām api tasya eva nārāyaṇasya upāsyatvena tad-avacchede-pakṣe \| na ca sahasra-śīrṣam iti-ādi-vihita-upāsa-nirdeśena pūrva-anuvāka-udita-upāsanā-sambandhāt unnīyate; asmin yat antas tad upāsitavyam iti upāsanāreka hṛt-pratyayena upāsyasya karma-go aṅgi-hitatvād anudhyāyya dvitīyā-anta-upapattiḥ \| viśvam evaṃ puruṣaḥ satyaṃ nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ iti-ādi-śrutyaminirdeśāt ca prathama-arthe dvitīyā veditavyā \| antar bahiḥ ca tat sarvaṃ vyāpya nārāyaṇaḥ sthitaḥ tasyāḥ śikhāyāḥ madhye paramātmā vyavasthitaḥ; sa brahmā sa śivaḥ sa-indraḥ saḥ akṣaraḥ paramaḥ svarāṭ iti nirdiṣṭa-sarvāntarātmatayo nārāyaṇa eva

sarva-upāsya iti nirṇīyamānatvāt ca prathama-arthe dvitīyā iti nirūpyate \|\| 43 \|\|

iti liṅga-bhūyastva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 19 \|\|

**atha pūrva-vikalpa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 20 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.44 \|\|

*pūrva-vikalpaḥ prakaraṇāt syāt kriyā mānasavat*

**Комментарий:**

vājasaneyake agni-rahasye manścita-ādayaḥ agnayaḥ śrūyante manścitaḥ vāk-ciṭaḥ prāṇa-citaḥ cakṣuḥ-citaḥ śrotra-citaḥ karma-citaḥ agni-citaḥ iti \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim ete manścita-ādayaḥ saṃpādi-dravyatvena vidyā-rūpā antargatāḥ kriyā-maya-kratu-praveśena kriyā-rūpāḥ, āho svid vidyā-maya-kratu-praveśena vidyā-rūpāḥ eva iti viśaye, kriyā-rūpatvaṃ tāvat āhuḥ—pūrva-vikalpa iti-ādinā \| vidhi-antena saṃpāditānām eteṣāṃ manścita-ādīnāṃ kratu-anupraveśa-ākāṅkṣāṇāṃ sva-iṣṭeṣu ṛtu-vidya-bhāvāt pūrvam aṣṭau ha iti ayam āsīt iti ati-daiśika-cintya-strāmeḥ prakṛtatvāt tasya ca kriyā-maya-kratva-rūpatva-aviruddhena tatra kratu-vidhāna-anta-prakaraṇa-gṛhītāḥ manścita-ādayaḥ tena anukacitena agninā vikalpamānāḥ kriyā-rūpāḥ eva syuḥ \| vidyā-rūpāṇām api kriyā-maya-kratu-praveśena kriyā-rūpatvaṃ mānasa-graha-vat upapadyate \| yathā dvādaśāhe ahīna-aṃśe daśame ahani mānasaṃ-grahaṃ mano-niṣpādya-graham-ādāna-stotra-brahma-pratyāharaṇa-lakṣaṇatvena vidyā-rūpasya api kriyā-maya-kratu-śeṣatvāt kriyā-rūpatvam; tathā iha api \|\| 44 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.45 \|\|

*atideśāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

itaḥ ca eka-citā ilāgninā manścita-ādīnāṃ vikalpaḥ kriyā-rūpatvaṃ ca avagamyate \| teṣām eke ekaḥ eva tāvān yāvān asau pūrvaḥ iti pūrveṣu eka-cit-sattva-sāmānyena evaṃ manścita-racittvāt idiśyate; tena tulya-kāryatvāt vikalpaḥ \| tataḥ ca eka-citat-sat-kratu-nirvartanena tat-aṅga-bhūtāḥ manścita-ādayaḥ (kriyā-maya-kratu-praveśena) kriyā-rūpāḥ eva iti \|\| 45 \|\|

**evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—**

## \|\| 3.3.46 \|\|

*vidyā eva tu nirdhāraṇāt darśanāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

tatra āhaḥ pakṣe vyāvartyārthaḥ; yuktāḥ manścita-ādayaḥ kriyā-maya-kratu-praveśena kriyā-rūpāḥ eva iti; na etat arhati; vidyā-rūpāḥ eva ete—vidyā-rūpakatva-viparītaḥ iti-arthaḥ \| kutaḥ? nirdhāraṇāt darśanāt ca \| nirdhāraṇaṃ tāvat—te ha ete vidyā-citāḥ eva vidyayā ha eva ete evaṃ-vidā āśritā bhavanti

iti \| vāṅ-manasaś-śarīra-indriya-prāṇa-agnihotra-ādi-vat cayanānupapatteḥ na manasā saṃpāditānitvena vidyā-rūpatve siddhe 'pi vidyācita eva vidyayā ha eva ete iti vākya-antaraṃ vidyāmayatva-vacane vidyā-ātmaka-śāpatya-artham iti niśreṣyate \| chadyate ca aṅgeṣu eva śeṣaḥ vidyā-rūpaḥ kratuḥ te manasā eva adhīyanta manasā eva acīyanta manasā eva grahāḥ agṛhyanta manasā astuvanta manasā āśaṃsan yat kiñca yajñe karma kriyate, yat kiñca yajñīyaṃ karma manasā eva teṣu mano-mayeṣu mano-niḥsṛṣu mano-mayam akriyata iti \| iṣṭakā-citeḥ vinirūpaṃ yat kriyā-mayaṃ yajñīyaṃ karma kriyate, tasmāt agnitvaṃ sarva-citta-asādhinatvam mano-mayeṣu eva agni-vat iti vacanāt kratuḥ api vidyā-mayaḥ atra pratīyate \|\| 46 \|\|

nanv asya vidhi-artha-aśravaṇāt phala-saṃbandha-apratīteḥ yeṣṭakacitānāṃ ca pratyakṣa-agni-kriyā-maya-kratu-prakaraṇāt vidyā-maya-kratu-vacanena vidyā-rūpatvaṃ bādhyeta; na iti āha—

## \|\| 3.3.47 \|\|

*śruty-ādi-valīyastvāc ca na bādhaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

śruti-liṅga-vākyānāṃ prakaraṇāt valīyastvena śruty-ādi-āghātaḥ kratoḥ evaṃ tat-anvayasya durbalena prakaraṇena bādhituṃ na śakyate \| śrutiḥ tāvat—te ha ete vidyācita eva iti \| tām viśṛṇoti vidyayā ha eva ete evaṃ-vidhaḥ citā bhavanti iti \| vidyayā vidyā-mayena jñānena saṃbaddha-manascitāḥ cayacitā bhavanti iti arthaḥ \| śrāḥ ha etān evam-vide sarvadā sarvāṇi bhūtāni cinvanti api svapate iti liṅgam \| vākyaṃ ca evam-vide cinvanti iti iti \| samabhivyāhāraḥ vākyam \| evam-vide vidyā-maya-kratu-mate sarvadā sarvāṇi bhūtāni cinvanti iti arthaḥ \| sarva-bhūta-kartṛkaṃ sarva-kāla-ādi api cayane manasā saṃpāditaṃ parimita-kartṛ-kāla-kriyā-maya-iṣṭakācita-ākhyāt areṇa kratu-praveśa-saṃbhavam atra abhāvaṃ vidyā-maya-kratoḥ anupraveśe liṅgaṃ bhavati \|\| 47 \|\|

yat ca idam uktaṃ—vidhi-artha-aśravaṇāt phala-saṃbandha-apratīteś ca kriyā-maya-kratoḥ vai teṣāṃ vidyā-aṅgaḥ kratuḥ saṃbhavati iti, tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.3.48 \|\|

*anubandha-ādibhyaḥ prajñā-antara-pṛthaktva-vat dṛṣṭaś ca tat uktam*

**Комментарий:**

iṣṭakacitām agnīnāṃ kriyā-maya-kratoḥ vidyā-mayaḥ ayaṃ kratuḥ pṛthaktvena anubandha-ādibhyaḥ pṛthaktva-hetubhyaḥ avagamyate \| anubandhaḥ prayāgu-vidhayo graha-stośastra-ādayaḥ manasā eva grahāḥ agṛhyanta manasā astuvanta manasā āśaṃsata iti ādinā pratipāditāḥ \| ādi-śabdena śruty-ādayaḥ pūrvokti gṛhyante \| śruty-ādi-siddhaḥ tāvat bandhaḥ vidyā-maya-kratuḥ pṛthak avagamyata iti arthaḥ \| prajñā-antara-pṛthaktva-vat ca—yathā prajñā-antaraṃ dahara-vidyā-ādi kriyā-maya-kratoḥ pṛthag-bhūtaṃ śruty-ādi-viśiṣṭam avagamyate, evam ayam api \| evaṃ ca anubandha-ādibhiḥ pṛthag-bhūte vidyā-maye yatheccham ānāne sati vidhiḥ parikalpyate \| bṛhat-prātuvratasya eva kalyamānaḥ vidhiḥ \| tat uktaṃ vacanāni tu apūrvattvāt iti \| phalaṃ ca teṣām ekai ka

eva tāvān yāvān asau pūrvaḥ \| iti atideśād ratha-kratu-śoḍaśi-citta-sthāni eva itaratvam, tad eva manniśrātādīnām api ratha-kratu-śoḍaśi-phalaṃ mithyā āgamyate \|\| 48 \|\|

yad-upasthita-deśena tulyam āṃpacyābāmāgnihotrāmayanakratu-praveśaḥ agamyata iti uktam, tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.3.49 \|\|

*na sāmānyād api upalabdheḥ mṛtyu-vat na hi loka-āpattiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

na avayavam atideśād eva ānantaryāpārasthyā api tulyatā bhavitavyam, yena kriyā-maya-kratvaṃ tu bhavet pūrvaḥ syāt, yasmāt saṃskara-sahita-sāmānya-mātram atideśa-upalabdheḥ \| upalabhyate hi asya pūrvam eva mṛtyum eva etasmin maṇḍale puruṣaḥ iti ādiṣu saṃhṛta-deśa-viśeṣa-sāmānya-mātra-atideśaḥ \| na hi tatra maṇḍala-puruṣa mṛtyu-vat tat-loka-āpattiḥ—tat-deśa-āpattiḥ api bhavati \| evam iha api manniśrātādīnām agnikcittānindriyam atideśa-mātreṇa agnikcittāgni-deśa-rūpa-kriyā-maya-kratva-praveśena api na bhavitavyam \| ata idamagnikcitāmāḥ ratha-kratu-śoḍaśaṃ yat phalam, tad eva manniśrātādīnām api vidyā-maya-kratu-śoḍaśena phalam iti atideśād avagamyate \|\| 49 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.50 \|\|

*pareṇa ca śabdasya tādvidhyaṃ bhūyastvāt tu anubandhaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

pareṇa ca brāhmaṇena asyā api manniśrātādi-vidyāgniḥ śabdasya tādvidhyaṃ—tad-vidhatvam, vidyā-prastuti-paratvam avagamyate \| pareṇa hi brāhmaṇena ayam vāva loka eṣaḥ agnicitaḥ tasya api eva pariśritaḥ iti ādinā sa yaḥ ha etān evaṃ veda lokān pṛṇamevaṃ, pṛṇāmevaṃ bhūmim eteṣu eva amṛtam āste iti pṛthak-phalatā vidhīyate, tathā vaiśvānara-vidyāto ca vidyā eva vidhīyate \| ataḥ agnirahastrasya kriyā-kratu-vṛttvaṃ nāsti \| evaṃ tarhi vidyā-mayā manniśrātādayo bṛhadāraṇyakeṣvabadbhyāḥ karmabhir nubadhyante? tatra ucyate—bhūyastvāt tu anubandhaḥ iti \| manniśrātāmbu-sampradāna-upayāmana-rūḍhānāṃ bhūyastvāt tatsavidyāvidhānubandhaḥ kṛtaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|

iti pūrva-vikalpādhikaraṇam \|\| 20 \|\|

**atha śārīre saṃbādhikaraṇam \|\| 21 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.51 \|\|

*eka ātmanaḥ śarīre bhāvāt*

**Комментарий:**

sarvāsu para-vidyāsu upāsyo guṇa-tad-rūpa-bahu-poṣaka-karūpeṣu api jñātatvena muktaṃ śrūyagaṇa-meva etan-mukhyāḥ pratyabhijñeti \| vakṣyati ca ayaṃ pratyag-ātmanaḥ paramātma-ātmakatvena anusandhānam ātmāna iti tu upagacchanti grāhayanti ca iti \| kim ayaṃ pratyag-ātmā jñātā kartā bhoktā hāpūtra saṃcāra-kṣamaḥ aṇu-svarūpaḥ, uta prajāpati-tākṣodita-apahata-pāpātmāditi-varūpaḥ? kiṃ yuktam?

jñātṛtva-ādi-ākāram atra iti eke manyate \| kutaḥ ? adhyopāsa-karmāsaktaḥ śarīre bhāvāt \| ātmani śarīre vartamānasya tadīhānmeva rūpam \| tāvantaṃ guṇa-samṛddhena tat-phala-asiddheś ca, paratreṣṭra \| na hi karma-bandhi-dhvastānāṃ svarga-ādi-phala-arthināṃ jñātṛtva-ādi-nirekeṇa phalānubhava-daśāyāṃ grāhyaṃ rūpam, tādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ sādhānuguṇa-tadaśāyām utpadyate’vyam \| tāvad eva sādhānuguṇānantakalpyotiḥsandarśatiriktajñānusukhena prayojanābhāvāt; tad-viśeṣāpekṣā api tathaiva \| nanu ca atra śrūyathāmṛtarasmin loke puruṣo bhavati tathetaḥ pretya bhavati iti viśeṣa-vacanāt apahṛta-pāpmatva-ādi-ākāraḥ eva anusandhātavyaḥ iti avagamyate \| na evaṃ, taṃ yathāyathopāsate iti upāsya-viṣaya-vedanāsam \|\| 51 \|\|

**evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—**

## \|\| 3.3.52 \|\|

*vyatirekaḥ tad-bhāva-bhāvitvāt na tu upalabdhi-vat*

**Комментарий:**

na tat eva asti—yad jñātṛtva-ādi-ākāraḥ eva anusandheya iti \| asya ayam ananta-saṃsāra-daśāyāḥ mokṣa-daśāyāṃ yo vyatirekaḥ, mokṣa-daśā-apahṛta-pāpmatvādihetujanmadhyaḥ; asya mokṣa-daśāyāṃ yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ, tādṛśam eva upāsana-velāyām ātmanaḥ anusandheya iti arthaḥ \| kutaḥ ? tad-bhāva-bhāvitvān tadṛpāpattiḥ; śrūyathāmṛtarasmin loke puruṣo bhavati tathā itaḥ pretya bhavati taṃ yathā yathā upāsate tad eva bhavati iti yathopāsanam eva hi prāptiḥ śrūyate \| na ca paratva-rūpa-mātra-viṣayam avedanīti vaktuṃ śakyate, pratyag-ātmanaḥ api upāsana-bhūta-tpara-brahma-śarīratayā upāsya koṭi-niviṣṭatvāt \| ataḥ prajāpatitvopadeśitāpahṛtapāpmatvādiviśiṣṭa-pratyag-ātma-śarīre-paramātmopāsanasya tad-rūpam eva prāpyam iti uktaṃ bhavati \| ata eva evaṃ kratuṃ loke pretyābhisambhavitāsmi iti ucyate \| tasmāt apahata-pāpma-ākāraḥ eva anusandheyaḥ \| upalabdhi-vat—brahma-upalabdhiḥ yathā brahmopalabdhi-rūpiṇī yathāvasthita-brahma-svarūpa-viṣayā, tathā ātmopalabdhiḥ api yathāvasthita-ātma-svarūpa-viṣayā iti arthaḥ \| karmasu ātma-svarūpānusandhānaṃ karmāṅgam; yo’reta svarga-kāma iti karmānuguṇam eva hi phalatvaṃ codyate \| dehātiriktātmādhātvyākārātma-smṛtiḥ kālāntarasthāvikāma-sādhana-karmādhikārārtha iti tāvan-mātram eva tatra apekṣitam iti na kiñcid avahīnam \|\| 52 \|\| iti śārīra-bhāṣyādhikaraṇam \|\| 21 \|\|

**atha aṅgāvabaddhādhikaraṇam \|\| 22 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.53 \|\|

*aṅgāvabaddhāḥ tu na śākhāsu hi prati-vedam*

**Комментарий:**

om iti etat akṣaram udgītham upāsīta lokeṣu pañca-vidhaṃ sāmaṃ upāsīta udgītham upāsīta iti te prajā vadanti tad idam eva abhyam iti evam pṛthivī ayam vāva lokaḥ eṣaḥ agni-citaḥ iti evaṃ-ādyāḥ kratu-aṅgeṣu atha upāsanā bhavanti \| tāḥ kiṃ yāsu śākhāsu śrūyante, tāsu eva niyatāḥ;

uta sarvāsu śākhāsu dṛṣṭa-upāsyeṣu saṃbadhyata iti vicāraḥ \| sarva-vedānta-pratyayatve sthite api prati-vedaṃ svara-bheda-durgāṇopacayo bhidyata iti tat-tantra vyavatiṣṭheran iti yuktā śaṅkā \| kiṃ yuktam ? vyavatiṣṭheran iti \| kutaḥ ? udgītham upāsīta iti sāma-mātreṇa udgītha-saṃbandhitayā śrutāyāḥ tat-tad-āśrayā eva śākhāyāṃ svara-viśeṣa-yuktasya udgītha-viśeṣasya saṃnidhānāt tasmin eva viśeṣe paryavasānaṃ yuktam iti, evam anyatra api śākhāsu vyavatiṣṭheran iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—anaṅgāvabaddhāḥ tu iti \| tulya-vadaḥ pakṣe vyāvartayati \| na hi udgīthādṛṣṭāvabaddhā upāsanāḥ tāsu eva śākhāsu vyavatiṣṭheran; api tu prati-vedaṃ saṃbadhyeta; sarvāsu śākhāsu iti arthaḥ \| dṛṣṭāntaḥ hetau \| yasmāt ca udgīthādi-anaṅgāvabaddhāḥ, tasmāt veṣopāsyāḥ, tatra sarvatra saṃbadhyeraṃ \| yady api svara-bhedena udgītha-vyaktayo bhidyante; tathā api sāmānyena udgītha-śrutayā sarvā vyaktayaḥ samabhihitā iti na kvacit vyavasthāyāṃ pramāṇam asti \| sarva-śākhā-pratyayatvena ca sarvāsu śākhāsu kratuḥ ekaḥ \| atas sarvāsu śākhāsu ekasya kratoḥ asaṃnidhānāt kratv-aṅga-bhūta-udgītha-vyaktayo api saṃnihitā iti na ekasya saṃnidhi-viśeṣaḥ asti iti na vyavasthā \|\| 53 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.54 \|\|

*mantra-ādi-vat vā avirodhaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

brāhmaṇa-prāyaṃ \| ādhikāreṇa jāti-guṇa-dravya-ādi-ātmaka-karmaṇi gṛhyante \| yathā mantra-ādīnām eka-kratu-avacchinnatāyām api śeṣiṇaḥ kratoḥ sarva-śākhāsu ekatvena yathā-yathaṃ śruty-ādi-missaṃbandhuṃ śākhāsu viniyogo na virudhyate; tad-vat iha api avirodhaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

iti aṅgāvabaddha-adhikaraṇam \|\| 22 \|\|

**atha bhūmjyāyastva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 23 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.55 \|\|

*bhūmnaḥ kratu-vaj-jyāyastvaṃ tathā hi darśayati*

**Комментарий:**

prācīnaśāla aupamanyavaḥ iti ārabhya vaiśvānara-vidyā āmnātā \| tatra vaiśvānaraḥ paramātmā trailokya-śarīra upāsyatvāt; sva-loka-āditya-vāyu-ākāśa-pṛthivī-avayavaḥ \| tatra ca dyaur-mūrdhā, ādityaḥ cakṣuḥ, vāyuḥ prāṇaḥ, ākāśaḥ sandehaḥ;—madhya-kāya iti arthaḥ, āpaḥ bastiḥ, pṛthivī pādau iti avayava-viśeṣāḥ \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim ayaṃ trailokya-śarīrasya vyastasya eva upāsanaṃ kartavyam, uta vyastasya samastasya ca, atha samastasya eva iti \| kiṃ yuktam ? vyasta-upāsanam eva pradeśāt \| tathā hi upariśyate—aupamanyava-ādayaḥ kilohālakuṣṭhāḥ kaikeyam aśvapatiṃ pary

ātmānam eva imaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ saṃprati adhyeṣi tam eva naḥ brūhi iti papracchuḥ \| sa ca tebhyaḥ pratyekaṃ sva-upāsya-mūrdha-mūrtim uktvā-iṣṭaḥ mūrdhā iti vyasta-upāsanaṃ tat-tat-phalaṃ ca uktavān atyeti pratyeti priyaṃ bhavati asya brahma-varcasī kule yaḥ evam etam ātmānaṃ vaiśvānaram upāste mūrdhā tu eṣa ātmana iti ha uvāca ātmā vaiśvānaraḥ iti-ādinā \| teṣu evaṃ pṛṣṭa-upāsana-upāsasya vaiśvānara-śabdaṃ ca āha \| ataḥ vyasteṣu eva upāsanaṃ kartavyam \| paraṃ yaḥ tu etam evaṃ prādeśa-mātram abhivimānam ātmānaṃ vaiśvānaram upāste iti ghu-pṛṣṭa-viśeṣa-śāli-cchamātre vaiśvānare uktasya mūrdhā-ādi-upāsanasya samāsena upasaṃhāraḥ iti etat na eva \| apara āha—evam eva samasta-upāsanaṃ kāryam iti, pṛthak-phala-nirdeśāt ca yaḥ tu etam eva prādeśa-mātram abhivimānam ātmānaṃ vaiśvānaram upāste saḥ sarveṣu lokeṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣu ātmasu annam atti iti \| na ca etāvatā vākya-bhedaḥ \| yathā bhūma-vidyā-upakrame nāma-ādi-upāsane tatat-phala-prāpti-siddhyai ṛg tu vā ativadati iti-ādinā bhūma-vidyām uddiśya saḥ svarāḍ bhavati tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu kāma-cāraḥ bhavati iti tat-phalaṃ vyapadiśati \| tatra bhūma-vidyā-paratve’pi vākyasya nāma-ādi-antara-upāsanaṃ tat-phalaṃ ca aṅgīkriyate, tathā iha api iti \|\| evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate—bhūmnaḥ jyāyastvam iti \| bhūmnaḥ—vipulatvasya, samastatvasya, jyāyastvaṃ—prāmāṇikatva-nirṇayaḥ; eka-vākyatva-avagateḥ \| tathā hi yāt śaunena āśāla aupamanyava iti upakramya uddālakaḥ ha vai bhagavantaḥ ayam āruṇiḥ saṃpratīm am ātmānaṃ vaiśvānaram adhyeti taṃ hanta abhyāgacchāma iti vaiśvānara-ātmasu bubhutsayā upananya-vādaḥ pañca maha-ṛṣayaḥ tam uddālakam upetya tatra vaiśvānara-ātma-saṃvedanam alabhamānāḥ tena ca mahā-kṣapatiṃ kaikeyaṃ vaiśvānara-ātma-vidam upasaṃgamya ātmānam eva imaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ saṃprati adhyeṣi tam eva naḥ brūhi iti pṛṣṭā tat-prakāśāt paramātmānaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ sva-abhīṣṭi-śṛṣṭi-sthiti-anta-sṛṣṭasya ca upāsanam anāmayam tat-phalaṃ ca sarva-loka-sarva-bhūta-sarva-ātma-saṃbhūta-abrahma-anubhavam avagatavanta iti upasaṃhārataḥ vākyasya ekatvam avagamyate \| ekasmin vākya-upadeśe sati tat-śayava-viśeṣa-upāsti-vacanaṃ phala-nirdeśaḥ ca samasta-upāsana-śeṣatayā upāttam arthaḥ iti niścīyate \| anubandha—yathā vaiśvānaraṃ dvādaśa-kapālaṃ purve jāte iti vihitasya eva kratoḥ eka-deśaḥ yat aṣṭa-kapālaḥ bhavati iti-ādibhiḥ tadvat eva, tathā samasta-upāsanasya eva nyāyyam, na vyasta-upāsanam \| tathā hi darśa-pūrṇamāse śrutiḥ vyasta-upāsane anarthaṃ bruvate sūrya te vyatiniyacchām na āgamiṣyaḥ iti, asamīḍhaḥ bhaviṣyo yasmāṃ na āgamiṣyaḥ iti-ādikā \| ataḥ idam api apāstam, yathā nāma-ādi-upāsana-sāmyam uktasya \| tatra hi nāma-ādi-upāsane vakṣya-arthaḥ na śrutaḥ, nāma-ādi-upāsanebhyaḥ bhūmopāsanasya āśāyikatvaṃ śrutam ṛg tu vā ativadati yaḥ satyena ativadati iti \| tat eva tatra bhūma-vidyā-paratve’pi vākyasya nāma-ādi-upāsanānāṃ sakalānāṃ vivakṣitatva-thuḥ; aṃśi-āsthiti-vitat-phala-nirmisatā-vacanena bhūma-vidyāsu tu anupapatter iti \| ataḥ samasta-upāsanam eva nyāyyam \|\|45\|\| iti bhūma-jyāyastva-adhikaraṇam \|\|23\|\|

**atha śabda-ādi-bheda-adhikaraṇam \|\| 24 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.56 \|\|

*nānā śabda-ādi-bhedāt*

**Комментарий:**

iha brahma-vidyāsu sarvāḥ satya-grāhi-rūpa-mokṣa-eka-phalāḥ sad-vidyā-bhūma-vidyā-dahara-vidyā-upakośala-vidyā-śāṇḍilya-vidyā-vaiśvānara-vidyā-ānandamaya-vidyā-akṣara-vidyā-ādikāḥ eka-śākhā-gatāḥ śākhā-antara-gatāḥ ca śrotāḥ; anyāḥ pramāṇaiḥ kartavyatāḥ ca \| kiṃ nu vidyā-ekyam, uta vidyā-bhedaḥ iti saṃśayate \| atra evaṃ parasmara-śeṣe sarvāsām satya-ekayā dahara-vidyā-ādikayā sarva-vedānta-pratyaya-nyāyaḥ \| kiṃ yuktam? vidyā-ekyam iti \| kutaḥ? vedyasya brahmaṇaḥ ekatvāt; vedye hi vidyānāṃ rūpam; ataḥ rūpa-ekyam api ca aikyaṃ kathayati \|\|

evaṃ prāpte ’bhidhīyate—nānā iti \| nānā-bhūtā vidyāḥ; kutaḥ? śabdāt bhedāt—ādi-śabdena abhyāsa-saṃjñā-guṇa-prakriyāṇām api etāni gṛhyante \| śabda-tantrā hi viśiṣṭā vidhiḥ ca hetavaḥ anubandha-bhedāḥ haryante \| atha api veda-upāsanīyatayā śabdaḥ pratyaya-vṛtti-abhidhāyitvam; pratyayāḥ ca brahma-viṣayāḥ; tathā api tat-tat-prakaraṇa-udita-tat-jana-vedaka-āra-gaṇa-vāk-manas-ādi-viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭa-brahma-ātma-vastu-vṛtty-ādṛṣṭa-vaidhāḥ pratyayā adhirūpāḥ vidyāḥ bhindanti \| brahma-agni-rūpa-phala-saṃbandhi-upāsana-viśeṣa-vidhāyīni ca nirākāṅkṣāṇi vākyāni prati-viṣayaṃ vilakṣaṇa-vidyā-vidhāyīni iti niścīyate \| antimam arthaṃ śabda-antare karma-bhedaḥ iti-ādibhiḥ pūrva-kāṇḍa-udita-tantra-sūtraiḥ siddhaiḥ api punaḥ iha pratipādanaṃ vedānta-vākyāni avidheya-jñāna-parāṇi iti ku-buddhi-nirāsanāya \| ataḥ vidyā-bhedaḥ iti sthitam \|\| 56 \|\| iti śabda-ādi-bheda-adhikaraṇam \|\| 24 \|\|

**atha vikalpa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 25 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.57 \|\|

*vikalpaḥ aviśiṣṭa-phalatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

satya-abhisandhānāṃ sad-vidyā-dahara-vidyā-ādīnāṃ nānātvam uktam \| idānīm āsāṃ vidyānām ekasmin puruṣe prayojanavattvena samuccayaḥ api saṃbhavati, uta prayojana-abhāvāt vikalpaḥ eva iti viṣaye, kiṃ yuktam? samuccayaḥ api saṃbhavati iti \| kutaḥ? eka-phalatāṃ bhidyamāna-arthānām api samuccaya-darśanāt \| iṣyate hi karmaṇāṃ svarga-ādi-sādhanatā-anāninihotra-darśa-pūrṇamāsa-ādīnāṃ tasya eva svargasya śīghratvāt ekasmin puruṣe samuccayaḥ \| evam iha api brahma-anubhava-bhūta-svāpekṣayā samuccayaḥ api saṃbhavati iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pratyucyate—vikalpaḥ eva; na samuccayaḥ saṃbhavati iti \| kutaḥ? aviśiṣṭa-phalatvāt—sarvāsāṃ hi brahma-vidyānām anavadhika-atiśaya-ānanda-brahma-anubhavaḥ phalam aviśiṣṭaṃ śrūyate

brahma-vit āpnoti param sa eko brahma ānandaḥ; audicyasya ca akāmu-hatasya yadā paśyaḥ paśyate rukma-varṇaṃ kartāram īśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahma-yonim \| tadā vidvān puṇya-pāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyam upaiti \|\| iti-ādibhyaḥ \| brahma hi svasya parasya ca svam anubhūyamānam anavadhika-atiśayānandaṃ bhavati \| sa ca tādṛśo brahmānubhavaḥ ekayā vidyayā avāpyate cet, kim anyubhiti na samuccaya-saṃbhavaḥ \| svābhīṣṭhiṃ deśataḥ kālataḥ svarūpataś ca parimitatvena tatra deśādi-apekṣayā bhūyastva-saṃbhavāt tādarthyena samuccaya-saṃbhavati; iha tu nirupādhikatve brahmaṇi tatra saṃbhavati \| sarvāś ca vidyāḥ brahmānubhava-virodhy-ādi-anādi-karma-vinivṛtti-nirata-mukha eva brahma-prāpti-phalāḥ iti aviśiṣṭa-phalatvāt sarvāsāṃ vikalpa eva \|\| 57 \|\|

brahma-prāpti-vyatirikta-phalāsu vidyāsu adhika-phala-karma-avacchedeṣu vikalpaḥ astu, samuccīyeran vā, tāsāṃ parimita-phalatvena bhūyastva-apekṣā-asaṃbhavāt \| tad āha—

## \|\| 3.3.58 \|\|

*kāmyāḥ tu yathā-kāmaṃ samuccīyeran vā pūrva-hetv-abhāvāt*

**Комментарий:**

aparimita-phalatva-abhāvāt iti arthaḥ \|\| 58 \|\| iti vikalpādhikaraṇam \|\| 25 \|\|

**atha yathāśraya-bhāvādhikaraṇam \|\| 26 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.3.59 \|\|

*aṅgeṣu yathāśraya-bhāvaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

udgīthādi-kratv-aṅgāśritāḥ om iti etad akṣaram udgītham upāsīta iti-ādikā vidyāḥ kim udgīthādi-vat kratv-arthayā kratuṣu niyamena upādeyāḥ; uta godohanādi-vat puruṣārthayā yathā-kāmam iti vicaye, niyamena upādeyāḥ iti yuktam \| nanu ca āsāṃ puruṣārthatā-niyamaḥ prati-vāditaḥ tad-adhīraṇa-aniyamaḥ tad-dṛṣṭeḥ; pṛthak hi apratibandhaḥ phalam iti atra \| satyam; tad eva brūhītuṃ kañcit-phalitendra-stuti-mukhe kathā ca ākṣipyate \| tatra hi tena ubhau kurutaḥ iti aniyama-darśanāt pṛthak-phalatvam uktam \| upāsanā-prabhūtena udgīthādi-vat upāsanā-mātra-avalambya-upāsanā-niyame bahavo hetava upalabhyante \| na hi atra godohanena paśu-kāmasya praṇayet iti-ādi-vat upāsanā-vidhi-vākye phala-sambandhaḥ śrūyate; udgītham upāsīta iti udgīthādi-sambandhitvena eva upāsanaṃ pratīyate \| yad eva vidyayā karoti śraddhayā upaniṣadā tad eva vīryavattaram iti vartamāna-upadeśa-rūpatā-ākhyāntarād dhi phala-sambandho jñāyate \| sva-vākyena ca āṭhy-abhicarita-kratu-sambandhi-udgīthādi-sambandhena nimitatvasya bhāṣyāntaratva-vartamāna-phala-sambandha-nirdeśaiḥ arthavāda-mātraṃ syāt, apāpekṣaika-prayāṇādi-vat \| ato yathedṛgīthasya upāsanā-āśrayaḥ kratv-aṅgatayā prayoga-vidhinā niyamena upādīyante; tathā tad-āśritāḥ ca upāsanāḥ sat-sambandhena kratv-aṅga-bhūtā iti niyamena upādeyā eva \|\| 59 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.60 \|\|

*śiṣṭeś ca*

**Комментарий:**

śiṣṭiḥ śāsanam, vidhānam iti arthaḥ \| udgītham upāsīta iti udgītha-ajñānayoḥ saṃnidhānāt ca upādāna-niyamaḥ \| go-reha enam paśu-kāmasya praṇayet iti ādi-vat vi-vidyā-pradeśa-vikārāntara-śravaṇāt jñānaṃ syāt bhāva eva hi vidheyaḥ iti gamyate \|\| 60 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.61 \|\|

*samāhārāt*

**Комментарий:**

hotṛ-ṣadanāt eva api dur-udgītham anusamāharati iti upāsanasya samāhāra-niyamaḥ dṛśyate \| ‘udgīthe vetana-vihema-anudgītham \| vetana-hānā-vacane samādhānaṃ tu vṛttasya niyamena upādānaṃ darśitam \|\| 61 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.62 \|\|

*guṇa-sādhāraṇya-śruteś ca*

**Комментарий:**

upāstana-guṇasya upāstāna-āśrayasya praṇavasya sa-upāsanasya tena eva trayī vidyā vartate oṃ iti āśraya-yogim iti śaṅkā-yogim udgīta-gatiḥ iti sādhāraṇa-śruteḥ ca upāsana-samāhāraḥ gamyate \| tena iti prakṛta-paramarśita-upāsane eva praṇava-saṃjñe saṃcarati \| ata upāsanasya praṇava-sambandhi-niyama-darśanāt ca udgīthāt upāsanāṃ udgītha-vidyā iti na upādānam \|\| 62 \|\|

**iti prāpte ucyate—**

## \|\| 3.3.63 \|\|

*na vā tat-sahabhāva-śruteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

na eva etat asti—yad udgīthāt upāsanānāṃ kratu-udgītha-vidy-upādāna-niyamaḥ iti \| kutaḥ ? tat-sahabhāva-śruteḥ—udgītha-ajñānābhyāṃ hi sahabhāva-niyamaḥ bhavati \| yady api udgītham upāsīta iti asmin padam udgītha-adhikāra-ātmakam na pratīyate; tathā api tat-antareṇa eva yat eva vidyayā karoti śraddhayā upaniṣadā tat eva vīryavat-taraṃ bhavati iti vidyāyāḥ kratu-aṅgebhyaḥ taratvaṃ prati sādhanatvaṃ pratipādyate \| tena kratu-phalāt pṛthak-bhūta-phala-sādhanabhūtā vidyā udgītham upāsīta iti kartavyatayā vidhīyate \| kratu-phalāt pṛthak-bhūta-phala-sādhanatayā avatārasya upāsanasya kratu-anṛṣṭa-bhūta-udgītha-aṅgatayā viniyogaḥ na upapadyate \| ata upāsanastha-adhyāyi-dekṣāyāṃ saṃnihitaḥ udgīthaḥ āśraya-mātraṃ bhavati \| udgīthasya kratu-aṅga-bhūtaḥ iti kratu-prayukta-udgītha-adhyakṣe upāsane kratu-adhikāriṇa eva kratu-arthaṃ vaitavyatva-va-icchā-nimitta-niyantṛtva-adhikārāntaratva iti na kratuṣu tat-upāsanā-niyamaḥ \| vīryavat-taratvaṃ ca kratu-phalasya prabala-karmāntara-phalaitā-pratibandhaḥ iti uktam \| kratoḥ akhila-kha-phalasya

nirarthāḥ \| pāñcarātrīṇāṃ tu śraddheya vidyayā karoti tat eva vīryavattaraṃ bhavati iti vidyāyāḥ phala-sādhanatva-vat aprakaraṇa-śravaṇādi-phalaṃ prati sākṣāt-sādhana-bhāvaḥ na śruta iti kratu-aṅga-bhūta-brahma-vidyā anyā viniyoga-virodhāt tad-aṅga-bhūtānāṃ phalāntara-sādhana-bhāva-kalpanā-anupapatteḥ tatra phala-śrutiḥ artha-vāda-mātraṃ syāt \|\| 63 \|\|

## \|\| 3.3.64 \|\|

*darśanāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

darśayati ca śrutiḥ upāsanā-niyamam evaṃ vit vai brahmā yajñaṃ yajamānaṃ sarvān ca ṛtvijaḥ abhirakṣati iti brahmaṇaḥ vedanena sarveṣāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ bruvatī \| udgātṛ-prabhṛtīnāṃ vedana-traya-aniyame saṅkoce anupapatteḥ \| anena liṅgena pūrvoktānāṃ samāhāra-ādi-liṅgānāṃ prābalyatvam avagamyate \| ataḥ aniyamam eva iti sthitam \|\| 64 \|\| iti yathā-āśraya-bhāva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 16 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye tṛtīyasya adhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**tṛtīyādhyāye—caturthaḥ pādaḥ—puruṣa-artha-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Pada 4

## \|\| 3.4.1 \|\|

*puruṣa-arthaḥ ataḥ śabdāt iti bādarāyaṇaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

guṇa-upasaṃhāra-anupasaṃhāra-kalā vicāra-eka-pratānatva-cintā kṛtā \| idānīṃ vidyāḥ puruṣa-arthāḥ, uta vidyā-aṅga-karmāṇi iti cintyate \| kiṃ yuktam? ataḥ—vidyā-taḥ puruṣa-artha iti bhagavān bādarāyaṇo manyate \| kutaḥ? śabdāt—śrūyate hi upaniṣadaḥ śabdaḥ vidyā-taḥ puruṣa-arthaṃ bruvan brahma-vit āpnoti param veda aham etam puruṣaṃ mahāntam, āditya-varṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt, tam evaṃ vidvān amṛtaḥ iha bhavati, na anyaḥ panthā vidyate ayanāya \|\| yathā nadyaḥ syandamānāḥ samudre astaṃ gacchanti nāma-rūpe vihāya \| tathā vidvān nāma-rūpāt vimuktaḥ parāt-paraṃ puruṣam upaiti divyam \|\| iti ādiḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha pūrva-pakṣī pratyavatiṣṭhate—**

## \|\| 3.4.2 \|\|

*śeṣatvāt puruṣa-artha-vādaḥ yathā anyeṣu iti jaiminiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

na etat evaṃ prasiddhāntaḥ puruṣa-artha-vidhi-miśra-śabda-avagamyata iti \| na hi eṣaḥ brahma-vit āpnoti iti param iti-ādi-śabdaḥ vedanāt puruṣa-artha-avāptim avagamyati, karmasu kartṛ-bhūtasya ātmanaḥ yāthātmya-vedana-

pratipādana-paratvāt \| ataḥ karmasu saṃskāra-rūpeṇa vidyāyāḥ kratu-śeṣatā tat-phala-bhūta-arthavāda-sāmarthyaṃ yathā-āśrayaṃ dravya-vat—iti jaiminiḥ ācāryaḥ manyate \| tat uktaṃ “dravya-saṃskāra-karmasu para-arthatvāt phala-śrutiḥ artha-vādaḥ syāt” iti \| nanu karmasu kartṛ-jīvāt anyaḥ mumukṣuḥ prāpyatayā vedānteṣu vedyaḥ upadiśyate iti pratīyate—“antarāt upapatteḥ” “bheda-vyapadeśāt ca” “anupraveśāt tu na śārīraḥ” “itara-parāmarśāt saḥ iti cet na asaṃbhavāt” iti evam-ādibhiḥ sūtraiḥ \| tat eva brahma tat tvam asi iti-ādi-sāmānādhikaraṇyena jīvāt an-atiriktam iti etat api “adhikaṃ tu bheda-nirdeśāt” iti evam-ādiḥ nirastam \| sāmānādhikaraṇya-nirdeśaḥ ca “śveta-aśvam idaṃ sarvam” “sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma” iti cetana-acetana-sādhāraṇaḥ; “yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan” “yaḥ ātmani tiṣṭhan” iti-ādinā jagatā tasya ātmatayā avasthitatva-nibandhanaḥ iti “avasthiteḥ iti kāśakṛtsnaḥ” iti-ādibhiḥ upapāditam \| tat kathaṃ karmasu kartuḥ ātmanaḥ yāthātmya-upadeśa-parāḥ vedānta-śabdāḥ iti vidyāyāḥ karma-aṅgatvaṃ pratipādyate ? ucyate—vedānta-vākya-vedyā eva vidyāyāḥ karma-sādhanatvaṃ sūcayadbhiḥ liṅgaiḥ teṣu eva hita-sāmānādhikaraṇya-nirdeśena ca vedānta-śabdāḥ deha-atirikta-jīva-svarūpa-yāthātmya-upadeśa-parāḥ iti balāt abhyupagamaḥ apekṣitaḥ pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ abhiprāyaḥ \| nanu ca kratu-saṃskāra-mukhena eva vidyāyāḥ kratu-praveśaḥ na śakyate vaktum, kartṛ-laukika-vaidika-sādhāraṇyena tasyāḥ cirita-kratuṣu avidhīyamānatvāt \| na evam, laukikasya karmaṇaḥ kartṛ-deha-avyatiriktatva-upapattā deha-atirikta-nitya-ātma-svarūpasya kratau eva upayogāt tat-svarūpa-pratipādana-mukhena kratu-pṛaveśaḥ na virudhyate \| ataḥ vidyāyāḥ kratu-śeṣatātrāt puruṣārthaḥ \|\| 2 \|\| kāni punaḥ tāni liṅgāni, yat-vaśyaṃ hita-sāmānādhikaraṇya-nirdeśena vedānta-śabdāḥ jīva-svarūpa-parāḥ iti nirṇīyante ? tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.4.3 \|\|

*ācāra-darśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

brahma-vidaṃ prādhānyena karmasu eva ācāraḥ dṛśyate aśvapatiḥ kaikayaḥ kila ātmavicchat sat—hi jānān yoṣmān tathā ca pratyāha—“yakṣyamāṇaḥ ha vai bhagavantaḥ aham asmi” iti \| tathā jānaśrutayaḥ brahma-vidyā-śravaṇa-anantaraḥ karma-niṣṭhaḥ smṛtiṣu dṛśyante “karmaṇā eva hi saṃsiddhim āsthitāḥ janaka-ādayaḥ” “udyāj roḍ api yuvahya yathāt jñāna-kṣapā-āśrayaḥ” iti \| ataḥ brahma-vidaṃ karma-pradhānatva-darśanāt vidyāyāḥ kartuḥ svarūpa-vedana-rūpatvena karma-aṅgatvam eva iti na vidyātaḥ puruṣārthaḥ \|\| 3 \|\| liṅga-viśeṣaṃ prādhānya-vratām inoti āha—

## \|\| 3.4.4 \|\|

*tat-śruteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

śrutiḥ eva hi vidyāyāḥ karma-aṅgatvam āha—“yat eva vidyayā karoti śraddhayā upaniṣadā tat eva vīryavattaraṃ bhavati” iti \| na iyaṃ śrutiḥ prakaraṇāt udgītha-atra-viṣayā iti vyavasthāpayituṃ

śakyāḥ; yataḥ prakaraṇa-cchinnatvādīni; yad eva vidyayā karoti iti vidyā-mātra-vidhavā heyā śrutiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.5 \|\|

*sambandha-ārambhaṇāt*

**Комментарий:**

taṃ vidyā-karmaṇī samanvārabhete iti vidyā-karmaṇoḥ sāhityaṃ ca dṛśyate \| sāhityaṃ ca etena nyāyena vidyāyāḥ karmāṅgatve saty eva bhavati \|\| 5 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.6 \|\|

*tad-vato vidhānāt*

**Комментарий:**

vidyāvataḥ karma-vidhānād vidyā karmāṅgam iti avagamyate \| ācārya-kulād vedam adhītya yathā-vidhānaṃ guroḥ karma-atiśeṣeṇa abhisamāvṛtya kuṭumbe śucau deśe ityādau vedam adhītya veda-adhyayana-vataḥ karmaṇi vidadhad yathā-bodha-paryanta-adhyayanavat eva vidadhāti \| artha-avabodha-paryante hi adhyayanam iti sthāpitam \| ato brahma-vidyā api karmasu viniyuktā iti na pṛthak-phalāya avakalpate \|\| 6 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.7 \|\|

*niyamāt*

**Комментарий:**

itaś ca na vidyātaḥ puruṣārthaḥ \| kurvan eva iha karmāṇi jijīviṣet śataṃ samāḥ iti ātmavidaḥ puruṣa-āyuṣasya sarvasya karmasu niyamena viniyogāt karmaṇa eva phalitvam avagamyate \| vidyā tu karmāṅgam iti \|\| 7 \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—

## \|\| 3.4.8 \|\|

*adhika-upadeśāt tu bādarāyaṇasya evaṃ tad-darśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

tu-śabdāt pakṣo vyāvṛttaḥ \| vidyāta eva puruṣārthaḥ \| kutaḥ ? adhika-upadeśāt—karmabhyaḥ kartuṃ jīvādid prapañcanokān avadhika-atiśaya-asaṅkhyeya-kalyāṇa-guṇa-ākaratvena adhika-rūpam iti smṛteḥ parasya brahmaṇo vedānta-upadeśāt, bhagavato bādarāyaṇasya vidyātaḥ phalam iti evam eva matam \| liṅgāni viśrutuḥ; vedānta-upadeśās tu jagat-kartṛ-pratyag-ātmanaḥ adhika-rūpam eva \| katham ? tad-darśanāt—pratyag-ātmasya śuddhe śuddhe svayaṃ-bhāva-niyamana-guṇa-ākarasya vaidyasya nirasta-nikhila-heyānādhayasya sva-saṅkalpa-kṛta-jagad-udaya-vibhava-laya-līlasya sarva-jñasya sarva-śakter vedaiḥ manasā aparicchedya-ānandasya jīvādhīśasya kṛtsna-praśāsituḥ parasya brahmaṇo vedānta-upadeśa-avaseṣu darśanāt \| apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ avijighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpaḥ tad eṣa etad bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tat tejaḥ asṛjata yaḥ sarva-jñaḥ sarvavit parā asya śaktir vividhā eva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca sa ekaḥ brahmaṇa ānandaḥ yato vāco nivartante, aprāpya manasā saha, ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān, na bibheti kutaś cana iti eṣa sarveśvara eva bhūtādhipatiḥ eṣa bhūta-pāla

evaṃ setu-vidharaṇaḥ sa kāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipādhipo na ca asya kaścit janitā na ca adhipaḥ etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi sūrya-candramasau vidhṛtau tiṣṭhataḥ (etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi dyāvā-pṛthivyau vidhṛte tiṣṭhataḥ \|) bhīṣā asmāt vātaḥ pavate, bhīṣā udeti sūryaḥ, bhīṣā asmāt agniḥ ca indraḥ ca, mṛtyuḥ dhāvati pañcamaḥ iti-ādiṣu \| tasmāt deha-indriya-upadeśa-bhedeṣu kṛtuḥ pratyag-ātma-sambandhākāra-upādhi-vidhi-dyu-sambandhayoḥ api asya gandhaḥ api na asti iti parama-puruṣa-viṣayatayā vidyāyāḥ tattvāmṛta-rūpa-mūlatvaṃ tatra-tatra śruti-smṛti-phalam iti vidyātaḥ puruṣārtha iti susūktaṃ \|\| 5 \|\|

liṅgāt ca api nirasyante—

## \|\| 3.4.9 \|\|

*tulyaṃ tu darśanam*

**Комментарий:**

muktaṃ brahma-vidaṃ karma-anuṣṭhāna-darśanāt vidyā karma-aṅgīm iti; tatra, vidyāyāḥ aṅgatvena api tulyaṃ darśanam—brahma-vidaṃ karma-anuṣṭhāna-darśanena kātinakṛttyaṃ; ananuṣṭhānasya api darśanāt \| dṛśyate hi brahma-vidaṃ karma-tyāgaḥ ṛṣayaḥ kāvaṣeyāḥ kim-arthaṃ vayam adhyeṣyāmahe kim-arthaṃ vayaṃ yakṣyāmahe iti-ādau \| ataḥ brahma-vidaṃ karma-tyāga-darśanāt vidyā karma-aṅgam \| katham atra upapadyate brahma-vidaṃ karma-anuṣṭhāna-samanuṣṭhānam ? phalābhisandhi-rahitasya gṛha-ādi-karmaṇo brahma-vidyā-aṅgatvāt tathā-vidhasya karmaṇaḥ anuṣṭhāna-darśanam upapadyate \| vakṣyati ca sarvāpekṣā ca yajña-ādi-śruteḥ aśvavat iti \| phalārthasya tat eva gṛhādaiḥ karmaṇi mokṣaika-phala-brahma-vidyā-virodhitvāt tasya ananuṣṭhāna-darśanam upapannataram \| vidyāyāḥ karma-aṅgatve karma-tyāgaḥ katham api na upapadyate \|\| 9 \|\|

yad uktaṃ śrutatvaṃ vidyāyāḥ karma-aṅgatvāvagata iti; tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.4.10 \|\|

*asārvatrikī*

**Комментарий:**

na sarva-vidyā-viṣaye iyaṃ śrutiḥ; api tu udgītha-vidyā-viṣaye yad eva vidyayā karoti iti yacchabda-sthānirdhārita-viṣayasya udgītham upāsīta iti prastuta-udgītha-viśeṣa-niṣṭhatvāt \| na hi yat karoti, tat vidyā iti sambadhyate; yad eva vidyayā karoti, tad eva vīryavattaram iti vidyayā kriyamāṇaṃ yacchabdena nirdiśya tasya hi vīryavattvam ucyate \|\| 10 \|\|

yat ca etad uktaṃ taṃ vidyā-karmaṇī samanvārabhete iti vidyā-karmaṇoḥ sāhitya-darśanāt vidyā karma-aṅgam iti; tatra āha—

## \|\| 3.4.11 \|\|

*vibhāgaḥ śatavat*

**Комментарий:**

taṃ vidyā-karmaṇī samanvārabhete iti atra uktena nyāyena vidyā-karmaṇoḥ phalavatā avidyā

svarūpe phalāya samanvāyate, karma ca svarūpe phalāya iti vibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ \| śratavat—yathā kṣetra-ratna-vikrayiṇaṃ kratu-kramaneta ity ukte kṣetrārthaṃ śatam, ratnārthaṃ śatam iti vibhāgaḥ pratīyeta; tathā iha api \|\| 11 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.12 \|\|

*adhyayana-mātrataḥ*

**Комментарий:**

yad uktaṃ vidyāvataḥ karma-vidhānād vidyā karmāṅgam iti; tat na etat, vedaṃ adhītya iti adhyayana-mātravatī vidhānāt \| na ca adhyayana-vidhiḥ eva artha-bodhe pravartayati, ādhānadv adhyayanasya akṣara-rāśi-grahaṇa-mātre paryavasānāt \| gṛhītasya ca svādhyāyasya phalavat-karma-avabodhikatva-darśanāt siddhyaṃ-phale tad-artha-vicāre puruṣa svayam eva pravartate; tataḥ karmārthī karma-ajñāne pravartate, mokṣārthī ca brahma-jñāna iti na vidyā karmāṅgam \| yad adhyayana-vidhiḥ eva artha-vicāre api pravartayati; tathā api na vidyā karmāṅgam, artha-jñānāc arthāntaratvād dhyāyāḥ \| yathā jyotiṣṭoma-ādi-karma-svarūpa-vijñānāt phala-sādhana-bhūtaṃ tat-karma-anuṣṭhānam arthāntaram; tathā artha-jñānād api brahma-svarūpa-vijñānād arthāntaram eva dhyāna-upāsana-ādi-śabda-vācyā puruṣārtha-sādhana-bhūtā vidyā iti na tasyāḥ karma-saṃbandha-gandho vidyate \|\| 12 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.13 \|\|

*na aviśeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

yat ca uktaṃ kurvan eva iha karmāṇi iti ādi-vidhaṃ jñānāṅgatva-arthaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ karma-anuguṇate niyamayatīti; tatra api ucyate; aviśeṣāt—na etad api niyamaḥ phala-sādhana-bhūta-svatantra-karma-viṣaya iti viśeṣa-hetu-rahita, vidyāṅga-bhūta-karma-viṣayatva-upapatteḥ \| karmaṇā eva hi saṃsiddhim āsthitā janakādayaḥ iti ca viṣṇu-stava-prabhṛtayaḥ anupāsanasya anuvartamānatvāt \|\| 13 \|\|

evam artha-svābhāvye na codyaṃ parihṛtya kurvan eva iha karmāṇi iti asya vākyasya artham āha—

## \|\| 3.4.14 \|\|

*stutaye anumatiḥ vā*

**Комментарий:**
