vā-śabdaḥ avadhāraṇārthaḥ; īśāvāsyam idaṃ sarvam iti vidyā-prakaraṇā avidyā-stutaye sarvadā karma-anuṣṭhāna-anumatiḥ iyam \| vidyā-māhātmyāt sarvadā karma kurvan api na lipyate karmabhir iti hi vidyā stutā bhavati \| vākyāśeṣaḥ ca tad eva darśayati—evaṃ tvayi na anyathetaḥ asti na karma lipyate nare iti \| ataḥ na karmāṅgaṃ vidyā \|\| 14 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.15 \|\|

*kāma-kāreṇa ca eke*

**Комментарий:**

api ca eke vaike śākhinaḥ kāma-kāreṇa brahma-vida-aniṣṭham gārhasthya-tyāgam adhīyate ki

**tṛtīya-adhyāye—caturtha-pāde—puruṣārtha-adhikaraṇam \|**

athavā dahara-vākya-gocarau nirupadhi-ātma-sākṣātkārau lokāḥ iti \| vidyayoḥ viraktasya kāma-kāreṇa gārhasthya-karma-tyāgaṃ śrutir idaṃ vacanaṃ brahma-vidyāyāḥ karma-nirapekṣatvaṃ darśayati \| prājāpatya-kāṇḍe hi vidyāyāḥ vidyā-nidhyasya kāma-kāreṇa gārhasthya-tyāgo na saṃbhavati \| ato na vidyā karma-nirapekṣā \|\| 15 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.16 \|\|

*upamardaṃ ca*

**Комментарий:**

puṇya-apuṇya-rūpasya samasta-saṃsārika-duḥkha-mūlasya karmaṇo brahma-vidyā-upamardaṃ ca prati-vedāntam adhīte śrīyate hṛdaya-granthi-cchidane sarva-saṃśayāḥ \| kṣīyante ca asya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare \|\| ity-ādikam \| tad-dhyānāḥ karma-nirbandhe na saṃghaṭṭate \|\| 16 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.17 \|\|

*ūrdhva-retassu ca śabde hi*

**Комментарий:**

ūrdhva-retassu āśrameṣu brahma-vidyā-darśanāt saṃbandhino homa-ādi-aṅga-pūrṇa-āśramika-karma-svārthasya ca na vidyā karma-nirapekṣā \| na ṛṣeḥ etam ātmānaṃ sannyasya, śmaśāna-jīva-karma-nivṛttāḥ juhoti iti ha ity-ādi-mārjini-homaṃ-pūrṇa-pātra-ādīnāṃ yāvaj-jīva-vidhi-kāra-śruteḥ; śruti-vikṣepāṃ stutīnām ca atra āgamāt \| ato āhuḥ—śabde hi iti \| vaidika eva hi śabde te dṛśyante śrayo dharma-skandhāḥ yajña-adhyayanaṃ dānam iti prathamaḥ tapa iti upāsate ete eva pradhānino lokam icchantaḥ pravrajanti ity-ādau \| yāvaj-jīva-śrutis tu avirala-viṣayā \|\| 17 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.18 \|\|

*paramarśaṃ jaiminiḥ acodanā ca apavadati hi*

**Комментарий:**

yadi evaṃ śrayo dharma-skandhāḥ ity-ādau vaidike śabde ūrdhvaretasa āśramā dṛśyante, atas te sannyestīti; na etat upapadyate; yataḥ śrayo dharma-skandhāḥ ity-ādiṣu vākyeṣu tu teṣām āśramāṇāṃ paramarśa-mātraṃ kriyate—anuvāda-smātra-nirṇayaḥ \| kutra etat ? acodanāt—avidhānād ity arthaḥ \| na hi atra vidhāyakaḥ śrūyate \| śrayo dharma-skandhā ity-ādinā hi prakṛtaṃ praṇavena brahma-upāsanaṃ stūyate, brahma-saṃsthaḥ amṛtatvam eti brahma-upahārāt \| ato adhyayana-yajña-dāna-mātrasya kriyate teṣām āśramāṇām \| śrī vaikhānasāḥ śraddhā tapa iti upāsate iti ca deva-yāna-vidhi-arthatvāt tasyā api na āśrama-antara-vidhi-saṃbhavaḥ \| api ca apavadati hi śrutiḥ āśrama-antaraṃ vīra-hā vā eṣa devānāṃ yaḥ agniṃ mudhā avasate ity-ādikā \| ata ūrdhvaretasa āśramā na santi iti jaiminiḥ ācāryo manyate \|\| 18 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.19 \|\|

*anuṣṭheyaṃ bādarāyaṇaḥ sāmya-śruteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

gṛhastha-āśrama-vat āśrama-antaram api anuṣṭheyaṃ bhagavān bādarāyaṇo manyate \| kutaḥ ? sāmya-śruteḥ—

upādeyatayā abhimata-gṛhasthāśramatāṃ hi vedānta-vyāśramāṇāṃ śrūyate \| śrūyate dharma-skandhā iti ādasya brahma-saṃstha-tṛtīyatvāt ṣaḍ iti tena gṛhasthāśramasya itareṣāṃ ca samānatvam \| atha gṛhasthāśramasya anuvādaḥ prāptaḥ satyām eva saṃbhavati iti tasya prāti-vidyam asambhava iti matam; tad-itareṣām api samānam adhyāhṛta-niveśāt \| na ca gārhasthya-dharma eva śruty-odhyayanaṃ dānaṃ tapaḥ yajñaḥ ca iti sarvaiḥ śabdaiḥ abhidhīyate, brahmacarya-tapasoḥ hi hasta-sthā eva saṃbhāvitam uktam; śrūyate dharma-skandhā iti tritvena saṃkhyā śrūyamo... dvitīyaḥ... tṛtīyaḥ iti vibhāga-vacanānupapatteḥ \| ataḥ śruty-odhyayanaṃ dānam iti gṛhasthāśrama ucyate \| adhyayana-śabdaḥ veda-abhyāsa-paraḥ \| tapaḥ-śabdena vaikhānasa-parigṛhyājyayogṛhyaṃ, tasmiḥ tapaḥ-pradhānatvāt \| tapaḥ-śabdaḥ hi kāṣṭha-vratī sthūḍaḥ; sa ca dṛṣṭy-ākṛti samānaḥ \| brahmacāri-śabdaḥ eva brahmacaryavattvena abhidhīyate \| brahma-saṃsthaḥ amṛtatvam eti iti paraṃ brahma-prāṇo brahma-saṃstha-śabdaḥ yogikaḥ sarvāśrama-sādhāraṇaḥ; sarveṣām āśramiṇāṃ brahma-saṃsthā-saṃbhavāt \| brahmaṇi saṃsthā—saṃsthitiḥ brahma-saṃsthattvam, tat ca sarveṣāṃ saṃbhavati eva \| brahma-niṣṭhā-vikalāḥ kevala-āśramiṇaḥ puṇya-loka-bhājaḥ; teṣu eva brahma-niṣṭhaḥ amṛtatva-bhāk bhavati \| tat eva vihitānuṣṭhāna-śaktau saṃpatatā parakāreṇa śrāvaṇatvasya brāhmaṇānām iti ārasya āhāryaṃ saṃnyāsināṃ smṛtīḥ ca iti antena varṇitāśramāṇāṃ ca kevalānāṃ brahma-loka-prāpty-antaṃ phalam abhidhāya śruti-kīrtita-tat-sadṛśā brahma-dhyāyinaḥ yoginaḥ hi ye \| teṣāṃ tat-paramaṃ sthānaṃ yade paśyanti sūrayaḥ \|\| iti teṣu eva brahma-niṣṭhānāṃ brahma-prāptim abhidhā \| ataḥ gṛhasthāśrama-mukhyāḥ ūrdhva-retasaḥ āśramā api hṛdyanta iti taiḍakucchyāḥ \| trayī ca itaranye śraddhā tapa iti upāsate iti ca araṇye iti tapaḥ-pradhānāśrama-prāpty-apekṣa-vādeḥ pratyāta-vidhānasya ā tatrāpi tat-sādhita-strīkaraṇīyā \|\| 19 \|\| parāmarśa-pakṣe vidhāna-pakṣe ca gṛhasthāśrama-tulyam eva amapadi-ordhvatvam iti upapādya vidheḥ na iyam āśramāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ na anuvāda iti upapādayitum āha—

## \|\| 3.4.20 \|\|

*vidhiḥ vā dhāraṇavat*

**Комментарий:**

vā-śabdaḥ avadhāraṇārthaḥ \| vidhiḥ eva ayam āśramāṇām; dhāraṇavat—yathā vidhāninaho atra adhastāt samidhaṃ dhārayan anudraved uparī hi devebhyo dhārayanti iti atra anuvāda-svarūpā api vākyasya upari dhāraṇasya āśramatvāt vidhiḥ āśrīyate \| yathoktaṃ śeṣa-lakṣaṇe vidhiḥ tu dhāraṇaḥ apūrvatvāt iti \| tathā atra api āśramatvāt vidhiḥ eva āśramaṇīyaḥ \| brahmacaryaṃ samāpya gṛhī bhavet gṛhāt dhvanī bhūtvā pravrajet yadi icchet tathā brahmacaryāt eva pravrajet gṛhā vā vanāt vā yad ahar eva virajet tad ahar eva pravrajet iti jābālānāṃ ca āśrama-vidhim anutarmin kurvatendra-parake api vākyebhyaḥ āśrama-prāptiḥ atra pramaṇīyā iti upapāditam \| evam āśramāntara-vidhānāhauśruti-viruddha-upasmṛti-vijñotra-bhūtir atyavādam utithrā-viruddha-viṣayā eva iti veditavyaḥ \| anyatra brahmavidaḥ karmaṇām āpravaṇatva-vaśya-kartavyatā-vidhāyiḥ ca; śrutayaḥ smṛtayaḥ ca

svādhyāyam adhīyīta \| ata ūrdhvaṃ retas tu ca brahma-vidyā-vidhāna-avidyātaḥ puruṣa-artha iti siddham \|\| 20 \|\| iti puruṣa-arthādhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha stuti-mātrādhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.21 \|\|

*stuti-mātram upādānāt iti cet na apūrvatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

iṣṭi-mātraṃ cintyate sa eṣa rasānāṃ rasatamaḥ parardhyaḥ aṣṭamo yat udgīthaḥ iti evaṃ-jātīyakāni vākyāni kratu-avayava-bhūta-udgītha-ādi-stuti-mātra-parāṇi, āhosvit udgītha-ādiṣu rasatva-ādi-viśiṣṭa-vidhāna-parāṇi iti \| atra pratipādita-puruṣārtha-paratva-buddhy-upāsanasya puruṣa-arthatvena kratu-anuṣṭhāna-aniyama uktaḥ \| kiṃ yuktam? stuti-mātra-parāṇi iti \| kutaḥ? udgītha-ādi-upādānāt \| kratu-aṅga-bhūtāni ca udgītha-ādi-upādāya teṣāṃ rasatamādi-tvaṃ pratipāditam; yathā juhū-ādīnāṃ pṛthivī-āditvaṃ pratipādayato vacanasya iyaṃ eva juhūḥ svargaḥ lokaḥ āhavanīyaḥ iti-ādikasya tat-tat-upāsana-paratve, teṣām api \| tadvat-upāsana-paratve api \| tasmāt udgīthe—stuti-mātram upādānāt iti cet iti \| udgītha-ādi-upādānāt stuti-mātram eva eṣāṃ vākyānāṃ viśiṣṭam iti cet \| atra uttaram—na apūrvatvāt iti \| na stuti-mātreṣu upakṣete; kutaḥ? apūrvatvāt—aprātatvāt \| na hi udgītha-ādyoḥ rasatamādi-tayā pramāṇāntareṇa pratipattāḥ; yena tat-praśaṃsā-buddhyā tat-arthaṃ rasatamādi-tvena upādeyatvaṃ \| na ca udgītha-ādi-viśiṣṭasya sahiṣṇutaḥ; yena iyaṃ eva juhūḥ svargaḥ lokaḥ āhavanīyaḥ iti-ādi-vat vaktavyatayā yathā-kathañcana viṣayā tat-vṛtti-paratvam āśrīyeta \| ataḥ kratvartha-vastaravāhikatva-siddhy-arthaṃ udgītha-ādiṣu rasatamādi-viśiṣṭa-vidhānam eva nyāyyam \|\| 21 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.22 \|\|

*bhāva-śabdāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

upāsīta iti-ādi-śravaṇa-śabdāt ca vidhi-paratvam eva nyāyyam \| vidhi-pratyaya-yukto hi kriyā-śabdo vidheyam eva sva-artham avagamayati \| tasmāt upāsana-vidhāna-arthā etāḥ śrutayaḥ \|\| 22 \|\| iti stuti-mātrādhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha pāriṣlava-arthādhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.23 \|\|

*pāriṣlava-arthā iti cet na viśeṣitatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

sad eva ha va idam agre āsīt ity asya priyaṃ dhāma-upajanam ha vai ketum uddālako āruṇeya āsa iti evam-ādīni vedānteṣu ākhyātāni kiṃ pāriṣlava-prayoga-arthāni, uta vidyā-viśeṣa-pratipādana-arthā-

na iti cintyām ākhyānāni śaṃsanti iti ākhyānānāṃ pāritplave viniyogasya vidhāna-pradhānatvaṃ hāsyasmi iti cet; na sarveṣām ākhyānāni pāritplava-prayoge viniyogam arhanti; kutaḥ? viśeṣita-vidhi-niyogāt \| ākhyānāni śaṃsanti iti uktvā tatra eva manuḥ vaivasvataḥ rājā iti-ādīnām manu-ādīnām ākhyānāni viśeṣayet; ataḥ ca eva tatra viniyogaḥ iti gamyate \| tasmāt sarvā vedānteṣu ākhyāna-śrutayaḥ pāritplava-prayoga-arthāḥ; api tu vidyā-vidheya-arthāḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.24 \|\|

*tathā ca eka-vākya-upabandhāt*

**Комментарий:**

ātmā vai are draṣṭavyaḥ iti-ādi-vidhīnām avayavatayā upabaddhānāṃ vidyā-vidheya-arthya eva tātparyam iti gamyate; yathā saḥ arodīt iti evam-ādeḥ karma-vidheyatvam; na pāritplava-arthatvam \|\| 24 \|\| iti pāritplava-arthādhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha agnīndhana-ādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.25 \|\|

*ata eva ca agnīndhana-ādi-anapekṣā*

**Комментарий:**

stuti-viśraṅkhala-dhyāna-aras-tv-aṅgati-virodhana-arthīyaṃ cintitam \| vidyā-antaḥ udgītetasya āśramiṇaḥ satyopayuktam uddharet ātmanā ātmānaṃ ca śabde hoti-ādi-vihita-sūtram \| idānīṃ pūrvaitasyo yathā-yatha-bhāvaḥ tad dhiṅkā vidyā na sambhavati iti āśaṅkya āha—ata eva ca agnīndhana-ādi-apekṣā iti \| yataḥ uddharetasya āśramiṇo vidyā-sambandhena śrutyā parigṛhyante ṛtam satyaṃ dhṛta-tapam iti ye ca ime araṇye śraddhā tapa iti upāsate etam eva pravājinaḥ lokam icchantaḥ pravrajanti yat icchantaḥ brahmacaryaṃ caranti iti-ādikayā; ata eva uddharetasya vidyā agnīndhana-ādi-apekṣā—agnīndhanam—agraya-ādānam; ādhāna-pūrvaka-agnīhotra-brahma-pūrṇamāsa-ādi-karma-nirapekṣā teṣu vidyā \| kevalatvāśrama-vihita-karma-apekṣā iti arthaḥ \|\| 25 \|\| iti agnīndhana-ādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha sarvāpekṣā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.26 \|\|

*sarvāpekṣā ca yajña-ādi-śruteḥ aśvavat*

**Комментарий:**

yadi vidyā yajña-ādi-apekṣām uktatvaṃ sādhyati; tarhi gṛhasthasya api tat-anapekṣatvaṃ sādhyam; yajña-ādi-śruteḥ api vividiṣanti iti śabdāt karmāṇi vedanāṅgatāṃ na pratipādayati iti \| ata āha—sarvāpekṣā \| agnihotra-ādi-sarva-karma-apekṣate vidyā karma-vatsu gṛhasthasya; kutaḥ? yajña-ādi-śruteḥ \| tam etaṃ vedānuvacanena brāhmaṇā vividiṣanti yajñena dānena tapasā anāśakena

iti ādinā yajñādayaḥ hi vidyā-aṅgatvena śrūyante \| yajñādinā vividiviṣanti—veditum icchanti, yajñādibhiḥ vedanaṃ prāptum icchanti iti arthaḥ \| yajñādīnāṃ jñāna-sādhanatve sati eva yajñādibhiḥ jñānaṃ prāptum icchanti iti vyapadeśaḥ upapadyate; yathā aśvehānna-sādhanatve sati aśvinā jigāṃsati iti vyapadeśaḥ \| ataḥ yajñādīnāṃ jñāna-sādhanatvam avagamyate \| jñānaṃ ca vākya-artha-jñātatva-arthi-tattva-bhūtaṃ dhyāna-upāsanādi-śabda-vācyaṃ kiṃcid-atama-pratyakṣatā-āpanna-smṛti-rūpaṃ niraticaya-priyam ahar-ahar upādhīyamānatvāt pramāṇa-

tat-anugṛhīta-mānaṃ mokṣa-sādhanam iti uktam asmābhiḥ pūrvam eva; vakṣyati ca “āvṛttiḥ asakṛt-upadeśāt” iti ādinā \| evaṃ ca dhyānam ahar-ahar anuṣṭhīyamāna-nitya-naimittika-karmabhiḥ parama-puruṣa-ārādhana-rūpaiḥ parama-puruṣa-prasāda-dvāreṇa jāyate iti yajñādinā vividiviṣanti iti śāstreṇa pratipādyate \| ataḥ karma-vastu gṛhastha eva yajñādi-nitya-naimittika-pratyavekṣā vidyā \| athavā—yathā gamana-sādhana-bhūtaḥ aśvaḥ sva-parikara-karma-apekṣam api apekṣaḥ; evaṃ mokṣa-sādhanabhūtā api vidyā nitya-naimittika-karma-parikara-apekṣā \| tat idam āha svapneva bhagavān “yajña-dāna-tapaḥ karma na tyājyaṃ kāryam eva tat \| yajñaḥ dānaṃ tapaḥ ca eva pāvanāni manīṣiṇām \|\|” “yataḥ pravṛttiḥ bhūtānāṃ yena sarvam idaṃ tataṃ \| sva-karmaṇā tam abhyarcya siddhiṃ vindati mānavaḥ \|\|” iti \|\| 26 \|\| iti sarva-apekṣā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

**atha śabdādi-sādhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.27 \|\|

*śabdādibhyaḥ antar pratiṣṭhānāt ca na tat-īkṣā-itara-dvīryaṃ teṣām asaṃya-vacanāt śucchrayatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

gṛhasthasya homanadīniyacchrayasyāpi, uta na iti cintāyām, anantara-adhikaraṇa-vyāpāra-rūpatvāt-karma-anuguṇasya, śama-samādīnāṃ tad-viruddheḥ ahitatvāt ca anucchrayāṇi \|\|

iti prāpte ucyate—yadyapi gṛhasthaḥ karaṇa-vyāpāra-rūpa-karmaṣu pravṛttaḥ; tathāpi sa vidyā-ārambha-samaye tebhyaḥ aśravitaḥ; kutaḥ? tadvaṅgatayā tad-viruddhaiḥ—vidyā-aṅgatayā teṣāṃ vidhaiḥ “tasmāt evaṃ vicchānto dānta uparatas titikṣuḥ samāhito bhūtvā ātmany eva ātmānaṃ paśyet” iti \| vidyopasthiti-niścita-mano-dhānāśakravena dṛḍhatpaviraktayā ātmavalokṣaṇāyāṃ, vidyā-aṅgini tāpe teṣāṃ—śamādīnām adhyavakṣyā-anucchrayatvāt ca tāni asya anucchrayāṇi \| na ca karaṇa-vyāpāra-rahita-puruṣeṇa karmaṇāṃ śama-samādīnāṃ ca paraspara-virodhaḥ; bhinna-viṣayatvāt—vihiteṣu karaṇa-vyāpāraḥ, avihiteṣu prayojana-bhūteṣu ca tat-uparama iti \| na ca karaṇa-vyāpāra-rūpa-karmaṣu vartamānasya vāsanā-vaśyāt śamādīnām anupapadyamānatva-saṃbhavaḥ; vihitānāṃ karmaṇāṃ parama-puruṣa-ārādhanatayā tat-prasāda-dvāreṇa nikhila-viparīta-vāsanā-ucchedakatvāt \| ataḥ gṛhasthasya śama-damādayaḥ anucchrayāḥ \|\| 27 \|\| iti śama-samādhi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha sarva-abhigamana-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.28 \|\|

*sarva-abhinumatiḥ ca prāṇa-atyaye tat-darśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

vājinaḥ chandogāḥ ca prāṇa-vidyāyāṃ na ha vā asya anannaṃ jagdhaṃ bhavati na annaṃ parigṛhītaṃ bhavati na ha vā evaṃ-vidi kiñcana anannaṃ bhavati iti śrāṇitaḥ sarva-anna-anumatiḥ saṃśrūyate \| kim iyaṃ prāṇa-vidyā-niṣṭhasya sarva-abhigama-anumatiḥ sarvadā, uta prāṇa-atyaya-āpanna-sthiti-viṣaye, viśeṣa-anupādānāt sarvadā \| iti prāpte ucyate—prāṇa-atyaye iti \| candrodaya-dvaraṇe prāṇa-atyaya-āpanna-āveśasya \| kutaḥ ? tat-darśanāt—dṛśyate hi anyatra brahma-vidām api prāṇa-atyaya-āpannasya eva sarvānābhyanujñā; kiṃ punaḥ prāṇa-vidaḥ \| upoṣitaḥ kila cākrāyaṇo brahma-vidyasya bhakta-ucchiṣṭeṣu kuluṣe dṛṣṭi-bhakṣyeti viṣaya-grāme vasamānena prāṇa-saṃśaya-āpannaḥ brahma-vidyā-niṣṭhasya eva prāṇānām anavasādam akārṣam aham iti ukta-bhaktān khādantaṃ bhinnāmṛtas tena ca ucchiṣṭa-udakāyane na vicchann iti pratyuktaḥ punar api etāṃ me dehi iti uktvā tena ca evaṃ eva ucchiṣṭeya āhṛtya datto kulmāṣān pratigṛhya anupānam alipsam abhyanoṣitaḥ ucchiṣṭaṃ vai me pītaṃ syāt iti tat-aṅgākaraṇāt, kiṃ no kulmāṣā anucchiṣṭhā iti itthena paryanuyuktaḥ na vā ajīviṣyam imān khādan kāmo ma udapānam iti kulmāṣa-khādane svasya prāṇa-saṃśaya-āpannena avasthānātreṇa vṛtta-prāṇasya svastha-ucchiṣṭa-udaka-pāne kāma-kāritāṃ nirviśiṣṭaṃ sthiti-smṛti-uccā svasthāsitaraṃ vai jāyāṃ dadvā hayā ca rakṣitān pareṣu bhañjītenārjiṣoṣayā jigamiṣuḥ punar api prāṇa-saṃśaya-āpannaḥ tān eva ucchiṣṭhān sva-ucchiṣṭa-bhūtān paryuṣitāṃś cakhād \| ataḥ brahma-vidām api prāṇa-saṃśaye eva sarva-anna-anumati-darśanāt tat-aviśeṣeṇa kausītakim api prāṇa-vit-sarvāśitṛtvaṃ prāṇātyayāpannatā eva iti niścīyate \|\| 28 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.29 \|\|

*abādhāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

āhāra-śuddhau sattva-śuddhiḥ sattva-śuddhau dhruvā smṛtiḥ iti brahma-vidyā-upakāra-āhāra-śuddhi-vidhāna-abādhāt api brahma-vidam sarva-bhojanatvāpaddhiṣyamadamabhyam \| evaṃ karma-vidām apiśiṣṭānāṃśatotām api sarva-bhojanatvasya āpaddhiṣyatvā-śravaṇa-virodha-paraiḥ sarva-anna-anumatiḥ āpaddhiṣu eva \|\| 29 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.30 \|\|

*api smaryate*

**Комментарий:**

api ca āpaddhiṣu eva sarva-bhojanaṃ brahma-vidām ṛṣīṇāṃ ca smaryate—śvapāka-anna-saṃsparśo yoga-smarati yat tas tataḥ \| lipyate na sa pāpena padma-patram iva ambhasā \|\| iti \|\| 30 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.31 \|\|

*śabdaḥ ca atokāmakāre*

**Комментарий:**

yato brahma-vidām ṛṣīṇāṃ ca sarva-bhojanatvam āpaddhiṣu eva; ata eva sarveṣām akāmākāre

śabdaḥ—kāma-kārasya pratibedhakaḥ śabdaḥ dante \| asti hi kaṭhānāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ kāma-kārasya pratibedhakaḥ śabdaḥ tasmāt brāhmaṇa-suraṃ na pibati pāpmanā na utsṛjā iti iti \| pāpmanā saṃsṛṣṭaḥ na bhavāmi iti matvā brāhmaṇaḥ surāṃ na pibed iti arthaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

iti sarva-annumati-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|

**atha vihitatva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.32 \|\|

*vihitatvāt ca āśrama-karma api*

**Комментарий:**

yajñādi-karmāṅgikā brahma-vidy-uktatvaḥ; tāni ca yajñādīni karmāṇi amumukṣuṇā vedanāśramiṇā api anuṣṭheyāni, uta na iti cintāyāṃ, vidyāṅgānāṃ satāṃ kevala-āśrama-dharme nityatva-anitya-saṃyoga-virodhaḥ prasajyata iti yajñādīnāṃ kevala-āśrama-dharmatvaṃ na saṃbhavati \|\|

iti prāpte ucyate—āśrama-karma api iti \| āśramasya karma api bhavati \| kevala-āśramiṇā api anuṣṭheyāni iti arthaḥ \| kutaḥ ? yāvat-jīvam agnihotraṃ juhoti ity-ādinā vihitatvāt—jīvana-nimittatayā nityatva-vihitatvāt iti arthaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

tathā vidyāṅgatayā ca tam etaṃ veda-anuvacanena ity-ādinā vihitatvāt vidyā-śeṣatayā api anuṣṭheyāni iti āha—

## \|\| 3.4.33 \|\|

*sahakāritvena ca*

**Комментарий:**

vidyotpatti-sthaireṇa vidyā-sahakāritayā api anuṣṭheyāni \| agnihotrādīnām eva jīvana-adhikāra-svarga-adhikāratvāt viniyoga-pṛthaktvena ubhaya-arthatvaṃ na virudhyata iti arthaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

tad eva karmi-niratatvam api na asti iti āha—

## \|\| 3.4.34 \|\|

*sarvathā api te eva ubhaya-liṅgāt*

**Комментарий:**

sarvathā—vidyārthatve āśrama-arthatve api, te eva yajñādayaḥ iti pratipattavyaḥ; na karma-svarūpa-bhedaḥ iti arthaḥ \| kutaḥ ? ubhaya-liṅgāt—ubhayatra śrutau yajñādi-śabdaiḥ pratyabhijñāya viniyogāt, karmaṇi aikya-bhede pramāṇa-abhāvāt ca \|\| 34 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.35 \|\|

*anabhibhavaṃ ca darśayati*

**Комментарий:**

dharmeṇa pāpam apnudati ity-ādinā api ca tān eva yajñādi-varmāṅgitvena nairvighnyāya anabhi-

bhavaṃ—pāpa-karmabhiḥ saha pitravidyābhāvaṃ darśayati \| aharahaḥ śuddhyamanasiṃha yajñādibhiḥ viśuddhāntaḥ-karaṇe prati-ahaṃ prati-upāsanā vicāryate \| ataḥ hi eṣa yasyā ādayaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

iti vihita-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|

**atha vidura-adhikaraṇam \|\| 9 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.36 \|\|

*antarā ca api tu tat-dṛṣṭeḥ*

**Комментарий:**

caturṇām āśramiṇāṃ brahma-vidyāyām adhikāraḥ asti; vidyā-sahakāriṇaḥ āśrama-dharmāḥ iti ca uktam \| ye punaḥ āśrama-antarā vartante vidura-ādayaḥ; teṣāṃ brahma-vidyāyām adhikāraḥ asti, na vā iti vicāre; āśrama-dharmita-kartavyatā-kara-vidyāyāḥ, anāśramiṇāṃ ca āśrama-dharma-abhāvāt anadhikāraḥ \| iti prāpte ucyate—antarā ca api tiṣṭhati \| tu-śabdaḥ pakṣa-vyāvṛtti-arthaḥ \| ca-śabdaḥ avadhāraṇe \| antarā vartamānānām—anāśramiṇām api vidyāyām adhikāraḥ asti eva \| kutaḥ? tat-dṛṣṭeḥ—dṛśyate hi raikva-bhīṣma-saṃvarta-vidura-matāśramiṇām api brahma-vidyā-niṣṭhatvam \| tadāśrama-dharmāḥ eva vidyā-anugrahaḥ iti śakyaṃ vaktum, yajñena dānena tapasā anāśakena iti dāna-ādīnām āśrameṣu anekāntikānām adhyupāyakatva-darśanāt \| yathoddeśataḥ tu vidyāṅga-bhūta-darśanādi-nihotrādi-cchativat tatsveva vidyāṅgatvaḥ kriyate; tathā anāśramiṇām api vidyā-darsanāt āśrama-aniyatā-rūpeṇa upāsana-devatā-rādhana-vidhi-vidyā-anugrahaḥ upapadyate kartum \|\| 36 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.37 \|\|

*api smaryate*

**Комментарий:**

api ca anāśramiṇām api japa-ādi-viśeṣeṇa vidyā-anugrahaḥ smaryate japyena api ca saṃsidhyet brāhmaṇo nātra saṃśayaḥ \| kuryāt anyan na vā kuryāt maitro brāhmaṇa ucyate \|\| iti \| saṃsidhyet—nitya-anugṛhītayā vidyayā siddho bhavatīty arthaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.38 \|\|

*viśeṣa-anugrahaḥ ca*

**Комментарий:**

na kevalaṃ nyāya-sūtritayām artha-sthāpanīyaḥ; śrūyate ca anāśramiṇo api evaṃ-viśeṣa-anugrahaḥ: tapasā brahmacaryeṇa śraddhayā vidyayā ātmānam anvicchet iti \|\| 38 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.39 \|\|

*ataḥ tu itara-jyāyo liṅgāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

tulya-uddeśa-darśanāt; ataḥ—anāśraminitvāt itarat—āśramitvam eva jyāyaḥ; anāśramatvam api idam \| śaktasya sva-āśramitvam eva upādeyam ity arthaḥ; bhūyaḥ adhikatara-dharma-karyatvāt \| liṅgāt ca—smṛteḥ ity arthaḥ \| smaryate ca śaktaṃ praty āśrama-upadeśavattvam anāśramī na

liṅge tu hi meṣām api dvijaḥ iti-ādikā \| nivṛtta-brahmacaryasya mūta-sparśasya ca aśaucāc ca sati dārārāmā āpadyata \|\| 39 \|\| iti vidhurādhikaraṇam \|\| 9 \|\|

**atha tad-bhūtādhikaraṇam \|\| 10 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.40 \|\|

*tad-bhūtasya tu na atad-bhāvaḥ jaiminer api niyamāt tad-rūpābhāvebhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

naiṣṭhika-brahmacarya-āśramā-janma-prameyaḥ pracyutānām api brahma-vidyām adhikāraḥ asti iti, na iti cintāyāṃ, vidhurādivat āśrama-kāntar-vṛtti-vidhi-pravidhāna-gṛha-saṃvāda-sthādhikāraḥ \|\| iti prāpta ucyate—tad-bhūtasya tu na atad-bhāvaḥ iti \| tu-śabdaḥ pakṣa-vyāvṛtti-arthaḥ \| tad-bhūtasya—naiṣṭhika-ādi-āśrama-niṣṭhasya, na atad-bhāvaḥ—atyathā-bhāvaḥ; anāśramitvena avasthānaṃ na saṃbhavati; kutaḥ? tad-rūpābhāvibhyo niyamāt—tad-rūpāṅga-śeṣaṃ naiṣṭhika-ādīnāṃ rūpāṇi—devāḥ, dharmā hṛdayaṃ; teṣām abhāvaḥ—tad-rūpābhāvaḥ; teṣāṃ śāstrair niyamāt \| naiṣṭhika-ādi-āśrama-vidhyā svāśrama-dharmānuvṛttibhyo niyacchanti hi śāstrāṇi brahmacārī vācamākuñcalavāsī vṛttīyodhyayanamātrān nagāyunikaleśamātyak iti, araṇyam iyāt tato na punar eyāt iti, saṃnyasya agniṃ na punar avarohet iti ca \| ataḥ vidhurādivat naiṣṭhika-ādīnām anāśramitve na avasthāna-saṃbhavāt tān adhikṛtya iti brahma-vidyā; jaimineḥ ācāryāṇāṃ darśanāt muktaṃ svāminitaṃ draḍhayati \|\| 40 \|\|

atha syāt—naiṣṭhika-ādīnāṃ brahmacaryasā cyutānāṃ prāyaścittādhikārasya saṃbhavati \| asti ca prāyaścittādhikāra-lakṣaṇe nirūhitam avakīrṇi-paśu-śrava tadvat iti \| ataḥ pracyuta-brahmacaryasya prāyaścitta-saṃbhavād gṛhasthāśramitī brahma-vidyām adhikariṣyati iti; tatrāha—

## \|\| 3.4.41 \|\|

*na ca adhikārikam api patana-anumānāt tad-ayogāt*

**Комментарий:**

adhikāra-lakṣaṇoktam api prāyaścittaṃ naiṣṭhika-ādīnāṃ tad-vṛddhānāṃ na saṃbhavati; kutaḥ? patana-anumānāt tad-ayogāt—naiṣṭhika-ādīnāṃ pracyutānāṃ patana-smṛteḥ tasya prāyaścittasya asaṃbhavāt— ārūḍho naiṣṭhikaṃ dharmaṃ yaḥ tu pracyavate dvijaḥ \| prāyaścittaṃ na paśyāmi yena śudhyeta ātmāha \|\| iti \| ataḥ adhikāra-lakṣaṇoktaṃ prāyaścittam itara-brahmacāri-viṣayam \|\| 41 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.42 \|\|

*upapūrvam api iti eke bhāvam aśanavat uktam*

**Комментарий:**

naiṣṭhika-ādīnāṃ brahmacarya-ādi-āśrama-cyutam apūrvaṃs—upapātakam, mahā-pātaka-vat aparigaṇitatvāt iti tatra prāyaścittasya bhāvaṃ—vidyamānatām iti eke ācāryāḥ manyante \| aśanatvat—yathā madhvaśanādi niṣedhatas prāyaścittaṃ cāndrāyaṇasya naiṣṭhika-ādīnāṃ ca samānam; tad uktaṃ smṛtikāraiḥ: uttareṣu

na eva avirodhiḥ iti \| gurukula-vāsino yat uktaṃ, tat svāśrama-virodhena rekhā-madhyāśramiṇāṃ sarvatra eva āryaḥ \| tad-viduḥ api brahmacaryaṃ vacane prāyaścitta-saṃbhavād brahma-vidyā-yojitā api asti \|\| 42 \|\|

## \|\| 3.4.43 \|\|

*bahiḥ tu ubhayathā api smṛteḥ ācārāc ca*

**Комментарий:**

tu-śabdaḥ matāntara-vyāvṛtti-arthaḥ \| upapātakatve mahā-pātakatve api ete bahiṣkṛtā eva brahma-vidyā-adhikāribhyaḥ; brahma-vidyāyām anadhikṛtā iti arthaḥ \| kutaḥ? smṛteḥ—pūrvoktāt patana-smaraṇāt \| yady api kalmṣa-nirharaṇāya kaiścid vacanaiḥ prāyaścitta-adhikāro vidyate, tathā api karma-adhikāra-anuguṇa-śuddhi-hetu-prāyaścittaṃ na saṃbhavati, prāyaścittaṃ na paśyāmi yena śudhyeta ātmāhā iti smṛteḥ iti arthaḥ \| ācārāc ca—śiṣṭā hi naiṣṭhikam enaṃ śaśvat kṛta-prāyaścittam api varjayanti, teṣāṃ teṣāṃ brahma-vidyā-adhikaṃ na upadiśanti; atas teṣāṃ na asti brahma-vidyāyām adhikāraḥ \|\| 43 \|\| iti tad-bhūta-adhikaraṇam \|\| 10 \|\|

**atha svāmi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 11 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.44 \|\|

*svāminaḥ phala-śruteḥ iti ātreyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

karma-aṅga-āśrayā madhu-goha-adhyupāsanāni kiṃ yajamāna-kartṛkāṇi, uta ṛtvik-kartṛkāṇi iti cintāyāṃ yajamāna-kartṛkāṇi ātreyo manyate \| kutaḥ? phala-śruteḥ—vedana-vidhiḥ hetuḥ dahara-śrupuāsanena phala-upāsanatyoḥ eka-śabda-vasaṃnidhau ca kratu-phala-prāpti-sambandha-prayojya-upāsana-phalasya yajamāna-āśrayatva-vyapagaṇi iti arthaḥ \| na ca godohanādivad iṅga-āśrayeṇa yajamāna-kartṛkatva-asambhavaḥ; godohanādipu dṛṣṭārtha-kartṛka-kraṇa-aṅga-āśraya-godohana-upāsana-madhye nāsākṣamam; idaṃ tu udgātṛ-kartṛke api udgīthe tat-saṃvedyāyādeḥ rasanatamavādusandhānāṃ yajamānena eva kartuṃ śakyate \|\| 44 \|\|

iti prāpte abhidhīyate—

## \|\| 3.4.45 \|\|

*ārtvijyam iti auḍulomis tasmai hi parikrīyate*

**Комментарий:**

ārtvijyam—ṛtvijaḥ karma-aṅga-āśrayā upāsanam iti auḍulomiḥ ācāryo manyate; kutaḥ? tasmai hi—prayojanāya ṛtvik parikrīyate; phala-sādhana-bhūtasya sāṅgasya kratoḥ upādānāya iti arthaḥ \| karma-vidhiṣu ṛtvijo vṛṇīte ṛtvigbhyo dakṣiṇāṃ dadāti iti ṛtvik-kartṛkaṃ kartavyatā-sūtraṇa phala-sādhana-bhūtaṃ sāṅgaṃ karma ṛtvigbhis tat-uddeśya-nirvāpaṇāya; tat-saṃgātāni kāni kāni mānasāni ca karmāṇi ṛtvik-kartṛkāṇi eva; na ca śakyas teṣu tasya niveśanam \| yathā catur-gṛhīta-ājya-upāsane puruṣārthaḥ, tathā api kratu-adhikṛta-adhikāritva-anukūlo asya sāṅgasya ṛtvik-kartṛtvād, yad eva vidyayā karoti... tad eva vīryavattaram iti ṛtvik-kartṛka-kriyā-upayogitvena vidyāyāḥ teṣu eva kartṛkaṃ kartavya-

śravaṇāt, artha-vivakṣāṃ kāṅkṣet ādi \| dahara-vikrūpa-mātreṣu artha-vivakṣāṃ kalpaya aśravaṇāt, śāstra-phalaṃ prayoktṛ iti nyāyāt phalavat-tva-kalpana eva \|\| 45 \|\| iti svāmi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 12 \|\|

**atha sahakāry-antara-vidyā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 13 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.46 \|\|

*sahakāry-antara-vidhiḥ pakṣeṇa tṛtīyaṃ tad-vato vidhy-ādi-vat*

**Комментарий:**

tam āṭṭrāhmaṇaḥ paṇḍityaṃ nirvidya bālyena tiṣṭhāset bālyaṃ ca paṇḍityaṃ ca nirvidya atha muniḥ iti etad vākyam pāṇḍitya-vat na enam api vidhīyate, uta anuṣṭhata iti viśaye; mauna-paṇḍitya-śabdayoḥ jñānārthatvāt, paṇḍityaṃ nirvidya iti vihitam eva jñānam u atha muniḥ iti ucyate; vidhi-śabdo na hi atra śrūyate iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte brūmaḥ—sahakāry-antara-vidhiḥ iti labdhataḥ vidyāvataḥ; ‘vidyādivad-vidhīyate’ iti yatra adṛṣṭa-sarvāśrama-dharmaḥ śama-dam-ādiś ca vidhi-śabdena ucyate; ādi-śabdena śravaṇa-manane gṛhyete; sahakāry-antara-vidhiḥ iti atra api vidhīyata iti vidhiḥ; sahakāry-antaraṃ vidhīyate iti sahakāry-antara-vidhiḥ \| etad uktaṃ bhavati—yathā tam etaṃ veda-anuvacanena brāhmaṇā vividiṣanti yajñena dānena iti ādinā śānto dānta iti ādinā ca sahakārī yajñādi-śama-dam-ādiś ca vidhīyate; yathā ca śrotavyo mantavyaḥ iti śravaṇa-manane vākyāntare vidyā-sahakāritvena gṛhyete; tathā tam āṭṭrāhmaṇaḥ pāṇḍityaṃ nirvidya iti ādinā pāṇḍityaṃ, bālyaṃ, maunam iti tritayaṃ vidyāyas sahakāry-antaraṃ vidhīyata iti \| maune ca pāṇḍityād artha-antarasya āha—pakṣeṇa iti \| muni-śabdasya pakṣeṇa prakṛta-manana-śīle vyutpattau prayoga-darśanāt maunaṃ pāṇḍitya-bālya-yogyehyaḥ tṛtīyas \| ‘yady api atha muniḥ iti asya vidhi-pratyayo na śrūyate; tathā api maunasya aśāstratvāt vidhyeyatvam aṅgīkriya-oghyam—atha muniḥ iti ādikaṃ \| idaṃ ca maunaṃ śravaṇa-pratighāta-artha-manatā-darya-antara-bhūta-bhūta-pratāna-ālambanasya punaḥ-punaḥ-śīlanaṃ tad-vṛtti-vāsanārtham \| tad evaṃ vākyārthaḥ—brāhmaṇaḥ vidyāvās pāṇḍityaṃ nirvidya-upāsyaṃ brahmatvaṃ pariśuddhaṃ paripūrṇaṃ ca vividitvā, śravaṇa-mananābhyāsābhyāṃ vedaiḥ pratipadyeta iti arthaḥ \| tac ca bhagavad-dṛṣṭi-sahakṛta-sarva-vividṛṣṭavaḥ; yathoktaṃ na ahaṃ vedaiḥ iti ārasya bhaktyā tu ananyayā śakyaḥ...jñātuṃ iti \| śrutiś ca yasya deve parā bhaktiḥ tasya eṣa ātmā pravṛṇute iti ādikā \| bālyena tiṣṭhāset; bālya-svarūpaṃ ca anantaraṃ vakṣyate \| bālyaṃ ca pāṇḍityaṃ ca nirvidya atha muniḥ tiṣṭhāt—bālya-pāṇḍitye yathāvat upāsya pariśuddhe paripūrṇe brahmaṇi manana-śīlo bhavet, nirdiṣṭh-āsanāna-rūpa-vidyāvāste \| evam eva tritaya-upādānena labdha-vidyo bhavati iti āha amānoṃ ca maunaṃ ca nirvidya atha brāhmaṇaḥ iti \| amānaṃ maunena rakṣakāri-kalāt; taṃ ca maunaṃ ca

yathā-bahu-pādatvāno vidyā-kāṇḍaṃ tad-eka-niṣṭhatvāt labheta aikyārthaḥ \| sa brāhmaṇaḥ kena syāt iti uktālugupārthibhantroḍupapadyata iti pṛṣṭe yena syān iha eva iti, yena mauna-paryantena brāhmaṇasthādinpuruṣas, tena eva eṣa syāt; na kena api anyena upāyeneti parihṛtam \| atas sarva-vedāntameṣu sthitasya viduṣo yathāditvāśrama-dharmaṇair api āṅgikaṃ mauna-hitaṃ vidyāyā sahakāry-antare vidhīyate \|\| 46 \|\|

atha syāt—yadi sarva-vedānteṣu sthitānāṃ viduṣāṃ tat-samāśrama-dharma-sahakāriṇī mauna-tṛtīya-dharmitā vidyā brahma-prāpti-sādhanam ucyate; kathaṃ tarhi cchandogye abhisamāvṛtya kuṭumbe śucau deśe ity ārasya san khalv evaṃ vartayan yāvāyuṣaṃ brahma-lokam abhisaṃpadyate na ca punar āvartate iti śruti-vākya-pūrṇaṃ gārhasthya-dharmāṇa-sthiti-darśanam upapadyate ? ata āha—

## \|\| 3.4.47 \|\|

*kṛtsna-bhāvāt tu gṛhiṇopasaṃhāraḥ*

**Комментарий:**

tu-śabdaś coddyāvarttī; kṛtsna-bhāvāt—kṛtsna eva bhāvād, kṛtsnaivāśram eva vidyāyāḥ sandhātrāt gṛhiṇopasaṃhāra iti tatropasaṃhāraḥ; tasmāt saṃdhyāśrama-dharma-śabdāntā api gṛhiṇopasaṃhāra iti amitraḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

tathaiva tinn api vākye ācāryaḥ putraiveṇāyaś ca vitteṣaṇāyāś ca lokaiṣaṇāyāś ca vyutthāya atha bhikṣācaryaṃ caranti iti pāriśeṣyeṇa ananta-dharmaṃ pratipādya tasmāt brāhmaṇaḥ pāṇḍityaṃ nirvidya ity-ādinā pāriśeṣya-dharma-sthita-hetuka-mauna-tṛtīya-sahakāri-vidhānaṃ pradarśanārtham iti āha—

## \|\| 3.4.48 \|\|

*mauna-vad itareṣām api upadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

sarveṣāṃ vinimuktasya bhikṣācaraṇa-pūrvaka-maunopadeśaḥ sarva-vedāśrama-dharmaṇāṃ pradarśanārthaḥ; kutaḥ ? evaṃ vidham anopadeśāv itareṣām āśrama-niyamam api trayo dharma-skandhāḥ ity ārasya brāhma-saṃsthaḥ amṛtatvam eti iti brahma-prāpty-upadeśāt \| upapāditaś ca pūrvaṃ brahma-saṃstha-śabdaḥ subhāśriti-misādhāraṇa iti \| atas sūktaṃ—yathā-smita-saviśrambha-vedya-mauna-tṛtīyaḥ pāriśeṣyādhi-vidhyā-sahakāritvena vidhīyata iti \|\| 48 \|\| iti sahakāry-antara-vidhy-adhikaraṇam \|\| 12 \|\|

**atha anāviṣkārādhikaraṇam \|\| 13 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.49 \|\|

*anāviṣkurvan anvayāt*

**Комментарий:**

tasmāt brāhmaṇaḥ pāṇḍityaṃ nirvidya bālyena tiṣṭhāsitavyasya viduṣā bālyam upaveyate vā śrutam \| bālasya bhāvaḥ, karma vā bālyam \| bāla-bhāvasya varṇāśrama-aviśeṣatvānupādeyatvāt karma-

veda gṛhyate \| tatra hi bālasya karma kāmacāra-ādikaṃ sarvaṃ vidy-upāstheyam, uta uṣmādi-vṛhitatvam edhiti viśaye viśeṣābhāvāt sarvam upādeyam; niyama-śāstrāṇi ca viśeṣa-vihitājñena bādhyanta iti \|\| evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate—anāvīkṛta-śrīr iti \| bālasya yat-prabhāva-anāvīṣkāra-rūpaṃ karma; tad upādānaṃ vartate vidyāsu \| kutaḥ ? avagamyāt \| bālyena tiṣṭhāset ityādi-smṛtivyo tathaiva hyavabhāsambhavaḥ; itaś ca vidyā-virodhi-lakṣaṇāt na avirato duścaritān na aśānto na asamāhitaḥ \| na aśānta-mānaso vā api prajñānena enam āpnuyāt \|\| āhāra-śuddhau sattva-śuddhiḥ ityādiṣu \|\| 49 \|\| iti anāvīkṛtādhikaraṇam \|\| 13 \|\|

**atha aihikādhikaraṇam \|\| 14 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.50 \|\|

*aihikam aprastuta-pratibandhe tad-darśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

dvi-vidyā vidyā—abhyudaya-phalā, mukti-phalā ca \| tatra abhyudaya-phalā sva-mādhāna-sūtaḥ puṇya-karmabhiḥ puṇya-karma-nirantaram eva utpadyate, uta anantaraṃ kālāntare vetye niyama iti saṃśayaḥ \| pūrva-pakṣaḥ puṇya-karmaṇi hi vidyāsu jāyate; yathoditaṃ bhagavatā catur-vidhā bhajante māṃ janāḥ sukṛtinaḥ arjuna iti \| sādhane niṣpanne vilamba-hetv-abhāvāt anantaram eva \| iti prāpta ucyate—aihikam aprastuta-pratibandha iti \| aihikam—abhyudaya-phalam upāsanam, aprastuta-pratibandhe-aprastute-prabala-karmāntara-pratibandhe asati anantaram, pratibandhe sati tad-utsara-kāla-kṛta-niyamaḥ \| kutaḥ ? tad-darśanāt—dṛśyate hi prabala-karmāntareṇa karma-phala-pratibandha-upapagamaḥ śrute yad eva vidyayā karoti śraddhayā upaniṣadā tad eva vīryavattaram iti abhyudaya-vidyā-yuktasya karmaṇaḥ phala-pratibandha-śravaṇāt \|\| 50 \|\| iti aihikādhikaraṇam \|\| 14 \|\|

**atha mukti-phalādhikaraṇam \|\| 15 \|\|**

## \|\| 3.4.51 \|\|

*evaṃ mukti-phala-aniyamas tad-avasthā-avadhṛtes tad-avasthā-avadhṛteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

mukti-phalasyāpi upāsanasya sva-mādhāna-sūter anirādhika-karmabhir atyanta-viveka eva kāla-aniyamaḥ, tasyāpi prabandha-pratibandha-abhāva-pratibandha-samābhihita-pāsthāryate—atha api tasya hetoḥ asamānatvāt iti arthaḥ \|\| sarvebhyaḥ karmabhyo mukti-kaivalya-sādhyasya karmaṇaḥ prabalatva-apratibandha-asaṃbhava iti adhikaraṇḍū \|\| tathāpi bahu-vidv-ācaraṇāṃ pūrva-kṛtānāṃ prabalānāṃ saṃbhavāt pratibandha-saṃbhava iti parihāraḥ \| hrihir iti api aparihāram iti yojyā api \|\| 51 \|\| iti muktī-phalādhikaraṇam \|\| 15 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye tṛtīyasya adhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 4 \|\| samāptaḥ adhyāyaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-grantha-mālāyāṃ śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣyam \|

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**caturthaḥ adhyāyaḥ—prathamaḥ pādaḥ—āvṛtti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Adhyaya 4

## Pada 1

## \|\| 4.1.1 \|\|

*āvṛttiḥ asakṛt upadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

tṛtīye adhyāye sādhana-sahe vidyā cintitā \| atha etānāṃ vidyānāṃ svarūpa-viśeṣa-nirūpakaṃ vidyā-phalaṃ cintyate \| tatra brahma-vit āpnoti param tam eva viditvā ati mṛtyum eti brahma veda brahma eva bhavati yadā paśyate rukma-varṇam iti-ādi-vedānta-vākyaiḥ brahmopāsana-sādhanatayā vihitaṃ vedanaṃ kiṃ sakṛt-kṛtam eva śāstra-arthaḥ, uta asakṛd-āvṛttam iti saṃśayaḥ \| kiṃ yuktam? sakṛt-kṛtam iti; kutaḥ? brahma veda brahma eva bhavati iti vedana-mātrasya eva vidhānāt, asakṛd-āvṛttau pramāṇa-abhāvāt \| na ca avaghāta-ādi-vat vedanasya brahmopādeyaṃ prati dṛṣṭopakārya-pādāpakāryam anir iti jñāyaṃ vaktum; vedanasya dṛṣṭopakāra-abhāvāt \| jyotiṣṭoma-adhikaraṇi, vedānta-vihitaṃ ca vedanaṃ parama-puruṣārtha-sādhanatvam, ārādhitatvāt ca parama-puruṣārtha-mokṣākhya-puruṣārtha-viśiṣṭati hi phalavat upacāritasya pratipattitaḥ \| ataḥ jyotiṣṭoma-ādi-vat yathā-śabdaṃ sakṛt-kṛtam eva śāstra-arthaḥ \|\| iti prāpte pracakṣmahe—āvṛttiḥ asakṛt iti \| asakṛd-āvṛttam eva vedanaṃ śāstra-arthaḥ; kutaḥ? upadeśāt—dhyāna-upāsana-paryāṇi vedana-śabdena upadeśāt \| tat-padārthasya ca vidyopāsiddhyupāya-bhūtānekaika-karma-viṣaye vedanopadeśa-paratantratvādhyu prayoga-darśanāt ca \| tathā hi mano brahma iti upāsīta iti upāsanopakrāntaḥ arthaḥ: bhāti ca tapati ca kīrtyā yaśasā brahma-varcasena ca evaṃ veda iti vihita-upasaṃhriyate \| tathā mano-mayaḥ prāṇa-śarīra-netā iti upakrānte vihitorthaṃ raihasyaṃ jñānam anu ma etāṃ bhagavo devatāṃ śādhi yāṃ devatām upāse iti upāsita-upasaṃhriyate \| tathā brahma-vit āpnoti param iti-ādi-vākya-samanārthaṃ vākyaṃ ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ śrotavyaḥ mantavyaḥ nididhyāsitavyaḥ sa tvan tu taṃ paśyati niṣkalaṃ dhyāyamānaḥ iti-ādi-vyu dhyāyatinā vedanam abhidhīyate \| dhyānaṃ ca cintanam \| cintanaṃ ca smṛti-santati-rūpam; na smṛti-mātram \| upāsir api tad-eha-arthaḥ; eka-artha-cintā-santati-rūpatvaṃ prayoga-darśanāt \| laghu-madhyakaḥ api asakṛd-āvṛtta-santata-smṛti-rūha brahma veda brahma eva bhavati jñātvā devaṃ mucyate sarva-pāśaiḥ iti-ādiṣu vedana-ādi-śabder abhidhīyate iti niścīyate \|\| 1 \|\|

## \|\| 4.1.2 \|\|

*liṅgāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

liṅgaṃ smṛtiḥ \| smṛteḥ ca upāsya-artha-avagamayati \| smaryate hi mokṣa-sādhana-bhūtaṃ vedanaṃ smṛti-santati-rūhaṃ tad-pratyayaḥ ca ekā santatiḥ āprayaṇāt susnigdhā \| tat-dhyānaṃ prathamaṃ ṣaḍbhiḥ aṅgaiḥ niṣpādyate tathā \|\| iti \| tasmāt asakṛd-āvṛttam eva vedanaṃ śāstra-arthaḥ \|\| 2 \|\| iti āvṛtti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha ātma-dvoṣāsana-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.1.3 \|\|

*ātmeti tu upagacchanti grāhayanti ca*

**Комментарий:**

idam idānīṃ cintyate—kim upāsyaṃ brahma upāsituḥ eva devayāna-upāsyam, uta upāsituḥ ātmatvena iti \| kiṃ yuktam? anyatvena iti \| kutaḥ? upāsitaḥ pratyag-ātmanaḥ arthāntaratvāt brahmaṇaḥ \| arthāntaratvaṃ ca adhikaṃ tu bheda-nirdeśāt adhika-upadeśāc ca na itaraḥ anupapatteḥ ity-ādi-bahu-pāditam \| yathāvasthitaṃ ca brahma-upāsyam; adhy-upāsane hi brahma-prāptiḥ iti abhūtā syāt, yathākratuḥ asmin loke puruṣaḥ bhavati tathā itaḥ pretya bhavati iti nyāyāt \| ataḥ anyatvena eva upāsanīyam \|\|

evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate—ātmeti tu iti \| tu-śabdaḥ avadhāraṇe \| upāsituḥ ātmatvena eva upāsyam, upāsitā pratyag-ātmā sva-śarīrasya svayaṃ yathā ātmā; tathā sva-ātmanaḥ api paraṃ brahma ātmatvena upāsita iti arthaḥ \| kutaḥ? evaṃ hi upāgacchanti pūrve upāsitāraḥ tvaṃ vā aham asmi bhagavo devate ahaṃ vai tvam asi iti \| upāsituḥ eva antarbhūtaṃ brahma-upāsitāraḥ adhyemīti kaṇva-śruti-upagacchanti iti atra āha—grāhayanti ca iti \| imam artham avihitam upaniṣadaḥ grāhayanti śāstrāṇi—tāt pratyag-upādāyatvena iti arthaḥ \| ya ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaraḥ yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ ya ātmānam antaraḥ yamayati sa ta ātmā antaryāmī amṛtaḥ iti; tathā san-mūlāḥ somya imāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ sat-āyatanāḥ sat-pratiṣṭhāḥ \| aitad-ātmyam idaṃ sarvaṃ satyaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma taj-jalān iti iti ca sarvasya cid-acid-vastunaḥ taj-jalatvāt tal-layatvāt tad-avāpti-kriyātmakatvāt tac-charīratvāc ca sarvasya ayam ātmā; aham asmi ta ātmā \| ataḥ yathā pratyag-ātmanaḥ sva-śarīraṃ praty-ātmatvāt devā aham manuṣyaḥ aham iti anusandhānam; tathā pratyag-ātmanaḥ api ātmatayā param-ātmanaḥ tasya api aham iti eva anusandhānaṃ yuktam iti \| evaṃ śāstraiḥ upapāditaṃ sarva-buddhīnāṃ brahmaika-niṣṭhatvena sarva-śabdānāṃ brahmaika-niṣṭhatvam abhyupagacchataḥ tvaṃ vā aham asmi bhagavo devate ahaṃ vai tvam asi bhagavo devate iti vyatikareṇa ullekhanataḥ \| evaṃ ca atha yaḥ anyāṃ devatām upāste anyaḥ asau anyaḥ aham asmi iti na sa veda akṛtsnaḥ hi eṣa ātmā iti eva upāsīta sarvaṃ taṃ parādāt yaḥ anyatra ātmanaḥ sarvaṃ veda iti ātmatva-anusandhāna-niṣedhaḥ; dṛśya-ātmānaṃ preritāraṃ ca matvā iti pṛthaktva-anusandhāna-vidhānaṃ ca aviruddham \| aham iti sva-ātmatayā-anusandhāna-ananyathā-anusandhāna-niṣedhe rakṣitaḥ \| sva-śarīrāt sva-ātmanaḥ adhikatva-anusandhāna-vat sva-ātmanaḥ api param-ātmanaḥ adhikatva-anusandhānāt pṛthaktva-anusandhāna-vidhānaṃ ca rakṣitam \| adhikatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pratyag-ātman ātmatayā tasya ca brahma-śarīratva-aviśeṣāc ca api akṛtsnaḥ hi eṣaḥ iti uktam \| ata upāsituḥ ātmatvena brahma-upāsyam iti sthitam \|\| 3 \|\|

iti ātma-dvoṣāsana-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha pratīka-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.1.4 \|\|

*na pratīke na hi saḥ*

**Комментарий:**

mano brahma iti upāsīta sa yaḥ nāma brahma iti upāste ity-ādiṣu aneka-upāsaneṣu upāsya-ātmatva-sambandhaṃ kāryam, uta na iti cintāyāṃ mano brahma iti upāsīta iti brahma-upāsanatva-sāmānyād brahma-śabda-abhidhurarāmavadārastha eva upāsīta iti \| evaṃ prāpte ’bhidhīyate—na pratīka iti \| pratīke na ātmatva-anusandhānaṃ kāryam; na hi saḥ—na aham parikhurarāmā pratīkaḥ \| pratīka-upāsanena hi nāma akṣarāṇi brahma-śabda-anusandhātum; tatra upāsyasya pratīka-rūpa-upāsituḥ ātmatva-bhāvanā na yuktā tathā anusandheyam \|\| 4 \|\|

nanu api brahma-upāsyam; brahmaṇaḥ upāsyatva-saṃbhave sati aham ātmā iti etanāmatulyoktīnāṃ na upāsyatva-śravaṇasya api avirodhāt \| ataḥ mana-ādi-dṛṣṭyā brahma-upāsyam iti; ata āha—

## \|\| 4.1.5 \|\|

*brahma-dṛṣṭiḥ utkarṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

mana-ādiṣu brahma-dṛṣṭiḥ yuktā, brahmaṇi mana-ādi-dṛṣṭiḥ; brahmaṇaḥ mana-ādibhyaḥ utkarṣāt; teṣāṃ ca viparyayāt \| utkṛṣṭe hi rājani bhṛtya-dṛṣṭiḥ pratyavakaroti; bhṛtye tu rāja-dṛṣṭiḥ abhyudayāya \|\| 5 \|\| iti pratīka-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha ādityādi-mati-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.1.6 \|\|

*ādityādi-matayaḥ ca aṅge upapatteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

yaḥ eva asau tapati tam udgītham upāsīta ity-ādiṣu karma-aṅga-āśreṣu upāsaneṣu saṃśayaḥ—kiṃ udgītha-ādi karma-aṅgam āditya-ādi-dṛṣṭiḥ kartavyā, uta āditya-ādi-dṛṣṭyā udgītha-ādi-dṛṣṭiḥ iti \| utkṛṣṭa-dṛṣṭiḥ nikṛṣṭe kartavyā iti nyāyāt, udgītha-ādīnāṃ ca phala-sādhana-bhūta-karma-aṅgatvena aphalasya āditya-ādibhyaḥ utkṛṣṭatvād āditya-ādi-dṛṣṭyā udgītha-ādi-dṛṣṭiḥ \|\| iti prāpte ’bhidhīyate—ādityādi-matayaḥ ca iti \| ca-śabdaḥ avadhāraṇe \| karmasu udgītha-ādau āditya-ādi-dṛṣṭyā eva kāryā; kutaḥ? upapatteḥ—āditya-ādīnāṃ ca udgītheṣu upapatteḥ \| āditya-ādi-devatā-rādhana-dvāreṇa hi karmaṇām api phala-sādhanatvam; ataḥ tat-udgīthādi aṅge \|\| 6 \|\| iti ādityādi-mati-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha āsīna-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.1.7 \|\|

*āsīnaḥ saṃbhavāt*

**Комментарий:**

mokṣa-sādhanatayā vedānta-śāstre vihitaṃ jñānaṃ dhyāna-upāsana-ādi-śabda-vācyam askṛd-āvṛttaṃ santata-smṛti-rūpam iti uktam \| tad-ukti-viprāsānīśrayānniyamitam ghaṭṭaś ca viśeṣābhāvāt aniyamena anuṣṭhitaṃ \|\| iha prām ucyate—āsāna iti \| āsīnaḥ upāsanam anu-tiṣṭhet; kutaḥ ? saṃbhavāt—āsīnasya eva hi ekāgrya-cittatā-saṃbhavaḥ; sthiti-nidrayoḥ prayatnāpekṣatvāt, śayane ca nidrā-saṃbhavāt \| paścād-adharaṇa-prayatna-nivṛttaye ca āsāpāśraye āsīnaḥ kuryāt \|\| 7 \|\|

## \|\| 4.1.8 \|\|

*dhyānāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

nidhyāyitavyaḥ iti dhyāna-śabdat upāsanasya, ekāgra-cittatādyaṅga-bhāvini \| dhyānaṃ hi vijātīya-pratyayāntara-vyavahitena ekaś cintanam iti uktam \|\| 8 \|\|

## \|\| 4.1.9 \|\|

*acalatvaṃ ca apekṣya*

**Комментарий:**

niścalatvaṃ ca apekṣya pṛthivī-antarikṣa-ādiṣu dhyāna-vācaḥ uktibhiḥ smṛte dhyāyati iva pṛthivī, dhyāyati iva antarikṣaṃ, dhyāyati iva dyauḥ, dhyāyanti iva āpaḥ, dhyāyanti iva parvatāḥ iti \| ataḥ pṛthivī-parvata-ādi-vat ekāgra-cintatayā niścalatvam upāsakasya aṅgena eva saṃbhavet \|\| 9 \|\|

## \|\| 4.1.10 \|\|

*smaranti ca*

**Комментарий:**

smaranti ca āsīnasya eva dhyānaṃ śucau deśe pratiṣṭhāpya sthiram āsanam ātmanaḥ \| na atyucchritaṃ na atinīcaṃ caila-ajina-kuśa-uttaram \| tatra ekāgraṃ manaḥ kṛtvā yata-citta-indriya-kriyaḥ \| upaviśya āsane yuñjyāt yogam ātma-śuddhaye \|\| iti \|\| 10 \|\|

## \|\| 4.1.11 \|\|

*yatra ekāgratā tatra aviśeṣāt*

**Комментарий:**

ekāgratā-atirikta-deśa-kāla-viśeṣa-śravaṇādeḥ kālatākuṭhalo yaḥ deśaḥ kālaś ca, sa eva upāsanasya deśaḥ kālaś ca \| manaḥ anukūle iti vacanāde kānta-kānta-deśam āhaḥ, na tu deśaṃ niyacchanti, sanonukūle iti vākya-yogāt \|\| 11 \|\|

iti āsīna-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

**atha āśrayāṇa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.1.12 \|\|

*āśrayāṇāṃ tu api hi dṛṣṭam*

**Комментарий:**

tat idam apavarga-sādhana-phala-lakṣaṇam upāsanaikāhya eva saṃpādyaṃ, uttarāśramāsthair gṛhastha-saṃvartanīyam iti vicārya, ekasmin eva ahni śāstra-arthasya kṛtsnatvāt tat-āśramatvena eva parisamāpanīyam \| iti prāpte ucyate—āśrayāṇām iti \| āśramāṇāṃ tu vartanīyam; kutaḥ? tathā api hi dṛṣṭam—upāsana-udyoga-prabhṛti ā-prayāṇāt madhye yaḥ kālaḥ, tatra sarveṣu api dṛṣṭam upāsanaṃ śrūyam evaṃ vartanīyam avadāyuktaṃ brahma-lokam abhisambhavati iti \|\| 12 \|\| iti āśrayāṇa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha tat-adhigama-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.1.13 \|\|

*tat-adhigame uttara-pūrva-āghayoḥ aśleṣa-vināśau tat-vyapadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ vidyā-svarūpaṃ viśodhya vidyā-phalaṃ vinirṇetum ārabhate; brahma-vidyā-yogī puruṣasya uttara-pūrva-āghayoḥ aśleṣa-vināśau śrūyete tathā puṣkara-palāśa āpaḥ na śliṣyante evam evaṃ-vidi pāpaṃ karma na śliṣyate tathā tam ā padavittaṃ viddhvā na karmaṇā lipyate pāpakena iti uttara-āgha-aśleṣaḥ; tat yathā iṣīkā-tūlam agnau protaṃ pradūyeta evaṃ ha asya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante kṣīyante ca asya karmaṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare iti pūrva-vit-vināśaḥ \| etau aśleṣa-vināśau vidyā-phalatayā upapadyete, na iti saṃśayaḥ \| kiṃ yuktam? na upapadyete iti \| kutaḥ? na abhuktaṃ kṣīyate karma kalpa-koṭi-śataiḥ api iti-ādi-vākya-virodhāt \| aśleṣa-vināśa-vacane tu mokṣa-sādhana-bhūta-vidyā-vidyādhināyakyoṣitāḥ kathaṃcit vidyā-anukūla-citta-pāṭavena upapadyete \| na ca vidyā pūrva-uttara-āghayoḥ prāyaścitta-tathā virodhyete; yena prāyaścittena āgha-vināśa ucyate \| vidyā hi brahma-vit āpnoti paraṃ brahma veda brahma eva bhavati iti brahma-prāpti-upāyatayā vidhīyate \| ataḥ vidyāyāṃ virodhyam apavināśa-aśleṣa-vacanaṃ paddeśa iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate—tat-adhigama iti \| vidyā-prāptau puruṣasya vidyā-māhātmyāt uttara-pūrva-āghayoḥ aśleṣa-vināśau upapadyete; kutaḥ? evaṃ-vidi hi vidyā-māhātmyam ananyāyate puṣkara-palāśa āpaḥ na śliṣyate evaṃ ha asya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante iti-ādi-vyapadeśāt \| na ca na abhuktaṃ kṣīyate karma iti anena śarīra-antyasya virodhaḥ, bhinna-viṣayatvāt \| tat dhi karmaṇāṃ phala-janana-sāmarthyaṃ dahati viṣayam; etat tu yathā vidyāyāḥ prākṛtatānāṃ pāpmāṃ phala-janana-śakti-vināśa-sāmarthyam adṛṣṭa-phalānāṃ ca phala-janana-sāmarthya-pratibandhaka-svarūpa-sāmarthyaṃ ca pratipādayati iti dvayoḥ viṣayo bhidyate \| yathā agni-jalayoḥ uṣṇatva-īcāraṇa-sāmarthya-viṣaye dvayoḥ pramāṇayoḥ api viṣaya-bhedāt aprāmāṇyam; evam atra api na kaścit virodhaḥ \| adhyayana-lekhana-vaidika-karmā-

yogya-saṃtāna-samabhyudaya-hetu-bandhu-pitṛ-pratibandha-karaṇam \| athāni kṛtāni puruṣasya vaidika-karma-yogyatām, saṃjātīya-karma-nirantarārambhaṃ ca, hṛdyatāṃ ca kurvanti \| asya vināśa-karaṇam—utpādya-asaṃśliṣṭa-vināśa-karaṇam \| śaktir api parama-puruṣa-prītir eva \| tad evaṃ vidyā vaidika-puṇya-atyartha-priyatvena svayam api niratiśaya-priyā satī vedya-bhūta-parama-puruṣārādhana-svarūpā pūrva-kṛtāgha-pracyuti-tat-parama-puruṣa-prāpti vināśayati; sa eva vidyā sva-utpatti-anantara-kāla-bhāvi-arjita-parama-puruṣa-prīti-utpattiṃ ca pratibadhnāti \| tad idam aśleṣa-vacanaṃ prāmādika-viṣayaṃ mantavyam; na avicikitsan duścaritāt ity-ādi-śiṣya-śāstra-upasaraṇa-ādara-haḥ-pradhānatayā uttarottara-ātiśaya-bhāginyaḥ vidyāyāḥ durvicarita-virodhi-viṣayatvāgamāt \|\| 13 \|\| iti tad-adhigama-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|

**atha itara-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.1.14 \|\|

*itarasya api evaṃ asaṃśleṣaḥ pāte tu*

**Комментарий:**

uttara-pūrva-āghayoḥ vidyayā aśleṣa-vināśau uktau \| itarasya—puṇyasya api, evam—uktena nyāyena asaṃśleṣa-vināśau vidyayā syātām, vidyā-phala-virodhitva-sāmānyād upadeśāc ca \| bhavati ca vyapadeśaḥ: ubhe sukṛta-duṣkṛte nirdhūya sarve pāpmānaḥ ato nivartante iti, tasya sukṛta-duṣkṛte dhūnute iti ca \| mumukṣoḥ niḥśreyasa-phalatvāt sukṛtasya api prāptatvena vyapadeśaḥ \| muktasya api śāstrīyatvāt tat-phalasya kaiṅkarya-viśeṣatva-darśanāc ca vidyayā avirodhād aśleṣo nivṛttir iti deśaḥ \| nanu viduṣaḥ api naitikartavyatā-kope asannihṛtya vṛddhy-amādi-phalāni śeṣyante; kathaṃ teṣāṃ virodhitayā ucyate ? tatra āha—pāte tu iti \| śarīra-pāte tu teṣāṃ vināśaḥ; śarīra-pātād ūrdhvaṃ tu vidyā-anuguṇāḥ phalāni muktāni na śakyanti ity-arthaḥ \|\| 14 \|\| iti itara-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|

**atha anārabdha-kārya-adhikaraṇam \|\| 9 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.1.15 \|\|

*anārabdha-kārye eva tu pūrve tad-avadheḥ*

**Комментарий:**

brahma-vidyā-niṣṭhasya pūrva-uttara-bhāvinoḥ sukṛta-duṣkṛtayoḥ aśleṣa-vināśau uktau; tataḥ pūrva-bhāvinoḥ sukṛta-duṣkṛtayoḥ kim aviśeṣeṇa vināśaḥ; uta anārabdha-kāryayoḥ eva iti viṣaye, sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante iti vidyā-phalasya aviśeṣa-śravaṇād acireṇa atipuruṣa-kāla-labha-vidyāś ca śarīra-sthiteḥ kulāla-cakra-bhramaṇa-ādi-vat-saṃskāra-vaśād upapatteḥ viśeṣeṇa \| iti prāpte ucyate—anārabdha-kārye eva tu pūrvam iti \| viśeṣopadatteḥ pūrvaṃ sukṛta-duṣkṛte anārabdha-kārye—aśruta-phale eva vidyayā vinaśyataḥ \| kutaḥ ? tad-avadheḥ—tasya tāvat eva ciraṃ yāvat na vimokṣye atha saṃpatsye iti śarīra-pāta-

vilamba-aviśeṣataḥ \| na ca puṇyāpuṇya-karma-janya-bhāva-rūpīya-śarīra-indriya-viṣaye-bhūta-saṃskāra-sadbhāve namādharmataḥ \|\| 15 \|\| iti anārabdha-bhoga-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha agnihotra-ādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 10 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.1.16 \|\|

*agnihotra-ādi tu tat-kāryāya eva tad-darśanāt*

**Комментарий:**

itarasyā api evam asaṃśleṣe iti vidyā-balāt sukṛta-duritayoḥ saṃśleṣa uktaḥ \| agnihotra-ādīnāṃ nitya-naimittikānāṃ svāśrama-dharmāṇām api sukṛtatva-sāmānyena tat-phala-tyāgena ca arthi-niṣṭhatvāt anuṣṭhāne prāpta ucyate—agnihotrādi tu iti \| brahma-vidyā-yukta-ātmanas tu eṣāṃ viśeṣa-arthaḥ \| agnihotra-ādi-āśrama-dharmaḥ phala-aśleṣa-saṃbhavāt duṣṭeṇa eva \| tad-darśanāt ca tat-kāryārthitvāt ca eṣām \| vidyā hi vidvad-agnihotra-ādi-anuṣṭhānam \| katham idam avagamyate ? tad-dharmāṇāṃ dṛśyate hi tam etaṃ veda-anuvacanena brāhmaṇā vividiṣanti yajñena dānena tapasā anāśakena ity-ādinā agnihotra-ādīnāṃ vidyā-sādhanatvam \| vidyā-apekṣayā pradhāna-ādi-aṅga-sādhya-atiśayāyā aharahar-upāsya-saduṣyartham-āśrama-karmasviharahar-anuṣṭheyam eva \| anyathā śrama-karma-lopaḥ dūṣitānantaḥkaraṇasya vidyotpattiḥ eva na syāt \|\| 16 \|\|

yadi agnihotra-ādi-sādhu-kriyā vidyotpattayarthāḥ; vidyotpatteḥ prācīnaṃ ca sukṛtaṃ yāvat saṃpātamuṣitvā atha etayā karmaṇaḥ iti ubhayena vinaśyatu; bhukta-aśiṣṭaṃ ca prārabdham astu; suhṛdaḥ sādhu-kṛtyām anvayasya ko viṣayaḥ ? tatrāha—

## \|\| 4.1.17 \|\|

*ataḥ anyā api hi ekeṣām ubhayoḥ*

**Комментарий:**

ataḥ—agnihotra-ādi-sādhu-kriyāyāḥ vidyotpattyarthitāḥ anyā api vidyā-dharma-sādhya-ubhaya-saṃśleṣayoḥ iva puṇya-karmaṇaḥ prabala-karma-nivṛtta-phalā sādhu-kriyā-anantā saṃbhaved eva; tad-viṣaya-smṛti ekeṣāṃ śākhinaṃ vacanaṃ tasya putrā dāyam upayanti suhṛdaḥ sādhu-kṛtyām iti \| vidyā-phaleṣu avināśa-śrutiḥ tu tad-viṣayā \|\| 17 \|\|

anuṣṭhitasyā api karmaṇaḥ phala-pratisambandha-saṃbhavaṃ pūrvoktaṃ smārayati—

## \|\| 4.1.18 \|\|

*yad eva vidyayā iti hi*

**Комментарий:**

yad eva vidyayā karoti… tad eva vīryavattaram iti ukta-yoga-vidyāyāḥ kratvalārthiti-saṃba-

phala-vacanena anubandhaḥ api karmaṇaḥ phala-pratisambandhaḥ ucyate hi \| ataḥ viduṣaḥ anuṣṭhita-pratiṣiddha-phala-viṣayaṃ suhṛt sādhu-kṛtyām iti śākhāntara-niṣedhaḥ \|\| 18 \|\| iti agnihotra-ādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 10 \|\|

**atha itara-kṣapaṇa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 11 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.1.19 \|\|

*bhogena tu itare kṣapayitvā atha saṃpadyate*

**Комментарий:**

yathā: puṇya-pāpayoḥ aśleṣa-vināśau sutau, tābhyām itare ārabdha-kārye puṇya-pāpe kiṃ vidyāyonirārogavatsāte, uta tat-jvara-rogavat-sāte śarīra-antara-ārambhāne deyāniyam iti saṃśaye, tasya tāvat eva ciraṃ yāvat na vimokṣye iti tat-śarīra-vimokṣa-avasānatva-śravaṇāt tat-avasāte \| iti prāpte ucyate—bhogena tu iti \| dukhavat pakṣa-dvārarthyaḥ \| itare ārabdha-kārye puṇya-pāpe sva-ārabdha-sphala-bhogena kṣapayitvā tat-phala-bhoga-samāpti-anantaraṃ brahma saṃpadyate \| te ca puṇya-pāpe ekārīreṇa bhogya-phale cet, tat-śarīra-avasāne saṃpadyate; aneka-śarīra-bhogya-phale cet, tat-avasāne saṃpadyate; bhogena eva kṣapayitavyatvād ārabdha-sphalayoḥ karmaṇoḥ \| tasya tāvat eva ciraṃ yāvat na vimokṣye iti ca bhogena

tayoḥ karmaṇoḥ vināśa ucyate, deha-avasāna-niyama-āśravaṇāt \| tat evaṃ brahma-vidyāyāḥ prāpta-anuṣṭhita-mukta-phalam ārabdha-sphalaṃ puṇya-pāpa-rūpaṃ karma antima-kāla-saṃcitam anantaṃ vidyā-māhātmyād vinaśyati; vidyā-ārambha-ananta-kālam anuṣṭhitaṃ ca na śliṣyati; tatra puṇya-rūpaṃ sarvaṃ viduṣaḥ suhṛdaḥ gṛhṇanti, pāpaṃ ca dviṣanta iti niravadyam \|\| 19 \|\| iti itara-kṣapaṇa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 11 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye caturtha-adhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**caturtha-adhyāye—dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ—vāk-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Pada 2

## \|\| 4.2.1 \|\|

*vāk manasi darśanāt śabdāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

idānīṃ viduṣo gati-prakāraṃ cintayitum ārabhate \| prathamaṃ tāvat utkrāntiḥ cintyate \| tad evaṃ bhagavatā asya somya puruṣasya prayataḥ vāk manasi saṃpadyate manaḥ prāṇe prāṇaḥ tejasi tejaḥ parasyāṃ devatāyām iti \| atra vāk manasi saṃpadyate iti vācaḥ manasi saṃpatti-śrutiḥ kiṃ vāk-vṛtti-mātra-viṣayā, uta vāk-indriya-vṛtti-viśaye; vṛtti-mātra-viṣayeṣu yuktam \| kutaḥ? manasaḥ dhātrṛ-kṛtitva-abhāvāt tasya vāk-śabda-vācyaṃ prati laya-saṃbhavāt \| daśamāt vṛttīnāṃ manasā eva hetutvena vṛtti-muktiḥ

kartr̥tvac upapadyata iti \| evaṃ prāpte 'bhidhīyate—vāk manasi \| vāk-svarūpam eva manasi saṃbadhate \| kutaḥ? darśanāt—dṛśyate hi vāk-indriya uparate 'pi manaḥ-pravṛttiḥ \| vṛtti-mātra-saṃbandhe 'pi tad upapadyata iti cet, tatra āha—śabdāt ca iti \| vāk manasi saṃpadyata iti vāk-svarūpa-paryantatayā eva hi śabdaḥ; na vṛtti-mātra-saṃpattau \| na hi tadānīṃ vṛtti-upaśame vāk-indriyaṃ pramāṇa-antareṇa upalabhyate; yena vṛtti-mātram eva saṃbadhata iti ucyeta \| yuktam manaso vākya-kṛtitva-abhāvāt ca svarūpi saṃpattinā upapadyata iti; tataḥ, vāk manasi saṃpadyata iti vacanāt manasā vāk saṃyujyate, na tu tatra līyata iti parihartavyam \|\| 1 \|\|

## \|\| 4.2.2 \|\|

*ata eva sarvāṇi anu*

**Комментарий:**

yato vāco manasā saṃyoga-mātraṃ saṃpattiḥ; na tu layaḥ; ata eva vāṅmanu sarvāṇi indriyāṇāṃ manasi saṃpatti-śrutiḥ upapadyate tasmād upaśānta-tejāḥ punar-bhava-indriyaiḥ manasi saṃpadyamānaiḥ iti \|\| 2 \|\| iti vāk-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha manaḥ-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.2.3 \|\|

*tat manaḥ prāṇe uttarāt*

**Комментарий:**

tatra—sarva-indriya-saṃyuktaṃ manaḥ prāṇe saṃbadhate—prāṇena saṃyujyate; na mano-vṛtti-mātram \| kutaḥ? uttarāt—manaḥ prāṇe iti vākyāt \|\| adhikāraś ca tu—akṣaramayaṃ hi saumya manaḥ iti vacanāt manaso 'cchukṛtivad bhavatpatteḥ; akṣarasya ca tā akṣaram ajanta iti akṣaramayatvaṃ liṅgam, āpomayaḥ prāṇaḥ iti vākya-kṛtitvaṃ prāṇasya avagamyate, ato manaḥ prāṇe saṃbadhata iti atra prāṇa-śabdena prāṇa-prakṛti-bhūtā apo nirdiśya sāsu manaḥ-saṃpatti-pratipādane paramparayā svīkāro laya iti saṃpatti-vacanam upapannaṃ bhavati iti \|\| parihāras tu akṣaramayaṃ hi saumya manaḥ; āpomayaḥ prāṇaḥ iti manaḥ-prāṇayoḥ eva nāḍī-dṛṣṭyā api āgamam ucyate, na tat-prakṛtitvam; āhārādhikṛtāmanasaḥ; ākāśa-adhikāratvāt ca prāṇasya \| prāṇa-śabdena apaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ ca sthagitam \|\| 3 \|\| iti manaḥ-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha adhyakṣa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.2.4 \|\|

*saḥ adhyakṣe tat-upagamādibhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

yathā vāk manasi saṃbadhate, manaḥ prāṇe iti vacana-anurodhena manaḥ-prāṇayoḥ eva vāṅmanasyayoḥ saṃpattiḥ; tathā prāṇas tejasi iti vacanāt tejasya eva prāṇasya saṃpadyate \| iti prāṇa

ucyate—śodhyatve iti \| saṃ-prāṇaḥ, adhyakṣe karaṇa-adhipe jīve saṃyate \| kutaḥ ? tad-upagamāt ihasya-prāṇasya jīva-upagama-sattvāvacchinnatve evam-evesābhāmānamanat-kāle sarve prāṇāḥ abhisaṃyanti iti \| tathā jīvena saha prāṇasya utkrāntiḥ śrūyate tam utkrāmantam prāṇaḥ anūtkrāmati iti \| pratiṣṭhā ca jīvena saha śrūyate kasmin nu aham utkrānte utkrāntaḥ bhaviṣyāmi kasmin nu ā pratiṣṭhite pratiṣṭhāsyāmi iti \| evaṃ jīvena saṃyujya hena saha tejaḥ saṃpattiḥ iha prāṇaḥ tejasi iti ucyate; yathā yamunāyāḥ gaṅgayā saṃyujya sāgaram eṣy api yamunā sāgaraṃ gacchati iti vacaḥ na virudhyate, tadvat \|\| 4 \|\| iti adhyakṣa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha bhūta-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.2.5 \|\|

*bhūteṣu tat-śruteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

prāṇaḥ tejasi iti jīva-saṃyuktasya prāṇasya tejasi saṃpattiḥ uktā; sā saṃpattiḥ kiṃ tejo-mātre, uta saṃhate eva sarveṣu bhūteṣu iti viṣaye tejo-mātra-śravaṇāt tejasi \| iti prāpte ucyate—bhūteṣu iti \| bhūteṣu saṃyate \| kutaḥ ? tat-śruteḥ pṛthivī-mayaḥ āpo-mayaḥ ... tejo-mayaḥ iti jīvasya samasta-tat-saṃbaddha-bhūta-mayatva-śruteḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

nanu tejaḥ-prabhṛtitve kaṅkāri-svarūpā krameṇa saṃpattau api pṛthivīmaya iti-ādikā śrutiḥ upapadyate, ata āha—

## \|\| 4.2.6 \|\|

*na ekasmin tu darśayato hi*

**Комментарий:**

na ekasmin, ekasya kārye sattvāt \| darśayataḥ hi asmin eva śruti-smṛtī anena jīvena ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi tāsāṃ tri-vṛtaṃ tri-vṛtam ekaikāṃ karavāṇi iti nāma-rūpa-vyākaraṇa-yogyatvāya tri-vṛt-karaṇam upadiśyate \| sānāthaḥ athiḥ pṛthak-bhūtāḥ tataḥ te deha iti vinā \| na aśaknuvan prajāḥ sraṣṭum asamāgamya kṛtsnaśaḥ \| sametya anyonya-saṃyogaṃ paraspara-samāśrayāḥ \| mahat-ādi-viśeṣāntāḥ hi aṇḍam utpādayanti te \|\| iti \| ataḥ prāṇaḥ tejasi iti tejaḥ-śabdena bhūta-antara-saṃsṛṣṭam eva tejaḥ abhidhīyate \| ataḥ bhūteṣu eva saṃpattiḥ \|\| 6 \|\| iti bhūta-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha āsṛti-upakrama-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.2.7 \|\|

*samānā ca āsṛti-upakramāt amṛtatvaṃ ca anupoṣya*

**Комментарий:**

iyam utkrāntiḥ kiṃ vidvat-aviduṣoḥ samānā, uta aviduṣaḥ eva iti cintāyām, aviduṣaḥ eva iti prāptam \| kutaḥ ? viduṣaḥ atra amṛtatva-vacanāt utkrānti-abhāvāt \| viduṣaḥ hi atra eva amṛtatvaṃ

śrūyate—yadā sarve pramucyante kāmāḥ ye asya hṛdi sthitāḥ \| atha martyaḥ amṛtaḥ bhavati atra brahma samaśnute \|\| iti \| evaṃ prāhaḍ api śrīyate—samānā bāhya-puruṣa-kramāvid iti \| viduṣaḥ api a-puruṣa-kramāt ukrānti-samānā \| ātharvaṇikāḥ ca—ātharvaṇika-amṛtaṃ, nāḍīḍaiveśāstra-praticayaḥ \| viduṣaḥ api hi nāḍī-viśeṣeṇa utkramaṇaṃ gatiḥ śrūyate—śataṃ ca ekā ca hṛdayasya nāḍyaḥ tāsāṃ mūrdhānam abhinissṛtā ekā \| tayā ūrdhvam āyan amṛtatvam eti viṣvaṅ anyāḥ utkramaṇe bhavanti \|\| iti \| evaṃ nāḍī-viśeṣeṇa gati-śravaṇāt viduṣaḥ api utkrāntiḥ avarjanīyā \| sā ca nāḍī-praveśāstra-praticayoḥ aśravaṇa-sambhāvā \| tatra praveśa-darśanāṃ ca viśeṣaḥ śrūyate etena pradyotena eṣa ātmā niṣkrāmati cakṣuṣaḥ vā mūrdhnaḥ vā anyebhyaḥ vā śarīra-deśebhyaḥ iti \| śataṃ ca ekā ca hṛdayasya eva ca yā śruti-ekārtha-niṣṭhāṃ niṣkramaṇe viddhi viduṣaḥ; itaratra aviduṣaḥ \| yad uktaṃ viduṣaḥ atra eva amṛtatvaṃ śrāvyata iti; tat na ucyate—amṛtatvaṃ ca anupauṣṭhikam \| cakra-bandhāvasādhāraṇe \| anupauṣṭya—śarīra-indriya-ādi-sambandha-nivṛttyā eva, yat-sattvasya—uttarāgha-vidyā-ārambhe avidyā-sāhaṃ prāpyate; tat ucyate yadā sarve pramucyante iti-ādikam iti arthaḥ \| atra brahma samaśnuta iti ca upāsana-velāyāṃ yaḥ brahma-anubhavaḥ; tat-vivakṣayā iti abhiprāyaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## \|\| 4.2.8 \|\|

*tad-āpīteḥ saṃsāra-vyapadeśāt*

**Комментарий:**

avaśyaṃ ca tat—amṛtatvam adhyadeha-sambandham eva iti vijñeyam \| kutaḥ ? āpīteḥ saṃsāra-vyapadeśāt—āpītiḥ—āpyayaḥ; brahma-prāptiḥ \| sā ca adhikāriṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ deśa-viśeṣe gamyate vakṣyate \| ātmatvasya śobhāṃ saṃsāraḥ deha-sambandha-lakṣaṇo hi vyapadiśyate—tasya tāvat eva ciraṃ yāvat na vimokṣye atha sampatsye iti, asya eva romāṇi vidhūya pāpaṃ candra iva rāhoḥ mukhāt pramucya \| dhūtvā śarīram akṛtaṃ kṛtātmā brahma-lokam abhisaṃbhavāni \|\| iti ca \|\| 8 \|\|

## \|\| 4.2.9 \|\|

*sūkṣmaṃ pramāṇataḥ ca tathā-upalabdheḥ*

**Комментарий:**

itaḥ ca viduṣaḥ api bandhaḥ na atra dagdhaḥ; yataḥ sūkṣmaṃ śarīram anuvartate \| kutaḥ etad avagamyate ? pramāṇataḥ tathā upalabdheḥ—upalabhyate hi devayānena pathā gacchato viduṣaḥ taṃ prati brūyāt satyaṃ brūyāt iti candramasā saṃvādanena śarīra-sadbhāvaḥ \| atas sūkṣma-śarīram anuvartate \| atas tra bandhaḥ na dagdhaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## \|\| 4.2.10 \|\|

*na upamardena ataḥ*

**Комментарий:**

ataḥ yadā sarve pramucyante kāmāḥ ye asya hṛdi sthitāḥ \| atha martyaḥ amṛtaḥ bhavati atra brahma samaśnute \|\| iti vacanaṃ na bandhopamardena amṛtatvaṃ vadati \|\| 10 \|\|

## \|\| 4.2.11 \|\|

*asya eva ca upapatteḥ ūṣmā*

**Комментарий:**

asya—sūkṣma-śarīrasya baddha-vidyamānatva-upapatteḥ; praśānta-tamasaḥ tu maraṇa-ārambha-bhūmā sthūle śarīre kvacit vīkṣā upalabhyate \| na ca sthūlasya eva śarīrasya ūṣmā, anyatra anupalabdheḥ \| tatra śobhanaḥ kvacit upatabdhiḥ baddha-sūkṣma-śarīra-sthī-kānti-nibandhanā iti gamyate \| tasmāt viduṣaḥ api ā-sūtra-pātāt manaḥ-loka-antara-varti sūkṣmatvam \|\| 11 \|\|

punar api viduṣaḥ utkrāntiṃ saṃbhavatī iti āśaṅkya parihriyate—

## \|\| 4.2.12 \|\|

*pratiṣedhāt iti cet na śārīrāt spaṣṭaḥ hi ekeṣām*

**Комментарий:**

yat uktaṃ viduṣaḥ api utkrānti-sambhāveti; tat na upapadyate, viduṣaḥ utkrānti-niṣedhāt \| tathā hi sa eṣa tejo-mātrā-samabhyādadānaḥ hṛdayam eva anuvakrāmati hṛdi akṣasya ca etat pradyotena eva ātmā niṣkrāmati cakṣuṣaḥ kramantaṃ prāṇaḥ anukrāmati iti aviduṣaḥ utkrāti-prakāram abhidhāya akṣaya-vattaraṃ kalyāṇa-taraṃ rūpaṃ kurute iti dehāntara-parigrahaṃ ca abhidhāya śādhyantaṃ karmaṇaḥ tatsya yā kiñca iha karoti ayam, tasmāt lokāt punaḥ eti asmai lokāya karmaṇā iti tu kāmayamānaḥ iti avidhi-purvaṃ parismāpya atha akāmayamānaḥ yaḥ akāmaḥ niṣkāmaḥ āpta-kāmaḥ ātmā-kāmaḥ na tasya prāṇāḥ utkrāmanti brahma eva san brahma apyeti iti viduṣaḥ utkrāntiḥ pratiṣidhyate \| tathā pūrvatra ātma-mātra-spṛhe api viduṣaḥ utkrānti-pratiṣedhaḥ dṛśyate atha munre’yaṃ jayati iti vidvān prastutya yājñavalkya iti ha uvāca yatra ayaṃ puruṣaḥ mriyate ut asmāt prāṇāḥ krāmanti āho na iti pṛṣṭaḥ na iti ha uvāca yājñavalkyaḥ atra eva samavalīyante sa ucchvayati ādhmātaḥ mṛtaḥ śete iti \| ataḥ vidvat-niṣehā-mūlatvaṃ prāptaḥ utri iti cet \|\|

tatra \| śārīrāt-pratyag-ātmanaḥ prāṇānām ukrānti-niṣedhaḥ pratiṣidhyate, na śarīrāt \| na tasya prāṇāḥ utkrāmanti iti atra tat-śabdena atha akāmayamānaḥ iti prakṛta-śarīra eva parāmṛśyate; na amuktaṃ śarīrasya \| tasya iti ṣaṣṭhyā prāṇānāṃ sambandhitvena śārīraḥ nirdiṣṭaḥ; na tu utkrānti-apādānatvena \| utkrāntiḥ hi apādānaṃ tu śarīram eva iti cet, apādāna-anapekṣamāṇa-śrutāt śarīrāt saṃbandhitayā śrutasya ātmana eva saṃnihitatvena apādānatayā api grāhyatvāt \| kiñca prāṇānāṃ jīva-saṃbandhitvasya prajñātānāṃ tat-saṃbandhakatve prayojana-abhāvāt saṃbandha-mātra-abhidhāyyā ṣaṣṭhyā apādānam eva viśeṣaḥ iti niścīyate; yathā na tasya śrutam iti \| na ca atra vivakṣitavyaḥ; svayaṃ hi karṇau—mādhyandinānām āmnāye śārīraḥ jīva eva apādānam iti yaḥ akāmaḥ niṣkāmaḥ āpta-kāmaḥ ātmā-kāmaḥ na tasmāt prāṇāḥ utkrāmanti iti \| śārīrāt prāṇānām ukrānti-niṣedhaḥ tu

bhāvāti-viśeṣo na upapadyata iti cet, tasya tāvat eva ciram iti বিদuṣa-śarīra-viyoga-kāle brahma-saṃpatti-vacanena prāṇa-gati-abhāvi tasmin kāle śarīra-indriyayoḥ viyogaḥ prasajyate; tataḥ ca deva-yānena pathā brahma-saṃpattiḥ iti api asat iti na tasya prāṇāḥ utkrāmanti—deva-yānena pathā brahma-prāptiḥ śarīra-indriya-dvārā api prāṇā na viślathayanti iti ucyate \| ātaṃśāstrayoḥ api yadā vidur-viṣayaḥ; tadā ayam eva parihāraḥ \| sa tu avidur-viṣayaḥ, tatra prasakta-pratiṣedhena brahma-vidyā-praśaṃsā-darśanāt \| tatra hi grahāti-graha-dṛṣṭi-upacita-indriya-artha-saṃghātaḥ; apām anyatvam, cira-mātraṃ jīvasya prāṇa-aparityāgaḥ; mṛtasya nāma-rūpāni akīrtane caritam; tasya ca puṇya-pāpa-anupagā iti-ādiḥ iṣyate arthaḥ prasakta-pūrvaḥ prastutaḥ \| tatra ca atha punaḥ evaṃ jñāyogi iti apām ānanda-brahmatva-khyātā darśanīyaḥ eva mṛtyu-jayaḥ ucyate \| ataḥ na atra viduṣaḥ prasaṅgaḥ \| aviduṣaḥ tu prāṇa-utkrānti-vacanam, sthūla-dehavat tvaprāṇā na muñcanti, api tu bhūta-sūkṣmavatvāt jīvam pariṣvajya gacchanti iti pratipādayati iti niravadyam \|\| 12 \|\|

## \|\| 4.2.13 \|\|

*smaryate ca*

**Комментарий:**

smaryate ca viduṣaḥ api mūrdhanya-nāḍī-utkrāntiḥ: ūrdhvam ekaḥ sthitaḥ teṣām yaḥ bhittvā sūrya-maṇḍalam \| brahma-lokam atikramya tena yāti parām gatim \|\| iti \|\| 13 \|\| iti ātmādi-utkrānti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

**atha para-saṃpatti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.2.14 \|\|

*tāni pare tathā hi āha*

**Комментарий:**

sakala-śrutiḥ: sat-rūpaḥ karaṇa-adhyakṣaḥ pratyag-ātmā utkrānti-velāyām tejaḥ-prabhṛti-bhūta-sūkṣmeṣu saṃpadyata iti uktam \| saṃghā saṃpattiḥ viduṣaḥ na vidyate iti āha—isṭu paridṛśyatām \| tāni punaḥ jīva-pariṣvaktāni bhūta-sūkṣmāṇi kim yathā-karmam yathā-vidyam ca sva-kāryāya gacchanti, uta param-ātmani saṃpadyante iti viṣaye madhye param-ātma-saṃpattau sukha-duḥkha-upabhoga-rūpa-kārya-adarśanāt, tat-upabhoga-anuguṇena yathā-karmam yathā-vidyam ca gacchanti iti \|\| iti prāpte ucyate—tāni pare iti \| tāni pariṣvaṅgamāni saṃpadyante; kutaḥ? tathā hi āha śrutiḥ—tejaḥ parasyām devatāyām iti \| yathā ca śruti-siddha-anupapattiḥ kāryam kalpaniya-arthāḥ \| supti-pralayayoḥ asya param-ātma-saṃpattyā sukha-duḥkha-upabhogāya arthi-bhāvaḥ; tad-advihi api \|\| 14 \|\| iti para-saṃpatti-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha avibhāga-adhikaraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.2.15 \|\|

*avibhāgaḥ vacanāt*

**Комментарий:**

deyaṃ paramātmani saṃpattiḥ kiṃ prāk-ukta-laya-prakārāpatti-rūpā, uta śruty-ādi-ukta-dahara-ādi-avidyā-vedya-vibhāga-sthiti-cintālayāṃ paramātmanaḥ svarūpaṃ yogi-nirmūtatvākārāpatti-rūpā \| iti prāpte ucyate—avibhāga iti \| apṛthag-bhāvaḥ pṛthak-vyavahārānarhaḥ sarvāṃ hṛti-arthaḥ \| kutaḥ ? vacanāt—śaśvataḥ parastāṃ devatāyām iti atra api śrāvayukram iti saṃhṛta iti atsaṃpadyata iti vacanasya mukhyatvāt, tasya ca saṃsarga-viśeṣa-bādhitvāt, anupakarasya avidyā-vedyasya pramāṇābhāvāt, utkānti-velāyāṃ kāraṇāpatti-prayojanābhāvāt, tasya ca saṃsṛti-viśeṣa-cintāyāṃ anupakarteṣu avidyā-vedyatve pramāṇābhāvāt, utkānti-velāyāṃ kāraṇāpatti-prayojanābhāvāt punaḥ saṃpadyakādi-dṛśya-vacanāc ca \|\| 15 \|\| iti avibhāga-adhikaraṇam \|\|7\|\|

**atha tad-oko-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.2.16 \|\|

*tad-oko-agra-jvalanaṃ tat-prakāśita-dvāro vidyā-sāmarthyāt tac-cheṣa-gaty-anusmṛti-yogāc ca hārdānugṛhītāḥ śatādhikayā*

**Комментарий:**

evaṃ garbhūtkramā-vidhi-vicchinna-viduroṣa-samānākāra utkānti-prakāra uktaḥ; idānīṃ viduṣo viśeṣa ucyate \| tatra etam āmnāyate—śataṃ ca ekā ca hṛdayasya nāḍyaḥ tāsāṃ mūrdhānam abhiniḥsṛtā ekā \| tayā ūrdhvam āyan amṛtatvam eti viṣvaṅ anyā utkramaṇe bhavanti \|\| iti \| anyā nāḍīnāṃ śatādhikayā mūrdhanya-nāḍyā ca viduṣo gamanam, anyābhiḥ eva ca aviduṣo gamanam iti ayaṃ niyama upapadyate, na iti saṃśayaḥ \| kiṃ yuktam ? niyamo na upapadyata iti \| kutaḥ ? nāḍīnāṃ bhūyastvāt ati-dussambhavāc ca durvicetatayā puruṣeṇa upādātum aśakyatvāt \| śataśaḥ ūrdhvam ātram amṛtatvam eti viṣvaṅ anyā utkramaṇe bhavanti iti yāhacchiko mukti-nirmayuktateti muktir mati \| evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—śatādhikayā iti \| vidvān śatādhikayā mūrdhanyayā eva nāḍyā utkrāmati \| na ca asyāḥ viduṣo durvicetattvam; vidvān hi parama-puruṣārādhana-bhūtātayartha-priya-vidyā-sāmarthyāt vidyā-śeṣa-bhūtatayā asmato'rtha-prāpaṇānusmaraṇa-yogāc ca prasanne na hārdena parama-puruṣeṇa anugṛhīto bhavati; tataḥ ca tad ekaḥ-tasya-jīvasya sthānaṃ hṛdayam, agra-jvalanaṃ bhavati-asya jvalanaṃ prakāśanaṃ yasya, tad-hṛdaya-jvalanaṃ parama-puruṣa-prasāda-prakāśita-dvāro vidvān tāṃ nāḍīṃ vijānāti iti tayā viduṣo gatiḥ upapadyate \|\| 16 \|\|

iti tad-oko-adhikaraṇam \|\| 8 \|\|

**atha ramyā-anusāra-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.2.17 \|\|

*raśmi-anusārī*

**Комментарий:**

viduṣo hṛdyāc chotādhikayā mūrdhanya-nāḍyā nirgatasya hārdya-rasmīn anusṛtya āditya-maṇḍala-gatiḥ śrūyate—atha yatra etasmāc charīrāt utkrāmati atha ibha raśmibhiḥ eva ākramate iti \| tatra raśmi-anusāreṇa eva asya gati-niyamaḥ saṃbhavati, na iti cintāyām, niśi mṛtasya viduṣo raśmi-anusāra-saṃbhavāt aniyamaḥ \| vacanaṃ tu pakṣa-prāpti-viṣayam \| iti prāpte ucyate—raśmi-anusārī iti \| raśmi-anusārī eva vidvān mūrdhanya gacchati \| kutaḥ? atha ibha raśmibhiḥ eva ākramatā; pāda-ākrānte hi ākāro’rthakasya atvāt \| yat uktaṃ niśi mṛtasya raśmi-asaṃbhavāt asmānugṛhya gamanaṃ na upapadyata iti; tathā, niśi api sūrya-raśmi-anusāra-saṃbhavāt \| lakṣyate hi niśi api nidāgha-samaye uṣṇa-upalabdhyā raśmi-sadbhāvaḥ \| hemanta-ādau tu himābhibhavāt duḥsahaḥ iva eṣāṃ anupalambhaḥ \| śrūyate ca nāḍī-raśmīnām sarvadā-saṃyoga-vacanam—atha etayā mahāpathyā ātatam ubhau grāmau gacchati imaṃ ca amuṃ ca evam eva etā ādityasya raśmayaḥ ubhau lokau gacchanti imaṃ ca amuṃ ca amuṣmāt ādityāt pratāyante tā āsu nāḍīṣu sṛptāḥ ābhyo nāḍībhyaḥ pratāyante te’muṣmin āditye sṛptāḥ iti \| tasmāt niśi api raśmi-saṃbhavāt vidhi mṛtānām api viduṣāṃ raśmi-anusāreṇa eva brahma-prāptiḥ asti eva \|\| 17 \|\| iti ramyā-anusāra-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

**atha niśā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 10 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.2.18 \|\|

*niśi na iti cet na sambandhasya yāvat-deha-bhāvitvād darśayati ca*

**Комментарий:**

idam idānīṃ cintyate—viduṣo niśi mṛtasya brahma-prāptiḥ asti, na iti \| yadyapi niśāyāṃ sūrya-raśmi-saṃbhavāt raśmi-anusāreṇa gati-niṣpādanam api saṃbhavati; tathā api niśā-haraṇasya śāstreṣu garhitatvāt tat-samuyathaiḥ lakṣaṇa-brahma-prāptiḥ niśā-mṛtasya na saṃbhavati \| śāstraiḥ divā-maraṇaṃ praśastam, viparītaṃ niśā-maraṇam—divā ca śukla-pakṣaś ca uttarāyaṇam eva ca \| muhūrtāḥ praśastāni viparītaṃ tu garhitam \|\| iti \| divā-maraṇa-niyāmarṇayoḥ praśastatva-viparītatve ca uttara-mārga-hetuḥ na svātantrya \| ataḥ niśi maraṇam ayogiti-hetutvāt aśa brahma-prāpti-hetuḥ iti cet; tat na, viduṣaḥ karma-saṃbandhasya yāvat-deha-bhāvitvāt \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—anārabdha-kāryāṇām arthāṃ gati-hetu-bhūtānāṃ karmaṇāṃ vidyā-saṃbandhāt eva vināśaḥ anuttareṣāṃ ca ārabdha-pracāra-karmasya ca carama-deha-avadhitvāt dvibādha-hetv-abhāvāt viduṣo niśā-mṛtasya api brahma-prāpti-siddheḥ \| darśayati ca śrutiḥ tasya tāvat eva ciraṃ yāvat na vimokṣye atha saṃpatsye iti \| divā ca śukla-pakṣaḥ ca iti-ādi-vacanam anindita-hṛdayam \|\| 18 \|\| iti niśā-adhikaraṇam \|\| 10 \|\|

**atha dakṣiṇa-ayana-adhikaraṇam \|\| 11 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.2.19 \|\|

*ataḥ ca ayane api dakṣiṇe*

**Комментарий:**

niśi mṛtasya api viduṣaḥ brahma-prāptiḥ yaḥ hetuḥ uktaḥ; tataḥ eva hetoḥ dakṣiṇa-upakramaṇe mṛtasya brahma-prāptiḥ siddhā \|\| adhikāraḥ ca tu atha yaḥ dakṣiṇe pramīyate pitṝṇām eva mahimānam gatvā candramasaḥ sāyujyam gacchanti iti dakṣiṇa-ayane mṛtasya candra-prāpti-śravaṇāt candram prāptānām ca teṣām yadā tat paryavaiti atha ime eva ākāśam punaḥ nivartante iti punar-āvṛtti-śravaṇāt, bhīṣma-ādīnām ca brahma-vidyā-niṣṭhānām uttara-ayana-pratīkṣā-darśanāt dakṣiṇa-ayane mṛtasya brahma-prāptiḥ na sambhavati iti \| parihāraḥ tu—aviduṣām pitṛ-yāṇena pathā candram prāptānām eva punar-āvṛttiḥ; viduṣaḥ tu candram prāptasya api tasmāt brahmaṇaḥ mahimānam āpnoti iti vākya-upanyastasya dakṣiṇa-ayana-mṛtasya candra-prāptiḥ brahma-premataḥ viśrama-hetu-mātram iti gamyate \| vākya-upanyāsa-abhāve api pūrva-uktaḥ eva brahma-hetu-bhāvāt viduṣaḥ candram prāptasya api brahma-prāptiḥ avāryā \| bhīṣma-ādīnām yoga-prabhāvāt sva-cchanda-maraṇānām dharma-pravartanāya uttara-ayana-prārabdha-pradarśana-arthatvāt vicāraḥ \|\| 19 \|\| nanu ca viduṣaḥ mūrdhūnam prati punar-āvṛtti-apunar-āvṛtti-hetutvena kāla-viśeṣa-vidhiḥ iṣyate yatra kāle tu anāvṛttim āvṛttim ca eva yoginaḥ \| prayātā yānti tam kālam vakṣyāmi bharata-rṣabha \|\| agniḥ jyotiḥ ahaḥ śuklaḥ ṣaṭ-māsāḥ uttara-āyaṇam \| tatra prayātā gacchanti brahma brahma-vidaḥ janāḥ \|\| dhūmaḥ rātriḥ tathā kṛṣṇaḥ ṣaṭ-māsāḥ dakṣiṇa-ayanam \| tatra cāndramasam jyotiḥ yogī prāpya nivartate \|\| śukla-kṛṣṇe gatī hi ete jagataḥ śāśvate mate \| ekayā yāti anāvṛttim anyayā āvartate punaḥ \|\| iti \| tatra āha—

## \|\| 4.2.20 \|\|

*yoginaḥ prati smaryete smārte ca ete*

**Комментарий:**

na atra mūrdhūnām prati maraṇa-kāla-viśeṣa-upādānam smaryate; api tu yoginaḥ—yoga-niṣṭhān prati smārte-smṛti-viṣaya-bhūte-smṛti-ukte deva-yāna-pitṛ-yāṇa-ākhye gatī smaryete yoga-aṣṭāṅga-yukta-anudhānam smṛteḥ \| tathā hi u parāśaraḥ: na ete sṛtī pārtha jānan yogī muhyati kaścana \| tasmāt sarveṣu kāleṣu yoga-yuktaḥ bhava arjuna \|\| iti \| agniḥ jyotiḥ ahaḥ śuklaḥ dhūmaḥ rātriḥ iti ca deva-yāna-pitṛ-yāṇe pratyabhijñāyete \| upakrame ca yatra kāle iti kāla-śabdaḥ kāla-abhimāni-devatā-abhidhāna-paraḥ; anyathā adeḥ kālatvāt asaṃbhavāt \| ataḥ taiḥ idam brahma saṃbhavati iti vihita-devatā-anugṛhītatvam

vidyā-niyataṃ prati vidhīyate, na mumūrṣuṃ prati maraṇa-kāla-viśeṣaḥ \|\| 20 \|\| iti dahara-āgati-adhikaraṇam \|\| 11 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śrī-śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye caturtha-adhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**caturtha-adhyāye—tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ—arcir-ādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Pada 3

## \|\| 4.3.1 \|\|

*arcir-ādinā tat-prathiteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

viduṣa utkrāntasya nāḍī-viśeṣeṇa hārdānugṛhītayā raśmi-utkrama uktaḥ \| tasya gacchataḥ mārgam idānīṃ nirṇīyate \| tatra śrutayaḥ mārga-prakārān bahudhā āmnāyante \| chāndogye tāvat “atha yat u ca eva asmin śavyaṃ kurvanti yat u ca na arcim eva abhisambhavanti arcisaḥ ahaḥ aha āpūryamāṇa-pakṣam āpūryamāṇa-pakṣāt yān ṣaṇ-māsān udaṅ āditya eti māsebhyaḥ deva-lokaṃ deva-lokāt ādityam ādityāt candramasaṃ candramasaḥ vidyutaṃ tat-puruṣaḥ amānavaḥ sa enān brahma gamayati eṣa deva-pathaḥ brahma-pathaḥ” etena pratipadyamānāḥ imaṃ mānavam āvartaṃ na āvartante iti \| tathā pañcāgni-vidyāyāṃ raśmibhiḥ candramākramante iti \| kauṣītakinaḥ ca deva-yāna-mārgaṃ pratyapādyata “sa etaṃ deva-yānaṃ panthānam āpadya agni-lokam āgacchati sa vāyu-lokaṃ sa varuṇa-lokaṃ sa āditya-lokaṃ sa indra-lokaṃ sa prajāpati-lokaṃ sa brahma-lokam” iti \| tathā bṛhad-āraṇyake “sa evam eva etaiḥ hṛdayasya nāḍībhiḥ samanvita arcir abhisambhavanti arcisaḥ ahaḥ aha āpūryamāṇa-pakṣam āpūryamāṇa-pakṣāt yān ṣaṇ-māsān udaṅ āditya eti māsebhyaḥ deva-lokaṃ deva-lokāt ādityam ādityāt vaidyutaṃ vaidyutāt puruṣaḥ amānavaḥ taṃ brahma-lokān gamayati” iti \| tathā eva punar anyathā “yadā vai puruṣaḥ asmāt lokāt praiti sa vāyum āgacchati tasmai sa tatra vijihīte yathā ratha-cakrasya khaṃ tena sa ūrdhvam ākramate sa ādityam āgacchati tasmai sa tatra vijihīte yathā āḍambarasya khaṃ tena sa ūrdhvam ākramate sa candramasam āgacchati tasmai sa tatra vijihīte yathā dundubheḥ kham” iti-ādi \| tatra saṃśayaḥ—kim vidyā-bhedakeṣu eva mārgā āmniṣyantebhyaḥ pratipadyanta iti, teṣu eva brahma gacchati vidvān? uta tasminn andhasyaitra mārgī iti, taivoddhena vety aniyamaḥ? iti \| kiṃ yuktam? aniyama iti \| kutaḥ? aneka-rūpavān-antro-peśravaṇāc ca iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate—arcir-ādir iti \| arcir-ādika eva mārgaḥ sarvatra pratipādyate \| ataḥ arcir-ādinā eva gacchati \| kutaḥ? tat-prathiteḥ—tasya eva sarvatra prathiteḥ \| prasiddhiḥ, tasya eva sarvatra pratyabhijñānāt iti arthaḥ \| pratyabhijñātasya eva mārgasya kvacit adhika-praveśena prati-

pāśvat iti vidyā-guṇopasaṃhāra-vat yogānām antracopasaṃhāraḥ kriyate \| chāndogye tāvat upakosala-vidyāyāṃ pañcāgni-vidyāyāṃ ca etad-rūpa eva āmnāyate \| vājasaneyake ca pañcāgni-vidyāyāṃ tad-devārcir-ādiḥ alpānantara āmnāyate; atas tatra api sa eva iti pratīyate \| anyatra api sarveṣām arcir-ādi-sad-bhāvaḥ pratyabhijñāyate \|\| 1 \|\| iti arciṣṭādi-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|

**atha vāyu-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.3.2 \|\|

*vāyum abdāt aviśeṣa-viśeṣābhyām*

**Комментарий:**

arcir-ādinā eva gacchanti vidvāṃsaḥ iti anantaram \| tatra arcir-ādike mārge chandogyāḥ māsa-ādityayoḥ antarāle saṃvatsaram adhīyate—māsebhyaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ saṃvatsarāt ādityam iti \| vājasaneyinaḥ tu tayoḥ iva antarāle devalokaṃ māsebhyo devalokaṃ devalokāt ādityam iti \| ubhayatra api mārga-saikatvāt ubhayam api upasaṃhāryam \| tatra māsāt ūrdhvam abhihitayoḥ saṃvatsara-devalokayoḥ pañcamyā abhihitasya śruta-kramasya tulyatve api arcir-ādi-haitun āpūryamāṇa-pakṣam āpūryamāṇa-pakṣādyān ṣaḍ-udaṅṅ-eti māsāṃs tān iti adhika-kālānāṃ nyūna-kālasya uttarottaratvena niveśa-darśanāt saṃvatsarasya eva māsānantaraṃ buddho viparītaḥ saṃvatsara eva māsāt ūrdhvaṃ niveśayitavya iti tataḥ ūrdhvaṃ devaloka iti niścīyate \| anyathā vājasaneyinaḥ yadā vai puruṣaḥ asmāl lokāt preti sa vāyum āgacchati tasmai sa tatra vijihīte yathā ratha-cakrasya khaṃ tena sa ūrdhvam ākramate sa ādityam āgacchati iti ādityāt ūrdhvaṃ vāyum adhīyate \| kauṣītakinaḥ tu sa etaṃ devayānaṃ panthānam āpadya agni-lokam āgacchati sa vāyu-lokam iti agni-loka-śabda-nirdiṣṭārcir-viṣayaḥ paraṃ vāyum adhīyate \| tatra kauṣītakinaṃ pāṭha-krama-aviṣayaḥ para-darśena prāmasya vāyoḥ yasmipanto’nna sa ūrdhvam ākramate sa ādityam āgacchati iti artha-śabda-nirdiṣṭha-śrauta-krameṇa pāṭha-kramāt ubalīyasā ādityāt ūrdhvaṃ praveśo niścīyate \| ataḥ ādityāt ūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarāt ūrdhvaṃ devalokaḥ vāyuḥ ca prāptau \| tatra evaṃ vicāryate—kiṃ devalokaḥ vāyuḥ ca yathā-śruti-antara-sthau yatheṣṭa-krameṇa vijñāna-bhinna-cchedyaḥ, uta artha-antaratvena saṃvatsarāt ūrdhvaṃ devalokaḥ santaṃ vāyum abhigacchati iti \| kiṃ yuktam ? miśrārthatvam, prasiddheḥ \| miśrārthatve ca ekā-śabdena pañcamyā ca ubhayya-saṃvatsarāditya-antarāle śruti-kramaṇa prāptatvāt, viśeṣābhāvāt ca yatheṣṭam \|\| iti prāpta ucyate—vāyum abdāt iti \| vāyuṃ saṃvatsarāt ūrdhvam abhigacchet \| kutaḥ ? aviśeṣa-viśeṣābhyāṃ vāyoḥ eva nirdeṣṭatvāt \| devaloka-śabdaḥ hi aviśeṣeṇa—sāmānyena, devānāṃ lokaḥ iti anena rūpeṇa vāyum abhidhatte \| sa vāyum āgacchati tasmai sa tatra vāyu-śabdaḥ viśeṣeṇa vāyum abhidhatte \| ato devaloka-vāyu-śabdasthayor aviśeṣa-viśeṣābhyāṃ vāyuḥ eva abhidhīyata iti saṃvatsarāt ūrdhvaṃ vāyum eva abhigacchet \| kauṣītakinaṃ vāyu-loka-śabdaḥ ca agni-loka-śabda-vat vāyu-śrayo

loka-sthiti nyūnasya vāyu-vaividhyena \| vāyuḥ ca devānām āvāsaḥ iti yatra śrūyate śreyāṃsaḥ ca eva eva devānāṃ gṛhaḥ iti \|\| 2 \|\| iti vāyu-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha varuṇa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.3.3 \|\|

*taḍitaḥ adhi varuṇaḥ sambandhāt*

**Комментарий:**

kauṣītakinaḥ śrūyate evaṃ deva-yānaṃ panthānam āpadya agni-lokam āgacchati sa vāyu-lokaṃ sa varuṇa-lokaṃ sa āditya-lokaṃ sa indra-lokaṃ sa prajāpati-lokaṃ sa brahma-lokam iti agni-loka-śabda-sthāniḥ paṭhyaite prāpyam artha-vibhaktāsu \| vāyoḥ eva saṃvatsarāt ūrdhvaṃ niveśa uktaḥ \| ādityasya api atra prāpta-apaṭha-kramo 'taḥ śaiva-lokāditiṣv āditya-adhyupagati vāyu-niva-koṭy-ubhikramādiha-lokā-śabda-saṃhitimādhye 'pi ari niveśa-siddhaḥ \| idānīṃ varuṇa-indrādi-pu cintā \| kim ete varuṇa-ādayaḥ yathā-pāṭha vāyo-lokasyaṃ niveśayitavyāḥ, āho svid vidyut-lokāt iti viśaye, acira-dyutit sarveṣu arciṣaḥ ahaḥ iti-ādi-śruti-krama-uparodhāt vidyutaḥ parastāt ca śruti-puruṣaḥ anavasaraḥ eva iti brahma gamayati iti vidyut-puruṣasya brahma-abhiyuktatva-pravaṇāt ca saṃvatsara-ākāśābhyām anantara-pāṭhaḥ vā upadeśāvecyathiyavasyāṃ kasyacin madhye te pāṭha-krama-anurodhena vayoḥ antaraṃ varuṇaḥ niveśayitavyaḥ \| vākya-ādityayoḥ kramasya bādhitatvena indra-prajāpatī api dvāre eva niveśayitavyau \|\| iti prāpta ucyate—taḍitaḥ adhi varuṇa iti \| varuṇaḥ tāvat vidyutaḥ upariṣṭāt niveśayitavyaḥ \| kutaḥ ? sambandhāt—megha-udara-sthitayā vidyutaḥ varuṇena sambandhaḥ loka-vedayoḥ prasiddhaḥ \| evam uktaṃ bhavati—varuṇa-ādīnām upadeśāvepyathā api sarve 'rcir-ādi-niveśāntitve ca sati pāṭha-kramādya-kramasya balīyastvāt vidyutaḥ adhi varuṇaḥ niveśayitavyaḥ \| tatra śramānavastha garbha-vidyutaṃ vyavadhāna-saṃbhinna-yava-madhye \| tasya ca vyavadhāna-saṃbhavāt indra-diśeṣa-vidhura-āśrayatva-niveśayitavyasya varuṇāt dvaya-puditiṣṭhayā anantara-āmnāne niveśayitavyatvāt ca varuṇāt upari indrādi niveśayitavya iti \|\| 3 \|\| iti varuṇa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha ātivāhika-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.3.4 \|\|

*ātivāhikāḥ tat-liṅgāt*

**Комментарий:**

idam idānīṃ cintyate—kim arcir-ādayaḥ mārga-aṅga-bhūtāḥ, uta bhoga-bhūmayaḥ, athavā viśvataḥ brahma preṣyatām ātivāhikā iti \| kiṃ tāvat uktam ? mārga-stha-bhūtā iti \| kutaḥ ? upadeśasya tathā-vidhatvāt \| dṛśyate hi loke grāma-ādeśaṃ prati gantum aiśvaryiyo deśika-upadeśaḥ—itaḥ

niṣkramya mukhaṃ vṛkṣa-mukhaṃ nadī-mukhaṃ ca parvata-pārśvaṃ garbha-aṅkuraṃ grāmaṃ gaccha iti \| athavā, bhauma-bhūmaya eva asyuḥ, kāla-viśeṣa-vatyā pratiṣṭhitānām aharahīnau mārgau iti dakṣiṇāyana-prapatter ayasya ca mārga-cihita-sūtrasya etad-artha-siddhy-arthatvāt \| bhoga-bhūmiḥ ca eṣā eva lokaḥ yad dehorājyaraddhamāsā māsā ṛtavassaṃvatsarāḥ iti eva rakṣīnāṃ lokatva-vacanād upapadyate \| ata eva ca kauṣītakinaḥ agni-lokam āgacchati iti-ādinā loka-pada-vidhyānena arcir-ādīn paṭhanti \| evaṃ śrāte brahmaḥ—ātivāhikāḥ iti \| viduṣām arcir-ādi parama-puruṣeṇa niyuktāḥ ātivāhikāḥ devatā-viśeṣāḥ etad-viṣayāḥ \| kutaḥ ? tat-liṅgāt—ātivāha-liṅgāt \| ativahanaṃ hi gantṝṇāṃ gamayitṛtvam \| gamayitṛtvaṃ ca tat-puruṣaḥ amānavaḥ sa enān brahma gamayati iti upasaṃhāre śrūyamāṇe pūrva-grāma-paryāyogeṣu tātaṃ sa eva saṃbandhaḥ iti gamyate \| vadanti ca arcir-ādayaḥ śabdāḥ arcir-ādy-abhimānibhiḥ anidevatā-viśeṣān; taṃ pṛthag-vamūrtī iti-vat \|\| 4 \|\|

yadi evaṃ tat-puruṣaḥ amānavaḥ sa enān brahma gamayati iti vaidutena eva puruṣasya brahma-gamayitṛtvam atividhutaḥ pareṣāṃ varuṇa-ādīnāṃ katham ātivāhikatvena anvayaḥ ity atra āha—

## \|\| 4.3.5 \|\|

*vaidutena eva tataḥ tac-chruteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

tataḥ—vidyuta upari, vaidutena—amānavena eva ātivāhikena viduṣām ākṣa-brahma-prāptiḥ gamyate \| kutaḥ ? tad-gruteḥ; sa enān brahma gamayati iti tasya eva gamayitṛtva-śruteḥ \| varuṇa-ādi-vat svagrāhakā iti teṣām amānava-ātivāhikatvena anvayaḥ vidyata eva \|\| 5 \|\| iti ātivāhikādhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha kāryādhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.3.6 \|\|

*kāryaṃ bādarir asya-gati-upapatteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

arcir-ādinā gantavyaṃ vivādāḥ; arcir-ādi-mārgāvatarasya gaṇa ātivāhikau vivādaṃ brahma gamayati iti uktam \| idaṃ cintyate \| kim aparicchinna-diko gaṇaḥ kāryaṃ hiraṇya-garbhaṃ prāpnoti na athavā iti ? uta paraṃ eva brahmopāsīnām ? atha paraṃ brahmopāsīnām pratyag-ātmānaṃ brahmālamkatayā upāsīnām ? iti vicārye, kāryam upāsīnān eva gamayati iti bādariḥ ācāryaḥ manyate \| kutaḥ ? asya–hiraṇya-garbham upāsīnasya eva gati-upapatteḥ \| na hi paripūrṇaṃ sarveśaṃ sarvāntaṃ sarvātma-bhūtaṃ paraṃ brahmopāsīnasya tat-prāptaye deśāntara-gatiḥ upapadyate, prāptasya eva \| nitya-prāpta-para-brahma-viṣayā-vidyā-nivṛtti-mātreṇa eva hi para-vidyā-kāryam \| kāryaṃ tu hiraṇya-garbha-rūpaṃ brahmopāsīnasya paricchinna-deśa-vasthita-prāpya-antaryaṃ gamanam upapadyate \| ataḥ arcir-ādi-rativāhika-gaṇaḥ tam eva nayati \|\| 6 \|\|

## \|\| 4.3.7 \|\|

*viśeṣitatvāc ca*

**Комментарий:**

sūcye mānasa eva brahma-loka-āgamayati loka-sṛṣṭena dahatvena enaṃ ca loka-viśeṣa-varṇane hiraṇya-garbha-samupāsīnaṃ eva āgamayati viśeṣyate \| kiñca prajāpateḥ sa-bhī veśma prapadye iti kāryaṃ hiraṇya-garbhasya samīpam annaṃ cira-diṇā na ataḥ praty-artha-saṃbandhe \|\| 7 \|\|

nanu evaṃ tat-sukha-upāsīnasya eva anyat brahma gamayati iti ayaṃ nirdeśaḥ na upapadyate; hiraṇya-garbhasya eva hi sa etān brahma āyaṃ gamayati iti nirdeśaḥ syāt \| ata āha—

## \|\| 4.3.8 \|\|

*sāmīpyāt tu tad-vyapadeśaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

ye brahmāṇaṃ vidardhati iti hiraṇya-garbhasya prathama-jatvena brahma-sāmīpyāt tasya brahma-śabdena vyapadeśaḥ iti gamya-upāsana-viśeṣa-ādibhiḥ ahetubhiḥ cintyobhyaḥ iti arthaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

atha syāt—arcir-ādinā hiraṇya-garbha-śarīrī parama deva-pathī brahmatvaṃ etena pratipadyamānāḥ imaṃ mānavam āvartaṃ na āvartante tayoḥ ūrdhvaṃ āyan amṛtatvam eti iti amṛtatva-prāptyu-punar-āvṛtti-nirvyapadeśaḥ na upapadyate, hiraṇya-garbhasya kārya-bhūtasya hi dārārdha-kalā-avasāne vināśa-śāstrāt, ā-brahma-bhuvanāt lokāḥ punar-āvartinaḥ ’rjuna iti vacanāt ūrdhva-gami-śrāmya punar-āvṛtteḥ ca iti; atra āha—

## \|\| 4.3.9 \|\|
