*kāryātyaye tad-adhyakṣeṇa saha ataḥ param abhidhānāt*

**Комментарий:**

kāryasya brahma-lokasya atyaye tad-adhyakṣeṇa hiraṇya-garbheṇa adhikṛta-viśeṣa-avasita-adhikāreṇa viduṣā saha svayam api tat-pradhāna-viśeṣaḥ ataḥ—kāryāt brahma-lokāt paraṃ brahma prāpnoti iti, arcir-ādinā gatasya bhūta-prāpty-apunar-āvṛtti-abhidhānāt ye te brahma-loke tu parānta-kāle paramṛtāt parimucyanti sarve iti vacanāt ca avagamyate \|\| 9 \|\|

## \|\| 4.3.10 \|\|

*smṛteś ca*

**Комментарий:**

smṛteḥ ca ayam arthaḥ avagamyate—brahmaṇā saha te sarve saṃprāpte pratisañcare \| parasya ante kṛtātmānaḥ praviśanti paraṃ padam \|\| iti \| ataḥ kārya-upāsanā eva vicāra-adhikāro guṇo nayati iti bādariḥ etat \|\| 10 \|\|

atha jaiminiḥ pakṣāntaram upagṛhya pratyavatiṣṭhate—

**caturtha-adhyāye—tṛtīya-pāde—kārya-adhikaraṇam \|**

## \|\| 4.3.11 \|\|

*paraṃ jaiminir mukhyatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

paraṃ brahma-upāsīnān arcir-ādinā jyotir iti jaiminir ācāryo manyate \| kutaḥ ? mukhyatvāt—tat-puruṣaḥ amānavaḥ sa enān brahma gamayati brahma-śabdasya parasminn eva brahmaṇi mukhyatvāt \| pramāṇa-antareṇa kāryatva-niścaye saty eva hi lākṣaṇikatvaṃ yuktam \| na ca gamana-anupapattiḥ; pramāṇam, parasya brahmaṇaḥ sarvagatatve api viduṣo viśeṣa-deśāntara-vyāpti-viśiṣṭānidhyāna-niṣṭhita-śāstrāt \| yathā hi vidyā-utpatti-pūrṇa-āśrama-dharma-arcir-ādi-ādi-kāla-ādi-apekṣā tasmin veda-yukta-cetasādhikāra-mātravādhyate, tathā nirdeśa-vidyā-nivartanā-rūpa-vidyā-niṣpattir api viśiṣṭa-deśāntara-apekṣā iti gati-śāstrāt avagamyate \| viduṣa utkrānti-pratiṣedha-ādi tu pūrvam eva parihṛtam \| yat tu sa khalu lokān iti loka-śabda-bahuvacana-madhyo viśeṣaṇa-ākāṅkṣā-sūci hiraṇyagarbha-prāptir iti; tad yuktam, niṣāda-sthapati-nyāyena brahma eva lokā brahma-lokā iti karma-dhārayasya yuktatvāt, arthasya caitatte niścite bahuvacanasya aditiḥ pāśān iti-vad upapatteḥ \| parasya brahmaṇaḥ paripūrṇasya sarvātmanaḥ satya-saṅkalpasya svecchā-parigṛhītāḥ sthāna-adhāraṇā anavacchinnāś ca lokā na atyantāya na santi, śruti-smṛti-itihāsa-purāṇa-pramāṇyāt \|\| 11 \|\|

## \|\| 4.3.12 \|\|

*darśanāc ca*

**Комментарий:**

darśayati ca śrutiḥ mūrdhanya yā nāḍyā niṣkramya deva-yānena gatasya para-brahma-prāptiṃ evaṃ saṃprasādaḥ asmāt śarīrāt samutthāya paraṃ jyotir upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate iti \|\| 12 \|\|

yat uktaṃ prajāpateḥ sabhāṃ veśma prapadye iti arcir-ādinā gatasya kāryaṃ pratyabhisiddhiḥ syat iti; tatra uttaram—

## \|\| 4.3.13 \|\|

*na ca kārye pratyabhisandhiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

na ca ayaṃ pratyabhisandhiḥ kārye hiraṇyagarbhe; api tu parasminn eva brahmaṇi; vākya-artha-śeṣe śyāmaḥ ahaṃ bhavāmi brāhmaṇānām iti tasya abhisaṃdhāna-anusāra-vidyā-viśeṣa-kṛtatvasya tasmād abhisaṃdhānāt, aśva iva romāṇi vidhūya pāpaṃ candra iva rāhoḥ mukhāt pramucya \| dhūtvā śarīram akṛtaṃ kṛtātmā brahma-lokam abhisaṃbhavāmi \|\| iti abhisaṃbhavasya brahmalokaraṇa-kṛtatva-śravaṇāt, sarva-bandha-vinirmokasya ca mokṣa-śabda-śravaṇāt \| ataḥ param eva brahma-upāsīnam arcir-ādir ātivāhiko gaṇo nayati iti jaimineḥ matam \|\| 13 \|\|

idānīṃ bādarāyaṇas tu bhagavān sva-matena siddhāntam āha—

## \|\| 4.3.14 \|\|

*apratīka-ālambana-nayati iti bādarāyaṇaḥ ubhayathā ca doṣāt tatkratuḥ ca \| 4.3.14 \|*

**Комментарий:**

apratīka-ālambana-pratīka-ālambana-varjita-rūpaṃ nyartha-cintā-diṛṣṭi-vidhāyiko gaṇa iti bhagavān bādarāyaṇo manyate \| etat uktaṃ bhavati—kārya-upāsīnān nayati iti na ayaṃ pakṣaḥ saṃbhavati; param eva upāsīnān iti ayam api niyamo nāsti; na ca pratīka-ālambanān api nayati \| apitu ye paraṃ brahma upāsate, ye ca ātmānaṃ prakṛti-viyuktaṃ brahma-ātmakam upāsate; tān ubhā-vidyā-āśrayati \| ye tu brahma-kārya-antargūta-nāma-ādikaṃ vastu devatādivu siṃhādi-dṛṣṭi-yutaṃ brahma-dṛṣṭyā, kevalaṃ vā tat tad vastu upāsate, na tān nayati \| ataḥ paraṃ brahma-upāsīnām ātmānāṃ ca prakṛti-viyuktaṃ brahma-ātmakam upāsīnaṃś ca nayati iti \| kutaḥ? ubhayathā ca doṣāt \| kāryam upāsīnān nayati iti pakṣe asamācchārīratvam arthāya paraṃ jyotir upasaṃpadya iti-ādikaḥ śrutayaḥ prakupyeyuḥ \| param eva upāsīnān iti niyame sva iyaṃ viduryaṃ cemasraye śraddhā tapa iti upāsate te 'rcir abhi saṃbhavanti iti pañcāgni-vidyā 'rcir-ādi-śraṇī nayati iti śrutiḥ prakupyeta \| ataḥ ubhayasmin api pakṣe doṣa-sadbhāvāt \| tasmāt ubhaya-vidyā-āśrayati iti \| tat etat āha—tatkratuḥ ca iti \| tatkratuḥ—yathā-upāsīna-saṃśraya prāpto 'to 'rthaḥ; yathā-kratuḥ asmin loke puruṣo bhavati tathā itaḥ pretya bhavati taṃ yathā yathā upāsate iti nyāyāt, pañcāgni-vidopāy arcir-ādinā gati-śravaṇāt, arcir-ādinā gamasya brahma-prāpti-uttara-āvṛtti-śravaṇam ca \| ata eva tatkratungathāśrutyatiriviniyukti-brahmātmakam ānubhavaṃ siddham \| nāma-ādi-śravaṇy-antargatīka-ālambanānāṃ tu ubhaya-vidhā-śruti-siddhopāsanā-abhāvāt arcir-ādinā gatiḥ tu smṛtiḥ ca na vidyate \|\| 14 \|\|

tasmin viśeṣaṃ śrutiḥ eva darśayati iti āha—

## \|\| 4.3.15 \|\|

*viśeṣaṃ ca darśayati*

**Комментарий:**

yāvacchamī gataṃ tatrāsya yathā-kāma-cāro bhavati iti-ādikā śrutiḥ nāma-ādi-śravaṇa-paryanta-prataīkam upāsīnānāṃ gantavyeṣu parimita-phala-viśeṣaṃ ca darśayati \| tasmāt arciṇirśaṃ kevalaṃ vāhni-dṛṣṭu brahma-dṛṣṭyā tadīyena eva ya upāsate, na tān nayati; apitu paraṃ brahma-upāsīnām ātmānāṃ ca prakṛti-viyuktaṃ brahma-ātmakam upāsīnānām iti ādhiko gaṇo nayati iti siddham \|\| 15 \|\| iti kāryādhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye caturtha-adhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ

**caturtha-adhyāye—caturthaḥ pādaḥ—saṃpatti-vibhāva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|**

## Pada 4

## \|\| 4.4.1 \|\|

*saṃpatti-vibhāvena aśabdatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

paraṃ brahma-upāsīnānām ātmatāṃ ca prakṛti-viyuktaṃ brahma-ātmakam upāsīnānām arcir-ādinā mārga-upapanna-prāpti-lakṣaṇā gatiḥ uktā \| idānīṃ muktānām aiśvarya-prakāraṃ cintayitum ārabhate \| idam ānayate—evam eva eṣa saṃprasādaḥ asmāt śarīrāt samutthāya paraṃ jyotiḥ upasampadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate iti \| kim asmāt śarīrāt samutthāya paraṃ-jyotiḥ-upasampadya daiva-adhipatya-saṃbandhena rūpeṇa saṃbandhena vā vācyena pratipādyate, uta svābhāvikasya svarūpasya āvirbhāvaḥ iti saṃśaye, sādhyena rūpeṇa saṃbandhaḥ iti yuktam \| anyathā hi apuruṣārtha-viśeṣatve mokṣa-śāstrasya syāt, svarūpasya svataḥ apuruṣārthatva-darśanāt \| na hi suṣuptau deha-indriya-vyāpāra-uparatau kevalasya ātma-svarūpasya puruṣārtha-saṃbandhaḥ dṛśyate \| na ca duḥkha-nivṛtti-mātraṃ paraṃ jyotiḥ-upasampannasya puruṣārthaḥ, yena svarūpa-āvirbhāvaḥ eva mokṣaḥ iṣyate; saḥ ekaḥ brahmaṇaḥ ānandaḥ, śrotriyasya ca akāma-hatasya rasaṃ hi eva ayaṃ labdhvā ānandī bhavati iti-ādi-śrutyaḥ muktasya sukha-antaratva-śravaṇāt \| na vā aparicchinna-ānanda-rūpa-caitanya-mātrasya svarūpam, tat ca saṃsāra-daśāyām avidyayā tirohitaṃ paraṃ-jyotiḥ-rūpa-saṃbandhāt āvirbhavati iti jñeyaṃ vaktum; jñāna-svarūpasya virodhāt asaṃbhavāt \| prakāśa-rūpasya jñānasya tirobhāvaṃ tad-vimokṣaḥ eva iti hi pūrvam eva uktam \| na vā prakāśa-mātrasya ānanda-tā saṃbhavati \| sukha-duḥkha-rūpatā jñāna-ānanda-rūpatā; sukha-duḥkhatvaṃ ca ātmanaḥ anukūlatvam \| prakāśa-mātrasya asvādinīḥ kathaṃ prakāśaḥ anukūla-vedanīyaḥ bhavet iti prakāśa-mātrasya asvādinīḥ kathaṃcit api ānanda-rūpatā duṣpapādā \| svarūpa-āpti-mātre ca sādhye svarūpasya nitya-niṣpannatvāt upasaṃbandhasya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyata iti vacanam anarthakaṃ syāt \| ataḥ apūrvaṃ sādhyena rūpeṇa saṃpadyate \| evaṃ ca abhiniṣpadyate iti vacanaṃ mukhya-artham eva bhavati \| svena rūpeṇa iti avyānandakena sva-ātma-sādhāraṇena abhiniṣpadyata iti suyuktam iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pracakṣmahe—saṃpatti-vibhāvaḥ iti \| ayaṃ pratyag-ātmā arcir-ādinā paraṃ-jyotiḥ-rūpa-saṃbandhaṃ daśā-viśeṣam āpadyate, saḥ svarūpa-āvirbhāva-rūpaḥ; na apūrva-ākāra-antara-lābhaḥ \| kutaḥ ? svena śabdāt—svena rūpeṇa iti viśeṣaṇa-upādānāt dhi arthaḥ \| āgantuka-viśeṣa-paratve hi svena rūpeṇa iti viśeṣaṇam anarthakaṃ syāt, aviśeṣaṇe api tasya svakīya-rūpatva-siddheḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

yat tu uktaṃ svarūpasya nitya-prāptatvāt upasaṃpadya abhiniṣpadyate iti vacanam anarthakam iti, tatra uttaram—

## \|\| 4.4.2 \|\|

*muktaḥ pratijñānāt*

**Комментарий:**

karma-saṃbandhāt kṛta-dehādi-vidhiḥ muktaḥ svābhāvika-brahma-guṇāvasthitaḥ atra śrutena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyata iti ucyate \| ataḥ nitya-prāptasya api svarūpasya karma-rūpāvidyā-tirohitasya tirodhāna-nivṛttiḥ eva abhiniṣpattiḥ ucyate \| kutaḥ? pratijñānāt—yā hi pratipādyatayā pratijñātā \| kutaḥ idam avagamyate? ya ātmā iti prakṛtaṃ pratyag-ātmānaṃ jāgaritādi-avasthā-traya-vyavidhi-muktaṃ;

priyāpriya-hetu-bhūta-karma-saṃbandha-tirodha-vinirmuktaṃ ca pratipādayitum etaṃ tu eva te bhūyaḥ anuvyākhyāsyāmi iti punaḥ-punaḥ akhyāya evam eva eṣa saṃprasādaḥ asmāt śarīrāt samutthāya paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate iti abhidhānāt \| ataḥ karmaṇā saṃbaddhasya paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya bandha-nivṛtti-rūpā muktiḥ svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpattiḥ ucyate \| ratha-haraṇa-ābhimukhyam abhinirvṛtta-pūrva-nikaṭaḥ hriyate, śukasya ākāśaḥ niṣpadyata iti ādi-vat \|\| 2 \|\|

granthaḥ uktiḥ—ātmanaḥ svarūpasya suṣuptau apuruṣārtha-darśanāt saṃsāratvaṃ bhāve mokṣa-śāstrasya apuruṣārthatva-bodhitvaṃ syāt iti kṛtvā devādi-avasthā-avasadṛśa-bandhya-avasthāntara-sādhitā iti vinirūpitaḥ iti; tatra uttaram—

## \|\| 4.4.3 \|\|

*ātmā prakaraṇāt*

**Комментарий:**

svarūpeṇa eva ayam ātmā apahata-pāpmatvādi-satya-saṃkalpatva-paryanta-guṇakaḥ prakaraṇāt avagamyate; ya ātmā apahata-pāpmā vijaraḥ vimṛtyuḥ viśokaḥ ajighatsaḥ apipāsaḥ satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṃkalpaḥ iti hi prajāpati-vākya-upakramaḥ \| idaṃ ca prakaraṇaṃ pratyag-ātma-svarūpa-sthitiḥ uttaratra ca etādṛśaṃ svarūpam astu iti atra pratipāditam \| ataḥ apahata-pāpmatvādi-svarūpaḥ eva ayam ātmā saṃsāra-daśāyāṃ karma-avidyā-tirohita-svarūpaḥ paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya viśruta-svarūpaḥ bhavati \| ataḥ pratyag-ātmanaḥ apahata-pāpmatvādayaḥ svābhāvikā guṇāḥ paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya viśvam eva eti; na utpadyante \| yathoktaṃ bhagavatā śaunakena api—yathā na kriyate jyotsnā mala-prakṣālanāt maṇeḥ \| doṣa-prahāṇāt na jñānam ātmanaḥ kriyate tathā \|\| yathā udapāna-karaṇāt kriyate na jala-ambaram \| sat eva nīyate vyaktim asataḥ saṃbhavaḥ kutaḥ \|\| tathā heya-guṇa-dhvaṃsāt avabodhādayaḥ guṇāḥ \| prakāśyante na janyante nityāḥ eva ātmanaḥ hi te \|\| iti \| ataḥ jñānānandādi-guṇānāṃ karmaṇā ātmani saṃkucitatāṃ paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya karma-rūpa-bandha-kṣaye vikāsa-rūpāviṣkāraḥ na apūrvaḥ sa iti śuddhaṃ saṃdhyā-divat iti \|\| 3 \|\| iti sampadya-āvirbhāva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha avibhāgena dṛṣṭatva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.4.4 \|\|

*avibhāgena dṛṣṭatvāt*

**Комментарий:**

kim ayaṃ paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya sarva-bandha-vinirmuktaḥ pratyag-ātmā svātmānaṃ paramātmanaḥ pṛthag-bhūtam anubhavati, uta tat-svarūpatayā tad-vibhaktim iti viśaye, “sa ya eṣaḥ aṇimā etad-ātmyaṃ sarvaṃ tat satyaṃ sa ātmā tat tvam asi śvetaketo” “brahmaṇaḥ viprascitā” “yadā paśyaḥ paśyate rukma-varṇaṃ kartāram īśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahma-yonim \| tadā vidvān puṇya-pāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyam upaiti \|\|” “idaṃ jñānam upāśritya mama sādharmyam āgatāḥ \| sarge api na upajāyante pralaye na vyathanti ca \|\|” ity-ādi-śruti-smṛtibhyaḥ muktasya pareṇa sāhitya-sāmya-sādharmya-āvimātraṃ pṛthag-bhūtam anubhavati \|\|

iti prāpta ucyate—avibhāgena iti \| paramātma-lakṣaṇaḥ svātmānaṃ vibhāgena anubhavati muktaḥ \| kutaḥ ? dṛṣṭatvāt—para-brahma-upasaṃpattyā nighūta-avidyā-tirodhānasya yathātathyena svātmanaḥ dṛṣṭatvāt \| svātmanaḥ svarūpaṃ hi “satyam jñānam anantaṃ brahma” “ānandātma brahma” “aitadātmyam idaṃ sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma” ity-ādi-sāmānādhikaraṇya-nirdeśaiḥ “ya ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmanaḥ antaraḥ yam ātmā na veda yasya ātmā śarīraṃ ya ātmānam antaraḥ yamayati sa ta ātmā antaryāmī amṛtaḥ” “ataḥ praviṣṭaḥ śāstā janatānāṃ sarveṣām” ity-ādi-viśva paramātma-ātmakaṃ tac-charīratayā tat-prakāram abhūtam iti pratipāditam “ayaṃ avasthiteḥ iti kāśakṛtsnaḥ” ity atra \| ataḥ vibhāgena ahaṃ brahma asmi iti eva anubhavati \| sāmya-sādharmya-upapadeko brahma-prakāra-bhūtasya eva pratyag-ātmanaḥ svarūpaṃ tat-sammiti veda-vid-grāhyatva-upalakṣaṇe brahma-sāmānādhurddhe pratipādayati \| saha śruti-artha-bhūtasya pratyag-ātmanaḥ prakāriṇā brahmaṇā saha tad-upānabhāvaṃ pratipādayati iti na kaścit virodhaḥ \| brahma-prakāratayā tad-vibhāga-uktiḥ “saṅkalpāt eva tat tu, tat-śruteḥ” ity-ādi na virudhyate, “adhikaṃ tu bheda-nirdeśāt” “adhika-upadeśāt” ity-ādi ca \|\| 4 \|\| iti avibhāgena dṛṣṭatva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|

**atha brāhma-adhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.4.5 \|\|

*brāhmeṇa jaiminiḥ upanyāsa-ādibhyaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

pratyag-ātmanaḥ paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya nighūta-tirodhānasya svarūpa-āvirbhāva eva etatvam \| tatra yena svarūpeṇa ayam āvirbhavati; tat-svarūpaṃ śruti-viśuddhyā nirūpyate \| kim apahata-pāpāmatva-ādikam eva asya svarūpam iti tena rūpeṇa ayam āvirbhavati, uta vijñāna-mātram eva iti tena rūpeṇa, atha ubhaya-virodha iti ubhaya-rūpeṇa iti \| “kiṃ tāvat prāptam ? brāhmeṇa iti jaiminiḥ ācāryo manyate \| brāhmeṇa—apahata-pāpmatvādinā iti arthaḥ \| apahata-pāpmatvādayo hi dahara-vākye brahma-saṃbandhitayā śrutāḥ \| brāhmeṇa iti kutra avagamyate ? upanyāsa-ādibhyaḥ; upanyasyate hi brahma-guṇāḥ apahata-pāpmatvādayaḥ pratyag-ātmanaḥ api prajāpati-vākye “ya ātmā apahata-pāpmā vijaro vimṛtyuḥ”

iti asūtreṇa \| aviśeṣād bodhena satya-sadṛśya-upapattiḥ viṣṇu-guṇayoḥ jagaj-janma-ādyaiḥ lakṣaṇa-ādyaiḥ lakṣakaḥ brahma-pramāṇam iti-ādi-doṣa-apagatā vyavahārāḥ gṛhyante \| atra eṣaḥ upanyāsa-ādikaḥ pratyag-ātmanaḥ vijñāna-mātra-svarūpatvaṃ na saṃbhavati iti jaimini-matam \|\| 5 \|\|

## \|\| 4.4.6 \|\|

*citi-tan-mātreṇa tad-ātmakatvāt iti auḍulomiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

caitanya-mātram eva asya svarūpam iti tena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpattiḥ iti auḍulomiḥ ācāryo manyate \| kutaḥ ? tad-ātmakatvāt—tāvan-mātra-ātmakatvād asya pratyag-ātmanaḥ \| sa yathā saindhava-ghanaḥ anantaraḥ abāhyaḥ kṛtsnaḥ rasa-ghanaḥ eva evaṃ vā are ayam ātmā anantaraḥ abāhyaḥ kṛtsnaḥ prajñāna-ghanaḥ eva iti vijñāna-ghanaḥ eva iti avadhāraṇāt vijñāna-mātram eva asya svarūpam iti avagamyate \| ataḥ asya guṇāntara-abhāvāt apahata-pāpmatva-ādi-śabdāḥ vikāra-subha-gṛhīta-avidyā-dharmāṇāṃ vyāvṛtti-parāḥ iti citi-tan-mātra-rūpeṇa abhiniṣpattiḥ iti auḍulomiḥ matam \|\| 6 \|\|

saṃprati bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ sva-matena siddhāntam āha—

## \|\| 4.4.7 \|\|

*evam api upanyāsāt pūrva-bhāvād avirodhaṃ bādarāyaṇaḥ*

**Комментарий:**

evam api—vijñāna-mātra-svarūpatvasya anupapattau sati api, satya-kāmatva-ādīnāṃ pūrvoktānāṃ guṇānām avirodhaṃ bādarāyaṇa ācāryo manyate \| kutaḥ ? upanyāsāt pūrva-bhāvāt—aupanisadatve ātma-svarūpam iti asya-ādi-upanyāsāt pramāṇāt pūrva-bhāvaḥ apahata-pāpmatva-satya-kāmatva-ādīnām api bhāvāt—vidyamānatvāt \| tulya-pramāṇa-jñātam itaretara-virodhe na yujyate iti arthaḥ \| na ca vastu-virodhād apahata-pāpmatva-ādīnām avidyā-parikalpitatvaṃ nyāyyam; viśeṣa-abhāvāt viparītaṃ kiṃ mātraṃ bhavati iti nyāyāt \| tulya-balatve hi ubhayasya api adhāraṇyāca-paratvam eva nyāyyam \| brahma-virodhaḥ iti asya padārthasya vaktuṃ jñāna-mātra-vedyatvaṃ svarūpaṃ tāvatkṛtiśvasitīyam arham \| jñāna-ghanaḥ eva iti-ādibhiḥ niṣpratiṣidhyata iti manyate \| kathaṃ hi vijñāna-ghanaḥ eva iti avadhāraṇa-śabdāḥ ? rasa-udghātasya jaḍatva-avyavaccheda-prakārāḥ; na anyāyatta-prakāśaḥ svarūpaḥ api pradeśaḥ asti iti ayam arthaḥ kiṃ cid eva mukhaḥ \| sa yathā saindhava-ghanaḥ anantaraḥ abāhyaḥ kṛtsnaḥ rasa-ghanaḥ eva evaṃ vā are ayam ātmā anantaraḥ abāhyaḥ kṛtsnaḥ prajñāna-ghanaḥ eva iti \| na evaṃ pratyag-ātmanaḥ dharmitva-svarūpasya kṛtsnasya jñāna-ghanatve api apahata-pāpmatva-satya-saṃkalpatva-ādi-vidharmi-śaṃbandho bādhyate, yathā saindhavanasya kṛtsnasya rasa-ghanatve rasana-indriya-grāhyatve ca akṣur-agrāhyatāḥ rūpa-kāṭhinya-ādayo na bādhyante \| idam atra vākya-tātparyam—yathā rasa-vatvāt cakṣur-ādi-viśeṣeṇa rasa-hetā api saindhava-ghanasya sarvaṃ ca eka-rasatvam; tathā ātmanaḥ api sarvaṃ vijñāna-svarūpatvam—svaprakāśaṃ hilvaṃ nityam iti arthaḥ \|\| 7 \|\| iti brāhmādhikaraṇam \|\| 3 \|\|

**atha saṅkalpa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.4.8 \|\|

*saṅkalpāt eva tat-śruteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

muktaḥ paraṃ brahma upasaṃpadya jñānatva-brahmopa-rūpāparāmṛtatvādi-satya-saṅkalpatva-paryanta-nirguṇaka āviṣkṛtāṣṭa-guṇaḥ, tasmiṃś ca satya-saṅkalpatva-śrutau vyavahāraḥ śrūyate sa tatra paryeti jakṣat krīḍan ramamāṇaḥ strībhiḥ vā yānaiḥ vā jñātibhiḥ vā iti \| kim asya jñānādi-vṛddhiḥ prayatnāntara-sāpekṣā, uta parama-puruṣaḥ eva saṅkalpa-mātrāt eva bhavati iti viśaye; loke rājādīnāṃ satya-saṅkalpatā iva vyavahṛti-pramāṇānāṃ kārya-niṣpadane prayatnāntara-sāpekṣatva-darśanāt asya api tat-sāpekṣā \| iti prāpte ucyate—saṅkalpāt eva iti \| kutaḥ? tat-śruteḥ—sa yadi pitṛ-loka-kāmaḥ bhavati saṅkalpāt eva asya pitaraḥ samuttiṣṭhanti iti hi saṅkalpāt eva pitṛ-ādīnāṃ samutthānaṃ śrūyate \| na ca prayatnāntara-sāpekṣatābhidhāyikā śrutyantaraṃ dṛśyate; yena asya saṅkalpāt eva iti avadhāraṇasya vijñāna-ghanaḥ eva iti vat vyavasthāpanaṃ kriyate \|\| 8 \|\|

## \|\| 4.4.9 \|\|

*ata eva ca ananya-adhipatiḥ*

**Комментарий:**

yataḥ muktasya satya-saṅkalpaḥ; ata eva ananya-adhipatiḥ ca \| anya-adhipatitvaṃ hi vividha-viṣaya-yogyasya \| vidhi-viṣaya-yogye hi pratiṣiddha-saṅkalpatvaṃ bhavet \| atas satya-saṅkalpatva-śrutyā vā ananya-adhipatitvaṃ ca siddham \| ata eva sa svarāṭ bhavati iti ucyate \|\| 9 \|\|

iti saṅkalpa-adhikaraṇam \|\| 4 \|\|

**atha abhāva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.4.10 \|\|

*abhāvaṃ bādariḥ āha hi evam*

**Комментарий:**

kiṃ muktasya deha-indriyāṇi na santi, uta santi, atha vā yathā-saṅkalpaṃ santi na santi iti viśaye; śarīra-indriyāṇām abhāvaṃ bādariḥ ācāryaḥ manyate \| kutaḥ? āha hi evam—na ha vai sa-śarīrasya sataḥ priya-apriyayoḥ apahatiḥ asti, aśarīraṃ vā va santaṃ na priya-apriye spṛśataḥ iti śarīra-saṃbandhe duḥkhasya avarjanīyatvam abhidhāya aśarīrāt samutthāya paraṃ jyotiḥ upasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇa abhiniṣpadyate iti muktasya aśarīratvaṃ hi āha śrutiḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## \|\| 4.4.11 \|\|

*bhāvaṃ jaiminiḥ vikalpa-amananāt*

**Комментарий:**

muktasya śarīra-indriya-bhāvaṃ jaiminiḥ ācāryaḥ manyate \| kutaḥ? vikalpa-amananāt—vividhāḥ kalpaḥ vikalpaḥ; vaividhyābhinayārthaḥ; sa ekadhā bhavati tridhā bhavati, pañcadhā saptadhā iti-ādi-śruteḥ \| āsan eka-sthāne api adhyāna-eka-thā-bhāva-asaṃbhavāt tridhā-bhāvādayaḥ śarīra-indraya-kṛtā iti avagamyate \| aśarīra-saṃvacanaṃ tu karma-nirmita-śarīrābhāva-param; tat eva hi śarīraṃ priya-apriya-hetuḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

**bhagavān tu bādarāyaṇaḥ sva-matena siddhāntam āha—**

## \|\| 4.4.12 \|\|

*dvādaśāha-vat ubhaya-vidhaṃ bādarāyaṇaḥ ataḥ*

**Комментарий:**

saḍḍūparyāvedayitena tat-śabdena parama-puruṣe; ata eva saṅkalpa-mātra, ubhaya-vidhaṃ sa-śarīram aśarīraṃ ca muktaṃ bhagavān bādarāyaṇo manyate \| pūrva-pakṣī śruti-rūpa-nyaste \| dvādaśāha-vat—yathā dvādaśāha-ṛddhi-kāmā upayuḥ; dvādaśāhena prajā-kāmaṃ yājayeta iti upaniṣat-karma-niyatana-tvayā saṅkalpena eva sa-śarīraṃ ca bhavati \|\| 12 \|\| yadā śarīrādi-upakaraṇa-vattvam, tadā tāni śarīrādi-upakaraṇāni svamena eva sṛjānīti nāsti niyama iti āha—

## \|\| 4.4.13 \|\|

*tanu-abhāve sandhyā-vat upapatteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

svena eva sṛjantu mukhya-upakaraṇa-abhāve parama-puruṣa-sṛṣṭaiḥ upakaraṇaiḥ bhoga-upapatteḥ; satya-saṅkalpaḥ api svayaṃ na sṛjati \| yathā svapne atha rathān ratha-yogān pathaḥ sṛjate iti ārasya atha veśma-antān puṣkariṇyaḥ sravantyaḥ sṛjate sa hi kartā iti, ya eṣu supteṣu jāgarti kāmaṃ kāmaṃ puruṣo nirmimāṇaḥ, tat eva śukraṃ tat brahma tat eva amṛtam ucyate, tasmin lokāḥ śritāḥ sarve tat u na atyeti kaścana iti īśvara-sṛṣṭaiḥ rathādi-upakaraṇa-jīvino bhuṅkte; tathā muktaḥ api līlā-rasa-vṛtti-netrāśreṇa sṛṣṭaiḥ pittṛ-lokādibhiḥ līlā-rasaiḥ bhuṅkte \|\| 13 \|\|

## \|\| 4.4.14 \|\|

*bhāve jāgrat-vat*

**Комментарий:**

sva-saṅkalpāt eva sṛṣṭantu muktasya pitṛ-lokādi-upakaraṇa-abhāve ātmānamuśāyāvaka-muktaḥ api līlā-rasaiḥ bhuṅkte; parama-puruṣaḥ api līlākhyaṃ dārāyatha-buddhyāvidhā-pitṛ-lokādikam ātmanaḥ sṛṣṭvā taimenupyaman-līlā-rasai yathā bhuṅkte; tathā muktānām api sva-līlāyaṃ pitṛ-lokādikaṃ svayam eva sṛjan karoti iti; kadācit ca muktāḥ satya-saṅkalpatva-labdha-pramukha-līlā-ananta-ānandita-prati-lokādikaṃ svayam eva sṛjanti iti saṃbabhūva ca \|\| 14 \|\| nanu lokānām upari-māṇa icchuktaḥ; katham aneika-kāra-utkṣepa-karaṇena ratna-giri-pāta-sambhava iti atra āha—

## \|\| 4.4.15 \|\|

*pradīpa-vat āveśaḥ tathā hi darśayati*

**Комментарий:**

yathā pradīpasya ekasya eva deśe vartamānasya sva-prabhayā deśāntara-āveśaḥ; tathā anantyādveśeṣv eva sva-prabhā-rūpeṇa caitanyena sarveśāṃtārāveśo na anupapannaḥ \| yathā candramasaḥ api dehe hṛdayāvekṣmardeśa-vartino api caitanya-vyāptyā sarvasmin dehe ātmābhimānaḥ; tad-vat \| dhyāna-viśeṣa-amuktasya karmaṇā saṅkucita-caitanyasya dehāntareṣu ātmābhimāna-anuguṇā vyāptir na saṃbhavati; muktasya

tvaṃ saṃkūcita-jñānatva yathā-aṇḍa-ḍimbha-mātra-abhimāna-tad-guṇā vyāptiḥ dharma iti grahaṇa-anuguṇā ca na anupapannā \| tathā hi darśayati bālāgra-śata-bhāgasya śatadhā kalpitasya ca \| bhāgo jīvasya vijñeyaḥ sācantyāya kalpate \|\| iti \| anuktasya karma-niyāmakaḥ; muktasya tu sva-icchā iti viśeṣaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

nanu paraṃ brahma prāpyatvena antar-āditya-sthāna-lāpe darśita śrutiḥ prājñena ātmanā saṃpariṣvakto na bāhyaṃ kiñcana veda na antaram iti; tat kathaṃ muktasya sārvajñyam ucyate ? tatra uttaram—

## \|\| 4.4.16 \|\|

*svāpyaya-saṃpattyoḥ anyatara-apekṣam āviṣkṛtaṃ hi*

**Комментарий:**

veda-vacanaṃ mukta-viṣayam; avidita svāpyaya-saṃpattyoḥ anyatara-apekṣam \| svāpyayaḥ—suṣuptiḥ; saṃpattiś ca—maraṇam, vāṅ manasi saṃpadyate iti ārasya tejaḥ parasyāṃ devatāyām iti vacanāt \| tayoḥ ātmāpyayoḥ prājña-prāpti-nirsaṃbodhatvaṃ ca vidyate \| anatas tayor anyatara-apekṣam idaṃ vacanam \| suṣuṅni-maraṇayoḥ nirsaṃbodhatvaṃ, muktasya ca sarva-jñatvam āviṣkṛtaṃ hi anyā nāha khalu ayam evaṃ saṃprati ātmānaṃ jānāyaṃ aham asmi iti no eva imāni bhūtāni vināśam eva āpīto bhavati na aham atra bhogyaṃ paśyāmi iti suṣupti-velāyāṃ nirsaṃbodhatvam uktvā tasmin eva vākye mukta-sarva-dhṛtatvaṃ sa vā eṣa dṛṣṭena cakṣuṣā manasā etān kāmān paśyan ramate ya ete brahma-loke iti sarva-jñatvam ucyate \| tathā sarvaṃ ha paśyaḥ paśyati sarvam āpnoti sarvaśaḥ iti ca spaṣṭam eva sarva-jñatvam ucyate \| tathā maraṇe ca nirsaṃbodhatvaṃ pṛthivyo bhūtebhyaḥ samutthāya tāni eva anu vinaśyati iti uktam \| vinaśyati—na paśyati iti arthaḥ \| ataḥ prājñena ātmanā iti vacanaṃ svāpyaya-saṃpattyoḥ anyatara-apekṣam \|\| 16 \|\| iti abhāva-adhikaraṇam \|\| 5 \|\|

**atha jagad-vyāpāra-varja-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|**

## \|\| 4.4.17 \|\|

*jagad-vyāpāra-varjaṃ prakaraṇāt asaṃnihitatvāc ca*

**Комментарий:**

kiṃ muktasya aiśvaryaṃ jagad-utpatti-ādi parama-puruṣa-sādhāraṇaṃ sarveśvaratvam api, uta tad-rahitaṃ kevala-parama-puruṣa-anubhava-mātra-viṣayam iti saṃśayaḥ \| kiṃ yuktam ? jagad-īśvaratvam api iti \| kutaḥ ? nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyam upaiti iti parama-puruṣeṇa parama-sāmya-āpatti-śruteḥ, sa-cara-kūṭasthasya eva \| na hi parama-sāmyasya tadaṃśa-kūṭatve sarveśvara-sādhāraṇa-jagat-triyamāneṇa vinā upapadyate \| atas tasya tadaṃśa-kūṭa-parama-sāmya-upapattyai samasta-jagat-niyamana-rūpam api muktasya aiśvaryam iti \|\|

evaṃ prāpte pratyakṣam āha—jagad-vyāpāra-varjam iti \| jagad-vyāpāraḥ—nikhila-cetāna-acetana-svarūpa-sthiti-pravṛtti-bheda-niyamanam \| tad-varjaṃ nirasta-samasta-nikhila-tarodhasya nitya-jijñāsā-anubhava-rūpaṃ

mukta-sambandham \| kutaḥ ? prakaraṇāt—nikhila-jagat-niyamane hi paraṃ brahma prakṛta-ātma-gatayā jyotiḥ vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante, yena jātāni jīvanti, yat prayanti abhisaṃviśanti, tat vijijñāsasva tat brahma iti \| yadā nikhila-jagat-niyamane muktānām api sādhāraṇaṃ syāt, tataḥ ca eva jagad-īśvaratva-rūpaṃ brahma-lakṣaṇaṃ na saṃgaccheta \| asādhāraṇasya hi lakṣaṇatvam \| tathā pradeśaḥ ca mātropāsīkā eva bodhitayā tat eva aita bahu syāṃ prajāyeya iti tan na jīyamānaṃ brahma vā idam agre āsīt ekam eva sat eva āsīt sa ha vyabhavat tat ca gau-rūpam atyṛjata kṣetraṃ yāni eva etāni devatra akṣatrāṇi indraḥ varuṇaḥ somaḥ rudraḥ parjanyaḥ yamaḥ mṛtyuḥ īśāna iti ātmā vā idam ekaḥ eva agre āsīt na anyat kiñcana miṣat sa īkṣata lokān nu sṛjā iti sa imān lokān asṛjata eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt na brahmā na īśānaḥ na ime dyāvā-pṛthivī na nakṣatrāṇi na āpaḥ na agniḥ na somaḥ na sūryaḥ sa ekākī na rameta tasya dhyāna-anta-sthasya ekā kanyā daśa-indriyāṇi ity-ādiṣu \| ataḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivyā antaro yaṃ pṛthivī na veda iti ārabhya ātmāni tiṣṭhan ātmānam antaro yaṃ ātmā na veda iti avadhiṣu ca nikhila-jagat-niyamanaṃ parama-puruṣa-prakṛtyā eva śrūyate \| asmin nihitatvāt ca—na ca etat nu nikhila-jagat-niyamana-pratyanīkatve muktasya sādhāraṇam asti, yena jagad-vyāpāra-ratyayā api syāt \|\| 17 \|\|

## \|\| 4.4.18 \|\|

*pratyakṣa-upadeśāt iti cet na ādhikārika-maṇḍala-stha-ukteḥ*

**Комментарий:**

sa svarāṭ bhavati tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu kāma-cāraḥ bhavati imān lokān kāmān nī kāma-rūpī anusaṃcara iti pratyakṣeṇa—śrutvā muktasya jagad-vyāpāraḥ upadiśyate; ataḥ na jagad-vyāpāra-vivarjitaṃ ca etat; tatra, ādhikārika-maṇḍala-stha-ukteḥ; ādhikārikaḥ-adhikāreṣu niyuktā hiraṇyagarbha-ādayaḥ, maṇḍalāni-teṣāṃ lokāḥ; tat-saṃsthā-bhogāḥ; mukta-vyāpāra-vaśyasya bhavanti ity arthaḥ \| tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu kāma-cāraḥ bhavati iti ādi na ucyate \| arka-mṛttikā-sthānāṃ muktaḥ vikāra-lokena brahma-vibhūti-bhūtam anubhūya yathā-kāmaṃ tṛpyati iti arthaḥ \| tat evaṃ vikāra-antar-vartinā ādhikārika-maṇḍala-sthān sarvān bhogān brahma-vibhūti-bhūtān anubhavati iti eva vākyāt ucyate; na jagad-vyāpāraḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

yadi saṃsāri-vat muktaḥ api vikāra-antar-vartinaḥ bhogān bhuṅkte, tarhi baddhasya muktasya api anantara-veda eva bhogya-jātam alam ca syāt; tatra āha—

## \|\| 4.4.19 \|\|

*vikāra-avarti ca tathā hi sthitim āha*

**Комментарий:**

vikāre—janma-ādike na vartata iti vikāra-avarti; siddhaṃ nikhila-vikāra-nirvidheya-pratyanīka-kalyāṇa-ekatānāṃ, niratiśaya-jñānaṃ paraṃ brahma sa-vibhūtiṃ anavadhika-atiśaya-asamkhyeya-kalyāṇa-guṇam anubhavati iti

muktaḥ \| tat dhi bhūyaḥ-darśanena vikāra-vartināṃ lokānām api mukti-bhogyatvam \| tathā hi parasmin brahmaṇi nirvikāre 'nadhikātiśaya-ānande muktasya anubhūyamāne sthitim āha śrutiḥ— “saḥ ha evaṃ evaṃ sahasra-śīrṣā-jñopaniṣad-ujjvalite 'smin pratiṣṭhāṃ vindate, atha saḥ abhayaṃ gataḥ bhavati bhavati” “rasaḥ vai saḥ; rasaṃ hi eva ayaṃ labdhvā ānandī bhavati” ity-ādikā \| tat dhi mukti-sūtreṣu ca jagat-tantraṃ vadante “tasmin lokāḥ śritāḥ sarve tat u na atyeti kaścana” iti śruteḥ \| ataḥ sarva-vibhūtikaṃ brahmānubhava-svāra-anantya-vinaḥ ādhikārika-maṇḍala-sthā api bhogyaṃ śuddhiḥ iti “sarveṣu lokeṣu kāma-cāra” iti āniniścīyate; na muktasya jagad-vyāpāraḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## \|\| 4.4.20 \|\|

*darśayataḥ ca evaṃ pratyakṣa-anumāne*

**Комментарий:**

asya pratyag-ātmanaḥ muktasya nitya-ātma-bhūtasya nitya-bhūta-parama-puruṣa-sādhāraṇe jagad-vyāpāra-rūḍhaṃ niyamanaṃ na saṃbhavati iti uktam \| nikhila-jagat-niyamana-rūpaḥ vyāpāraḥ parama-puruṣa-sādhāraṇaḥ iti darśayataḥ śruti-smṛtī— “bhīṣā asmāt vātaḥ pavate, bhīṣā udeti sūryaḥ: bhīṣā asmāt agniḥ ca indraḥ ca, mṛtyuḥ dhāvati pañcamaḥ” iti “yasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi sūrya-candramasau vidhṛtau tiṣṭhataḥ” ity-ādi \| tathā “eṣaḥ sarva-īśvaraḥ eṣaḥ bhūta-adhipatiḥ eṣaḥ bhūta-pālaḥ eṣaḥ setuḥ vidharaṇaḥ eṣāṃ lokānām asaṃbhedāya” iti ca śrutiḥ \| smṛtiḥ api “mayā adhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate \| hetunā anena kaunteya jagat dhi parivartate \|\|” iti, “viṣṭabhya aham idaṃ kṛtsnam eka-aṃśena sthitaḥ jagat” iti ca \| tathā muktasya satya-saṅkalpatva-ādi-pūrvokta-svānandatvasya parama-puruṣaḥ eva hetuḥ iti śruti-smṛtī darśayataḥ “eṣaḥ hi eva ānandayāti, kaṃ ca hi eva anyāt kaḥ prāṇyāt cet eṣaḥ ākāśaḥ ānandaḥ na syāt \| eṣaḥ guṇān samatītya etān brahma-bhūyāya kalpate \|\| brahmaṇaḥ hi pratiṣṭhā aham amṛtasya avyayasya ca \| śāśvatasya ca dharmasya sukhasya eka-antikasya ca \|\|” iti \| yady api aṣṭā-pāśruti-viṣayasya satya-saṅkalpatva-paryantaḥ guṇa-gaṇaḥ pratyag-ātmanaḥ svābhāvikaḥ eva āvirbhūtaḥ; tathā api tasya tathā-vidhatvam eva parama-puruṣa-āyattam; tasya nitya-sthitiḥ ca tat-āyattā; parama-puruṣasya antarikṣāyāḥ nitya-īśvaratvāt nityatayā vartate iti na kaścit virodhaḥ \| evam eva parama-puruṣa-bhoga-upakaraṇasya līlā-upakaraṇasya ca nityatayā śāstra-avagatasya parama-puruṣasya nitya-īśvaratvena tathā-avasthānam asti iti śāstrāt avagamyate \| ataḥ muktasya satya-saṅkalpatvaṃ parama-puruṣa-sādhyaṃ ca jagad-vyāpāra-varjam \|\| 20 \|\|

## \|\| 4.4.21 \|\|

*bhoga-mātra-sāmya-liṅgāt ca*

**Комментарий:**

brahma-upāsakasya muktasya āhāra-rūpa-bhoga-mātre muktasya brahma-sāmya-pratipādanāt ca liṅgāt jagad-vyāpāra-varjitam eva avagamyate “saḥ aśnute sarvān kāmān saha, brahmaṇā vipaścitā” iti \| ataḥ muktasya parama-puruṣa-sāmyaṃ satya-saṅkalpatvaṃ ca parama-puruṣa-ātmatā-ādhārāt nikhila-jagat-viṣaya-na-kṣudhā-anuguṇena varṇanīyam iti jagad-vyāpāra-varjam eva mukta-aiśvaryaṃ \|\| 21 \|\|

yadi parama-puruṣa-dhyānaṃ mokṣopāyam, tarhi tasya svatantrayā eva tat-tad-īrūpa-upāsanasya punar-āvṛtti-saṃbhavād iti atra āha—

## \|\| 4.4.22 \|\|

*anāvṛtti-śabdāt anāvṛtti-śabdāt*

**Комментарий:**

yathā nikhila-heya-pratyanīkatānaika-kalyāṇa-guṇatāno jāgaj-janmādi-kāraṇaṃ samasta-vastu-vida-lakṣaṇa-sarvajña-satyaṅkalpaḥ āśrita-vātsalya-eka-jaladhiḥ parama-kāruṇikaḥ nirasta-samābhyadhikābhāvaḥ para-brahma-abhidhānaḥ parama-puruṣaḥ asti iti śabdāt avagamyate; evam ahar-ahar upacīyamāna-āvarṇya-śreṣṭha-dhyāna-anuguṇīta-tad-upāsana-rūpa-tat-saṃārādhana-prītaḥ upāsīnām anādi-kāla-pravṛtta-ananta-duṣṭara-karma-saṃcayaṃ kṣapayitvā vidhi-nivṛttaṃ sva-yāthātmya-yāthātmyaṃ bhagavān adhikāti-nirayānandaṃ prāpya punar nivartayati iti śabdād eva avagamyate \| śabdaḥ ca sa khalu evaṃ vartayan yāvad-āyuṣaṃ brahma-lokam abhisaṃpadyate na ca punaḥ āvartate na ca punaḥ āvartate iti-ādikaḥ \| tathā ca bhagavān svayam eva uktaṃ mām upetya punar-janma duḥkhālayaṃ aśāśvatam \| na āpnuvanti mahātmānaḥ saṃsiddhiṃ paramāṃ gatāḥ \|\| ā-brahma-bhuvanāl lokāḥ punar-āvartinaḥ arjuna \| mām upetya tu kaunteya punar-janma na vidyate \|\| iti \| na ca aniṣṭhya-saṃvedyatayā asaṃkucita-jñānasya para-brahma-anubhava-eka-rasa-bhāvasya tad-eka-priya-sanā-vidhikātiśayānandaṃ brahma-anubhava-udaka-apekṣā tad-arthaṃ sādhu-saṃvāda-purvānityāḍhyaḥ \| na ca parama-puruṣa-saṃśleṣaḥ api arthitīyaṃ jñāninaṃ laṭavyā kadācit avāñchitā iti; yaḥ evaṃ āha— priyaḥ hi jñāninaḥ atyartham ahaṃ sa ca mama priyaḥ \| udārāḥ sarve eva ete jñānī tu ātmā eva me matam \|\| āsthitaḥ sa hi yukta-ātmā mām eva anuttamāṃ gatim \| bahūnāṃ janmanām ante jñānavān māṃ prapadyate \|\| vāsudevaḥ sarvam iti sa mahātmā su-durlabhaḥ iti \| sūtra-abhyāsa-śāstra-parisamāptiṃ dyotayati \| iti sarvaṃ samañjasam \|\| 22 \|\|

iti jagad-vyāpāra-varja-adhikaraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|

iti śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-viracite śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣye caturthasya adhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

samāptaḥ ca adhyāyaḥ \|\| 4 \|\| śāstraṃ ca parisamāptam \|\|

śrī-bhagavad-rāmānuja-grathyālalāṃ śārīraka-mīmāṃsā-bhāṣyaṃ samāptam \|\|
